(1,1.1 ) ye triṣaptāḥ pariyanti viśvā rūpāṇi bibhrataḥ |
(1,1.1 ) vācaspatirbalā teṣāṃ tanvo adya dadhātu me ||1||

(1,1.2 ) punarehi vacaspate devena manasā saha |
(1,1.2 ) vasoṣpate ni ramaya mayyevāstu mayi śrutam ||2||

(1,1.3 ) ihaivābhi vi tanūbhe ārtnī iva jyayā |
(1,1.3 ) vācaspatirni yachatu mayyevāstu mayi śrutam ||3||

(1,1.4 ) upahūto vācaspatirupāsmān vācaspatirhvayatām |
(1,1.4 ) saṃ śrutena gamemahi mā śrutena vi rādhiṣi ||4||



(1,2.1 ) vidmā śarasya pitaraṃ parjanyaṃ bhūridhāyasam |
(1,2.1 ) vidmo ṣvasya mātaraṃ pṛthivīṃ bhūrivarpasam ||1||

(1,2.2 ) jyāke pari ṇo namāśmānaṃ tanvaṃ kṛdhi |
(1,2.2 ) vīḍurvarīyo'rātīrapa dveṣāṃsyā kṛdhi ||2||

(1,2.3 ) vṛkṣaṃ yadgāvaḥ pariṣasvajānā anusphuraṃ śaramarcantyṛbhum |
(1,2.3 ) śarumasmadyāvaya didyumindra ||3||

(1,2.4 ) yathā dyāṃ ca pṛthivīṃ cāntastiṣṭhati tejanam |
(1,2.4 ) evā rogaṃ cāsrāvaṃ cāntastiṣṭhatu muñja it||4||



(1,3.1 ) vidmā śarasya pitaraṃ parjanyaṃ śatavṛṣṇyam |
(1,3.1 ) tenā te tanve śaṃ karaṃ pṛthivyāṃ te niṣecanaṃ bahiṣṭe astu bāliti ||1||

(1,3.2 ) vidmā śarasya pitaraṃ mitraṃ śatavṛṣṇyam |
(1,3.2 ) tenā te tanve śaṃ karaṃ pṛthivyāṃ te niṣecanaṃ bahiṣṭe astu bāliti ||2||

(1,3.3 ) vidmā śarasya pitaraṃ varuṇaṃ śatavṛṣṇyam |
(1,3.3 ) tenā te tanve śaṃ karaṃ pṛthivyāṃ te niṣecanaṃ bahiṣṭe astu bāliti ||3||

(1,3.4 ) vidmā śarasya pitaraṃ candraṃ śatavṛṣṇyam |
(1,3.4 ) tenā te tanve śaṃ karaṃ pṛthivyāṃ te niṣecanaṃ bahiṣṭe astu bāliti ||4||

(1,3.5 ) vidmā śarasya pitaraṃ sūryaṃ śatavṛṣṇyam |
(1,3.5 ) tenā te tanve śaṃ karaṃ pṛthivyāṃ te niṣecanaṃ bahiṣṭe astu bāliti ||5||

(1,3.6 ) yadāntreṣu gavīnyoryadvastāvadhi saṃśritam |
(1,3.6 ) evā te mūtraṃ mucyatāṃ bahirbāliti sarvakam ||6||

(1,3.7 ) pra te bhinadmi mehanaṃ vartraṃ veśantyā iva |
(1,3.7 ) evā te mūtraṃ mucyatāṃ bahirbāliti sarvakam ||7||

(1,3.8 ) viṣitaṃ te vastibilaṃ samudrasyodadheriva |
(1,3.8 ) evā te mūtraṃ mucyatāṃ bahirbāliti sarvakam ||8||

(1,3.9 ) yatheṣukā parāpatadavasṛṣṭādhi dhanvanaḥ |
(1,3.9 ) evā te mūtraṃ mucyatāṃ bahirbāliti sarvakam ||9||



(1,4.1 ) ambayo yantyadhvabhirjāmayo adhvarīyatām |
(1,4.1 ) pṛñcatīrmadhunā payaḥ ||1||

(1,4.2 ) amūryā upa sūrye yābhirvā sūryaḥ saha |
(1,4.2 ) tā no hinvantvadhvaram ||2||

(1,4.3 ) apo devīrupa hvaye yatra gāvaḥ pibanti naḥ |
(1,4.3 ) sindhubhyaḥ kartvaṃ haviḥ ||3||

(1,4.4 ) apsvantaramṛtamapsu bheṣajam |
(1,4.4 ) apāmuta praśastibhiraśvā bhavatha vājino gāvo bhavatha vājinīḥ ||4||



(1,5.1 ) āpo hi ṣṭhā mayobhuvastā na ūrje dadhātana |
(1,5.1 ) mahe raṇāya cakṣase ||1||

(1,5.2 ) yo vaḥ śivatamo rasastasya bhājayateha naḥ |
(1,5.2 ) uśatīriva mātaraḥ ||2||

(1,5.3 ) tasmā araṃ gamāma vo yasya kṣayāya jinvatha |
(1,5.3 ) āpo janayathā ca naḥ ||3||

(1,5.4 ) īśānā vāryāṇāṃ kṣayantīścarṣaṇīnām |
(1,5.4 ) apo yācāmi bheṣajam ||4||



(1,6.1 ) śaṃ no devīrabhiṣṭaya āpo bhavantu pītaye |
(1,6.1 ) śaṃ yorabhi sravantu naḥ ||1||

(1,6.2 ) apsu me somo abravīdantarviśvāni bheṣajā |
(1,6.2 ) agniṃ ca viśvaśaṃbhuvam ||2||

(1,6.3 ) āpaḥ pṛṇīta bheṣajaṃ varūthaṃ tanve mama |
(1,6.3 ) jyokca sūryaṃ dṛśe ||3||

(1,6.4 ) śaṃ na āpo dhanvanyāḥ śamu santvanūpyāḥ |
(1,6.4 ) śaṃ naḥ khanitrimā āpaḥ śamu yāḥ kumbha ābhṛtāḥ |
(1,6.4 ) śivā naḥ santu vārṣikīḥ ||4||



(1,7.1 ) stuvānamagna ā vaha yātudhānaṃ kimīdinam |
(1,7.1 ) tvaṃ hi deva vandito hantā dasyorbabhūvitha ||1||

(1,7.2 ) ājyasya parameṣṭhin jātavedastanūvaśin |
(1,7.2 ) agne taulasya prāśāna yātudhānān vi lāpaya ||2||

(1,7.3 ) vi lapantu yātudhānā attriṇo ye kimīdinaḥ |
(1,7.3 ) athedamagne no havirindraśca prati haryatam ||3||

(1,7.4 ) agniḥ pūrva ā rabhatāṃ prendro nudatu bāhumān |
(1,7.4 ) bravītu sarvo yātumān ayamasmītyetya ||4||

(1,7.5 ) paśyāma te vīryaṃ jātavedaḥ pra ṇo brūhi yātudhānān nṛcakṣaḥ |
(1,7.5 ) tvayā sarve paritaptāḥ purastātta ā yantu prabruvāṇā upedam ||5||

(1,7.6 ) ā rabhasva jātavedo'smākārthāya jajñiṣe |
(1,7.6 ) dūto no agne bhūtvā yātudhānān vi lāpaya ||6||

(1,7.7 ) tvamagne yātudhānān upabaddhāmihā vaha |
(1,7.7 ) athaiṣāmindro vajreṇāpi śīrṣāṇi vṛścatu ||7||



(1,8.1 ) idaṃ haviryātudhānān nādī phenamivā vahat|
(1,8.1 ) ya idaṃ strī pumān akariha sa stuvatāṃ janaḥ ||1||

(1,8.2 ) ayaṃ stuvāna āgamadimaṃ sma prati haryata |
(1,8.2 ) bṛhaspate vaśe labdhvāgnīṣomā vi vidhyatam ||2||

(1,8.3 ) yātudhānasya somapa jahi prajāṃ nayasva ca |
(1,8.3 ) ni stuvānasya pātaya paramakṣyutāvaram ||3||

(1,8.4 ) yatraiṣāmagne janimāni vettha guhā satāmattriṇāṃ jātavedaḥ |
(1,8.4 ) tāṃstvaṃ brahmaṇā vāvṛdhāno jahyeṣāṃ śatatarhamagne ||4||



(1,9.1 ) asmin vasu vasavo dhārayantvindraḥ pūṣā varuṇo mitro agniḥ |
(1,9.1 ) imamādityā uta viśve ca devā uttarasmin jyotiṣi dhārayantu ||1||

(1,9.2 ) asya devāḥ pradiśi jyotirastu sūryo agniruta vā hiraṇyam |
(1,9.2 ) sapatnā asmadadhare bhavantūttamaṃ nākamadhi rohayemam ||2||

(1,9.3 ) yenendrāya samabharaḥ payāṃsyuttamena brahmaṇā jātavedaḥ |
(1,9.3 ) tena tvamagna iha vardhayemaṃ sajātānāṃ śraiṣṭhya ā dhehyenam ||3||

(1,9.4 ) aiṣāṃ yajñamuta varco dade'haṃ rāyaspoṣamuta cittānyagne |
(1,9.4 ) sapatnā asmadadhare bhavantūttamaṃ nākamadhi rohayemam ||4||



(1,10.1 ) ayaṃ devānāmasuro vi rājati vaśā hi satyā varuṇasya rājñaḥ |
(1,10.1 ) tataspari brahmaṇā śāśadāna ugrasya manyorudimaṃ nayāmi ||1||

(1,10.2 ) namaste rajan varuṇāstu manyave viśvaṃ hyugra nicikeṣi drugdham |
(1,10.2 ) sahasramanyān pra suvāmi sākaṃ śataṃ jīvāti śaradastavāyam ||2||

(1,10.3 ) yaduvakthānṛtaṃ jihvayā vṛjinaṃ bahu |
(1,10.3 ) rājñastvā satyadharmaṇo muñcāmi varuṇādaham ||3||

(1,10.4 ) muñcāmi tvā vaiśvānarādarṇavān mahataspari |
(1,10.4 ) sajātān ugrehā vada brahma cāpa cikīhi naḥ ||4||



(1,11.1 ) vaṣaṭte pūṣann asmintsūtāvaryamā hotā kṛṇotu vedhāḥ |
(1,11.1 ) sisratāṃ nāryṛtaprajātā vi parvāṇi jihatāṃ sūtavā u ||1||

(1,11.2 ) catasro divaḥ pradiśaścatasro bhūmyā uta |
(1,11.2 ) devā garbhaṃ samairayan taṃ vyūrṇuvantu sūtave ||2||

(1,11.3 ) sūṣā vyūrṇotu vi yoniṃ hāpayāmasi |
(1,11.3 ) śrathayā sūṣaṇe tvamava tvaṃ biṣkale sṛja ||3||

(1,11.4 ) neva māṃse na pīvasi neva majjasvāhatam |
(1,11.4 ) avaitu pṛśni śevalaṃ śune jarāyvattave'va jarāyu padyatām ||4||

(1,11.5 ) vi te bhinadmi mehanaṃ vi yoniṃ vi gavīnike |
(1,11.5 ) vi mātaraṃ ca putraṃ ca vi kumāraṃ jarāyuṇāva jarāyu padyatām ||5||

(1,11.6 ) yathā vāto yathā mano yathā patanti pakṣiṇaḥ |
(1,11.6 ) evā tvaṃ daśamāsya sākaṃ jarāyuṇā patāva jarāyu padyatām ||6||



(1,12.1 ) jarāyujaḥ prathama usriyo vṛṣā vātābhrajā stanayann eti vṛṣṭyā |
(1,12.1 ) sa no mṛḍāti tanva ṛjugo rujan ya ekamojastredhā vicakrame ||1||

(1,12.2 ) aṅgeaṅge śociṣā śiśriyāṇaṃ namasyantastvā haviṣā vidhema |
(1,12.2 ) aṅkāntsamaṅkān haviṣā vidhema yo agrabhītparvāsyā grabhītā ||2||

(1,12.3 ) muñca śīrṣaktyā uta kāsa enaṃ paruṣparurāviveśā yo asya |
(1,12.3 ) yo abhrajā vātajā yaśca śuṣmo vanaspatīntsacatāṃ parvatāṃśca ||3||

(1,12.4 ) śaṃ me parasmai gātrāya śamastvavarāya me |
(1,12.4 ) śaṃ me caturbhyo aṅgebhyaḥ śamastu tanve mama ||4||



(1,13.1 ) namaste astu vidyute namaste stanayitnave |
(1,13.1 ) namaste astvaśmane yenā dūḍāśe asyasi ||1||

(1,13.2 ) namaste pravato napādyatastapaḥ samūhasi |
(1,13.2 ) mṛḍayā nastanūbhyo mayastokebhyaskṛdhi ||2||

(1,13.3 ) pravato napān nama evāstu tubhyaṃ namaste hetaye tapuṣe ca kṛṇmaḥ |
(1,13.3 ) vidma te dhāma paramaṃ guhā yatsamudre antarnihitāsi nābhiḥ ||3||

(1,13.4 ) yāṃ tvā devā asṛjanta viśva iṣuṃ kṛṇvānā asanāya dhṛṣṇum |
(1,13.4 ) sā no mṛḍa vidathe gṛṇānā tasyai te namo astu devi ||4||



(1,14.1 ) bhagamasyā varca ādiṣyadhi vṛkṣādiva srajam |
(1,14.1 ) mahābudhna iva parvato jyokpitṛṣvāstām ||1||

(1,14.2 ) eṣā te rājan kanyā vadhūrni dhūyatāṃ yama |
(1,14.2 ) sā māturbadhyatāṃ gṛhe'tho bhrāturatho pituḥ ||2||

(1,14.3 ) eṣā te kulapā rājan tāmu te pari dadmasi |
(1,14.3 ) jyokpitṛṣvāsātā ā śīrṣṇaḥ śamopyāt||3||

(1,14.4 ) asitasya te brahmaṇā kaśyapasya gayasya ca |
(1,14.4 ) antaḥkośamiva jāmayo'pi nahyāmi te bhagam ||4 ||



(1,15.1 ) saṃ saṃ sravantu sindhavaḥ saṃ vātāḥ saṃ patatriṇaḥ |
(1,15.1 ) imaṃ yajñaṃ pradivo me juṣantāṃ saṃsrāvyeṇa haviṣā juhomi ||1||

(1,15.2 ) ihaiva havamā yāta ma iha saṃsrāvaṇā utemaṃ vardhayatā giraḥ |
(1,15.2 ) ihaitu sarvo yaḥ paśurasmin tiṣṭhatu yā rayiḥ ||2||

(1,15.3 ) ye nadīnāṃ saṃsravantyutsāsaḥ sadamakṣitāḥ |
(1,15.3 ) tebhirme sarvaiḥ saṃsrāvairdhanaṃ saṃ srāvayāmasi ||3||

(1,15.4 ) ye sarpiṣaḥ saṃsravanti kṣīrasya codakasya ca
(1,15.4 ) tebhirme sarvaiḥ saṃsrāvairdhanaṃ saṃ srāvayāmasi ||4||
(1,16.1 ) ye'māvāsyāṃ rātrimudasthurvrājamattriṇaḥ |
(1,16.1 ) agnisturīyo yātuhā so asmabhyamadhi bravat||1||

(1,16.2 ) sīsāyādhyāha varuṇaḥ sīsāyāgnirupāvati |
(1,16.2 ) sīsaṃ ma indraḥ prāyachattadaṅga yātucātanam ||2||

(1,16.3 ) idaṃ viṣkandhaṃ sahata idaṃ bādhate attriṇaḥ |
(1,16.3 ) anena viśvā sasahe yā jātāni piśācyāḥ ||3||

(1,16.4 ) yadi no gāṃ haṃsi yadyaśvaṃ yadi pūruṣam |
(1,16.4 ) taṃ tvā sīsena vidhyāmo yathā no'so avīrahā ||4||



(1,17.1 ) amūryā yanti yoṣito hirā lohitavāsasaḥ |
(1,17.1 ) abhrātara iva jāmayastiṣṭhantu hatavarcasaḥ ||1||

(1,17.2 ) tiṣṭhāvare tiṣṭha para uta tvaṃ tiṣṭha madhyame |
(1,17.2 ) kaniṣṭhikā ca tiṣṭhati tiṣṭhādiddhamanirmahī ||2||

(1,17.3 ) śatasya dhamanīnāṃ sahasrasya hirāṇām |
(1,17.3 ) asthurin madhyamā imāḥ sākamantā araṃsata ||3||

(1,17.4 ) pari vaḥ sikatāvatī dhanūrbṛhatyakramīt|
(1,17.4 ) tiṣṭhatelayatā su kam ||4||



(1,18.1 ) nirlakṣmyaṃ lalāmyaṃ nirarātiṃ suvāmasi |
(1,18.1 ) atha yā bhadrā tāni naḥ prajāyā arātiṃ nayāmasi ||1||

(1,18.2 ) niraraṇiṃ savitā sāviṣakpadornirhastayorvaruṇo mitro aryamā |
(1,18.2 ) nirasmabhyamanumatī rarāṇā premāṃ devā asāviṣuḥ saubhagāya ||2||

(1,18.3 ) yatta ātmani tanvāṃ ghoramasti yadvā keśeṣu praticakṣaṇe vā |
(1,18.3 ) sarvaṃ tadvācāpa hanmo vayaṃ devastvā savitā sūdayatu ||3||

(1,18.4 ) riśyapadīṃ vṛṣadatīṃ goṣedhāṃ vidhamāmuta |
(1,18.4 ) vilīḍhyaṃ lalāmyaṃ tā asman nāśayāmasi ||4||


(1,19.1 ) mā no vidan vivyādhino mo abhivyādhino vidan |
(1,19.1 ) ārāccharavyā asmadviṣūcīrindra pātaya ||1||

(1,19.2 ) viṣvañco asmaccharavaḥ patantu ye astā ye cāsyāḥ |
(1,19.2 ) daivīrmanuṣyesavo mamāmitrān vi vidhyata ||2||

(1,19.3 ) yo naḥ svo yo araṇaḥ sajāta uta niṣṭyo yo asmāmabhidāsati |
(1,19.3 ) rudraḥ śaravyayaitān mamāmitrān vi vidhyatu ||3||

(1,19.4 ) yaḥ sapatno yo'sapatno yaśca dviṣan chapāti naḥ |
(1,19.4 ) devāstaṃ sarve dhūrvantu brahma varma mamāntaram ||4||



(1,20.1 ) adārasṛdbhavatu deva somāsmin yajñe maruto mṛḍatā naḥ |
(1,20.1 ) mā no vidadabhibhā mo aśastirmā no vidadvṛjinā dveṣyā yā ||1||

(1,20.2 ) yo adya senyo vadho'ghāyūnāmudīrate |
(1,20.2 ) yuvaṃ taṃ mitrāvaruṇāvasmadyāvayataṃ pari ||2||

(1,20.3 ) itaśca yadamutaśca yadvadhaṃ varuṇa yāvaya |
(1,20.3 ) vi mahaccharma yacha varīyo yāvayā vadham ||3||

(1,20.4 ) śāsa itthā mahāmasyamitrasāho astṛtaḥ |
(1,20.4 ) na yasya hanyate sakhā na jīyate kadā cana ||4||



(1,21.1 ) svastidā viśāṃ patirvṛtrahā vimṛdho vaśī |
(1,21.1 ) vṛṣendraḥ pura etu naḥ somapā abhayaṃkaraḥ ||1||

(1,21.2 ) vi na indra mṛdho jahi nīcā yacha pṛtanyataḥ |
(1,21.2 ) adhamaṃ gamayā tamo yo asmāmabhidāsati ||2||

(1,21.3 ) vi rakṣo vi mṛdho jahi vi vṛtrasya hanū ruja |
(1,21.3 ) vi manyumindra vṛtrahann amitrasyābhidāsataḥ ||3||

(1,21.4 ) apendra dviṣato mano'pa jijyāsato vadham |
(1,21.4 ) vi mahaccharma yacha varīyo yāvayā vadham ||4||



(1,22.1 ) anu sūryamudayatāṃ hṛddyoto harimā ca te |
(1,22.1 ) go rohitasya varṇena tena tvā pari dadhmasi ||1||

(1,22.2 ) pari tvā rohitairvarṇairdīrghāyutvāya dadhmasi |
(1,22.2 ) yathāyamarapā asadatho aharito bhuvat||2||

(1,22.3 ) yā rohiṇīrdevatyā gāvo yā uta rohiṇīḥ |
(1,22.3 ) rūpaṃrūpaṃ vayovayastābhiṣṭvā pari dadhmasi ||3||

(1,22.4 ) śukeṣu te harimāṇaṃ ropaṇākāsu dadhmasi |
(1,22.4 ) atho hāridraveṣu te harimāṇaṃ ni dadhmasi ||4||



(1,23.1 ) naktaṃjātāsi oṣadhe rāme kṛṣṇe asikni ca |
(1,23.1 ) idaṃ rajani rajaya kilāsaṃ palitaṃ ca yat||1||

(1,23.2 ) kilāsaṃ ca palitaṃ ca nirito nāśayā pṛṣat|
(1,23.2 ) ā tvā svo viśatāṃ varṇaḥ parā śuklāni pātaya ||2||

(1,23.3 ) asitaṃ te pralayanamāsthānamasitaṃ tava |
(1,23.3 ) asiknī asyoṣadhe nirito nāśayā pṛṣat||3||

(1,23.4 ) asthijasya kilāsasya tanūjasya ca yattvaci |
(1,23.4 ) dūṣyā kṛtasya brahmaṇā lakṣma śvetamanīnaśam ||4||


(1,24.1 ) suparṇo jātaḥ prathamastasya tvaṃ pittamāsitha |
(1,24.1 ) tadāsurī yudhā jitā rūpaṃ cakre vanaspatīn ||1||
(1,24.2 ) āsurī cakre prathamedaṃ kilāsabheṣajamidaṃ kilāsanāśanam |
(1,24.2 ) anīnaśatkilāsaṃ sarūpāmakarattvacam ||2||

(1,24.3 ) sarūpā nāma te mātā sarūpo nāma te pitā |
(1,24.3 ) sarūpakṛttvamoṣadhe sā sarūpamidaṃ kṛdhi ||3||

(1,24.4 ) śyāmā sarūpaṃkaraṇī pṛthivyā adhyudbhṛtā |
(1,24.4 ) idamū ṣu pra sādhaya punā rūpāṇi kalpaya ||4||



(1,25.1 ) yadagnirāpo adahatpraviśya yatrākṛṇvan dharmadhṛto namāṃsi |
(1,25.1 ) tatra ta āhuḥ paramaṃ janitraṃ sa naḥ saṃvidvān pari vṛṅgdhi takman ||1||

(1,25.2 ) yadyarciryadi vāsi śociḥ śakalyeṣi yadi vā te janitram |
(1,25.2 ) hrūḍurnāmāsi haritasya deva sa naḥ saṃvidvān pari vṛṅgdhi takman ||2||

(1,25.3 ) yadi śoko yadi vābhiśoko yadi vā rājño varuṇasyāsi putraḥ |
(1,25.3 ) hrūḍurnāmāsi haritasya deva sa naḥ saṃvidvān pari vṛṅgdhi takman ||3||

(1,25.4 ) namaḥ śītāya takmane namo rūrāya śociṣe kṛṇomi |
(1,25.4 ) yo anyedyurubhayadyurabhyeti tṛtīyakāya namo astu takmane ||4||



(1,26.1 ) āre'sāvasmadastu hetirdevāso asat|
(1,26.1 ) āre aśmā yamasyatha ||1||

(1,26.2 ) sakhāsāvasmabhyamastu rātiḥ sakhendro bhagaḥ |
(1,26.2 ) savitā citrarādhāḥ ||2||

(1,26.3 ) yūyaṃ naḥ pravato napān marutaḥ sūryatvacasaḥ |
(1,26.3 ) śarma yachatha saprathāḥ ||3||

(1,26.4 ) suṣūdata mṛḍata mṛḍayā nastanūbhyo |
(1,26.4 ) mayastokebhyaskṛdhi ||4||



(1,27.1 ) amūḥ pāre pṛdākvastriṣaptā nirjarāyavaḥ |
(1,27.1 ) tāsāṃ jarāyubhirvayamakṣyāvapi vyayāmasyaghāyoḥ paripanthinaḥ ||1||

(1,27.2 ) viṣūcyetu kṛntatī pinākamiva bibhratī |
(1,27.2 ) viṣvakpunarbhuvā mano'samṛddhā aghāyavaḥ ||2||

(1,27.3 ) na bahavaḥ samaśakan nārbhakā abhi dādhṛṣuḥ |
(1,27.3 ) veṇoradgā ivābhito'samṛddhā aghāyavaḥ ||3||

(1,27.4 ) pretaṃ pādau pra sphurataṃ vahataṃ pṛṇato gṛhān |
(1,27.4 ) indrānyetu prathamājītāmuṣitā puraḥ ||4||


(1,28.1 ) upa prāgāddevo agnī rakṣohāmīvacātanaḥ |
(1,28.1 ) dahann apa dvayāvino yātudhānān kimīdinaḥ ||1||

(1,28.2 ) prati daha yātudhānān prati deva kimīdinaḥ |
(1,28.2 ) pratīcīḥ kṛṣṇavartane saṃ daha yātudhānyaḥ ||2||

(1,28.3 ) yā śaśāpa śapanena yāghaṃ mūramādadhe |
(1,28.3 ) yā rasasya haraṇāya jātamārebhe tokamattu sā ||3||

(1,28.4 ) putramattu yātudhānīḥ svasāramuta naptyam |
(1,28.4 ) adhā mitho vikeśyo vi ghnatāṃ yātudhānyo vi tṛhyantāmarāyyaḥ ||4||



(1,29.1 ) abhīvartena maṇinā yenendro abhivavṛdhe |
(1,29.1 ) tenāsmān brahmaṇaspate'bhi rāṣṭrāya vardhaya ||1||

(1,29.2 ) abhivṛtya sapatnān abhi yā no arātayaḥ |
(1,29.2 ) abhi pṛtanyantaṃ tiṣṭhābhi yo no durasyati ||2||

(1,29.3 ) abhi tvā devaḥ savitābhi ṣomo avīvṛdhat|
(1,29.3 ) abhi tvā viśvā bhūtānyabhīvarto yathāsasi ||3||

(1,29.4 ) abhīvarto abhibhavaḥ sapatnakṣayaṇo maṇiḥ |
(1,29.4 ) rāṣṭrāya mahyaṃ badhyatāṃ sapatnebhyaḥ parābhuve ||4||

(1,29.5 ) udasau sūryo agādudidaṃ māmakaṃ vacaḥ |
(1,29.5 ) yathāhaṃ śatruho'sānyasapatnaḥ sapatnahā ||5||

(1,29.6 ) sapatnakṣayaṇo vṛṣābhiraṣṭro viṣāsahiḥ |
(1,29.6 ) yathāhameṣāṃ vīrāṇāṃ virājāni janasya ca ||6||



(1,30.1 ) viśve devā vasavo rakṣatemamutādityā jāgṛta yūyamasmin |
(1,30.1 ) memaṃ sanābhiruta vānyanābhirmemaṃ prāpatpauruṣeyo vadho yaḥ ||1||

(1,30.2 ) ye vo devāḥ pitaro ye ca putrāḥ sacetaso me śṛṇutedamuktam |
(1,30.2 ) sarvebhyo vaḥ pari dadāmyetaṃ svastyenaṃ jarase vahātha ||2||

(1,30.3 ) ye devā divi ṣṭha ye pṛthivyāṃ ye antarikṣa oṣadhīṣu paśuṣvapsvantaḥ |
(1,30.3 ) te kṛṇuta jarasamāyurasmai śatamanyān pari vṛṇaktu mṛtyūn ||3||

(1,30.4 ) yeṣāṃ prayājā uta vānuyājā hutabhāgā ahutādaśca devāḥ |
(1,30.4 ) yeṣāṃ vaḥ pañca pradiśo vibhaktāstān vo asmai satrasadaḥ kṛṇomi ||4||



(1,31.1 ) āśānāmāśāpālebhyaścaturbhyo amṛtebhyaḥ |
(1,31.1 ) idaṃ bhūtasyādhyakṣebhyo vidhema haviṣā vayam ||1||

(1,31.2 ) ya āśānāmāśāpālāścatvāra sthana devāḥ |
(1,31.2 ) te no nirṛtyāḥ pāśebhyo muñcatāṃhasoaṃhasaḥ ||2||

(1,31.3 ) asrāmastvā haviṣā yajāmyaśloṇastvā ghṛtena juhomi |
(1,31.3 ) ya āśānāmāśāpālasturīyo devaḥ sa naḥ subhūtameha vakṣat||3||

(1,31.4 ) svasti mātra uta pitre no astu svasti gobhyo jagate puruṣebhyaḥ |
(1,31.4 ) viśvaṃ subhūtaṃ suvidatraṃ no astu jyogeva dṛśema sūryam ||4||


(1,32.1 ) idaṃ janāso vidatha mahadbrahma vadiṣyati |
(1,32.1 ) na tatpṛthivyāṃ no divi yena prāṇanti vīrudhaḥ ||1||

(1,32.2 ) antarikṣa āsāṃ sthāma śrāntasadāmiva |
(1,32.2 ) āsthānamasya bhūtasya viduṣṭadvedhaso na vā ||2||

(1,32.3 ) yadrodasī rejamāne bhūmiśca niratakṣatam |
(1,32.3 ) ārdraṃ tadadya sarvadā samudrasyeva śrotyāḥ ||3||

(1,32.4 ) viśvamanyāmabhīvāra tadanyasyāmadhi śritam |
(1,32.4 ) dive ca viśvavedase pṛthivyai cākaraṃ namaḥ ||4||



(1,33.1 ) hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucayaḥ pāvakā yāsu jātaḥ savitā yāsvagniḥ |
(1,33.1 ) yā agniṃ garbhaṃ dadhire suvarṇāstā na āpaḥ śaṃ syonā bhavantu ||1||

(1,33.2 ) yāsāṃ rājā varuṇo yāti madhye satyānṛte avapaśyan janānām |
(1,33.2 ) yā agniṃ garbhaṃ dadhire suvarṇāstā na āpaḥ śaṃ syonā bhavantu ||2||

(1,33.3 ) yāsāṃ devā divi kṛṇvanti bhakṣaṃ yā antarikṣe bahudhā bhavanti |
(1,33.3 ) yā agniṃ garbhaṃ dadhire suvarṇāstā na āpaḥ śaṃ syonā bhavantu ||3||

(1,33.4 ) śivena mā cakṣuṣā paśyatāpaḥ śivayā tanvopa spṛśata tvacaṃ me |
(1,33.4 ) ghṛtaścutaḥ śucayo yāḥ pāvakāstā na āpaḥ śaṃ syonā bhavantu ||4||



(1,34.1 ) iyaṃ vīrun madhujātā madhunā tvā khanāmasi |
(1,34.1 ) madhoradhi prajātāsi sā no madhumataskṛdhi ||1||

(1,34.2 ) jihvāyā agre madhu me jihvāmūle madhūlakam |
(1,34.2 ) mamedaha kratāvaso mama cittamupāyasi ||2||

(1,34.3 ) madhuman me nikramaṇaṃ madhuman me parāyaṇam |
(1,34.3 ) vācā vadāmi madhumadbhūyāsaṃ madhusaṃdṛśaḥ ||3||
(1,34.4 ) madhorasmi madhutaro madughān madhumattaraḥ |
(1,34.4 ) māmitkila tvaṃ vanāḥ śākhāṃ madhumatīmiva ||4||

(1,34.5 ) pari tvā paritatnunekṣuṇāgāmavidviṣe |
(1,34.5 ) yathā māṃ kaminyaso yathā man nāpagā asaḥ ||5||
(1,35.1 ) yadābadhnan dākṣāyaṇā hiraṇyaṃ śatānīkāya sumanasyamānāḥ |
(1,35.1 ) tatte badnāmyāyuṣe varcase balāya dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya ||1||

(1,35.2 ) nainaṃ rakṣāṃsi na piśācāḥ sahante devānāmojaḥ prathamajaṃ hyetat|
(1,35.2 ) yo bibharti dākṣāyaṇaṃ hiraṇyaṃ sa jīveṣu kṛṇute dīrghamāyuḥ ||2||

(1,35.3 ) apāṃ tejo jyotirojo balaṃ ca vanaspatīnāmuta vīryāṇi |
(1,35.3 ) indra ivendriyāṇyadhi dhārayāmo asmin taddakṣamāṇo bibharaddhiraṇyam ||3||

(1,35.4 ) samānāṃ māsāmṛtubhiṣṭvā vayaṃ saṃvatsarasya payasā piparmi |
(1,35.4 ) indrāgnī viśve devāste'nu manyantāmahṛṇīyamānāḥ ||4||



(2,1.1 ) venastatpaśyatparamaṃ guhā yadyatra viśvaṃ bhavatyekarūpam |
(2,1.1 ) idaṃ pṛśniraduhajjāyamānāḥ svarvido abhyanūṣata vrāḥ ||1||

(2,1.2 ) pra tadvocedamṛtasya vidvān gandharvo dhāma paramaṃ guhā yat|
(2,1.2 ) trīṇi padāni nihitā guhāsya yastāni veda sa pituṣpitāsat||2||

(2,1.3 ) sa naḥ pitā janitā sa uta bandhurdhāmāni veda bhuvanāni viśvā |
(2,1.3 ) yo devānāṃ nāmadha eka eva taṃ saṃpraśnaṃ bhuvanā yanti sarvā ||3||

(2,1.4 ) pari dyāvāpṛthivī sadya āyamupātiṣṭhe prathamajāmṛtasya |
(2,1.4 ) vācamiva vaktari bhuvaneṣṭhā dhāsyureṣa nanveṣo agniḥ ||4||

(2,1.5 ) pari viśvā bhuvanānyāyamṛtasya tantuṃ vitataṃ dṛśe kam |
(2,1.5 ) yatra devā amṛtamānaśānāḥ samāne yonāvadhyairayanta ||5||



(2,2.1 ) divyo gandharvo bhuvanasya yaspatireka eva namasyo vikṣvīḍyaḥ |
(2,2.1 ) taṃ tvā yaumi brahmaṇā divya deva namaste astu divi te sadhastham ||1||

(2,2.2 ) divi spṛṣṭo yajataḥ sūryatvagavayātā haraso daivyasya |
(2,2.2 ) mṛḍātgandharvo bhuvanasya yaspatireka eva namasyaḥ suśevāḥ ||2||

(2,2.3 ) anavadyābhiḥ samu jagma ābhirapsarāsvapi gandharva āsīt|
(2,2.3 ) samudra āsāṃ sadanaṃ ma āhuryataḥ sadya ā ca parā ca yanti ||3||

(2,2.4 ) abhriye didyun nakṣatriye yā viśvāvasuṃ gandharvaṃ sacadhve |
(2,2.4 ) tābhyo vo devīrnama itkṛṇomi ||4||

(2,2.5 ) yāḥ klandāstamiṣīcayo'kṣakāmā manomuhaḥ |
(2,2.5 ) tābhyo gandharvabhyo'psarābhyo'karaṃ namaḥ ||5||



(2,3.1 ) ado yadavadhāvatyavatkamadhi parvatāt|
(2,3.1 ) tatte kṛṇomi bheṣajaṃ subheṣajaṃ yathāsasi ||1||

(2,3.2 ) ādaṅgā kuvidaṅga śataṃ yā bheṣajāni te |
(2,3.2 ) teṣāmasi tvamuttamamanāsrāvamarogaṇam ||2||

(2,3.3 ) nīcaiḥ khanantyasurā arusrāṇamidaṃ mahat|
(2,3.3 ) tadāsrāvasya bheṣajaṃ tadu rogamanīnaśat||3||

(2,3.4 ) upajīkā udbharanti samudrādadhi bheṣajam |
(2,3.4 ) tadāsrāvasya bheṣajaṃ tadu rogamaśīśamat||4||

(2,3.5 ) arusrāṇamidaṃ mahatpṛthivyā adhyudbhṛtam |
(2,3.5 ) tadāsrāvasya bheṣajaṃ tadu rogamanīnaśat||5||

(2,3.6 ) śaṃ no bhavantvapa oṣadhayaḥ śivāḥ |
(2,3.6 ) indrasya vajro apa hantu rakṣasa ārādvisṛṣṭā iṣavaḥ patantu rakṣasām ||6||



(2,4.1 ) dīrghāyutvāya bṛhate raṇāyāriṣyanto dakṣamāṇāḥ sadaiva |
(2,4.1 ) maṇiṃ viṣkandhadūṣaṇaṃ jaṅgiḍaṃ bibhṛmo vayam ||1||

(2,4.2 ) jaṅgiḍo jambhādviśarādviṣkandhādabhiśocanāt|
(2,4.2 ) maṇiḥ sahasravīryaḥ pari ṇaḥ pātu viśvataḥ ||2||

(2,4.3 ) ayaṃ viṣkandhaṃ sahate'yaṃ bādhate attriṇaḥ |
(2,4.3 ) ayaṃ no viśvabheṣajo jaṅgiḍaḥ pātvaṃhasaḥ ||3||

(2,4.4 ) devairdattena maṇinā jaṅgiḍena mayobhuvā |
(2,4.4 ) viṣkandhaṃ sarvā rakṣāṃsi vyāyāme sahāmahe ||4||

(2,4.5 ) śaṇaśca mā jaṅgiḍaśca viṣkandhādabhi rakṣatām |
(2,4.5 ) araṇyādanya ābhṛtaḥ kṛṣyā anyo rasebhyaḥ ||5||

(2,4.6 ) kṛtyādūṣirayaṃ maṇiratho arātidūṣiḥ |
(2,4.6 ) atho sahasvān jaṅgiḍaḥ pra ṇa āyuṃṣi tāriṣat||6||



(2,5.1 ) indra juṣasva pra vahā yāhi śūra haribhyām |
(2,5.1 ) pibā sutasya materiha madhoścakānaścārurmadāya ||1||

(2,5.2 ) indra jaṭharaṃ navyo na pṛṇasva madhordivo na |
(2,5.2 ) asya sutasya svarṇopa tvā madāḥ suvāco aguḥ ||2||

(2,5.3 ) indrasturāṣāṇmitro vṛtraṃ yo jaghāna yatīrna |
(2,5.3 ) bibheda valaṃ bhṛgurna sasahe śatrūn made somasya ||3||

(2,5.4 ) ā tvā viśantu sutāsa indra pṛṇasva kukṣī viḍḍhi śakra dhiyehyā naḥ |
(2,5.4 ) śrudhī havaṃ giro me juṣasvendra svayugbhirmatsveha mahe raṇāya ||4||

(2,5.5 ) indrasya nu pra vocaṃ vīryāṇi yāni cakāra prathamāni vajrī |
(2,5.5 ) ahann ahimanu apastatarda pra vakṣaṇā abhinatparvatānām ||5||

(2,5.6 ) ahann ahiṃ parvate śiśriyāṇaṃ tvaṣṭāsmai vajraṃ svaryaṃ tatakṣa |
(2,5.6 ) vāśrā iva dhenavaḥ syandamānā añjaḥ samudramava jagmurāpaḥ ||6||

(2,5.7 ) vṛṣāyamāṇo avṛṇīta somaṃ trikadrukeṣu apibatsutasya |
(2,5.7 ) ā sāyakaṃ maghavādatta vajramahann enaṃ prathamajāmahīnām ||7||



(2,6.1 ) samāstvāgna ṛtavo vardhayantu saṃvatsarā ṛṣayo yāni satyā |
(2,6.1 ) saṃ divyena dīdihi rocanena viśvā ā māhi pradiśaścatasraḥ ||1||

(2,6.2 ) saṃ cedhyasvāgne pra ca vardhayemamucca tiṣṭha mahate saubhagāya |
(2,6.2 ) mā te riṣann upasattāro agne brahmāṇaste yaśasaḥ santu mānye ||2||

(2,6.3 ) tvāmagne vṛṇate brāhmaṇā ime śivo agne saṃvaraṇe bhavā naḥ |
(2,6.3 ) sapatnahāgne abhimātijidbhava sve gaye jāgṛhyaprayuchan ||3||

(2,6.4 ) kṣatreṇāgne svena saṃ rabhasva mitreṇāgne mitradhā yatasva |
(2,6.4 ) sajātānāṃ madhyameṣṭhā rājñāmagne vihavyo dīdihīha ||4||

(2,6.5 ) ati niho ati sṛdho'tyacittīrati dviṣaḥ |
(2,6.5 ) viśvā hyagne duritā tara tvamathāsmabhyaṃ sahavīraṃ rayiṃ dāḥ ||5||



(2,7.1 ) aghadviṣṭā devajātā vīrucchapathayopanī |
(2,7.1 ) āpo malamiva prāṇaikṣītsarvān macchapathāmadhi ||1||

(2,7.2 ) yaśca sāpatnaḥ śapatho jāmyāḥ śapathaśca yaḥ |
(2,7.2 ) brahmā yan manyutaḥ śapātsarvaṃ tan no adhaspadam ||2||

(2,7.3 ) divo mūlamavatataṃ pṛthivyā adhyuttatam |
(2,7.3 ) tena sahasrakāṇḍena pari ṇaḥ pāhi viśvataḥ ||3||

(2,7.4 ) pari māṃ pari me prajāṃ pari ṇaḥ pāhi yaddhanam |
(2,7.4 ) arātirno mā tārīn mā nastāriśurabhimātayaḥ ||4||

(2,7.5 ) śaptārametu śapatho yaḥ suhārttena naḥ saha |
(2,7.5 ) cakṣurmantrasya durhārdaḥ pṛṣṭīrapi śṛṇīmasi ||5||


(2,8.1 ) udagātāṃ bhagavatī vicṛtau nāma tārake |
(2,8.1 ) vi kṣetriyasya muñcatāmadhamaṃ pāśamuttamam ||1||

(2,8.2 ) apeyaṃ rātryuchatvapochantvabhikṛtvarīḥ |
(2,8.2 ) vīrutkṣetriyanāśanyapa kṣetriyamuchatu ||2||

(2,8.3 ) babhrorarjunakāṇḍasya yavasya te palālyā tilasya tilapiñjyā |
(2,8.3 ) vīrutkṣetriyanāśanyapa kṣetriyamuchatu ||3||

(2,8.4 ) namaste lāṅgalebhyo nama īṣāyugebhyaḥ |
(2,8.4 ) vīrutkṣetriyanāśanyapa kṣetriyamuchatu ||4||

(2,8.5 ) namaḥ sanisrasākṣebhyo namaḥ saṃdeśyebhyaḥ |
(2,8.5 ) namaḥ kṣetrasya pataye vīrutkṣetriyanāśanyapa kṣetriyamuchatu ||5||



(2,9.1 ) daśavṛkṣa muñcemaṃ rakṣaso grāhyā adhi yainaṃ jagrāha parvasu |
(2,9.1 ) atho enaṃ vanaspate jīvānāṃ lokamun naya ||1||

(2,9.2 ) āgādudagādayaṃ jīvānāṃ vrātamapyagāt|
(2,9.2 ) abhūdu putrāṇāṃ pitā nṛṇāṃ ca bhagavattamaḥ ||2||

(2,9.3 ) adhītīradhyagādayamadhi jīvapurā agān |
(2,9.3 ) śataṃ hyasya bhiṣajaḥ sahasramuta vīrudhaḥ ||3||

(2,9.4 ) devāste cītimavidan brahmāṇa uta vīrudhaḥ |
(2,9.4 ) cītiṃ te viśve devā avidan bhūmyāmadhi ||4||

(2,9.5 ) yaścakāra sa niṣkaratsa eva subhiṣaktamaḥ |
(2,9.5 ) sa eva tubhyaṃ bheṣajāni kṛṇavadbhiṣajā śuciḥ ||5||



(2,10.1 ) kṣetriyāttvā nirṛtyā jāmiśaṃsāddruho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt|
(2,10.1 ) anāgasaṃ brahmaṇā tvā kṛṇomi śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām ||1||

(2,10.2 ) śaṃ te agniḥ sahādbhirastu śaṃ somaḥ sahauṣadhībhiḥ |
(2,10.2 ) evāhaṃ tvāṃ kṣetriyān nirṛtyā jāmiśaṃsāddruho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt|
(2,10.2 ) anāgasaṃ brahmaṇā tvā kṛṇomi śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām ||2||

(2,10.3 ) śaṃ te vāto antarikṣe vayo dhācchaṃ te bhavantu pradiśaścatasraḥ |
(2,10.3 ) evāhaṃ tvāṃ kṣetriyān nirṛtyā jāmiśaṃsāddruho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt|
(2,10.3 ) anāgasaṃ brahmaṇā tvā kṛṇomi śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām ||3||

(2,10.4 ) imā yā devīḥ pradiśaścatasro vātapatnīrabhi sūryo vicaṣṭe |
(2,10.4 ) evāhaṃ tvāṃ kṣetriyān nirṛtyā jāmiśaṃsāddruho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt|
(2,10.4 ) anāgasaṃ brahmaṇā tvā kṛnomi śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām ||4||

(2,10.5 ) tāsu tvāntarjarasyā dadhāmi pra yakṣma etu nirṛtiḥ parācaiḥ |
(2,10.5 ) evāhaṃ tvāṃ kṣetriyān nirṛtyā jāmiśaṃsāddruho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt|
(2,10.5 ) anāgasaṃ brahmaṇā tvā kṛṇomi śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām ||5||

(2,10.6 ) amukthā yakṣmādduritādavadyāddruhaḥ pāśādgrāhyāścodamukthāḥ |
(2,10.6 ) evāhaṃ tvāṃ kṣetriyān nirṛtyā jāmiśaṃsāddruho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt|
(2,10.6 ) anāgasaṃ brahmaṇā tvā kṛṇomi śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām ||6||

(2,10.7 ) ahā arātimavidaḥ syonamapyabhūrbhadre sukṛtasya loke |
(2,10.7 ) evāhaṃ tvāṃ kṣetriyān nirṛtyā jāmiśaṃsāddruho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt|
(2,10.7 ) anāgasaṃ brahmaṇā tvā kṛṇomi śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām ||7||

(2,10.8 ) sūryamṛtaṃ tamaso grāhyā adhi devā muñcanto asṛjan nirenasaḥ |
(2,10.8 ) evāhaṃ tvāṃ kṣetriyān nirṛtyā jāmiśaṃsāddruho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt|
(2,10.8 ) anāgasaṃ brahmaṇā tvā kṛṇomi śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām ||8||



(2,11.1 ) dūṣyā dūṣirasi hetyā hetirasi menyā menirasi |
(2,11.1 ) āpnuhi śreyāṃsamati samaṃ krāma ||1||

(2,11.2 ) sraktyo'si pratisaro'si pratyabhicaraṇo'si |
(2,11.2 ) āpnuhi śreyāṃsamati samaṃ krāma ||2||

(2,11.3 ) prati tamabhi cara yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ |
(2,11.3 ) āpnuhi śreyāṃsamati samaṃ krāma ||3||
(2,11.4 ) sūrirasi varcodhā asi tanūpāno'si |
(2,11.4 ) āpnuhi śreyāṃsamati samaṃ krāma ||4||

(2,11.5 ) śukro'si bhrājo'si svarasi jyotirasi |
(2,11.5 ) āpnuhi śreyāṃsamati samaṃ krāma ||5||



(2,12.1 ) dyāvāpṛthivī urvantarikṣaṃ kṣetrasya patnyurugāyo'dbhutaḥ |
(2,12.1 ) utāntarikṣamuru vātagopaṃ ta iha tapyantāṃ mayi tapyamāne ||1||

(2,12.2 ) idaṃ devāḥ śṛṇuta ye yajñiyā stha bharadvājo mahyamukthāni śaṃsati |
(2,12.2 ) pāśe sa baddho durite ni yujyatāṃ yo asmākaṃ mana idaṃ hinasti ||2||

(2,12.3 ) idamindra śṛṇuhi somapa yattvā hṛdā śocatā johavīmi |
(2,12.3 ) vṛścāmi taṃ kuliśeneva vṛkṣaṃ yo asmākaṃ mana idaṃ hinasti ||3||

(2,12.4 ) aśītibhistisṛbhiḥ sāmagebhirādityebhirvasubhiraṅgirobhiḥ |
(2,12.4 ) iṣṭāpūrtamavatu naḥ pitṝṇāmāmuṃ dade harasā daivyena ||4||

(2,12.5 ) dyāvāpṛthivī anu mā dīdhīthāṃ viśve devāso anu mā rabhadhvam |
(2,12.5 ) aṅgirasaḥ pitaraḥ somyāsaḥ pāpamā ṛchatvapakāmasya kartā ||5||

(2,12.6 ) atīva yo maruto manyate no brahma vā yo nindiṣatkriyamāṇam |
(2,12.6 ) tapūṃṣi tasmai vṛjināni santu brahmadviṣaṃ dyaurabhisaṃtapāti ||6||

(2,12.7 ) sapta prāṇān aṣṭau manyastāṃste vṛścāmi brahmaṇā |
(2,12.7 ) ayā yamasya sādanamagnidūto araṃkṛtaḥ ||7||

(2,12.8 ) ā dadhāmi te padaṃ samiddhe jātavedasi |
(2,12.8 ) agniḥ śarīraṃ veveṣṭvasuṃ vāgapi gachatu ||8||



(2,13.1 ) āyurdā agne jarasaṃ vṛṇāno ghṛtapratīko ghṛtapṛṣṭho agne |
(2,13.1 ) ghṛtaṃ pītvā madhu cāru gavyaṃ piteva putrān abhi rakṣatādimam ||1||

(2,13.2 ) pari dhatta dhatta no varcasemaṃ jarāmṛtyuṃ kṛṇuta dīrghamāyuḥ |
(2,13.2 ) bṛhaspatiḥ prāyachadvāsa etatsomāya rājñe paridhātavā u ||2||
(2,13.3 ) parīdaṃ vāso adhithāḥ svastaye'bhūrgṛṣṭīnāmabhiśastipā u |
(2,13.3 ) śataṃ ca jīva śaradaḥ purūcī rāyaśca poṣamupasaṃvyayasva ||3||

(2,13.4 ) ehyaśmānamā tiṣṭhāśmā bhavatu te tanūḥ |
(2,13.4 ) kṛṇvantu viśve devā āyuṣṭe śaradaḥ śatam ||4||

(2,13.5 ) yasya te vāsaḥ prathamavāsyaṃ harāmastaṃ tvā viśve'vantu devāḥ |
(2,13.5 ) taṃ tvā bhrātaraḥ suvṛdhā vardhamānamanu jāyantāṃ bahavaḥ sujātam ||5||


(2,14.1 ) niḥsālāṃ dhṛṣṇuṃ dhiṣaṇamekavādyāṃ jighatsvam |
(2,14.1 ) sarvāścaṇḍasya naptyo nāśayāmaḥ sadānvāḥ ||1||

(2,14.2 ) nirvo goṣṭhādajāmasi nirakṣān nirupānaśāt|
(2,14.2 ) nirvo magundyā duhitaro gṛhebhyaścātayāmahe ||2||

(2,14.3 ) asau yo adharādgṛhastatra santvarāyyaḥ |
(2,14.3 ) tatra sedirnyucyatu sarvāśca yātudhānyaḥ ||3||

(2,14.4 ) bhūtapatirnirajatvindraścetaḥ sadānvāḥ |
(2,14.4 ) gṛhasya budhna āsīnāstā indro vajreṇādhi tiṣṭhatu ||4||

(2,14.5 ) yadi stha kṣetriyāṇāṃ yadi vā puruṣeṣitāḥ |
(2,14.5 ) yadi stha dasyubhyo jātā naśyatetaḥ sadānvāḥ ||5||

(2,14.6 ) pari dhāmānyāsāmāśurgāṣṭhāmivāsaram |
(2,14.6 ) ajaiṣaṃ sarvān ājīn vo naśyatetaḥ sadānvāḥ ||6||



(2,15.1 ) yathā dyauśca pṛthivī ca na bibhīto na riṣyataḥ |
(2,15.1 ) evā me prāṇa mā bibheḥ ||1||

(2,15.2 ) yathāhaśca rātrī ca na bibhīto na riṣyataḥ |
(2,15.2 ) evā me prāṇa mā bibheḥ ||2||

(2,15.3 ) yathā sūryaśca candraśca na bibhīto na riṣyataḥ |
(2,15.3 ) evā me prāṇa mā bibheḥ ||3||

(2,15.4 ) yathā brahma ca kṣatraṃ ca na bibhīto na riṣyataḥ |
(2,15.4 ) evā me prāṇa mā bibheḥ ||4||

(2,15.5 ) yathā satyaṃ cānṛtaṃ ca na bibhīto na riṣyataḥ |
(2,15.5 ) evā me prāṇa mā bibheḥ ||5||

(2,15.6 ) yathā bhūtaṃ ca bhavyaṃ ca na bibhīto na riṣyataḥ |
(2,15.6 ) evā me prāṇa mā bibheḥ ||6||



(2,16.1 ) prāṇāpānau mṛtyormā pātaṃ svāhā ||1||

(2,16.2 ) dyāvāpṛthivī upaśrutyā mā pātaṃ svāhā ||2||

(2,16.3 ) sūrya cakṣuṣā mā pāhi svāhā ||3||

(2,16.4 ) agne vaiśvānara viśvairmā devaiḥ pāhi svāhā ||4||

(2,16.5 ) viśvambhara viśvena mā bharasā pāhi svāhā ||5||



(2,17.1 ) ojo'syojo me dāḥ svāhā |1||

(2,17.2 ) saho'si saho me dāḥ svāhā ||2||

(2,17.3 ) balamasi balaṃ dāḥ svāhā ||3||

(2,17.4 ) āyurasyāyurme dāḥ svāha ||4||

(2,17.5 ) śrotramasi śrotraṃ me dāḥ svāha ||5||

(2,17.6 ) cakṣurasi cakṣurme dāḥ svāha ||6||

(2,17.7 ) paripāṇamasi paripāṇaṃ me dāḥ svāha ||7||



(2,18.1 ) bhrātṛvyakṣayaṇamasi bhrātṛvyacātanaṃ me dāḥ svāha ||1||

(2,18.2 ) sapatnakṣayaṇamasi sapatnacātanaṃ me dāḥ svāha ||2||

(2,18.3 ) arāyakṣayaṇamasyarāyacātanaṃ me dāḥ svāha ||3||

(2,18.4 ) piśācakṣayaṇamasi piśācacātanaṃ me dāḥ svāha ||4||

(2,18.5 ) sadānvākṣayaṇamasi sadānvācātanaṃ me dāḥ svāha ||5||



(2,19.1 ) agne yatte tapastena taṃ prati tapa yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||1||

(2,19.2 ) agne yatte harastena taṃ prati hara yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||2||

(2,19.3 ) agne yatte'rcistena taṃ pratyarca yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||3||

(2,19.4 ) agne yatte śocistena taṃ prati śoca yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||4||

(2,19.5 ) agne yatte tejastena tamatejasaṃ kṛṇu yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||5||



(2,20.1 ) vāyo yatte tapastena taṃ prati tapa yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||1||

(2,20.2 ) vāyo yatte harastena taṃ prati hara yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||2||

(2,20.3 ) vāyo yatte'rcistena taṃ pratyarca yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||3||

(2,20.4 ) vāyo yatte śocistena taṃ prati śoca yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||4||

(2,20.5 ) vāyo yatte tejastena tamatejasaṃ kṛṇu yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||5||



(2,21.1 ) sūrya yatte tapastena taṃ prati tapa yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||1||

(2,21.2 ) sūrya yatte harastena taṃ prati hara yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||2||

(2,21.3 ) sūrya yatte'rcistena taṃ pratyarca yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||3||

(2,21.4 ) sūrya yatte śocistena taṃ prati śoca yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||4||

(2,21.5 ) sūrya yatte tejastena tamatejasaṃ kṛṇu yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||5||



(2,22.1 ) candra yatte tapastena taṃ prati tapa yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||1||

(2,22.2 ) candra yatte harastena taṃ prati hara yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||2||

(2,22.3 ) candra yatte'rcistena taṃ pratyarca yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||3||

(2,22.4 ) candra yatte śocistena taṃ prati śoca yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||4||

(2,22.5 ) candra yatte tejastena tamatejasaṃ kṛṇu yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||5||



(2,23.1 ) āpo yadvastapastena taṃ prati tapata yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||1||

(2,23.2 ) āpo yadvasharastena taṃ prati harata yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||2||

(2,23.3 ) āpo yadvas'rcistena taṃ prati arcata yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||3||

(2,23.4 ) āpo yadvasśocistena taṃ prati śocata yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||4||

(2,23.5 ) āpo yadvastejastena tamatejasaṃ kṛṇuta yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||5||



(2,24.1 ) śerabhaka śerabha punarvo yantu yātavaḥ punarhetiḥ kimīdinaḥ |
(2,24.1 ) yasya stha tamatta yo vo prāhaittamatta svā māṃsānyatta ||1||

(2,24.2 ) śevṛdhaka śevṛdha punarvo yantu yātavaḥ punarhetiḥ kimīdinaḥ |
(2,24.2 ) yasya stha tamatta yo vo prāhaittamatta svā māṃsānyatta ||2||

(2,24.3 ) mrokānumroka punarvo yantu yātavaḥ punarhetiḥ kimīdinaḥ |
(2,24.3 ) yasya stha tamatta yo vo prāhaittamatta svā māṃsānyatta ||3||

(2,24.4 ) sarpānusarpa punarvo yantu yātavaḥ punarhetiḥ kimīdinaḥ |
(2,24.4 ) yasya stha tamatta yo vo prāhaittamatta svā māṃsānyatta ||4||

(2,24.5 ) jūrṇi punarvo yantu yātavaḥ punarhetiḥ kimīdinaḥ |
(2,24.5 ) yasya stha tamatta yo vo prāhaittamatta svā māṃsānyatta ||5||

(2,24.6 ) upabde punarvo yantu yātavaḥ punarhetiḥ kimīdinaḥ |
(2,24.6 ) yasya stha tamatta yo vo prāhaittamatta svā māṃsānyatta ||6||

(2,24.7 ) arjuni punarvo yantu yātavaḥ punarhetiḥ kimīdinaḥ |
(2,24.7 ) yasya stha tamatta yo vo prāhaittamatta svā māṃsānyatta ||7||

(2,24.8 ) bharūji punarvo yantu yātavaḥ punarhetiḥ kimīdinaḥ |
(2,24.8 ) yasya stha tamatta yo vo prāhaittamatta svā māṃsānyatta ||8||



(2,25.1 ) śaṃ no devī pṛśniparṇyaśaṃ nirṛtyā akaḥ |
(2,25.1 ) ugrā hi kaṇvajambhanī tāmabhakṣi sahasvatīm ||1||

(2,25.2 ) sahamāneyaṃ prathamā pṛśniparṇyajāyata |
(2,25.2 ) tayāhaṃ durṇāmnāṃ śiro vṛścāmi śakuneriva ||2||

(2,25.3 ) arāyamasṛkpāvānaṃ yaśca sphātiṃ jihīrṣati |
(2,25.3 ) garbhādaṃ kaṇvaṃ nāśaya pṛśniparṇi sahasva ca ||3||

(2,25.4 ) girimenāmā veśaya kaṇvān jīvitayopanān |
(2,25.4 ) tāṃstvaṃ devi pṛśniparṇyagnirivānudahann ihi ||4||

(2,25.5 ) parāca enān pra ṇuda kaṇvān jīvitayopanān |
(2,25.5 ) tamāṃsi yatra gachanti tatkravyādo ajīgamam ||5||


(2,26.1 ) eha yantu paśavo ye pareyurvāyuryeṣāṃ sahacāraṃ jujoṣa |
(2,26.1 ) tvaṣṭā yeṣāṃ rūpadheyāni vedāsmin tān goṣṭhe savitā ni yachatu ||1||

(2,26.2 ) imaṃ goṣṭhaṃ paśavaḥ saṃ sravantu bṛhaspatirā nayatu prajānan |
(2,26.2 ) sinīvālī nayatvāgrameṣāmājagmuṣo anumate ni yacha ||2||

(2,26.3 ) saṃ saṃ sravantu paśavaḥ samaśvāḥ samu pūruṣāḥ |
(2,26.3 ) saṃ dhānyasya yā sphātiḥ saṃsrāvyeṇa haviṣā juhomi ||3||

(2,26.4 ) saṃ siñcāmi gavāṃ kṣīraṃ samājyena balaṃ rasam |
(2,26.4 ) saṃsiktā asmākaṃ vīrā dhruvā gāvo mayi gopatau ||4||

(2,26.5 ) ā harāmi gavāṃ kṣīramāhārṣaṃ dhānyaṃ rasam |
(2,26.5 ) āhṛtā asmākaṃ vīrā ā patnīridamastakam ||5||



(2,27.1 ) necchatruḥ prāśaṃ jayāti sahamānābhibhūrasi |
(2,27.1 ) prāśaṃ pratiprāśo jahyarasān kṛṇvoṣadhe ||1||

(2,27.2 ) suparṇastvānvavindatsūkarastvākhanan nasā |
(2,27.2 ) prāśaṃ pratiprāśo jahyarasān kṛṇvoṣadhe ||2||

(2,27.3 ) indro ha cakre tvā bāhāvasurebhya starītave |
(2,27.3 ) prāśaṃ pratiprāśo jahyarasān kṛṇvoṣadhe ||3||

(2,27.4 ) pāṭāmindro vyāśnādasurebhya starītave |
(2,27.4 ) prāśaṃ pratiprāśo jahyarasān kṛṇvoṣadhe ||4||

(2,27.5 ) tayāhaṃ śatrūntsākṣa indraḥ sālāvṛkāmiva |
(2,27.5 ) prāśaṃ pratiprāśo jahyarasān kṛṇvoṣadhe ||5||

(2,27.6 ) rudra jalāṣabheṣaja nīlaśikhaṇḍa karmakṛt|
(2,27.6 ) prāśaṃ pratiprāśo jahyarasān kṛṇvoṣadhe ||6||

(2,27.7 ) tasya prāśaṃ tvaṃ jahi yo na indrābhidāsati |
(2,27.7 ) adhi no brūhi śaktibhiḥ prāśi māmuttaraṃ kṛdhi ||7||



(2,28.1 ) tubhyameva jariman vardhatāmayaṃ memamanye mṛtyavo hiṃsiṣuḥ śataṃ ye |
(2,28.1 ) māteva putraṃ pramanā upasthe mitra enaṃ mitriyātpātvaṃhasaḥ ||1||

(2,28.2 ) mitra enaṃ varuṇo vā riśādā jarāmṛtyuṃ kṛṇutāṃ saṃvidānau |
(2,28.2 ) tadagnirhotā vayunāni vidvān viśvā devānāṃ janimā vivakti ||2||

(2,28.3 ) tvamīśiṣe paśūnāṃ pārthivānāṃ ye jātā uta vā ye janitrāḥ |
(2,28.3 ) memaṃ prāṇo hāsīn mo apāno memaṃ mitrā vadhiṣurmo amitrāḥ ||3||

(2,28.4 ) dyauṣṭvā pitā pṛthivī mātā jarāmṛtyuṃ kṛṇutāṃ saṃvidāne |
(2,28.4 ) yathā jīvā aditerupasthe prāṇāpānābhyāṃ gupitaḥ śataṃ himāḥ ||4||

(2,28.5 ) imamagne āyuṣe varcase naya priyaṃ reto varuṇa mitra rājan |
(2,28.5 ) mātevāsmā adite śarma yacha viśve devā jaradaṣṭiryathāsat||5||



(2,29.1 ) pārthivasya rase devā bhagasya tanvo bale |
(2,29.1 ) āyuṣyamasmā agniḥ sūryo varca ā dhādbṛhaspatiḥ ||1||

(2,29.2 ) āyurasmai dhehi jātavedaḥ prajāṃ tvaṣṭaradhinidhehi asmai |
(2,29.2 ) rāyaspoṣaṃ savitarā suvāsmai śataṃ jīvāti śaradastavāyam ||2||

(2,29.3 ) āśīrṇa ūrjamuta sauprajāstvaṃ dakṣaṃ dhattaṃ draviṇaṃ sacetasau |
(2,29.3 ) jayaṃ kṣetrāṇi sahasāyamindra kṛṇvāno anyān adharāntsapatnān ||3||

(2,29.4 ) indreṇa datto varuṇena śiṣṭo marudbhirugraḥ prahito no āgan |
(2,29.4 ) eṣa vāṃ dyāvāpṛthivī upasthe mā kṣudhan mā tṛṣat||4||

(2,29.5 ) ūrjamasmā ūrjasvatī dhattaṃ payo asmai payasvatī dhattam |
(2,29.5 ) ūrjamasmai dyāvapṛthivī adhātāṃ viśve devā maruta ūrjamāpaḥ ||5||

(2,29.6 ) śivābhiṣṭe hṛdayaṃ tarpayāmyanamīvo modiṣīṣṭhāḥ suvarcāḥ |
(2,29.6 ) savāsinau pibatāṃ manthametamaśvino rūpaṃ paridhāya māyām ||6||

(2,29.7 ) indra etāṃ sasṛje viddho agra ūrjāṃ svadhāmajarāṃ sā ta eṣā |
(2,29.7 ) tayā tvaṃ jīva śaradaḥ suvarcā mā ta ā susrodbhiṣajaste akran ||7||


(2,30.1 ) yathedaṃ bhūmyā adhi tṛṇaṃ vāto mathāyati |
(2,30.1 ) evā mathnāmi te mano yathā māṃ kāminyaso yathā man nāpagā asaḥ ||1||

(2,30.2 ) saṃ cen nayātho aśvinā kāminā saṃ ca vakṣathaḥ |
(2,30.2 ) saṃ vāṃ bhagāso agmata saṃ cittāni samu vratā ||2||

(2,30.3 ) yatsuparṇā vivakṣavo anamīvā vivakṣavaḥ |
(2,30.3 ) tatra me gachatāddhavaṃ śalya iva kulmalaṃ yathā ||3||

(2,30.4 ) yadantaraṃ tadbāhyaṃ yadbāhyaṃ tadantaram |
(2,30.4 ) kanyānāṃ viśvarūpāṇāṃ mano gṛbhāyauṣadhe ||4||

(2,30.5 ) eyamagan patikāmā janikāmo'hamāgamam |
(2,30.5 ) aśvaḥ kanikradadyathā bhagenāhaṃ sahāgamam ||5||



(2,31.1 ) indrasya yā mahī dṛṣatkrimerviśvasya tarhaṇī |
(2,31.1 ) tayā pinaṣmi saṃ krimīn dṛṣadā khalvāmiva ||1||

(2,31.2 ) dṛṣṭamadṛṣṭamatṛhamatho kurūrumatṛham |
(2,31.2 ) algaṇḍūntsarvān chalunān krimīn vacasā jambhayāmasi ||2||

(2,31.3 ) algaṇḍūn hanmi mahatā vadhena dūnā adūnā arasā abhūvan |
(2,31.3 ) śiṣṭān aśiṣṭān ni tirāmi vācā yathā krimīṇāṃ nakiruchiṣātai ||3||

(2,31.4 ) anvāntryaṃ śīrṣaṇyamatho pārṣṭeyaṃ krimīn |
(2,31.4 ) avaskavaṃ vyadhvaraṃ krimīn vacasā jambhayāmasi ||4||

(2,31.5 ) ye krimayaḥ parvateśu vaneṣvoṣadhīṣu paśuṣvapsvantaḥ |
(2,31.5 ) ye asmākaṃ tanvamāviviśuḥ sarvaṃ taddhanmi janima krimīṇām ||5||



(2,32.1 ) udyann ādityaḥ krimīn hantu nimrocan hantu raśmibhiḥ |
(2,32.1 ) ye antaḥ krimayo gavi ||1||

(2,32.2 ) viśvarūpaṃ caturakṣaṃ krimiṃ sāraṅgamarjunam |
(2,32.2 ) śṛṇāmyasya pṛṣṭīrapi vṛścāmi yacchiraḥ ||2||

(2,32.3 ) atrivadvaḥ krimayo hanmi kaṇvavajjamadagnivat|
(2,32.3 ) agastyasya brahmaṇā saṃ pinaṣmyahaṃ krimīn ||3||

(2,32.4 ) hato rājā krimīṇāmutaiṣāṃ sthapatirhataḥ |
(2,32.4 ) hato hatamātā krimirhatabhrātā hatasvasā ||4||

(2,32.5 ) hatāso asya veśaso hatāsaḥ pariveśasaḥ |
(2,32.5 ) atho ye kṣullakā iva sarve te krimayo hatāḥ ||5||

(2,32.6 ) pra te śṛṇāmi śṛṅge yābhyāṃ vitudāyasi |
(2,32.6 ) bhinādmi te kuṣumbhaṃ yaste viṣadhānaḥ ||6||



(2,33.1 ) akṣībhyāṃ te nāsikābhyāṃ karṇābhyāṃ chubukādadhi |
(2,33.1 ) yakṣmaṃ śīrṣaṇyaṃ mastiṣkājjihvāyā vi vṛhāmi te ||1||

(2,33.2 ) grīvābhyasta uṣṇihābhyaḥ kīkasābhyo anūkyāt|
(2,33.2 ) yakṣmaṃ doṣaṇyamaṃsābhyāṃ bāhubhyāṃ vi vṛhāmi te ||2||

(2,33.3 ) hṛdayātte pari klomno halīkṣṇātpārśvābhyām |
(2,33.3 ) yakṣmaṃ matasnābhyāṃ plīhno yaknaste vi vṛhāmasi ||3||

(2,33.4 ) āntrebhyaste gudābhyo vaniṣṭhorudarādadhi |
(2,33.4 ) yakṣmaṃ kukṣibhyāṃ plāśernābhyā vi vṛhāmi te ||4||

(2,33.5 ) ūrubhyāṃ te aṣṭhīvadbhyāṃ pārṣṇibhyāṃ prapadābhyām |
(2,33.5 ) yakṣmaṃ bhasadyaṃ śroṇibhyāṃ bhāsadaṃ bhaṃsaso vi vṛhāmi te ||5||

(2,33.6 ) asthibhyaste majjabhyaḥ snāvabhyo dhamanibhyaḥ |
(2,33.6 ) yakṣmaṃ pāṇibhyāmaṅgulibhyo nakhebhyo vi vṛhāmi te ||6||

(2,33.7 ) aṅgeaṅge lomnilomni yaste parvaṇiparvaṇi |
(2,33.7 ) yakṣmaṃ tvacasyaṃ te vayaṃ kaśyapasya vībarheṇa viṣvañcaṃ vi vṛhāmasi ||7||



(2,34.1 ) ya īśe paśupatiḥ paśūnāṃ catuṣpadāmuta yo dvipadām |
(2,34.1 ) niṣkrītaḥ sa yajñiyaṃ bhāgametu rāyaspoṣā yajamānaṃ sacantām ||1||

(2,34.2 ) pramuñcanto bhuvanasya reto gātuṃ dhatta yajamānāya devāḥ |
(2,34.2 ) upākṛtaṃ śaśamānaṃ yadasthātpriyaṃ devānāmapyetu pāthaḥ ||2||

(2,34.3 ) ye badhyamānamanu dīdhyānā anvaikṣanta manasā cakṣuṣā ca |
(2,34.3 ) agniṣṭān agre pra mumoktu devo viśvakarmā prajayā saṃrarāṇaḥ ||3||

(2,34.4 ) ye grāmyāḥ paśavo viśvarūpā virūpāḥ santo bahudhaikarūpāḥ |
(2,34.4 ) vāyuṣṭān agre pra mumoktu devaḥ prajāpatiḥ prajayā saṃrarāṇaḥ ||4||

(2,34.5 ) prajānantaḥ prati gṛhṇantu pūrve prāṇamaṅgebhyaḥ paryācarantam |
(2,34.5 ) divaṃ gacha prati tiṣṭhā śarīraiḥ svargaṃ yāhi pathibhirdevayānaiḥ ||5||



(2,35.1 ) ye bhakṣayanto na vasūnyānṛdhuryān agnayo anvatapyanta dhiṣṇyāḥ |
(2,35.1 ) yā teṣāmavayā duriṣṭiḥ sviṣṭiṃ nastāṃ kṛṇavadviśvakarmā ||1||

(2,35.2 ) yajñapatimṛṣayaḥ enasāhurnirbhaktaṃ prajā anutapyamānam |
(2,35.2 ) mathavyāntstokān apa yān rarādha saṃ naṣṭebhiḥ sṛjatu viśvakarmā ||2||

(2,35.3 ) adānyāntsomapān manyamāno yajñasya vidvāntsamaye na dhīraḥ |
(2,35.3 ) yadenaścakṛvān baddha eṣa taṃ viśvakarman pra muñcā svastaye ||3||

(2,35.4 ) ghorā ṛṣayo namo astvebhyaścakṣuryadeṣāṃ manasaśca satyam |
(2,35.4 ) bṛhaspataye mahiṣa dyumann namo viśvakarman namaste pāhyasmān ||4||

(2,35.5 ) yajñasya cakṣuḥ prabhṛtirmukhaṃ ca vācā śrotreṇa manasā juhomi |
(2,35.5 ) imaṃ yajñaṃ vitataṃ viśvakarmaṇā devā yantu sumanasyamānāḥ ||5||


(2,36.1 ) ā no agne sumatiṃ saṃbhalo gamedimāṃ kumārīṃ saha no bhagena |
(2,36.1 ) juṣṭā vareṣu samaneṣu valguroṣaṃ patyā saubhagamastu asyai ||1||

(2,36.2 ) somajuṣṭaṃ brahmajuṣṭamaryamnā saṃbhṛtaṃ bhagam |
(2,36.2 ) dhāturdevasya satyena kṛṇomi pativedanam ||2||

(2,36.3 ) iyamagne nārī patiṃ videṣṭa somo hi rājā subhagāṃ kṛṇoti |
(2,36.3 ) suvānā putrān mahiṣī bhavāti gatvā patiṃ subhagā vi rājatu ||3||

(2,36.4 ) yathākharo maghavaṃścārureṣa priyo mṛgāṇāṃ suṣadā babhūva |
(2,36.4 ) evā bhagasya juṣṭeyamastu nārī saṃpriyā patyāvirādhayantī ||4||

(2,36.5 ) bhagasya nāvamā roha pūrṇāmanupadasvatīm |
(2,36.5 ) tayopapratāraya yo varaḥ pratikāmyaḥ ||5||

(2,36.6 ) ā krandaya dhanapate varamāmanasaṃ kṛṇu |
(2,36.6 ) sarvaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛṇu yo varaḥ pratikāmyaḥ ||6||

(2,36.7 ) idaṃ hiraṇyaṃ gulgulvayamaukṣo atho bhagaḥ |
(2,36.7 ) ete patibhyastvāmaduḥ pratikāmāya vettave ||7
(2,36.7 ) ā te nayatu savitā nayatu patiryaḥ pratikāmyaḥ |



(3,1.1 ) agnirnaḥ śatrūn pratyetu vidvān pratidahann abhiśastimarātim |
(3,1.1 ) sa senāṃ mohayatu pareṣāṃ nirhastāṃśca kṛṇavajjātavedāḥ ||1||

(3,1.2 ) yūyamugrā maruta īdṛśe sthābhi preta mṛṇata sahadhvam |
(3,1.2 ) amīmṛṇan vasavo nāthitā ime agnirhyeṣāṃ dūtaḥ pratyetu vidvān ||2||

(3,1.3 ) amitrasenāṃ maghavann asmān chatrūyatīmabhi |
(3,1.3 ) yuvaṃ tāmindra vṛtrahann agniśca dahataṃ prati ||3||

(3,1.4 ) prasūta indra pravatā haribhyāṃ pra te vajraḥ pramṛṇann etu śatrūn |
(3,1.4 ) jahi pratīco anūcaḥ parāco viṣvaksatyaṃ kṛṇuhi cittameṣām ||4||

(3,1.5 ) indra senāṃ mohayāmitrāṇām |
(3,1.5 ) agnervātasya dhrājyā tān viṣūco vi nāśaya ||5||

(3,1.6 ) indraḥ senāṃ mohayatu maruto ghnantvojasā |
(3,1.6 ) cakṣūṃsyagnirā dattāṃ punaretu parājitā ||6||



(3,2.1 ) agnirno dūtaḥ pratyetu vidvān pratidahann abhiśastimarātim |
(3,2.1 ) sa cittāni mohayatu pareṣāṃ nirhastāṃśca kṛṇavajjātavedāḥ ||1||

(3,2.2 ) ayamagniramūmuhadyāni cittāni vo hṛdi |
(3,2.2 ) vi vo dhamatvokasaḥ pra vo dhamatu sarvataḥ ||2||

(3,2.3 ) indra cittāni mohayann arvāṅākūtyā cara |
(3,2.3 ) agnervātasya dhrājyā tān viṣūco vi nāśaya ||3||

(3,2.4 ) vyākūtaya eṣāmitātho cittāni muhyata |
(3,2.4 ) atho yadadyaiṣāṃ hṛdi tadeṣāṃ pari nirjahi ||4||

(3,2.5 ) amīṣāṃ cittāni pratimohayantī gṛhāṇāṅgānyapve parehi |
(3,2.5 ) abhi prehi nirdaha hṛtsu śokairgrāhyāmitrāṃstamasā vidhya śatrūn ||5||

(3,2.6 ) asau yā senā marutaḥ pareṣāmasmān aityabhyojasā spardhamānā |
(3,2.6 ) tāṃ vidhyata tamasāpavratena yathaiṣāmanyo anyaṃ na jānāt||6||



(3,3.1 ) acikradatsvapā iha bhuvadagne vyacasva rodasī urūcī |
(3,3.1 ) yuñjantu tvā maruto viśvavedasa āmuṃ naya namasā rātahavyam ||1||

(3,3.2 ) dūre citsantamaruṣāsa indramā cyāvayantu sakhyāya vipram |
(3,3.2 ) yadgāyatrīṃ bṛhatīmarkamasmai sautrāmaṇyā dadhṛṣanta devāḥ ||2||

(3,3.3 ) adbhyastvā rāja varuṇo hvayatu somastvā hvayatu parvatebhyaḥ |
(3,3.3 ) indrastvā hvayatu viḍbhya ābhyaḥ śyeno bhūtvā viśa ā patemāḥ ||3|| {4}

(3,3.4 ) śyeno havyaṃ nayatvā parasmādanyakṣetre aparuddhaṃ carantam |
(3,3.4 ) aśvinā panthāṃ kṛṇutāṃ sugaṃ ta imaṃ sajātā abhisaṃviśadhvam ||4|| {5}

(3,3.5 ) hvayantu tvā pratijanāḥ prati mitrā avṛṣata |
(3,3.5 ) indrāgnī viśve devāste viśi kṣemamadīdharan ||5|| {6}

(3,3.6 ) yaste havaṃ vivadatsajāto yaśca niṣṭyaḥ |
(3,3.6 ) apāñcamindra taṃ kṛtvāthemamihāva gamaya ||6|| {7}



(3,4.1 ) ā tvā gan rāṣtraṃ saha varcasodihi prāṅviśāṃ patirekarāṭtvaṃ vi rāja |
(3,4.1 ) sarvāstvā rājan pradiśo hvayantūpasadyo namasyo bhaveha ||1||

(3,4.2 ) tvāṃ viśo vṛṇatāṃ rājyāya tvāmimāḥ pradiśaḥ pañca devīḥ |
(3,4.2 ) varṣman rāṣṭrasya kakudi śrayasva tato na ugro vi bhajā vasūni ||2||

(3,4.3 ) acha tvā yantu havinaḥ sajātā agnirdūto ajiraḥ saṃ carātai |
(3,4.3 ) jāyāḥ putrāḥ sumanaso bhavantu bahuṃ baliṃ prati paśyāsā ugraḥ ||3||

(3,4.4 ) aśvinā tvāgre mitrāvaruṇobhā viśve devā marutastvā hvayantu |
(3,4.4 ) adhā mano vasudeyāya kṛṇuṣva tato na ugro vi bhajā vasūni ||4||

(3,4.5 ) ā pra drava paramasyāḥ parāvataḥ śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām |
(3,4.5 ) tadayaṃ rājā varuṇastathāha sa tvāyamahvatsa upedamehi ||5||

(3,4.6 ) indrendra manuṣyāḥ parehi saṃ hyajñāsthā varuṇaiḥ saṃvidānaḥ |
(3,4.6 ) sa tvāyamahvatsve sadhasthe sa devān yakṣatsa u kalpayadviśaḥ ||6||

(3,4.7 ) pathyā revatīrbahudhā virūpāḥ sarvāḥ saṃgatya varīyaste akran |
(3,4.7 ) tāstvā sarvāḥ saṃvidānā hvayantu daśamīmugraḥ sumanā vaśeha ||7||



(3,5.1 ) āyamagan parṇamaṇirbalī balena pramṛṇantsapatnān |
(3,5.1 ) ojo devānāṃ paya oṣadhīnāṃ varcasā mā jinvantvaprayāvan ||1||

(3,5.2 ) mayi kṣatraṃ parṇamaṇe mayi dhārayatādrayim |
(3,5.2 ) ahaṃ rāṣṭrasyābhīvarge nijo bhūyāsamuttamaḥ ||2||

(3,5.3 ) yaṃ nidadhurvanaspatau guhyaṃ devāḥ priyaṃ maṇim |
(3,5.3 ) tamasmabhyaṃ sahāyuṣā devā dadatu bhartave ||3||

(3,5.4 ) somasya parṇaḥ saha ugramāgann indreṇa datto varuṇena śiṣṭaḥ |
(3,5.4 ) taṃ priyāsaṃ bahu rocamāno dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya ||4||

(3,5.5 ) ā mārukṣatparṇamaṇirmahyā ariṣṭatātaye |
(3,5.5 ) yathāhamuttaro'sānyaryamṇa uta saṃvidaḥ ||5||

(3,5.6 ) ye dhīvāno rathakārāḥ karmārā ye manīṣiṇaḥ |
(3,5.6 ) upastīn parṇa mahyaṃ tvaṃ sarvān kṛṇvabhito janān ||6||

(3,5.7 ) ye rājāno rājakṛtaḥ sūtā grāmaṇyaśca ye |
(3,5.7 ) upastīn parṇa mahyaṃ tvaṃ sarvān kṛṇvabhito janān ||7||

(3,5.8 ) parṇo'si tanūpānaḥ sayonirvīro vīreṇa mayā |
(3,5.8 ) saṃvatsarasya tejasā tena badhnāmi tvā maṇe ||8||



(3,6.1 ) pumān puṃsaḥ parijāto'śvatthaḥ khadirādadhi |
(3,6.1 ) sa hantu śatrūn māmakān yān ahaṃ dveṣmi ye ca mām ||1||

(3,6.2 ) tān aśvattha niḥ śṛṇīhi śatrūn vaibādhadodhataḥ |
(3,6.2 ) indreṇa vṛtraghnā medī mitreṇa varuṇena ca ||2||

(3,6.3 ) yathāśvattha nirabhano'ntarmahatyarṇave |
(3,6.3 ) evā tāntsarvān nirbhaṅgdhi yān ahaṃ dveṣmi ye ca mām ||3||

(3,6.4 ) yaḥ sahamānaścarasi sāsahāna iva ṛṣabhaḥ |
(3,6.4 ) tenāśvattha tvayā vayaṃ sapatnāntsahiṣīmahi ||4||

(3,6.5 ) sinātvenān nirṛtirmṛtyoḥ pāśairamokyaiḥ |
(3,6.5 ) aśvattha śatrūn māmakān yān ahaṃ dveṣmi ye ca mām ||5||

(3,6.6 ) yathāśvattha vānaspatyān ārohan kṛṇuṣe'dharān |
(3,6.6 ) evā me śatrormūrdhānaṃ viṣvagbhinddhi sahasva ca ||6||

(3,6.7 ) te'dharāñcaḥ pra plavantāṃ chinnā nauriva bandhanāt|
(3,6.7 ) na vaibādhapraṇuttānāṃ punarasti nivartanam ||7||

(3,6.8 ) praiṇān nude manasā pra cittenota brahmaṇā |
(3,6.8 ) praiṇān vṛkṣasya śākhayāśvatthasya nudāmahe ||8||

(3,7.1 ) hariṇasya raghuṣyado'dhi śīrṣaṇi bheṣajam |
(3,7.1 ) sa kṣetriyaṃ viṣāṇayā viṣūcīnamanīnaśat||1||
(3,7.2 ) anu tvā hariṇo vṛṣā padbhiścaturbhirakramīt|
(3,7.2 ) viṣāṇe vi ṣya guṣpitaṃ yadasya kṣetriyaṃ hṛdi ||2||

(3,7.3 ) ado yadavarocate catuṣpakṣamiva chadiḥ |
(3,7.3 ) tenā te sarvaṃ kṣetriyamaṅgebhyo nāśayāmasi ||3||

(3,7.4 ) amū ye divi subhage vicṛtau nāma tārake |
(3,7.4 ) vi kṣetriyasya muñcatāmadhamaṃ pāśamuttamam ||4||

(3,7.5 ) āpa idvā u bheṣajīrāpo amīvacātanīḥ |
(3,7.5 ) āpo viśvasya bheṣajīstāstvā muñcantu kṣetriyāt||5||

(3,7.6 ) yadāsuteḥ kriyamānāyāḥ kṣetriyaṃ tvā vyānaśe |
(3,7.6 ) vedāhaṃ tasya bheṣajaṃ kṣetriyaṃ nāśayāmi tvat||6||

(3,7.7 ) apavāse nakṣatrāṇāmapavāsa uṣasāmuta |
(3,7.7 ) apāsmatsarvaṃ durbhūtamapa kṣetriyamuchatu ||7||



(3,8.1 ) ā yātu mitra ṛtubhiḥ kalpamānaḥ saṃveśayan pṛthivīmusriyābhiḥ |
(3,8.1 ) athāsmabhyaṃ varuṇo vāyuragnirbṛhadrāṣṭraṃ saṃveśyaṃ dadhātu ||1||

(3,8.2 ) dhātā rātiḥ savitedaṃ juśantāmindrastvaṣṭā prati haryantu me vacaḥ |
(3,8.2 ) huve devīmaditiṃ śūraputrāṃ sajātānāṃ madhyameṣṭhā yathāsāni ||2||

(3,8.3 ) huve somaṃ savitāraṃ namobhirviśvān ādityāmahamuttaratve |
(3,8.3 ) ayamagnirdīdāyaddīrghameva sajātairiddho'pratibruvadbhiḥ ||3||

(3,8.4 ) ihedasātha na paro gamātheryo gopāḥ puṣṭapatirva ājat|
(3,8.4 ) asmai kāmāyopa kāminīrviśve vo devā upasaṃyantu ||4||

(3,8.5 ) saṃ vo manāṃsi saṃ vratā samākūtīrnamāmasi |
(3,8.5 ) amī ye vivratā sthana tān vaḥ saṃ namayāmasi ||5||

(3,8.6 ) ahaṃ gṛbhṇāmi manasā manāṃsi mama cittamanu cittebhireta |
(3,8.6 ) mama vaśeṣu hṛdayāni vaḥ kṛṇomi mama yātamanuvartmāna eta ||6||



(3,9.1 ) karśaphasya viśaphasya dyauḥ pitā pṛthivī mātā |
(3,9.1 ) yathābhicakra devāstathāpa kṛṇutā punaḥ ||1||

(3,9.2 ) aśreṣmāṇo adhārayan tathā tan manunā kṛtam |
(3,9.2 ) kṛṇomi vadhri viṣkandhaṃ muṣkābarho gavāmiva ||2||

(3,9.3 ) piśaṅge sūtre khṛgalaṃ tadā badhnanti vedhasaḥ |
(3,9.3 ) śravasyuṃ śuṣmaṃ kābavaṃ vadhriṃ kṛṇvantu bandhuraḥ ||3||

(3,9.4 ) yenā śravasyavaścaratha devā ivāsuramāyayā |
(3,9.4 ) śunāṃ kapiriva dūṣaṇo bandhurā kābavasya ca ||4||

(3,9.5 ) duṣṭyai hi tvā bhatsyāmi dūṣayiṣyāmi kābavam |
(3,9.5 ) udāśavo rathā iva śapathebhiḥ sariṣyatha ||5||

(3,9.6 ) ekaśataṃ viṣkandhāni viṣṭhitā pṛthivīmanu |
(3,9.6 ) teṣāṃ tvāmagre ujjaharurmaṇiṃ viṣkandhadūṣaṇam ||6||



(3,10.1 ) prathamā ha vyuvāsa sā dhenurabhavadyame |
(3,10.1 ) sā naḥ payasvatī duhāmuttarāmuttarāṃ samām ||1||

(3,10.2 ) yāṃ devāḥ pratinandanti rātriṃ dhenumupāyatīm |
(3,10.2 ) saṃvatsarasya yā patnī sā no astu sumaṅgalī ||2||

(3,10.3 ) saṃvatsarasya pratimāṃ yāṃ tvā rātryupāsmahe |
(3,10.3 ) sā na āyuṣmatīṃ prajāṃ rāyaspoṣeṇa saṃ sṛja ||3||

(3,10.4 ) iyameva sā yā prathamā vyauchadāsvitarāsu carati praviṣṭā |
(3,10.4 ) mahānto asyāṃ mahimāno antarvadhūrjigāya navagajjanitrī ||4||

(3,10.5 ) vānaspatyā grāvāṇo ghoṣamakrata haviṣkṛṇvantaḥ parivatsarīṇam |
(3,10.5 ) ekāṣṭake suprajasaḥ suvīrā vayaṃ syāma patayo rayīṇām ||5||

(3,10.6 ) iḍāyāspadaṃ ghṛtavatsarīsṛpaṃ jātavedaḥ prati havyā gṛbhāya |
(3,10.6 ) ye grāmyāḥ paśavo viśvarūpāsteṣāṃ saptānāṃ mayi rantirastu ||6||

(3,10.7 ) ā mā puṣṭe ca poṣe ca rātri devānāṃ sumatau syāma |
(3,10.7 ) pūrṇā darve parā pata supūrṇā punarā pata |
(3,10.7 ) sarvān yajñāntsaṃbhuñjatīṣamūrjaṃ na ā bhara ||7||

(3,10.8 ) āyamagantsaṃvatsaraḥ patirekāṣṭake tava |
(3,10.8 ) sā na āyuṣmatīṃ prajāṃ rāyaspoṣeṇa saṃ sṛja ||8||

(3,10.9 ) ṛtūn yaja ṛtupatīn ārtavān uta hāyanān |
(3,10.9 ) samāḥ saṃvatsarān māsān bhūtasya pataye yaje ||9||

(3,10.10 ) ṛtubhyaṣṭvārtavebhyo mādbhyaḥ saṃvatsarebhyaḥ |
(3,10.10 ) dhātre vidhātre samṛdhe bhūtasya pataye yaje ||10||

(3,10.11 ) iḍayā juhvato vayaṃ devān ghṛtavatā yaje |
(3,10.11 ) gṛhān alubhyato vayaṃ saṃ viśemopa gomataḥ ||11||

(3,10.12 ) ekāṣṭakā tapasā tapyamānā jajāna garbhaṃ mahimānamindram |
(3,10.12 ) tena devā vyasahanta śatrūn hantā dasyūnāmabhavacchacīpatiḥ ||12||

(3,10.13 ) indraputre somaputre duhitāsi prajāpateḥ |
(3,10.13 ) kāmān asmākaṃ pūraya prati gṛhṇāhi no haviḥ ||13||



(3,11.1 ) muñcāmi tvā haviṣā jīvanāya kamajñātayakṣmāduta rājayakṣmāt|
(3,11.1 ) grāhirjagrāha yadyetadenaṃ tasyā indrāgnī pra mumuktamenam ||1||

(3,11.2 ) yadi kṣitāyuryadi vā pareto yadi mṛtyorantikameva |
(3,11.2 ) tamā harāmi nirṛterupasthādaspārśamenaṃ śataśāradāya ||2||

(3,11.3 ) sahasrākṣeṇa śatavīryeṇa śatāyuṣā haviṣāhārṣamenam |
(3,11.3 ) indro yathainaṃ śarado nayātyati viśvasya duritasya pāram ||3||

(3,11.4 ) śataṃ jīva śarado vardhamānaḥ śataṃ hemantān chatamu vasantān |
(3,11.4 ) śataṃ te indro agniḥ savitā bṛhaspatiḥ śatāyuṣā haviṣāhārṣamenam ||4||

(3,11.5 ) pra viśataṃ prāṇāpānāvanaḍvāhāviva vrajam |
(3,11.5 ) vyanye yantu mṛtyavo yān āhuritarān chatam ||5||

(3,11.6 ) ihaiva staṃ prāṇāpānau māpa gātamito yuvam |
(3,11.6 ) śarīramasyāṅgāni jarase vahataṃ punaḥ ||6||

(3,11.7 ) jarāyai tvā pari dadāmi jarāyai ni dhuvāmi tvā |
(3,11.7 ) jarā tvā bhadrā neṣṭa vyanye yantu mṛtyavo yān āhuritarān chatam ||7||

(3,11.8 ) abhi tvā jarimāhita gāmukṣaṇamiva rajjvā |
(3,11.8 ) yastvā mṛtyurabhyadhatta jāyamānaṃ supāśayā |
(3,11.8 ) taṃ te satyasya hastābhyāmudamuñcadbṛhaspatiḥ ||8||



(3,12.1 ) ihaiva dhruvāṃ ni minomi śālāṃ kṣeme tiṣṭhāti ghṛtamukṣamāṇā |
(3,12.1 ) tāṃ tvā śāle sarvavīrāḥ suvīrā ariṣṭavīrā upa saṃ carema ||1||

(3,12.2 ) ihaiva dhruvā prati tiṣṭha śāle'śvāvatī gomatī sūnṛtāvatī |
(3,12.2 ) ūrjasvatī ghṛtavatī payasvatyucchrayasva mahate saubhagāya ||2||

(3,12.3 ) dharuṇyasi śāle bṛhachandāḥ pūtidhānyā |
(3,12.3 ) ā tvā vatso gamedā kumāra ā dhenavaḥ sāyamāspandamānāḥ ||3||

(3,12.4 ) imāṃ śālāṃ savitā vāyurindro bṛhaspatirni minotu prajānan |
(3,12.4 ) ukṣantūdnā maruto ghṛtena bhago no rājā ni kṛṣiṃ tanotu ||4||

(3,12.5 ) mānasya patni śaraṇā syonā devī devebhirnimitāsyagre |
(3,12.5 ) tṛṇaṃ vasānā sumanā asastvamathāsmabhyaṃ sahavīraṃ rayiṃ dāḥ ||5||

(3,12.6 ) ṛtena sthūṇāmadhi roha vaṃśogro virājann apa vṛṅkṣva śatrūn |
(3,12.6 ) mā te riṣann upasattāro gṛhāṇāṃ śāle śataṃ jīvema śaradaḥ sarvavīrāḥ ||6||

(3,12.7 ) emāṃ kumārastaruṇa ā vatso jagatā saha |
(3,12.7 ) emāṃ parisrutaḥ kumbha ā dadhnaḥ kalaśairaguḥ ||7||

(3,12.8 ) pūrṇaṃ nāri pra bhara kumbhametaṃ ghṛtasya dhārāmamṛtena saṃbhṛtām |
(3,12.8 ) imāṃ pātṝn amṛtena samaṅgdhīṣṭāpūrtamabhi rakṣātyenām ||8||

(3,12.9 ) imā āpaḥ pra bharāmyayakṣmā yakṣmanāśanīḥ |
(3,12.9 ) gṛhān upa pra sīdāmyamṛtena sahāgninā ||9||



(3,13.1 ) yadadaḥ saṃprayatīrahāvanadatā hate |
(3,13.1 ) tasmādā nadyo nāma stha tā vo nāmāni sindhavaḥ ||1||

(3,13.2 ) yatpreṣitā varuṇenācchībhaṃ samavalgata |
(3,13.2 ) tadāpnodindro vo yatīstasmādāpo anu ṣṭhana ||2||

(3,13.3 ) apakāmaṃ syandamānā avīvarata vo hi kam |
(3,13.3 ) indro vaḥ śaktibhirdevīstasmādvārnāma vo hitam ||3||

(3,13.4 ) ekaḥ vo devo'pyatiṣṭhatsyandamānā yathāvaśam |
(3,13.4 ) udāniṣurmahīriti tasmādudakamucyate ||4||

(3,13.5 ) āpo bhadrā ghṛtamidāpa āsann agnīṣomau bibhratyāpa ittāḥ |
(3,13.5 ) tīvro raso madhupṛcāmaraṃgama ā mā prāṇena saha varcasā gamet||5||

(3,13.6 ) āditpaśyāmyuta vā śṛṇomyā mā ghoṣo gachati vāṅmāsām |
(3,13.6 ) manye bhejāno amṛtasya tarhi hiraṇyavarṇā atṛpaṃ yadā vaḥ ||6||
(3,13.7 ) idaṃ va āpo hṛdayamayaṃ vatsa ṛtāvarīḥ |
(3,13.7 ) ihetthameta śakvarīryatredaṃ veśayāmi vaḥ ||7||



(3,14.1 ) saṃ vo goṣṭhena suṣadā saṃ rayyā saṃ subhūtyā |
(3,14.1 ) aharjātasya yan nāma tenā vaḥ saṃ sṛjāmasi ||1||

(3,14.2 ) saṃ vaḥ sṛjatvaryamā saṃ pūṣā saṃ bṛhaspatiḥ |
(3,14.2 ) samindro yo dhanaṃjayo mayi puṣyata yadvasu ||2||

(3,14.3 ) saṃjagmānā abibhyuṣīrasmin goṣṭhe karīṣiṇīḥ |
(3,14.3 ) bibhratīḥ somyaṃ madhvanamīvā upetana ||3||

(3,14.4 ) ihaiva gāva etaneho śakeva puṣyata |
(3,14.4 ) ihaivota pra jāyadhvaṃ mayi saṃjñānamastu vaḥ ||4||

(3,14.5 ) śivo vo goṣṭho bhavatu śāriśākeva puṣyata |
(3,14.5 ) ihaivota pra jāyadhvaṃ mayā vaḥ saṃ sṛjāmasi ||5||

(3,14.6 ) mayā gāvo gopatinā sacadhvamayaṃ vo goṣṭha iha poṣayiṣṇuḥ |
(3,14.6 ) rāyaspoṣeṇa bahulā bhavantīrjīvā jīvantīrupa vaḥ sadema ||6||



(3,15.1 ) indramahaṃ vaṇijaṃ codayāmi sa na aitu puraetā no astu |
(3,15.1 ) nudann arātiṃ paripanthinaṃ mṛgaṃ sa īśāno dhanadā astu mahyam ||1||

(3,15.2 ) ye panthāno bahavo devayānā antarā dyāvāpṛthivī saṃcaranti |
(3,15.2 ) te mā juṣantāṃ payasā ghṛtena yathā krītvā dhanamāharāṇi ||2||

(3,15.3 ) idhmenāgna ichamāno ghṛtena juhomi havyaṃ tarase balāya |
(3,15.3 ) yāvadīśe brahmaṇā vandamāna imāṃ dhiyaṃ śataseyāya devīm ||3||

(3,15.4 ) imāmagne śaraṇiṃ mīmṛṣo no yamadhvānamagāma dūram |
(3,15.4 ) śunaṃ no astu prapaṇo vikrayaśca pratipaṇaḥ phalinaṃ mā kṛṇotu |
(3,15.4 ) idaṃ havyaṃ saṃvidānau juṣethāṃ śunaṃ no astu caritamutthitaṃ ca ||4||

(3,15.5 ) yena dhanena prapaṇaṃ carāmi dhanena devā dhanamichamānaḥ |
(3,15.5 ) tan me bhūyo bhavatu mā kanīyo'gne sātaghno devān haviṣā ni ṣedha ||5||

(3,15.6 ) yena dhanena prapaṇaṃ carāmi dhanena devā dhanamichamānaḥ |
(3,15.6 ) tasmin ma indro rucimā dadhātu prajāpatiḥ savitā somo agniḥ ||6||

(3,15.7 ) upa tvā namasā vayaṃ hotarvaiśvānara stumaḥ |
(3,15.7 ) sa naḥ prajāsvātmasu goṣu prāṇeṣu jāgṛhi ||7||

(3,15.8 ) viśvāhā te sadamidbharemāśvāyeva tiṣṭhate jātavedaḥ |
(3,15.8 ) rāyaspoṣeṇa samiṣā madanto mā te agne prativeśā riṣāma ||8||



(3,16.1 ) prātaragniṃ prātarindraṃ havāmahe prātarmitrāvaruṇā prātaraśvinā |
(3,16.1 ) prātarbhagaṃ pūṣaṇaṃ brahmaṇaspatiṃ prātaḥ somamuta rudraṃ havāmahe ||1||

(3,16.2 ) prātarjitaṃ bhagamugraṃ havāmahe vayaṃ putramaditeryo vidhartā |
(3,16.2 ) ādhraścidyaṃ manyamānasturaścidrājā cidyaṃ bhagaṃ bhakṣītyāha ||2||

(3,16.3 ) bhaga praṇetarbhaga satyarādho bhagemāṃ dhiyamudavā dadan naḥ |
(3,16.3 ) bhaga pra ṇo janaya gobhiraśvairbhaga pra nṛbhirnṛvantaḥ syāma ||3||

(3,16.4 ) utedānīṃ bhagavantaḥ syāmota prapitva uta madhye ahnām |
(3,16.4 ) utoditau maghavantsūryasya vayaṃ devānāṃ sumatau syāma ||4||

(3,16.5 ) bhaga eva bhagavāmastu devastenā vayaṃ bhagavantaḥ syāma |
(3,16.5 ) taṃ tvā bhaga sarva ijjohavīmi sa no bhaga puraetā bhaveha ||5||

(3,16.6 ) samadhvarāyoṣaso namanta dadhikrāveva śucaye padāya |
(3,16.6 ) arvācīnaṃ vasuvidaṃ bhagaṃ me rathamivāśvā vājina ā vahantu ||6||

(3,16.7 ) aśvāvatīrgomatīrna uṣāso vīravatīḥ sadamuchantu bhadrāḥ |
(3,16.7 ) ghṛtaṃ duhānā viśvataḥ prapītā yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||7||



(3,17.1 ) sīrā yuñjanti kavayo yugā vi tanvate pṛthak|
(3,17.1 ) dhīrā deveṣu sumnayau ||1||

(3,17.2 ) yunakta sīrā vi yugā tanota kṛte yonau vapateha bījam |
(3,17.2 ) virājaḥ śnuṣṭiḥ sabharā asan no nedīya itsṛṇyaḥ pakvamā yavan ||2||

(3,17.3 ) lāṅgalaṃ pavīravatsuśīmaṃ somasatsaru |
(3,17.3 ) udidvapatu gāmaviṃ prasthāvadrathavāhanaṃ pībarīṃ ca prapharvyam ||3||
(3,17.4 ) indraḥ sītāṃ ni gṛhṇātu tāṃ pūṣābhi rakṣatu |
(3,17.4 ) sā naḥ payasvatī duhāmuttarāmuttarāṃ samām ||4||

(3,17.5 ) śunaṃ suphālā vi tudantu bhūmiṃ śunaṃ kīnāśā anu yantu vāhān |
(3,17.5 ) śunāsīrā haviṣā tośamānā supippalā oṣadhīḥ kartamasmai ||5||
(3,17.6 ) śunaṃ vāhāḥ śunaṃ naraḥ śunaṃ kṛṣatu lāṅgalam |
(3,17.6 ) śunaṃ varatrā badhyantāṃ śunamaṣṭrāmudiṅgaya ||6||

(3,17.7 ) śunāsīreha sma me juṣethām |
(3,17.7 ) yaddivi cakrathuḥ payastenemāmupa siñcatam ||7||

(3,17.8 ) sīte vandāmahe tvārvācī subhage bhava |
(3,17.8 ) yathā naḥ sumanā aso yathā naḥ suphalā bhuvaḥ ||8||

(3,17.9 ) ghṛtena sītā madhunā samaktā viśvairdevairanumatā marudbhiḥ |
(3,17.9 ) sā naḥ sīte payasābhyāvavṛtsvorjasvatī ghṛtavatpinvamānā ||9||



(3,18.1 ) imāṃ khanāmyoṣadhiṃ vīrudhāṃ balavattamām |
(3,18.1 ) yayā sapatnīṃ bādhate yayā saṃvindate patim ||1||

(3,18.2 ) uttānaparṇe subhage devajūte sahasvati |
(3,18.2 ) sapatnīṃ me parā ṇuda patiṃ me kevalaṃ kṛdhi ||2||

(3,18.3 ) nahi te nāma jagrāha no asmin ramase patau |
(3,18.3 ) parāmeva parāvataṃ sapatnīṃ gamayāmasi ||3||

(3,18.4 ) uttarāhamuttara uttareduttarābhyaḥ |
(3,18.4 ) adhaḥ sapatnī yā mamādharā sādharābhyaḥ ||4||

(3,18.5 ) ahamasmi sahamānātho tvamasi sāsahiḥ |
(3,18.5 ) ubhe sahasvatī bhūtvā sapatnīṃ me sahāvahai ||5||

(3,18.6 ) abhi te'dhāṃ sahamānāmupa te'dhāṃ sahīyasīm |
(3,18.6 ) māmanu pra te mano vatsaṃ gauriva dhāvatu pathā vāriva dhāvatu ||6||



(3,19.1 ) saṃśitaṃ ma idaṃ brahma saṃśitaṃ vīryaṃ balam |
(3,19.1 ) saṃśitaṃ kṣatramajaramastu jiṣṇuryeṣāmasmi purohitaḥ ||1||

(3,19.2 ) samahameṣāṃ rāṣṭraṃ syāmi samojo vīryaṃ balam |
(3,19.2 ) vṛścāmi śatrūṇāṃ bāhūn anena haviṣā aham ||2||

(3,19.3 ) nīcaiḥ padyantāmadhare bhavantu ye naḥ sūriṃ maghavānaṃ pṛtanyān |
(3,19.3 ) kṣiṇāmi brahmaṇāmitrān un nayāmi svān aham ||3||

(3,19.4 ) tīkṣṇīyāṃsaḥ paraśoragnestīkṣṇatarā uta |
(3,19.4 ) indrasya vajrāttīkṣṇīyāṃso yeṣāmasmi purohitaḥ ||4||

(3,19.5 ) eṣāmahamāyudhā saṃ syāmyeṣāṃ rāṣṭraṃ suvīraṃ vardhayāmi |
(3,19.5 ) eṣāṃ kṣatramajaramastu jiṣṇveṣāṃ cittaṃ viśve'vantu devāḥ ||5||

(3,19.6 ) uddharṣantāṃ maghavan vājinānyudvīrāṇāṃ jayatāmetu ghoṣaḥ |
(3,19.6 ) pṛthagghoṣā ululayaḥ ketumanta udīratām |
(3,19.6 ) devā indrajyeṣṭhā maruto yantu senayā ||6||

(3,19.7 ) pretā jayatā nara ugrā vaḥ santu bāhavaḥ |
(3,19.7 ) tīkṣṇeṣavo'baladhanvano hatogrāyudhā abalān ugrabāhavaḥ ||7||

(3,19.8 ) avasṛṣṭā parā pata śaravye brahmasaṃśite |
(3,19.8 ) jaya amitrān pra padyasva jahyeṣāṃ varaṃvaraṃ māmīṣāṃ moci kaścana ||8||



(3,20.1 ) ayaṃ te yonirṛtviyo yato jāto arocathāḥ |
(3,20.1 ) taṃ jānann agna ā rohādhā no vardhaya rayim ||1||

(3,20.2 ) agne achā vadeha naḥ pratyaṅnaḥ sumanā bhava |
(3,20.2 ) pra ṇo yacha viśāṃ pate dhanadā asi nastvam ||2||

(3,20.3 ) pra ṇo yachatvaryamā pra bhagaḥ pra bṛhaspatiḥ |
(3,20.3 ) pra devīḥ prota sūnṛtā rayiṃ devī dadhātu me ||3||

(3,20.4 ) somaṃ rājānamavase'gniṃ gīrbhirhavāmahe |
(3,20.4 ) ādityaṃ viṣṇuṃ sūryaṃ brahmāṇaṃ ca bṛhaspatim ||4||

(3,20.5 ) tvaṃ no agne agnibhirbrahma yajñaṃ vardhaya |
(3,20.5 ) tvaṃ no deva dātave rayiṃ dānāya codaya ||5||

(3,20.6 ) indravāyū ubhāviha suhaveha havāmahe |
(3,20.6 ) yathā naḥ sarva ijjanaḥ saṃgatyāṃ sumanā asaddānakāmaśca no bhuvat||6||

(3,20.7 ) aryamaṇaṃ bṛhaspatimindraṃ dānāya codaya |
(3,20.7 ) vātaṃ viṣṇuṃ sarasvatīṃ savitāraṃ ca vājinam ||7||

(3,20.8 ) vājasya nu prasave saṃ babhūvimemā ca viśvā bhuvanāni antaḥ |
(3,20.8 ) utāditsantaṃ dāpayatu prajānan rayiṃ ca naḥ sarvavīraṃ ni yacha ||8||

(3,20.9 ) duhrāṃ me pañca pradiṣo duhrāmurvīryathābalam |
(3,20.9 ) prāpeyaṃ sarvā ākūtīrmanasā hṛdayena ca ||9||

(3,20.10 ) gosaniṃ vācamudeyaṃ varcasā mābhyudihi |
(3,20.10 ) ā rundhāṃ sarvato vāyustvaṣṭā poṣaṃ dadhātu me ||10||



(3,21.1 ) ye agnayo apsvantarye vṛtre ye puruṣe ye aśmasu |
(3,21.1 ) ya āviveśoṣadhīryo vanaspatīṃstebhyo agnibhyo hutamastvetat||1||

(3,21.2 ) yaḥ some antaryo goṣvantarya āviṣṭo vayaḥsu yo mṛgeṣu |
(3,21.2 ) ya āviveśa dvipado yascatuṣpadastebhyo agnibhyo hutamastvetat||2||

(3,21.3 ) ya indreṇa sarathaṃ yāti devo vaiśvānara uta viśvadāvyaḥ |
(3,21.3 ) yaṃ johavīmi pṛtanāsu sāsahiṃ tebhyo agnibhyo hutamastvetat||3||

(3,21.4 ) yo devo viśvādyamu kāmamāhuryaṃ dātāraṃ pratigṛhṇantamāhuḥ |
(3,21.4 ) yo dhīraḥ śakraḥ paribhūradābhyastebhyo agnibhyo hutamastvetat||4||

(3,21.5 ) yaṃ tvā hotāraṃ manasābhi saṃvidustrayodaśa bhauvanāḥ pañca mānavāḥ |
(3,21.5 ) varcodhase yaśase sūnṛtāvate tebhyo agnibhyo hutamastvetat||5||

(3,21.6 ) ukṣānnāya vaśānnāya somapṛṣṭhāya vedhase |
(3,21.6 ) vaiśvānarajyeṣṭhebhyastebhyo agnibhyo hutamastvetat||6||

(3,21.7 ) divaṃ pṛthivīmanvantarikṣaṃ ye vidyutamanusaṃcaranti |
(3,21.7 ) ye dikṣvantarye vāte antastebhyo agnibhyo hutamastvetat||7||

(3,21.8 ) hiraṇyapāṇiṃ savitāramindraṃ bṛhaspatiṃ varuṇaṃ mitramagnim |
(3,21.8 ) viśvān devān aṅgiraso havāmahe imaṃ kravyādaṃ śamayantvagnim ||8||

(3,21.9 ) śānto agniḥ kravyācchāntaḥ puruṣareṣaṇaḥ |
(3,21.9 ) atho yo viśvadāvyastaṃ kravyādamaśīśamam ||9||

(3,21.10 ) ye parvatāḥ somapṛṣṭhā āpa uttānaśīvarīḥ |
(3,21.10 ) vātaḥ parjanya ādagniste kravyādamaśīśaman ||10||



(3,22.1 ) hastivarcasaṃ prathatāṃ bṛhadyaśo adityā yattanvaḥ saṃbabhūva |
(3,22.1 ) tatsarve samadurmahyametadviśve devā aditiḥ sajoṣāḥ ||1||

(3,22.2 ) mitraśca varuṇaścendro rudraśca cetatu |
(3,22.2 ) devāso viśvadhāyasaste māñjantu varcasā ||2||

(3,22.3 ) yena hastī varcasā saṃbabhūva yena rājā manuṣyesvapsvantaḥ |
(3,22.3 ) yena devā devatāmagra āyan tena māmadya varcasāgne varcasvinaṃ kṛṇu ||3||

(3,22.4 ) yatte varco jātavedo bṛhadbhavatyāhuteḥ |
(3,22.4 ) yāvatsūryasya varca āsurasya ca hastinaḥ |
(3,22.4 ) tāvan me aśvinā varca ā dhattāṃ puṣkarasrajā ||4||

(3,22.5 ) yāvaccatasraḥ pradiśaścakṣuryāvatsamaśnute |
(3,22.5 ) tāvatsamaitvindriyaṃ mayi taddhastivarcasam ||5||

(3,22.6 ) hastī mṛgāṇāṃ suṣadāmatiṣṭhāvān babhūva hi |
(3,22.6 ) tasya bhagena varcasābhi ṣiñcāmi māmaham ||6||



(3,23.1 ) yena vehadbabhūvitha nāśayāmasi tattvat|
(3,23.1 ) idaṃ tadanyatra tvadapa dūre ni dadhmasi ||1||

(3,23.2 ) ā te yoniṃ garbha etu pumān bāṇa iveṣudhim |
(3,23.2 ) ā vīro'tra jāyatāṃ putraste daśamāsyaḥ ||2||

(3,23.3 ) pumāṃsaṃ putraṃ janaya taṃ pumān anu jāyatām |
(3,23.3 ) bhavāsi putrāṇāṃ mātā jātānāṃ janayāśca yān ||3||

(3,23.4 ) yāni badrāṇi bījānyṛṣabhā janayanti ca |
(3,23.4 ) taistvaṃ putraṃ vindasva sā prasūrdhenukā bhava ||4||

(3,23.5 ) kṛṇomi te prājāpatyamā yoniṃ garbha etu te |
(3,23.5 ) vindasva tvaṃ putraṃ nāri yastubhyaṃ śamasacchamu tasmai tvaṃ bhava ||5||

(3,23.6 ) yāsāṃ dyauḥ pitā pṛthivī mātā samudro mūlaṃ vīrudhāṃ babhūva |
(3,23.6 ) tāstvā putravidyāya daivīḥ prāvantvoṣadhayaḥ ||6||



(3,24.1 ) payasvatīroṣadhayaḥ payasvan māmakaṃ vacaḥ |
(3,24.1 ) atho payasvatīnāmā bhare'haṃ sahasraśaḥ ||1||

(3,24.2 ) vedāhaṃ payasvantaṃ cakāra dhānyaṃ bahu |
(3,24.2 ) saṃbhṛtvā nāma yo devastaṃ vayaṃ havāmahe yoyo ayajvano gṛhe ||2||

(3,24.3 ) imā yāḥ pañca pradiśo mānavīḥ pañca kṛṣṭayaḥ |
(3,24.3 ) vṛṣṭe śāpaṃ nadīriveha sphātiṃ samāvahān ||3||

(3,24.4 ) udutsaṃ śatadhāraṃ sahasradhāramakṣitam |
(3,24.4 ) evāsmākedaṃ dhānyaṃ sahasradhāramakṣitam ||4||

(3,24.5 ) śatahasta samāhara sahasrahasta saṃ kira |
(3,24.5 ) kṛtasya kāryasya ceha sphātiṃ samāvaha ||5||

(3,24.6 ) tisro mātrā gandharvāṇāṃ catasro gṛhapatnyāḥ |
(3,24.6 ) tāsāṃ yā sphātimattamā tayā tvābhi mṛśāmasi ||6||

(3,24.7 ) upohaśca samūhaśca kṣattārau te prajāpate |
(3,24.7 ) tāvihā vahatāṃ sphātiṃ bahuṃ bhūmānamakṣitam ||7||



(3,25.1 ) uttudastvottudatu mā dhṛthāḥ śayane sve |
(3,25.1 ) iṣuḥ kāmasya yā bhīmā tayā vidhyāmi tvā hṛdi ||1||

(3,25.2 ) ādhīparṇāṃ kāmaśalyāmiṣuṃ saṃkalpakulmalām |
(3,25.2 ) tāṃ susaṃnatāṃ kṛtvā kāmo vidhyatu tvā hṛdi ||2||

(3,25.3 ) yā plīhānaṃ śoṣayati kāmasyeṣuḥ susaṃnatā |
(3,25.3 ) prācīnapakṣā vyoṣā tayā vidhyāmi tvā hṛdi ||3||

(3,25.4 ) śucā viddhā vyoṣayā śuṣkāsyābhi sarpa mā |
(3,25.4 ) mṛdurnimanyuḥ kevalī priyavādinyanuvratā ||4||

(3,25.5 ) ājāmi tvājanyā pari māturatho pituḥ |
(3,25.5 ) yathā mama kratāvaso mama cittamupāyasi ||5||

(3,25.6 ) vyasyai mitrāvaruṇau hṛdaścittānyasyatam |
(3,25.6 ) athaināmakratuṃ kṛtvā mamaiva kṛṇutaṃ vaśe ||6||



(3,26.1 ) ye'syāṃ stha prācyāṃ diśi hetayo nāma devāsteṣāṃ vo agniriṣavaḥ |
(3,26.1 ) te no mṛḍata te no'dhi brūta tebhyo vo namastebhyo vaḥ svāhā ||1||

(3,26.2 ) ye'syāṃ stha dakṣiṇāyāṃ diśyaviṣyavo nāma devāsteṣāṃ vaḥ kāma iṣavaḥ |
(3,26.2 ) te no mṛḍata te no'dhi brūta tebhyo vo namastebhyo vaḥ svāhā ||2||

(3,26.3 ) ye'syāṃ stha pratīcyāṃ diśi vairājā nāma devāsteṣāṃ va āpa iṣavaḥ |
(3,26.3 ) te no mṛḍata te no'dhi brūta tebhyo vo namastebhyo vaḥ svāhā ||3||

(3,26.4 ) ye'syāṃ sthodīcyāṃ diśi pravidhyanto nāma devāsteṣāṃ vo vāta iṣavaḥ |
(3,26.4 ) te no mṛḍata te no'dhi brūta tebhyo vo namastebhyo vaḥ svāhā ||4||

(3,26.5 ) ye'syāṃ stha dhruvāyāṃ diśi nilimpā nāma devāsteṣāṃ va oṣadhīriṣavaḥ |
(3,26.5 ) te no mṛḍata te no'dhi brūta tebhyo vo namastebhyo vaḥ svāhā ||5||

(3,26.6 ) ye'syāṃ sthordhvāyāṃ diśyavasvanto nāma devāsteṣāṃ vo bṛhaspatiriṣavaḥ |
(3,26.6 ) te no mṛḍata te no'dhi brūta tebhyo vo namastebhyo vaḥ svāhā ||6||



(3,27.1 ) prācī digagniradhipatirasito rakṣitādityā iṣavaḥ |
(3,27.1 ) tebhyo namo'dhipatibhyo namo rakṣitṛbhyo nama iṣubhyo nama ebhyo astu |
(3,27.1 ) yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmastaṃ vo jambhe dadhmaḥ ||1||

(3,27.2 ) dakṣiṇā digindro'dhipatistiraścirājī rakṣitā pitara iṣavaḥ |
(3,27.2 ) tebhyo namo'dhipatibhyo namo rakṣitṛbhyo nama iṣubhyo nama ebhyo astu |
(3,27.2 ) yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmastaṃ vo jambhe dadhmaḥ ||2||

(3,27.3 ) pratīcī digvaruṇo'dhipatiḥ pṛdākū rakṣitānnamiṣavaḥ |
(3,27.3 ) tebhyo namo'dhipatibhyo namo rakṣitṛbhyo nama iṣubhyo nama ebhyo astu |
(3,27.3 ) yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmastaṃ vo jambhe dadhmaḥ ||3||

(3,27.4 ) udīcī diksomo'dhipatiḥ svajo rakṣitāśaniriṣavaḥ |
(3,27.4 ) tebhyo namo'dhipatibhyo namo rakṣitṛbhyo nama iṣubhyo nama ebhyo astu |
(3,27.4 ) yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmastaṃ vo jambhe dadhmaḥ ||4||

(3,27.5 ) dhruvā digviṣṇuradhipatiḥ kalmāṣagrīvo rakṣitā vīrudha iṣavaḥ |
(3,27.5 ) tebhyo namo'dhipatibhyo namo rakṣitṛbhyo nama iṣubhyo nama ebhyo astu |
(3,27.5 ) yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmastaṃ vo jambhe dadhmaḥ ||5||

(3,27.6 ) ūrdhvā digbṛhaspatiradhipatiḥ śvitro rakṣitā varṣamiṣavaḥ |
(3,27.6 ) tebhyo namo'dhipatibhyo namo rakṣitṛbhyo nama iṣubhyo nama ebhyo astu |
(3,27.6 ) yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmastaṃ vo jambhe dadhmaḥ ||6||


(3,28.1 ) ekaikayaiṣā sṛṣṭyā saṃ babhūva yatra gā asṛjanta bhūtakṛto viśvarūpāḥ |
(3,28.1 ) yatra vijāyate yaminyapartuḥ sā paśūn kṣiṇāti riphatī ruśatī ||1||

(3,28.2 ) eṣā paśūntsaṃ kṣiṇāti kravyādbhūtvā vyadvarī |
(3,28.2 ) utaināṃ brahmaṇe dadyāttathā syonā śivā syāt||2||

(3,28.3 ) śivā bhava puruṣebhyo gobhyo aśvebhyaḥ śivā |
(3,28.3 ) śivāsmai sarvasmai kṣetrāya śivā na ihaidhi ||3||

(3,28.4 ) iha puṣṭiriha rasa iha sahasrasātamā bhava |
(3,28.4 ) paśūn yamini poṣaya ||4||

(3,28.5 ) yatrā suhārdaḥ sukṛto madanti vihāya rogaṃ tanvaḥ svāyāḥ |
(3,28.5 ) taṃ lokaṃ yaminyabhisaṃbabhūva sā no mā hiṃsītpuruṣān paśūṃśca ||5||

(3,28.6 ) yatrā suhārdāṃ sukṛtāmagnihotrahutāṃ yatra lokaḥ |
(3,28.6 ) taṃ lokaṃ yaminyabhisaṃbabhūva sā no mā hiṃsītpuruṣān paśūṃśca ||6||



(3,29.1 ) yadrājāno vibhajanta iṣṭāpūrtasya ṣoḍaśaṃ yamasyāmī sabhāsadaḥ |
(3,29.1 ) avistasmātpra muñcati dattaḥ śitipātsvadhā ||1||

(3,29.2 ) sarvān kāmān pūrayatyābhavan prabhavan bhavan |
(3,29.2 ) ākūtipro'virdattaḥ śitipānn nopa dasyati ||2||

(3,29.3 ) yo dadāti śitipādamaviṃ lokena saṃmitam |
(3,29.3 ) sa nākamabhyārohati yatra śulko na kriyate abalena balīyase ||3||

(3,29.4 ) pañcāpūpaṃ śitipādamaviṃ lokena saṃmitam |
(3,29.4 ) pradātopa jīvati pitṝṇāṃ loke'kṣitam ||4||

(3,29.5 ) pañcāpūpaṃ śitipādamaviṃ lokena saṃmitam |
(3,29.5 ) pradātopa jīvati sūryāmāsayorakṣitam ||5||

(3,29.6 ) ireva nopa dasyati samudra iva payo mahat|
(3,29.6 ) devau savāsināviva śitipān nopa dasyati ||6||

(3,29.7 ) ka idaṃ kasmā adātkāmaḥ kāmāyādāt|
(3,29.7 ) kāmo dātā kāmaḥ pratigrahītā kāmaḥ samudramā viveśa |
(3,29.7 ) kāmena tvā prati gṛhnāmi kāmaitatte ||7||
(3,29.8 ) bhūmiṣṭvā prati gṛhṇātvantarikṣamidaṃ mahat|
(3,29.8 ) māhaṃ prāṇena mātmanā mā prajayā pratigṛhya vi rādhiṣi ||8||



(3,30.1 ) sahṛdayaṃ sāṃmanasyamavidveṣaṃ kṛṇomi vaḥ |
(3,30.1 ) anyo anyamabhi haryata vatsaṃ jātamivāghnyā ||1||

(3,30.2 ) anuvrataḥ pituḥ putro mātrā bhavatu saṃmanāḥ |
(3,30.2 ) jāyā patye madhumatīṃ vācaṃ vadatu śantivām ||2||

(3,30.3 ) mā bhrātā bhrātaraṃ dvikṣan mā svasāramuta svasā |
(3,30.3 ) samyañcaḥ savratā bhūtvā vācaṃ vadata bhadrayā ||3||

(3,30.4 ) yena devā na viyanti no ca vidviṣate mithaḥ |
(3,30.4 ) tatkṛṇmo brahma vo gṛhe saṃjñānaṃ puruṣebhyaḥ ||4||

(3,30.5 ) jyāyasvantaścittino mā vi yauṣṭa saṃrādhayantaḥ sadhurāścarantaḥ |
(3,30.5 ) anyo anyasmai valgu vadanta eta sadhrīcīnān vaḥ saṃmanasaskrṇomi ||5||

(3,30.6 ) samānī prapā saha vo'nnabhāgaḥ samāne yoktre saha vo yunajmi |
(3,30.6 ) samyañco'gniṃ saparyatārā nābhimivābhitaḥ ||6||

(3,30.7 ) sadhrīcīnān vaḥ saṃmanasaskṛṇomyekaśnuṣṭīntsaṃvananena sarvān |
(3,30.7 ) devā ivāmṛtaṃ rakṣamāṇāḥ sāyaṃprātaḥ saumanaso vo astu ||7||



(3,31.1 ) vi devā jarasāvṛtan vi tvamagne arātyā |
(3,31.1 ) vyahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa samāyuṣā ||1||

(3,31.2 ) vyārtyā pavamāno vi śakraḥ pāpakṛtyayā |
(3,31.2 ) vyahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa samāyuṣā ||2||

(3,31.3 ) vi grāmyāḥ paśava āraṇyairvyāpastṛṣṇayāsaran |
(3,31.3 ) vyahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa samāyuṣā ||3||

(3,31.4 ) vī me dyāvāpṛthivī ito vi panthāno diśaṃdiśam |
(3,31.4 ) vyahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa samāyuṣā ||4||

(3,31.5 ) tvaṣṭā duhitre vahatuṃ yunaktītīdaṃ viśvaṃ bhuvanaṃ vi yāti |
(3,31.5 ) vyahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa samāyuṣā ||5||

(3,31.6 ) agniḥ prāṇāntsaṃ dadhāti candraḥ prāṇena saṃhitaḥ |
(3,31.6 ) vyahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa samāyuṣā ||6||

(3,31.7 ) prāṇena viśvatovīryaṃ devāḥ sūryaṃ samairayan |
(3,31.7 ) vyahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa samāyuṣā ||7||

(3,31.8 ) āyuṣmatāmāyuṣkṛtāṃ prāṇena jīva mā mṛthāḥ |
(3,31.8 ) vyahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa samāyuṣā ||8||

(3,31.9 ) prāṇena prāṇatāṃ prāṇehaiva bhava mā mṛthāḥ |
(3,31.9 ) vyahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa samāyuṣā ||9||

(3,31.10 ) udāyuṣā samāyuṣodoṣadhīnāṃ rasena |
(3,31.10 ) vyahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa samāyuṣā ||10||

(3,31.11 ) ā parjanyasya vṛṣṭyodasthāmāmṛtā vayam |
(3,31.11 ) vyahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa samāyuṣā ||11||



(4,1.1 ) brahma jajñānaṃ prathamaṃ purastādvi sīmataḥ suruco vena āvaḥ |
(4,1.1 ) sa budhnyā upamā asya viṣṭhāḥ sataśca yonimasataśca vi vaḥ ||1||

(4,1.2 ) iyaṃ pitryā rāṣṭryetvagre prathamāya januṣe bhuvaneṣṭhāḥ |
(4,1.2 ) tasmā etaṃ surucaṃ hvāramahyaṃ gharmaṃ śrīṇantu prathamāya dhāsyave ||2||

(4,1.3 ) pra yo jajñe vidvān asya bandhurviśvā devānāṃ janimā vivakti |
(4,1.3 ) brahma brahmaṇa ujjabhāra madhyān nicairuccaiḥ svadhā abhi pra tasthau ||3||

(4,1.4 ) sa hi vidaḥ sa pṛthivyā ṛtasthā mahī kṣemaṃ rodasī askabhāyat|
(4,1.4 ) mahān mahī askabhāyadvi jāto dyāṃ sadma pārthivaṃ ca rajaḥ ||4||

(4,1.5 ) sa bhudhnyādāṣṭra januṣo'bhyagraṃ bṛhaspatirdevatā tasya samrāṭ|
(4,1.5 ) aharyacchukraṃ jyotiṣo janiṣṭātha dyumanto vi vasantu viprāḥ ||5||

(4,1.6 ) nūnaṃ tadasya kāvyo hinoti maho devasya pūrvyasya dhāma |
(4,1.6 ) eṣa jajñe bahubhiḥ sākamitthā pūrve ardhe viṣite sasan nu ||6||

(4,1.7 ) yo'tharvāṇaṃ pitaraṃ devabandhuṃ bṛhaspatiṃ namasāva ca gachāt|
(4,1.7 ) tvaṃ viśveṣāṃ janitā yathāsaḥ kavirdevo na dabhāyatsvadhāvān ||7||



(4,2.1 ) ya ātmadā baladā yasya viśva upāsate praśiṣaṃ yasya devāḥ |
(4,2.1 ) yo'syeśe dvipado yaścatuṣpadaḥ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema ||1||

(4,2.2 ) yaḥ prāṇato nimiṣato mahitvaiko rājā jagato babhūva |
(4,2.2 ) yasya chāyāmṛtaṃ yasya mṛtyuḥ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema ||2||

(4,2.3 ) yaṃ krandasī avataścaskabhāne bhiyasāne rodasī ahvayethām |
(4,2.3 ) yasyāsau panthā rajaso vimānaḥ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema ||3||

(4,2.4 ) yasya dyaururvī pṛthivī ca mahī yasyāda urvantarikṣam |
(4,2.4 ) yasyāsau sūro vitato mahitvā kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema ||4||

(4,2.5 ) yasya viśve himavanto mahitvā samudre yasya rasāmidāhuḥ |
(4,2.5 ) imāśca pradiśo yasya bāhū kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema ||5||

(4,2.6 ) āpo agre viśvamāvan garbhaṃ dadhānā amṛtā ṛtajñāḥ |
(4,2.6 ) yāsu devīṣvadhi deva āsītkasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema ||6||

(4,2.7 ) hiraṇyagarbhaḥ samavartatāgre bhūtasya jātaḥ patireka āsīt|
(4,2.7 ) sa dādhāra pṛthivīmuta dyāṃ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema ||7||

(4,2.8 ) āpo vatsaṃ janayantīrgarbhamagre samairayan |
(4,2.8 ) tasyota jāyamānasyolba āsīddhiraṇyayaḥ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema ||8||



(4,3.1 ) uditastrayo akraman vyāghraḥ puruṣo vṛkaḥ |
(4,3.1 ) hirugghi yanti sindhavo hirugdevo vanaspatirhiruṅnamantu śatravaḥ ||1||

(4,3.2 ) pareṇaitu pathā vṛkaḥ parameṇota taskaraḥ |
(4,3.2 ) pareṇa datvatī rajjuḥ pareṇāghāyurarṣatu ||2||

(4,3.3 ) akṣyau ca te mukhaṃ ca te vyāghra jambhayāmasi |
(4,3.3 ) ātsarvān viṃśatiṃ nakhān ||3||

(4,3.4 ) vyāghraṃ datvatāṃ vayaṃ prathamaṃ jambhayāmasi |
(4,3.4 ) ādu ṣṭenamatho ahiṃ yātudhānamatho vṛkam ||4||

(4,3.5 ) yo adya stena āyati sa saṃpiṣṭo apāyati |
(4,3.5 ) pathāmapadhvaṃsenaitvindro vajreṇa hantu tam ||5||

(4,3.6 ) mūrṇā mṛgasya dantā apiśīrṇā u pṛṣṭayaḥ |
(4,3.6 ) nimrukte godhā bhavatu nīcāyacchaśayurmṛgaḥ ||6||

(4,3.7 ) yatsaṃyamo na vi yamo vi yamo yan na saṃyamaḥ |
(4,3.7 ) indrajāḥ somajā ātharvaṇamasi vyāghrajambhanam ||7||



(4,4.1 ) yāṃ tvā gandharvo akhanadvaruṇāya mṛtabhraje |
(4,4.1 ) tāṃ tvā vayaṃ khanāmasyoṣadhiṃ śepaharṣaṇīm ||1||

(4,4.2 ) uduṣā udu sūrya udidaṃ māmakaṃ vacaḥ |
(4,4.2 ) udejatu prajāpatirvṛṣā śuṣmeṇa vājinā ||2||

(4,4.3 ) yathā sma te virohato'bhitaptamivānati |
(4,4.3 ) tataste śuṣmavattaramiyaṃ kṛṇotvoṣadhiḥ ||3||

(4,4.4 ) ucchuṣmauṣadhīnāṃ sārā ṛṣabhāṇām |
(4,4.4 ) saṃ puṃsāmindra vṛṣṇyamasmin dhehi tanūvaśin ||4||

(4,4.5 ) apāṃ rasaḥ prathamajo'tho vanaspatīnām |
(4,4.5 ) uta somasya bhrātāsyutārśamasi vṛṣṇyam ||5||

(4,4.6 ) adyāgne adya savitaradya devi sarasvati |
(4,4.6 ) adyāsya brahmaṇaspate dhanurivā tānayā pasaḥ ||6||

(4,4.7 ) āhaṃ tanomi te paso adhi jyāmiva dhanvani |
(4,4.7 ) kramasvarśa iva rohitamanavaglāyatā sadā ||7||

(4,4.8 ) aśvasyāśvatarasyājasya petvasya ca |
(4,4.8 ) atha ṛṣabhasya ye vājāstān asmin dhehi tanūvaśin ||8||



(4,5.1 ) sahasraśṛṅgo vṛṣabho yaḥ samudrādudācarat|
(4,5.1 ) tenā sahasyenā vayaṃ ni janāntsvāpayāmasi ||1||

(4,5.2 ) na bhūmiṃ vāto ati vāti nāti paśyati kaścana |
(4,5.2 ) striyaśca sarvāḥ svāpaya śunaścendrasakhā caran ||2||

(4,5.3 ) proṣṭheśayāstalpeśayā nārīryā vahyaśīvarīḥ |
(4,5.3 ) striyo yāḥ puṇyagandhayastāḥ sarvāḥ svāpayāmasi ||3||

(4,5.4 ) ejadejadajagrabhaṃ cakṣuḥ prāṇamajagrabham |
(4,5.4 ) aṅgānyajagrabhaṃ sarvā rātrīṇāmatiśarvare ||4||

(4,5.5 ) ya āste yaścarati yaśca tiṣṭhan vipaśyati |
(4,5.5 ) teṣāṃ saṃ dadhmo akṣīṇi yathedaṃ harmyaṃ tathā ||5||

(4,5.6 ) svaptu mātā svaptu pitā svaptu śvā svaptu viśpatiḥ |
(4,5.6 ) svapantvasyai jñātayaḥ svaptvayamabhito janaḥ ||6||

(4,5.7 ) svapna svapnābhikaraṇena sarvaṃ ni svāpayā janam |
(4,5.7 ) otsūryamanyāntsvāpayāvyuṣaṃ jāgṛtādahamindra ivāriṣṭo akṣitaḥ ||7||



(4,6.1 ) brāhmaṇo jajñe prathamo daśaśīrṣo daśāsyaḥ |
(4,6.1 ) sa somaṃ prathamaḥ papau sa cakārārasaṃ viṣam ||1||

(4,6.2 ) yāvatī dyāvāpṛthivī varimṇā yāvatsapta sindhavo vitaṣṭhire |
(4,6.2 ) vācaṃ viṣasya dūṣaṇīṃ tāmito niravādiṣam ||2||

(4,6.3 ) suparṇastvā garutmān viṣa prathamamāvayat|
(4,6.3 ) nāmīmado nārūrupa utāsmā abhavaḥ pituḥ ||3||

(4,6.4 ) yasta āsyatpañcāṅgurirvakrāccidadhi dhanvanaḥ |
(4,6.4 ) apaskambhasya śalyān niravocamahaṃ viṣam ||4||

(4,6.5 ) śalyādviṣaṃ niravocaṃ prāñjanāduta parṇadheḥ |
(4,6.5 ) apāṣṭhācchṛṅgātkulmalān niravocamahaṃ viṣam ||5||

(4,6.6 ) arasasta iṣo śalyo'tho te arasaṃ viṣam |
(4,6.6 ) utārasasya vṛkṣasya dhanuṣṭe arasārasam ||6||

(4,6.7 ) ye apīṣan ye adihan ya āsyan ye avāsṛjan |
(4,6.7 ) sarve te vadhrayaḥ kṛtā vadhrirviṣagiriḥ kṛtaḥ ||7||

(4,6.8 ) vadhrayaste khanitāro vadhristvamasyoṣadhe |
(4,6.8 ) vadhriḥ sa parvato giriryato jātamidaṃ viṣam ||8||



(4,7.1 ) vāridaṃ vārayātai varaṇāvatyāmadhi |
(4,7.1 ) tatrāmṛtasyāsiktaṃ tenā te vāraye viṣam ||1||

(4,7.2 ) arasaṃ prācyaṃ viṣamarasaṃ yadudīcyam |
(4,7.2 ) athedamadharācyaṃ karambheṇa vi kalpate ||2||

(4,7.3 ) karambhaṃ kṛtvā tiryaṃ pībaspākamudārathim |
(4,7.3 ) kṣudhā kila tvā duṣṭano jakṣivāntsa na rūrupaḥ ||3||

(4,7.4 ) vi te madaṃ madāvati śaramiva pātayāmasi |
(4,7.4 ) pra tvā carumiva yeṣantaṃ vacasā sthāpayāmasi ||4||

(4,7.5 ) pari grāmamivācitaṃ vacasā sthāpayāmasi |
(4,7.5 ) tiṣṭhā vṛkṣa iva sthāmnyabhrikhāte na rūrupaḥ ||5||
(4,7.6 ) pavastaistvā paryakrīṇan dūrśebhirajinairuta |
(4,7.6 ) prakrīrasi tvamoṣadhe'bhrikhāte na rūrupaḥ ||6||

(4,7.7 ) anāptā ye vaḥ prathamā yāni karmāṇi cakrire |
(4,7.7 ) vīrān no atra mā dabhan tadva etatpuro dadhe ||7||


(4,8.1 ) bhūto bhūteṣu paya ā dadhāti sa bhūtānāmadhipatirbabhūva |
(4,8.1 ) tasya mṛtyuścarati rājasūyaṃ sa rājā anu manyatāmidam ||1||

(4,8.2 ) abhi prehi māpa vena ugraścettā sapatnahā |
(4,8.2 ) ā tiṣṭha mitravardhana tubhyaṃ devā adhi bruvan ||2||
