(4,8.3 ) ātiṣṭhantaṃ pari viśve abhūṣaṃ chriyaṃ vasānaścarati svarociḥ |
(4,8.3 ) mahattadvṛṣṇo asurasya nāmā viśvarūpo amṛtāni tasthau ||3||

(4,8.4 ) vyāghro adhi vaiyāghre vi kramasva diśo mahīḥ |
(4,8.4 ) viśastvā sarvā vāñchantvāpo divyāḥ payasvatīḥ ||4||

(4,8.5 ) yā āpo divyāḥ payasā madantyantarikṣa uta vā pṛthivyām |
(4,8.5 ) tāsāṃ tvā sarvāsāmapāmabhi ṣiñcāmi varcasā ||5||

(4,8.6 ) abhi tvā varcasāsicann āpo divyāḥ payasvatīḥ |
(4,8.6 ) yathāso mitravardhanastathā tvā savitā karat||6||

(4,8.7 ) enā vyāghraṃ pariṣasvajānāḥ siṃhaṃ hinvanti mahate saubhagāya |
(4,8.7 ) samudraṃ na subhuvastasthivāṃsaṃ marmṛjyante dvīpinamapsvantaḥ ||7||



(4,9.1 ) ehi jīvaṃ trāyamāṇaṃ parvatasyāsyakṣyam |
(4,9.1 ) viśvebhirdevairdattaṃ paridhirjīvanāya kam ||1||

(4,9.2 ) paripāṇaṃ puruṣāṇāṃ paripāṇaṃ gavāmasi |
(4,9.2 ) aśvānāmarvatāṃ paripāṇāya tasthiṣe ||2||

(4,9.3 ) utāsi paripāṇaṃ yātujambhanamāñjana |
(4,9.3 ) utāmṛtasya tvaṃ vetthātho asi jīvabhojanamatho haritabheṣajam ||3||

(4,9.4 ) yasyāñjana prasarpasyaṅgamaṅgaṃ paruṣparuḥ |
(4,9.4 ) tato yakṣmaṃ vi bādhasa ugro madhyamaśīriva ||4||

(4,9.5 ) nainaṃ prāpnoti śapatho na kṛtyā nābhiśocanam |
(4,9.5 ) nainaṃ viṣkandhamaśnute yastvā bibhartyāñjana ||5||

(4,9.6 ) asanmantrādduṣvapnyādduṣkṛtācchamalāduta |
(4,9.6 ) durhārdaścakṣuṣo ghorāttasmān naḥ pāhyāñjana ||6||

(4,9.7 ) idaṃ vidvān āñjana satyaṃ vakṣyāmi nānṛtam |
(4,9.7 ) saneyamaśvaṃ gāmahamātmānaṃ tava pūruṣa ||7||

(4,9.8 ) trayo dāsā āñjanasya takmā balāsa ādahiḥ |
(4,9.8 ) varṣiṣṭhaḥ parvatānāṃ trikakun nāma te pitā ||8||

(4,9.9 ) yadāñjanaṃ traikakudaṃ jātaṃ himavataspari |
(4,9.9 ) yātūṃśca sarvāñjambhayatsarvāśca yātudhānyaḥ ||9||

(4,9.10 ) yadi vāsi traikakudaṃ yadi yāmunamucyase |
(4,9.10 ) ubhe te bhadre nāmnī tābhyāṃ naḥ pāhyāñjana ||10||



(4,10.1 ) vātājjāto antarikṣādvidyuto jyotiṣaspari |
(4,10.1 ) sa no hiraṇyajāḥ śaṅkhaḥ kṛśanaḥ pātvaṃhasaḥ ||1||

(4,10.2 ) yo agrato rocanānāṃ samudrādadhi jajñiṣe |
(4,10.2 ) śaṅkhena hatvā rakṣāṃsyattriṇo vi ṣahāmahe ||2||

(4,10.3 ) śaṅkhenāmīvāmamatiṃ śaṅkhenota sadānvāḥ |
(4,10.3 ) śaṅkho no viśvabheṣajaḥ kṛśanaḥ pātvaṃhasaḥ ||3||

(4,10.4 ) divi jātaḥ samudrajaḥ sindhutasparyābhṛtaḥ |
(4,10.4 ) sa no hiraṇyajāḥ śaṅkha āyuṣprataraṇo maṇiḥ ||4||

(4,10.5 ) samudrājjāto maṇirvṛtrājjāto divākaraḥ |
(4,10.5 ) so asmāntsarvataḥ pātu hetyā devāsurebhyaḥ ||5||

(4,10.6 ) hiraṇyānāmeko'si somāttvamadhi jajñiṣe |
(4,10.6 ) rathe tvamasi darśata iṣudhau rocanastvaṃ pra ṇa āyūṃṣi tāriṣat||6||

(4,10.7 ) devānāmasthi kṛśanaṃ babhūva tadātmanvaccaratyapsvantaḥ |
(4,10.7 ) tatte badhnāmyāyuṣe varcase balāya dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya kārśanastvābhi rakṣatu ||7||



(4,11.1 ) anaḍvān dādhāra pṛthivīmuta dyāmanaḍvān dādhārorvantarikṣam |
(4,11.1 ) anaḍvān dādhāra pradiśaḥ ṣaḍurvīranaḍvān viśvaṃ bhuvanamā viveśa ||1||

(4,11.2 ) anaḍvān indraḥ sa paśubhyo vi caṣṭe trayāṃ chakro vi mimīte adhvanaḥ |
(4,11.2 ) bhūtaṃ bhaviṣyadbhuvanā duhānaḥ sarvā devānāṃ carati vratāni ||2||

(4,11.3 ) indro jāto manuṣyeṣvantargharmastaptaścarati śośucānaḥ |
(4,11.3 ) suprajāḥ santsa udāre na sarṣadyo nāśnīyādanaḍuho vijānan ||3||

(4,11.4 ) anaḍvān duhe sukṛtasya loka ainaṃ pyāyayati pavamānaḥ purastāt|
(4,11.4 ) parjanyo dhārā maruta ūdho asya yajñaḥ payo dakṣiṇā doho asya ||4||

(4,11.5 ) yasya neśe yajñapatirna yajño nāsya dāteśe na pratigrahītā |
(4,11.5 ) yo viśvajidviśvabhṛdviśvakarmā gharmaṃ no brūta yatamaścatuṣpāt||5||

(4,11.6 ) yena devāḥ svarāruruhurhitvā śarīramamṛtasya nābhim |
(4,11.6 ) tena geṣma sukṛtasya lokaṃ gharmasya vratena tapasā yaśasyavaḥ ||6||

(4,11.7 ) indro rūpeṇāgnirvahena prajāpatiḥ parameṣṭhī virāṭ|
(4,11.7 ) viśvānare akramata vaiśvānare akramatānaduhyakramata |
(4,11.7 ) so'dṛṃhayata so'dhārayata ||7||

(4,11.8 ) madhyametadanaḍuho yatraiṣa vaha āhitaḥ |
(4,11.8 ) etāvadasya prācīnaṃ yāvān pratyaṅsamāhitaḥ ||8||

(4,11.9 ) yo vedānaduho dohān saptānupadasvataḥ |
(4,11.9 ) prajāṃ ca lokaṃ cāpnoti tathā saptaṛṣayo viduḥ ||9||

(4,11.10 ) padbhiḥ sedimavakrāmann irāṃ jaṅghābhirutkhidan |
(4,11.10 ) srameṇānaḍvān kīlālaṃ kīnāśaścābhi gachataḥ ||10||

(4,11.11 ) dvādaśa vā etā rātrīrvratyā āhuḥ prajāpateḥ |
(4,11.11 ) tatropa brahma yo veda tadvā anaḍuho vratam ||11||

(4,11.12 ) duhe sāyaṃ duhe prātarduhe madhyaṃdinaṃ pari |
(4,11.12 ) dohā ye asya saṃyanti tān vidmānupadasvataḥ ||12||



(4,12.1 ) rohaṇyasi rohaṇyasthnaśchinnasya rohaṇī |
(4,12.1 ) rohayedamarundhati ||1||

(4,12.2 ) yatte riṣṭaṃ yatte dyuttamasti peṣṭraṃ ta ātmani |
(4,12.2 ) dhātā tadbhadrayā punaḥ saṃ dadhatparuṣā paruḥ ||2||

(4,12.3 ) saṃ te majjā majjñā bhavatu samu te paruṣā paruḥ |
(4,12.3 ) saṃ te māṃsasya visrastaṃ samasthyapi rohatu ||3||

(4,12.4 ) majjā majñā saṃ dhīyatāṃ carmaṇā carma rohatu |
(4,12.4 ) asṛkte asthi rohatu māṃsaṃ māṃsena rohatu ||4||

(4,12.5 ) loma lomnā saṃ kalpayā tvacā saṃ kalpayā tvacam |
(4,12.5 ) asṛkte asthi rohatu chinnaṃ saṃ dhehyoṣadhe ||5||

(4,12.6 ) sa uttiṣṭha prehi pra drava rathaḥ sucakraḥ |
(4,12.6 ) supaviḥ sunābhiḥ prati tiṣṭhordhvaḥ ||6||

(4,12.7 ) yadi kartaṃ patitvā saṃśaśre yadi vāśmā prahṛto jaghāna |
(4,12.7 ) ṛbhū rathasyevāṅgāni saṃ dadhatparuṣā paruḥ ||7||



(4,13.1 ) uta devā avahitaṃ devā un nayathā punaḥ |
(4,13.1 ) utāgaścakruṣaṃ devā devā jīvayathā punaḥ ||1||

(4,13.2 ) dvāvimau vātau vāta ā sindhorā parāvataḥ |
(4,13.2 ) dakṣaṃ te anya āvātu vyanyo vātu yadrapaḥ ||2||

(4,13.3 ) ā vāta vāhi bheṣajaṃ vi vāta vāhi yadrapaḥ |
(4,13.3 ) tvaṃ hi viśvabheṣaja devānāṃ dūta īyase ||3||
(4,13.4 ) trāyantāmimaṃ devāstrāyantāṃ marutāṃ gaṇāḥ |
(4,13.4 ) trāyantāṃ viśvā bhūtāni yathāyamarapā asat||4||

(4,13.5 ) ā tvāgamaṃ śaṃtātibhiratho ariṣṭatātibhiḥ |
(4,13.5 ) dakṣaṃ ta ugramābhāriṣaṃ parā yakṣmaṃ suvāmi te ||5||

(4,13.6 ) ayaṃ me hasto bhagavān ayaṃ me bhagavattaraḥ |
(4,13.6 ) ayaṃ me viśvabheṣajo'yaṃ śivābhimarśanaḥ ||6||

(4,13.7 ) hastābhyāṃ daśaśākhābhyāṃ jihvā vācaḥ purogavī |
(4,13.7 ) anāmayitnubhyāṃ hastābhyāṃ tābhyāṃ tvābhi mṛśāmasi ||7||



(4,14.1 ) ajo hyagnerajaniṣṭa śokātso apaśyajjanitāramagre |
(4,14.1 ) tena devā devatāmagrā āyan tena rohān ruruhurmedhyāsaḥ ||1||

(4,14.2 ) kramadhvamagninā nākamukhyān hasteṣu bibhrataḥ |
(4,14.2 ) divaspṛṣṭhaṃ svargatvā miśrā devebhirādhvam ||2||

(4,14.3 ) pṛṣṭhātpṛthivyā ahamantarikṣamāruhamantarikṣāddivamāruham |
(4,14.3 ) divo nākasya pṛṣṭhātsvarjyotiragāmaham ||3||

(4,14.4 ) svaryanto nāpekṣanta ā dyāṃ rohanti rodasī |
(4,14.4 ) yajñaṃ ye viśvatodhāraṃ suvidvāṃso vitenire ||4||

(4,14.5 ) agne prehi prathamo devatānāṃ cakṣurdevānāmuta mānuṣānām |
(4,14.5 ) iyakṣamāṇā bhṛgubhiḥ sajoṣāḥ svaryantu yajamānāḥ svasti ||5||

(4,14.6 ) ajamanajmi payasā ghṛtena divyaṃ suparnaṃ payasaṃ bṛhantam |
(4,14.6 ) tena geṣma sukṛtasya lokaṃ svarārohanto abhi nākamuttamam ||6||

(4,14.7 ) pañcaudanaṃ pañcabhiraṅgulibhirdarvyoddhara pañcadhaitamodanam |
(4,14.7 ) prācyāṃ diśi śiro ajasya dhehi dakṣiṇāyāṃ diśi dakṣiṇaṃ dhehi pārśvam ||7||

(4,14.8 ) pratīcyāṃ diśi bhasadamasya dhehyuttarasyāṃ diśyuttaraṃ dhehi pārśvam |
(4,14.8 ) ūrdhvāyāṃ diśyajasyānūkaṃ dhehi diśi dhruvāyāṃ dhehi pājasyamantarikṣe madhyato madhyamasya ||8||

(4,14.9 ) śṛtamajaṃ śṛtayā prorṇuhi tvacā sarvairaṅgaiḥ saṃbhṛtaṃ viśvarūpam |
(4,14.9 ) sa uttisṭheto abhi nākamuttamaṃ padbhiścaturbhiḥ prati tiṣṭha dikṣu ||9||



(4,15.1 ) samutpatantu pradiśo nabhasvatīḥ samabhrāṇi vātajūtāni yantu |
(4,15.1 ) mahaṛṣabhasya nadato nabhasvato vāśrā āpaḥ pṛthivīṃ tarpayantu ||1||

(4,15.2 ) samīkṣayantu taviṣāḥ sudānavo'pāṃ rasā oṣadhībhiḥ sacantām |
(4,15.2 ) varṣasya sargā mahayantu bhūmiṃ pṛthagjāyantāmoṣadhayo viśvarūpāḥ ||2||

(4,15.3 ) samīkṣayasva gāyato nabhāṃsyapāṃ vegāsaḥ pṛthagudvijantām |
(4,15.3 ) varṣasya sargā mahayantu bhūmiṃ pṛthagjāyantāṃ vīrudho viśvarūpāḥ ||3||

(4,15.4 ) gaṇāstvopa gāyantu mārutāḥ parjanya ghoṣiṇaḥ pṛthak|
(4,15.4 ) sargā varṣasya varṣato varṣantu pṛthivīmanu ||4||

(4,15.5 ) udīrayata marutaḥ samudratastveṣo arko nabha utpātayātha |
(4,15.5 ) mahaṛṣabhasya nadato nabhasvato vāśrā āpaḥ pṛthivīṃ tarpayantu ||5||

(4,15.6 ) abhi kranda stanayārdayodadhiṃ bhūmiṃ parjanya payasā samaṅdhi |
(4,15.6 ) tvayā sṛṣṭaṃ bahulamaitu varṣamāśāraiṣī kṛśaguretvastam ||6||

(4,15.7 ) saṃ vo'vantu sudānava utsā ajagarā uta |
(4,15.7 ) marudbhiḥ pracyutā meghā varṣantu pṛthivīmanu ||7||

(4,15.8 ) āśāmāśāṃ vi dyotatāṃ vātā vāntu diśodiśaḥ |
(4,15.8 ) marudbhiḥ pracyutā meghāḥ saṃ yantu pṛthivīmanu ||8||

(4,15.9 ) āpo vidyudabhraṃ varṣaṃ saṃ vo'vantu sudānava utsā ajagarā uta |
(4,15.9 ) marudbhiḥ pracyutā meghāḥ prāvantu pṛthivīmanu ||9||

(4,15.10 ) apāmagnistanūbhiḥ saṃvidāno ya oṣadhīnāmadhipā babhūva |
(4,15.10 ) sa no varṣaṃ vanutāṃ jātavedāḥ prāṇaṃ prajābhyo amṛtaṃ divaspari ||10||

(4,15.11 ) prajāpatiḥ salilādā samudrādāpa īrayann udadhimardayāti |
(4,15.11 ) pra pyāyatāṃ vṛṣṇo aśvasya reto'rvān etena stanayitnunehi ||11||

(4,15.12 ) apo niṣiñcann asuraḥ pitā naḥ śvasantu gargarā apāṃ varuṇāva nīcīrapaḥ sṛja |
(4,15.12 ) vadantu pṛśnibāhavo maṇḍūkā iriṇānu ||12||

(4,15.13 ) saṃvatsaraṃ śaśayānā brāhmaṇā vratacāriṇaḥ |
(4,15.13 ) vācaṃ parjanyajinvitāṃ pra maṇdūkā avādiṣuḥ ||13||

(4,15.14 ) upapravada maṇḍūki varṣamā vada tāduri |
(4,15.14 ) madhye hradasya plavasva vigṛhya caturaḥ padaḥ ||14||

(4,15.15 ) khaṇvakhā3  khaimakhā3  madhye taduri |
(4,15.15 ) varṣaṃ vanudhvaṃ pitaro marutāṃ mana ichata ||15||

(4,15.16 ) mahāntaṃ kośamudacābhi ṣiñca savidyutaṃ bhavatu vātu vātaḥ |
(4,15.16 ) tanvatāṃ yajñaṃ bahudhā visṛṣṭā ānandinīroṣadhayo bhavantu ||16||



(4,16.1 ) bṛhann eṣāmadhiṣṭhātā antikādiva paśyati |
(4,16.1 ) ya stāyan manyate carantsarvaṃ devā idaṃ viduḥ ||1||

(4,16.2 ) yastiṣṭhati carati yaśca vañcati yo nilāyaṃ carati yaḥ prataṅkam |
(4,16.2 ) dvau saṃniṣadya yan mantrayete rājā tadveda varuṇastṛtīyaḥ ||2||

(4,16.3 ) uteyaṃ bhūmirvaruṇasya rājña utāsau dyaurbṛhatī dūreantā |
(4,16.3 ) uto samudrau varuṇasya kukṣī utāsminn alpa udake nilīnaḥ ||3||

(4,16.4 ) uta yo dyāmatisarpātparastān na sa mucyātai varuṇasya rājñaḥ |
(4,16.4 ) diva spaśaḥ pra carantīdamasya sahasrākṣā ati paśyanti bhūmim ||4||

(4,16.5 ) sarvaṃ tadrājā varuṇo vi caṣṭe yadantarā rodasī yatparastāt|
(4,16.5 ) saṃkhyātā asya nimiṣo janānāmakṣān iva śvaghnī ni minoti tāni ||5||

(4,16.6 ) ye te pāśā varuṇa saptasapta tredhā tiṣṭhanti viṣitā ruṣantaḥ |
(4,16.6 ) chinantu sarve anṛtaṃ vadantaṃ yaḥ satyavādyati taṃ sṛjantu ||6||

(4,16.7 ) śatena pāśairabhi dhehi varuṇainaṃ mā te mocyanṛtavāṅnṛcakṣaḥ |
(4,16.7 ) āstāṃ jālma udaraṃ śraṃśayitvā kośa ivābandhaḥ parikṛtyamānaḥ ||7||

(4,16.8 ) yaḥ samābhyo varuṇo yo vyābhyo yaḥ saṃdeśyo varuṇo yo videśyo |
(4,16.8 ) yo daivo varuṇo yaśca mānuṣaḥ ||8||

(4,16.9 ) taistvā sarvairabhi ṣyāmi pāśairasāvāmuṣyāyaṇāmuṣyāḥ putra |
(4,16.9 ) tān u te sarvān anusaṃdiśāmi ||9||


(4,17.1 ) īśāṇāṃ tvā bheṣajānāmujjeṣa ā rabhāmahe |
(4,17.1 ) cakre sahasravīryaṃ sarvasmā oṣadhe tvā ||1||

(4,17.2 ) satyajitaṃ śapathayāvanīṃ sahamānāṃ punaḥsarām |
(4,17.2 ) sarvāḥ samahvyoṣadhīrito naḥ pārayāditi ||2||

(4,17.3 ) yā śaśāpa śapanena yāghaṃ mūramādadhe |
(4,17.3 ) yā rasasya haraṇāya jātamārebhe tokamattu sā ||3||

(4,17.4 ) yāṃ te cakrurāme pātre yāṃ cakrurnīlalohite |
(4,17.4 ) āme māṃse kṛtyāṃ yāṃ cakrustayā kṛtyākṛto jahi ||4||

(4,17.5 ) dauṣvapnyaṃ daurjīvityaṃ rakṣo abhvamarāyyaḥ |
(4,17.5 ) durṇāmnīḥ sarvā durvācastā asman nāśayāmasi ||5||

(4,17.6 ) kṣudhāmāraṃ tṛṣṇāmāramagotāmanapatyatām |
(4,17.6 ) apāmārga tvayā vayaṃ sarvaṃ tadapa mṛjmahe ||6||

(4,17.7 ) tṛṣṇāmāraṃ kṣudhāmāramatho akṣaparājayam |
(4,17.7 ) apāmārga tvayā vayaṃ sarvaṃ tadapa mṛjmahe ||7||

(4,17.8 ) apāmārga oṣadhīnāṃ sarvāsāmeka idvaśī |
(4,17.8 ) tena te mṛjma āsthitamatha tvamagadaścara ||8||



(4,18.1 ) samaṃ jyotiḥ sūryeṇāhnā rātrī samāvatī |
(4,18.1 ) kṛṇomi satyamūtaye'rasāḥ santu kṛtvarīḥ ||1||

(4,18.2 ) yo devāḥ kṛtyāṃ kṛtvā harādaviduṣo gṛham |
(4,18.2 ) vatso dhāruriva mātaraṃ taṃ pratyagupa padyatām ||2||

(4,18.3 ) amā kṛtvā pāpmānaṃ yastenānyaṃ jighāṃsati |
(4,18.3 ) aśmānastasyāṃ dagdhāyāṃ bahulāḥ phaṭkarikrati ||3||

(4,18.4 ) sahasradhāman viśikhān vigrīvāṃ chāyayā tvam |
(4,18.4 ) prati sma cakruṣe kṛtyāṃ priyāṃ priyāvate hara ||4||

(4,18.5 ) anayāhamoṣadhyā sarvāḥ kṛtyā adūduṣam |
(4,18.5 ) yāṃ kṣetre cakruryāṃ goṣu yāṃ vā te puruṣeṣu ||5||

(4,18.6 ) yaścakāra na śaśāka kartuṃ śaśre pādamaṅgurim |
(4,18.6 ) cakāra bhadramasmabhyamātmane tapanaṃ tu saḥ ||6||

(4,18.7 ) apāmārgo'pa mārṣṭu kṣetriyaṃ śapathaśca yaḥ |
(4,18.7 ) apāha yātudhānīrapa sarvā arāyyaḥ ||7||

(4,18.8 ) apamṛjya yātudhānān apa sarvā arāyyaḥ |
(4,18.8 ) apāmārga tvayā vayaṃ sarvaṃ tadapa mṛjmahe ||8||



(4,19.1 ) uto asyabandhukṛduto asi nu jāmikṛt|
(4,19.1 ) uto kṛtyākṛtaḥ prajāṃ nadamivā chindhi vārṣikam ||1||

(4,19.2 ) brāhmaṇena paryuktāsi kaṇvena nārṣadena |
(4,19.2 ) senevaiṣi tviṣīmatī na tatra bhayamasti yatra prāpnoṣyoṣadhe ||2||

(4,19.3 ) agrameṣyoṣadhīnāṃ jyotiṣevābhidīpayan |
(4,19.3 ) uta trātāsi pākasyātho hantāsi rakṣasaḥ ||3||

(4,19.4 ) yadado devā asurāṃstvayāgre nirakurvata |
(4,19.4 ) tatastvamadhyoṣadhe'pāmārgo ajāyathāḥ ||4||

(4,19.5 ) vibhindatī śataśākhā vibhindan nāma te pitā |
(4,19.5 ) pratyagvi bhindhi tvaṃ taṃ yo asmāmabhidāsati ||5||

(4,19.6 ) asadbhūmyāḥ samabhavattadyāmeti mahadvyacaḥ |
(4,19.6 ) tadvai tato vidhūpāyatpratyakkartāramṛchatu ||6||

(4,19.7 ) pratyaṅhi saṃbabhūvitha pratīcīnaphalastvam |
(4,19.7 ) sarvān macchapathāmadhi varīyo yāvayā vadham ||7||

(4,19.8 ) śatena mā pari pāhi sahasreṇābhi rakṣā mā |
(4,19.8 ) indraste vīrudhāṃ pata ugra ojmānamā dadhat||8||


(4,20.1 ) ā paśyati prati paśyati parā paśyati paśyati |
(4,20.1 ) divamantarikṣamādbhūmiṃ sarvaṃ taddevi paśyati ||1||

(4,20.2 ) tisro divastisraḥ pṛthivīḥ ṣaṭcemāḥ pradiśāḥ pṛthak|
(4,20.2 ) tvayāhaṃ sarvā bhūtāni paśyāni devyoṣadhe ||2||

(4,20.3 ) divyasya suparṇasya tasya hāsi kanīnikā |
(4,20.3 ) sā bhūmimā rurohitha vahyaṃ śrāntā vadhūriva ||3||

(4,20.4 ) tāṃ me sahasrākṣo devo dakṣiṇe hasta ā dadhat|
(4,20.4 ) tayāhaṃ sarvaṃ paśyāmi yaśca śūdra utāryaḥ ||4||

(4,20.5 ) āviṣkṛṇuṣva rūpāni mātmānamapa gūhathāḥ |
(4,20.5 ) atho sahasracakṣo tvaṃ prati paśyāḥ kimīdinaḥ ||5||

(4,20.6 ) darśaya mā yātudhānān darśaya yātudhānyaḥ |
(4,20.6 ) piśācāntsarvān darśayeti tvā rabha oṣadhe ||6||

(4,20.7 ) kaśyapasya cakṣurasi śunyāśca caturakṣyāḥ |
(4,20.7 ) vīdhre sūryamiva sarpantaṃ mā piśācaṃ tiraskaraḥ ||7||

(4,20.8 ) udagrabhaṃ paripāṇādyātudhānaṃ kimīdinam |
(4,20.8 ) tenāhaṃ sarvaṃ paśyāmyuta śūdramutāryam ||8||

(4,20.9 ) yo antarikṣeṇa patati divaṃ yaśca atisarpati |
(4,20.9 ) bhūmiṃ yo manyate nāthaṃ taṃ piśācaṃ pra darśaya ||9||



(4,21.1 ) ā gāvo agmann uta bhadramakrantsīdantu goṣṭhe raṇayantvasme |
(4,21.1 ) prajāvatīḥ pururūpā iha syurindrāya pūrvīruṣaso duhānāḥ ||1||

(4,21.2 ) indro yajvane gṛṇate ca śikṣata upeddadāti na svaṃ muṣāyati |
(4,21.2 ) bhūyobhūyo rayimidasya vardhayann abhinne khilye ni dadhāti devayum ||2||

(4,21.3 ) na tā naśanti na dabhāti taskaro nāsāmāmitro vyathirā dadharṣati |
(4,21.3 ) devāṃśca yābhiryajate dadāti ca jyogittābhiḥ sacate gopatiḥ saha ||3||

(4,21.4 ) na tā arvā reṇukakāṭo'śnute na saṃskṛtatramupa yanti tā abhi |
(4,21.4 ) urugāyamabhayaṃ tasya tā anu gāvo martasya vi caranti yajvanaḥ ||4||

(4,21.5 ) gāvo bhago gāva indro ma ichādgāva somasya prathamasya bhakṣaḥ |
(4,21.5 ) imā yā gāvaḥ sa janāsa indra ichāmi hṛdā manasā cidindram ||5||

(4,21.6 ) yūyaṃ gāvo medayatha kṛśaṃ cidaśrīraṃ citkṛṇuthā supratīkam |
(4,21.6 ) bhadraṃ gṛhaṃ kṛṇutha bhadravāco bṛhadvo vaya ucyate sabhāsu ||6||

(4,21.7 ) prajāvatīḥ sūyavase ruśantīḥ śuddhā apaḥ suprapāṇe pibantīḥ |
(4,21.7 ) mā va stena īśata māghaśaṃsaḥ pari vo rudrasya hetirvṛṇaktu ||7||



(4,22.1 ) imamindra vardhaya kṣatriyaṃ me imaṃ viśāmekavṛṣaṃ kṛṇu tvam |
(4,22.1 ) niramitrān akṣṇuhyasya sarvāṃstān randhayāsmā ahamuttareṣu ||1||

(4,22.2 ) emaṃ bhaja grāme aśveṣu goṣu niṣṭaṃ bhaja yo amitro asya |
(4,22.2 ) varṣma kṣatrāṇāmayamastu rājendra śatruṃ randhaya sarvamasmai ||2||

(4,22.3 ) ayamastu dhanapatirdhanānāmayaṃ viśāṃ viśpatirastu rājā |
(4,22.3 ) asminn indra mahi varcāṃsi dhehyavarcasaṃ kṛṇuhi śatrumasya ||3||

(4,22.4 ) asmai dyāvāpṛthivī bhūri vāmaṃ duhāthāṃ gharmadughe iva dhenū |
(4,22.4 ) ayaṃ rājā priya indrasya bhūyātpriyo gavāmoṣadhīnāṃ paśūnām ||4||

(4,22.5 ) yunajmi ta uttarāvantamindraṃ yena jayanti na parājayante |
(4,22.5 ) yastvā karadekavṛṣaṃ janānāmuta rājñāmuttamaṃ mānavānām ||5||

(4,22.6 ) uttarastvamadhare te sapatnā ye ke ca rājan pratiśatravaste |
(4,22.6 ) ekavṛṣa indrasakhā jigīvāṃ chatrūyatāmā bharā bhojanāni ||6||

(4,22.7 ) siṃhapratīko viśo addhi sarvā vyāghrapratīko'va bādhasva śatrūn |
(4,22.7 ) ekavṛṣa indrasakhā jigīvāṃ chatrūyatāmā khidā bhojanāni ||7||



(4,23.1 ) agnermanve prathamasya pracetasaḥ pāñcajanyasya bahudhā yamindhate |
(4,23.1 ) viśoviśaḥ praviśivāṃsamīmahe sa no muñcatvaṃhasaḥ ||1||

(4,23.2 ) yathā havyaṃ vahasi jātavedo yathā yajñaṃ kalpayasi prajānan |
(4,23.2 ) evā devebhyaḥ sumatiṃ na ā vaha sa no muñcatvaṃhasaḥ ||2||

(4,23.3 ) yāmanyāmann upayuktaṃ vahiṣṭhaṃ karmaṅkarmann ābhagamagnimīḍe |
(4,23.3 ) rakṣohaṇaṃ yajñavṛdhaṃ ghṛtāhutaṃ sa no muñcatvaṃhasaḥ ||3||

(4,23.4 ) sujātaṃ jātavedasamagniṃ vaiśvānaraṃ vibhum |
(4,23.4 ) havyavāhaṃ havāmahe sa no muñcatvaṃhasaḥ ||4||

(4,23.5 ) yena ṛṣayo balamadyotayan yujā yenāsurāṇāmayuvanta māyāḥ |
(4,23.5 ) yenāgninā paṇīn indro jigāya sa no muñcatvaṃhasaḥ ||5||

(4,23.6 ) yena devā amṛtamanvavindan yenauṣadhīrmadhumatīrakṛṇvan |
(4,23.6 ) yena devāḥ svarābharantsa no muñcatvaṃhasaḥ ||6||

(4,23.7 ) yasyedaṃ pradiśi yadvirocate yajjātaṃ janitavyaṃ ca kevalam |
(4,23.7 ) staumyagniṃ nāthito johavīmi sa no muñcatvaṃhasaḥ ||7||



(4,24.1 ) indrasya manmahe śaśvadidasya manmahe vṛtraghna stomā upa mema āguḥ |
(4,24.1 ) yo dāśuṣaḥ sukṛto havameti sa no muñcatvaṃhasaḥ ||1||

(4,24.2 ) ya ugrīṇāmugrabāhuryayuryo dānavānāṃ balamāruroja |
(4,24.2 ) yena jitāḥ sindhavo yena gāvaḥ sa no muñcatvaṃhasaḥ ||2||

(4,24.3 ) yaścarṣaṇipro vṛṣabhaḥ svarvidyasmai grāvāṇaḥ pravadanti nṛmṇam |
(4,24.3 ) yasyādhvaraḥ saptahotā madiṣṭhaḥ sa no muñcatvaṃhasaḥ ||3||

(4,24.4 ) yasya vaśāsa ṛṣabhāsa ukṣaṇo yasmai mīyante svaravaḥ svarvide |
(4,24.4 ) yasmai śukraḥ pavate brahmaśumbhitaḥ sa no muñcatvaṃhasaḥ ||4||

(4,24.5 ) yasya juṣṭiṃ sominaḥ kāmayante yaṃ havanta iṣumantaṃ gaviṣṭau |
(4,24.5 ) yasminn arkaḥ śiśriye yasminn ojaḥ sa no muñcatvaṃhasaḥ ||5||

(4,24.6 ) yaḥ prathamaḥ karmakṛtyāya jajñe yasya vīryaṃ prathamasyānubuddham |
(4,24.6 ) yenodyato vajro'bhyāyatāhiṃ sa no muñcatvaṃhasaḥ ||6||

(4,24.7 ) yaḥ saṃgrāmān nayati saṃ yudhe vaśī yaḥ puṣṭāni saṃsṛjati dvayāni |
(4,24.7 ) staumīndraṃ nāthito johavīmi sa no muñcatvaṃhasaḥ ||7||



(4,25.1 ) vāyoḥ saviturvidathāni manmahe yāvātmanvadviśatho yau ca rakṣathaḥ |
(4,25.1 ) yau viśvasya paribhū babhūvathustau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||1||

(4,25.2 ) yayoḥ saṃkhyātā varimā pārhivāni yābhyāṃ rajo yupitamantarikṣe |
(4,25.2 ) yayoḥ prāyaṃ nānvānaśe kaścana tau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||2||

(4,25.3 ) tava vrate ni viśante janāsastvayyudite prerate citrabhāno |
(4,25.3 ) yuvaṃ vāyo savitā ca bhuvanāni rakṣathastau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||3||

(4,25.4 ) apeto vāyo savitā ca duṣkṛtamapa rakṣāṃsi śimidāṃ ca sedhatam |
(4,25.4 ) saṃ hyūrjayā sṛjathaḥ saṃ balena tau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||4||

(4,25.5 ) rayiṃ me poṣaṃ savitota vāyustanū dakṣamā suvatāṃ suśevam |
(4,25.5 ) ayakṣmatātiṃ maha iha dhattaṃ tau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||5||

(4,25.6 ) pra sumatiṃ savitarvāya ūtaye mahasvantaṃ matsaraṃ mādayāthaḥ |
(4,25.6 ) arvāgvāmasya pravato ni yachataṃ tau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||6||

(4,25.7 ) upa śreṣṭhā na āśiṣo devayordhāmann asthiran |
(4,25.7 ) staumi devaṃ savitāraṃ ca vāyuṃ tau no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||7||



(4,26.1 ) manve vāṃ dyāvāpṛthivī subhojasau sacetasau ye aprathethāmamitā yojanāni |
(4,26.1 ) pratiṣṭhe hyabhavataṃ vasūnāṃ te no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||1||

(4,26.2 ) pratiṣṭhe hyabhavataṃ vasūnāṃ pravṛddhe devī subhage urūcī |
(4,26.2 ) dyāvāpṛthivī bhavataṃ me syone te no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||2||

(4,26.3 ) asantāpe sutapasau huve'hamurvī gambhīre kavibhirnamasye |
(4,26.3 ) dyāvāpṛthivī bhavataṃ me syone te no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||3||

(4,26.4 ) ye amṛtaṃ bibhṛtho ye havīṃṣi ye srotyā bibhṛtho ye manuṣyān |
(4,26.4 ) dyāvāpṛthivī bhavataṃ me syone te no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||4||

(4,26.5 ) ye usriyā bibhṛtho ye vanaspatīn yayorvāṃ viśvā bhuvanānyantaḥ |
(4,26.5 ) dyāvāpṛthivī bhavataṃ me syone te no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||5||

(4,26.6 ) ye kīlālena tarpayatho ye ghṛtena yābhyāmṛte na kiṃ cana śaknuvanti |
(4,26.6 ) dyāvāpṛthivī bhavataṃ me syone te no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||6||

(4,26.7 ) yan medamabhiśocati yenayena vā kṛtaṃ pauruṣeyān na daivāt|
(4,26.7 ) staumi dyāvāpṛthivī nāthito johavīmi te no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||7||



(4,27.1 ) marutāṃ manve adhi me bruvantu premaṃ vājaṃ vājasāte avantu |
(4,27.1 ) āśūn iva suyamān ahva ūtaye te no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||1||

(4,27.2 ) utsamakṣitaṃ vyacanti ye sadā ya āsiñcanti rasamoṣadhīṣu |
(4,27.2 ) puro dadhe marutaḥ pṛśnimātṝṃste no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||2||
(4,27.3 ) payo dhenūnāṃ rasamoṣadhīnāṃ javamarvatāṃ kavayo ya invatha |
(4,27.3 ) śagmā bhavantu maruto naḥ syonāste no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||3||

(4,27.4 ) apaḥ samudrāddivamudvahanti divaspṛthivīmabhi ye sṛjanti |
(4,27.4 ) ye adbhirīśānā marutaścaranti te no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||4||

(4,27.5 ) ye kīlālena tarpayanti ye ghṛtena ye vā vayo medasā saṃsṛjanti |
(4,27.5 ) ye adbhirīśānā maruto varṣayanti te no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||5||

(4,27.6 ) yadīdidaṃ maruto mārutena yadi devā daivyenedṛgāra |
(4,27.6 ) yūyamīśidhve vasavastasya niṣkṛteste no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||6||

(4,27.7 ) tigmamanīkaṃ viditaṃ sahasvan mārutaṃ śardhaḥ pṛtanāsūgram |
(4,27.7 ) staumi maruto nāthito johavīmi te no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||7||



(4,28.1 ) bhavāśarvau manve vāṃ tasya vittaṃ yayorvāmidaṃ pradiśi yadvirocate |
(4,28.1 ) yāvasyeśāthe dvipado yau catuṣpadastau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||1||

(4,28.2 ) yayorabhyabhva uta yaddūre cidyau viditāviṣubhṛtāmasiṣṭhau |
(4,28.2 ) yāvasyeśathe dvipado yau catuṣpadastau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||2||

(4,28.3 ) sahasrākṣau vṛtrahanā huvehaṃ dūregavyūtī stuvann emyugrau |
(4,28.3 ) yāvasyeśathe dvipado yau catuṣpadastau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||3||

(4,28.4 ) yāvārebhāthe bahu sākamagre pra cedasrāṣṭramabhibhāṃ janeṣu |
(4,28.4 ) yāvasyeśathe dvipado yau catuṣpadastau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||4||

(4,28.5 ) yayorvadhān nāpapadyate kaścanāntardeveṣūta mānuṣeṣu |
(4,28.5 ) yāvasyeśathe dvipado yau catuṣpadastau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||5||

(4,28.6 ) yaḥ kṛtyākṛn mūlakṛdyātudhāno ni tasmin dhattaṃ vajramugrau |
(4,28.6 ) yāvasyeśathe dvipado yau catuṣpadastau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||6||

(4,28.7 ) adhi no brūtaṃ pṛtanāsūgrau saṃ vajreṇa sṛjataṃ yaḥ kimīdī |
(4,28.7 ) staumi bhavāśarvau nāthito johavīmi tau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||7||



(4,29.1 ) manve vāṃ mitrāvaruṇāvṛtāvṛdhau sacetasau druhvaṇo yau nudethe |
(4,29.1 ) pra satyāvānamavatho bhareṣu tau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||1||

(4,29.2 ) sacetasau druhvaṇo yau nudethe pra satyāvānamavatho bhareṣu |
(4,29.2 ) yau gachatho nṛcakṣasau babhruṇā sutaṃ tau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||2||

(4,29.3 ) yāvaṅgirasamavatho yāvagastiṃ mitrāvaruṇā jamadagnimattrim |
(4,29.3 ) yau kaśyapamavatho yau vasiṣṭhaṃ tau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||3||

(4,29.4 ) yau śyāvāśvamavatho vādhryaśvaṃ mitrāvaruṇā purumīḍhamattrim |
(4,29.4 ) yau vimadamavatho saptavadhriṃ tau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||4||

(4,29.5 ) yau bharadvājamavatho yau gaviṣṭhiraṃ viśvāmitraṃ varuṇa mitra kutsam |
(4,29.5 ) yau kakṣīvantamavatho prota kaṇvaṃ tau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||5||

(4,29.6 ) yau medhātithimavatho yau triśokaṃ mitrāvaruṇāvuśanāṃ kāvyaṃ yau |
(4,29.6 ) yau gotamamavatho prota mugdalaṃ tau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||6||

(4,29.7 ) yayo rathaḥ satyavartma rjuraśmirmithuyā carantamabhiyāti dūṣayan |
(4,29.7 ) staumi mitrāvaruṇau nāthito johavīmi tau no muñcatamaṃhasaḥ ||7||



(4,30.1 ) ahaṃ rudrebhirvasubhiścarāmyahamādityairuta viśvadevaiḥ |
(4,30.1 ) ahaṃ mitrāvarunobhā bibharmyahamindrāgnī ahamaśvinobhā ||1||

(4,30.2 ) ahaṃ rāṣṭrī saṃgamanī vasūnāṃ cikituṣī prathamā yajñiyānām |
(4,30.2 ) tāṃ mā devā vyadadhuḥ purutrā bhūristhātrāṃ bhūryāveśayantaḥ ||2||

(4,30.3 ) ahameva svayamidaṃ vadāmi juṣṭaṃ devānāmuta mānuṣāṇām |
(4,30.3 ) yaṃ kāmaye tantamugraṃ kṛṇomi taṃ brahmāṇaṃ tamṛṣiṃ taṃ sumedhām ||3||

(4,30.4 ) mayā so'nnamatti yo vipaśyati yaḥ prāṇati ya īṃ śṛṇotyuktam |
(4,30.4 ) amantavo māṃ ta upa kṣiyanti śrudhi śruta śruddheyaṃ te vadāmi ||4||

(4,30.5 ) ahaṃ rudrāya dhanurā tanomi brahmadviṣe śarave hantavā u |
(4,30.5 ) ahaṃ janāya samadaṃ kṛṇomi ahaṃ dyāvāpṛthivī ā viveśa ||5||

(4,30.6 ) ahaṃ somamāhanasaṃ bibharmyahaṃ tvaṣṭāramuta pūṣaṇaṃ bhagam |
(4,30.6 ) ahaṃ dadhāmi draviṇā haviṣmate suprāvyā yajamānāya sunvate ||6||

(4,30.7 ) ahaṃ suve pitaramasya mūrdhan mama yonirapsvantaḥ samudre |
(4,30.7 ) tato vi tiṣṭhe bhuvanāni viśvotāmūṃ dyāṃ varṣmaṇopa spṛśāmi ||7||

(4,30.8 ) ahameva vātaiva pra vāmyārabhamāṇā bhuvanāni viśvā |
(4,30.8 ) paro divā para enā pṛthivyaitāvatī mahimnā saṃ babhūva ||8||



(4,31.1 ) tvayā manyo sarathamārujanto harṣamāṇā hṛṣitāso marutvan |
(4,31.1 ) tigmeṣava āyudhā saṃśiśānā upa pra yantu naro agnirūpāḥ ||1||

(4,31.2 ) agniriva manyo tviṣitaḥ sahasva senānīrnaḥ sahure hūta edhi |
(4,31.2 ) hatvāya śatrūn vi bhajasva veda ojo mimāno vi mṛdho nudasva ||2||

(4,31.3 ) sahasva manyo abhimātimasmai rujan mṛṇan pramṛṇan prehi śatrūn |
(4,31.3 ) ugraṃ te pājo nanvā rurudhre vaśī vaśaṃ nayāsā ekaja tvam ||3||

(4,31.4 ) eko bahūnāmasi manya īḍitā viśaṃviśaṃ yuddhāya saṃ śiśādhi |
(4,31.4 ) akṛttaruktvayā yujā vayaṃ dyumantaṃ ghoṣaṃ vijayāya kṛṇmasi ||4||

(4,31.5 ) vijeṣakṛdindra ivānavabravo'smākaṃ manyo adhipā bhaveha |
(4,31.5 ) priyaṃ te nāma sahure gṛṇīmasi vidmā tamutsaṃ yata ābabhūtha ||5||

(4,31.6 ) ābhūtyā sahajā vajra sāyaka saho bibharṣi sahabhūta uttaram |
(4,31.6 ) kratvā no manyo saha medyedhi mahādhanasya puruhūta saṃsṛji ||6||

(4,31.7 ) saṃsṛṣṭaṃ dhanamubhayaṃ samākṛtamasmabhyaṃ dhattāṃ varuṇaśca manyuḥ |
(4,31.7 ) bhiyo dadhānā hṛdayeṣu śatravaḥ parājitāso apa ni layantām ||7||



(4,32.1 ) yaste manyo'vidhadvajra sāyaka saha ojaḥ puṣyati viśvamānuṣak|
(4,32.1 ) sāhyāma dāsamāryaṃ tvayā yujā vayaṃ sahaskṛtena sahasā sahasvatā ||1||

(4,32.2 ) manyurindro manyurevāsa devo manyurhotā varuṇo jātavedāḥ |
(4,32.2 ) manyuṃ viśa īḍate mānuṣīryāḥ pahi no manyo tapasā sajoṣāḥ ||2||

(4,32.3 ) abhīhi manyo tavasastavīyān tapasā yujā vi jahi śatrūn |
(4,32.3 ) amitrahā vṛtrahā dasyuhā ca viśvā vasūnyā bharā tvaṃ naḥ ||3||

(4,32.4 ) tvaṃ hi manyo abhibhūtyojāḥ svayaṃbhūrbhāmo abhimātiṣāhaḥ |
(4,32.4 ) viśvacarṣaṇiḥ sahuriḥ sahīyān asmāsvojaḥ pṛtanāsu dhehi ||4||

(4,32.5 ) abhāgaḥ sann apa pareto asmi tava kratvā taviṣasya pracetaḥ |
(4,32.5 ) taṃ tvā manyo akraturjihīḍāhaṃ svā tanūrbaladāvā na ehi ||5||

(4,32.6 ) ayaṃ te asmyupa na ehyarvāṅpratīcīnaḥ sahure viśvadāvan |
(4,32.6 ) manyo vajrinn abhi na ā vavṛtsva hanāva dasyūṃruta bodhyāpeḥ ||6||

(4,32.7 ) abhi prehi dakṣiṇato bhavā no'dhā vṛtrāṇi jaṅghanāva bhūri |
(4,32.7 ) juhomi te dharuṇaṃ madhvo agramubhāvupāṃśu prathamā pibāva ||7||



(4,33.1 ) apa naḥ śośucadaghamagne śuśugdhyā rayim |
(4,33.1 ) apa naḥ śośucadagham ||1||

(4,33.2 ) sukṣetriyā sugātuyā vasūyā ca yajāmahe |
(4,33.2 ) apa naḥ śośucadagham ||2||

(4,33.3 ) pra yadbhandiṣṭha eṣāṃ prāsmākāsaśca sūrayaḥ |
(4,33.3 ) apa naḥ śośucadagham ||3||

(4,33.4 ) pra yatte agne sūrayo jāyemahi pra te vayam |
(4,33.4 ) apa naḥ śośucadagham ||4||

(4,33.5 ) pra yadagneḥ sahasvato viśvato yanti bhānavaḥ |
(4,33.5 ) apa naḥ śośucadagham ||5||

(4,33.6 ) tvaṃ hi viśvatomukha viśvataḥ paribhūrasi |
(4,33.6 ) apa naḥ śośucadagham ||6||

(4,33.7 ) dviṣo no viśvatomukhāti nāveva pāraya |
(4,33.7 ) apa naḥ śośucadagham ||7||

(4,33.8 ) sa naḥ sindhumiva nāvāti parṣa svastaye |
(4,33.8 ) apa naḥ śośucadagham ||8||



(4,34.1 ) brahmāsya śīrṣaṃ bṛhadasya pṛṣṭhaṃ vāmadevyamudaramodanasya |
(4,34.1 ) chandāṃsi pakṣau mukhamasya satyaṃ viṣṭārī jātastapaso'dhi yajñaḥ ||1||

(4,34.2 ) anasthāḥ pūtāḥ pavanena śuddhāḥ śucayaḥ śucimapi yanti lokam |
(4,34.2 ) naiṣāṃ śiśnaṃ pra dahati jātavedāḥ svarge loke bahu straiṇameṣām ||2||

(4,34.3 ) viṣṭāriṇamodanaṃ ye pacanti nainān avartiḥ sacate kadā cana |
(4,34.3 ) āste yama upa yāti devāntsaṃ gandharvairmadate somyebhiḥ ||3||

(4,34.4 ) viṣṭāriṇamodanaṃ ye pacanti nainān yamaḥ pari muṣṇāti retaḥ |
(4,34.4 ) rathī ha bhūtvā rathayāna īyate pakṣī ha bhūtvāti divaḥ sameti ||4||

(4,34.5 ) eṣa yajñānāṃ vitato vahiṣṭho viṣṭāriṇaṃ paktvā divamā viveśa |
(4,34.5 ) āṇḍīkaṃ kumudaṃ saṃ tanoti bisaṃ śālūkaṃ śaphako mulālī |
(4,34.5 ) etāstvā dhārā upa yantu sarvāḥ svarge loke madhumatpinvamānā upa tvā tiṣṭhantu puṣkariṇīḥ samantāḥ ||5||

(4,34.6 ) ghṛtahradā madhukūlāḥ surodakāḥ kṣīreṇa pūrṇā udakena dadhnā |
(4,34.6 ) etāstvā dhārā upa yantu sarvāḥ svarge loke madhumatpinvamānā upa tvā tiṣṭhantu puṣkariṇīḥ samantāḥ ||6||

(4,34.7 ) caturaḥ kumbhāṃścaturdhā dadāmi kṣīreṇa pūrnāmudakena dadhnā |
(4,34.7 ) etāstvā dhārā upa yantu sarvāḥ svarge loke madhumatpinvamānā upa tvā tiṣṭhantu puṣkariṇīḥ samantāḥ ||7||

(4,34.8 ) imamodanaṃ ni dadhe brāhmaṇeṣu viṣṭāriṇaṃ lokajitaṃ svargam |
(4,34.8 ) sa me mā kṣeṣṭa svadhayā pinvamāno viśvarūpā dhenuḥ kāmadughā me astu ||8||



(4,35.1 ) yamodanaṃ prathamajā ṛtasya prajāpatistapasā brahmaṇe'pacat|
(4,35.1 ) yo lokānāṃ vidhṛtirnābhireṣāttenaudanenāti tarāṇi mṛtyum ||1||

(4,35.2 ) yenātaran bhūtakṛto'ti mṛtyuṃ yamanvavindan tapasā śrameṇa |
(4,35.2 ) yaṃ papāca brahmaṇe brahma pūrvaṃ tenaudanenāti tarāṇi mṛtyum ||2||

(4,35.3 ) yo dādhāra pṛthivīṃ viśvabhojasaṃ yo antarikṣamāpṛṇādrasena |
(4,35.3 ) yo astabhnāddivamūrdhvo mahimnā tenaudanenāti tarāṇi mṛtyum ||3||

(4,35.4 ) yasmān māsā nirmitāstriṃśadarāḥ saṃvatsaro yasmān nirmito dvādaśāraḥ |
(4,35.4 ) ahorātrā yaṃ pariyanto nāpustenaudanenāti tarāṇi mṛtyum ||4||

(4,35.5 ) yaḥ prāṇadaḥ prāṇadavān babhūva yasmai lokā ghṛtavantaḥ kṣaranti |
(4,35.5 ) jyotiṣmatīḥ pradiśo yasya sarvāstenaudanenāti tarāṇi mṛtyum ||5||

(4,35.6 ) yasmātpakvādamṛtaṃ saṃbabhūva yo gāyatryā adhipatirbabhūva |
(4,35.6 ) yasmin vedā nihitā viśvarūpāstenaudanenāti tarāṇi mṛtyum ||6||

(4,35.7 ) ava bādhe dviṣantaṃ devapīyuṃ sapatnā ye me'pa te bhavantu |
(4,35.7 ) brahmaudanaṃ viśvajitaṃ pacāmi śṛṇvantu me śraddadhānasya devāḥ ||7||



(4,36.1 ) tāntsatyaujāḥ pra dahatvagnirvaiśvānaro vṛṣā |
(4,36.1 ) yo no durasyāddipsāccātho yo no arātiyāt||1||

(4,36.2 ) yo no dipsādadipsato dipsato yaśca dipsati |
(4,36.2 ) vaiśvānarasya daṃṣṭrayoragnerapi dadhāmi tam ||2||

(4,36.3 ) ya āgare mṛgayante pratikrośe'māvāsye |
(4,36.3 ) kravyādo anyān dipsataḥ sarvāṃstāntsahasā sahe ||3||

(4,36.4 ) sahe piśācāntsahasaiṣāṃ draviṇaṃ dade |
(4,36.4 ) sarvān durasyato hanmi saṃ ma ākūtirṛdhyatām ||4||

(4,36.5 ) ye devāstena hāsante sūryeṇa mimate javam |
(4,36.5 ) nadīṣu parvateṣu ye saṃ taiḥ paśubhirvide ||5||

(4,36.6 ) tapano asmi piśācānāṃ vyāghro gomatāmiva |
(4,36.6 ) śvānaḥ siṃhamiva dṛṣṭvā te na vindante nyañcanam ||6||

(4,36.7 ) na piśācaiḥ saṃ śaknomi na stenaiḥ na vanargubhiḥ |
(4,36.7 ) piśācāstasmān naśyanti yamahaṃ grāmamāviśe ||7||

(4,36.8 ) yaṃ grāmamāviśata idamugraṃ saho mama |
(4,36.8 ) piśācāstasmān naśyanti na pāpamupa jānate ||8||

(4,36.9 ) ye mā krodhayanti lapitā hastinaṃ maśakā iva |
(4,36.9 ) tān ahaṃ manye durhitān jane alpaśayūn iva ||9||

(4,36.10 ) abhi taṃ nirṛtirdhattāmaśvamiva aśvābhidhānyā |
(4,36.10 ) malvo yo mahyaṃ krudhyati sa u pāśān na mucyate ||10||



(4,37.1 ) tvayā pūrvamatharvāṇo jaghnū rakṣāṃsyoṣadhe |
(4,37.1 ) tvayā jaghāna kaśyapastvayā kaṇvo agastyaḥ ||1||

(4,37.2 ) tvayā vayamapsaraso gandharvāṃscātayāmahe |
(4,37.2 ) ajaśṛṅgyaja rakṣaḥ sarvān gandhena nāśaya ||2||

(4,37.3 ) nadīṃ yantvapsaraso'pāṃ tāramavaśvasam |
(4,37.3 ) gulgulūḥ pīlā naladyaukṣagandhiḥ pramandanī |
(4,37.3 ) tatparetāpsarasaḥ pratibuddhā abhūtana ||3||

(4,37.4 ) yatrāśvatthā nyagrodhā mahāvṛkṣāḥ śikhaṇḍinaḥ |
(4,37.4 ) tatparetāpsarasaḥ pratibuddhā abhūtana ||4||

(4,37.5 ) yatra vaḥ preṅkhā haritā arjunā uta yatrāghātāḥ karkaryaḥ saṃvadanti |
(4,37.5 ) tatparetāpsarasaḥ pratibuddhā abhūtana ||5||

(4,37.6 ) eyamagann oṣadhīnāṃ vīrudhāṃ vīryāvatī |
(4,37.6 ) ajaśṛṅgyarāṭakī tīkṣṇaśṛṅgī vyṛṣatu ||6||

(4,37.7 ) ānṛtyataḥ śikhaṇḍino gandharvasyāpsarāpateḥ |
(4,37.7 ) bhinadmi muṣkāvapi yāmi śepaḥ ||7||

(4,37.8 ) bhīmā indrasya hetayaḥ śatamṛṣṭīrayasmayīḥ |
(4,37.8 ) tābhirhaviradān gandharvān avakādān vyṛṣatu ||8||

(4,37.9 ) bhīmā indrasya hetayaḥ śatamṛṣṭīrhiraṇyayīḥ |
(4,37.9 ) tābhirhaviradān gandharvān avakādān vyṛṣatu ||9||
(4,37.10 ) avakādān abhiśocān apsu jyotaya māmakān |
(4,37.10 ) piśācāntsarvān oṣadhe pra mṛṇīhi sahasva ca ||10||

(4,37.11 ) śvevaikaḥ kapirivaikaḥ kumāraḥ sarvakeśakaḥ |
(4,37.11 ) priyo dṛśa iva bhūtvā gandharvaḥ sacate striyas|
(4,37.11 ) tamito nāśayāmasi brahmaṇā vīryāvatā ||11||

(4,37.12 ) jāyā idvo apsaraso gandharvāḥ patayo yuyam |
(4,37.12 ) apa dhāvatāmartyā martyān mā sacadhvam ||12||


(4,38.1 ) udbhindatīṃ saṃjayantīmapsarāṃ sādhudevinīm |
(4,38.1 ) glahe kṛtāni kṛṇvānāmapsarāṃ tāmiha huve ||1||
(4,38.2 ) vicinvatīmākirantīmapsarāṃ sādhudevinīm |
(4,38.2 ) glahe kṛtāni gṛhṇānāmapsarāṃ tāmiha huve ||2||

(4,38.3 ) yāyaiḥ parinṛtyatyādadānā kṛtaṃ glahāt|
(4,38.3 ) sā naḥ kṛtāni sīṣatī prahāmāpnotu māyayā |
(4,38.3 ) sā naḥ payasvatyaitu mā no jaiṣuridaṃ dhanam ||3||
(4,38.4 ) yā akṣeṣu pramodante śucaṃ krodhaṃ ca bibhratī |
(4,38.4 ) ānandinīṃ pramodinīmapsarāṃ tāmiha huve ||4||

(4,38.5 ) sūryasya raśmīn anu yāḥ sañcaranti marīcīrvā yā anusañcaranti |
(4,38.5 ) yāsāmṛṣabho dūrato vājinīvāntsadyaḥ sarvān lokān paryeti rakṣan |
(4,38.5 ) sa na aitu homamimaṃ jusāṇo'ntarikṣeṇa saha vājinīvān ||5||

(4,38.6 ) antarikṣena saha vājinīvan karkīṃ vatsāmiha rakṣa vājin |
(4,38.6 ) ime te stokā bahulā ehyarvāṅiyaṃ te karkīha te mano'stu ||6||

(4,38.7 ) antarikṣeṇa saha vājinīvan karkīṃ vatsāmiha rakṣa vājin |
(4,38.7 ) ayaṃ ghāso ayaṃ vraja iha vatsāṃ ni badhnīmaḥ |
(4,38.7 ) yathānāma va īśmahe svāhā ||7||



(4,39.1 ) pṛthivyāmagnaye samanamantsa ārdhnot|
(4,39.1 ) yathā pṛthivyāmagnaye samanamann evā mahyaṃ saṃnamaḥ saṃ namantu ||1||

(4,39.2 ) pṛthivī dhenustasyā agnirvatsaḥ |
(4,39.2 ) sā me'gninā vatseneṣamūrjaṃ kāmaṃ duhām |
(4,39.2 ) āyuḥ prathamaṃ prajāṃ poṣaṃ rayiṃ svāhā ||2||

(4,39.3 ) antarikṣe vāyave samanamantsa ārdhnot|
(4,39.3 ) yathāntarikṣe vāyave samanamann evā mahyaṃ saṃnamaḥ saṃ namantu ||3||

(4,39.4 ) antarikṣaṃ dhenustasyā vatsaḥ |
(4,39.4 ) sā me vāyunā vatseneṣamūrjaṃ kāmaṃ duhām |
(4,39.4 ) āyuḥ prathamaṃ prajāṃ poṣaṃ rayiṃ svāhā ||4||

(4,39.5 ) divyādityāya samanamantsa ārdhnot|
(4,39.5 ) yathā divyādityāya samanamann evā mahyaṃ saṃnamaḥ saṃ namantu ||5||

(4,39.6 ) dyaurdhenustasyā ādityo vatsaḥ |
(4,39.6 ) sā ma ādityena vatseneṣamūrjaṃ kāmaṃ duhām |
(4,39.6 ) āyuḥ prathamaṃ prajāṃ poṣaṃ rayiṃ svāhā ||6||

(4,39.7 ) dikṣu candrāya samanamantsa ārdhnot|
(4,39.7 ) yathā dikṣu candrāya samanamann evā mahyaṃ saṃnamaḥ saṃ namantu ||7||

(4,39.8 ) diśo dhenavastāsāṃ candro vatsaḥ |
(4,39.8 ) tā me candreṇa vatseneṣamūrjaṃ kāmaṃ duhāmāyuḥ prathamaṃ prajāṃ posaṃ rayiṃ svāhā ||8||

(4,39.9 ) agnāvagniścarati praviṣṭa ṛṣīṇāṃ putro abhiśastipā u |
(4,39.9 ) namaskāreṇa namasā te juhomi mā devānāṃ mithuyā karma bhāgam ||9||

(4,39.10 ) hṛdā pūtaṃ manasā jātavedo viśvāni deva vayunāni vidvān |
(4,39.10 ) saptāsyāni tava jātavedastebhyo juhomi sa juṣasva havyam ||10||



(4,40.1 ) ye purastājjuhvati jātavedaḥ prācyā diśo'bhidāsantyasmān |
(4,40.1 ) agnimṛtvā te parāñco vyathantāṃ pratyagenān pratisareṇa hanmi ||1||

(4,40.2 ) ye dakṣiṇato juhvati jātavedo dakṣiṇāyā diśo'bhidāsantyasmān |
(4,40.2 ) yamamṛtvā te parāñco vyathantāṃ pratyagenā pratisareṇa hanmi ||2||

(4,40.3 ) ye paścājjuhvati jātavedaḥ pratīcyā diśo'bhidāsantyasmān |
(4,40.3 ) varuṇamṛtvā te parāñco vyathantāṃ pratyagenān pratisareṇa hanmi ||3||

(4,40.4 ) ya uttarato juhvati jātaveda udīcyā diśo'bhidāsantyasmān |
(4,40.4 ) somamṛtvā te parāñco vyathantāṃ pratyagenān pratisareṇa hanmi ||4||

(4,40.5 ) ye'dhastājjuhvati jātaveda udīcyā diśo'bhidāsantyasmān |
(4,40.5 ) bhūmimṛtvā te parāñco vyathantāṃ pratyagenān pratisareṇa hanmi ||5||

(4,40.6 ) ye'ntarikṣājjuhvati jātavedo vyadhvāyā diśo'bhidāsantyasmān |
(4,40.6 ) vāyumṛtvā te parāñco vyathantāṃ pratyagenān pratisareṇa hanmi ||6||

(4,40.7 ) ya upariṣṭājjuhvati jātaveda ūrdhvāyā diśo'bhidāsantyasmān |
(4,40.7 ) sūryamṛtvā te parāñco vyathantāṃ pratyagenān pratisareṇa hanmi ||7||

(4,40.8 ) ye diśāmantardeśebhyo juhvati jātavedaḥ sarvābhyo digbhyo'bhidāsanti asmān |
(4,40.8 ) brahma rtvā te parāñco vyathantāṃ pratyagenān pratisareṇa hanmi ||8||



(5,1.1 ) ṛdhaṅmantro yoniṃ ya ābabhūvāmṛtāsurvardhamānaḥ sujanmā |
(5,1.1 ) adabdhāsurbhrājamāno'heva trito dhartā dādhāra trīṇi ||1||

(5,1.2 ) ā yo dharmāṇi prathamaḥ sasāda tato vapūṃṣi kṛṇuṣe purūṇi |
(5,1.2 ) dhāsyuryoniṃ prathama ā viveśā yo vācamanuditāṃ ciketa ||2||

(5,1.3 ) yaste śokāya tanvaṃ rireca kṣaraddhiraṇyaṃ śucayo'nu svāḥ |
(5,1.3 ) atrā dadhete amṛtāni nāmāsme vastrāṇi viśa erayantām ||3||

(5,1.4 ) pra yadete prataraṃ pūrvyaṃ guḥ sadaḥsada ātiṣṭhanto ajuryam |
(5,1.4 ) kaviḥ śuṣasya mātarā rihāṇe jāmyai dhuryaṃ patimerayethām ||4||

(5,1.5 ) tadū ṣu te mahatpṛthujman namaḥ kaviḥ kāvyenā kṛṇomi |
(5,1.5 ) yatsamyañcāvabhiyantāvabhi kṣāmatrā mahī rodhacakre vāvṛdhete ||5||

(5,1.6 ) sapta maryādāḥ kavayastatakṣustāsāmidekāmabhyaṃhuro gāt|
(5,1.6 ) āyorha skambha upamasya nīḍe pathāṃ visarge dharuṇeṣu tasthau ||6||

(5,1.7 ) utāmṛtāsurvrata emi kṛnvann asurātmā tanvastatsumadguḥ |
(5,1.7 ) uta vā śakro ratnaṃ dadhātyūrjayā vā yatsacate havirdāḥ ||7||
(5,1.8 ) uta putraḥ pitaraṃ kṣatramīḍe jyeṣṭhaṃ maryādamahvayantsvastaye |
(5,1.8 ) darśan nu tā varuṇa yāste viṣṭhā āvarvratataḥ kṛṇavo vapūṃṣi ||8||

(5,1.9 ) ardhamardhena payasā pṛṇakṣyardhena śuṣma vardhase amura |
(5,1.9 ) aviṃ vṛdhāma śagmiyaṃ sakhāyaṃ varuṇaṃ putramadityā iṣiram |
(5,1.9 ) kaviśastānyasmai vapūṃṣyavocāma rodasī satyavācā ||9||



(5,2.1 ) tadidāsa bhuvaneṣu jyeṣṭhaṃ yato yajña ugrastveṣanṛmṇaḥ |
(5,2.1 ) sadyo jajñāno ni riṇāti śatrūn anu yadenaṃ madanti viśva ūmāḥ ||1||

(5,2.2 ) vavṛdhānaḥ śavasā bhūryojāḥ śatrurdāsāya bhiyasaṃ dadhāti |
(5,2.2 ) avyanacca vyanacca sasni saṃ te navanta prabhṛtā madeṣu ||2||

(5,2.3 ) tve kratumapi pṛñcanti bhūri dviryadete trirbhavantyūmāḥ |
(5,2.3 ) svadoḥ svādīyaḥ svādunā sṛjā samadaḥ su madhu madhunābhi yodhīḥ ||3||

(5,2.4 ) yadi cin nu tvā dhanā jayantaṃ raṇeraṇe anumadanti viprāḥ |
(5,2.4 ) ojīyaḥ śuṣmintsthiramā tanuṣva mā tvā dabhan durevāsaḥ kaśokāḥ ||4||

(5,2.5 ) tvayā vayaṃ śāśadmahe raṇeṣu prapaśyanto yudhenyāni bhūri |
(5,2.5 ) codayāmi ta āyudhā vacobhiḥ saṃ te śiśāmi brahmaṇā vayāṃsi ||5||

(5,2.6 ) ni taddadhiṣe'vare pare ca yasminn āvithāvasā duroṇe |
(5,2.6 ) ā sthāpayata mātaraṃ jigatnumata invata karvarāṇi bhūri ||6||

(5,2.7 ) stuṣva varṣman puruvartmānaṃ samṛbhvāṇaminatamamāptamāptyānām |
(5,2.7 ) ā darśati śavasā bhūryojāḥ pra sakṣati pratimānaṃ pṛthivyāḥ ||7||

(5,2.8 ) imā brahma bṛhaddivaḥ kṛṇavadindrāya śūṣamagriyaḥ svarṣāḥ |
(5,2.8 ) maho gotrasya kṣayati svarājā turaścidviśvamarṇavattapasvān ||8||

(5,2.9 ) evā mahān bṛhaddivo atharvāvocatsvāṃ tanvamindrameva |
(5,2.9 ) svasārau mātaribhvarī aripre hinvanti caine śavasā vardhayanti ca ||9||



(5,3.1 ) mamāgne varco vihaveṣvastu vayaṃ tvendhānāstanvaṃ puṣema |
(5,3.1 ) mahyaṃ namantāṃ pradiśaścatasrastvayādhyakṣeṇa pṛtanā jayema ||1||

(5,3.2 ) agne manyuṃ pratinudan pareṣāṃ tvaṃ no gopāḥ pari pāhi viśvataḥ |
(5,3.2 ) apāñco yantu nivatā durasyavo'maiṣāṃ cittaṃ prabudhāṃ vi neśat||2||

(5,3.3 ) mama devā vihave santu sarva indravanto maruto viṣṇuragniḥ |
(5,3.3 ) mamāntarikṣamurulokamastu mahyaṃ vātaḥ pavatāṃ kāmāyāsmai ||3||

(5,3.4 ) mahyaṃ yajantāṃ mama yānīṣṭākūtiḥ satyā manaso me astu |
(5,3.4 ) eno mā ni gāṃ katamaccanāhaṃ viśve devā abhi rakṣantu meha ||4||

(5,3.5 ) mayi devā draviṇamā yajantāṃ mayi āśīrastu mayi devahūtiḥ |
(5,3.5 ) daivāḥ hotāraḥ saniṣan na etadariṣṭāḥ syāma tanvā suvīrāḥ ||5||

(5,3.6 ) daivīḥ ṣaḍurvīruru naḥ kṛṇota viśve devāsa iha mādayadhvam |
(5,3.6 ) mā no vidadabhibhā mo aśastirmā no vidadvṛjinā dveṣyā yā ||6||

(5,3.7 ) tisro devīrmahi naḥ śarma yachata prajāyai nastanve yacca puṣṭam |
(5,3.7 ) mā hāsmahi prajayā mā tanūbhirmā radhāma dviṣate soma rājan ||7||

(5,3.8 ) uruvyacā no mahiṣaḥ śarma yachatvasmin have puruhūtaḥ purukṣu |
(5,3.8 ) sa naḥ prajāyai haryaśva mṛḍendra mā no rīriṣo mā parā dāḥ ||8||

(5,3.9 ) dhātā vidhātā bhuvanasya yaspatirdevaḥ savitābhimātiṣāhaḥ |
(5,3.9 ) ādityā rudrā aśvinobhā devāḥ pāntu yajamānaṃ nirṛthāt||9||

(5,3.10 ) ye naḥ sapatnā apa te bhavantvindrāgnibhyāmava bādhāmaha enān |
(5,3.10 ) ādityā rudrā uparispṛśo no ugraṃ cettāramadhirājamakrata ||10||

(5,3.11 ) arvāñcamindramamuto havāmahe yo gojiddhanajidaśvajidyaḥ |
(5,3.11 ) imaṃ no yajñaṃ vihave śṛṇotvasmākamabhūrharyaśva medī ||11||



(5,4.1 ) yo giriṣvajāyathā vīrudhāṃ balavattamaḥ |
(5,4.1 ) kuṣṭhehi takmanāśana takmānaṃ nāśayann itaḥ ||1||

(5,4.2 ) suparṇasuvane girau jātaṃ himavataspari |
(5,4.2 ) dhanairabhi śrutvā yanti vidurhi takmanāśanam ||2||

(5,4.3 ) aśvattho devasadanastṛtīyasyāmito divi |
(5,4.3 ) tatrāmṛtasya cakṣaṇaṃ devāḥ kuṣṭhamavanvata ||3||

(5,4.4 ) hiraṇyayī nauracaraddhiraṇyabandhanā divi |
(5,4.4 ) tatrāmṛtasya puṣpaṃ devāḥ kuṣṭhamavanvata ||4||

(5,4.5 ) hiraṇyayāḥ panthāna āsann aritrāṇi hiraṇyayā |
(5,4.5 ) nāvo hiraṇyayīrāsan yābhiḥ kuṣṭhaṃ nirāvahan ||5||

(5,4.6 ) imaṃ me kuṣṭha pūruṣaṃ tamā vaha taṃ niṣkuru |
(5,4.6 ) tamu me agadaṃ kṛdhi ||6||

(5,4.7 ) devebhyo adhi jāto'si somasyāsi sakhā hitaḥ |
(5,4.7 ) sa prāṇāya vyānāya cakṣuṣe me asmai mṛḍa ||7||

(5,4.8 ) udaṅjāto himavataḥ sa prācyāṃ nīyase janam |
(5,4.8 ) tatra kuṣṭhasya nāmānyuttamāni vi bhejire ||8||

(5,4.9 ) uttamo nāma kuṣṭhasyuttamo nāma te pitā |
(5,4.9 ) yakṣmaṃ ca sarvaṃ nāśaya takmānaṃ cārasaṃ kṛdhi ||9||

(5,4.10 ) śīrṣāmayamupahatyāmakṣyostanvo rapaḥ |
(5,4.10 ) kuṣṭhastatsarvaṃ niṣkaraddaivaṃ samaha vṛṣṇyam ||10||



(5,5.1 ) rātrī mātā nabhaḥ pitāryamā te pitāmahaḥ |
(5,5.1 ) silācī nāma vā asi sā devānāmasi svasā ||1||

(5,5.2 ) yastvā pibati jīvati trāyase puruṣaṃ tvam |
(5,5.2 ) bhartrī hi śaśvatāmasi janānāṃ ca nyañcanī ||2||

(5,5.3 ) vṛkṣaṃvṛkṣamā rohasi vṛṣaṇyantīva kanyalā |
(5,5.3 ) jayantī pratyātiṣṭhantī sparaṇī nāma vā asi ||3||

(5,5.4 ) yaddaṇḍena yadiṣvā yadvārurharasā kṛtam |
(5,5.4 ) tasya tvamasi niṣkṛtiḥ semaṃ niṣkṛdhi pūruṣam ||4||

(5,5.5 ) bhadrātplakṣān nistiṣṭhasyaśvatthātkhadirāddhavāt|
(5,5.5 ) bhadrān nyagrodhātparṇātsā na ehyarundhati ||5||

(5,5.6 ) hiraṇyavarṇe subhage sūryavarṇe vapuṣṭame |
(5,5.6 ) rutaṃ gachāsi niṣkṛte niṣkṛtirnāma vā asi ||6||

(5,5.7 ) hiraṇyavarṇe subhage śuṣme lomaśavakṣane |
(5,5.7 ) apāmasi svasā lākṣe vāto hātmā babhūva te ||7||

(5,5.8 ) silācī nāma kānīno'jababhru pitā tava |
(5,5.8 ) aśvo yamasya yaḥ śyāvastasya hāsnāsyukṣitā ||8||

(5,5.9 ) aśvasyāsnaḥ saṃpatitā sā vṛkṣāmabhi siṣyade |
(5,5.9 ) sarā patatriṇī bhūtvā sā na ehyarundhati ||9||



(5,6.1 ) brahma jajñānaṃ prathamaṃ purastādvi sīmataḥ suruco vena āvaḥ |
(5,6.1 ) sa budhnyā upamā asya viṣṭhāḥ sataśca yonimasataśca vi vaḥ ||1||

(5,6.2 ) anāptā ye vaḥ prathamā yāni karmāṇi cakrire |
(5,6.2 ) vīrān no atra mā dabhan tadva etatpuro dadhe ||2||

(5,6.3 ) sahasradhāra eva te samasvaran divo nāke madhujihvā asaścataḥ |
(5,6.3 ) tasya spaśo na ni miṣanti bhūrṇayaḥ padepade pāśinaḥ santi setave ||3||

(5,6.4 ) paryū ṣu pra dhanvā vājasātaye pari vṛtrāṇi sakṣaṇiḥ |
(5,6.4 ) dviṣastadadhyarṇaveneyase sanisraso nāmāsi trayodaśo māsa indrasya gṛhaḥ ||4||

(5,6.5 ) nvetenārātsīrasau svāhā |
(5,6.5 ) tigmāyudhau tigmahetī suśevau somārudrāviha su mṛḍataṃ naḥ ||5||

(5,6.6 ) avaitenārātsīrasau svāhā |
(5,6.6 ) tigmāyudhau tigmahetī suśevau somārudrāviha su mṛḍataṃ naḥ ||6||

(5,6.7 ) apaitenārātsīrasau svāhā |
(5,6.7 ) tigmāyudhau tigmahetī suśevau somārudrāviha su mṛḍataṃ naḥ ||7||

(5,6.8 ) mumuktamasmān duritādavadyājjuṣethāṃ yajñamamṛtamasmāsu dhattam ||8||

(5,6.9 ) cakṣuṣo hete manaso hete brahmaṇo hete tapasaśca hete |
(5,6.9 ) menyā menirasyamenayaste santu ye'smāmabhyaghāyanti ||9||

(5,6.10 ) yo'smāṃścakṣuṣā manasā cittyākūtyā ca yo aghāyurabhidāsāt|
(5,6.10 ) tvaṃ tān agne menyāmenīn kṛṇu svāhā ||10||
(5,6.11 ) indrasya gṛho'si |
(5,6.11 ) taṃ tvā pra padye taṃ tvā pra viśāmi sarvaguḥ sarvapūruṣaḥ sarvātmā sarvatanūḥ saha yan me'sti tena ||11||

(5,6.12 ) indrasya śarmāsi |
(5,6.12 ) taṃ tvā pra padye taṃ tvā pra viśāmi sarvaguḥ sarvapūruṣaḥ sarvātmā sarvatanūḥ saha yan me'sti tena ||12||

(5,6.13 ) indrasya varmāsi |
(5,6.13 ) taṃ tvā pra padye taṃ tvā pra viśāmi sarvaguḥ sarvapūruṣaḥ sarvātmā sarvatanūḥ saha yan me'sti tena ||13||

(5,6.14 ) indrasya varūthamasi |
(5,6.14 ) taṃ tvā pra padye taṃ tvā pra viśāmi sarvaguḥ sarvapūruṣaḥ sarvātmā sarvatanūḥ saha yan me'sti tena ||14||



(5,7.1 ) ā no bhara mā pari ṣṭhā arāte mā no rakṣīrdakṣiṇāṃ nīyamānām |
(5,7.1 ) namo vīrtsāyā asamṛddhaye namo astvarātaye ||1||

(5,7.2 ) yamarāte purodhatse puruṣaṃ parirāpiṇam |
(5,7.2 ) namaste tasmai kṛṇmo mā vaniṃ vyathayīrmama ||2||

(5,7.3 ) pra ṇo vanirdevakṛtā divā naktaṃ ca kalpatām |
(5,7.3 ) arātimanupremo vayaṃ namo astvarātaye ||3||

(5,7.4 ) sarasvatīmanumatiṃ bhagaṃ yanto havāmahe |
(5,7.4 ) vācaṃ juṣṭāṃ madhumatīmavādiṣaṃ devānāṃ devahūtiṣu ||4||

(5,7.5 ) yaṃ yācāmyahaṃ vācā sarasvatyā manoyujā |
(5,7.5 ) śraddhā tamadya vindatu dattā somena babhruṇā ||5||

(5,7.6 ) mā vaniṃ mā vācaṃ no vīrtsīrubhāvindrāgnī ā bharatāṃ no vasūni |
(5,7.6 ) sarve no adya ditsanto'rātiṃ prati haryata ||6||

(5,7.7 ) paro'pehyasamṛddhe vi te hetiṃ nayāmasi |
(5,7.7 ) veda tvāhaṃ nimīvantīṃ nitudantīmarāte ||7||

(5,7.8 ) uta nagnā bobhuvatī svapnayā sacase janam |
(5,7.8 ) arāte cittaṃ vīrtsantyākūtiṃ puruṣasya ca ||8||

(5,7.9 ) yā mahatī mahonmānā viśvā āśā vyānaśe |
(5,7.9 ) tasyai hiraṇyakeśyai nirṛtyā akaraṃ namaḥ ||9||

(5,7.10 ) hiraṇyavarṇā subhagā hiraṇyakaśipurmahī |
(5,7.10 ) tasyai hiraṇyadrāpaye'rātyā akaraṃ namaḥ ||10||



(5,8.1 ) vaikaṅkatenedhmena devebhya ājyaṃ vaha |
(5,8.1 ) agne tāmiha mādaya sarva ā yantu me havam ||1||

(5,8.2 ) indrā yāhi me havamidaṃ kariṣyāmi tacchṛṇu |
(5,8.2 ) ima aindrā atisarā ākūtiṃ saṃ namantu me |
(5,8.2 ) tebhiḥ śakema vīryaṃ jātavedastanūvaśin ||2||

(5,8.3 ) yadasāvamuto devā adevaḥ saṃścikīrṣati |
(5,8.3 ) mā tasyāgnirhavyaṃ vākṣīddhavaṃ devā asya mopa gurmamaiva havametana ||3||

(5,8.4 ) ati dhāvatātisarā indrasya vacasā hata |
(5,8.4 ) aviṃ vṛka iva mathnīta sa vo jīvan mā moci prāṇamasyāpi nahyata ||4||

(5,8.5 ) yamamī purodadhire brahmāṇamapabhūtaye |
(5,8.5 ) indra sa te adhaspadaṃ taṃ pratyasyāmi mṛtyave ||5||

(5,8.6 ) yadi preyurdevapurā brahma varmāṇi cakrire |
(5,8.6 ) tanūpānaṃ paripāṇaṃ kṛṇvānā yadupocire sarvaṃ tadarasaṃ kṛdhi ||6||

(5,8.7 ) yān asāvatisarāṃścakāra kṛṇavacca yān |
(5,8.7 ) tvaṃ tān indra vṛtrahan pratīcaḥ punarā kṛdhi yathāmuṃ tṛṇahāṃ janam ||7||

(5,8.8 ) yathendra udvācanaṃ labdhvā cakre adhaspadam |
(5,8.8 ) kṛṇve'hamadharān tathā amūñchaśvatībhyaḥ samābhyaḥ ||8||

(5,8.9 ) atrainān indra vṛtrahann ugro marmaṇi vidhya |
(5,8.9 ) atraivainān abhi tiṣṭhendra medyahaṃ tava |
(5,8.9 ) anu tvendrā rabhāmahe syāma sumatau tava ||9||



(5,9.1 ) dive svāhā ||1||

(5,9.2 ) pṛthivyai svāhā ||2||

(5,9.3 ) antarikṣāya svāhā ||3||

(5,9.4 ) antarikṣāya svāhā ||4||

(5,9.5 ) dive svāhā ||5||

(5,9.6 ) pṛthivyai svāhā ||6||

(5,9.7 ) sūryo me cakṣurvātaḥ prāṇo'ntarikṣamātmā pṛthivī śarīram |
(5,9.7 ) astṛto nāmāhamayamasmi sa ātmānaṃ ni dadhe dyāvāpṛthivībhyāṃ gopīthāya ||7||

(5,9.8 ) udāyurudbalamutkṛtamutkṛtyāmun manīṣāmudindriyam |
(5,9.8 ) āyuṣkṛdāyuṣpatnī svadhāvantau gopā me staṃ gopāyataṃ mā |
(5,9.8 ) ātmasadau me staṃ mā mā hiṃsiṣṭam ||8||



(5,10.1 ) aśmavarma me'si yo mā prācyā diśo'ghāyurabhidāsāt|
(5,10.1 ) etatsa ṛchāt||1||

(5,10.2 ) aśmavarma me'si yo mā dakṣiṇāyā diśo'ghāyurabhidāsāt|
(5,10.2 ) etatsa ṛchāt||2||

(5,10.3 ) aśmavarma me'si yo mā pratīcyā diśo'ghāyurabhidāsāt|
(5,10.3 ) etatsa ṛchāt||3||

(5,10.4 ) aśmavarma me'si yo modīcyā diśo'ghāyurabhidāsāt|
(5,10.4 ) etatsa ṛchāt||4||

(5,10.5 ) aśmavarma me'si yo mā dhruvāyā diśo'ghāyurabhidāsāt|
(5,10.5 ) etatsa ṛchāt||5||

(5,10.6 ) aśmavarma me'si yo mordhvāyā diśo'ghāyurabhidāsāt|
(5,10.6 ) etatsa ṛchāt||6||

(5,10.7 ) aśmavarma me'si yo mā diśāmantardeśebhyo'ghāyurabhidāsāt|
(5,10.7 ) etatsa ṛchāt||7||

(5,10.8 ) bṛhatā mana upa hvaye mātariśvanā prāṇāpānau |
(5,10.8 ) sūryāccakṣurantarikṣācchrotraṃ pṛthivyāḥ śarīram |
(5,10.8 ) sarasvatyā vācamupa hvayāmahe manoyujā ||8||



(5,11.1 ) kathaṃ mahe asurāyābravīriha kathaṃ pitre haraye tveṣanṛmṇaḥ |
(5,11.1 ) pṛśniṃ varuṇa dakṣiṇāṃ dadāvān punarmagha tvaṃ manasācikitsīḥ ||1||

(5,11.2 ) na kāmena punarmagho bhavāmi saṃ cakṣe kaṃ pṛśnimetāmupāje |
(5,11.2 ) kena nu tvamatharvan kāvyena kena jātenāsi jātavedāḥ ||2||

(5,11.3 ) satyamahaṃ gabhīraḥ kāvyena satyaṃ jātenāsmi jātavedāḥ |
(5,11.3 ) na me dāso nāryo mahitvā vrataṃ mīmāya yadahaṃ dhariṣye ||3||

(5,11.4 ) na tvadanyaḥ kavitaro na medhayā dhīrataro varuṇa svadhāvan |
(5,11.4 ) tvaṃ tā viśvā bhuvanāni vettha sa cin nu tvajjano māyī bibhāya ||4||

(5,11.5 ) tvaṃ hyaṅga varuṇa svadhāvan viśvā vettha janima supraṇīte |
(5,11.5 ) kiṃ rajasa enā paro anyadastyenā kiṃ pareṇāvaramamura ||5||

(5,11.6 ) ekaṃ rajasa enā paro anyadastyenā para ekena durṇaśaṃ cidarvāk|
(5,11.6 ) tatte vidvān varuṇa pra bravīmyadhovacasaḥ paṇayo bhavantu nīcairdāsā upa sarpantu bhūmim ||6||

(5,11.7 ) tvaṃ hyaṅga varuṇa bravīṣi punarmagheṣvavadyāni bhūri |
(5,11.7 ) mo ṣu paṇīṃrabhyetāvato bhūn mā tvā vocann arādhasaṃ janāsaḥ ||7||

(5,11.8 ) mā mā vocann arādhasaṃ janāsaḥ punaste pṛśniṃ jaritardadāmi |
(5,11.8 ) stotraṃ me viśvamā yāhi śacībhirantarviśvāsu mānuṣīṣu dikṣu ||8||

(5,11.9 ) ā te stotrāṇyudyatāni yantvantarviśvāsu mānuṣīṣu dikṣu |
(5,11.9 ) dehi nu me yan me adatto asi yujyo me saptapadaḥ sakhāsi ||9||

(5,11.10 ) samā nau bandhurvaruṇa samā jā vedāhaṃ tadyan nāveṣā samā jā |
(5,11.10 ) dadāmi tadyatte adatto asmi yujyaste saptapadaḥ sakhāsmi ||10||

(5,11.11 ) devo devāya gṛṇate vayodhā vipro viprāya stuvate sumedhāḥ |
(5,11.11 ) ajījano hi varuṇa svadhāvann atharvāṇaṃ pitaraṃ devabandhum |
(5,11.11 ) tasmā u rādhaḥ kṛṇuhi supraśastaṃ sakhā no asi paramaṃ ca bandhuḥ ||11||



(5,12.1 ) samiddho adya manuṣo duroṇe devo devān yajasi jātavedaḥ |
(5,12.1 ) ā ca vaha mitramahaścikitvān tvaṃ dūtaḥ kavirasi pracetāḥ ||1||

(5,12.2 ) tanūnapātpatha ṛtasya yānān madhvā samañjantsvadayā sujihva |
(5,12.2 ) manmāni dhībhiruta yajñamṛndhan devatrā ca kṛṇuhyadhvaraṃ naḥ ||2||

(5,12.3 ) ājuhvāna īḍyo bandyaścā yāhyagne vasubhiḥ sajoṣāḥ |
(5,12.3 ) tvaṃ devānāmasi yahva hotā sa enān yakṣīṣito yajīyān ||3||

(5,12.4 ) prācīnaṃ barhiḥ pradiśā pṛthivyā vastorasyā vṛjyate agre ahnām |
(5,12.4 ) vyu prathate vitaraṃ varīyo devebhyo aditaye syonam ||4||

(5,12.5 ) vyacasvatīrurviyā vi śrayantāṃ patibhyo na janayaḥ śumbhamānāḥ |
(5,12.5 ) devīrdvāro bṛhatīrviśvaminvā devebhyo bhavata suprāyaṇāḥ ||5||

(5,12.6 ) ā suṣvayantī yajate upāke uṣāsānaktā sadatāṃ ni yonau |
(5,12.6 ) divye yoṣaṇe bṛhatī surukme adhi śriyaṃ śukrapiśaṃ dadhāne ||6||

(5,12.7 ) daivyā hotārā prathamā suvācā mimānā yajñaṃ manuṣo yajadhyai |
(5,12.7 ) pracodayantā vidatheṣu kārū prācīnaṃ jyotiḥ pradiśā diśantā ||7||

(5,12.8 ) ā no yajñaṃ bhāratī tūyametviḍā manuṣvadiha cetayantī |
(5,12.8 ) tisro devīrbarhiredaṃ syonaṃ sarasvatīḥ svapasaḥ sadantām ||8||

(5,12.9 ) ya ime dyāvāpṛthivī janitrī rūpairapiṃśadbhuvanāni viśvā |
(5,12.9 ) tamadya hotariṣito yajīyān devaṃ tvaṣṭāramiha yakṣi vidvān ||9||

(5,12.10 ) upāvasṛja tmanyā samañjan devānāṃ pātha ṛtuthā havīṃṣi |
(5,12.10 ) vanaspatiḥ śamitā devo agniḥ svadantu havyaṃ madhunā ghṛtena ||10||

(5,12.11 ) sadyo jāto vyamimīta yajñamagnirdevānāmabhavatpurogāḥ |
(5,12.11 ) asya hotuḥ praśiṣyṛtasya vāci svāhākṛtaṃ haviradantu devāḥ ||11||


(5,13.1 ) dadirhi mahyaṃ varuṇo divaḥ kavirvacobhirugrairni riṇāmi te viṣam |
(5,13.1 ) khātamakhātamuta saktamagrabhamireva dhanvan ni jajāsa te viṣam ||1||

(5,13.2 ) yatte apodakaṃ viṣaṃ tatta etāsvagrabham |
(5,13.2 ) gṛhṇāmi te madhyamamuttamaṃ rasamutāvamaṃ bhiyasā neśadādu te ||2||

(5,13.3 ) vṛṣā me ravo nabhasā na tanyaturugreṇa te vacasā bādha ādu te |
(5,13.3 ) ahaṃ tamasya nṛbhiragrabhaṃ rasaṃ tamasa iva jyotirudetu sūryaḥ ||3||

(5,13.4 ) cakṣuṣā te cakṣurhanmi viṣeṇa hanmi te viṣam |
(5,13.4 ) ahe mriyasva mā jīvīḥ pratyagabhyetu tvā viṣam ||4||
(5,13.5 ) kairāta pṛśna upatṛṇya babhra ā me śṛṇutāsitā alīkāḥ |
(5,13.5 ) mā me sakhyuḥ stāmānamapi ṣṭhātāśrāvayanto ni viṣe ramadhvam ||5||

(5,13.6 ) asitasya taimātasya babhrorapodakasya ca |
(5,13.6 ) sātrāsāhasyāhaṃ manyorava jyāmiva dhanvano vi muñcāmi rathāmiva ||6||

(5,13.7 ) āligī ca viligī ca pitā ca matā ca |
(5,13.7 ) vidma vaḥ sarvato bandhvarasāḥ kiṃ kariṣyatha ||7||

(5,13.8 ) urugūlāyā duhitā jātā dāsyasiknyā |
(5,13.8 ) prataṅkaṃ dadruṣīṇāṃ sarvāsāmarasaṃ viṣam ||8||
(5,13.9 ) karṇā śvāvittadabravīdgireravacarantikā |
(5,13.9 ) yāḥ kāścemāḥ khanitrimāstāsāmarasatamaṃ viṣam ||9||

(5,13.10 ) tābuvaṃ na tābuvaṃ na ghettvamasi tābuvam |
(5,13.10 ) tābuvenārasaṃ viṣam ||10||

(5,13.11 ) tastuvaṃ na tastuvaṃ na ghettvamasi tastuvam |
(5,13.11 ) tastuvenārasaṃ viṣam ||11||



(5,14.1 ) suparṇastvānvavindatsūkarastvākhanan nasā |
(5,14.1 ) dipsauṣadhe tvaṃ dipsantamava kṛtyākṛtaṃ jahi ||1||

(5,14.2 ) ava jahi yātudhānān ava kṛtyākṛtaṃ jahi |
(5,14.2 ) atho yo asmān dipsati tamu tvaṃ jahyoṣadhe ||2||

(5,14.3 ) riśyasyeva parīśāsaṃ parikṛtya pari tvacaḥ |
(5,14.3 ) kṛtyāṃ kṛtyākṛte devā niṣkamiva prati muñcata ||3||

(5,14.4 ) punaḥ kṛtyāṃ kṛtyākṛte hastagṛhya parā ṇaya |
(5,14.4 ) samakṣamasmā ā dhehi yathā kṛtyākṛtaṃ hanat||4||

(5,14.5 ) kṛtyāḥ santu kṛtyākṛte śapathaḥ śapathīyate |
(5,14.5 ) sukho ratha iva vartatāṃ kṛtyā kṛtyākṛtaṃ punaḥ ||5||

(5,14.6 ) yadi strī yadi vā pumān kṛtyāṃ cakāra pāpmane |
(5,14.6 ) tāmu tasmai nayāmasyaśvamivāśvābhidhānyā ||6||

(5,14.7 ) yadi vāsi devakṛtā yadi vā puruṣaiḥ kṛtā |
(5,14.7 ) tāṃ tvā punarṇayāmasīndreṇa sayujā vayam ||7||
(5,14.8 ) agne pṛtanāṣāṭpṛtanāḥ sahasva |
(5,14.8 ) punaḥ kṛtyāṃ kṛtyākṛte pratiharaṇena harāmasi ||8||

(5,14.9 ) kṛtavyadhani vidhya taṃ yaścakāra tamijjahi |
(5,14.9 ) na tvāmacakruṣe vayaṃ vadhāya saṃ śiśīmahi ||9||

(5,14.10 ) putra iva pitaraṃ gacha svaja ivābhiṣṭhito daśa |
(5,14.10 ) bandhamivāvakrāmī gacha kṛtye kṛtyākṛtaṃ punaḥ ||10||

(5,14.11 ) udeṇīva vāraṇyabhiskandaṃ mṛgīva |
(5,14.11 ) kṛtyā kartāramṛchatu ||11||

(5,14.12 ) iṣvā ṛjīyaḥ patatu dyāvāpṛthivī taṃ prati |
(5,14.12 ) sā taṃ mṛgamiva gṛhṇātu kṛtyā kṛtyākṛtaṃ punaḥ ||12||

(5,14.13 ) agnirivaitu pratikūlamanukūlamivodakam |
(5,14.13 ) sukho ratha iva vartatāṃ kṛtyā kṛtyākṛtaṃ punaḥ ||13||


(5,15.1 ) ekā ca me daśa ca me'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5,15.1 ) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ||1||
(5,15.2 ) dve ca me viṃśatiśca me'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5,15.2 ) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ||2||

(5,15.3 ) tisraśca me triṃśacca me'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5,15.3 ) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ||3||

(5,15.4 ) catasraśca me catvāriṃśacca me'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5,15.4 ) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ||4||

(5,15.5 ) pañca ca me pañcāśacca me'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5,15.5 ) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ||5||

(5,15.6 ) ṣaṭca me ṣaṣṭiśca me'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5,15.6 ) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ||6||

(5,15.7 ) sapta ca me saptatiśca me'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5,15.7 ) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ||7||

(5,15.8 ) aṣṭa ca me'śītiśca me'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5,15.8 ) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ||8||

(5,15.9 ) nava ca me navatiśca me'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5,15.9 ) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ||9||

(5,15.10 ) daśa ca me śataṃ ca me'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5,15.10 ) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ||10||

(5,15.11 ) śataṃ ca me sahasraṃ cāpavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5,15.11 ) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ||11||



(5,16.1 ) yadyekavṛṣo'si sṛjāraso'si ||1||

(5,16.2 ) yadi dvivṛṣo'si sṛjāraso'si ||2||

(5,16.3 ) yadi trivṛso'si sṛjāraso'si ||3||

(5,16.4 ) yadi caturvṛṣo'si sṛjāraso'si ||4||

(5,16.5 ) yadi pañcavṛṣo'si sṛjāraso'si ||5||

(5,16.6 ) yadi ṣaḍvṛṣo'si sṛjāraso'si ||6||

(5,16.7 ) yadi saptavṛṣo'si sṛjāraso'si ||7||

(5,16.8 ) yadyaṣṭavṛṣo'si sṛjāraso'si ||8||

(5,16.9 ) yadi navavṛṣo'si sṛjāraso'si ||9||

(5,16.10 ) yadi daśavṛṣo'si sṛjāraso'si ||10||

(5,16.11 ) yadyekādaśo'si so'podako'si ||11||



(5,17.1 ) te'vadan prathamā brahmakilbiṣe'kūpāraḥ salilo mātariśvā |
(5,17.1 ) vīḍuharāstapa ugraṃ mayobhūrāpo devīḥ prathamajā ṛtasya ||1||

(5,17.2 ) somo rājā prathamo brahmajāyāṃ punaḥ prāyachadahṛṇīyamānaḥ |
(5,17.2 ) anvartitā varuṇo mitra āsīdagnirhotā hastagṛhyā nināya ||2||

(5,17.3 ) hastenaiva grāhya ādhirasyā brahmajāyeti cedavocat|
(5,17.3 ) na dūtāya praheyā tastha eṣā tathā rāṣṭraṃ gupitaṃ kṣatriyasya ||3||

(5,17.4 ) yāmāhustārakaiṣā vikeśīti duchunāṃ grāmamavapadyamānām |
(5,17.4 ) sā brahmajāyā vi dunoti rāṣṭraṃ yatra prāpādi śaśa ulkuṣīmān ||4||

(5,17.5 ) brahmacārī carati veviṣadviṣaḥ sa devānāṃ bhavatyekamaṅgam |
(5,17.5 ) tena jāyāmanvavindadbṛhaspatiḥ somena nītāṃ juhvaṃ na devāḥ ||5||

(5,17.6 ) devā vā etasyāmavadanta pūrve saptaṛṣayastapasā ye niṣeduḥ |
(5,17.6 ) bhīmā jāyā brāhmaṇasyāpanītā durdhāṃ dadhāti parame vyoman ||6||

(5,17.7 ) ye garbhā avapadyante jagadyaccāpalupyate |
(5,17.7 ) vīrā ye tṛhyante mitho brahmajāyā hinasti tān ||7||

(5,17.8 ) uta yatpatayo daśa striyāḥ pūrve abrāhmaṇāḥ |
(5,17.8 ) brahmā ceddhastamagrahītsa eva patirekadhā ||8||

(5,17.9 ) brāhmaṇa eva patirna rājanyo na vaiśyaḥ |
(5,17.9 ) tatsūryaḥ prabruvann eti pañcabhyo mānavebhyaḥ ||9||

(5,17.10 ) punarvai devā adaduḥ punarmanuṣyā adaduḥ |
(5,17.10 ) rājānaḥ satyaṃ gṛhṇānā brahmajāyāṃ punardaduḥ ||10||

(5,17.11 ) punardāya brahmajāyāṃ kṛtvā devairnikilbiṣam |
(5,17.11 ) ūrjaṃ pṛthivyā bhaktvorugāyamupāsate ||11||

(5,17.12 ) nāsya jāyā śatavāhī kalyāṇī talpamā śaye |
(5,17.12 ) yasmin rāṣṭre nirudhyate brahmajāyācittyā ||12||

(5,17.13 ) na vikarṇaḥ pṛthuśirāstasmin veśmani jāyate |
(5,17.13 ) yasmin rāṣṭre nirudhyate brahmajāyācittyā ||13||

(5,17.14 ) nāsya kṣattā niṣkagrīvaḥ sūnānāmetyagrataḥ |
(5,17.14 ) yasmin rāṣṭre nirudhyate brahmajāyācittyā ||14||

(5,17.15 ) nāsya śvetaḥ kṛṣṇakarṇo dhuri yukto mahīyate |
(5,17.15 ) yasmin rāṣṭre nirudhyate brahmajāyācittyā ||15||

(5,17.16 ) nāsya kṣetre puṣkariṇī nāṇḍīkaṃ jāyate bisam |
(5,17.16 ) yasmin rāṣṭre nirudhyate brahmajāyācittyā ||16||

(5,17.17 ) nāsmai pṛśniṃ vi duhanti ye'syā dohamupāsate |
(5,17.17 ) yasmin rāṣṭre nirudhyate brahmajāyācittyā ||17||

(5,17.18 ) nāsya dhenuḥ kalyāṇī nānaḍvāntsahate dhuram |
(5,17.18 ) vijāniryatra brahmaṇo rātriṃ vasati pāpayā ||18||



(5,18.1 ) naitāṃ te devā adadustubhyaṃ nṛpate attave |
(5,18.1 ) mā brāhmaṇasya rājanya gāṃ jighatso anādyām ||1||

(5,18.2 ) akṣadrugdho rājanyaḥ pāpa ātmaparājitaḥ |
(5,18.2 ) sa brāhmaṇasya gāmadyādadya jīvāni mā śvaḥ ||2||

(5,18.3 ) āviṣṭitāghaviṣā pṛdākūriva carmaṇā |
(5,18.3 ) sā brāhmaṇasya rājanya tṛṣṭaiṣā gauranādyā ||3||

(5,18.4 ) nirvai kṣatraṃ nayati hanti varco'gnirivārabdho vi dunoti sarvam |
(5,18.4 ) yo brāhmaṇaṃ manyate annameva sa viṣasya pibati taimātasya ||4||

(5,18.5 ) ya enaṃ hanti mṛduṃ manyamāno devapīyurdhanakāmo na cittāt|
(5,18.5 ) saṃ tasyendro hṛdaye'gnimindhe ubhe enaṃ dviṣṭo nabhasī carantam ||5||

(5,18.6 ) na brāhmaṇo hiṃsitavyo'gniḥ priyatanoriva |
(5,18.6 ) somo hyasya dāyāda indro asyābhiśastipāḥ ||6||

(5,18.7 ) śatāpāṣṭhāṃ ni girati tāṃ na śaknoti niḥkhidam |
(5,18.7 ) annaṃ yo brahmaṇāṃ malvaḥ svādvadmīti manyate ||7||

(5,18.8 ) jihvā jyā bhavati kulmalaṃ vāṅnāḍīkā dantāstapasābhidigdhāḥ |
(5,18.8 ) tebhirbrahmā vidhyati devapīyūn hṛdbalairdhanurbhirdevajūtaiḥ ||8||

(5,18.9 ) tīkṣṇeṣavo brāhmaṇā hetimanto yāmasyanti śaravyāṃ na sā mṛṣā |
(5,18.9 ) anuhāya tapasā manyunā cota durādava bhindantyenam ||9||

(5,18.10 ) ye sahasramarājann āsan daśaśatā uta |
(5,18.10 ) te brāhmaṇasya gāṃ jagdhvā vaitahavyāḥ parābhavan ||10||

(5,18.11 ) gaureva tān hanyamānā vaitahavyāmavātirat|
(5,18.11 ) ye kesaraprābandhāyāścaramājāmapeciran ||11||

(5,18.12 ) ekaśataṃ tā janatā yā bhūmirvyadhūnuta |
(5,18.12 ) prajāṃ hiṃsitvā brāhmaṇīmasaṃbhavyaṃ parābhavan ||12||

(5,18.13 ) devapīyuścarati martyeṣu garagīrṇo bhavatyasthibhūyān |
(5,18.13 ) yo brāhmaṇaṃ devabandhuṃ hinasti na sa pitṛyāṇamapyeti lokam ||13||

(5,18.14 ) agnirvai naḥ padavāyaḥ somo dāyāda ucyate |
(5,18.14 ) hantābhiśastendrastathā tadvedhaso viduḥ ||14||

(5,18.15 ) iṣuriva digdhā nṛpate pṛdākūriva gopate |
(5,18.15 ) sā brāhmaṇasyeṣurghorā tayā vidhyati pīyataḥ ||15||



(5,19.1 ) atimātramavardhanta nodiva divamaspṛśan |
(5,19.1 ) bhṛguṃ hiṃsitvā sṛñjayā vaitahavyāḥ parābhavan ||1||

(5,19.2 ) ye bṛhatsāmānamāṅgirasamārpayan brāhmaṇaṃ janāḥ |
(5,19.2 ) petvasteṣāmubhayādamavistokānyāvayat||2||

(5,19.3 ) ye brāhmaṇaṃ pratyaṣṭhīvan ye vāsmiñchulkamīṣire |
(5,19.3 ) asnaste madhye kulyāyāḥ keśān khādanta āsate ||3||

(5,19.4 ) brahmagavī pacyamānā yāvatsābhi vijaṅgahe |
(5,19.4 ) tejo rāṣṭrasya nirhanti na vīro jāyate vṛṣā ||4||

(5,19.5 ) krūramasyā āśasanaṃ tṛṣṭaṃ piśitamasyate |
(5,19.5 ) kṣīraṃ yadasyāḥ pīyate tadvai pitṛṣu kilbiṣam ||5||

(5,19.6 ) ugro rājā manyamāno brāhmaṇaṃ yo jighatsati |
(5,19.6 ) parā tatsicyate rāṣṭraṃ brāhmaṇo yatra jīyate ||6||

(5,19.7 ) aṣṭāpadī caturakṣī catuḥśrotrā caturhanuḥ |
(5,19.7 ) dvyāsyā dvijihvā bhūtvā sā rāṣṭramava dhūnute brahmajyasya ||7||

(5,19.8 ) tadvai rāṣṭramā sravati nāvaṃ bhinnāmivodakam |
(5,19.8 ) brahmāṇaṃ yatra hiṃsanti tadrāṣṭraṃ hanti duchunā ||8||

(5,19.9 ) taṃ vṛkṣā apa sedhanti chāyāṃ no mopa gā iti |
(5,19.9 ) yo brāhmaṇasya saddhanamabhi nārada manyate ||9||

(5,19.10 ) viṣametaddevakṛtaṃ rājā varuṇo'bravīt|
(5,19.10 ) na brāhmaṇasya gāṃ jagdhvā rāstre jāgāra kaścana ||10||

(5,19.11 ) navaiva tā navatayo yā bhūmirvyadhūnuta |
(5,19.11 ) prajāṃ hiṃsitvā brāhmaṇīmasaṃbhavyaṃ parābhavan ||11||

(5,19.12 ) yāṃ mṛtāyānubadhnanti kūdyaṃ padayopanīm |
(5,19.12 ) tadvai brahmajya te devā upastaraṇamabruvan ||12||

(5,19.13 ) aśrūṇi kṛpamānasya yāni jītasya vāvṛtuḥ |
(5,19.13 ) taṃ vai brahmajya te devā apāṃ bhāgamadhārayan ||13||

(5,19.14 ) yena mṛtaṃ snapayanti śmaśrūṇi yenondate |
(5,19.14 ) taṃ vai brahmajya te devā apāṃ bhāgamadhārayan ||14||

(5,19.15 ) na varṣaṃ maitrāvaruṇaṃ brahmajyamabhi varṣati |
(5,19.15 ) nāsmai samitiḥ kalpate na mitraṃ nayate vaśam ||15||



(5,20.1 ) uccairghoṣo dundubhiḥ satvanāyan vānaspatyaḥ saṃbhṛta usṛiyābhiḥ |
(5,20.1 ) vācaṃ kṣuṇuvāno damayantsapatnāntsiṃha iva jeṣyann abhi taṃstanīhi ||1||

(5,20.2 ) siṃha ivāstānīddruvayo vibaddho'bhikrandann ṛṣabho vāsitāmiva |
(5,20.2 ) vṛṣā tvaṃ vadhrayaste sapatnā aindraste śuṣmo abhimātiṣāhaḥ ||2||

(5,20.3 ) vṛṣeva yūthe sahasā vidāno gavyann abhi ruva saṃdhanājit|
(5,20.3 ) śucā vidhya hṛdayaṃ pareṣāṃ hitvā grāmān pracyutā yantu śatravaḥ ||3||

(5,20.4 ) saṃjayan pṛtanā ūrdhvamāyurgṛhyā gṛhṇāno bahudhā vi cakṣva |
(5,20.4 ) daivīṃ vācaṃ dundubha ā gurasva vedhāḥ śatrūṇāmupa bharasva vedaḥ ||4||

(5,20.5 ) dundubhervācaṃ prayatāṃ vadantīmāśṛṇvatī nāthitā ghoṣabuddhā |
(5,20.5 ) nārī putraṃ dhāvatu hastagṛhyāmitrī bhītā samare vadhānām ||5||

(5,20.6 ) pūrvo dundubhe pra vadāsi vācaṃ bhūmyāḥ pṛṣṭhe vada rocamānaḥ |
(5,20.6 ) amitrasenāmabhijañjabhāno dyumadvada dundubhe sūnṛtāvat||6||

(5,20.7 ) antareme nabhasī ghoṣo astu pṛthakte dhvanayo yantu śībham |
(5,20.7 ) abhi kranda stanayotpipānaḥ ślokakṛn mitratūryāya svardhī ||7||

(5,20.8 ) dhībhiḥ kṛtaḥ pra vadāti vācamuddharṣaya satvanāmāyudhāni |
(5,20.8 ) indramedī satvano ni hvayasva mitrairamitrāmava jaṅghanīhi ||8||

(5,20.9 ) saṃkrandanaḥ pravado dhṛṣṇuṣeṇaḥ pravedakṛdbahudhā grāmaghoṣī |
(5,20.9 ) śriyo vanvano vayunāni vidvān kīrtiṃ bahubhyo vi hara dvirāje ||9||

(5,20.10 ) śreyaḥketo vasujitsahīyāntsaṃgrāmajitsaṃśito brahmaṇāsi |
(5,20.10 ) aṃśūn iva grāvādhiṣavaṇe adrirgavyan dundubhe'dhi nṛtya vedaḥ ||10||

(5,20.11 ) śatrūṣāṇnīṣādabhimātiṣāho gaveṣaṇaḥ sahamāna udbhit|
(5,20.11 ) vāgvīva mantraṃ pra bharasva vācaṃ sāṃgrāmajityāyeṣamudvadeha ||11||

(5,20.12 ) acyutacyutsamado gamiṣṭho mṛdho jetā puraetāyodhyaḥ |
(5,20.12 ) indreṇa gupto vidathā nicikyaddhṛddyotano dviṣatāṃ yāhi śībham ||12||


(5,21.1 ) vihṛdayaṃ vaimanasyaṃ vadāmitreṣu dundubhe |
(5,21.1 ) vidveṣaṃ kaśmaśaṃ bhayamamitreṣu ni dadhmasyava enān dundubhe jahi ||1||

(5,21.2 ) udvepamānā manasā cakṣuṣā hṛdayena ca |
(5,21.2 ) dhāvantu bibhyato'mitrāḥ pratrāsenājye hute ||2||

(5,21.3 ) vānaspatyaḥ saṃbhṛta usriyābhirviśvagotryaḥ |
(5,21.3 ) pratrāsamamitrebhyo vadājyenābhighāritaḥ ||3||

(5,21.4 ) yathā mṛgāḥ saṃvijanta āraṇyāḥ puruṣādadhi |
(5,21.4 ) eva tvaṃ dundubhe'mitrān abhi kranda pra trāsayātho cittāni mohaya ||4||

(5,21.5 ) yathā vṛkādajāvayo dhāvanti bahu bibhyatīḥ |
(5,21.5 ) eva tvaṃ dundubhe'mitrān abhi kranda pra trāsayātho cittāni mohaya ||5||

(5,21.6 ) yathā śyenātpatatriṇaḥ saṃvijante ahardivi siṃhasya stanathoryathā |
(5,21.6 ) eva tvaṃ dundubhe'mitrān abhi kranda pra trāsayātho cittāni mohaya ||6||

(5,21.7 ) parāmitrān dundubhinā hariṇasyājinena ca |
(5,21.7 ) sarve devā atitrasan ye saṃgrāmasyeṣate ||7||

(5,21.8 ) yairindraḥ prakrīḍate padghoṣaiśchāyayā saha |
(5,21.8 ) tairamitrāstrasantu no'mī ye yantyanīkaśaḥ ||8||
(5,21.9 ) jyāghoṣā dundubhayo'bhi krośantu yā diśaḥ |
(5,21.9 ) senāḥ parājitā yatīramitrāṇāmanīkaśaḥ ||9||

(5,21.10 ) āditya cakṣurā datsva marīcayo'nu dhāvata |
(5,21.10 ) patsaṅginīrā sajantu vigate bāhuvīrye ||10||

(5,21.11 ) yūyamugrā marutaḥ pṛśnimātara indreṇa yujā pra mṛnīta śatrūn |
(5,21.11 ) somo rājā varuṇo rājā mahādeva uta mṛtyurindraḥ ||11||

(5,21.12 ) etā devasenāḥ sūryaketavaḥ sacetasaḥ |
(5,21.12 ) amitrān no jayantu svāhā ||12||



(5,22.1 ) agnistakmānamapa bādhatāmitaḥ somo grāvā varuṇaḥ pūtadakṣāḥ |
(5,22.1 ) vedirbarhiḥ samidhaḥ śośucānā apa dveṣāṃsyamuyā bhavantu ||1||

(5,22.2 ) ayaṃ yo viśvān haritān kṛṇoṣyucchocayann agnirivābhidunvan |
(5,22.2 ) adhā hi takmann araso hi bhūyā adhā nyaṅṅadharān vā parehi ||2||

(5,22.3 ) yaḥ paruṣaḥ pāruṣeyo'vadhvaṃsa ivāruṇaḥ |
(5,22.3 ) takmānaṃ viśvadhāvīryādharāñcaṃ parā suvā ||3||

(5,22.4 ) adharāñcaṃ pra hiṇomi namaḥ kṛtvā takmane |
(5,22.4 ) śakambharasya muṣṭihā punaretu mahāvṛṣān ||4||

(5,22.5 ) oko asya mūjavanta oko asya mahāvṛṣāḥ |
(5,22.5 ) yāvajjātastakmaṃstāvān asi balhikeṣu nyocaraḥ ||5||

(5,22.6 ) takman vyāla vi gada vyaṅga bhūri yāvaya |
(5,22.6 ) dāsīṃ niṣṭakvarīmicha tāṃ vajreṇa samarpaya ||6||

(5,22.7 ) takman mūjavato gacha balhikān vā parastarām |
(5,22.7 ) śūdrāmicha prapharvyaṃ tāṃ takman vīva dhūnuhi ||7||

(5,22.8 ) mahāvṛṣān mūjavato bandhvaddhi paretya |
(5,22.8 ) praitāni takmane brūmo anyakṣetrāṇi vā imā ||8||

(5,22.9 ) anyakṣetre na ramase vaśī san mṛḍayāsi naḥ |
(5,22.9 ) abhūdu prārthastakmā sa gamiṣyati balhikān ||9||

(5,22.10 ) yattvaṃ śīto'tho rūraḥ saha kāsāvepayaḥ |
(5,22.10 ) bhīmāste takman hetayastābhiḥ sma pari vṛṅgdhi naḥ ||10||

(5,22.11 ) mā smaitāntsakhīn kuruthā balāsaṃ kāsamudyugam |
(5,22.11 ) mā smāto'rvāṅaiḥ punastattvā takmann upa bruve ||11||

(5,22.12 ) takman bhrātrā balāsena svasrā kāsikayā saha |
(5,22.12 ) pāpmā bhrātṛvyeṇa saha gachāmumaraṇaṃ janam ||12||

(5,22.13 ) tṛtīyakaṃ vitṛtīyaṃ sadandimuta śāradam |
(5,22.13 ) takmānaṃ śītaṃ rūraṃ graiṣmaṃ nāśaya vārṣikam ||13||

(5,22.14 ) gandhāribhyo mūjavadbhyo'ṅgebhyo magadhebhyaḥ |
(5,22.14 ) praiṣyan janamiva śevadhiṃ takmānaṃ pari dadmasi ||14||



(5,23.1 ) ote me dyāvāpṛthivī otā devī sarasvatī |
(5,23.1 ) otau ma indraścāgniśca krimiṃ jambhayatāmiti ||1||

(5,23.2 ) asyendra kumārasya krimīn dhanapate jahi |
(5,23.2 ) hatā viśvā arātaya ugreṇa vacasā mama ||2||

(5,23.3 ) yo akṣyau parisarpati yo nāse parisarpati |
(5,23.3 ) datāṃ yo madhyaṃ gachati taṃ krimiṃ jambhayāmasi ||3||

(5,23.4 ) sarūpau dvau virūpau dvau kṛṣṇau dvau rohitau dvau |
(5,23.4 ) babhruśca babhrukarṇaśca gṛdhraḥ kokaśca te hatāḥ ||4||

(5,23.5 ) ye krimayaḥ śitikakṣā ye kṛṣṇāḥ śitibāhavaḥ |
(5,23.5 ) ye ke ca viśvarūpāstān krimīn jambhayāmasi ||5||

(5,23.6 ) utpurastātsūrya eti viśvadṛṣṭo adṛṣṭahā |
(5,23.6 ) dṛṣṭāṃśca ghnann adṛṣṭāṃśca sarvāṃśca pramṛṇan krimīn ||6||

(5,23.7 ) yevāṣāsaḥ kaṣkaṣāsa ejatkāḥ śipavitnukāḥ |
(5,23.7 ) dṛṣṭaśca hanyatāṃ krimirutādṛṣṭaśca hanyatām ||7||

(5,23.8 ) hato yevāṣaḥ krimīṇāṃ hato nadanimota |
(5,23.8 ) sarvān ni maṣmaṣākaraṃ dṛṣadā khalvāmiva ||8||

(5,23.9 ) triśīrṣāṇaṃ trikakudaṃ krimiṃ sāraṅgamarjunam |
(5,23.9 ) śṛṇāmyasya pṛṣṭīrapi vṛścāmi yacchiraḥ ||9||

(5,23.10 ) atrivadvaḥ krimayo hanmi kaṇvavajjamadagnivat|
(5,23.10 ) agastyasya brahmaṇā saṃ pinaṣmyahaṃ krimīn ||10||

(5,23.11 ) hato rājā krimīṇāmutaiṣāṃ sthapatirhataḥ |
(5,23.11 ) hato hatamātā krimirhatabhrātā hatasvasā ||11||

(5,23.12 ) hatāso asya veśaso hatāsaḥ pariveśasaḥ |
(5,23.12 ) atho ye kṣullakā iva sarve te krimayo hatāḥ ||12||

(5,23.13 ) sarveṣāṃ ca krimīṇāṃ sarvāsāṃ ca krimīnām |
(5,23.13 ) bhinadmyaśmanā śiro dahāmyagninā mukham ||13||



(5,24.1 ) savitā prasavānāmadhipatiḥ sa māvatu |
(5,24.1 ) asmin brahmaṇyasmin karmaṇyasyāṃ purodhāyāmasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmasyām |
(5,24.1 ) cittyāmasyāmākūtyāmasyāmāśiṣyasyāṃ devahūtyāṃ svāhā ||1||

(5,24.2 ) agnirvanaspatīnāmadhipatiḥ sa māvatu |
(5,24.2 ) asmin brahmaṇyasmin karmaṇyasyāṃ purodhāyāmasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmasyām |
(5,24.2 ) cittyāmasyāmākūtyāmasyāmāśiṣyasyāṃ devahūtyāṃ svāhā ||2||

(5,24.3 ) dyāvāpṛthivī dātṝṇāmadhipatiḥ sa māvatu |
(5,24.3 ) asmin brahmaṇyasmin karmaṇyasyāṃ purodhāyāmasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmasyām |
(5,24.3 ) cittyāmasyāmākūtyāmasyāmāśiṣyasyāṃ devahūtyāṃ svāhā ||3||

(5,24.4 ) varuṇo'pāmadhipatiḥ sa māvatu |
(5,24.4 ) asmin brahmaṇyasmin karmaṇyasyāṃ purodhāyāmasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmasyām |
(5,24.4 ) cittyāmasyāmākūtyāmasyāmāśiṣyasyāṃ devahūtyāṃ svāhā ||4||

(5,24.5 ) mitrāvaruṇau vṛṣṭyādhipatī tau māvatām |
(5,24.5 ) asmin brahmaṇyasmin karmaṇyasyāṃ purodhāyāmasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmasyām |
(5,24.5 ) cittyāmasyāmākūtyāmasyāmāśiṣyasyāṃ devahūtyāṃ svāhā ||5||

(5,24.6 ) marutaḥ parvatānāmadhipatayaste māvantu |
(5,24.6 ) asmin brahmaṇyasmin karmaṇyasyāṃ purodhāyāmasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmasyām |
(5,24.6 ) cittyāmasyāmākūtyāmasyāmāśiṣyasyāṃ devahūtyāṃ svāhā ||6||

(5,24.7 ) somo vīrudhāmadhipatiḥ sa māvatu |
(5,24.7 ) asmin brahmaṇyasmin karmaṇyasyāṃ purodhāyāmasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmasyām |
(5,24.7 ) cittyāmasyāmākūtyāmasyāmāśiṣyasyāṃ devahūtyāṃ svāhā ||7||

(5,24.8 ) vāyurantarikṣasyādhipatiḥ sa māvatu |
(5,24.8 ) asmin brahmaṇyasmin karmaṇyasyāṃ purodhāyāmasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmasyām |
(5,24.8 ) cittyāmasyāmākūtyāmasyāmāśiṣyasyāṃ devahūtyāṃ svāhā ||8||

(5,24.9 ) sūryaścakṣuṣāmadhipatiḥ sa māvatu |
(5,24.9 ) asmin brahmaṇyasmin karmaṇyasyāṃ purodhāyāmasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmasyām |
(5,24.9 ) cittyāmasyāmākūtyāmasyāmāśiṣyasyāṃ devahūtyāṃ svāhā ||9||

(5,24.10 ) candramā nakṣatrāṇāmadhipatiḥ sa māvatu |
(5,24.10 ) asmin brahmaṇyasmin karmaṇyasyāṃ purodhāyāmasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmasyām |
(5,24.10 ) cittyāmasyāmākūtyāmasyāmāśiṣyasyāṃ devahūtyāṃ svāhā ||10||

(5,24.11 ) indro divo'dhipatiḥ sa māvatu |
(5,24.11 ) asmin brahmaṇyasmin karmaṇyasyāṃ purodhāyāmasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmasyām |
(5,24.11 ) cittyāmasyāmākūtyāmasyāmāśiṣyasyāṃ devahūtyāṃ svāhā ||11||

(5,24.12 ) marutāṃ pitā paśūnāmadhipatiḥ sa māvatu |
(5,24.12 ) asmin brahmaṇyasmin karmaṇyasyāṃ purodhāyāmasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmasyām |
(5,24.12 ) cittyāmasyāmākūtyāmasyāmāśiṣyasyāṃ devahūtyāṃ svāhā ||12||

(5,24.13 ) mṛtyuḥ prajānāmadhipatiḥ sa māvatu |
(5,24.13 ) asmin brahmaṇyasmin karmaṇyasyāṃ purodhāyāmasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmasyām |
(5,24.13 ) cittyāmasyāmākūtyāmasyāmāśiṣyasyāṃ devahūtyāṃ svāhā ||13||

(5,24.14 ) yamaḥ pitṝṇāmadhipatiḥ sa māvatu |
(5,24.14 ) asmin brahmaṇyasmin karmaṇyasyāṃ purodhāyāmasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmasyām |
(5,24.14 ) cittyāmasyāmākūtyāmasyāmāśiṣyasyāṃ devahūtyāṃ svāhā ||14||

(5,24.15 ) pitaraḥ pare te māvantu |
(5,24.15 ) asmin brahmaṇyasmin karmaṇyasyāṃ purodhāyāmasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmasyām |
(5,24.15 ) cittyāmasyāmākūtyāmasyāmāśiṣyasyāṃ devahūtyāṃ svāhā ||15||

(5,24.16 ) tatā avare te māvantu |
(5,24.16 ) asmin brahmaṇyasmin karmaṇyasyāṃ purodhāyāmasyāṃ pratisṭhāyāmasyām |
(5,24.16 ) cittyāmasyāmākūtyāmasyāmāśiṣyasyāṃ devahūtyāṃ svāhā ||16||

(5,24.17 ) tatastatāmahāste māvantu |
(5,24.17 ) asmin brahmaṇyasmin karmaṇyasyāṃ purodhāyāmasyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmasyām |
(5,24.17 ) cittyāmasyāmākūtyāmasyāmāśiṣyasyāṃ devahūtyāṃ svāhā ||17||



(5,25.1 ) parvatāddivo yoneraṅgādaṅgātsamābhṛtam |
(5,25.1 ) śepo garbhasya retodhāḥ sarau parṇamivā dadhat||1||

(5,25.2 ) yatheyaṃ pṛthivī mahī bhūtānāṃ garbhamādadhe |
(5,25.2 ) evā dadhāmi te garbhaṃ tasmai tvāmavase huve ||2||

(5,25.3 ) garbhaṃ dhehi sinīvāli garbhaṃ dhehi sarasvati |
(5,25.3 ) garbhaṃ te aśvinobhā dhattāṃ puṣkarasrajā ||3||

(5,25.4 ) garbhaṃ te mitrāvaruṇau garbhaṃ devo bṛhaspatiḥ |
(5,25.4 ) garbhaṃ ta indraścāgniśca garbhaṃ dhātā dadhātu te ||4||

(5,25.5 ) viṣṇuryoniṃ kalpayatu tvaṣṭā rūpāṇi piṃśatu |
(5,25.5 ) ā siñcatu prajāpatirdhātā garbhaṃ dadhātu te ||5||

(5,25.6 ) yadveda rājā varuṇo yadvā devī sarasvatī |
(5,25.6 ) yadindro vṛtrahā veda tadgarbhakaraṇaṃ piba ||6||

(5,25.7 ) garbho asyoṣadhīnāṃ garbho vanaspatīnām |
(5,25.7 ) garbho viśvasya bhūtasya so agne garbhameha dhāḥ ||7||

(5,25.8 ) adhi skanda vīrayasva garbhamā dhehi yonyām |
(5,25.8 ) vṛṣāsi vṛṣṇyāvan prajāyai tvā nayāmasi ||8||

(5,25.9 ) vi jihīṣva bārhatsāme garbhaste yonimā śayām |
(5,25.9 ) aduṣṭe devāḥ putraṃ somapā ubhayāvinam ||9||

(5,25.10 ) dhātaḥ śreṣṭhena rūpeṇāsyā nāryā gavīnyoḥ |
(5,25.10 ) pumāṃsaṃ putramā dhehi daśame māsi sūtave ||10||

(5,25.11 ) tvaṣṭaḥ śreṣṭhena rūpeṇāsyā nāryā gavīnyoḥ |
(5,25.11 ) pumāṃsaṃ putramā dhehi daśame māsi sūtave ||11||

(5,25.12 ) savitaḥ śreṣṭhena rūpeṇāsyā nāryā gavīnyoḥ |
(5,25.12 ) pumāṃsaṃ putramā dhehi daśame māsi sūtave ||12||

(5,25.13 ) prajāpate śreṣṭhena rūpeṇāsyā nāryā gavīnyoḥ |
(5,25.13 ) pumāṃsaṃ putramā dhehi daśame māsi sūtave ||13||



(5,26.1 ) yajūṃṣi yajñe samidhaḥ svāhāgniḥ pravidvān iha vo yunaktu ||1||

(5,26.2 ) yunaktu devaḥ savitā prajānann asmin yajñe mahiṣaḥ svāhā ||2||

(5,26.3 ) indra ukthāmadānyasmin yajñe pravidvān yunaktu suyujaḥ svāhā ||3||

(5,26.4 ) praiṣā yajñe nividaḥ svāhā śiṣṭāḥ patnībhirvahateha yuktāḥ ||4||

(5,26.5 ) chandāṃsi yajñe marutaḥ svāhā māteva putraṃ pipṛteha yuktāḥ ||5||

(5,26.6 ) eyamagan barhiṣā prokṣaṇībhiryajñaṃ tanvānāditiḥ svāhā ||6||

(5,26.7 ) viṣṇuryunaktu bahudhā tapāṃsyasmin yajñe suyujaḥ svāhā ||7||

(5,26.8 ) tvaṣṭā yunaktu bahudhā nu rūpā asmin yajñe yunaktu suyujaḥ svāhā ||8||

(5,26.9 ) bhago yunaktvāśiṣo nvasmā asmin yajñe pravidvān yunaktu suyujaḥ svāhā ||9||

(5,26.10 ) somo yunaktu bahudhā payāṃsyasmin yajñe suyujaḥ svāhā ||10||

(5,26.11 ) indro yunaktu bahudhā payāṃsyasmin yajñe suyujaḥ svāhā ||11||

(5,26.12 ) aśvinā brahmaṇā yātamarvāñcau vaṣaṭkāreṇa yajñaṃ vardhayantau |
(5,26.12 ) bṛhaspate brahmaṇā yāhyarvāṅyajño ayaṃ svaridaṃ yajamānāya svāhā ||12||



(5,27.1 ) ūrdhvā asya samidho bhavantyūrdhvā śukrā śocīṣyagneḥ |
(5,27.1 ) dyumattamā supratīkaḥ sasūnustanūnapādasuro bhūripāṇiḥ ||1||

(5,27.2 ) devo deveṣu devaḥ patho anakti madhvā ghṛtena ||2||

(5,27.3 ) madhvā yajñaṃ nakṣati praiṇāno narāśaṃso agniḥ sukṛddevaḥ savitā viśvavāraḥ ||3||

(5,27.4 ) achāyameti śavasā ghṛtā cidīdāno vahnirnamasā ||4||

(5,27.5 ) agniḥ sruco adhvareṣu prayakṣu sa yakṣadasya mahimānamagneḥ ||5||

(5,27.6 ) tarī mandrāsu prayakṣu vasavaścātiṣṭhan vasudhātaraśca ||6||

(5,27.7 ) dvāro devīranvasya viśve vrataṃ rakṣanti viśvahā ||7||

(5,27.8 ) uruvyacasāgnerdhāmnā patyamāne |
(5,27.8 ) ā suṣvayantī yajate upāke uṣāsānaktemaṃ yajñamavatāmadhvaraṃ naḥ ||8||
(5,27.9 ) daivā hotāra ūrdhvamadhvaraṃ no'gnerjihvayābhi gṛnata gṛnatā naḥ sviṣṭaye |
(5,27.9 ) tisro devīrbarhiredaṃ sadantāmiḍā sarasvatī mahī bhāratī gṛṇānā ||9||

(5,27.10 ) tan nasturīpamadbhutaṃ purukṣu |
(5,27.10 ) deva tvaṣṭā rāyaspoṣaṃ vi ṣya nābhimasya ||10||

(5,27.11 ) vanaspate'va sṛjā rarāṇaḥ |
(5,27.11 ) tmanā devebhyo agnirhavyaṃ śamitā svadayatu ||11||

(5,27.12 ) agne svāhā kṛṇuhi jātavedaḥ |
(5,27.12 ) indrāya yajñaṃ viśve devā haviridaṃ juṣantām ||12||


(5,28.1 ) nava prāṇān navabhiḥ saṃ mimīte dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya |
(5,28.1 ) harite trīṇi rajate trīṇyayasi trīṇi tapasāviṣṭitāni ||1||

(5,28.2 ) agniḥ sūryaścandramā bhūmirāpo dyaurantarikṣaṃ pradiśo diśaśca |
(5,28.2 ) ārtavā ṛtubhiḥ saṃvidānā anena mā trivṛtā pārayantu ||2||

(5,28.3 ) trayaḥ poṣāstrivṛti śrayantāmanaktu pūṣā payasā ghṛtena |
(5,28.3 ) annasya bhūmā puruṣasya bhūmā bhūmā paśūnāṃ ta iha śrayantām ||3||

(5,28.4 ) imamādityā vasunā samukṣatemamagne vardhaya vavṛdhānaḥ |
(5,28.4 ) imamindra saṃ sṛja vīryeṇāsmin trivṛcchrayatāṃ poṣayiṣṇu ||4||

(5,28.5 ) bhūmiṣṭvā pātu haritena viśvabhṛdagniḥ pipartvayasā sajoṣāḥ |
(5,28.5 ) vīrudbhiṣṭe arjunaṃ saṃvidānaṃ dakṣaṃ dadhātu sumanasyamānam ||5||

(5,28.6 ) tredhā jātaṃ janmanedaṃ hiraṇyamagnerekaṃ priyatamaṃ babhūva somasyaikaṃ hiṃsitasya parāpatat|
(5,28.6 ) apāmekaṃ vedhasāṃ reta āhustatte hiraṇyaṃ trivṛdastvāyuṣe ||6||

(5,28.7 ) tryāyuṣaṃ jamadagneḥ kaśyapasya tryāyuṣam |
(5,28.7 ) tredhāmṛtasya cakṣaṇaṃ trīṇyāyūṃṣi te'karam ||7||

(5,28.8 ) trayaḥ suparṇāstrivṛtā yadāyann ekākṣaramabhisaṃbhūya śakrāḥ |
(5,28.8 ) pratyauhan mṛtyumamṛtena sākamantardadhānā duritāni viśvā ||8||

(5,28.9 ) divastvā pātu haritaṃ madhyāttvā pātvarjunam |
(5,28.9 ) bhūmyā ayasmayaṃ pātu prāgāddevapurā ayam ||9||

(5,28.10 ) imāstisro devapurāstāstvā rakṣantu sarvataḥ |
(5,28.10 ) tāstvaṃ bibhradvarcasvyuttaro dviṣatāṃ bhava ||10||

(5,28.11 ) puraṃ devānāmamṛtaṃ hiraṇyaṃ ya ābedhe prathamo devo agre |
(5,28.11 ) tasmai namo daśa prācīḥ kṛṇomyanu manyatāṃ trivṛdābadhe me ||11||

(5,28.12 ) ā tvā cṛtatvaryamā pūṣā bṛhaspatiḥ |
(5,28.12 ) aharjātasya yan nāma tena tvāti cṛtāmasi ||12||

(5,28.13 ) ṛtubhiṣṭvārtavairāyuṣe varcase tvā |
(5,28.13 ) saṃvatsarasya tejasā tena saṃhanu kṛṇmasi ||13||

(5,28.14 ) ghṛtādulluptaṃ madhunā samaktaṃ bhūmidṛṃhamacyutaṃ pārayiṣṇu |
(5,28.14 ) bhindatsapatnān adharāṃśca kṛṇvadā mā roha mahate saubhagāya ||14||



(5,29.1 ) purastādyukto vaha jātavedo'gne viddhi kriyamāṇaṃ yathedam |
(5,29.1 ) tvaṃ bhiṣagbheṣajasyāsi kartā tvayā gāmaśvaṃ puruṣaṃ sanema ||1||

(5,29.2 ) tathā tadagne kṛṇu jātavedo viśvebhirdevaiḥ saha saṃvidānaḥ |
(5,29.2 ) yo no dideva yatamo jaghāsa yathā so asya paridhiṣpatāti ||2||

(5,29.3 ) yathā so asya paridhiṣpatāti tathā tadagne kṛṇu jātavedaḥ |
(5,29.3 ) viśvebhirdevairsaha saṃvidānaḥ ||3||

(5,29.4 ) akṣyau ni vidhya hṛdayaṃ ni vidhya jihvāṃ ni tṛnddhi pra dato mṛṇīhi |
(5,29.4 ) piśāco asya yatamo jaghāsāgne yaviṣṭha prati śṛṇīhi ||4||

(5,29.5 ) yadasya hṛtaṃ vihṛtaṃ yatparābhṛtamātmano jagdhaṃ yatamatpiśācaiḥ |
(5,29.5 ) tadagne vidvān punarā bhara tvaṃ śarīre māṃsamasumerayāmaḥ ||5||

(5,29.6 ) āme supakve śabale vipakve yo mā piśāco aśane dadambha |
(5,29.6 ) tadātmanā prajayā piśācā vi yātayantāmagado'yamastu ||6||

(5,29.7 ) kṣire mā manthe yatamo dadambhākṛṣṭapacye aśane dhānye yaḥ |
(5,29.7 ) tadātmanā prajayā piśācā vi yātayantāmagado'yamastu ||7||

(5,29.8 ) apāṃ mā pāne yatamo dadambha kravyādyātūnāṃ śayane śayānam |
(5,29.8 ) tadātmanā prajayā piśācā vi yātayantāmagado'yamastu ||8||

(5,29.9 ) divā mā naktaṃ yatamo dadambha kravyādyātūnāṃ śayane śayānam |
(5,29.9 ) tadātmanā prajayā piśācā vi yātayantāmagado'yamastu ||9||

(5,29.10 ) kravyādamagne rudhiraṃ piśācaṃ manohanaṃ jahi jātavedaḥ |
(5,29.10 ) tamindro vājī vajreṇa hantu chinattu somaḥ śiro asya dhṛṣṇuḥ ||10||

(5,29.11 ) sanādagne mṛṇasi yātudhānān na tvā rakṣāṃsi pṛtanāsu jigyuḥ |
(5,29.11 ) sahamūrān anu daha kravyādo mā te hetyā mukṣata daivyāyāḥ ||11||

(5,29.12 ) samāhara jātavedo yaddhṛtaṃ yatparābhṛtam |
(5,29.12 ) gātrāṇyasya vardhantāmaṃśurivā pyāyatāmayam ||12||

(5,29.13 ) somasyeva jātavedo aṃśurā pyāyatāmayam |
(5,29.13 ) agne virapśinaṃ medhyamayakṣmaṃ kṛṇu jīvatu ||13||

(5,29.14 ) etāste agne samidhaḥ piśācajambhanīḥ |
(5,29.14 ) tāstvaṃ juṣasva prati cainā gṛhāṇa jātavedaḥ ||14||

(5,29.15 ) tārṣṭāghīragne samidhaḥ prati gṛhṇāhyarciṣā |
(5,29.15 ) jahātu kravyādrūpaṃ yo asya māṃsaṃ jihīrṣati ||15||



(5,30.1 ) āvatasta āvataḥ parāvatasta āvataḥ |
(5,30.1 ) ihaiva bhava mā nu gā mā pūrvān anu gāḥ pitṝn asuṃ badhnāmi te dṛḍham ||1||

(5,30.2 ) yattvābhiceruḥ puruṣaḥ svo yadaraṇo janaḥ |
(5,30.2 ) unmocanapramocane ubhe vācā vadāmi te ||2||

(5,30.3 ) yaddudrohitha śepiṣe striyai puṃse acittyā |
(5,30.3 ) unmocanapramocane ubhe vācā vadāmi te ||3||
