(5,30.4 ) yatenaso mātṛkṛtāccheṣe pitṛkṛtācca yat|
(5,30.4 ) unmocanapramocane ubhe vācā vadāmi te ||4||

(5,30.5 ) yatte mātā yatte pitā jamirbhrātā ca sarjataḥ |
(5,30.5 ) pratyaksevasva bheṣajaṃ jaradaṣṭiṃ kṛṇomi tvā ||5||

(5,30.6 ) ihaidhi puruṣa sarveṇa manasā saha |
(5,30.6 ) dūtau yamasya mānu gā adhi jīvapurā ihi ||6||

(5,30.7 ) anuhūtaḥ punarehi vidvān udayanaṃ pathaḥ |
(5,30.7 ) ārohaṇamākramaṇaṃ jīvatojīvato'yanam ||7||

(5,30.8 ) mā bibherna mariṣyasi jaradaṣṭiṃ kṛṇomi tvā |
(5,30.8 ) niravocamahaṃ yakṣmamaṅgebhyo aṅgajvaraṃ tava ||8||

(5,30.9 ) aṅgabhedo aṅgajvaro yaśca te hṛdayāmayaḥ |
(5,30.9 ) yakṣmaḥ śyena iva prāpaptadvacā sāḍhaḥ parastarām ||9||

(5,30.10 ) ṛṣī bodhapratībodhāvasvapno yaśca jāgṛviḥ |
(5,30.10 ) tau te prāṇasya goptārau divā naktaṃ ca jāgṛtām ||10||

(5,30.11 ) ayamagnirupasadya iha sūrya udetu te |
(5,30.11 ) udehi mṛtyorgambhīrātkṛṣṇāccittamasaspari ||11||

(5,30.12 ) namo yamāya namo astu mṛtyave namaḥ pitṛbhya uta ye nayanti |
(5,30.12 ) utpāraṇasya yo veda tamagniṃ puro dadhe'smā ariṣṭatātaye ||12||

(5,30.13 ) aitu prāṇa aitu mana aitu cakṣuratho balam |
(5,30.13 ) śarīramasya saṃ vidāṃ tatpadbhyāṃ prati tiṣṭhatu ||13||

(5,30.14 ) prāṇenāgne cakṣuṣā saṃ sṛjemaṃ samīraya tanvā saṃ balena |
(5,30.14 ) vetthāmṛtasya mā nu gān mā nu bhūmigṛho bhuvat||14||

(5,30.15 ) mā te prāṇa upa dasan mo apāno'pi dhāyi te |
(5,30.15 ) sūryastvādhipatirmṛtyorudāyachatu raśmibhiḥ ||15||

(5,30.16 ) iyamantarvadati jihvā baddhā paniṣpadā |
(5,30.16 ) tvayā yakṣmaṃ niravocaṃ śataṃ ropīśca takmanaḥ ||16||

(5,30.17 ) ayaṃ lokaḥ priyatamo devānāmaparājitaḥ |
(5,30.17 ) yasmai tvamiha mṛtyave diṣṭaḥ puruṣa jajñiṣe |
(5,30.17 ) sa ca tvānu hvayāmasi mā purā jaraso mṛthāḥ ||17||



(5,31.1 ) yāṃ te cakrurāme pātre yāṃ cakrurmiśradhānye |
(5,31.1 ) āme māṃse kṛtyāṃ yāṃ cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām ||1||

(5,31.2 ) yāṃ te cakruḥ kṛkavākāvaje vā yāṃ kurīriṇi |
(5,31.2 ) avyāṃ te kṛtyāṃ yāṃ cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām ||2||

(5,31.3 ) yāṃ te cakrurekaśaphe paśūnāmubhayādati |
(5,31.3 ) gardabhe kṛtyāṃ yāṃ cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām ||3||

(5,31.4 ) yāṃ te cakruramūlāyāṃ valagaṃ vā narācyām |
(5,31.4 ) kṣetre te kṛtyāṃ yāṃ cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām ||4||
(5,31.5 ) yāṃ te cakrurgārhapatye pūrvāgnāvuta duścitaḥ |
(5,31.5 ) śālāyāṃ kṛtyāṃ yāṃ cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām ||5||

(5,31.6 ) yāṃ te cakruḥ sabhāyāṃ yāṃ cakruradhidevane |
(5,31.6 ) akṣeṣu kṛtyāṃ yāṃ cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām ||6||

(5,31.7 ) yāṃ te cakruḥ senāyāṃ yāṃ cakruriṣvāyudhe |
(5,31.7 ) dundubhau kṛtyāṃ yāṃ cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām ||7||

(5,31.8 ) yāṃ te kṛtyāṃ kūpe'vadadhuḥ śmaśāne vā nicakhnuḥ |
(5,31.8 ) sadmani kṛtyāṃ yāṃ cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām ||8||

(5,31.9 ) yāṃ te cakruḥ puruṣāsthe agnau saṃkasuke ca yām |
(5,31.9 ) mrokaṃ nirdāhaṃ kravyādaṃ punaḥ prati harāmi tām ||9||
(5,31.10 ) apathenā jabhāraināṃ tāṃ pathetaḥ pra hiṇmasi |
(5,31.10 ) adhīro maryādhīrebhyaḥ saṃ jabhārācittyā ||10||

(5,31.11 ) yaścakāra na śaśāka kartuṃ śaśre pādamaṅgurim |
(5,31.11 ) cakāra bhadramasmabhyamabhago bhagavadbhyaḥ ||11||

(5,31.12 ) kṛtyākṛtaṃ valaginaṃ mūlinaṃ śapatheyyam |
(5,31.12 ) indrastaṃ hantu mahatā vadhenāgnirvidhyatvastayā ||12||



(6,1.1 ) doṣo gāya bṛhadgāya dyumaddhehi |
(6,1.1 ) ātharvaṇa stuhi devaṃ savitāram ||1||

(6,1.2 ) tamu ṣṭuhi yo antaḥ sindhau sūnuḥ |
(6,1.2 ) satyasya yuvānamadroghavācaṃ suśevam ||2||

(6,1.3 ) sa ghā no devaḥ savitā sāviṣadamṛtāni bhūri |
(6,1.3 ) ubhe suṣṭutī sugātave ||3||


(6,2.1 ) indrāya somamṛtvijaḥ sunotā ca dhāvata |
(6,2.1 ) stoturyo vacaḥ śṛṇavaddhavaṃ ca me ||1||

(6,2.2 ) ā yaṃ viśantīndavo vayo na vṛkṣamandhasaḥ |
(6,2.2 ) virapśin vi mṛdho jahi rakṣasvinīḥ ||2||

(6,2.3 ) sunotā somapāvne somamindrāya vajriṇe |
(6,2.3 ) yuvā jeteśānaḥ sa puruṣṭutaḥ ||3||



(6,3.1 ) pātaṃ na indrāpūṣaṇāditiḥ pāntu marutaḥ |
(6,3.1 ) apāṃ napātsindhavaḥ sapta pātana pātu no viṣṇuruta dyauḥ ||1||

(6,3.2 ) pātāṃ no dyāvāpṛthivī abhiṣṭaye pātu grāvā pātu somo no aṃhasaḥ |
(6,3.2 ) pātu no devī subhagā sarasvatī pātvagniḥ śivā ye asya pāyavaḥ ||2||

(6,3.3 ) pātāṃ no devāśvinā śubhaspatī uṣāsānaktota na uruṣyatām |
(6,3.3 ) apāṃ napādabhihrutī gayasya ciddeva tvaṣṭarvardhaya sarvatātaye ||3||



(6,4.1 ) tvaṣṭā me daivyaṃ vacaḥ parjanyo brahmaṇaspatiḥ |
(6,4.1 ) putrairbhrātṛbhiraditirnu pātu no duṣṭaraṃ trāyamāṇaṃ sahaḥ ||1||

(6,4.2 ) aṃśo bhago varuṇo mitro aryamāditiḥ pāntu marutaḥ |
(6,4.2 ) apa tasya dveṣo gamedabhihruto yāvayacchatrumantitam ||2||

(6,4.3 ) dhiye samaśvinā prāvataṃ na uruṣyā ṇa urujmann aprayuchan |
(6,4.3 ) dyauṣpitaryāvaya duchunā yā ||3||



(6,5.1 ) udenamuttaraṃ nayāgne ghṛtenāhuta |
(6,5.1 ) samenaṃ varcasā sṛja prajayā ca bahuṃ kṛdhi ||1||

(6,5.2 ) indremaṃ prataraṃ kṛdhi sajātānāmasadvaśī |
(6,5.2 ) rāyaspoṣeṇa saṃ sṛja jīvātave jarase naya ||2||

(6,5.3 ) yasya kṛṇmo havirgṛhe tamagne vardhayā tvam |
(6,5.3 ) tasmai somo adhi bravadayaṃ ca brahmaṇaspatiḥ ||3||



(6,6.1 ) yo'smān brahmaṇaspate'devo abhimanyate |
(6,6.1 ) sarvaṃ taṃ randhayāsi me yajamānāya sunvate ||1||

(6,6.2 ) yo naḥ soma suśaṃsino duḥśaṃsa ādideśati |
(6,6.2 ) vajreṇāsya mukhe jahi sa saṃpiṣṭo apāyati ||2||

(6,6.3 ) yo naḥ somābhidāsati sanābhiryaśca niṣṭyaḥ |
(6,6.3 ) apa tasya balaṃ tira mahīva dyaurvadhatmanā ||3||



(6,7.1 ) yena somāditiḥ pathā mitrā vā yantyadruhaḥ |
(6,7.1 ) tenā no'vasā gahi ||1||

(6,7.2 ) yena soma sāhantyāsurān randhayāsi naḥ |
(6,7.2 ) tenā no adhi vocata ||2||

(6,7.3 ) yena devā asurāṇāmojāṃsyavṛṇīdhvam |
(6,7.3 ) tenā naḥ śarma yachata ||3||



(6,8.1 ) yathā vṛkṣaṃ libujā samantaṃ pariṣasvaje |
(6,8.1 ) evā pari ṣvajasva māṃ yathā māṃ kāminyaso yathā man nāpagā asaḥ ||1||
(6,8.2 ) yathā suparṇaḥ prapatan pakṣau nihanti bhūmyām |
(6,8.2 ) evā ni hanmi te mano yathā māṃ kāminyaso yathā man nāpagā asaḥ ||2||

(6,8.3 ) yatheme dyāvāpṛthivī sadyaḥ paryeti sūryaḥ |
(6,8.3 ) evā paryemi te mano yathā māṃ kāminyaso yathā man nāpagā asaḥ ||3||


(6,9.1 ) vāñcha me tanvaṃ pādau vāñchākṣyau vāñcha sakthyau |
(6,9.1 ) akṣyau vṛṣaṇyantyāḥ keśā māṃ te kāmena śuṣyantu ||1||

(6,9.2 ) mama tvā doṣaṇiśriṣaṃ kṛṇomi hṛdayaśriṣam |
(6,9.2 ) yathā mama kratāvaso mama cittamupāyasi ||2||

(6,9.3 ) yāsāṃ nābhirārehaṇaṃ hṛdi saṃvananaṃ kṛtaṃ gāvo ghṛtasya mātaro'mūṃ saṃ vānayantu me ||3||



(6,10.1 ) pṛthivyai śrotrāya vanaspatibhyo'gnaye'dhipataye svāhā ||1||

(6,10.2 ) prāṇāyāntarikṣāya vayobhyo vāyave'dhipataye svāhā ||2||

(6,10.3 ) dive cakṣuṣe nakṣatrebhyaḥ sūryāyādhipataye svāhā ||3||



(6,11.1 ) śamīmaśvattha ārūḍhastatra puṃsuvanaṃ kṛtam |
(6,11.1 ) tadvai putrasya vedanaṃ tatstrīṣvā bharāmasi ||1||

(6,11.2 ) puṃsi vai reto bhavati tatstriyāmanu ṣicyate |
(6,11.2 ) tadvai putrasya vedanaṃ tatprajāpatirabravīt||2||

(6,11.3 ) prajāpatiranumatiḥ sinīvālyacīkḷpat|
(6,11.3 ) straiṣūyamanyatra dadhatpumāṃsamu dadhatiha ||3||



(6,12.1 ) pari dyāmiva sūryo'hīnāṃ janimāgamam |
(6,12.1 ) rātrī jagadivānyaddhaṃsāttenā te vāraye viṣam ||1||

(6,12.2 ) yadbrahmabhiryadṛṣibhiryaddevairviditaṃ purā |
(6,12.2 ) yadbhūtaṃ bhavyamāsanvattenā te vāraye viṣam ||2||

(6,12.3 ) madhvā pṛñce nadyaḥ parvatā girayo madhu |
(6,12.3 ) madhu paruṣṇī śīpālā śamāsne astu śaṃ hṛde ||3||



(6,13.1 ) namo devavadhebhyo namo rājavadhebhyaḥ |
(6,13.1 ) atho ye viśyānāṃ vadhāstebhyo mṛtyo namo'stu te ||1||

(6,13.2 ) namaste adhivākāya parāvākāya te namaḥ |
(6,13.2 ) sumatyai mṛtyo te namo durmatyai te idaṃ namaḥ ||2||

(6,13.3 ) namaste yātudhānebhyo namaste bheṣajebhyaḥ |
(6,13.3 ) namaste mṛtyo mūlebhyo brāhmaṇebhya idaṃ namaḥ ||3||



(6,14.1 ) asthisraṃsaṃ parusraṃsamāsthitaṃ hṛdayāmayam |
(6,14.1 ) balāsaṃ sarvaṃ nāśayāṅgeṣṭhā yaśca parvasu ||1||

(6,14.2 ) nirbalāsaṃ balāsinaḥ kṣiṇomi muṣkaraṃ yathā |
(6,14.2 ) chinadmyasya bandhanaṃ mūlamurvārvā iva ||2||

(6,14.3 ) nirbalāsetaḥ pra patāśuṅgaḥ śiśuko yathā |
(6,14.3 ) atho ita iva hāyano'pa drāhyavīrahā ||3||



(6,15.1 ) uttamo asyoṣadhīnāṃ tava vṛkṣā upastayaḥ |
(6,15.1 ) upastirastu so'smākaṃ yo asmāmabhidāsati ||1||

(6,15.2 ) sabandhuścāsabandhuśca yo asmāmabhidāsati |
(6,15.2 ) teṣāṃ sā vṛkṣāṇāmivāhaṃ bhūyāsamuttamaḥ ||2||

(6,15.3 ) yathā soma oṣadhīnāmuttamo haviṣāṃ kṛtaḥ |
(6,15.3 ) talāśā vṛkṣāṇāmivāhaṃ bhūyāsamuttamaḥ ||3||



(6,16.1 ) ābayo anābayo rasasta ugra ābayo |
(6,16.1 ) ā te karambhamadmasi ||1||

(6,16.2 ) vihahlo nāma te pitā madāvatī nāma te mātā |
(6,16.2 ) sa hina tvamasi yastvamātmānamāvayaḥ ||2||

(6,16.3 ) tauvilike'velayāvāyamailaba ailayīt|
(6,16.3 ) babhruśca babhrukarṇaścāpehi nirāla ||3||

(6,16.4 ) alasālāsi pūrva silāñjālāsyuttarā |
(6,16.4 ) nīlāgalasāla ||4||


(6,17.1 ) yatheyaṃ pṛthivī mahī bhūtānāṃ garbhamādadhe |
(6,17.1 ) evā te dhriyatāṃ garbho anu sūtuṃ savitave ||1||

(6,17.2 ) yatheyaṃ pṛthivī mahī dādhāremān vanaspatīn |
(6,17.2 ) evā te dhriyatāṃ garbho anu sūtuṃ savitave ||2||

(6,17.3 ) yatheyaṃ pṛthivī mahī dādhāra parvatān girīn |
(6,17.3 ) evā te dhriyatāṃ garbho anu sūtuṃ savitave ||3||

(6,17.4 ) yatheyaṃ pṛthivī mahī dādhāra viṣṭhitaṃ jagat|
(6,17.4 ) evā te dhriyatāṃ garbho anu sūtuṃ savitave ||4||



(6,18.1 ) īrṣyāyā dhrājiṃ prathamāṃ prathamasyā utāparām |
(6,18.1 ) agniṃ hṛdayyaṃ śokaṃ taṃ te nirvāpayāmasi ||1||

(6,18.2 ) yathā bhūmirmṛtamanā mṛtān mṛtamanastarā |
(6,18.2 ) yathota mamruṣo mana everṣyormṛtaṃ manaḥ ||2||

(6,18.3 ) ado yatte hṛdi śritaṃ manaskaṃ patayiṣṇukam |
(6,18.3 ) tatasta īrṣyāṃ muñcāmi nirūṣmāṇaṃ dṛteriva ||3||


(6,19.1 ) punantu mā devajanāḥ punantu manavo dhiyā |
(6,19.1 ) punantu viśvā bhūtāni pavamānaḥ punātu mā ||1||

(6,19.2 ) pavamānaḥ punātu mā kratve dakṣāya jīvase |
(6,19.2 ) atho ariṣṭatātaye ||2||

(6,19.3 ) ubhābhyāṃ deva savitaḥ pavitreṇa savena ca |
(6,19.3 ) asmān punīhi cakṣase ||3||



(6,20.1 ) agnerivāsya dahata eti śuṣmiṇa uteva matto vilapann apāyati |
(6,20.1 ) anyamasmadichatu kaṃ cidavratastapurvadhāya namo astu takmane ||1||

(6,20.2 ) namo rudrāya namo astu takmane namo rājñe varuṇāya tviṣīmate |
(6,20.2 ) namo dive namaḥ pṛthivyai nama oṣadhībhyaḥ ||2||

(6,20.3 ) ayaṃ yo abhiśocayiṣṇurviśvā rūpāṇi haritā kṛṇoṣi |
(6,20.3 ) tasmai te'ruṇāya babhrave namaḥ kṛṇomi vanyāya takmane ||3||



(6,21.1 ) imā yāstisraḥ pṛthivīstāsāṃ ha bhūmiruttamā |
(6,21.1 ) tāsāmadhi tvaco ahaṃ bheṣajaṃ samu jagrabham ||1||

(6,21.2 ) śreṣṭhamasi bheṣajānāṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ vīrudhānām |
(6,21.2 ) somo bhaga iva yāmeṣu deveṣu varuṇo yathā ||2||

(6,21.3 ) revatīranādhṛṣaḥ siṣāsavaḥ siṣāsatha |
(6,21.3 ) uta stha keśadṛmhaṇīratho ha keśavardhanīḥ ||3||



(6,22.1 ) kṛṣṇaṃ niyānaṃ harayaḥ suparṇā apo vasānā divamutpatanti |
(6,22.1 ) ta āvavṛtrantsadanādṛtasyādidghṛtena pṛthivīṃ vyūduḥ ||1||

(6,22.2 ) payasvatīḥ kṛṇuthāpa oṣadhīḥ śivā yadejathā maruto rukmavakṣasaḥ |
(6,22.2 ) ūrjaṃ ca tatra sumatiṃ ca pinvata yatrā naro marutaḥ siñcathā madhu ||2||

(6,22.3 ) udapruto marutastāmiyarta vṛṣṭiryā viśvā nivataspṛṇāti |
(6,22.3 ) ejāti glahā kanyeva tunnairuṃ tundānā patyeva jāyā ||3||



(6,23.1 ) sasruṣīstadapaso divā naktaṃ ca sasruṣīḥ |
(6,23.1 ) vareṇyakraturahamapo devīrupa hvaye ||1||

(6,23.2 ) otā āpaḥ karmaṇyā muñcantvitaḥ praṇītaye |
(6,23.2 ) sadyaḥ kṛṇvantvetave ||2||

(6,23.3 ) devasya savituḥ save karma kṛṇvantu mānuṣāḥ |
(6,23.3 ) śaṃ no bhavantvapa oṣadhīḥ śivāḥ ||3||



(6,24.1 ) himavataḥ pra sravanti sindhau samaha saṅgamaḥ |
(6,24.1 ) āpo ha mahyaṃ taddevīrdadan hṛddyotabheṣajam ||1||

(6,24.2 ) yan me akṣyorādidyota pārṣṇyoḥ prapadośca yat|
(6,24.2 ) āpastatsarvaṃ niṣkaran bhiṣajāṃ subhiṣaktamāḥ ||2||

(6,24.3 ) sindhupatnīḥ sindhurājñīḥ sarvā yā nadya sthana |
(6,24.3 ) datta nastasya bheṣajaṃ tenā vo bhunajāmahai ||3||



(6,25.1 ) pañca ca yāḥ pañcāśacca saṃyanti manyā abhi |
(6,25.1 ) itastāḥ sarvā naśyantu vākā apacitāmiva ||1||

(6,25.2 ) sapta ca yāḥ saptatiśca saṃyanti graivyā abhi |
(6,25.2 ) itastāḥ sarvā naśyantu vākā apacitāmiva ||2||

(6,25.3 ) nava ca yā navatiśca saṃyanti skandhyā abhi |
(6,25.3 ) itastāḥ sarvā naśyantu vākā apacitāmiva ||3||



(6,26.1 ) ava mā pāpmantsṛja vaśī san mṛdayāsi naḥ |
(6,26.1 ) ā mā bhadrasya loke pāpman dhehyavihrutam ||1||

(6,26.2 ) yo naḥ pāpman na jahāsi tamu tvā jahimo vayam |
(6,26.2 ) pathāmanu vyāvartane'nyaṃ pāpmānu padyatām ||2||

(6,26.3 ) anyatrāsman nyucyatu sahasrākṣo amartyaḥ |
(6,26.3 ) yaṃ dveṣāma tamṛchatu yamu dviṣmastamijjahi ||3||



(6,27.1 ) devāḥ kapota iṣito yadichan dūto nirṛtyā idamājagāma |
(6,27.1 ) tasmā arcāma kṛṇavāma niṣkṛtiṃ śaṃ no astu dvipade śaṃ catuṣpade ||1||

(6,27.2 ) śivaḥ kapota iṣito no astvanāgā devāḥ śakuno gṛhaṃ naḥ |
(6,27.2 ) agnirhi vipro juṣatāṃ havirnaḥ pari hetiḥ pakṣiṇī no vṛṇaktu ||2||

(6,27.3 ) hetiḥ pakṣiṇī na dabhātyasmān āṣṭrī padaṃ kṛṇute agnidhāne |
(6,27.3 ) śivo gobhya uta puruṣebhyo no astu mā no devā iha hiṃsītkapota ||3||



(6,28.1 ) ṛcā kapotaṃ nudata praṇodamiṣaṃ madantaḥ pari gāṃ nayāmaḥ |
(6,28.1 ) saṃlobhayanto duritā padāni hitvā na ūrjaṃ pra padātpathiṣṭhaḥ ||1||

(6,28.2 ) parīme'gnimarṣata parīme gāmaneṣata |
(6,28.2 ) deveṣvakrata śravaḥ ka imāmā dadharṣati ||2||

(6,28.3 ) yaḥ prathamaḥ pravatamāsasāda bahubhyaḥ panthāmanupaspaśānaḥ |
(6,28.3 ) yo'syeśe dvipado yaścatuṣpadastasmai yamāya namo astu mṛtyave ||3||



(6,29.1 ) amūn hetiḥ patatriṇī nyetu yadulūko vadati moghametat|
(6,29.1 ) yadvā kapota padamagnau kṛṇoti ||1||

(6,29.2 ) yau te dūtau nirṛta idameto'prahitau prahitau vā gṛhaṃ naḥ |
(6,29.2 ) kapotolūkābhyāmapadaṃ tadastu ||2||

(6,29.3 ) avairahatyāyedamā papatyātsuvīratāyā idamā sasadyāt|
(6,29.3 ) parāṅeva parā vada parācīmanu saṃvatam |
(6,29.3 ) yathā yamasya tvā gṛhe'rasaṃ praticākaśān ābhūkaṃ praticākaśān ||3||



(6,30.1 ) devā imaṃ madhunā saṃyutaṃ yavaṃ sarasvatyāmadhi maṇāvacarkṛṣuḥ |
(6,30.1 ) indra āsītsīrapatiḥ śatakratuḥ kīnāśā āsan marutaḥ sudānavaḥ ||1||

(6,30.2 ) yaste mado'vakeśo vikeśo yenābhihasyaṃ puruṣaṃ kṛṇoṣi |
(6,30.2 ) ārāttvadanyā vanāni vṛkṣi tvaṃ śami śatavalśā vi roha ||2||

(6,30.3 ) bṛhatpalāśe subhage varṣavṛddha ṛtāvari |
(6,30.3 ) māteva putrebhyo mṛḍa keśebhyaḥ śami ||3||



(6,31.1 ) āyaṃ gauḥ pṛśnirakramīdasadan mātaraṃ puraḥ |
(6,31.1 ) pitaraṃ ca prayantsvaḥ ||1||

(6,31.2 ) antaścarati rocanā asya prāṇādapānataḥ |
(6,31.2 ) vyakhyan mahiṣaḥ svaḥ ||2||

(6,31.3 ) triṃśaddhāmā vi rājati vākpataṅgo aśiśriyat|
(6,31.3 ) prati vastorahardyubhiḥ ||3||



(6,32.1 ) antardāve juhuta svetadyātudhānakṣayaṇaṃ ghṛtena |
(6,32.1 ) ārādrakṣāṃsi prati daha tvamagne na no gṛhāṇāmupa tītapāsi ||1||

(6,32.2 ) rudro vo grīvā aśaraitpiśācāḥ pṛṣṭīrvo'pi śṛṇātu yātudhānāḥ |
(6,32.2 ) vīrudvo viśvatovīryā yamena samajīgamat||2||

(6,32.3 ) abhayaṃ mitrāvaruṇāvihāstu no'rciṣāttriṇo nudataṃ pratīcaḥ |
(6,32.3 ) mā jñātāraṃ mā pratiṣṭhāṃ vidanta mitho vighnānā upa yantu mṛtyum ||3||



(6,33.1 ) yasyedamā rajo yujastuje janā navaṃ svaḥ |
(6,33.1 ) indrasya rantyaṃ bṛhat||1||
(6,33.2 ) nādhṛṣa ā dadhṛṣate dhṛṣāṇo dhṛṣitaḥ śavaḥ |
(6,33.2 ) purā yathā vyathiḥ śrava indrasya nādhṛṣe śavaḥ ||2||
(6,33.3 ) sa no dadātu tāṃ rayimuruṃ piśaṅgasaṃdṛśam |
(6,33.3 ) indraḥ patistuviṣṭamo janeṣvā ||3||



(6,34.1 ) prāgnaye vācamīraya vṛṣabhāya kṣitīnām |
(6,34.1 ) sa naḥ parṣadati dviṣaḥ ||1||

(6,34.2 ) yo rakṣāṃsi nijūrvatyagnistigmena śociṣā |
(6,34.2 ) sa naḥ parṣadati dviṣaḥ ||2||

(6,34.3 ) yaḥ parasyāḥ parāvatastiro dhanvātirocate |
(6,34.3 ) sa naḥ parṣadati dviṣaḥ ||3||

(6,34.4 ) yo viśvābhi vipaśyati bhuvanā saṃ ca paśyati |
(6,34.4 ) sa naḥ parṣadati dviṣaḥ ||4||

(6,34.5 ) yo asya pāre rajasaḥ śukro agnirajāyata |
(6,34.5 ) sa naḥ parṣadati dviṣaḥ ||5||



(6,35.1 ) vaiśvānaro na ūtaya ā pra yātu parāvataḥ |
(6,35.1 ) agnirnaḥ suṣṭutīrupa ||1||

(6,35.2 ) vaiśvānaro na āgamadimaṃ yajñaṃ sajūrupa |
(6,35.2 ) agniruktheṣvaṃhasu ||2||

(6,35.3 ) vaiśvānaro'ṅgirasāṃ stomamukthaṃ ca cākḷpat|
(6,35.3 ) aiṣu dyumnaṃ svaryamat||3||



(6,36.1 ) ṛtāvānaṃ vaiśvānaramṛtasya jyotiṣaspatim |
(6,36.1 ) ajasraṃ gharmamīmahe ||1||

(6,36.2 ) sa viśvā prati cākḷpa ṛtūṃrutsṛjate vaśī |
(6,36.2 ) yajñasya vaya uttiran ||2||

(6,36.3 ) agniḥ pareṣu dhāmasu kāmo bhūtasya bhavyasya |
(6,36.3 ) samrādeko vi rājati ||3||



(6,37.1 ) upa prāgātsahasrākṣo yuktvā śapatho ratham |
(6,37.1 ) śaptāramanvichan mama vṛka ivāvimato gṛham ||1||

(6,37.2 ) pari ṇo vṛṅgdhi śapatha hradamagnirivā dahan |
(6,37.2 ) śaptāramatra no jahi divo vṛkṣamivāśaniḥ ||2||

(6,37.3 ) yo naḥ śapādaśapataḥ śapato yaśca naḥ śapāt|
(6,37.3 ) śune peṣṭramivāvakṣāmaṃ taṃ pratyasyāmi mṛtyave ||3||



(6,38.1 ) siṃhe vyāghra uta yā pṛdākau tviṣiragnau brāhmaṇe sūrye yā |
(6,38.1 ) indraṃ yā devī subhagā jajāna sā na aitu varcasā saṃvidānā ||1||

(6,38.2 ) yā hastini dvīpini yā hiraṇye tviṣirapsu goṣu yā puruṣeṣu |
(6,38.2 ) indraṃ yā devī subhagā jajāna sā na aitu varcasā saṃvidānā ||2||

(6,38.3 ) rathe akṣeṣvṛṣabhasya vāje vāte parjanye varuṇasya śuṣme |
(6,38.3 ) indraṃ yā devī subhagā jajāna sā na aitu varcasā saṃvidānā ||3||

(6,38.4 ) rājanye dundubhāvāyatāyāmaśvasya vāje puruṣasya māyau |
(6,38.4 ) indraṃ yā devī subhagā jajāna sā na aitu varcasā samvidānā ||4||



(6,39.1 ) yaśo havirvardhatāmindrajūtaṃ sahasravīryaṃ subhṛtaṃ sahaskṛtam |
(6,39.1 ) prasarsrāṇamanu dīrghāya cakṣase haviṣmantaṃ mā vardhaya jyeṣṭhatātaye ||1||

(6,39.2 ) achā na indraṃ yaśasaṃ yaśobhiryaśasvinaṃ namasānā vidhema |
(6,39.2 ) sa no rāsva rāṣṭramindrajūtaṃ tasya te rātau yaśasaḥ syāma ||2||

(6,39.3 ) yaśā indro yaśā agniryaśāḥ somo ajāyata |
(6,39.3 ) yaśā viśvasya bhūtasya ahamasmi yaśastamaḥ ||3||



(6,40.1 ) abhayaṃ dyāvāpṛthivī ihāstu no'bhayaṃ somaḥ savitā naḥ kṛṇotu |
(6,40.1 ) abhayaṃ no'stūrvantarikṣaṃ saptaṛṣīṇāṃ ca haviṣābhayaṃ no astu ||1||

(6,40.2 ) asmai grāmāya pradiśaścatasra ūrjaṃ subhūtaṃ svasti savitā naḥ kṛṇotu |
(6,40.2 ) aśatrvindro abhayaṃ naḥ kṛṇotvanyatra rājñāmabhi yātu manyuḥ ||2||

(6,40.3 ) anamitraṃ no adharādanamitraṃ na uttarāt|
(6,40.3 ) indrānamitraṃ naḥ paścādanamitraṃ puraskṛdhi ||3||



(6,41.1 ) manase cetase dhiya ākūtaya uta cittaye |
(6,41.1 ) matyai śrutāya cakṣase vidhema haviṣā vayam ||1||

(6,41.2 ) apānāya vyānāya prāṇāya bhūridhāyase |
(6,41.2 ) sarasvatyā uruvyace vidhema haviṣā vayam ||2||

(6,41.3 ) mā no hāsiṣurṛṣayo daivyā ye tanūpā ye nastanvastanūjāḥ |
(6,41.3 ) amartyā martyāmabhi naḥ sacadhvamāyurdhatta prataraṃ jīvase naḥ ||3||



(6,42.1 ) ava jyāmiva dhanvano manyuṃ tanomi te hṛdaḥ |
(6,42.1 ) yathā saṃmanasau bhūtvā sakhāyāviva sacāvahai ||1||

(6,42.2 ) sakhāyāviva sacāvahā ava manyuṃ tanomi te |
(6,42.2 ) adhaste aśmano manyumupāsyāmasi yo guruḥ ||2||

(6,42.3 ) abhi tiṣṭhāmi te manyuṃ pārṣṇyā prapadena ca |
(6,42.3 ) yathāvaśo na vādiṣo mama cittamupāyasi ||3||



(6,43.1 ) ayaṃ darbho vimanyukaḥ svāya cāraṇāya ca |
(6,43.1 ) manyorvimanyukasyāyaṃ manyuśamana ucyate ||1||

(6,43.2 ) ayaṃ yo bhūrimūlaḥ samudramavatiṣṭhati |
(6,43.2 ) darbhaḥ pṛthivyā utthito manyuśamana ucyate ||2||

(6,43.3 ) vi te hanavyāṃ śaraṇiṃ vi te mukhyāṃ nayāmasi |
(6,43.3 ) yathāvaśo na vādiṣo mama cittamupāyasi ||3||



(6,44.1 ) asthāddyaurasthātpṛthivyasthādviśvamidaṃ jagat|
(6,44.1 ) asthurvṛkṣā ūrdhvasvapnāstiṣṭhādrogo ayaṃ tava ||1||

(6,44.2 ) śataṃ yā bheṣajāni te sahasraṃ saṃgatāni ca |
(6,44.2 ) śreṣṭhamāsrāvabheṣajaṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ roganāśanam ||2||

(6,44.3 ) rudrasya mūtramasyamṛtasya nābhiḥ |
(6,44.3 ) viṣāṇakā nāma vā asi pitṝṇāṃ mūlādutthitā vātīkṛtanāśanī ||3||



(6,45.1 ) paro'pehi manaspāpa kimaśastāni śaṃsasi |
(6,45.1 ) parehi na tvā kāmaye vṛkṣāṃ vanāni saṃ cara gṛheṣu goṣu me manaḥ ||1||

(6,45.2 ) avaśasā niḥśasā yatparāśasopārima jāgrato yatsvapantaḥ |
(6,45.2 ) agnirviśvānyapa duṣkṛtānyajuṣṭānyāre asmaddadhātu ||2||

(6,45.3 ) yadindra brahmaṇaspate'pi mṛṣā carāmasi |
(6,45.3 ) pracetā na āṅgiraso duritātpātvaṃhasaḥ ||3||



(6,46.1 ) yo na jīvo'si na mṛto devānāmamṛtagarbho'si svapna |
(6,46.1 ) varuṇānī te mātā yamaḥ pitārarurnāmāsi ||1||

(6,46.2 ) vidma te svapna janitraṃ devajāmīnāṃ putro'si yamasya karaṇaḥ |
(6,46.2 ) antako'si mṛtyurasi taṃ tvā svapna tathā saṃ vidma sa naḥ svapna duṣvapnyātpāhi ||2||

(6,46.3 ) yathā kalāṃ yathā śaphaṃ yatharṇaṃ saṃnayanti |
(6,46.3 ) evā duṣvapnyaṃ sarvaṃ dviṣate saṃ nayāmasi ||3||



(6,47.1 ) agniḥ prātaḥsavane pātvasmān vaiśvānaro viśvakṛdviśvaśaṃbhūḥ |
(6,47.1 ) sa naḥ pāvako draviṇe dadhātvāyuṣmantaḥ sahabhakṣāḥ syāma ||1||

(6,47.2 ) viśve devā maruta indro asmān asmin dvitīye savane na jahyuḥ |
(6,47.2 ) āyuṣmantaḥ priyameṣāṃ vadanto vayaṃ devānāṃ sumatau syāma ||2||

(6,47.3 ) idaṃ tṛtīyaṃ savanaṃ kavīnāmṛtena ye camasamairayanta |
(6,47.3 ) te saudhanvanāḥ svarānaśānāḥ sviṣṭiṃ no abhi vasyo nayantu ||3||



(6,48.1 ) śyeno'si gāyatrachandā anu tvā rabhe |
(6,48.1 ) svasti mā saṃ vahāsya yajñasyodṛci svāhā ||1||

(6,48.2 ) ṛbhurasi jagachandā anu tvā rabhe |
(6,48.2 ) svasti mā saṃ vahāsya yajñasyodṛci svāhā ||2||

(6,48.3 ) vṛṣāsi triṣṭupchandā anu tvā rabhe |
(6,48.3 ) svasti mā saṃ vahāsya yajñasyodṛci svāhā ||3||



(6,49.1 ) nahi te agne tanvaḥ krūramānaṃśa martyaḥ |
(6,49.1 ) kapirbabhasti tejanaṃ svaṃ jarāyu gauriva ||1||

(6,49.2 ) meṣa iva vai saṃ ca vi corvacyase yaduttaradrāvuparaśca khādataḥ |
(6,49.2 ) śīrṣṇā śiro'psasāpso ardayann aṃśūn babhasti haritebhirāsabhiḥ ||2||

(6,49.3 ) suparṇā vācamakratopa dyavyākhare kṛṣṇā iṣirā anartiṣuḥ |
(6,49.3 ) ni yan niyanti uparasya niṣkṛtiṃ purū reto dadhire sūryaśritaḥ ||3||



(6,50.1 ) hataṃ tardaṃ samaṅkamākhumaśvinā chintaṃ śiro api pṛṣṭīḥ śṛṇītam |
(6,50.1 ) yavān nedadān api nahyataṃ mukhamathābhayaṃ kṛṇutaṃ dhānyāya ||1||

(6,50.2 ) tarda hai pataṅga hai jabhya hā upakvasa |
(6,50.2 ) brahmevāsaṃsthitaṃ haviranadanta imān yavān ahiṃsanto apodita ||2||

(6,50.3 ) tardāpate vaghāpate tṛṣṭajambhā ā śṛṇota me |
(6,50.3 ) ya āraṇyā vyadvarā ye ke ca stha vyadvarāstāntsarvān jambhayāmasi ||3||



(6,51.1 ) vāyoḥ pūtaḥ pavitreṇa pratyaṅsomo ati drutaḥ |
(6,51.1 ) indrasya yujaḥ sakhā ||1||

(6,51.2 ) āpo asmān mātaraḥ sūdayantu ghṛtena no ghṛtapvaḥ punantu |
(6,51.2 ) viśvaṃ hi ripraṃ pravahanti devīrudidābhyaḥ śucirā pūta emi ||2||

(6,51.3 ) yatkiṃ cedaṃ varuṇa daivye jane'bhidrohaṃ manuṣyāścaranti |
(6,51.3 ) acittyā cettava dharma yuyopima mā nastasmādenaso deva rīriṣaḥ ||3||


(6,52.1 ) utsūryo diva eti puro rakṣāṃsi nijūrvan |
(6,52.1 ) ādityaḥ parvatebhyo viśvadṛṣṭo adṛṣṭahā ||1||

(6,52.2 ) ni gāvo goṣṭhe asadan ni mṛgāso avikṣata |
(6,52.2 ) nyūrmayo nadīnaṃ nyadṛṣṭā alipsata ||2||

(6,52.3 ) āyurdadaṃ vipaścitaṃ śrutāṃ kaṇvasya vīrudham |
(6,52.3 ) ābhāriṣaṃ viśvabheṣajīmasyādṛṣṭān ni śamayat||3||



(6,53.1 ) dyauśca ma idaṃ pṛthivī ca pracetasau śukro bṛhan dakṣiṇayā pipartu |
(6,53.1 ) anu svadhā cikitāṃ somo agnirvāyurnaḥ pātu savitā bhagaśca ||1||

(6,53.2 ) punaḥ prāṇaḥ punarātmā na aitu punaścakṣuḥ punarasurna aitu |
(6,53.2 ) vaiśvānaro no adabdhastanūpā antastiṣṭhāti duritāni viśvā ||2||

(6,53.3 ) saṃ varcasā payasā saṃ tanūbhiraganmahi manasā saṃ śivena |
(6,53.3 ) tvaṣṭā no atra varīyaḥ kṛṇotvanu no mārṣṭu tanvo yadviriṣṭam ||3||


(6,54.1 ) idaṃ tadyuja uttaramindraṃ śumbhāmyaṣṭaye |
(6,54.1 ) asya kṣatraṃ śriyaṃ mahīṃ vṛṣṭiriva vardhayā tṛṇam ||1||

(6,54.2 ) asmai kṣatramagnīṣomāvasmai dhārayataṃ rayim |
(6,54.2 ) imaṃ rāṣṭrasyābhīvarge kṛṇutaṃ yuja uttaram ||2||

(6,54.3 ) sabandhuścāsabandhuśca yo asmāmabhidāsati |
(6,54.3 ) sarvaṃ taṃ randhayāsi me yajamānāya sunvate ||3||



(6,55.1 ) ye panthāno bahavo devayānā antarā dyāvāpṛthivī saṃcaranti |
(6,55.1 ) teṣāmajyāniṃ yatamo vahāti tasmai mā devāḥ pari datteha sarve ||1||

(6,55.2 ) grīṣmo hemantaḥ śiśiro vasantaḥ śaradvarṣāḥ svite no dadhāta |
(6,55.2 ) ā no goṣu bhajatā prajāyāṃ nivāta idvaḥ śaraṇe syāma ||2||

(6,55.3 ) idāvatsarāya parivatsarāya saṃvatsarāya kṛṇutā bṛhan namaḥ |
(6,55.3 ) teṣāṃ vayaṃ sumatau yajñiyānāmapi bhadre sau manase syāma ||3||



(6,56.1 ) mā no devā ahirvadhītsatokāntsahapuruṣān |
(6,56.1 ) samyataṃ na vi ṣparadvyāttaṃ na saṃ yaman namo devajanebhyaḥ |

(6,56.2 ) namo'stvasitāya namastiraścirājaye |
(6,56.2 ) svajāya babhrave namo namo devajanebhyaḥ ||2||

(6,56.3 ) saṃ te hanmi datā dataḥ samu te hanvā hanū |
(6,56.3 ) saṃ te jihvayā jihvāṃ saṃ vāsnāha āsyam ||3||



(6,57.1 ) idamidvā u bheṣajamidaṃ rudrasya bheṣajam |
(6,57.1 ) yeneṣumekatejanāṃ śataśalyāmapabravat||1||

(6,57.2 ) jālāṣeṇābhi ṣiñcata jālāṣeṇopa siñcata |
(6,57.2 ) jālāṣamugraṃ bheṣajaṃ tena no mṛḍa jīvase ||2||

(6,57.3 ) śaṃ ca no mayaśca no mā ca naḥ kiṃ canāmamat|
(6,57.3 ) kṣamā rapo viśvaṃ no astu bheṣajaṃ sarvaṃ no astu bheṣajam ||3||



(6,58.1 ) yaśasaṃ mendro maghavān kṛṇotu yaśasaṃ dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe ime |
(6,58.1 ) yaśasaṃ mā devaḥ savitā kṛṇotu priyo dāturdakṣiṇāyā iha syām ||1||

(6,58.2 ) yathendro dyāvāpṛthivyoryaśasvān yathāpa oṣadhīṣu yaśasvatīḥ |
(6,58.2 ) evā viśveṣu deveṣu vayaṃ sarveṣu yaśasaḥ syāma ||2||

(6,58.3 ) yaśā indro yaśā agniryaśāḥ somo ajāyata |
(6,58.3 ) yaśā viśvasya bhūtasyāhamasmi yaśastamaḥ ||3||



(6,59.1 ) anaḍudbhyastvaṃ prathamaṃ dhenubhyastvamarundhati |
(6,59.1 ) adhenave vayase śarma yacha catuṣpade ||1||

(6,59.2 ) śarma yachatvoṣadhiḥ saha devīrarundhatī |
(6,59.2 ) karatpayasvantaṃ goṣṭhamayakṣmāmuta pūruṣān ||2||

(6,59.3 ) viśvarūpāṃ subhagāmachāvadāmi jīvalām |
(6,59.3 ) sā no rudrasyāstāṃ hetiṃ dūraṃ nayatu gobhyaḥ ||3||



(6,60.1 ) ayamā yātyaryamā purastādviṣitastupaḥ |
(6,60.1 ) asyā ichann agruvai patimuta jāyāmajānaye ||1||

(6,60.2 ) aśramadiyamaryamann anyāsāṃ samanaṃ yatī |
(6,60.2 ) aṅgo nvaryamann asyā anyāḥ samanamāyati ||2||

(6,60.3 ) dhātā dādhāra pṛthivīṃ dhātā dyāmuta sūryam |
(6,60.3 ) dhātāsyā agruvai patiṃ dadhātu pratikāmyam ||3||



(6,61.1 ) mahyamāpo madhumaderayantāṃ mahyaṃ sūro abharajjyotiṣe kam |
(6,61.1 ) mahyaṃ devā uta viśve tapojā mahyaṃ devaḥ savitā vyaco dhāt||1||

(6,61.2 ) ahaṃ viveca pṛthivīmuta dyāmahamṛtūṃrajanayaṃ sapta sākam |
(6,61.2 ) ahaṃ satyamanṛtaṃ yadvadāmyahaṃ daivīṃ pari vācaṃ viśaśca ||2||

(6,61.3 ) ahaṃ jajāna pṛthivīmuta dyāmahamṛtūṃrajanayaṃ sapta sindhūn |
(6,61.3 ) ahaṃ satvamanṛtaṃ yadvadāmi yo agnīṣomāvajuṣe sakhāyā ||3||



(6,62.1 ) vaiśvānaro raśmibhirnaḥ punātu vātaḥ prāṇeneṣiro nabhobhiḥ |
(6,62.1 ) dyāvāpṛthivī payasā payasvatī ṛtāvarī yajñiye na punītām ||1||

(6,62.2 ) vaiśvānarīṃ sūnṛtāmā rabhadhvaṃ yasyā āśāstanvo vītapṛṣṭhāḥ |
(6,62.2 ) tayā gṛṇantaḥ sadhamādeṣu vayaṃ syāma patayo rayīnām ||2||

(6,62.3 ) vaiśvānarīṃ varcasa ā rabhadhvaṃ śuddhā bhavantaḥ śucayaḥ pāvakāḥ |
(6,62.3 ) iheḍayā sadhamādaṃ madanto jyokpaśyema sūryamuccarantam ||3||



(6,63.1 ) yatte devī nirṛtirābabandha dāma grīvāsvavimokyaṃ yat|
(6,63.1 ) tatte vi ṣyāmyāyuṣe varcase balāyādomadamannamaddhi prasūtaḥ ||1||

(6,63.2 ) namo'stu te nirṛte tigmatejo'yasmayān vi cṛtā bandhapāśān |
(6,63.2 ) yamo mahyaṃ punarittvāṃ dadāti tasmai yamāya namo astu mṛtyave ||2||

(6,63.3 ) ayasmaye drupade bedhiṣa ihābhihito mṛtyubhirye sahasram |
(6,63.3 ) yamena tvaṃ pitṛbhiḥ saṃvidāna uttamaṃ nākamadhi rohayemam ||3||

(6,63.4 ) saṃsamidyuvase vṛṣann agne viśvānyarya ā |
(6,63.4 ) iḍaspade samidhyase sa no vasūnyā bhara ||4||



(6,64.1 ) saṃ jānīdhvaṃ saṃ pṛcyadhvaṃ saṃ vo manāṃsi jānatām |
(6,64.1 ) devā bhāgaṃ yathā pūrve samjānānā upāsate ||1||

(6,64.2 ) samāno mantraḥ samitiḥ samānī samānaṃ vrataṃ saha cittameṣām |
(6,64.2 ) samānena vo haviṣā juhomi samānaṃ ceto abhisaṃviśadhvam ||2||

(6,64.3 ) samānī va ākūtiḥ samānā hṛdayāni vaḥ |
(6,64.3 ) samānamastu vo manaḥ yathā vaḥ susahāsati ||3||



(6,65.1 ) ava manyuravāyatāva bāhū manoyujā |
(6,65.1 ) parāśara tvaṃ teṣāṃ parāñcaṃ śuṣmamardayādhā no rayimā kṛdhi ||1||

(6,65.2 ) nirhastebhyo nairhastaṃ yaṃ devāḥ śarumasyatha |
(6,65.2 ) vṛścāmi śatrūṇāṃ bāhūn anena haviṣā'ham ||2||

(6,65.3 ) indraścakāra prathamaṃ nairhastamasurebhyaḥ |
(6,65.3 ) jayantu satvāno mama sthireṇendreṇa medinā ||3||



(6,66.1 ) nirhastaḥ śatrurabhidāsann astu ye senābhiryudhamāyantyasmān |
(6,66.1 ) samarpayendra mahatā vadhena drātveṣāmaghahāro vividdhaḥ ||1||

(6,66.2 ) ātanvānā āyachanto'syanto ye ca dhāvatha |
(6,66.2 ) nirhastāḥ śatravaḥ sthanendro vo'dya parāśarīt||2||

(6,66.3 ) nirhastāḥ santu śatravo'ṅgaiṣāṃ mlāpayāmasi |
(6,66.3 ) athaiṣāmindra vedāṃsi śataśo vi bhajāmahai ||3||


(6,67.1 ) pari vartmāni sarvata indraḥ pūṣā ca sasratuḥ |
(6,67.1 ) muhyantvadyāmūḥ senā amitrāṇāṃ parastarām ||1||

(6,67.2 ) mūḍhā amitrāścaratāśīrṣāṇa ivāhayaḥ |
(6,67.2 ) teṣāṃ vo agnimūḍhānāmindro hantu varaṃvaram ||2||

(6,67.3 ) aiṣu nahya vṛṣājinaṃ hariṇasya bhiyaṃ kṛdhi |
(6,67.3 ) parāṅamitra eṣatvarvācī gaurupeṣatu ||3||



(6,68.1 ) āyamagantsavitā kṣureṇoṣṇena vāya udakenehi |
(6,68.1 ) ādityā rudrā vasava undantu sacetasaḥ somasya rājño vapata pracetasaḥ ||1||

(6,68.2 ) aditiḥ śmaśru vapatvāpa undantu varcasā |
(6,68.2 ) cikitsatu prajāpatirdīrghāyutvāya cakṣase ||2||

(6,68.3 ) yenāvapatsavitā kṣureṇa somasya rājño varuṇasya vidvān |
(6,68.3 ) tena brahmāṇo vapatedamasya gomān aśvavān ayamastu prajāvān ||3||



(6,69.1 ) girāvaragarāṭeṣu hiranye goṣu yadyaśaḥ |
(6,69.1 ) surāyāṃ sicyamānāyāṃ kīlāle madhu tan mayi ||1||

(6,69.2 ) aśvinā sāragheṇa mā madhunāṅktaṃ śubhaspatī |
(6,69.2 ) yathā bhargasvatīṃ vācamāvadāni janāmanu ||2||

(6,69.3 ) mayi varco atho yaśo'tho yajñasya yatpayaḥ |
(6,69.3 ) tan mayi prajāpatirdivi dyāmiva dṛṃhatu ||3||



(6,70.1 ) yathā māṃsaṃ yathā surā yathākṣā adhidevane |
(6,70.1 ) yathā puṃso vṛṣaṇyata striyāṃ nihanyate manaḥ |
(6,70.1 ) evā te aghnye mano'dhi vatse ni hanyatām ||1||

(6,70.2 ) yathā hastī hastinyāḥ padena padamudyuje |
(6,70.2 ) yathā puṃso vṛṣaṇyata striyāṃ nihanyate manaḥ |
(6,70.2 ) evā te aghnye mano'dhi vatse ni hanyatām ||2||

(6,70.3 ) yathā pradhiryathopadhiryathā nabhyaṃ pradhāvadhi |
(6,70.3 ) yathā puṃso vṛṣaṇyata striyāṃ nihanyate manaḥ |
(6,70.3 ) evā te aghnye mano'dhi vatse ni hanyatām ||3||



(6,71.1 ) yadannamadmi bahudhā virūpaṃ hiraṇyamaśvamuta gāmajāmavim |
(6,71.1 ) yadeva kiṃ ca pratijagrahāhamagniṣṭaddhotā suhutaṃ kṛnotu ||1||

(6,71.2 ) yan mā hutamahutamājagāma dattaṃ pitṛbhiranumataṃ manuṣyaiḥ |
(6,71.2 ) yasmān me mana udiva rārajītyagniṣṭaddhotā suhutaṃ kṛṇotu ||2||

(6,71.3 ) yadannamadmyanṛtena devā dāsyann adāsyann uta saṃgṛṇāmi |
(6,71.3 ) vaiśvānarasya mahato mahimnā śivaṃ mahyaṃ madhumadastvannam ||3||



(6,72.1 ) yathāsitaḥ prathayate vaśāmanu vapūṃṣi kṛṇvann asurasya māyayā |
(6,72.1 ) evā te śepaḥ sahasāyamarko'ṅgenāṅgaṃ saṃsamakaṃ kṛṇotu ||1||

(6,72.2 ) yathā pasastāyādaraṃ vātena sthūlabhaṃ kṛtam |
(6,72.2 ) yāvatparasvataḥ pasastāvatte vardhatāṃ pasaḥ ||2||

(6,72.3 ) yāvadaṅgīnaṃ pārasvataṃ hāstinaṃ gārdabhaṃ ca yat|
(6,72.3 ) yāvadaśvasya vājinastāvatte vardhatāṃ pasaḥ ||3||



(6,73.1 ) eha yātu varuṇaḥ somo agnirbṛhaspatirvasubhireha yātu |
(6,73.1 ) asya śriyamupasaṃyāta sarva ugrasya cettuḥ saṃmanasaḥ sajātāḥ ||1||

(6,73.2 ) yo vaḥ śuṣmo hṛdayeṣvantarākūtiryā vo manasi praviṣṭā |
(6,73.2 ) tāntsīvayāmi haviṣā ghṛtena mayi sajātā ramatirvo astu ||2||

(6,73.3 ) ihaiva sta māpa yātādhyasmatpūṣā parastādapathaṃ vaḥ kṛṇotu |
(6,73.3 ) vāstoṣpatiranu vo johavītu mayi sajātā ramatiḥ vo astu ||3||



(6,74.1 ) saṃ vaḥ pṛcyantāṃ tanvaḥ saṃ manāṃsi samu vratā |
(6,74.1 ) saṃ vo'yaṃ brahmaṇaspatirbhagaḥ saṃ vo ajīgamat||1||

(6,74.2 ) samjñapanaṃ vo manaso'tho samjñapanaṃ hṛdaḥ |
(6,74.2 ) atho bhagasya yacchrāntaṃ tena saṃjñapayāmi vaḥ ||2||

(6,74.3 ) yathādityā vasubhiḥ sambabhūvurmarudbhirugrā ahṛṇīyamānāḥ |
(6,74.3 ) evā triṇāmann ahṛṇīyamāna imān janāntsaṃmanasaskṛdhīha ||3||



(6,75.1 ) niramuṃ nuda okasaḥ sapatno yaḥ pṛtanyati |
(6,75.1 ) nairbādhyena haviṣendra enaṃ parāśarīt||1||

(6,75.2 ) paramāṃ taṃ parāvatamindro nudatu vṛtrahā |
(6,75.2 ) yato na punarāyati śaśvatībhyaḥ samābhyaḥ ||2||

(6,75.3 ) etu tisraḥ parāvata etu pañca janāmati |
(6,75.3 ) etu tisro'ti rocanā yato na punarāyati |
(6,75.3 ) śaśvatībhyaḥ samābhyo yāvatsūryo asaddivi ||3||



(6,76.1 ) ya enaṃ pariṣīdanti samādadhati cakṣase |
(6,76.1 ) saṃpreddho agnirjihvābhirudetu hṛdayādadhi ||1||

(6,76.2 ) agneḥ sāmtapanasyāhamāyuṣe padamā rabhe |
(6,76.2 ) addhātiryasya paśyati dhūmamudyantamāsyataḥ ||2||

(6,76.3 ) yo asya samidhaṃ veda kṣatriyeṇa samāhitām |
(6,76.3 ) nābhihvāre padaṃ ni dadhāti sa mṛtyave ||3||

(6,76.4 ) nainaṃ ghnanti paryāyiṇo na sannāmava gachati |
(6,76.4 ) agneryaḥ kṣatriyo vidvān nāma gṛhnāti āyuṣe ||4||



(6,77.1 ) asthāddyaurasthātpṛthivyasthādviśvamidaṃ jagat|
(6,77.1 ) āsthāne parvatā asthu sthāmnyaśvāmatiṣṭhipam ||1||

(6,77.2 ) ya udānatparāyaṇaṃ ya udānaṇnyāyanam |
(6,77.2 ) āvartanaṃ nivartanaṃ yo gopā api taṃ huve ||2||

(6,77.3 ) jātavedo ni vartaya śataṃ te santvāvṛtaḥ |
(6,77.3 ) sahasraṃ ta upāvṛtastābhirnaḥ punarā kṛdhi ||3||



(6,78.1 ) tena bhūtena haviṣāyamā pyāyatāṃ punaḥ |
(6,78.1 ) jāyāṃ yāmasmā āvākṣustāṃ rasenābhi vardhatām ||1||

(6,78.2 ) abhi vardhatāṃ payasābhi rāṣṭreṇa vardhatām |
(6,78.2 ) rayyā sahasravarcasemau stāmanupakṣitau ||2||

(6,78.3 ) tvaṣṭā jāyāmajanayattvaṣṭāsyai tvāṃ patim |
(6,78.3 ) tvaṣṭā sahasramāyuṃṣi dīrghamāyuḥ kṛṇotu vām ||3||



(6,79.1 ) ayaṃ no nabhasaspatiḥ saṃsphāno abhi rakṣatu |
(6,79.1 ) asamātiṃ gṛheṣu naḥ ||1||

(6,79.2 ) tvaṃ no nabhasaspate ūrjaṃ gṛhesu dhāraya |
(6,79.2 ) ā puṣṭametvā vasu ||2||

(6,79.3 ) deva saṃsphāna sahasrāpoṣasyeśiṣe |
(6,79.3 ) tasya no rāsva tasya no dhehi tasya te bhaktivāmsaḥ syāma ||3||



(6,80.1 ) antarikṣeṇa patati viśvā bhūtāvacākaśat|
(6,80.1 ) śuno divyasya yan mahastenā te haviṣā vidhema ||1||

(6,80.2 ) ye trayaḥ kālakāñjā divi devā iva śritāḥ |
(6,80.2 ) tāntsarvān ahva ūtaye'smā ariṣṭatātaye ||2||
(6,80.3 ) apsu te janma divi te sadhasthaṃ samudre antarmahimā te pṛthivyām |
(6,80.3 ) śuno divyasya yan mahastenā te haviṣā vidhema ||3||



(6,81.1 ) yantāsi yachase hastāvapa rakṣāṃsi sedhasi |
(6,81.1 ) prajāṃ dhanaṃ ca gṛhṇānaḥ parihasto abhūdayam ||1||

(6,81.2 ) parihasta vi dhāraya yoniṃ garbhāya dhātave |
(6,81.2 ) maryāde putramā dhehi taṃ tvamā gamayāgame ||2||

(6,81.3 ) yaṃ parihastamabibharaditiḥ putrakāmyā |
(6,81.3 ) tvaṣṭā tamasyā ā badhnādyathā putraṃ janād||3||



(6,82.1 ) āgachata āgatasya nāma gṛhṇāmyāyataḥ |
(6,82.1 ) indrasya vṛtraghno vanve vāsavasya śatakratoḥ ||1||

(6,82.2 ) yena sūryāṃ sāvitrīmaśvinohatuḥ pathā |
(6,82.2 ) tena māmabravīdbhago jayāmā vahatāditi ||2||

(6,82.3 ) yaste'ṅkuśo vasudāno bṛhann indra hiraṇyayaḥ |
(6,82.3 ) tenā janiyate jāyāṃ mahyaṃ dhehi śacīpate ||3||



(6,83.1 ) apacitaḥ pra patata suparṇo vasateriva |
(6,83.1 ) sūryaḥ kṛṇotu bheṣajaṃ candramā vo'pochatu ||1||

(6,83.2 ) enyekā śyenyekā kṛṣṇaikā rohiṇī dve |
(6,83.2 ) sarvāsāmagrabhaṃ nāmāvīraghnīrapetana ||2||

(6,83.3 ) asūtikā rāmāyaṇyapacitpra patiṣyati |
(6,83.3 ) glauritaḥ pra patiṣyati sa galunto naśiṣyati ||3||

(6,83.4 ) vīhi svāmāhutiṃ juṣāno manasā svāhā manasā yadidaṃ juhomi ||4||



(6,84.1 ) yasyāsta āsani ghore juhomyeṣāṃ baddhānāmavasarjanāya kam |
(6,84.1 ) bhūmiriti tvābhipramanvate janā nirṛtiriti tvāhaṃ pari veda sarvataḥ ||1||

(6,84.2 ) bhūte haviṣmatī bhavaiṣa te bhāgo yo asmāsu |
(6,84.2 ) muñcemān amūn enasaḥ svāhā ||2||

(6,84.3 ) evo ṣvasman nirṛte'nehā tvamayasmayān vi cṛtā bandhapāśān |
(6,84.3 ) yamo mahyaṃ punarittvāṃ dadāti tasmai yamāya namo astu mṛtyave ||3||

(6,84.4 ) ayasmaye drupade bedhiṣa ihābhihito mṛtyubhirye sahasram |
(6,84.4 ) yamena tvaṃ pitṛbhiḥ saṃvidāna uttamaṃ nākamadhi rohayemam ||4||



(6,85.1 ) varaṇo vārayātā ayaṃ devo vanaspatiḥ |
(6,85.1 ) yakṣmo yo asminn āviṣṭastamu devā avīvaran ||1||

(6,85.2 ) indrasya vacasā vayaṃ mitrasya varuṇasya ca |
(6,85.2 ) devānāṃ sarveṣāṃ vācā yakṣmaṃ te vārayāmahe ||2||

(6,85.3 ) yathā vṛtra imā āpastastambha viśvadhā yatīḥ |
(6,85.3 ) evā te agninā yakṣmaṃ vaiśvānareṇa vāraye ||3||



(6,86.1 ) vṛṣendrasya vṛṣā divo vṛsā pṛthivyā ayam |
(6,86.1 ) vṛṣā viśvasya bhūtasya tvamekavṛṣo bhava ||1||

(6,86.2 ) samudra īśe sravatāmagniḥ pṛthivyā vaśī |
(6,86.2 ) candramā nakṣatrāṇāmīśe tvamekavṛṣo bhava ||2||

(6,86.3 ) samrāḍasyasurāṇāṃ kakun manuṣyānām |
(6,86.3 ) devānāmardhabhāgasi tvamekavṛṣo bhava ||3||



(6,87.1 ) ā tvāhārṣamantarabhūrdhruvastiṣṭhāvicācalat|
(6,87.1 ) viśastvā sarvā vāñchantu mā tvadrāṣṭramadhi bhraśat||1||

(6,87.2 ) ihaivaidhi māpa cyoṣṭhāḥ parvata ivāvicācalat|
(6,87.2 ) indra iveha dhruvastiṣṭheha rāṣṭramu dhāraya ||2||

(6,87.3 ) indra etamadīdharatdhruvaṃ dhruveṇa haviṣā |
(6,87.3 ) tasmai somo adhi bravadayaṃ ca brahmaṇaspatiḥ ||3||



(6,88.1 ) dhruvā dyaurdhruvā pṛthivī dhruvaṃ viśvamidaṃ jagat|
(6,88.1 ) dhruvāsaḥ parvatā ime dhruvo rājā viśāmayam ||1||

(6,88.2 ) dhruvaṃ te rājā varuṇo dhruvaṃ devo bṛhaspatiḥ |
(6,88.2 ) dhruvaṃ ta indraścāgniśca rāṣṭraṃ dhārayatāṃ dhruvam ||2||

(6,88.3 ) dhruvo'cyutaḥ pra mṛṇīhi śatrūn chatrūyato'dharān pādayasva |
(6,88.3 ) sarvā diśaḥ saṃmanasaḥ sadhrīcīrdhruvāya te samitiḥ kalpatāmiha ||3||



(6,89.1 ) idaṃ yatpreṇyaḥ śiro dattaṃ somena vṛṣṇyam |
(6,89.1 ) tataḥ pari prajātena hārdiṃ te śocayāmasi ||1||

(6,89.2 ) śocayāmasi te hārdiṃ śocayāmasi te manaḥ |
(6,89.2 ) vātaṃ dhūma iva sadhryaṅmāmevānvetu ye manaḥ ||2||

(6,89.3 ) mahyaṃ tvā mitrāvaruṇau mahyaṃ devī sarasvatī |
(6,89.3 ) mahyaṃ tvā madhyaṃ bhūmyā ubhāvantau samasyatām ||3||



(6,90.1 ) yāṃ te rudra iṣumāsyadaṅgebhyo hṛdayāya ca |
(6,90.1 ) idaṃ tāmadya tvadvayaṃ viṣūcīṃ vi vṛhāmasi ||1||

(6,90.2 ) yāste śataṃ dhamanayo'ṅgānyanu viṣṭhitāḥ |
(6,90.2 ) tāsāṃ te sarvāsāṃ vayaṃ nirviṣāṇi hvayāmasi ||2||

(6,90.3 ) namaste rudrāsyate namaḥ pratihitāyai |
(6,90.3 ) namo visṛjyamānāyai namo nipatitāyai ||3||



(6,91.1 ) imaṃ yavamaṣṭāyogaiḥ ṣadyogebhiracarkṛṣuḥ |
(6,91.1 ) tenā te tanvo rapo'pācīnamapa vyaye ||1||

(6,91.2 ) nyagvāto vāti nyaktapati sūryaḥ |
(6,91.2 ) nīcīnamaghnyā duhe nyagbhavatu te rapaḥ ||2||

(6,91.3 ) āpa idvā u bheṣajīrāpo amīvacātanīḥ |
(6,91.3 ) āpo viśvasya bheṣajīstāste kṛṇvantu bheṣajam ||3||



(6,92.1 ) vātaraṃhā bhava vājin yujamāna indrasya yāhi prasave manojavāḥ |
(6,92.1 ) yuñjantu tvā maruto viśvavedasa ā te tvastā patsu javaṃ dadhātu ||1||

(6,92.2 ) javaste arvan nihito guhā yaḥ śyene vāte uta yo'caratparīttaḥ |
(6,92.2 ) tena tvaṃ vājin balavān balenājiṃ jaya samane parayiṣṇuḥ ||2||

(6,92.3 ) tanūṣṭe vājin tanvaṃ nayantī vāmamasmabhyaṃ dhāvatu śarma tubhyam |
(6,92.3 ) ahruto maho dharuṇāya devo divīva jyotiḥ svamā mimīyāt||3||



(6,93.1 ) yamo mṛtyuraghamāro nirṛtho babhruḥ śarvo'stā nīlaśikhaṇḍaḥ |
(6,93.1 ) devajanāḥ senayottasthivāṃsaste asmākaṃ pari vṛñjantu vīrān ||1||

(6,93.2 ) manasā homairharasā ghṛtena śarvāyāstra uta rājñe bhavāya |
(6,93.2 ) namasyebhyo nama ebhyaḥ kṛṇomyanyatrāsmadaghaviṣā nayantu ||2||

(6,93.3 ) trāyadhvaṃ no aghaviṣābhyo vadhādviśve devā maruto viśvavedasaḥ |
(6,93.3 ) agnīṣomā varuṇaḥ pūtadakṣā vātāparjanyayoḥ sumatau syāma ||3||



(6,94.1 ) saṃ vo manāṃsi saṃ vratā samākūtīrnamāmasi |
(6,94.1 ) amī ye vivratā sthana tān vaḥ saṃ namayāmasi ||1||

(6,94.2 ) ahaṃ gṛbhṇāmi manasā manāṃsi mama cittamanu cittebhireta |
(6,94.2 ) mama vaśeṣu hṛdayāni vaḥ kṛṇomi mama yātamanuvartmāna eta ||2||

(6,94.3 ) ote me dyāvāpṛthivī otā devī sarasvatī |
(6,94.3 ) otau ma indraścāgniśca rdhyāsmedaṃ sarasvati ||3||



(6,95.1 ) aśvattho devasadanastṛtīyasyāmito divi |
(6,95.1 ) tatrāmṛtasya cakṣaṇaṃ devāḥ kuṣṭhamavanvata ||1||

(6,95.2 ) hiraṇyayī nauracaraddhiraṇyabandhanā divi |
(6,95.2 ) tatrāmṛtasya puṣpaṃ devāḥ kuṣṭhamavanvata ||2||

(6,95.3 ) garbho asyoṣadhīnāṃ garbho himavatāmuta |
(6,95.3 ) garbho viśvasya bhūtasyemaṃ me agadaṃ kṛdhi ||3||


(6,96.1 ) yā oṣadhayaḥ somarājñīrbahvīḥ śatavicakṣaṇāḥ |
(6,96.1 ) bṛhaspatiprasūtāstā no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||1||

(6,96.2 ) muñcantu mā śapathyādatho varuṇyāduta |
(6,96.2 ) atho yamasya paḍvīśādviśvasmāddevakilbiṣāt||2||

(6,96.3 ) yaccakṣuṣā manasā yacca vācopārima jāgrato yatsvapantaḥ |
(6,96.3 ) somastāni svadhayā naḥ punātu ||3||



(6,97.1 ) abhibhūryajño abhibhūragnirabhibhūḥ somo abhibhūrindraḥ |
(6,97.1 ) abhyahaṃ viśvāḥ pṛtanā yathāsānyevā vidhemāgnihotrā idaṃ haviḥ ||1||

(6,97.2 ) svadhāstu mitrāvaruṇā vipaścitā prajāvatkṣatraṃ madhuneha pinvatam |
(6,97.2 ) bādhethāṃ dūraṃ nirṛtiṃ parācaiḥ kṛtaṃ cidenaḥ pra mumuktamasmat||2||

(6,97.3 ) imaṃ vīramanu harṣadhvamugramindraṃ sakhāyo anu saṃ rabhadhvam |
(6,97.3 ) grāmajitaṃ gojitaṃ vajrabāhuṃ jayantamajma pramṛṇantamojasā ||3||



(6,98.1 ) indro jayāti na parā jayātā adhirājo rājasu rājayātai |
(6,98.1 ) carkṛtya īḍyo vandyaścopasadyo namasyśbhaveha ||1||

(6,98.2 ) tvamindrādhirājaḥ śravasyustvaṃ bhūrabhibhūtirjanānām |
(6,98.2 ) tvaṃ daivīrviśa imā vi rājāyuṣmatkṣatramajaraṃ te astu ||2||

(6,98.3 ) prācyā diśastvamindrāsi rājotodīcyā diśo vṛtrahan chatruho'si |
(6,98.3 ) yatra yanti srotyāstajjitaṃ te dakṣiṇato vṛṣabha eṣi havyaḥ ||3||



(6,99.1 ) abhi tvendra varimataḥ purā tvāṃhūraṇāddhuve |
(6,99.1 ) hvayāmyugraṃ cettāraṃ puruṇāmānamekajam ||1||

(6,99.2 ) yo adya senyo vadho jighāṃsan na udīrate |
(6,99.2 ) indrasya tatra bāhū samantaṃ pari dadmaḥ ||2||

(6,99.3 ) pari dadma indrasya bāhū samantaṃ trātustrāyatāṃ naḥ |
(6,99.3 ) deva savitaḥ soma rājantsumanasaṃ mā kṛṇu svastaye ||3||



(6,100.1 ) devā aduḥ sūryo dyauradātpṛthivyadāt|
(6,100.1 ) tisraḥ sarasvatiraduḥ sacittā viṣadūṣaṇam ||1||

(6,100.2 ) yadvo devā upajīkā āsiñcan dhanvanyudakam |
(6,100.2 ) tena devaprasūtenedaṃ dūṣayatā viṣam ||2||
(6,100.3 ) asurāṇāṃ duhitāsi sā devānāmasi svasā |
(6,100.3 ) divaspṛthivyāḥ saṃbhūtā sā cakarthārasaṃ viṣam ||3||



(6,101.1 ) ā vṛṣāyasva śvasihi vardhasva prathayasva ca |
(6,101.1 ) yathāṅgaṃ vardhatāṃ śepastena yoṣitamijjahi ||1||

(6,101.2 ) yena kṛṣaṃ vājayanti yena hinvantyāturam |
(6,101.2 ) tenāsya brahmaṇaspate dhanurivā tānayā pasaḥ ||2||

(6,101.3 ) āhaṃ tanomi te paso adhi jyāmiva dhanvani |
(6,101.3 ) kramasva ṛṣa iva rohitamanavaglāyatā sadā ||3||



(6,102.1 ) yathāyaṃ vāho aśvinā samaiti saṃ ca vartate |
(6,102.1 ) evā māmabhi te manaḥ samaitu saṃ ca vartatām ||1||

(6,102.2 ) āhaṃ khidāmi te mano rājāśvaḥ pṛṣṭyāmiva |
(6,102.2 ) reṣmachinnaṃ yathā tṛṇaṃ mayi te veṣṭatāṃ manaḥ ||2||

(6,102.3 ) āñjanasya madughasya kuṣṭhasya naladasya ca |
(6,102.3 ) turo bhagasya hastābhyāmanurodhanamudbhare ||3||



(6,103.1 ) saṃdānaṃ vo bṛhaspatiḥ saṃdānaṃ savitā karat|
(6,103.1 ) saṃdānaṃ mitro aryamā saṃdānaṃ bhago aśvinā ||1||

(6,103.2 ) saṃ paramāntsamavamān atho saṃ dyāmi madhyamān |
(6,103.2 ) indrastān paryahārdāmnā tān agne saṃ dyā tvam ||2||

(6,103.3 ) amī ye yudhamāyanti ketūn kṛtvānīkaśaḥ |
(6,103.3 ) indrastān paryahārdāmna tān agne saṃ dyā tvam ||3||


(6,104.1 ) ādānena saṃdānenāmitrān ā dyāmasi |
(6,104.1 ) apānā ye caiṣāṃ prāṇā asunāsūntsamachidan ||1||

(6,104.2 ) idamādānamakaraṃ tapasendreṇa saṃśitam |
(6,104.2 ) amitrā ye'tra naḥ santi tān agna ā dyā tvam ||2||

(6,104.3 ) ainān dyatāmindrāgnī somo rājā ca medinau |
(6,104.3 ) indro marutvān ādānamamitrebhyaḥ kṛṇotu naḥ ||3||



(6,105.1 ) yathā mano manasketaiḥ parāpatatyāśumat|
(6,105.1 ) evā tvaṃ kāse pra pata manaso'nu pravāyyam ||1||

(6,105.2 ) yathā bāṇaḥ susaṃśitaḥ parāpatatyāśumat|
(6,105.2 ) evā tvaṃ kāse pra pata pṛthivyā anu saṃvatam ||2||

(6,105.3 ) yathā sūryasya raśmayaḥ parāpatantyāśumat|
(6,105.3 ) evā tvaṃ kāse pra pata samudrasyānu vikṣaram ||3||



(6,106.1 ) āyane te parāyane dūrvā rohantu puṣpiṇīḥ |
(6,106.1 ) utso vā tatra jāyatāṃ hrado vā puṇḍarīkavān ||1||

(6,106.2 ) apāmidaṃ nyayanaṃ samudrasya niveśanam |
(6,106.2 ) madhye hradasya no gṛhāḥ parācīnā mukhā kṛdhi ||2||

(6,106.3 ) himasya tvā jarāyuṇā śāle pari vyayāmasi |
(6,106.3 ) śītahradā hi no bhuvo'gniṣkṛṇotu bheṣajam ||3||



(6,107.1 ) viśvajittrāyamāṇāyai mā pari dehi |
(6,107.1 ) trāyamāṇe dvipācca sarvaṃ no rakṣa catuṣpāḍyacca naḥ svam ||1||

(6,107.2 ) trāyamāṇe viśvajite mā pari dehi |
(6,107.2 ) viśvajiddvipācca sarvaṃ no rakṣa catuṣpāḍyacca naḥ svam ||2||

(6,107.3 ) viśvajitkalyāṇyai mā pari dehi |
(6,107.3 ) kalyāṇi dvipācca sarvaṃ no rakṣa catuṣpāḍyacca naḥ svam ||3||

(6,107.4 ) kalyāṇi sarvavide mā pari dehi |
(6,107.4 ) sarvaviddvipācca sarvaṃ no rakṣa catuṣpāḍyacca naḥ svam ||4||



(6,108.1 ) tvaṃ no medhe prathamā gobhiraśvebhirā gahi |
(6,108.1 ) tvaṃ sūryasya raśmibhistvaṃ no asi yajñiyā ||1||

(6,108.2 ) medhāmahaṃ prathamāṃ brahmaṇvatīṃ brahmajūtāmṛṣiṣṭutām |
(6,108.2 ) prapītāṃ brahmacāribhirdevānāmavase huve ||2||

(6,108.3 ) yāṃ medhāmṛbhavo viduryāṃ medhāmasurā viduḥ |
(6,108.3 ) ṛṣayo bhadrāṃ medhāṃ yāṃ vidustāṃ mayyā veśayāmasi ||3||

(6,108.4 ) yāmṛṣayo bhūtakṛto medhāṃ medhāvino viduḥ |
(6,108.4 ) tayā māmadya medhayāgne medhāvinaṃ kṛṇu ||4||

(6,108.5 ) medhāṃ sāyaṃ medhāṃ prātarmedhāṃ madhyandinaṃ pari |
(6,108.5 ) medhāṃ sūryasya raśmibhirvacasā veśayāmahe ||5||



(6,109.1 ) pippalī kṣiptabheṣajyutātividdhabheṣajī |
(6,109.1 ) tāṃ devāḥ samakalpayann iyaṃ jīvitavā alam ||1||

(6,109.2 ) pippalyaḥ samavadantāyatīrjananādadhi |
(6,109.2 ) yaṃ jīvamaśnavāmahai na sa riṣyāti pūruṣaḥ ||2||

(6,109.3 ) asurāstvā nyakhanan devāstvodavapan punaḥ |
(6,109.3 ) vātīkṛtasya bheṣajīmatho kṣiptasya bheṣajīm ||3||



(6,110.1 ) pratno hi kamīḍyo adhvareṣu sanācca hotā navyaśca satsi |
(6,110.1 ) svāṃ cāgne tanvaṃ piprāyasvāsmabhyaṃ ca saubhagamā yajasva ||1||

(6,110.2 ) jyeṣṭhaghnyāṃ jāto vicṛtoryamasya mūlabarhaṇātpari pāhyenam |
(6,110.2 ) atyenaṃ neṣadduritāni viśvā dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya ||2||

(6,110.3 ) vyāghre'hnyajaniṣṭa vīro nakṣatrajā jāyamānaḥ suvīraḥ |
(6,110.3 ) sa mā vadhītpitaraṃ vardhamāno mā mātaraṃ pra minījjanitrīm ||3||



(6,111.1 ) imaṃ me agne puruṣaṃ mumugdhyayaṃ yo baddhaḥ suyato lālapīti |
(6,111.1 ) ato'dhi te kṛṇavadbhāgadheyaṃ yadānunmadito'sati ||1||

(6,111.2 ) agniṣṭe ni śamayatu yadi te mana udyutam |
(6,111.2 ) kṛṇomi vidvān bheṣajaṃ yathānunmadito'sasi ||2||

(6,111.3 ) devainasādunmaditamunmattaṃ rakṣasaspari |
(6,111.3 ) kṛṇomi vidvān bheṣajaṃ yadānunmadito'sati ||3||

(6,111.4 ) punastvā durapsarasaḥ punarindraḥ punarbhagaḥ |
(6,111.4 ) punastvā durviśve devā yathānunmadito'sasi ||4||


(6,112.1 ) mā jyeṣṭhaṃ vadhīdayamagna eṣāṃ mūlabarhaṇātpari pāhyenam |
(6,112.1 ) sa grāhyāḥ pāśān vi cṛta prajānan tubhyaṃ devā anu jānantu viśve ||1||

(6,112.2 ) un muñca pāśāṃstvamagna eṣāṃ trayastribhirutsitā yebhirāsan |
(6,112.2 ) sa grāhyāḥ pāśān vi cṛta prajānan pitāputrau mātaraṃ muñca sarvān ||2||

(6,112.3 ) yebhiḥ pāśaiḥ parivitto vibaddho'ṅgeaṅga ārpita utsitaśca |
(6,112.3 ) vi te mucyantaṃ vimuco hi santi bhrūṇaghni pūṣan duritāni mṛkṣva ||3||



(6,113.1 ) trite devā amṛjataitadenastrita enan manuṣyeṣu mamṛje |
(6,113.1 ) tato yadi tvā grāhirānaśe tāṃ te devā brahmaṇā nāśayantu ||1||

(6,113.2 ) marīcīrdhūmān pra viśānu pāpmann udārān gachota vā nīhārān |
(6,113.2 ) nadīnaṃ phenāmanu tān vi naśya bhrūṇaghni pūṣan duritāni mṛkṣva ||2||

(6,113.3 ) dvādaśadhā nihitaṃ tritasyāpamṛṣṭaṃ manuṣyainasāni |
(6,113.3 ) tato yadi tvā grāhirānaśe tāṃ te devā brahmaṇā nāśayantu ||3||



(6,114.1 ) yaddevā devaheḍanaṃ devāsaścakṛma vayam |
(6,114.1 ) ādityāstasmān no yuyamṛtasya ṛtena muñcata ||1||

(6,114.2 ) ṛtasya ṛtenādityā yajatrā muñcateha naḥ |
(6,114.2 ) yajñaṃ yadyajñavāhasaḥ śikṣanto nopaśekima ||2||

(6,114.3 ) medasvatā yajamānāḥ srucājyāni juhvataḥ |
(6,114.3 ) akāmā viśve vo devāḥ śikṣanto nopa śekima ||3||



(6,115.1 ) yadvidvāṃso yadavidvāṃsa enāṃsi cakṛmā vayam |
(6,115.1 ) yūyaṃ nastasmān muñcata viśve devāḥ sajoṣasaḥ ||1||

(6,115.2 ) yadi jāgradyadi svapann ena enasyo'karam |
(6,115.2 ) bhūtaṃ mā tasmādbhavyaṃ ca drupadādiva muñcatām ||2||

(6,115.3 ) drupadādiva mumucānaḥ svinnaḥ snātvā malādiva |
(6,115.3 ) pūtaṃ pavitreṇevājyaṃ viśve śumbhantu mainasaḥ ||3||



(6,116.1 ) yadyāmaṃ cakrurnikhananto agre kārṣīvaṇā annavido na vidyayā |
(6,116.1 ) vaivasvate rājani tajjuhomyatha yajñiyaṃ madhumadastu no'nnam ||1||

(6,116.2 ) vaivasvataḥ kṛṇavadbhāgadheyaṃ madhubhāgo madhunā saṃ sṛjāti |
(6,116.2 ) māturyadena iṣitaṃ na āgan yadvā pitā'parāddho jihīde ||2||

(6,116.3 ) yadīdaṃ māturyadi piturnaḥ pari bhrātuḥ putrāccetasa ena āgan |
(6,116.3 ) yāvanto asmān pitaraḥ sacante teṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ śivo astu manyuḥ ||3||



(6,117.1 ) apamityamapratīttaṃ yadasmi yamasya yena balinā carāmi |
(6,117.1 ) idaṃ tadagne anṛṇo bhavāmi tvaṃ pāśān vicṛtaṃ vettha sarvān ||1||

(6,117.2 ) ihaiva santaḥ prati dadma enajjīvā jīvebhyo ni harāma enat|
(6,117.2 ) apamitya dhānyaṃ yajjaghasāhamidaṃ tadagne anṛṇo bhavāmi ||2||

(6,117.3 ) anṛṇā asminn anṛṇāḥ parasmin tṛtīye loke anṛṇāḥ syāma |
(6,117.3 ) ye devayānāḥ pitṛyāṇaśca lokāḥ sarvān patho anṛṇā ā kṣiyema ||3||



(6,118.1 ) yaddhastābhyāṃ cakṛma kilbiṣāṇyakṣāṇāṃ gatnumupalipsamānāḥ |
(6,118.1 ) ugraṃpaśye ugrajitau tadadyāpsarasāvanu dattāmṛṇaṃ naḥ ||1||

(6,118.2 ) ugraṃpaśye rāṣṭrabhṛtkilbiṣāṇi yadakṣavṛttamanu dattaṃ na etat|
(6,118.2 ) ṛṇān no na ṛṇamertsamāno yamasya loke adhirajjurāyat||2||

(6,118.3 ) yasmā ṛṇaṃ yasya jāyāmupaimi yaṃ yācamāno abhyaimi devāḥ |
(6,118.3 ) te vācaṃ vādiṣurmottarāṃ maddevapatnī apsarasāvadhītam ||3||



(6,119.1 ) yadadīvyann ṛṇamahaṃ kṛṇomyadāsyann agne uta saṃgṛṇāmi |
(6,119.1 ) vaiśvānaro no adhipā vasiṣṭha udin nayāti sukṛtasya lokam ||1||

(6,119.2 ) vaiśvānarāya prati vedayāmi yadi ṛṇaṃ saṃgaro devatāsu |
(6,119.2 ) sa etān pāśān vicṛtaṃ veda sarvān atha pakvena saha saṃ bhavema ||2||

(6,119.3 ) vaiśvānaraḥ pavitā mā punātu yatsaṃgaramabhidhāvāmyāśām |
(6,119.3 ) anājānan manasā yācamāno yattatraino apa tatsuvāmi ||3||



(6,120.1 ) yadantarikṣaṃ pṛthivīmuta dyāṃ yan mātaraṃ pitaraṃ vā jihiṃsima |
(6,120.1 ) ayaṃ tasmādgārhapatyo no agnirudin nayāti sukṛtasya lokam ||1||

(6,120.2 ) bhūmirmātāditirno janitraṃ bhrātāntarikṣamabhiśastyā naḥ |
(6,120.2 ) dyaurnaḥ pitā pitryācchaṃ bhavāti jāmimṛtvā māva patsi lokāt||2||

(6,120.3 ) yatrā suhārdaḥ sukṛto madanti vihāya rogaṃ tanvaḥ svāyāḥ |
(6,120.3 ) aślonā aṅgairahrutāḥ svarge tatra paśyema pitarau ca putrān ||3||



(6,121.1 ) viṣāṇā pāśān vi ṣyādhyasmadya uttamā adhamā vāruṇā ye |
(6,121.1 ) duṣvapnyaṃ duritaṃ ni ṣvāsmadatha gachema sukṛtasya lokam ||1||

(6,121.2 ) yaddāruṇi badhyase yacca rajjvāṃ yadbhūmyāṃ badhyase yacca vācā |
(6,121.2 ) ayaṃ tasmādgārhapatyo no agnirudin nayāti sukṛtasya lokam ||2||

(6,121.3 ) udagātāṃ bhagavatī vicṛtau nāma tārake |
(6,121.3 ) prehāmṛtasya yachatāṃ praitu baddhakamocanam ||3||

(6,121.4 ) vi jihīṣva lokaṃ kṛṇu bandhān muñcāsi baddhakam |
(6,121.4 ) yonyā iva pracyuto garbhaḥ pathaḥ sarvāmanu kṣiya ||4||



(6,122.1 ) etaṃ bhāgaṃ pari dadāmi vidvān viśvakarman prathamajā ṛtasya |
(6,122.1 ) asmābhirdattaṃ jarasaḥ parastādachinnaṃ tantumanu saṃ tarema ||1||

(6,122.2 ) tataṃ tantumanveke taranti yeṣāṃ dattaṃ pitryamāyanena |
(6,122.2 ) abandhveke dadataḥ prayachanto dātuṃ cecchikṣāntsa svarga eva ||2||

(6,122.3 ) anvārabhethāmanusaṃrabhethāmetaṃ lokaṃ śraddadhānāḥ sacante |
(6,122.3 ) yadvāṃ pakvaṃ pariviṣṭamagnau tasya guptaye dampatī saṃ śrayethām ||3||

(6,122.4 ) yajñaṃ yantaṃ manasā bṛhantamanvārohāmi tapasā sayoniḥ |
(6,122.4 ) upahūtā agne jarasaḥ parastāttṛtīye nāke sadhamādaṃ madema ||4||

(6,122.5 ) śuddhāḥ pūtā yoṣito yajñiyā imā brahmaṇāṃ hasteṣu prapṛthaksādayāmi |
(6,122.5 ) yatkāma idamabhiṣiñcāmi vo'hamindro marutvāntsa dadātu tan me ||5||



(6,123.1 ) etaṃ sadhasthāḥ pari vo dadāmi yaṃ śevadhimāvahājjātavedaḥ |
(6,123.1 ) anvāgantā yajamānaḥ svasti taṃ sma jānīta parame vyoman ||1||

(6,123.2 ) jānīta smainaṃ parame vyoman devāḥ sadhasthā vida lokamatra |
(6,123.2 ) anvāgantā yajamānaḥ svastīṣṭāpūrtaṃ sma kṛṇutāvirasmai ||2||

(6,123.3 ) devāḥ pitaraḥ pitaro devāḥ |
(6,123.3 ) yo asmi so asmi ||3||

(6,123.4 ) sa pacāmi sa dadāmi |
(6,123.4 ) sa yaje sa dattān mā yūṣam ||4||

(6,123.5 ) nāke rājan prati tiṣṭha tatraitatprati tiṣṭhatu |
(6,123.5 ) viddhi pūrtasya no rājantsa deva sumanā bhava ||5||



(6,124.1 ) divo nu māṃ bṛhato antarikṣādapāṃ stoko abhyapaptadrasena |
(6,124.1 ) samindriyena payasāhamagne chandobhiryajñaiḥ sukṛtāṃ kṛtena ||1||

(6,124.2 ) yadi vṛkṣādabhyapaptatphalaṃ tadyadyantarikṣātsa u vāyureva |
(6,124.2 ) yatrāspṛkṣattanvo yacca vāsasa āpo nudantu nirṛtiṃ parācaiḥ ||2||

(6,124.3 ) abhyañjanaṃ surabhi sā samṛddhirhiraṇyaṃ varcastadu pūtrimameva |
(6,124.3 ) sarvā pavitrā vitatādhyasmattan mā tārīn nirṛtirmo arātiḥ ||3||



(6,125.1 ) vanaspate vīḍvaṅgo hi bhūyā asmatsakhā prataraṇaḥ suvīraḥ |
(6,125.1 ) gobhiḥ saṃnaddho asi vīḍayasvāsthātā te jayatu jetvāni ||1||

(6,125.2 ) divaspṛthivyāḥ paryoja udbhṛtaṃ vanaspatibhyaḥ paryābhṛtaṃ sahaḥ |
(6,125.2 ) apāmojmānaṃ pari gobhirāvṛtamindrasya vajraṃ haviṣā rathaṃ yaja ||2||

(6,125.3 ) indrasyaujo marutāmanīkaṃ mitrasya garbho varuṇasya nābhiḥ |
(6,125.3 ) sa imāṃ no havyadātiṃ juṣāṇo deva ratha prati havyā gṛbhāya ||3||


(6,126.1 ) upa śvāsaya pṛthivīmuta dyaṃ purutrā te vanvatāṃ viṣṭhitaṃ jagat|
(6,126.1 ) sa dundubhe sajūrindreṇa devairdūrāddavīyo apa sedha śatrūn ||1||

(6,126.2 ) ā krandaya balamojo na ā dhā abhi ṣṭana duritā bādhamānaḥ |
(6,126.2 ) apa sedha dundubhe duchunāmita indrasya muṣṭirasi vīḍayasva ||2||
(6,126.3 ) prāmūṃ jayābhīme jayantu ketumaddundubhirvāvadītu |
(6,126.3 ) samaśvaparṇāḥ patantu no naro'smākamindra rathino jayantu ||3||



(6,127.1 ) vidradhasya balāsasya lohitasya vanaspate |
(6,127.1 ) visalpakasyauṣadhe mocchiṣaḥ piśitaṃ cana ||1||

(6,127.2 ) yau te balāsa tiṣṭhataḥ kakṣe muṣkāvapaśritau |
(6,127.2 ) vedāhaṃ tasya bheṣajaṃ cīpudrurabhicakṣaṇam ||2||

(6,127.3 ) yo aṅgyo yaḥ karṇyo yo akṣyorvisalpakaḥ |
(6,127.3 ) vi vṛhāmo visalpakaṃ vidradhaṃ hṛdayāmayam |
(6,127.3 ) parā tamajñātaṃ yakṣmamadharāñcaṃ suvāmasi ||3||



(6,128.1 ) śakadhūmaṃ nakṣatrāṇi yadrājānamakurvata |
(6,128.1 ) bhadrāhamasmai prāyachan idaṃ rāṣṭramasāditi ||1||

(6,128.2 ) bhadrāhaṃ no madhyaṃdine bhadrāhaṃ sāyamastu naḥ |
(6,128.2 ) bhadrāhaṃ no ahnāṃ prātā rātrī bhadrāhamastu naḥ ||2||

(6,128.3 ) ahorātrābhyāṃ nakṣatrebhyaḥ suryācandramasābhyām |
(6,128.3 ) bhadrāhamasmabhyaṃ rājan chakadhūma tvaṃ kṛdhi ||3||

(6,128.4 ) yo no bhadrāhamakaraḥ sāyaṃ naktamatho divā |
(6,128.4 ) tasmai te nakṣatrarāja śakadhūma sadā namaḥ ||4||



(6,129.1 ) bhagena mā śāṃśapena sākamindreṇa medinā |
(6,129.1 ) kṛṇomi bhaginaṃ māpa drāntvarātayaḥ ||1||

(6,129.2 ) yena vṛkṣāmabhyabhavo bhagena varcasā saha |
(6,129.2 ) tena mā bhaginaṃ kṛṇvapa drāntvarātayaḥ ||2||

(6,129.3 ) yo andho yaḥ punaḥsaro bhago vṛkṣeṣvāhitaḥ |
(6,129.3 ) tena mā bhaginaṃ kṛṇvapa drāntvarātayaḥ ||3||



(6,130.1 ) rathajitāṃ rāthajiteyīnāmapsarasāmayaṃ smaraḥ |
(6,130.1 ) devāḥ pra hiṇuta smaramasau māmanu śocatu ||1||

(6,130.2 ) asau me smaratāditi priyo me smaratāditi |
(6,130.2 ) devāḥ pra hiṇuta smaramasau māmanu śocatu ||2||

(6,130.3 ) yathā mama smarādasau nāmuṣyāhaṃ kadā cana |
(6,130.3 ) devāḥ pra hiṇuta smaramasau māmanu śocatu ||3||

(6,130.4 ) un mādayata maruta udantarikṣa mādaya |
(6,130.4 ) agna un mādayā tvamasau māmanu śocatu ||4||



(6,131.1 ) ni śīrṣato ni pattata ādhyo ni tirāmi te |
(6,131.1 ) devāḥ pra hiṇuta smaramasau māmanu śocatu ||1||

(6,131.2 ) anumate'nvidaṃ manyasvākute samidaṃ namaḥ |
(6,131.2 ) devāḥ pra hiṇuta smaramasau māmanu śocatu ||2||

(6,131.3 ) yaddhāvasi triyojanaṃ pañcayojanamāśvinam |
(6,131.3 ) tatastvaṃ punarāyasi putrāṇāṃ no asaḥ pitā ||3||



(6,132.1 ) yaṃ devāḥ smaramasiñcann apsvantaḥ śośucānaṃ sahādhyā |
(6,132.1 ) taṃ te tapāmi varuṇasya dharmaṇā ||1||

(6,132.2 ) yaṃ viśve devāḥ smaramasiñcann apsvantaḥ śośucānaṃ sahādhyā |
(6,132.2 ) taṃ te tapāmi varuṇasya dharmaṇā ||2||

(6,132.3 ) yamindrāṇī smaramasiñcadapsvantaḥ śośucānaṃ sahādhyā |
(6,132.3 ) taṃ te tapāmi varuṇasya dharmaṇā ||3||

(6,132.4 ) yamindrāgnī smaramasiñcatāmapsvantaḥ śośucānaṃ sahādhyā |
(6,132.4 ) taṃ te tapāmi varuṇasya dharmaṇā ||4||

(6,132.5 ) yaṃ mitrāvaruṇau smaramasiñcatāmapsvantaḥ śośucānaṃ sahādhyā |
(6,132.5 ) taṃ te tapāmi varuṇasya dharmaṇā ||5||



(6,133.1 ) ya imāṃ devo mekhalāmābabandha yaḥ saṃnanāha ya u no yuyoja |
(6,133.1 ) yasya devasya praśiṣā carāmaḥ sa pāramichātsa u no vi muñcāt||1||

(6,133.2 ) āhutāsyabhihuta ṛṣīṇāmasyāyudham |
(6,133.2 ) pūrvā vratasya prāśnatī vīraghnī bhava mekhale ||2||

(6,133.3 ) mṛtyorahaṃ brahmacārī yadasmi niryācan bhūtātpuruṣaṃ yamāya |
(6,133.3 ) tamahaṃ brahmaṇā tapasā śrameṇānayainaṃ mekhalayā sināmi ||3||

(6,133.4 ) śraddhāyā duhitā tapaso'dhi jātā svasā ṛṣīṇāṃ bhūtakṛtāṃ babhūva |
(6,133.4 ) sā no mekhale matimā dhehi medhāmatho no dhehi tapa indriyaṃ ca ||4||

(6,133.5 ) yāṃ tvā pūrve bhūtakṛta ṛṣayaḥ paribedhire |
(6,133.5 ) sā tvaṃ pari ṣvajasva māṃ dīrghāyutvāya mekhale ||5||



(6,134.1 ) ayaṃ vajrastarpayatāmṛtasyāvāsya rāṣṭramapa hantu jīvitam |
(6,134.1 ) śṛṇātu grīvāḥ pra śṛṇātūṣṇihā vṛtrasyeva śacīpatiḥ ||1||

(6,134.2 ) adharo'dhara uttarebhyo gūḍhaḥ pṛthivyā motsṛpat|
(6,134.2 ) vajreṇāvahataḥ śayām ||2||

(6,134.3 ) yo jināti tamanvicha yo jināti tamijjahi |
(6,134.3 ) jinato vajra tvaṃ sīmantamanvañcamanu pātaya ||3||



(6,135.1 ) yadaśnāmi balaṃ kurva itthaṃ vajramā dade |
(6,135.1 ) skandhān amuṣya śātayan vṛtrasyeva śacīpatiḥ ||1||

(6,135.2 ) yatpibāmi saṃ pibāmi samudra iva saṃpibaḥ |
(6,135.2 ) prāṇān amuṣya saṃpāya saṃ pibāmo amuṃ vayam ||2||

(6,135.3 ) yadgirāmi saṃ girāmi samudra iva saṃgiraḥ |
(6,135.3 ) prāṇān amuṣya saṃgīrya saṃ girāmo amuṃ vayam ||3||



(6,136.1 ) devī devyāmadhi jātā pṛthivyāmasyoṣadhe |
(6,136.1 ) tāṃ tvā nitatni keśebhyo dṛṃhaṇāya khanāmasi ||1||

(6,136.2 ) dṛṃha pratnān janayājātān jātān u varṣīyasaskṛdhi ||2||

(6,136.3 ) yaste keśo'vapadyate samūlo yaśca vṛścate |
(6,136.3 ) idaṃ taṃ viśvabheṣajyābhi ṣiñcāmi vīrudhā ||3||



(6,137.1 ) yāṃ jamadagnirakhanadduhitre keśavardhanīm |
(6,137.1 ) tāṃ vītahavya ābharadasitasya gṛhebhyaḥ ||1||

(6,137.2 ) abhīśunā meyā āsan vyāmenānumeyāḥ |
(6,137.2 ) keśā naḍā iva vardhantāṃ śīrṣṇaste asitāḥ pari ||2||

(6,137.3 ) dṛṃha mūlamāgraṃ yacha vi madhyaṃ yāmayauṣadhe |
(6,137.3 ) keśā naḍā iva vardhantāṃ śīrṣṇaste asitāḥ pari ||3||



(6,138.1 ) tvaṃ vīrudhāṃ śreṣṭhatamābhiśrutāsyoṣadhe |
(6,138.1 ) imaṃ me adya puruṣaṃ klībamopaśinaṃ kṛdhi ||1||

(6,138.2 ) klībaṃ kṛdhyopaśinamatho kurīriṇaṃ kṛdhi |
(6,138.2 ) athāsyendro grāvabhyāmubhe bhinattvāṇḍyau ||2||

(6,138.3 ) klība klībaṃ tvākaraṃ vadhre vadhriṃ tvākaramarasārasaṃ tvākaram |
(6,138.3 ) kurīramasya śīrṣaṇi kumbaṃ cādhinidadhmasi ||3||

(6,138.4 ) ye te nādyau devakṛte yayostiṣṭhati vṛṣṇyam |
(6,138.4 ) te te bhinadmi śamyayāmuṣyā adhi muṣkayoḥ ||4||

(6,138.5 ) yathā naḍaṃ kaśipune striyo bhindantyaśmanā |
(6,138.5 ) evā bhinadmi te śepo'muṣyā adhi muṣkayoḥ ||5||



(6,139.1 ) nyastikā rurohitha subhagaṃkaraṇī mama |
(6,139.1 ) śataṃ tava pratānāstrayastriṃśan nitānāḥ ||
(6,139.1 ) tayā sahasraparṇyā hṛdayaṃ śoṣayāmi te ||1||

(6,139.2 ) śuṣyatu mayi te hṛdayamatho śuṣyatvāsyam |
(6,139.2 ) atho ni śuṣya māṃ kāmenātho śuṣkāsyā cara ||2||

(6,139.3 ) saṃvananī samuṣpalā babhru kalyāṇi saṃ nuda |
(6,139.3 ) amūṃ ca māṃ ca saṃ nuda samānaṃ hṛdayaṃ kṛdhi ||3||

(6,139.4 ) yathodakamapapuṣo'paśuṣyatyāsyam |
(6,139.4 ) evā ni śuṣya māṃ kāmenātho śuṣkāsyā cara ||4||

(6,139.5 ) yathā nakulo vichidya saṃdadhātyahiṃ punaḥ |
(6,139.5 ) evā kāmasya vichinnaṃ saṃ dhehi vīryāvati ||5||



(6,140.1 ) yau vyāghrāvavarūdhau jighatsataḥ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ ca |
(6,140.1 ) tau dantaṃ brahmaṇaspate śivau kṛṇu jātavedaḥ ||1||

(6,140.2 ) vrīhimattaṃ yavamattamatho māṣamatho tilam |
(6,140.2 ) eṣa vāṃ bhāgo nihito ratnadheyāya dantau mā hiṃsiṣṭaṃ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ ca ||2||

(6,140.3 ) upahūtau sayujau syonau dantau sumaṅgalau |
(6,140.3 ) anyatra vāṃ ghoraṃ tanvaḥ paraitu dantau mā hiṃsiṣṭaṃ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ ca ||3||



(6,141.1 ) vāyurenāḥ samākarattvaṣṭā poṣāya dhriyatām |
(6,141.1 ) indra ābhyo adhi bravadrudro bhūmne cikitsatu ||1||

(6,141.2 ) lohitena svadhitinā mithunaṃ karṇayoḥ kṛdhi |
(6,141.2 ) akartāmaśvinā lakṣma tadastu prajayā bahu ||2||

(6,141.3 ) yathā cakrurdevāsurā yathā manuṣyā uta |
(6,141.3 ) evā sahasrapoṣāya kṛṇutaṃ lakṣmāśvinā ||3||


(6,142.1 ) ucchrayasva bahurbhava svena mahasā yava |
(6,142.1 ) mṛṇīhi viśvā pātrāṇi mā tvā divyāśanirvadhīt||1||

(6,142.2 ) āśṛṇvantaṃ yavaṃ devaṃ yatra tvāchāvadāmasi |
(6,142.2 ) taducchrayasva dyauriva samudra ivaidhyakṣitaḥ ||2||

(6,142.3 ) akṣitāsta upasado'kṣitāḥ santu rāśayaḥ |
(6,142.3 ) pṛṇanto akṣitāḥ santvattāraḥ santvakṣitāḥ ||3||



(7,1.1 ) dhītī vā ye anayan vāco agraṃ manasā vā ye'vadann ṛtāni |
(7,1.1 ) tṛtīyena brahmaṇā vāvṛdhānāsturīyeṇāmanvata nāma dhenoḥ ||1||

(7,1.2 ) sa veda putraḥ pitaraṃ sa mātaraṃ sa sūnurbhuvatsa bhuvatpunarmaghaḥ |
(7,1.2 ) sa dyāmaurṇodantarikṣaṃ svaḥ sa idaṃ viśvamabhavatsa ābharat||2||



(7,2.1 ) atharvāṇaṃ pitaraṃ devabandhuṃ māturgarbhaṃ piturasuṃ yuvānam |
(7,2.1 ) ya imaṃ yajñaṃ manasā ciketa pra ṇo vocastamiheha bravaḥ ||1||



(7,3.1 ) ayā viṣṭhā janayan karvarāṇi sa hi ghṛṇirururvarāya gātuḥ |
(7,3.1 ) sa pratyudaiddharuṇaṃ madhvo agraṃ svayā tanvā tanvamairayata ||1||


(7,4.1 ) ekayā ca daśabhiśca suhute dvābhyāmiṣṭaye viṃśatyā ca |
(7,4.1 ) tisṛbhiśca vahase triṃśatā ca viyugbhirvāya iha tā vi muñca ||1||



(7,5.1 ) yajñena yajñamayajanta devāstāni dharmāṇi prathamānyāsan |
(7,5.1 ) te ha nākaṃ mahimānaḥ sacanta yatra pūrve sādhyāḥ santi devāḥ ||1||

(7,5.2 ) yajño babhūva sa ā babhūva sa pra jajñe sa u vāvṛdhe punaḥ |
(7,5.2 ) sa devānāmadhipatirbabhūva so asmāsu draviṇamā dadhātu ||2||

(7,5.3 ) yaddevā devān haviṣā'yajantāmartyān manasā martyena |
(7,5.3 ) madema tatra parame vyoman paśyema taduditau sūryasya ||3||

(7,5.4 ) yatpuruṣeṇa haviṣā yajñaṃ devā atanvata |
(7,5.4 ) asti nu tasmādojīyo yadvihavyenejire ||4||

(7,5.5 ) mugdhā devā uta śunā'yajantota goraṅgaiḥ purudhā'yajanta |
(7,5.5 ) ya imaṃ yajñaṃ manasā ciketa pra ṇo vocastamiheha bravaḥ ||5||



(7,6.1 ) aditirdyauraditirantarikṣamaditirmātā sa pitā sa putraḥ |
(7,6.1 ) viśve devā aditirpañca janā aditirjātamaditirjanitvam ||1||

(7,6.2 ) mahīmū ṣu mātaraṃ suvratānāmṛtasya patnīmavase havāmahe |
(7,6.2 ) tuvikṣatrāmajarantīmurūcīṃ suśarmāṇamaditiṃ supraṇītim ||2||

(7,6.3 ) sutrāmāṇaṃ pṛthivīṃ dyāmanehasaṃ suśarmāṇamaditiṃ supraṇītim |
(7,6.3 ) daivīṃ nāvaṃ svaritrāmanāgaso asravantīmā ruhemā svastaye ||1||

(7,6.4 ) vājasya nu prasave mātaraṃ mahīmaditiṃ nāma vacasā karāmahe |
(7,6.4 ) yasyā upastha urvantarikṣaṃ sā naḥ śarma trivarūthaṃ ni yachāt||2||



(7,7.1 ) diteḥ putrāṇāmaditerakāriṣamava devānāṃ bṛhatāmanarmaṇām |
(7,7.1 ) teṣāṃ hi dhāma gabhiṣaksamudriyaṃ nainān namasā paro asti kaścana ||1||


(7,8.1 ) bhadrādadhi śreyaḥ prehi bṛhaspatiḥ puraetā te astu |
(7,8.1 ) athemamasyā vara ā pṛthivyā āreśatruṃ kṛṇuhi sarvavīram ||1||



(7,9.1 ) prapathe pathāmajaniṣṭa pūṣā prapathe divaḥ prapathe pṛthivyāḥ |
(7,9.1 ) ubhe abhi priyatame sadhasthe ā ca parā ca carati prajānan ||1||

(7,9.2 ) pūṣemā āśā anu veda sarvāḥ so asmāmabhayatamena neṣat|
(7,9.2 ) svastidā āghṛṇiḥ sarvavīro'prayuchan pura etu prajānan ||2||

(7,9.3 ) pūṣan tava vrate vayaṃ na riṣyema kadā cana |
(7,9.3 ) stotārasta iha smasi ||3||

(7,9.4 ) pari pūṣā parastāddhastaṃ dadhātu dakṣiṇam |
(7,9.4 ) punarno naṣṭamājatu saṃ naṣṭena gamemahi ||4||



(7,10.1 ) yaste stanaḥ śaśayuryo mayobhūryaḥ sumnayuḥ suhavo yaḥ sudatraḥ |
(7,10.1 ) yena viśvā puṣyasi vāryāṇi sarasvati tamiha dhātave kaḥ ||1||



(7,11.1 ) yaste pṛthu stanayitnurya ṛṣvo daivaḥ keturviśvamābhūṣatīdam |
(7,11.1 ) mā no vadhīrvidyutā deva sasyaṃ mota vadhī raśmibhiḥ sūryasya ||1||



(7,12.1 ) sabhā ca mā samitiścāvatāṃ prajāpaterduhitarau saṃvidāne |
(7,12.1 ) yenā saṃgachā upa mā sa śikṣāccāru vadāni pitaraḥ saṃgateṣu ||1||

(7,12.2 ) vidma te sabhe nāma nariṣṭā nāma vā asi |
(7,12.2 ) ye te ke ca sabhāsadaste me santu savācasaḥ ||2||

(7,12.3 ) eṣāmahaṃ samāsīnānāṃ varco vijñānamā dade |
(7,12.3 ) asyāḥ sarvasyāḥ saṃsado māmindra bhaginaṃ kṛṇu ||3||

(7,12.4 ) yadvo manaḥ parāgataṃ yadbaddhamiha veha vā |
(7,12.4 ) tadva ā vartayāmasi mayi vo ramatāṃ manaḥ ||4||



(7,13.1 ) yathā sūryo nakṣatrāṇāmudyaṃstejāṃsyādade |
(7,13.1 ) evā strīṇāṃ ca puṃsāṃ ca dviṣatāṃ varca ā dade ||1||

(7,13.2 ) yāvanto mā sapatnānāmāyantaṃ pratipaśyatha |
(7,13.2 ) udyantsūrya iva suptānāṃ dviṣatāṃ varca ā dade ||2||



(7,14.1 ) abhi tyaṃ devaṃ savitāramoṇyoḥ kavikratum |
(7,14.1 ) arcāmi satyasavaṃ ratnadhāmabhi priyaṃ matim ||1||

(7,14.2 ) urdhvā yasyāmatirbhā adidyutatsavīmani |
(7,14.2 ) hiraṇyapāṇiramimīta sukratuḥ kṛpātsvaḥ ||2||

(7,14.3 ) sāvīrhi deva prathamāya pitre varṣmāṇamasmai varimāṇamasmai |
(7,14.3 ) athāsmabhyaṃ savitarvāryāṇi divodiva ā suvā bhūri paśvaḥ ||3||

(7,14.4 ) damūnā devaḥ savitā vareṇyo dadhadratnaṃ pitṛbhya āyūṃṣi |
(7,14.4 ) pibātsomaṃ mamadadenamiṣṭe parijmā citkramate asya dharmaṇi ||4||



(7,15.1 ) tāṃ savitaḥ satyasavāṃ sucitrāmāhaṃ vṛṇe sumatiṃ viśvavārām |
(7,15.1 ) yāmasya kaṇvo aduhatprapīnāṃ sahasradhārāṃ mahiṣo bhagāya ||1||



(7,16.1 ) bṛhaspate savitarvardhayainaṃ jyotayainaṃ mahate saubhagāya |
(7,16.1 ) saṃśitaṃ citsaṃtaraṃ saṃ śiśādhi viśva enamanu madantu devāḥ ||1||



(7,17.1 ) dhātā dadhātu no rayimīśāno jagataspatiḥ |
(7,17.1 ) sa naḥ pūrṇena yachatu ||1||

(7,17.2 ) dhātā dadhātu dāśuṣe prācīṃ jīvātumakṣitām |
(7,17.2 ) vayaṃ devasya dhīmahi sumatiṃ viśvarādhasaḥ ||2||

(7,17.3 ) dhātā viśvā vāryā dadhātu prajākāmāya dāśuṣe duroṇe |
(7,17.3 ) tasmai devā amṛtaṃ saṃ vyayantu viśve devā aditiḥ sajoṣāḥ ||3||

(7,17.4 ) dhātā rātiḥ savitedaṃ juṣantāṃ prajāpatirnidhipatirno agniḥ |
(7,17.4 ) tvaṣṭā viṣṇuḥ prajayā saṃrarāṇo yajamānāya draviṇaṃ dadhātu ||4||



(7,18.1 ) pra nabhasva pṛthivi bhinddhīdaṃ divyaṃ nabhaḥ |
(7,18.1 ) udno divyasya no dhātarīśāno vi ṣyā dṛtim ||1||

(7,18.2 ) na ghraṃstatāpa na himo jaghāna pra nabhatāṃ pṛthivī jīradānuḥ |
(7,18.2 ) āpaścidasmai ghṛtamitkṣaranti yatra somaḥ sadamittatra bhadram ||2||



(7,19.1 ) prajāpatirjanayati prajā imā dhātā dadhātu sumanasyamānaḥ |
(7,19.1 ) saṃjānānāḥ saṃmanasaḥ sayonayo mayi puṣṭaṃ puṣṭapatirdadhātu ||1||



(7,20.1 ) anvadya no'numatiryajñaṃ deveṣu manyatām |
(7,20.1 ) agniśca havyavāhano bhavatāṃ dāśuṣe mama ||1||

(7,20.2 ) anvidanumate tvaṃ maṃsase śaṃ ca naskṛdhi |
(7,20.2 ) juṣasva havyamāhutaṃ prajāṃ devi rarāsva naḥ ||2||

(7,20.3 ) anu manyatāmanumanyamānaḥ prajāvantaṃ rayimakṣīyamāṇam |
(7,20.3 ) tasya vayaṃ heḍasi māpi bhūma sumṛḍīke asya sumatau syāma ||3||

(7,20.4 ) yatte nāma suhavaṃ supraṇīte'numate anumataṃ sudānu |
(7,20.4 ) tenā no yajñaṃ pipṛhi viśvavāre rayiṃ no dhehi subhage suvīram ||4||

(7,20.5 ) emaṃ yajñamanumatirjagāma sukṣetratāyai suvīratāyai sujātam |
(7,20.5 ) bhadrā hyasyāḥ pramatirbabhūva semaṃ yajñamavatu devagopā ||5||

(7,20.6 ) anumatiḥ sarvamidaṃ babhūva yattiṣṭhati carati yadu ca viśvamejati |
(7,20.6 ) tasyāste devi sumatau syāmānumate anu hi maṃsase naḥ ||6||



(7,21.1 ) sameta viśve vacasā patiṃ diva eko vibhūratithirjanānām |
(7,21.1 ) sa pūrvyo nūtanamāvivāsattaṃ vartaniranu vāvṛta ekamitpuru ||1||

(7,22.1 ) ayaṃ sahasramā no dṛśe kavīnāṃ matirjyotirvidharmaṇi ||1||

(7,22.2 ) bradhnaḥ samīcīruṣasaḥ samairayan |
(7,22.2 ) arepasaḥ sacetasaḥ svasare manyumattamāścite goḥ ||2||



(7,23.1 ) dauṣvapnyaṃ daurjīvityaṃ rakṣo abhvamarāyyaḥ |
(7,23.1 ) durṇāmnīḥ sarvā durvācastā asman nāśayāmasi ||1||



(7,24.1 ) yan na indro akhanadyadagnirviśve devā maruto yatsvarkāḥ |
(7,24.1 ) tadasmabhyaṃ savitā satyadharmā prajāpatiranumatirni yachāt||1||



(7,25.1 ) yayorojasā skabhitā rajāṃsi yau vīryairvīratamā śaviṣṭhā |
(7,25.1 ) yau patyete apratītau sahobhirviṣṇumagan varuṇaṃ pūrvahūtiḥ ||1||

(7,25.2 ) yasyedaṃ pradiśi yadvirocate pra cānati vi ca caṣṭe śacībhiḥ |
(7,25.2 ) purā devasya dharmaṇā sahobhirviṣṇumagan varuṇaṃ pūrvahūtiḥ ||2||



(7,26.1 ) viṣṇornu kaṃ prā vocaṃ vīryāṇi yaḥ pārthivāni vimame rajāṃsi |
(7,26.1 ) yo askabhāyaduttaraṃ sadhasthaṃ vicakramāṇastredhorugāyaḥ ||1||

(7,26.2 ) pra tadviṣṇu stavate vīryāṇi mṛgo na bhīmaḥ kucaro giriṣṭhāḥ |
(7,26.2 ) parāvata ā jagamyātparasyāḥ ||2||

(7,26.3 ) yasyoruṣu triṣu vikramaneṣvadhikṣiyanti bhuvanāni viśvā |
(7,26.3 ) uru viṣṇo vi kramasvoru kṣayāya naskṛdhi |
(7,26.3 ) ghṛtaṃ ghṛtayone piba prapra yajñapatiṃ tira ||3||

(7,26.4 ) idaṃ viṣṇurvi cakrame tredhā ni dadhe padā |
(7,26.4 ) samūḍhamasya paṃsure ||4||

(7,26.5 ) trīṇi padā vi cakrame viṣṇurgopā adābhyaḥ |
(7,26.5 ) ito dharmāṇi dhārayan ||5||

(7,26.6 ) viṣṇoḥ karmāṇi paśyata yato vratāni paspaśe |
(7,26.6 ) indrasya yujyaḥ sakhā ||6||

(7,26.7 ) tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ sadā paśyanti sūrayaḥ |
(7,26.7 ) divīva cakṣurātatam ||7||

(7,26.8 ) divo viṣṇa uta pṛthivyā maho viṣṇa urorantarikṣāt|
(7,26.8 ) hastau pṛṇasva bahubhirvasavyairāprayacha dakṣiṇādota savyāt||8||


(7,27.1 ) iḍaivāsmāmanu vastāṃ vratena yasyāḥ pade punate devayantaḥ |
(7,27.1 ) ghṛtapadī śakvarī somapṛṣṭhopa yajñamasthita vaiśvadevī ||1||


(7,28.1 ) vedaḥ svastirdrughaṇaḥ svastiḥ paraśurvediḥ paraśurnaḥ svasti |
(7,28.1 ) haviṣkṛto yajñiyā yajñakāmāste devāso yajñamimaṃ juṣantām ||1||



(7,29.1 ) agnāviṣṇū mahi tadvāṃ mahitvaṃ pātho ghṛtasya guhyasya nāma |
(7,29.1 ) damedame sapta ratnā dadhānau prati vāṃ jihvā ghṛtamā caraṇyāt||1||

(7,29.2 ) agnāviṣṇū mahi dhāma priyaṃ vāṃ vītho ghṛtasya guhyā juṣāṇau |
(7,29.2 ) damedame suṣṭutyā vāvṛdhānau prati vāṃ jihvā ghṛtamuccaraṇyāt||2||



(7,30.1 ) svāktaṃ me dyāvāpṛthivī svāktaṃ mitro akarayam |
(7,30.1 ) svāktaṃ me brahmaṇaspatiḥ svāktaṃ savitā karat||1||


(7,31.1 ) indrotibhirbahulābhirno adya yāvacchreṣṭhābhirmaghavan chūra jinva |
(7,31.1 ) yo no dveṣṭyadhara saspadīṣṭa yamu dviṣmastamu prāṇo jahātu ||1||



(7,32.1 ) upa priyaṃ panipnataṃ yuvānamāhutīvṛdham |
(7,32.1 ) aganma bibhrato namo dīrghamāyuḥ kṛṇotu me ||1||



(7,33.1 ) saṃ mā siñcantu marutaḥ saṃ pūṣā saṃ bṛhaspatiḥ |
(7,33.1 ) saṃ māyamagniḥ siñcatu prajayā ca dhanena ca dīrghamāyuḥ kṛṇotu me ||1||



(7,34.1 ) agne jātān pra ṇudā me sapatnān pratyajātān jātavedo nudasva |
(7,34.1 ) adhaspadaṃ kṛṇuṣva ye pṛtanyavo'nāgasaste vayamaditaye syāma ||1||



(7,35.1 ) prānyāntsapatnāntsahasā sahasva pratyajātān jātavedo nudasva |
(7,35.1 ) idaṃ rāṣṭraṃ pipṛhi saubhagāya viśva enamanu madantu devāḥ ||1||

(7,35.2 ) imā yāste śataṃ hirāḥ sahasraṃ dhamanīruta |
(7,35.2 ) tāsāṃ te sarvāsāmahamaśmanā bilamapyadhām ||2||

(7,35.3 ) paraṃ yoneravaraṃ te kṛṇomi mā tvā prajābhi bhūn mota sūtuḥ |
(7,35.3 ) asvaṃ tvāprajasaṃ kṛṇomyaśmānaṃ te apidhānaṃ kṛṇomi ||3||



(7,36.1 ) akṣyau nau madhusaṃkāśe anīkaṃ nau samañjanam |
(7,36.1 ) antaḥ kṛṣṇuṣva māṃ hṛdi mana in nau sahāsati ||1||



(7,37.1 ) abhi tvā manujātena dadhāmi mama vāsasā |
(7,37.1 ) yāthā'so mama kevalo nānyāsāṃ kīrtayāścana ||1||


(7,38.1 ) idaṃ khanāmi bheṣajaṃ māṃpaśyamabhirorudam |
(7,38.1 ) parāyato nivartanamāyataḥ pratinandanam ||1||
(7,38.2 ) yenā nicakra āsurīndraṃ devebhyaspari |
(7,38.2 ) tenā ni kurve tvāmahaṃ yathā te'sāni supriyā ||2||

(7,38.3 ) pratīcī somamasi pratīcī uta sūryam |
(7,38.3 ) pratīcī viśvān devān tāṃ tvāchāvadāmasi ||3||

(7,38.4 ) ahaṃ vadāmi nettvaṃ sabhāyāmaha tvaṃ vada |
(7,38.4 ) mamedasastvaṃ kevalo nānyāsāṃ kīrtayāścana ||4||
(7,38.5 ) yadi vāsi tirojanaṃ yadi vā nadyastiraḥi |
(7,38.5 ) iyaṃ ha mahyaṃ tvāmoṣadhirbaddhveva nyānayat||5||



(7,39.1 ) divyaṃ suparṇaṃ payasaṃ bṛhantamapāṃ garbhaṃ vṛṣabhamoṣadhīnām |
(7,39.1 ) abhīpato vṛṣṭyā tarpayantamā no goṣṭhe rayiṣṭhāṃ sthāpayāti ||1||


(7,40.1 ) yasya vrataṃ paśavo yanti sarve yasya vrata upatiṣṭhanta āpaḥ |
(7,40.1 ) yasya vrate puṣṭapatirniviṣṭastaṃ sarasvantamavase havāmahe ||1||

(7,40.2 ) ā pratyañcaṃ dāśuṣe dāśvaṃsaṃ sarasvantaṃ puṣṭapatiṃ rayiṣṭhām |
(7,40.2 ) rāyaspoṣaṃ śravasyuṃ vasānā iha sadanaṃ rayīṇām ||2||



(7,41.1 ) ati dhanvānyatyapastatarda śyeno nṛcakṣā avasānadarśaḥ |
(7,41.1 ) taran viśvānyavarā rajaṃsīndreṇa sakhyā śiva ā jagamyāt||1||

(7,41.2 ) śyeno nṛcakṣā divyaḥ suparṇaḥ sahasrapācchatayonirvayodhāḥ |
(7,41.2 ) sa no ni yachādvasu yatparābhṛtamasmākamastu pitṛṣu svadhāvat||2||



(7,42.1 ) somārudrā vi vṛhataṃ viṣūcīmamīvā yā no gayamāviveśa |
(7,42.1 ) bādhethāṃ dūraṃ nirṛtiṃ parācaiḥ kṛtaṃ cidenaḥ pra mumuktamasmat||1||

(7,42.2 ) somārudrā yuvametānyasmadviśvā tanūṣu bheṣajāni dhattam |
(7,42.2 ) ava syataṃ muñcataṃ yan no asattanūṣu baddhaṃ kṛtameno asmat||2||



(7,43.1 ) śivāsta ekā aśivāsta ekāḥ sarvā bibharṣi sumanasyamānaḥ |
(7,43.1 ) tisro vāco nihitā antarasmin tāsāmekā vi papātānu ghoṣam ||1||



(7,44.1 ) ubhā jigyathurna parā jayethe na parā jigye kataraścanainayoḥ |
(7,44.1 ) indraśca viṣṇo yadapaspṛdhethāṃ tredhā sahasraṃ vi tadairayethām ||1||



(7,45.1 ) janādviśvajanīnātsindhutasparyābhṛtam |
(7,45.1 ) dūrāttvā manya udbhṛtamīrṣyāyā nāma bheṣajam ||1||

(7,45.2 ) agnerivāsya dahato dāvasya dahataḥ pṛthak|
(7,45.2 ) etāmetasyerṣyāmudrāgnimiva śamaya ||1||


(7,46.1 ) sinīvāli pṛthuṣṭuke yā devānāmasi svasā |
(7,46.1 ) juṣasva havyamāhutaṃ prajāṃ devi didiḍḍhi naḥ ||1||

(7,46.2 ) yā subāhuḥ svaṅguriḥ suṣūmā bahusūvarī |
(7,46.2 ) tasyai viśpatnyai haviḥ sinīvālyai juhotana ||2||

(7,46.3 ) yā viśpatnīndramasi pratīcī sahasrastukābhiyantī devī |
(7,46.3 ) viṣṇoḥ patni tubhyaṃ rātā havīṃṣi patiṃ devi rādhase codayasva ||3||



(7,47.1 ) kuhūṃ devīṃ sukṛtaṃ vidmanāpasamasmin yajñe suhavā johavīmi |
(7,47.1 ) sā no rayiṃ viśvavāraṃ ni yachāddadātu vīraṃ śatadāyamukthyam ||1||

(7,47.2 ) kuhūrdevānāmamṛtasya patnī havyā no asya haviṣo juṣeta |
(7,47.2 ) śṛnotu yajñamuśatī no adya rāyaspoṣaṃ cikituṣī dadhātu ||2||



(7,48.1 ) rākāmahaṃ suhavā suṣṭutī huve śṛṇotu naḥ subhagā bodhatu tmanā |
(7,48.1 ) sīvyatvapaḥ sūcyāchidyamānayā dadātu vīraṃ śatadāyamukthyam ||1||

(7,48.2 ) yāste rāke sumatayaḥ supeśaso yābhirdadāsi dāśuṣe vasūni |
(7,48.2 ) tābhirno adya sumanā upāgahi sahasrāpoṣaṃ subhage rarāṇā ||2||



(7,49.1 ) devānāṃ patnīruśatīravantu naḥ prāvantu nastujaye vājasātaye |
(7,49.1 ) yāḥ pārthivāso yā apāmapi vrate tā no devīḥ suhavāḥ śarma yachantu ||1||

(7,49.2 ) uta gnā vyantu devapatnīrindrāṇyagnāyyaśvinī rāṭ|
(7,49.2 ) ā rodasī varunānī śṛṇotu vyantu devīrya ṛturjanīnām ||2||



(7,50.1 ) yathā vṛkṣamaśanirviśvāhā hantyaprati |
(7,50.1 ) evāhamadya kitavān akṣairbadhyāsamaprati ||1||

(7,50.2 ) turāṇāmaturāṇāṃ viśāmavarjuṣīṇām |
(7,50.2 ) samaitu viśvato bhago antarhastaṃ kṛtaṃ mama ||2||

(7,50.3 ) īḍe agniṃ svāvasuṃ namobhiriha prasakto vi cayatkṛtaṃ naḥ |
(7,50.3 ) rathairiva pra bhare vājayadbhiḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ marutāṃ stomamṛdhyām ||3||

(7,50.4 ) vayaṃ jayema tvayā yujā vṛtamasmākamaṃśamudava bharebhare |
(7,50.4 ) asmabhyamindra varīyaḥ sugaṃ kṛdhi pra śatrūṇāṃ maghavan vṛṣṇyā ruja ||4||

(7,50.5 ) ajaiṣaṃ tvā saṃlikhitamajaiṣamuta saṃrudham |
(7,50.5 ) aviṃ vṛko yathā mathadevā mathnāmi te kṛtam ||5||

(7,50.6 ) uta prahāmatidīvā jayati kṛtamiva śvaghnī vi cinoti kāle |
(7,50.6 ) yo devakāmo na dhanaṃ ruṇaddhi samittaṃ rāyaḥ sṛjati svadhābhiḥ ||6||

(7,50.7 ) gobhiṣṭaremāmatiṃ durevāṃ yavena vā kṣudhaṃ puruhūta viśve |
(7,50.7 ) vayaṃ rājasu prathamā dhanānyariṣṭāso vṛjanībhirjayema ||7||

(7,50.8 ) kṛtaṃ me dakṣiṇe haste jayo me savya āhitaḥ |
(7,50.8 ) gojidbhūyāsamaśvajiddhanaṃjayo hiraṇyajit||8||

(7,50.9 ) akṣāḥ phalavatīṃ dyuvaṃ datta gāṃ kṣīriṇīmiva |
(7,50.9 ) saṃ mā kṛtasya dhārayā dhanuḥ snāvneva nahyata ||9||



(7,51.1 ) bṛhaspatirnaḥ pari pātu paścādutottarasmādadharādaghayoḥ |
(7,51.1 ) indraḥ purastāduta madhyato naḥ sakhā sakhibhyo varīyaḥ kṛṇotu ||1||



(7,52.1 ) saṃjñānaṃ naḥ svebhiḥ saṃjñānamaraṇebhiḥ |
(7,52.1 ) saṃjñānamaśvinā yuvamihāsmāsu ni yachatam ||1||

(7,52.2 ) saṃ jānāmahai manasā saṃ cikitvā mā yuṣmahi manasā daivyena |
(7,52.2 ) mā ghoṣā utsthurbahule vinirhate meṣuḥ paptadindrasyāhanyāgate ||2||



(7,53.1 ) amutrabhūyādadhi yadyamasya bṛhaspate abhiśasteramuñcaḥ |
(7,53.1 ) pratyauhatāmaśvinā mṛtyumasmaddevānāmagne bhiṣajā śacībhiḥ ||1||

(7,53.2 ) saṃ krāmataṃ mā jahītaṃ śarīraṃ prāṇāpānau te sayujāviha stām |
(7,53.2 ) śataṃ jīva śarado vardhamāno'gniṣṭe gopā adhipā vasiṣṭhaḥ ||2||

(7,53.3 ) āyuryatte atihitaṃ parācairapānaḥ prāṇaḥ punarā tāvitām |
(7,53.3 ) agniṣṭadāhārnirṛterupasthāttadātmani punarā veśayāmi te ||3||

(7,53.4 ) memaṃ prāṇo hāsīn mo apāno'vahāya parā gāt|
(7,53.4 ) saptarṣibhya enaṃ pari dadāmi te enaṃ svasti jarase vahantu ||4||

(7,53.5 ) pra viṣataṃ prāṇāpānāvanaḍvāhāviva vrajam |
(7,53.5 ) ayaṃ jarimnaḥ śevadhirariṣṭa iha vardhatām ||5||

(7,53.6 ) ā te prāṇaṃ suvāmasi parā yakṣmaṃ suvāmi te |
(7,53.6 ) āyurno viśvato dadhadayamagnirvareṇyaḥ ||6||

(7,53.7 ) udvayaṃ tamasaspari rohanto nākamuttamam |
(7,53.7 ) devaṃ devatrā sūryamaganma jyotiruttamam ||7||



(7,54.1 ) ṛcaṃ sāma yajāmahe yābhyāṃ karmāṇi kurvate |
(7,54.1 ) ete sadasi rājato yajñaṃ deveṣu yachataḥ ||1||

(7,54.2 ) ṛcaṃ sāma yadaprākṣaṃ havirojo yajurbalam |
(7,54.2 ) eṣa mā tasmān mā hiṃsīdvedaḥ pṛṣṭaḥ śacīpate ||1||



(7,55.1 ) ye te panthāno'va divo yebhirviśvamairayaḥ |
(7,55.1 ) tebhiḥ sumnayā dhehi no vaso ||2||



(7,56.1 ) tiraścirājerasitātpṛdākoḥ pari saṃbhṛtam |
(7,56.1 ) tatkaṅkaparvaṇo viṣamiyaṃ vīrudanīnaśat||1||

(7,56.2 ) iyaṃ vīrun madhujātā madhuścun madhulā madhūḥ |
(7,56.2 ) sā vihrutasya bheṣajyatho maśakajambhanī ||2||

(7,56.3 ) yato daṣṭaṃ yato dhītaṃ tataste nirhvayāmasi |
(7,56.3 ) arbhasya tṛpradaṃśino maśakasyārasaṃ viṣam ||3||

(7,56.4 ) ayaṃ yo vakro viparurvyaṅgo mukhāni vakrā vṛjinā kṛṇoṣi |
(7,56.4 ) tāni tvaṃ brahmaṇaspate iṣīkāmiva saṃ namaḥ ||4||

(7,56.5 ) arasasya śarkoṭasya nīcīnasyopasarpataḥ |
(7,56.5 ) viṣaṃ hyasyādiṣyatho enamajījabham ||5||

(7,56.6 ) na te bāhvorbalamasti na śīrṣe nota madhyataḥ |
(7,56.6 ) atha kiṃ pāpayā'muyā puche bibharṣyarbhakam ||6||

(7,56.7 ) adanti tvā pipīlikā vi vṛścanti mayūryaḥ |
(7,56.7 ) sarve bhala bravātha śārkoṭamarasaṃ viṣam ||7||

(7,56.8 ) ya ubhābhyāṃ praharasi puchena cāsyena ca |
(7,56.8 ) āsye na te viṣaṃ kimu te puchadhāvasat||8||



(7,57.1 ) yadāśasā vadato me vicukṣubhe yadyācamānasya carato janāmanu |
(7,57.1 ) yadātmani tanvo me viriṣṭaṃ sarasvatī tadā pṛṇadghṛtena ||1||

(7,57.2 ) sapta kṣaranti siśave marutvate pitre putrāso apyavīvṛtann ṛtāni |
(7,57.2 ) ubhe idasyobhe asya rājata ubhe yatete ubhe asya puṣyataḥ ||2||



(7,58.1 ) indrāvaruṇā sutapāvimaṃ sutaṃ somaṃ pibataṃ madyaṃ dhṛtavratau |
(7,58.1 ) yuvo ratho adhvaro devavītaye prati svasaramupa yātu pītaye ||1||

(7,58.2 ) indrāvaruṇā madhumattamasya vṛṣṇaḥ somasya vṛṣaṇā vṛṣethām |
(7,58.2 ) idaṃ vāmandhaḥ pariṣiktamāsadyāsmin barhiṣi mādayethām ||2||



(7,59.1 ) yo naḥ śapādaśapataḥ śapato yaśca naḥ śapāt|
(7,59.1 ) vṛkṣa iva vidyutā hata ā mūlādanu śuṣyatu ||1||



(7,60.1 ) ūrjaṃ bibhradvasuvaniḥ sumedhā aghoreṇa cakṣuṣā mitriyeṇa |
(7,60.1 ) gṛhān aimi sumanā vandamāno ramadhvaṃ mā bibhīta mat||1||

(7,60.2 ) ime gṛhā mayobhuva ūrjasvantaḥ payasvantaḥ |
(7,60.2 ) pūrṇā vāmena tiṣṭhantaste no jānantvāyataḥ ||2||

(7,60.3 ) yeṣāmadhyeti pravasan yeṣu saumanaso bahuḥ |
(7,60.3 ) gṛhān upa hvayāmahe te no jānantvāyataḥ ||3||

(7,60.4 ) upahūtā bhūridhanāḥ sakhāyaḥ svādusaṃmudaḥ |
(7,60.4 ) akṣudhyā atṛṣyā sta gṛhā māsmadbibhītana ||4||

(7,60.5 ) upahūtā iha gāva upahūtā ajāvayaḥ |
(7,60.5 ) atho annasya kīlāla upahūto gṛheṣu ||5||

(7,60.6 ) sūnṛtāvantaḥ subhagā irāvanto hasāmudāḥ |
(7,60.6 ) atṛṣyā akṣudhyā sta gṛhā māsmadbibhītana ||6||

(7,60.7 ) ihaiva sta mānu gāta viśvā rūpāṇi puṣyata |
(7,60.7 ) aiṣyāmi bhadreṇā saha bhūyāṃso bhavatā mayā ||7||



(7,61.1 ) yadagne tapasā tapa upatapyāmahe tapaḥ |
(7,61.1 ) priyāḥ śrutasya bhūyāsmāyuṣmantaḥ sumedhasaḥ ||1||

(7,61.2 ) agne tapastapyāmaha upa tapyāmahe tapaḥ |
(7,61.2 ) śrutāni śṛṇvantaḥ vayamāyuṣmantaḥ sumedhasaḥ ||2||


(7,62.1 ) ayamagniḥ satpatirvṛddhavṛṣṇo rathīva pattīn ajayatpurohitaḥ |
(7,62.1 ) nābhā pṛthivyāṃ nihito davidyutadadhaspadaṃ kṛṇutāṃ ye pṛtanyavaḥ ||1||



(7,63.1 ) pṛtanājitaṃ sahamānamagnimukthyairhavāmahe paramātsadhasthāt|
(7,63.1 ) sa naḥ parṣadati durgāṇi viśvā kṣāmaddevo'ti duritānyagniḥ ||1||



(7,64.1 ) idaṃ yatkṛṣṇaḥ śakunirabhiniṣpatann apīpatat|
(7,64.1 ) āpo mā tasmātsarvasmādduritātpāntvaṃhasaḥ ||1||

(7,64.2 ) idaṃ yatkṛṣṇaḥ śakuniravāmṛkṣan nirṛte te mukhena |
(7,64.2 ) agnirmā tasmādenaso gārhapatyaḥ pra muñcatu ||2||


(7,65.1 ) pratīcīnaphalo hi tvamapāmārga rurohitha |
(7,65.1 ) sarvān macchapathāmadhi varīyo yavayā itaḥ ||1||

(7,65.2 ) yadduṣkṛtaṃ yacchamalaṃ yadvā cerima pāpayā |
(7,65.2 ) tvayā tadviśvatomukhāpāmārgāpa mṛjmahe ||2||

(7,65.3 ) śyāvadatā kunakhinā baṇḍena yatsahāsima |
(7,65.3 ) apāmārga tvayā vayaṃ sarvaṃ tadapa mṛjmahe ||3||


(7,66.1 ) yadyantarikṣe yadi vāta āsa yadi vṛkṣeṣu yadi volapeṣu |
(7,66.1 ) yadaśravan paśava udyamānaṃ tadbrāhmaṇaṃ punarasmān upaitu ||1||



(7,67.1 ) punarmaitvindriyaṃ punarātmā draviṇaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ ca |
(7,67.1 ) punaragnayo dhiṣṇyā yathāsthāma kalpayantāmihaiva ||1||


(7,68.1 ) sarasvati vrateṣu te divyeṣu devi dhāmasu |
(7,68.1 ) juṣasva havyamāhutaṃ prajāṃ devi rarāsva naḥ ||1||

(7,68.2 ) idaṃ te havyaṃ ghṛtavatsarasvatīdaṃ pitṝṇāṃ havirāsyaṃ yat|
(7,68.2 ) imāni ta uditā śamtamāni tebhirvayaṃ madhumantaḥ syāma ||2||

(7,68.3 ) śivā naḥ śaṃtamā bhava sumṛḍīkā sarasvati |
