(7,68.3 ) mā te yuyoma saṃdṛśaḥ ||1||



(7,69.1 ) śaṃ no vāto vātu śaṃ nastapatu sūryaḥ |
(7,69.1 ) ahāni śaṃ bhavantu naḥ śaṃ rātrī prati dhīyatām |
(7,69.1 ) śamuṣā no vyuchatu ||1||


(7,70.1 ) yatkiṃ cāsau manasā yacca vācā yajñairjuhoti haviṣā yajuṣā |
(7,70.1 ) tan mṛtyunā nirṛtiḥ saṃvidānā purā satyādāhutiṃ hantvasya ||1||

(7,70.2 ) yātudhānā nirṛtirādu rakṣaste asya ghnantvanṛtena satyam |
(7,70.2 ) indreṣitā devā ājamasya mathnantu mā tatsaṃ pādi yadasau juhoti ||2||

(7,70.3 ) ajirādhirājau śyenau saṃpātināviva |
(7,70.3 ) ājyaṃ pṛtanyato hatāṃ yo naḥ kaścābhyaghāyati ||3||

(7,70.4 ) apāñcau ta ubhau bāhū api nahyāmyāsyam |
(7,70.4 ) agnerdevasya manyunā tena te'vadhiṣaṃ haviḥ ||4||
(7,70.5 ) api nahyāmi te bāhū api nahyāmyāsyam |
(7,70.5 ) agnerghorasya manyunā tena'vadhiṣaṃ haviḥ ||5||



(7,71.1 ) pari tvāgne puraṃ vayaṃ vipraṃ sahasya dhīmahi |
(7,71.1 ) dhṛṣadvarṇaṃ divedive hantāraṃ bhaṅgurāvataḥ ||1||



(7,72.1 ) uttiṣṭhatāva paśyatendrasya bhāgamṛtviyam |
(7,72.1 ) yadi śrātaṃ juhotana yadyaśrātaṃ mamattana ||1||

(7,72.2 ) śrātaṃ haviro ṣvindra pra yāhi jagāma sūro adhvano vi madhyam |
(7,72.2 ) pari tvāsate nidhibhiḥ sakhāyaḥ kulapā na vrājapatiṃ carantam ||2||

(7,72.3 ) śrātaṃ manya ūdhani śrātamagnau suśṛtaṃ manye tadṛtaṃ navīyaḥ |
(7,72.3 ) mādhyandinasya savanasya dadhnaḥ pibendra vajrin purukṛjjuṣāṇaḥ ||1||


(7,73.1 ) samiddho agnirvṛṣaṇā rathī divastapto gharmo duhyate vāmiṣe madhu |
(7,73.1 ) vayaṃ hi vāṃ purudamāso aśvinā havāmahe sadhamādeṣu kāravaḥ ||1||

(7,73.2 ) samiddho agniraśvinā tapto vāṃ gharma ā gatam |
(7,73.2 ) duhyante nūnaṃ vṛṣaṇeha dhenavo dasrā madanti vedhasaḥ ||2||

(7,73.3 ) ivāhākṛtaḥ śucirdeveṣu yajño yo aśvinoścamaso devapānaḥ |
(7,73.3 ) tamu viśve amṛtāso juṣāṇā gandharvasya pratyāsnā rihanti ||3||

(7,73.4 ) yadusriyāsvāhutaṃ ghṛtaṃ payo'yaṃ sa vāmaśvinā bhāga ā gatam |
(7,73.4 ) mādhvī dhartārā vidathasya satpatī taptaṃ gharmaṃ pibataṃ divaḥ ||4||

(7,73.5 ) tapto vāṃ gharmo nakṣatu svahotā pra vāmadhvaryuścaratu payasvān |
(7,73.5 ) madhordugdhasyāśvinā tanāyā vītaṃ pātaṃ payasa usriyāyāḥ ||5||

(7,73.6 ) upa drava payasā godhugoṣamā gharme siñca paya usriyāyāḥ |
(7,73.6 ) vi nākamakhyatsavitā vareṇyo'nuprayāṇamuṣaso vi rājati ||6||

(7,73.7 ) upa hvaye sudughāṃ dhenumetāṃ suhasto godhuguta dohadenām |
(7,73.7 ) śreṣṭhaṃ savaṃ savitā sāviṣan no'bhīddho gharmastadu ṣu pra vocat||7||

(7,73.8 ) hiṅkṛṇvatī vasupatnī vasūnāṃ vatsamichantī manasā nyāgan |
(7,73.8 ) duhāmaśvibhyāṃ payo aghnyeyaṃ sā vardhatāṃ mahate saubhagāya ||8||

(7,73.9 ) juṣṭo damūnā atithirduroṇa imaṃ no yajñamupa yāhi vidvān |
(7,73.9 ) viśvā agne abhiyujo vihatya śatrūyatāmā bharā bhojanāni ||9||

(7,73.10 ) agne śardha mahate saubhagāya tava dyumnānyuttamāni santu |
(7,73.10 ) saṃ jāspatyaṃ suyamamā kṛṇuṣva śatrūyatāmabhi tiṣṭhā mahāṃsi ||10||

(7,73.11 ) sūyavasādbhagavatī hi bhūyā adhā vayaṃ bhagavantaḥ syāma |
(7,73.11 ) addhi tṛṇamaghnye viśvadānīṃ piba śuddhamudakamācarantī ||11||



(7,74.1 ) apacitāṃ lohinīnāṃ kṛṣṇā māteti śuśruma |
(7,74.1 ) munerdevasya mūlena sarvā vidhyāmi tā aham ||1||

(7,74.2 ) vidhyāmyāsāṃ prathamāṃ vidhyāmi uta madhyamām |
(7,74.2 ) idaṃ jaghanyāmāsāmā chinadmi stukāmiva ||2||

(7,74.3 ) tvāṣṭreṇāhaṃ vacasā vi ta īrṣyāmamīmadam |
(7,74.3 ) atho yo manyuṣṭe pate tamu te śamayāmasi ||3||

(7,74.4 ) vratena tvaṃ vratapate samakto viśvāhā sumanā dīdihīha |
(7,74.4 ) taṃ tvā vayaṃ jātavedaḥ samiddhaṃ prajāvanta upa sadema sarve ||4||



(7,75.1 ) prajāvatīḥ sūyavase ruśantīḥ śuddhā apaḥ suprapāṇe pibantīḥ |
(7,75.1 ) mā va stena īśata māghaśaṃsaḥ pari vo rudrasya hetirvṛṇaktu ||1||

(7,75.2 ) padajñā stha ramatayaḥ saṃhitā viśvanāmnīḥ |
(7,75.2 ) upa mā devīrdevebhireta |
(7,75.2 ) imaṃ goṣṭhamidaṃ sado ghṛtenāsmāntsamukṣata ||2||



(7,76.1 ) ā susrasaḥ susraso asatībhyo asattarāḥ |
(7,76.1 ) sehorarasatarā havaṇādvikledīyasīḥ ||1||

(7,76.2 ) yā graivyā apacito'tho yā upapakṣyāḥ |
(7,76.2 ) vijāmni yā apacitaḥ svayaṃsrasaḥ ||2||

(7,76.3 ) yaḥ kīkasāḥ praśṛṇāti talīdyamavatiṣṭhati |
(7,76.3 ) nirhāstaṃ sarvaṃ jāyānyaṃ yaḥ kaśca kakudi śritaḥ ||3||

(7,76.4 ) pakṣī jāyānyaḥ patati sa ā viśati pūruṣam |
(7,76.4 ) tadakṣitasya bheṣajamubhayoḥ sukṣatasya ca ||4||

(7,76.5 ) vidma vai te jāyānya jānaṃ yato jāyānya jāyase |
(7,76.5 ) kathaṃ ha tatra tvaṃ hano yasya kṛṇmo havirgṛhe ||1||

(7,76.6 ) dhṛṣatpiba kalaśe somamindra vṛtrahā śūra samare vasūnām |
(7,76.6 ) mādhyandine savana ā vṛṣasva rayiṣṭhāno rayimasmāsu dhehi ||2||



(7,77.1 ) sāṃtapanā idaṃ havirmarutastajjujuṣṭana |
(7,77.1 ) asmākotī riśādasaḥ ||1||

(7,77.2 ) yo no marto maruto durhṛṇāyustiraścittāni vasavo jighāṃsati |
(7,77.2 ) druhaḥ pāśān prati muñcatāṃ sastapiṣṭhena tapasā hantanā tam ||2||

(7,77.3 ) samvatsarīṇā marutaḥ svarkā urukṣayāḥ sagaṇā mānuṣāsaḥ |
(7,77.3 ) te asmatpāśān pra muñcantvenasassāṃtapanā matsarā mādayiṣṇavaḥ ||3||



(7,78.1 ) vi te muñcāmi raśanāṃ vi yoktraṃ vi niyojanam |
(7,78.1 ) ihaiva tvamajasra edhyagne ||1||

(7,78.2 ) asmai kṣatrāṇi dhārayantamagne yunajmi tvā brahmaṇā daivyena |
(7,78.2 ) dīdihyasmabhyaṃ draviṇeha bhadraṃ premaṃ voco havirdāṃ devatāsu ||2||



(7,79.1 ) yatte devā akṛṇvan bhāgadheyamamāvāsye saṃvasanto mahitvā |
(7,79.1 ) tenā no yajñaṃ pipṛhi viśvavāre rayiṃ no dhehi subhage suvīram ||1||

(7,79.2 ) ahamevāsmyamāvāsyā māmā vasanti sukṛto mayīme |
(7,79.2 ) mayi devā ubhaye sādyāścendrajyeṣṭhāḥ samagachanta sarve ||2||

(7,79.3 ) āgan rātrī saṅgamanī vasūnāmūrjaṃ puṣṭaṃ vasvāveśayantī |
(7,79.3 ) amāvāsyāyai haviṣa vidhemorjaṃ duhānā payasā na āgan ||3||

(7,79.4 ) amāvāsye na tvadetānyanyo viśvā rūpāṇi paribhūrjajāna |
(7,79.4 ) yatkāmāste juhumastan no astu vayaṃ syāma patayo rayiṇām ||4||



(7,80.1 ) paurṇamāsī jigāya |
(7,80.1 ) tasyāṃ devaiḥ saṃvasanto mahitvā nākasya pṛṣṭhe samiṣā madema ||1||

(7,80.2 ) vṛṣabhaṃ vājinaṃ vayaṃ paurṇamāsaṃ yajāmahe |
(7,80.2 ) sa no dadātvakṣitāṃ rayimanupadasvatīm ||2||

(7,80.3 ) prajāpate na tvadetānyanyo viśvā rūpāṇi paribhūrjajāna |
(7,80.3 ) yatkāmāste juhumastan no astu vayaṃ syāma patayo rayīṇām ||3||

(7,80.4 ) paurṇamāsī prathamā yajñiyāsīdahnāṃ rātrīṇāmatiśarvareṣu |
(7,80.4 ) ye tvāṃ yajñairyajñiye ardhayantyamī te nāke sukṛtaḥ praviṣṭāḥ ||4||



(7,81.1 ) pūrvāparaṃ carato mayayaitau śiśū krīḍantau pari yāto'rṇavam |
(7,81.1 ) viśvānyo bhuvanā vicaṣṭa ṛtūṃranyo vidadhajjāyase navaḥ ||1||

(7,81.2 ) navonavo bhavasi jāyamāno'hnāṃ keturuṣasāmeṣyagram |
(7,81.2 ) bhāgaṃ devebhyo vi dadhāsyāyan pra candramastirase dhīrghamāyuḥ ||2||

(7,81.3 ) somasyāmśo yudhāṃ pate'nūno nāma vā asi |
(7,81.3 ) anūnaṃ darśa mā kṛdhi prajayā ca dhanena ca ||3||

(7,81.4 ) darśo'si darśato'si samagro'si samantaḥ |
(7,81.4 ) samagraḥ samanto bhūyāsaṃ gobhiraśvaiḥ prajayā paśubhirgṛhairdhanena ||4||

(7,81.5 ) yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmastasya tvaṃ prāṇenā pyāyasva |
(7,81.5 ) ā vayaṃ pyāsiṣīmahi gobhiraśvaiḥ prajayā paśubhirgṛhairdhanena ||5||

(7,81.6 ) yaṃ devā aṃśumāpyāyayanti yamakṣitamakṣitā bhakṣayanti |
(7,81.6 ) tenāsmān indro varuṇo bṛhaspatirā pyāyayantu bhuvanasya gopāḥ ||6||



(7,82.1 ) abhyarcata suṣṭutiṃ gavyamājimasmāsu bhadrā draviṇāni dhatta |
(7,82.1 ) imaṃ yajñaṃ nayata devatā no ghṛtasya dhārā madhumatpavantām ||1||

(7,82.2 ) mayyagre agniṃ gṛhṇāmi saha kṣatreṇa varcasā balena |
(7,82.2 ) mayi prajāṃ mayyāyurdadhāmi svāhā mayyagnim ||2||

(7,82.3 ) ihaivāgne adhydhārayā rayiṃ mā tvā ni kran pūrvacittā nikāriṇaḥ |
(7,82.3 ) kṣatreṇāgne suyamamastu tubhyamupasattā vardhatāṃ te aniṣṭṛtaḥ ||3||

(7,82.4 ) anvagniruṣasāmagramakhyadanvahāni prathamo jātavedāḥ |
(7,82.4 ) anu sūrya uṣaso anu raśmīn anu dyāvāpṛthivī ā viveśa ||4||

(7,82.5 ) pratyagniruṣasāmagramakhyatprati ahāni prathamo jātavedāḥ |
(7,82.5 ) prati sūryasya purudhā ca raśmīn prati dyāvāpṛthivī ā tatāna ||5||

(7,82.6 ) ghṛtaṃ te agne divye sadhasthe ghṛtena tvāṃ manuradyā samindhe |
(7,82.6 ) ghṛtaṃ te devīrnaptya ā vahantu ghṛtaṃ tubhyaṃ duhratāṃ gāvo agne ||6||



(7,83.1 ) apsu te rājan varuṇa gṛho hiraṇyayo mitaḥ |
(7,83.1 ) tato dhṛtavrato rājā sarvā dhāmāni muñcatu ||1||

(7,83.2 ) dāmnodāmno rājann ito varuṇa muñca naḥ |
(7,83.2 ) yadāpo aghnyā iti varuṇeti yadūcima tato varuṇa muñca naḥ ||2||

(7,83.3 ) uduttamaṃ varuṇa pāśamasmadavādhamaṃ vi madhyamaṃ śrathāya |
(7,83.3 ) adhā vayamāditya vrate tavānāgaso aditaye syāma ||3||

(7,83.4 ) prāsmatpāśān varuṇa muñca sarvān ya uttamā adhamā vāruṇā ye |
(7,83.4 ) duṣvapnyaṃ duritaṃ ni ṣvāsmadatha gachema sukṛtasya lokam ||4||



(7,84.1 ) anādhṛṣyo jātavedā amartyo virāḍagne kṣatrabhṛddīdihīha |
(7,84.1 ) viśvā amīvāḥ pramuñcan mānuṣībhiḥ śivābhiradya pari pāhi no gayam ||1||

(7,84.2 ) indra kṣatramabhi vāmamojo'jāyathā vṛṣabha carṣaṇīnām |
(7,84.2 ) apānudo janamamitrayantamuruṃ devebhyo akṛṇoru lokam ||2||

(7,84.3 ) mṛgo na bhīmaḥ kucaro giriṣṭhāḥ parāvata ā jagamyātparasyāḥ |
(7,84.3 ) sṛkaṃ saṃśāya pavimindra tigmaṃ vi śatrūn tāḍhi vi mṛdho nudasva ||3||



(7,85.1 ) tyamū ṣu vājinaṃ devajūtaṃ sahovānaṃ tarutāraṃ rathānām |
(7,85.1 ) ariṣṭanemiṃ pṛtanājimāśuṃ svastaye tārkṣyamihā huvema ||1||



(7,86.1 ) trātāramindramavitāramindraṃ havehave suhavaṃ śūramindram |
(7,86.1 ) huve nu śakraṃ puruhūtamindraṃ svasti na indro maghavān kṛṇotu ||1||


(7,87.1 ) yo agnau rudro yo apsvantarya oṣadhīrvīrudha āviveśa |
(7,87.1 ) ya imāviśvā bhuvanāni cākḷpe tasmai rudrāya namo astvagnaye ||1||



(7,88.1 ) apehyarirasyarirvā asi viṣe viṣamapṛkthā viṣamidvā apṛkthāḥ |
(7,88.1 ) ahimevābhyapehi taṃ jahi ||1||


(7,89.1 ) apo divyā acāyiṣaṃ rasena samapṛkṣmahi |
(7,89.1 ) payasvān agna āgamaṃ taṃ mā saṃ sṛja varcasā ||1||

(7,89.2 ) saṃ māgne varcasā sṛja saṃ prajayā samāyuṣā |
(7,89.2 ) vidyurme asya devā indro vidyātsaha ṛṣibhiḥ ||2||

(7,89.3 ) idamāpaḥ pra vahatāvadyaṃ ca malaṃ ca yat|
(7,89.3 ) yaccābhidudrohānṛtaṃ yacca śepe abhīruṇam ||3||

(7,89.4 ) edho'syedhiṣīya samidasi samedhiṣīya |
(7,89.4 ) tejo'si tejo mayi dhehi ||4||



(7,90.1 ) api vṛśca purāṇavadvratateriva guṣpitam |
(7,90.1 ) ojo dāsasya dambhaya ||1||

(7,90.2 ) vayaṃ tadasya sambhṛtaṃ vasvindrena vi bhajāmahai |
(7,90.2 ) mlāpayāmi bhrajaḥ śibhraṃ varuṇasya vratena te ||2||

(7,90.3 ) yathā śepo apāyātai strīṣu cāsadanāvayāḥ |
(7,90.3 ) avasthasya knadīvataḥ śāṅkurasya nitodinaḥ |
(7,90.3 ) yadātatamava tattanu yaduttataṃ ni tattanu ||3||



(7,91.1 ) indraḥ sutrāmā svavāmavobhiḥ sumṛḍīko bhavatu viśvavedāḥ |
(7,91.1 ) bādhatāṃ dveṣo abhayaṃ naḥ kṛṇotu suvīryasya patayaḥ syāma ||1||



(7,92.1 ) sa sutrāmā svavāmindro asmadārācciddveṣaḥ sanutaryuyotu |
(7,92.1 ) tasya vayaṃ sumatau yajñiyasyāpi bhadre saumanase syāma ||1||



(7,93.1 ) indreṇa manyunā vayamabhi ṣyāma pṛtanyataḥ |
(7,93.1 ) ghnanto vṛtrāṇyaprati ||1||



(7,94.1 ) dhruvaṃ dhruveṇa haviṣāva somaṃ nayāmasi |
(7,94.1 ) yathā na indraḥ kevalīrviśaḥ saṃmanasaskarat||1||



(7,95.1 ) udasya śyāvau vithurau gṛdhrau dyāmiva petatuḥ |
(7,95.1 ) ucchocanapraśocanavasyocchocanau hṛdaḥ ||1||

(7,95.2 ) ahamenāvudatiṣṭhipaṃ gāvau śrāntasadāviva |
(7,95.2 ) kurkurāviva kūjantāvudavantau vṛkāviva ||2||

(7,95.3 ) ātodinau nitodināvatho saṃtodināvuta |
(7,95.3 ) api nahyāmyasya meḍhraṃ ya itaḥ strī pumān jabhāra ||3||



(7,96.1 ) asadan gāvaḥ sadane'paptadvasatiṃ vayaḥ |
(7,96.1 ) āsthāne parvatā asthuḥ sthāmni vṛkkāvatiṣṭhipam ||1||



(7,97.1 ) yadadya tvā prayati yajñe asmin hotaścikitvann avṛṇīmahīha |
(7,97.1 ) dhruvamayo dhruvamutā śaviṣṭhaipravidvān yajñamupa yāhi somam ||1||

(7,97.2 ) samindra no manasā neṣa gobhiḥ saṃ sūribhirharivantsaṃ svastyā |
(7,97.2 ) saṃ brahmaṇā devahitaṃ yadasti saṃ devānāṃ sumatau yajñiyānām ||2||

(7,97.3 ) yān āvaha uśato deva devāṃstān preraya sve agne sadhasthe |
(7,97.3 ) jakṣivāṃsaḥ papivāṃso madhūnyasmai dhatta vasavo vasūni ||3||

(7,97.4 ) sugā vo devāḥ sadanā akarma ya ājagma savane mā juṣāṇāḥ |
(7,97.4 ) vahamānā bharamāṇāḥ svā vasūni vasuṃ gharmaṃ divamā rohatānu ||4||

(7,97.5 ) yajña yajñaṃ gacha yajñapatiṃ gacha |
(7,97.5 ) svāṃ yoniṃ gacha svāhā ||5||

(7,97.6 ) eṣa te yajño yajñapate sahasūktavākaḥ |
(7,97.6 ) suvīryaḥ svāhā ||6||

(7,97.7 ) vaṣaḍdhutebhyo vaṣaḍahutebhyaḥ |
(7,97.7 ) devā gātuvido gātuṃ vittvā gātumita ||7||



(7,97.8 ) manasaspata imaṃ no divi deveṣu yajñam |
(7,97.8 ) svāhā divi svāhā pṛthivyāṃ svāhāntarikṣe svāhā vāte dhāṃ svāhā ||8||



(7,98.1 ) saṃ barhiraktaṃ haviṣā ghṛtena samindreṇa vasunā saṃ marudbhiḥ |
(7,98.1 ) saṃ devairviśvadevebhiraktamindraṃ gachatu haviḥ svāhā ||1||



(7,99.1 ) pari stṛṇīhi pari dhehi vediṃ mā jāmiṃ moṣīramuyā śayānām |
(7,99.1 ) hotṛṣadanaṃ haritaṃ hiraṇyayaṃ niṣkā ete yajamānasya loke ||1||



(7,100.1 ) paryāvarte duṣvapnyātpāpātsvapnyādabhūtyāḥ |
(7,100.1 ) brahmāhamantaraṃ kṛṇve parā svapnamukhāḥ śucaḥ ||1||



(7,101.1 ) yatsvapne annamaśnāmi na prātaradhigamyate |
(7,101.1 ) sarvaṃ tadastu me śivaṃ nahi taddṛṣyate divā ||1||



(7,102.1 ) namaskṛtya dyāvāpṛthivībhyāmantarikṣāya mṛtyave |
(7,102.1 ) mekṣāmyūrdhvastiṣṭhan mā mā hiṃsiṣurīśvarāḥ ||1||



(7,103.1 ) ko asyā no druho'vadyavatyā un neṣyati kṣatriyo vasya ichan |
(7,103.1 ) ko yajñakāmaḥ ka u pūrtikāmaḥ ko deveṣu vanute dīrghamāyuḥ ||1||



(7,104.1 ) kaḥ pṛśniṃ dhenuṃ varuṇena dattāmatharvane sudughāṃ nityavatsām |
(7,104.1 ) bṛhaspatinā sakhyaṃ juṣaṇo yathāvaśaṃ tanvaḥ kalpayāti ||1||



(7,105.1 ) apakrāman pauruṣeyādvṛṇāno daivyaṃ vacaḥ |
(7,105.1 ) praṇītīrabhyāvartasva viśvebhiḥ sakhibhiḥ saha ||1||



(7,106.1 ) yadasmṛti cakṛma kiṃ cidagna upārima caraṇe jātavedaḥ |
(7,106.1 ) tataḥ pāhi tvaṃ naḥ pracetaḥ śubhe sakhibhyo amṛtatvamastu naḥ ||1||



(7,107.1 ) ava divastārayanti sapta sūryasya raśmayaḥ |
(7,107.1 ) āpaḥ samudriyā dhārāstāsśalyamasisrasan ||1||



(7,108.1 ) yo na stāyaddipsati yo na āviḥ svo vidvān araṇo vā no agne |
(7,108.1 ) pratīcyetvaraṇī datvatī tān maiṣāmagne vāstu bhūn mo apatyam ||1||

(7,108.2 ) yo naḥ suptān jāgrato vābhidāsāttiṣṭhato vā carato jātavedaḥ |
(7,108.2 ) vaiśvānareṇa sayujā sajoṣāstān pratīco nirdaha jātavedaḥ ||2||



(7,109.1 ) idamugrāya babhrave namo yo akṣeṣu tanūvaśī |
(7,109.1 ) ghṛtena kaliṃ śikṣāmi sa no mṛḍātīdṛśe ||1||

(7,109.2 ) ghṛtamapsarābhyo vaha tvamagne pāṃsūn akṣebhyaḥ sikatā apaśca |
(7,109.2 ) yathābhagaṃ havyadātiṃ juṣāṇā madanti devā ubhayāni havyā ||2||

(7,109.3 ) apsarasaḥ sadhamādaṃ madanti havirdhānamantarā sūryaṃ ca |
(7,109.3 ) tā me hastau saṃ sṛjantu ghṛtena sapatnaṃ me kitavaṃ randhayantu ||3||

(7,109.4 ) ādinavaṃ pratidīvne ghṛtenāsmāmabhi kṣara |
(7,109.4 ) vṛkṣamivāśanyā jahi yo asmān pratidīvyati ||4||

(7,109.5 ) yo no dyuve dhanamidaṃ cakāra yo akṣāṇāṃ glahanaṃ śeṣaṇaṃ ca |
(7,109.5 ) sa no devo haviridaṃ juṣāṇo gandharvebhiḥ sadhamādaṃ madema ||5||

(7,109.6 ) saṃvasava iti vo nāmadheyamugraṃpaśyā rāṣṭrabhṛto hyakṣāḥ |
(7,109.6 ) tebhyo va indavo haviṣā vidhema vayaṃ syāma patayo rayīṇām ||6||

(7,109.7 ) devān yan nāthito huve brahmacaryaṃ yadūṣima |
(7,109.7 ) akṣān yadbabhrūn ālabhe te no mṛḍantvīdṛśe ||7||


(7,110.1 ) agna indraśca dāśuṣe hato vṛtrāṇyaprati |
(7,110.1 ) ubhā hi vṛtrahantamā ||1||

(7,110.2 ) yābhyāmajayantsvaragra eva yāvātasthaturbhuvanāni viśvā |
(7,110.2 ) pra carṣaṇīvṛṣaṇā vajrabāhū agnimindraṃ vṛtrahaṇā huve'ham ||2||

(7,110.3 ) upa tvā devo agramīccamasena bṛhaspatiḥ |
(7,110.3 ) indra gīrbhirna ā viśa yajamānāya sunvate ||3||



(7,111.1 ) indrasya kukṣirasi somadhāna ātmā devānāmuta mānuṣāṇām |
(7,111.1 ) iha prajā janaya yāsta āsu yā anyatreha tāste ramantām ||1||



(7,112.1 ) śumbhanī dyāvāpṛthivī antisumne mahivrate |
(7,112.1 ) āpaḥ sapta susruvurdevīstā no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||1||

(7,112.2 ) muñcantu mā śapathyādatho varuṇyāduta |
(7,112.2 ) atho yamasya paḍvīśādviśvasmāddevakilbiṣāt||2||


(7,113.1 ) tṛṣṭike tṛṣṭavandana udamūṃ chindhi tṛṣṭike |
(7,113.1 ) yathā kṛtadviṣṭāso'muṣmai śepyāvate ||1||

(7,113.2 ) tṛṣṭāsi tṛṣṭikā viṣā viṣātakyasi |
(7,113.2 ) parivṛktā yathāsasyṛṣabhasya vaśeva ||2||



(7,114.1 ) ā te dade vakṣaṇābhya ā te'haṃ hṛdayāddade |
(7,114.1 ) ā te mukhasya saṅkāśātsarvaṃ te varca ā dade ||1||

(7,114.2 ) preto yantu vyādhyaḥ prānudhyāḥ pro aśastayaḥ |
(7,114.2 ) agnī rakṣasvinīrhantu somo hantu durasyatīḥ ||2||



(7,115.1 ) pra patetaḥ pāpi lakṣmi naśyetaḥ prāmutaḥ pata |
(7,115.1 ) ayasmayenāṅkena dviṣate tvā sajāmasi ||1||

(7,115.2 ) yā mā lakṣmīḥ patayālūrajuṣṭābhicaskanda vandaneva vṛkṣam |
(7,115.2 ) anyatrāsmatsavitastāmito dhā hiraṇyahasto vasu no rarāṇaḥ ||2||

(7,115.3 ) ekaśataṃ lakṣmyo martyasya sākaṃ tanvā januṣo'dhi jātāḥ |
(7,115.3 ) tāsāṃ pāpiṣṭhā niritaḥ pra hiṇmaḥ śivā asmabhyaṃ jātavedo niyacha ||3||

(7,115.4 ) etā enā vyākaraṃ khile gā viṣṭhitā iva |
(7,115.4 ) ramantāṃ puṇyā lakṣmīryāḥ pāpīstā anīnaśam ||4||



(7,116.1 ) namo rūrāya cyavanāya nodanāya dhṛṣṇave |
(7,116.1 ) namaḥ śītāya pūrvakāmakṛtvane ||1||

(7,116.2 ) yo anyedyurubhayadyurabhyetīmaṃ maṇḍūkam |
(7,116.2 ) abhyetvavrataḥ ||2||



(7,117.1 ) ā mandrairindra haribhiryāhi mayūraromabhiḥ |
(7,117.1 ) mā tvā ke cidvi yaman viṃ na pāśino'ti dhanveva tāmihi ||1||

(7,118.1 ) marmāṇi te varmaṇā chādayāmi somastvā rājāmṛtenānu vastām |
(7,118.1 ) urorvarīyo varuṇaste kṛṇotu jayantaṃ tvānu devā madantu ||1||



(8,1.1 ) antakāya mṛtyave namaḥ prānā apānā iha te ramantām |
(8,1.1 ) ihāyamastu puruṣaḥ sahāsunā sūryasya bhāge amṛtasya loke ||1||

(8,1.2 ) udenaṃ bhago agrabhīdudenaṃ somo aṃśumān |
(8,1.2 ) udenaṃ maruto devā udindrāgnī svastaye ||2||

(8,1.3 ) iha te'suriha prāṇa ihāyuriha te manaḥ |
(8,1.3 ) uttvā nirṛtyāḥ pāśebhyo daivyā vacā bharāmasi ||3||

(8,1.4 ) utkrāmātaḥ puruṣa māva patthā mṛtyoḥ paḍvīṣamavamuñcamānaḥ |
(8,1.4 ) mā chitthā asmāllokādagneḥ sūryasya saṃdṛśaḥ ||4||

(8,1.5 ) tubhyaṃ vātaḥ pavatāṃ mātariśvā tubhyaṃ varṣantvamṛtānyāpaḥ |
(8,1.5 ) sūryaste tanve śaṃ tapāti tvāṃ mṛtyurdayatāṃ mā pra meṣṭhāḥ ||5||

(8,1.6 ) udyānaṃ te puruṣa nāvayānaṃ jīvātuṃ te dakṣatātiṃ kṛnomi |
(8,1.6 ) ā hi rohemamamṛtaṃ sukhaṃ rathamatha jirvirvidathamā vadāsi ||6||

(8,1.7 ) mā te manastatra gān mā tiro bhūn mā jīvebhyaḥ pra mado mānu gāḥ pitṝn |
(8,1.7 ) viśve devā abhi rakṣantu tveha ||7||

(8,1.8 ) mā gatānāmā dīdhīthā ye nayanti parāvatam |
(8,1.8 ) ā roha tamaso jyotirehyā te hastau rabhāmahe ||8||

(8,1.9 ) śyāmaśca tvā mā śabalaśca preṣitau yamasya yau pathirakṣī śvānau |
(8,1.9 ) arvāṅehi mā vi dīdhyo mātra tiṣṭhaḥ parāṅmanāḥ ||9||
(8,1.10 ) maitaṃ panthāmanu gā bhīma eṣa yena pūrvaṃ neyatha taṃ bravīmi |
(8,1.10 ) tama etatpuruṣa mā pra patthā bhayaṃ parastādabhayaṃ te arvāk||10|| {1}

(8,1.11 ) rakṣantu tvāgnayo ye apsvantā rakṣatu tvā manuṣyā yamindhate |
(8,1.11 ) vaiśvānaro rakṣatu jātavedā divyastvā mā pra dhāgvidyutā saha ||11||

(8,1.12 ) mā tvā kravyādabhi maṃstārātsaṃkasukāccara rakṣatu tvā dyau rakṣatu |
(8,1.12 ) pṛthivī sūryaśca tvā rakṣatāṃ candramāśca |
(8,1.12 ) antarikṣaṃ rakṣatu devahetyāḥ ||12||

(8,1.13 ) bodhaśca tvā pratibodhaśca rakṣatāmasvapnaśca tvānavadrāṇaśca rakṣatām |
(8,1.13 ) gopāyaṃśca tvā jāgṛviśca rakṣatām ||13||

(8,1.14 ) te tvā rakṣantu te tvā gopāyantu tebhyo namastebhyaḥ svāhā ||14||

(8,1.15 ) jīvebhyastvā samude vāyurindro dhātā dadhātu savitā trāyamāṇaḥ |
(8,1.15 ) mā tvā prāṇo balaṃ hāsīdasuṃ te'nu hvayāmasi ||15||

(8,1.16 ) mā tvā jambhaḥ saṃhanurmā tamo vidan mā jihvā barhispramayuḥ kathā syāḥ |
(8,1.16 ) uttvādityā vasavo bharantūdindrāgnī svastaye ||16||

(8,1.17 ) uttvā dyaurutpṛthivyutprajāpatiragrabhīt|
(8,1.17 ) uttvā mṛtyoroṣadhayaḥ somarājñīrapīparan ||17||

(8,1.18 ) ayaṃ devā ihaivāstvayaṃ māmutra gāditaḥ |
(8,1.18 ) imaṃ sahasravīryeṇa mṛtyorutpārayāmasi ||18||

(8,1.19 ) uttvā mṛtyorapīparaṃ saṃ dhamantu vayodhasaḥ |
(8,1.19 ) mā tvā vyastakeśyo mā tvāgharudo rudan ||19||

(8,1.20 ) āhārṣamavidaṃ tvā punarāgāḥ punarṇavaḥ |
(8,1.20 ) sarvāṅga sarvaṃ te cakṣuḥ sarvamāyuśca te'vidam ||20||

(8,1.21 ) vyavātte jyotirabhūdapa tvattamo akramīt|
(8,1.21 ) apa tvan mṛtyuṃ nirṛtimapa yakṣmaṃ ni dadhmasi ||21|| {2}



(8,2.1 ) ā rabhasvemāmamṛtasya śnuṣṭimachidyamānā jaradaṣṭirastu te |
(8,2.1 ) asuṃ ta āyuḥ punarā bharāmi rajastamo mopa gā mā pra meṣṭhāḥ ||1||

(8,2.2 ) jīvatāṃ jyotirabhyehyarvāṅā tvā harāmi śataśāradāya |
(8,2.2 ) avamuñcan mṛtyupāśān aśastiṃ drāghīya āyuḥ prataraṃ te dadhāmi ||2||

(8,2.3 ) vātātte prānamavidaṃ sūryāccakṣurahaṃ tava |
(8,2.3 ) yatte manastvayi taddhārayāmi saṃ vitsvāṅgairvada jihvayālapan ||3||

(8,2.4 ) prāṇena tvā dvipadāṃ catuṣpadāmagnimiva jātamabhi saṃ dhamāmi |
(8,2.4 ) namaste mṛtyo cakṣuṣe namaḥ prāṇāya te'karam ||4||

(8,2.5 ) ayaṃ jīvatu mā mṛtemaṃ samīrayāmasi |
(8,2.5 ) kṛṇomyasmai bheṣajaṃ mṛtyo mā puruṣaṃ vadhīḥ ||5||

(8,2.6 ) jīvalāṃ naghāriṣāṃ jīvantīmoṣadhīmaham |
(8,2.6 ) trāyamāṇāṃ sahamānāṃ sahasvatīmiha huve'smā ariṣṭatātaye ||6||

(8,2.7 ) adhi brūhi mā rabhathāḥ sṛjemaṃ tavaiva santsarvahāyāḥ ihāstu |
(8,2.7 ) bhavāśarvau mṛḍataṃ śarma yachatamapasidhya duritaṃ dhattamāyuḥ ||7||

(8,2.8 ) asmai mṛtyo adhi brūhīmaṃ dayasvodito'yametu |
(8,2.8 ) ariṣṭaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ suśrujjarasā śatahāyana ātmanā bhujamaśnutām ||8||

(8,2.9 ) devānāṃ hetiḥ pari tvā vṛṇaktu pārayāmi tvā rajasa uttvā mṛtyorapīparam |
(8,2.9 ) ārādagniṃ kravyādaṃ nirūhaṃ jīvātave te paridhiṃ dadhāmi ||9||

(8,2.10 ) yatte niyānaṃ rajasaṃ mṛtyo anavadharṣyam |
(8,2.10 ) patha imaṃ tasmādrakṣanto brahmāsmai varma kṛṇmasi ||10|| {3}

(8,2.11 ) kṛṇomi te prāṇāpānau jarāṃ mṛtyuṃ dīrghamāyuḥ svasti |
(8,2.11 ) vaivasvatena prahitān yamadūtāṃścarato'pa sedhāmi sarvān ||11||

(8,2.12 ) ārādarātiṃ nirṛtiṃ paro grāhiṃ kravyādaḥ piśācān |
(8,2.12 ) rakṣo yatsarvaṃ durbhūtaṃ tattama ivāpa hanmasi ||12||

(8,2.13 ) agneṣṭa prānamamṛtādāyuṣmato vanve jātavedasaḥ |
(8,2.13 ) yathā na riṣyā amṛtaḥ sajūrasastatte kṛṇomi tadu te samṛdhyatām ||13||

(8,2.14 ) śive te stāṃ dyāvāpṛthivī asaṃtāpe abhiśriyau |
(8,2.14 ) śaṃ te sūrya ā tapatu śaṃ vāto vātu te hṛde |
(8,2.14 ) śivā abhi kṣarantu tvāpo divyāḥ payasvatīḥ ||14||

(8,2.15 ) śivāste santvoṣadhaya uttvāhārṣamadharasyā uttarāṃ pṛthivīmabhi |
(8,2.15 ) tatra tvādityau rakṣatāṃ sūryācandramasāvubhā ||15||

(8,2.16 ) yatte vāsaḥ paridhānaṃ yāṃ nīviṃ kṛṇuṣe tvam |
(8,2.16 ) śivaṃ te tanve tatkṛṇmaḥ saṃsparśe'drūkṣṇamastu te ||16||

(8,2.17 ) yatkṣureṇa marcayatā sutejasā vaptā vapasi keśaśmaśru |
(8,2.17 ) śubhaṃ mukhaṃ mā na āyuḥ pra moṣīḥ ||17||

(8,2.18 ) śivau te stāṃ vrīhiyavāvabalāsāvadomadhau |
(8,2.18 ) etau yakṣmaṃ vi bādhete etau muñcato aṃhasaḥ ||18||

(8,2.19 ) yadaśnāsi yatpibasi dhānyaṃ kṛṣyāḥ payaḥ |
(8,2.19 ) yadādyaṃ yadanādyaṃ sarvaṃ te annamaviṣaṃ kṛṇomi ||19||

(8,2.20 ) ahne ca tvā rātraye cobhābhyāṃ pari dadmasi |
(8,2.20 ) arāyebhyo jighatsubhya imaṃ me pari rakṣata ||20|| {4}

(8,2.21 ) śataṃ te'yutaṃ hāyanān dve yuge trīṇi catvāri kṛṇmaḥ |
(8,2.21 ) indrāgnī viśve devāste'nu manyantāmahṛṇīyamānāḥ ||21||

(8,2.22 ) śarade tvā hemantāya vasantāya grīṣmāya pari dadmasi |
(8,2.22 ) varṣāṇi tubhyaṃ syonāni yeṣu vardhanta oṣadhīḥ ||22||

(8,2.23 ) mṛtyurīśe dvipadāṃ mṛtyurīśe catuṣpadām |
(8,2.23 ) tasmāttvāṃ mṛtyorgopaterudbharāmi sa mā bibheḥ ||23||

(8,2.24 ) so'riṣṭa na mariṣyasi na mariṣyasi mā bibheḥ |
(8,2.24 ) na vai tatra mriyante no yanti adhamaṃ tamaḥ ||24||

(8,2.25 ) sarvo vai tatra jīvati gauraśvaḥ puruṣaḥ paśuḥ |
(8,2.25 ) yatredaṃ brahma kriyate paridhirjīvanāya kam ||25||

(8,2.26 ) pari tvā pātu samānebhyo'bhicārātsabandhubhyaḥ |
(8,2.26 ) amamrirbhavāmṛto'tijīvo mā te hāsiṣurasavaḥ śarīram ||26||

(8,2.27 ) ye mṛtyava ekaśataṃ yā nāṣṭrā atitāryāḥ |
(8,2.27 ) muñcantu tasmāttvāṃ devā agnervaiśvānarādadhi ||27||

(8,2.28 ) agneḥ śarīramasi pārayiṣṇu rakṣohāsi sapatnahā |
(8,2.28 ) atho amīvacātanaḥ pūtudrurnāma bheṣajam ||28|| {5}



(8,3.1 ) rakṣohaṇaṃ vājinamā jigharmi mitraṃ prathiṣṭhamupa yāmi śarma |
(8,3.1 ) śiśāno agniḥ kratubhiḥ samiddhaḥ sa no divā sa riṣaḥ pātu naktam ||1||

(8,3.2 ) ayodaṃṣṭro arciṣā yātudhānān upa spṛśa jātavedaḥ samiddhaḥ |
(8,3.2 ) ā jihvayā mūradevān rabhasva kravyādo vṛṣṭvāpi dhatsvāsan ||2||

(8,3.3 ) ubhobhayāvinn upa dhehi daṃṣṭrau hiṃsraḥ śiśāno'varaṃ paraṃ ca |
(8,3.3 ) utāntarikṣe pari yāhyagne jambhaiḥ saṃ dhehyabhi yātudhānān ||3||

(8,3.4 ) agne tvacaṃ yātudhānasya bhindhi hiṃsrāśanirharasā hantvenam |
(8,3.4 ) pra parvāṇi jātavedaḥ śṛṇīhi kravyātkraviṣṇurvi cinotvenam ||4||

(8,3.5 ) yatredānīṃ paśyasi jātavedastiṣṭhantamagna uta vā carantam |
(8,3.5 ) utāntarikṣe patantaṃ yātudhānaṃ tamastā vidhya śarvā śiśānaḥ ||5||

(8,3.6 ) yajñairiṣūḥ saṃnamamāno agne ivācā śalyāmaśanibhirdihānaḥ |
(8,3.6 ) tābhirvidhya hṛdaye yātudhānān pratīco bāhūn prati bhaṅgdhyeṣām ||6||

(8,3.7 ) utārabdhāntspṛnuhi jātaveda utārebhāṇāmṛṣṭibhiryātudhānān |
(8,3.7 ) agne pūrvo ni jahi śośucāna āmādaḥ kṣviṅkāstamadantvenīḥ ||7||

(8,3.8 ) iha pra brūhi yatamaḥ so agne yātudhāno ya idaṃ kṛṇoti |
(8,3.8 ) tamā rabhasva samidhā yaviṣṭha nṛcakṣasaścakṣuṣe randhayainam ||8||

(8,3.9 ) tīkṣṇenāgne cakṣuṣā rakṣa yajñaṃ prāñcaṃ vasubhyaḥ pra ṇaya pracetaḥ |
(8,3.9 ) hiṃsraṃ rakṣāṃsyabhi śośucānaṃ mā tvā dabhan yātudhānā nṛcakṣaḥ ||9||

(8,3.10 ) nṛcakṣā rakṣaḥ pari paśya vikṣu tasya trīṇi prati śṛṇīhyagrā |
(8,3.10 ) tasyāgne pṛṣṭīrharasā śṛṇīhi tredhā mūlaṃ yātudhānasya vṛśca ||10|| {6}

(8,3.11 ) triryātudhānaḥ prasitiṃ ta etvṛtaṃ yo agne anṛtena hanti |
(8,3.11 ) tamarciṣā sphūrjayan jātavedaḥ samakṣamenaṃ gṛṇate ni yuṅgdhi ||11||

(8,3.12 ) yadagne adya mithunā śapāto yadvācastṛṣṭaṃ janayanta rebhāḥ |
(8,3.12 ) manyormanasaḥ śaravyā jāyate yā tayā vidhya hṛdaye yātudhānān ||12||

(8,3.13 ) parā śṛṇīhi tapasā yātudhānān parāgne rakṣo harasā śṛṇīhi |
(8,3.13 ) parārciṣā mūradevān chṛṇīhi parāsutṛpaḥ śośucataḥ śṛṇīhi ||13||

(8,3.14 ) parādya devā vṛjinaṃ śṛṇantu pratyagenaṃ śapathā yantu sṛṣṭāḥ |
(8,3.14 ) vācāstenaṃ śarava ṛchantu marman viśvasyaitu prasitiṃ yātudhānaḥ ||14||

(8,3.15 ) yaḥ pauruṣeyeṇa kraviṣā samaṅkte yo aśvyena paśunā yātudhānaḥ |
(8,3.15 ) yo aghnyāyā bharati kṣīramagne teṣāṃ śīrṣāṇi harasāpi vṛśca ||15||

(8,3.16 ) viṣaṃ gavāṃ yātudhānā bharantāmā vṛścantāmaditaye durevāḥ |
(8,3.16 ) paraiṇān devaḥ savitā dadātu parā bhāgamoṣadhīnāṃ jayantām ||16||

(8,3.17 ) saṃvatsarīṇaṃ paya usriyāyāstasya māśīdyātudhāno nṛcakṣaḥ |
(8,3.17 ) pīyūṣamagne yatamastitṛpsāttaṃ pratyañcamarciṣā vidhya marmaṇi ||17||

(8,3.18 ) sanādagne mṛṇasi yātudhānān na tvā rakṣāṃsi pṛtanāsu jigyuḥ |
(8,3.18 ) sahamūrān anu daha kravyādo mā te hetyā mukṣata daivyāyāḥ ||18||

(8,3.19 ) tvaṃ no agne adharādudaktastvaṃ paścāduta rakṣā purastāt|
(8,3.19 ) prati tye te ajarāsastapiṣṭhā aghaśaṃsaṃ śośucato dahantu ||19||

(8,3.20 ) paścātpurastādadharādutottarātkaviḥ kāvyena pari pāhyagne |
(8,3.20 ) sakhā sakhāyamajaro jarimne agne martāmamartyastvaṃ naḥ ||20|| {7}

(8,3.21 ) tadagne cakṣuḥ prati dhehi rebhe śaphārujo yena paśyasi yātudhānān |
(8,3.21 ) atharvavajjyotiṣā daivyena satyaṃ dhūrvantamacitaṃ nyoṣa ||21||

(8,3.22 ) pari tvāgne puraṃ vayaṃ vipraṃ sahasya dhīmahi |
(8,3.22 ) dhṛṣadvarṇaṃ divedive hantāraṃ bhaṅgurāvataḥ ||22||

(8,3.23 ) viṣeṇa bhaṅgurāvataḥ prati sma rakṣaso jahi |
(8,3.23 ) agne tigmena śociṣā tapuragrābhirarcibhiḥ ||23||

(8,3.24 ) vi jyotiṣā bṛhatā bhātyagnirāvirviśvāni kṛṇute mahitvā |
(8,3.24 ) prādevīrmāyāḥ sahate durevāḥ śiśīte śṛṅge rakṣobhyo vinikṣe ||24||

(8,3.25 ) ye te śṛṅge ajare jātavedastigmahetī brahmasaṃśite |
(8,3.25 ) tābhyāṃ durhārdamabhidāsantaṃ kimīdinam |
(8,3.25 ) pratyañcamarciṣā jātavedo vi nikṣva ||25||

(8,3.26 ) agnī rakṣāṃsi sedhati śukraśociramartyaḥ |
(8,3.26 ) śuciḥ pāvaka īḍyaḥ ||26|| {8}



(8,4.1 ) indrāsomā tapataṃ rakṣa ubjataṃ nyarpayataṃ vṛṣaṇā tamovṛdhaḥ |
(8,4.1 ) parā śṛṇītamacito nyoṣataṃ hataṃ nudethāṃ ni śiśītamattriṇaḥ ||1||

(8,4.2 ) indrāsomā samaghaśaṃsamabhyaghaṃ tapuryayastu caruragnimāmiva |
(8,4.2 ) brahmadviṣe kravyāde ghoracakṣase dveṣo dhattamanavāyaṃ kimīdine ||2||

(8,4.3 ) indrāsomā duṣkṛto vavre antaranārambhaṇe tamasi pra vidhyatam |
(8,4.3 ) yato naiṣāṃ punarekaścanodayattadvāmastu sahase manyumacchavaḥ ||3||

(8,4.4 ) indrāsomā vartayataṃ divo vadhaṃ saṃ pṛthivyā aghaśaṃsāya tarhaṇam |
(8,4.4 ) uttakṣataṃ svaryaṃ1 parvatebhyo yena rakṣo vāvṛdhānaṃ nijūrvathaḥ ||4||

(8,4.5 ) indrāsomā vartayataṃ divasparyagnitaptebhiryuvamaśmahanmabhiḥ |
(8,4.5 ) tapurvadhebhirajarebhirattriṇo ni parśāne vidhyataṃ yantu nisvaram ||5||

(8,4.6 ) indrāsomā pari vāṃ bhūtu viśvata iyaṃ matiḥ kakṣyāśveva vājinā |
(8,4.6 ) yāṃ vāṃ hotrāṃ parihinomi medhayemā brahmāṇi nṛpatī iva jinvatam ||6||

(8,4.7 ) prati smarethāṃ tujayadbhirevairhataṃ druho rakṣaso bhaṅgurāvataḥ |
(8,4.7 ) indrāsomā duṣkṛte mā sugaṃ bhūdyo mā kadā cidabhidāsati druhuḥ ||7||

(8,4.8 ) yo mā pākena manasā carantamabhicaṣṭe anṛtebhirvacobhiḥ |
(8,4.8 ) āpa iva kāśinā samgṛbhītā asann astvasataḥ indra vaktā ||8||

(8,4.9 ) ye pākaśaṃsaṃ viharanta evairye vā bhadraṃ dūṣayanti svadhābhiḥ |
(8,4.9 ) ahaye vā tān pradadātu soma ā vā dadhātu nirṛterupaṣṭhe ||9||

(8,4.10 ) yo no rasaṃ dipsati pitvo agne aśvānāṃ gavāṃ yastanūnām |
(8,4.10 ) ripu stena steyakṛddabhrametu ni ṣa hīyatāṃ tanvā tanā ca ||10|| {9}

(8,4.11 ) paraḥ so astu tanvā tanā ca tisraḥ pṛthivīradho astu viśvāḥ |
(8,4.11 ) prati śuṣyatu yaśo asya devā yo mā divā dipsati yaśca naktam ||11||

(8,4.12 ) suvijñānaṃ cikituṣe janāya saccāsacca vacasī paspṛdhāte |
(8,4.12 ) tasyoryatsatyaṃ yataradṛjīyastaditsomo'vati hantyasat||12||

(8,4.13 ) na vā u somo vṛjinaṃ hinoti na kṣatriyaṃ mithuyā dhārayantam |
(8,4.13 ) hanti rakṣo hantyāsadvadantamubhāvindrasya prasitau śayāte ||13||

(8,4.14 ) yadi vāhamanṛtadevo asmi moghaṃ vā devāmapyūhe agne |
(8,4.14 ) kimasmabhyaṃ jātavedo hṛṇīṣe droghavācaste nirṛthaṃ sacantām ||14||

(8,4.15 ) adyā murīya yadi yātudhāno asmi yadi vāyustatapa puruṣasya |
(8,4.15 ) adhā sa vīrairdaśabhirvi yūyā yo mā moghaṃ yātudhānetyāha ||15||

(8,4.16 ) yo māyātuṃ yātudhānetyāha yo vā rakṣāḥ śicirasmītyāha |
(8,4.16 ) indrastaṃ hantu mahatā vadhena viśvasya jantoradhamaspadīṣṭa ||16||

(8,4.17 ) pra yā jigāti khargaleva naktamapa druhustanvaṃ1 gūhamānā |
(8,4.17 ) vavramanantamava sā padīṣṭia grāvāṇo ghnantu rakṣasa upabdaiḥ ||17||

(8,4.18 ) vi tiṣṭhadhvaṃ maruto vikṣvichata gṛbhāyata rakṣasaḥ saṃ pinaṣṭan |
(8,4.18 ) vayo ye bhūtvā patayanti naktabhirye vā ripo dadhire deve adhvare ||18||

(8,4.19 ) pra vartaya divo'śmānamindra somaśitaṃ maghavantsaṃ śiśādhi |
(8,4.19 ) prākto apākto adharādudakto'bhi jahi rakṣasaḥ parvatena ||19||

(8,4.20 ) eta u tye patayanti śvayātava indraṃ dipsanti dipsavo'dābhyam |
(8,4.20 ) śiśīte śakraḥ piśunebhyo vadhaṃ nunaṃ sṛjadaśaniṃ yātumadbhyaḥ ||20|| {10}

(8,4.21 ) indro yātūnāmabhavatparāśaro havirmathīnāmabhyāvivāsatām |
(8,4.21 ) abhīdu śakraḥ paraśuryathā vanaṃ pātreva bhindantsata etu rakṣasaḥ ||21||

(8,4.22 ) ulūkayātuṃ śuśulūkayātuṃ jahi śvayātumuta kokayātum |
(8,4.22 ) suparṇayātumuta gṛdhrayātuṃ dṛṣadeva pra mṛṇa rakṣa indra ||22||

(8,4.23 ) mā no rakṣo abhi naḍyātumāvadapochantu mithunā ye kimīdinaḥ |
(8,4.23 ) pṛthivī naḥ pārthivātpātvaṃhaso'ntarikṣaṃ divyātpātvasmān ||23||

(8,4.24 ) indra jahi pumāṃsaṃ yātudhānamuta striyaṃ māyayā śāśadānām |
(8,4.24 ) vigrīvāso mūradevā ṛdantu mā te dṛśantsūryamuccarantam ||24||
(8,4.25 ) prati cakṣva vi cakṣvendraśca soma jāgṛtam |
(8,4.25 ) rakṣobhyo vadhamasyatamaśaniṃ yātumadbhyaḥ ||25|| {11}



(8,5.1 ) ayaṃ pratisaro maṇirvīro vīrāya badhyate |
(8,5.1 ) vīryavāntsapatnahā śūravīraḥ paripāṇaḥ sumaṅgalaḥ ||1||

(8,5.2 ) ayaṃ maṇiḥ sapatnahā suvīraḥ sahasvān vājī sahamāna ugraḥ |
(8,5.2 ) pratyakkṛtyā dūṣayann eti vīraḥ ||2||

(8,5.3 ) anenendro maṇinā vṛtramahann anenāsurān parābhāvayan manīṣī |
(8,5.3 ) anenājayaddyāvāpṛthivī ubhe ime anenājayatpradiśaścatasraḥ ||3||

(8,5.4 ) ayaṃ srāktyo maṇiḥ pratīvartaḥ pratisaraḥ |
(8,5.4 ) ojasvān vimṛdho vaśī so asmān pātu sarvataḥ ||4||

(8,5.5 ) tadagnirāha tadu soma āha bṛhaspatiḥ savitā tadindraḥ |
(8,5.5 ) te me devāḥ purohitāḥ pratīcīḥ kṛtyāḥ pratisarairajantu ||5||

(8,5.6 ) antardadhe dyāvāpṛthivī utāharuta sūryam |
(8,5.6 ) te me devāḥ purohitāḥ pratīcīḥ kṛtyāḥ pratisarairajantu ||6||

(8,5.7 ) ye srāktyaṃ maṇiṃ janā varmāṇi kṛṇvate |
(8,5.7 ) sūrya iva divamāruhya vi kṛtyā bādhate vaśī ||7||

(8,5.8 ) srāktyena maṇinā ṛṣiṇeva manīṣiṇā |
(8,5.8 ) ajaiṣaṃ sarvāḥ pṛtanā vi mṛdho hanmi rakṣasaḥ ||8||

(8,5.9 ) yāḥ kṛtyā āṅgirasīryāḥ kṛtyā āsurīryāḥ |
(8,5.9 ) kṛtyāḥ svayaṃkṛtā yā u cānyebhirābhṛtāḥ |
(8,5.9 ) ubhayīstāḥ parā yantu parāvato navatiṃ nāvyā ati ||9||

(8,5.10 ) asmai maṇiṃ varma badhnantu devā indro viṣṇuḥ savitā rudro agniḥ |
(8,5.10 ) prajāpatiḥ parameṣṭhī virāḍvaiśvānara ṛṣayaśca sarve ||10|| {12}

(8,5.11 ) uttamo asyoṣadhīnāmanaḍvān jagatāmiva vyāghraḥ śvapadāmiva |
(8,5.11ḍ) yamaichāmāvidāma taṃ pratispāśanamantitam ||11||

(8,5.12 ) sa idvyāghro bhavatyatho siṃho atho vṛṣā |
(8,5.12 ) atho sapatnakarśano yo bibhartīmaṃ maṇim ||12||

(8,5.13 ) nainaṃ ghnantyapsaraso na gandharvā na martyāḥ |
(8,5.13 ) sarvā diśo vi rājati yo bibhartīmaṃ maṇim ||13||

(8,5.14 ) kaśyapastvāmasṛjata kaśyapastvā samairayat|
(8,5.14 ) abibhastvendro mānuṣe bibhratsaṃśreṣiṇe'jayat|
(8,5.14 ) maṇiṃ sahasravīryaṃ varma devā akṛṇvata ||14||

(8,5.15 ) yastvā kṛtyābhiryastvā dīkṣābhiryajñairyastvā jighāṃsati |
(8,5.15 ) pratyaktvamindra taṃ jahi vajreṇa śataparvaṇā ||15||

(8,5.16 ) ayamidvai pratīvarta ojasvān saṃjayo maṇiḥ |
(8,5.16 ) prajāṃ dhanaṃ ca rakṣatu paripāṇaḥ sumaṅgalaḥ ||16||

(8,5.17 ) asapatnaṃ no adharādasapatnaṃ na uttarāt|
(8,5.17 ) indrāsapatnaṃ naḥ paścājjyotiḥ śūra puraskṛdhi ||17||

(8,5.18 ) varma me dyāvāpṛthivī varmāharvarma sūryaḥ |
(8,5.18 ) varma ma indraścāgniśca varma dhātā dadhātu me ||18||

(8,5.19 ) aindrāgnaṃ varma bahulaṃ yadugraṃ viśve devā nātividhyanti sarve |
(8,5.19 ) tan me tanvaṃ trāyatāṃ sarvato bṛhadāyuṣmāṃ jaradaṣṭiryathāsāni ||19||

(8,5.20 ) ā mārukṣaddevamaṇirmahyā ariṣṭatātaye |
(8,5.20 ) imaṃ methimabhisaṃviśadhvaṃ tanūpānaṃ trivarūthamojase ||20||

(8,5.21 ) asminn indro ni dadhātu nṛmṇamimaṃ devāso abhisaṃviśadhvam |
(8,5.21 ) dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāyāyuṣmān jaradaṣṭiryathāsat||21||

(8,5.22 ) svastidā viśāṃ patirvṛtrahā vimṛdho vaśī |
(8,5.22 ) indro badhnātu te maṇiṃ jigīvāmaparājitaḥ |
(8,5.22 ) somapā abhayaṅkaro vṛṣā |



(8,6.1 ) yau te mātonmamārja jātāyāḥ pativedanau |
(8,6.1 ) durṇāmā tatra mā gṛdhadaliṃśa uta vatsapaḥ ||1||

(8,6.2 ) palālānupalālau śarkuṃ kokaṃ malimlucaṃ palījakam |
(8,6.2 ) āśreṣaṃ vavrivāsasamṛkṣagrīvaṃ pramīlinam ||2||

(8,6.3 ) mā saṃ vṛto mopa sṛpa ūrū māva sṛpo'ntarā |
(8,6.3 ) kṛṇomyasyai bheṣajaṃ bajaṃ durṇāmacātanam ||3||

(8,6.4 ) durṇāmā ca sunāmā cobhā samvṛtamichataḥ |
(8,6.4 ) arāyān apa hanmaḥ sunāmā straiṇamichatām ||4||

(8,6.5 ) yaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ keśyasura stambaja uta tuṇḍikaḥ |
(8,6.5 ) arāyān asyā muṣkābhyāṃ bhaṃsaso'pa hanmasi ||5||

(8,6.6 ) anujighraṃ pramṛśantaṃ kravyādamuta reriham |
(8,6.6 ) arāyāṃ chvakiṣkiṇo bajaḥ piṅgo anīnaśat||6||

(8,6.7 ) yastvā svapne nipadyate bhrātā bhūtvā piteva ca |
(8,6.7 ) bajastāntsahatāmitaḥ klībarūpāṃstirīṭinaḥ ||7||

(8,6.8 ) yastvā svapantīṃ tsarati yastvā dipsati jāgratīm |
(8,6.8 ) chāyāmiva pra tāntsūryaḥ parikrāmann anīnaśat||8||

(8,6.9 ) yaḥ kṛṇoti mṛtavatsāmavatokāmimāṃ striyam |
(8,6.9 ) tamoṣadhe tvaṃ nāśayāsyāḥ kamalamañjivam ||9||

(8,6.10 ) ye śālāḥ parinṛtyanti sāyaṃ gardabhanādinaḥ |
(8,6.10 ) kusūlā ye ca kukṣilāḥ kakubhāḥ karumāḥ srimāḥ |
(8,6.10 ) tān oṣadhe tvaṃ gandhena viṣūcīnān vi nāśaya ||10|| {14}

(8,6.11 ) ye kukundhāḥ kukirabhāḥ kṛttīrdūrśāni bibhrati |
(8,6.11 ) klībā iva pranṛtyanto vane ye kurvate ghoṣaṃ tān ito nāśayāmasi ||11||

(8,6.12 ) ye sūryaṃ na titikṣanta ātapantamamuṃ divaḥ |
(8,6.12 ) arāyān bastavāsino durgandhīṃllohitāsyān makakān nāśayāmasi ||12||

(8,6.13 ) ya ātmānamatimātramaṃsa ādhāya bibhrati |
(8,6.13 ) strīṇāṃ śroṇipratodina indra rakṣāṃsi nāśaya ||13||

(8,6.14 ) ye pūrve badhvo yanti haste śṛṅgāni bibhrataḥ |
(8,6.14 ) āpākesthāḥ prahāsina stambe ye kurvate jyotistān ito nāśayāmasi ||14||

(8,6.15 ) yeṣāṃ paścātprapadāni puraḥ pārṣṇīḥ puro mukhā |
(8,6.15 ) khalajāḥ śakadhūmajā uruṇḍā ye ca maṭmaṭāḥ kumbhamuṣkā ayāśavaḥ |
(8,6.15 ) tān asyā brahmaṇaspate pratībodhena nāśaya ||15||

(8,6.16 ) paryastākṣā apracaṅkaśā astraiṇāḥ santu paṇḍagāḥ |
(8,6.16 ) ava bheṣaja pādaya ya imāṃ saṃvivṛtsatyapatiḥ svapatiṃ striyam ||16||

(8,6.17 ) uddharṣiṇaṃ munikeśaṃ jambhayantaṃ marīmṛśam |
(8,6.17 ) upeṣantamudumbalaṃ tuṇḍelamuta śāluḍam |
(8,6.17 ) padā pra vidhya pārṣṇyā sthālīṃ gauriva spandanā ||17||

(8,6.18 ) yaste garbhaṃ pratimṛśājjātaṃ vā mārayāti te |
(8,6.18 ) piṅgastamugradhanvā kṛṇotu hṛdayāvidham ||18||

(8,6.19 ) ye amno jatān mārayanti sūtikā anuśerate |
(8,6.19 ) strībhāgān piṅgo gandharvān vāto abhramivājatu ||19||

(8,6.20 ) parisṛṣṭaṃ dharayatu yaddhitaṃ māva pādi tat|
(8,6.20 ) garbhaṃ ta ugrau rakṣatāṃ bheṣajau nīvibhāryau ||20|| {15}

(8,6.21 ) pavīnasāttaṅgalvācchāyakāduta nagnakāt|
(8,6.21 ) prajāyai patye tvā piṅgaḥ pari pātu kimīdinaḥ ||21||

(8,6.22 ) dvyāsyāccaturakṣātpañcapadādanaṅgureḥ |
(8,6.22 ) vṛntādabhi prasarpataḥ pari pāhi varīvṛtāt||22||

(8,6.23 ) ya āmaṃ māṃsamadanti pauruṣeyaṃ ca ye kraviḥ |
(8,6.23 ) garbhān khādanti keśavāstān ito nāśayāmasi ||23||

(8,6.24 ) ye sūryātparisarpanti snuṣeva śvaśurādadhi |
(8,6.24 ) bajaśca teṣāṃ piṅgaśca hṛdaye'dhi ni vidhyatām ||24||

(8,6.25 ) piṅga rakṣa jāyamānaṃ mā pumāṃsaṃ striyaṃ kran |
(8,6.25 ) āṇḍādo garbhān mā dabhan bādhasvetaḥ kimīdinaḥ ||25||

(8,6.26 ) aprajāstvaṃ mārtavatsamādrodamaghamāvayam |
(8,6.26 ) vṛkṣādiva srajaṃ kṛtvāpriye prati muñca tat||26|| {16}



(8,7.1 ) yā babhravo yāśca śukrā rohiṇīruta pṛśnayaḥ |
(8,7.1 ) asiknīḥ kṛṣṇā oṣadhīḥ sarvā achāvadāmasi ||1||

(8,7.2 ) trāyantāmimaṃ purusaṃ yakṣmāddeveṣitādadhi |
(8,7.2 ) yāsāṃ dyauḥ pitā pṛthivī mātā samudro mūlaṃ vīrudhāṃ babhūva ||2||

(8,7.3 ) āpo agraṃ divyā oṣadhayaḥ |
(8,7.3 ) tāste yakṣmamenasyamaṅgādaṅgādanīnaśan ||3||

(8,7.4 ) prastṛṇatī stambinīrekaśuṅgāḥ pratanvatīroṣadhīrā vadāmi |
(8,7.4 ) aṃśumatīḥ kaṇḍinīryā viśākhā hvayāmi te vīrudho vaiśvadevīrugrāḥ puruṣajīvanīḥ ||4||

(8,7.5 ) yadvaḥ sahaḥ sahamānā vīryaṃ1 yacca vo balam |
(8,7.5 ) tenemamasmādyakṣmātpuruṣaṃ muñcatauṣadhīratho kṛṇomi bheṣajam ||5||

(8,7.6 ) jīvalāṃ naghāriṣāṃ jīvantīmoṣadhīmaham |
(8,7.6 ) arundhatīmunnayantīṃ puṣpāṃ madhumatīmiha huve'smā ariṣṭatātaye ||6||

(8,7.7 ) ihā yantu pracetaso medinīrvacaso mama |
(8,7.7 ) yathemaṃ pārayāmasi puruṣaṃ duritādadhi ||7||

(8,7.8 ) agnerghāso apāṃ garbho yā rohanti punarṇavāḥ |
(8,7.8 ) dhruvāḥ sahasranāmnīrbheṣajīḥ santvābhṛtāḥ ||8||

(8,7.9 ) avakolbā udakātmāna oṣadhayaḥ |
(8,7.9 ) vyṛṣantu duritaṃ tīkṣṇaśṛṅgyaḥ ||9||

(8,7.10 ) unmuñcantīrvivaruṇā ugrā yā viṣadūṣanīḥ |
(8,7.10 ) atho balāsanāśanīḥ kṛtyādūṣaṇīśca yāstā ihā yantvoṣadhīḥ ||10|| {17}

(8,7.11 ) apakrītāḥ sahīyasīrvīrudho yā abhiṣṭutāḥ |
(8,7.11 ) trāyantāmasmin grāme gāmaśvaṃ puruṣaṃ paśum ||11||

(8,7.12 ) madhuman mūlaṃ madhumadagramāsāṃ madhuman madhyaṃ vīrudhāṃ babhūva |
(8,7.12 ) madhumatparṇaṃ madhumatpuṣpamāsāṃ madhoḥ sambhaktā amṛtasya bhakṣo ghṛtamannaṃ duhratāṃ gopurogavam ||12||

(8,7.13 ) yāvatīḥ kiyatīścemāḥ pṛthivyāmadhyoṣadhīḥ |
(8,7.13 ) tā mā sahasraparṇyo mṛtyormuñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||13||

(8,7.14 ) vaiyāghro maṇirvīrudhāṃ trāyamāno'bhiśastipāḥ |
(8,7.14 ) amīvāḥ sarvā rakṣāṃsyapa hantvadhi dūramasmat||14||

(8,7.15 ) siṃhasyeva stanathoḥ saṃ vijante'gneriva vijante ābhṛtābhyaḥ |
(8,7.15 ) gavāṃ yakṣmaḥ puruṣāṇāṃ vīrudbhiratinutto nāvyā etu srotyāḥ ||15||

(8,7.16 ) mumucānā oṣadhayo'gnervaiśvānarādadhi |
(8,7.16 ) bhūmiṃ saṃtanvatīrita yāsāṃ rājā vanaspatiḥ ||16||

(8,7.17 ) yā rohantyāṅgirasīḥ parvateṣu sameṣu ca |
(8,7.17 ) tā naḥ payasvatīḥ śivā oṣadhīḥ santu śaṃ hṛde ||17||

(8,7.18 ) yāścāhaṃ veda vīrudho yāśca paśyāmi cakṣuṣā |
(8,7.18 ) ajñātā jānīmaśca yā yāsu vidma ca saṃbhṛtam ||18||

(8,7.19 ) sarvāḥ samagrā oṣadhīrbodhantu vacaso mama |
(8,7.19 ) yathemaṃ pārayāmasi puruṣaṃ duritādadhi ||19||

(8,7.20 ) aśvattho darbho vīrudhāṃ somo rājāmṛtaṃ haviḥ |
(8,7.20 ) vrīhiryavaśca bheṣajau divasi putrāvamartyau ||20|| {18}

(8,7.21 ) ujjihīdhve stanayatyabhikrandatyoṣadhīḥ |
(8,7.21 ) yadā vaḥ pṛśnimātaraḥ parjanyo retasāvati ||21||
(8,7.22 ) tasyāmṛtasyemaṃ balaṃ puruṣaṃ payayāmasi |
(8,7.22 ) atho kṛṇomi bheṣajaṃ yathāsacchatahāyanaḥ ||22||

(8,7.23 ) varāho veda vīrudhaṃ nakulo veda bheṣajīm |
(8,7.23 ) sarpā gandharvā yā vidustā asmā avase huve ||23||

(8,7.24 ) yāḥ suparṇā āṅgirasīrdivyā yā raghato viduḥ |
(8,7.24 ) vayāṃsi haṃsā yā viduryāsca sarve patatriṇaḥ |
(8,7.24 ) mṛgā yā viduroṣadhīstā asmā avase huve ||24||

(8,7.25 ) yāvatīnāmoṣadhīnāṃ gāvaḥ prāśnantyaghnyā yavatīnāmajāvayaḥ |
(8,7.25 ) tāvatīstubhyamoṣadhīḥ śarma yachantvābhṛtāḥ ||25||

(8,7.26 ) yāvatīṣu manuṣyā bheṣajaṃ bhiṣajo viduḥ |
(8,7.26 ) tāvatīrviśvabheṣajīrā bharāmi tvāmabhi ||26||

(8,7.27 ) puṣpavatīḥ prasūmatīḥ phalinīraphalā uta |
(8,7.27 ) saṃmātara iva duhrāmasmā ariṣṭatātaye ||27||

(8,7.28 ) uttvāhārṣaṃ pañcaśalādatho daśaśalāduta |
(8,7.28 ) atho yamasya paḍvīśādviśvasmāddevakilbiṣāt||28|| {19}


(8,8.1 ) indro manthatu manthitā śakraḥ śūraḥ puraṃdaraḥ |
(8,8.1 ) yathā hanāma senā amitrāṇāṃ sahasraśaḥ ||1||

(8,8.2 ) pūtirajjurupadhmānī pūtiṃ senāṃ kṛṇotvamūm |
(8,8.2 ) dhūmamagniṃ parādṛśyā'mitrā hṛtsvā dadhatāṃ bhayam ||2||

(8,8.3 ) amūn aśvattha niḥ śṛṇīhi khādāmūn khadirājiram |
(8,8.3 ) tājadbhaṅga iva bhajantāṃ hantvenān vadhako vadhaiḥ ||3||

(8,8.4 ) paruṣān amūn paruṣāhvaḥ kṛṇotu hantvenān vadhako vadhaiḥ |
(8,8.4 ) kṣipraṃ śara iva bhajantāṃ bṛhajjālena saṃditāḥ ||4||

(8,8.5 ) antarikṣaṃ jālamāsījjāladaṇḍā diśo mahīḥ |
(8,8.5 ) tenābhidhāya dasyūnāṃ śakraḥ senāmapāvapat||5||

(8,8.6 ) bṛhaddhi jālaṃ bṛhataḥ śakrasya vājinīvataḥ |
(8,8.6 ) tena śatrūn abhi sarvān nyubja yathā na mucyātai katamaścanaiṣām ||6||

(8,8.7 ) bṛhatte jālaṃ bṛhata indra śūra sahasrārghasya śatavīryasya |
(8,8.7 ) tena śataṃ sahasramayutaṃ nyarbudaṃ jaghāna śakro dasyūnāmabhidhāya senayā ||7||

(8,8.8 ) ayaṃ loko jālamāsīcchakrasya mahato mahān |
(8,8.8 ) tenāhamindrajālenāmūṃstamasābhi dadhāmi sarvān ||8||

(8,8.9 ) sedirugrā vyṛddhirārtiścānapavācanā |
(8,8.9 ) śramastandrīśca mohaśca tairamūn abhi dadhāmi sarvān ||9||

(8,8.10 ) mṛtyave'mūn pra yachāmi mṛtyupāśairamī sitāḥ |
(8,8.10 ) mṛtyorye aghalā dūtāstebhya enān prati nayāmi baddhvā ||10|| {20}

(8,8.11 ) nayatāmūn mṛtyudūtā yamadūtā apombhata |
(8,8.11 ) paraḥsahasrā hanyantāṃ tṛṇeḍhvenān matyaṃ bhavasya ||11||

(8,8.12 ) sādhyā ekaṃ jāladaṇḍamudyatya yantyojasā |
(8,8.12 ) rudrā ekaṃ vasava ekamādityaireka udyataḥ ||12||

(8,8.13 ) viśve devāḥ upariṣṭādubjanto yantvojasā |
(8,8.13 ) madhyena ghnanto yantu senāmaṅgiraso mahīm ||13||

(8,8.14 ) vanaspatīn vānaspatyān oṣadhīruta vīrudhaḥ |
(8,8.14 ) dvipāccatuṣpādiṣṇāmi yathā senāmamūṃ hanan ||14||

(8,8.15 ) gandharvāpsarasaḥ sarpān devān puṇyajanān pitṝn |
(8,8.15 ) dṛṣṭān adṛṣṭān iṣṇāmi yathā senāmamūṃ hanan ||15||

(8,8.16 ) ima uptā mṛtyupāśā yān ākramya na mucyase |
(8,8.16 ) amuṣyā hantu senāyā idaṃ kūṭaṃ sahasraśaḥ ||16||

(8,8.17 ) gharmaḥ samiddho agnināyaṃ homaḥ sahasrahaḥ |
(8,8.17 ) bhavaśca pṛśnibāhuśca śarva senāmamūṃ hatam ||17||

(8,8.18 ) mṛtyorāṣamā padyantāṃ kṣudhaṃ sediṃ vadhaṃ bhayam |
(8,8.18 ) indraścākṣujālābhyāṃ śarva senāmamūṃ hatam ||18||

(8,8.19 ) parājitāḥ pra trasatāmitrā nuttā dhāvata brahmaṇā |
(8,8.19 ) bṛhaspatipranuttānāṃ māmīṣāṃ moci kaścana ||19||

(8,8.20 ) ava padyantāmeṣāmāyudhāni mā śakan pratidhāmiṣum |
(8,8.20 ) athaiṣāṃ bahu bibhyatāmiṣavaḥ ghnantu marmaṇi ||20||

(8,8.21 ) saṃ krośatāmenān dyāvāpṛthivī samantarikṣaṃ saha devatābhiḥ |
(8,8.21 ) mā jñātāraṃ mā pratiṣṭhāṃ vidanta mitho vighnānā upa yantu mṛtyum ||21||

(8,8.22 ) diśaścatasro'śvataryo devarathasya purodāśāḥ śaphā antarikṣamuddhiḥ |
(8,8.22 ) dyāvāpṛthivī pakṣasī ṛtavo'bhīśavo'ntardeśāḥ kimkarā vākparirathyam ||22||

(8,8.23 ) saṃvatsaro rathaḥ parivatsaro rathopastho virāḍīṣāgnī rathamukham |
(8,8.23 ) indraḥ savyaṣṭhāścandramāḥ sārathiḥ ||23||

(8,8.24 ) ito jayeto vi jaya saṃ jaya jaya svāhā |
(8,8.24 ) ime jayantu parāmī jayantāṃ svāhaibhyo durāhāmībhyaḥ |
(8,8.24 ) nīlalohitenāmūn abhyavatanomi ||24|| {21}



(8,9.1 ) kutastau jātau katamaḥ so ardhaḥ kasmāllokātkatamasyāḥ pṛthivyāḥ |
(8,9.1 ) vatsau virājaḥ salilādudaitāṃ tau tvā pṛchāmi katareṇa dugdhā ||1||

(8,9.2 ) yo akrandayatsalilaṃ mahitvā yoniṃ kṛtvā tribhujaṃ śayānaḥ |
(8,9.2 ) vatsaḥ kāmadugho virājaḥ sa guhā cakre tanvaḥ parācaiḥ ||2||

(8,9.3 ) yāni trīṇi bṛhanti yeṣāṃ caturthaṃ viyunakti vācam |
(8,9.3 ) brahmainadvidyāttapasā vipaścidyasminn ekaṃ yujyate yasminn ekam ||3||

(8,9.4 ) bṛhataḥ pari sāmāni ṣaṣṭhātpañcādhi nirmitā |
(8,9.4 ) bṛhadbṛhatyā nirmitaṃ kuto'dhi bṛhatī mitā ||4||

(8,9.5 ) bṛhatī pari mātrāyā māturmātrādhi nirmitā |
(8,9.5 ) māyā ha jajñe māyāyā māyāyā mātalī pari ||5||

(8,9.6 ) vaiśvānarasya pratimopari dyauryāvadrodasī vibabādhe agniḥ |
(8,9.6 ) tataḥ ṣaṣṭhādāmuto yanti stomā udito yantyabhi ṣaṣṭhamahnaḥ ||6||

(8,9.7 ) ṣaṭtvā pṛchāma ṛṣayaḥ kaśyapeme tvaṃ hi yuktaṃ yuyukṣe yogyaṃ ca |
(8,9.7 ) virājamāhurbrahmaṇaḥ pitaraṃ tāṃ no vi dhehi yatidhā sakhibhyaḥ ||7||

(8,9.8 ) yāṃ pracyutāmanu yajñāḥ pracyavanta upatiṣṭhanta upatiṣṭhamānām |
(8,9.8 ) yasyā vrate prasave yakṣamejati sā virāṭṛṣayaḥ parame vyoman ||8||

(8,9.9 ) aprāṇaiti prāṇena prāṇatīnāṃ virāṭsvarājamabhyeti paścāt|
(8,9.9 ) viśvaṃ mṛśantīmabhirūpāṃ virājaṃ paśyanti tve na tve paśyantyenām ||9||

(8,9.10 ) ko virājo mithunatvaṃ pra veda ka ṛtūn ka u kalpamasyāḥ |
(8,9.10 ) kramān ko asyāḥ katidhā vidugdhān ko asyā dhāma katidhā vyuṣṭīḥ ||10|| {22}

(8,9.11 ) iyameva sā yā prathamā vyauchadāsvitarāsu carati praviṣṭā |
(8,9.11 ) mahānto asyāṃ mahimāno antarvadhūrjigāya navagajjanitrī ||11||

(8,9.12 ) chandaḥpakṣe uṣasā pepiśāne samānaṃ yonimanu saṃ careme |
(8,9.12 ) sūryapatnī saṃ carataḥ prajānatī ketumatī ajare bhūriretasā ||12||

(8,9.13 ) ṛtasya panthāmanu tisra āgustrayo gharmā anu reta āguḥ |
(8,9.13 ) prajāmekā jinvatyūrjamekā rāṣṭramekā rakṣati devayūnām ||13||

(8,9.14 ) agnīṣomāvadadhuryā turīyāsīdyajñasya pakṣāvṛṣayaḥ kalpayantaḥ |
(8,9.14 ) gāyatrīṃ triṣṭubhaṃ jagatīmanuṣṭubhaṃ bṛhadarkīṃ yajamānāya svarābharantīm ||14||

(8,9.15 ) pañca vyuṣṭīranu pañca dohā gāṃ pañcanāmnīmṛtavo'nu pañca |
(8,9.15 ) pañca diśaḥ pañcadaśena kḷptāstā ekamūrdhnīrabhi lokamekam ||15||

(8,9.16 ) ṣaṭjātā bhūtā prathamajā ṛtasya ṣaṭu sāmāni ṣaṭahaṃ vahanti |
(8,9.16 ) ṣaṭyogaṃ sīramanu sāmasāma ṣaṭāhurdyāvāpṛthivīḥ ṣaṭurvīḥ ||16||

(8,9.17 ) ṣaḍāhuḥ śītān ṣaḍu māsa uṣṇān ṛtuṃ no brūta yatamo'tiriktaḥ |
(8,9.17 ) sapta suparṇāḥ kavayo ni ṣeduḥ sapta chandāṃsyanu sapta dīkṣāḥ ||17||

(8,9.18 ) sapta homāḥ samidho ha sapta madhūni sapta ṛtavo ha sapta |
(8,9.18 ) saptājyāni pari bhūtamāyan tāḥ saptagṛdhrā iti śuśrumā vayam ||18||

(8,9.19 ) sapta chandāṃsi caturuttarāṇyanyo anyasminn adhyārpitāni |
(8,9.19 ) kathaṃ stomāḥ prati tiṣṭhanti teṣu tāni stomeṣu kathamārpitāni ||19||

(8,9.20 ) kathaṃ gāyatrī trivṛtaṃ vyāpa kathaṃ triṣṭuppañcadaśena kalpate |
(8,9.20 ) trayastriṃśena jagatī kathamanuṣṭupkathamekaviṃśaḥ ||20|| {23}

(8,9.21 ) aṣṭa jātā bhūtā prathamajā ṛtasyāṣṭendra ṛtvijo daivyā ye |
(8,9.21 ) aṣṭayoniraditiraṣṭaputrāstamīṃ rātrimabhi havyameti ||21||

(8,9.22 ) itthaṃ śreyo manyamānedamāgamaṃ yuṣmākaṃ sakhye ahamasmi śevā |
(8,9.22 ) samānajanmā kraturasti vaḥ śivaḥ sa vaḥ sarvāḥ saṃ carati prajānan ||22||
(8,9.23 ) aṣṭendrasya ṣaḍyamasya ṛṣīṇāṃ sapta saptadhā |
(8,9.23 ) apo manuṣyān oṣadhīstāmu pañcānu secire ||23||

(8,9.24 ) kevalīndrāya duduhe hi gṛṣṭirvaśaṃ pīyūṣaṃ prathamaṃ duhānā |
(8,9.24 ) athātarpayaccaturaścaturdhā devān manuṣyāmasurān uta ṛṣīn ||24||

(8,9.25 ) ko nu gauḥ ka ekaṛṣiḥ kimu dhāma kā āśiṣaḥ |
(8,9.25 ) yakṣaṃ pṛthivyāmekavṛdekartuḥ katamo nu saḥ ||25||

(8,9.26 ) eko gaureka ekaṛṣirekaṃ dhāmaikadhāśiṣaḥ |
(8,9.26 ) yakṣaṃ pṛthivyāmekavṛdekarturnāti ricyate ||26|| {24}



(8,10.1 ) virāḍvā idamagra āsīttasyā jātāyāḥ sarvamabibhediyamevedaṃ bhaviṣyatīti ||1||

(8,10.2 ) sodakrāmatsā gārhapatye nyakrāmat|
(8,10.2 ) gṛhamedhī gṛhapatirbhavati ya evaṃ veda ||2||

(8,10.3 ) sodakrāmatsāhavanīye nyakrāmat|
(8,10.3 ) yantyasya devā devahūtiṃ priyo devānāṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||3||

(8,10.4 ) sodakrāmatsā dakṣiṇāgnau nyakrāmat|
(8,10.4 ) yajñarto dakṣiṇīyo vāsateyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||4||

(8,10.5 ) sodakrāmatsā sabhāyāṃ nyakrāmat|
(8,10.5 ) yantyasya sabhāṃ sabhyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||5||

(8,10.6 ) sodakrāmatsā samitau nyakrāmat|
(8,10.6 ) yantyasya samitiṃ sāmityo bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||6||

(8,10.7 ) sodakrāmatsāmantraṇe nyakrāmat|
(8,10.7 ) yantyasyāmantraṇamāmantraṇīyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||7|| {25}

(8,10.8 ) sodakrāmatsāntarikṣe caturdhā vikrāntātiṣṭhat||8||

(8,10.9 ) tāṃ devamanuṣyā abruvann iyameva tadveda yadubhaya upajīvememāmupa hvayāmahā iti ||9||

(8,10.10 ) tāmupāhvayanta ||10||

(8,10.11 ) ūrja ehi svadha ehi sūnṛta ehīrāvatyehīti ||11||

(8,10.12 ) tasyā indro vatsa āsīdgāyatryabhidhānyabhramūdhaḥ ||12||

(8,10.13 ) bṛhacca rathaṃtaraṃ ca dvau stanāvāstāṃ yajñāyajñiyaṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca dvau ||13||

(8,10.14 ) oṣadhīreva rathaṃtareṇa devā aduhran vyaco bṛhatā ||14||

(8,10.15 ) apo vāmadevyena yajñaṃ yajñāyajñiyena ||15||

(8,10.16 ) oṣadhīrevāsmai rathaṃtaraṃ duhe vyaco bṛhat||16||

(8,10.17 ) apo vāmadevyaṃ yajñaṃ yajñāyajñiyaṃ ya veda ||17|| {26}

(8,10.18 ) sodakrāmatsā vanaspatīn āgachattāṃ vanaspatayo'ghnata sā saṃvatsare samabhavat|
(8,10.18 ) tasmādvanaspatīnāṃ saṃvatsare vṛkṇamapi rohati vṛścate'syāpriyo bhrātṛvyo ya evaṃ veda ||18||

(8,10.19 ) sodakrāmatsā pitṝn āgachattāṃ pitaro'ghnata sā māsi samabhavat|
(8,10.19 ) tasmātpitṛbhyo māsyupamāsyaṃ dadati pra pitṛyāṇaṃ panthāṃ jānāti ya evaṃ veda ||19||

(8,10.20 ) sodakrāmatsā devān āgachattāṃ devā aghnata sārdhamāse samabhavat|
(8,10.20 ) tasmāddevebhyo'rdhamāse vaṣaṭkurvanti pra devayānaṃ panthāṃ jānāti ya evaṃ veda ||20||

(8,10.21 ) sodakrāmatsā manuṣyān āgachattāṃ manuṣyā aghnata sā sadyaḥ samabhavat|
(8,10.21 ) tasmān manuṣyebhya ubhayadyurupa harantyupāsya gṛhe haranti ya evaṃ veda ||21|| {27}

(8,10.22 ) sodakrāmatsāsurān āgachattāmasurā upāhvayanta māya ehīti |
(8,10.22 ) tasyā virocanaḥ prāhrādirvatsa āsīdayaspātraṃ pātram |
(8,10.22 ) tāṃ dvimūrdhārtvyo'dhoktāṃ māyāmevādhok||
(8,10.22 ) tāṃ māyāmasurā upa jīvantyupajīvanīyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||22||

(8,10.23 ) sodakrāmatsā pitṝn āgachattāṃ pitara upāhvayanta svadha ehīti |
(8,10.23 ) tasyā yamo rājā vatsa āsīdrajatapātraṃ pātram |
(8,10.23 ) tāmantako mārtyavo'dhoktāṃ svadhāmevādhok|
(8,10.23 ) tāṃ svadhāṃ pitara upa jīvantyupajīvanīyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||23||

(8,10.24 ) sodakrāmatsā manuṣyān āgachattāṃ manuṣyā upāhvayanterāvatyehīti |
(8,10.24 ) tasyā manurvaivasvato vatsa āsītpṛthivī pātram |
(8,10.24 ) tāṃ pṛthī vainyo'dhoktāṃ kṛṣiṃ ca sasyaṃ cādhok|
(8,10.24 ) te svadhāṃ kṛṣiṃ ca sasyaṃ ca manuṣyā upa jīvanti kṛṣṭarādhirupajīvanīyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||24||

(8,10.25 ) sodakrāmatsā saptaṛṣīn āgachattāṃ saptaṛṣaya upāhvayanta brahmaṇvatyehīti |
(8,10.25 ) tasyāḥ somo rājā vatsa āsīcchandaḥ pātram |
(8,10.25 ) tāṃ bṛhaspatirāṅgiraso'dhoktāṃ brahma ca tapaścādhok|
(8,10.25 ) tadbrahma ca tapaśca saptaṛṣaya upa jīvanti brahmavarcasyupajīvanīyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||25|| {28}

(8,10.26 ) sodakrāmatsā devān āgachattāṃ devā upāhvayantorja ehīti |
(8,10.26 ) tasyā indro vatsa āsīccamasaḥ pātram |
(8,10.26 ) tāṃ devaḥ savitādhoktāmūrjāmevādhok|
(8,10.26 ) tāmūrjāṃ devā upa jīvantyupajīvanīyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||26||

(8,10.27 ) sodakrāmatsā gandharvāpsarasa āgachattāṃ gandharvāpsarasa upāhvayanta puṇyagandha ehīti |
(8,10.27 ) tasyāścitrarathaḥ sauryavarcaso vatsa āsītpuṣkaraparṇaṃ pātram |
(8,10.27 ) tāṃ vasuruciḥ sauryavarcaso'dhoktāṃ puṇyameva gandhamadhok|
(8,10.27 ) taṃ puṇyaṃ gandhaṃ gandharvāpsarasa upa jīvanti puṇyagandhirupajīvanīyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||27||

(8,10.28 ) sodakrāmatsetarajanān āgachattāmitarajanā upāhvayanta tirodha ehīti |
(8,10.28 ) tasyāḥ kubero vaiśravaṇo vatsa āsīdāmapātraṃ pātram |
(8,10.28 ) tāṃ rajatanābhiḥ kaberako'dhoktāṃ tirodhāmevādhok|
(8,10.28 ) tāṃ tirodhāmatirajanā pitara upa jīvanti tiro dhatte sarvaṃ pāpmānamupajīvanīyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||28||

(8,10.29 ) sodakrāmatsā sarpān āgachattāṃ sarpā upāhvayanta viṣavatyehīti |
(8,10.29 ) tasyāstakṣako vaiśaleyo vatsa āsīdalābupātraṃ pātram |
(8,10.29 ) tāṃ dhṛtarāṣṭra airāvato'dhoktāṃ viṣamevādhok|
(8,10.29 ) tadviṣaṃ sarvā upa jīvantyupajīvanīyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||29|| {29}

(8,10.30 ) tadyasmā evaṃ viduṣe'lābunābhiṣiñcetpratyāhanyāt||30||

(8,10.31 ) na ca pratyāhanyān manasā tvā pratyāhanmīti pratyāhanyāt||31||

(8,10.32 ) yatpratyāhanti viṣameva tatpratyāhanti ||32||

(8,10.33 ) viṣamevāsyāpriyaṃ bhrātṛvyamanuviṣicyate ya evaṃ veda ||33|| {30}


(9,1.1 ) divaspṛthivyā antarikṣātsamudrādagnervātān madhukaśā hi jajñe |
(9,1.1 ) tāṃ cāyitvāmṛtaṃ vasānāṃ hṛdbhiḥ prajāḥ prati nandanti sarvāḥ ||1||

(9,1.2 ) mahatpayo viśvarūpamasyāḥ samudrasya tvota reta āhuḥ |
(9,1.2 ) yata aiti madhukaśā rarāṇā tatprāṇastadamṛtaṃ niviṣṭam ||2||

(9,1.3 ) paśyantyasyāścaritaṃ pṛthivyāṃ pṛthaṅnaro bahudhā mīmāṃsamānāḥ |
(9,1.3 ) agnervātān madhukaśā hi jajñe marutāmugrā naptiḥ ||3||

(9,1.4 ) mātādityānāṃ duhitā vasūnāṃ prāṇaḥ prajānāmamṛtasya nābhiḥ |
(9,1.4 ) hiraṇyavarṇā madhukaśā ghṛtācī mahān bhargaścarati martyeṣu ||4||

(9,1.5 ) madhoḥ kaśāmajanayanta devāstasyā garbho abhavadviśvarūpaḥ |
(9,1.5 ) taṃ jātaṃ taruṇaṃ piparti mātā sa jāto viśvā bhuvanā vi caṣṭe ||5||

(9,1.6 ) kastaṃ pra veda ka u taṃ ciketa yo asyā hṛdaḥ kalaśaḥ somadhāno akṣitaḥ |
(9,1.6 ) brahmā sumedhāḥ so asmin madeta ||6||

(9,1.7 ) sa tau pra veda sa u tau ciketa yāvasyāḥ stanau sahasradhārāvakṣitau |
(9,1.7 ) ūrjaṃ duhāte anapasphurantau ||7||

(9,1.8 ) hiṅkarikratī bṛhatī vayodhā uccairghoṣābhyeti yā vratam |
(9,1.8 ) trīn gharmān abhi vāvaśānā mimāti māyuṃ payate payobhiḥ ||8||

(9,1.9 ) yāmāpīnāmupasīdantyāpaḥ śākvarā vṛṣabhā ye svarājaḥ |
(9,1.9 ) te varṣanti te varṣayanti tadvide kāmamūrjamāpaḥ ||9||

(9,1.10 ) stanayitnuste vākprajāpate vṛṣā śuṣmaṃ kṣipasi bhūmyāmadhi |
(9,1.10 ) agnervātān madhukaśā hi jajñe marutāmugrā naptiḥ ||10|| {1}

(9,1.11 ) yathā somaḥ prātaḥsavane aśvinorbhavati priyaḥ |
(9,1.11 ) evā me aśvinā varca ātmani dhriyatām ||11||

(9,1.12 ) yathā somo dvitīye savana indrāgnyorbhavati priyaḥ |
(9,1.12 ) evā ma indrāgnī varca ātmani dhriyatām ||12||

(9,1.13 ) yathā somastṛtīye savana ṛbhūṇāṃ bhavati priyaḥ |
(9,1.13 ) evā ma ṛbhavo varca ātmani dhriyatām ||13||

(9,1.14 ) madhu janiṣīya madhu vaṃsiṣīya |
(9,1.14 ) payasvān agna āgamaṃ taṃ mā saṃ sṛja varcasā ||14||
(9,1.15 ) saṃ māgne varcasā sṛja saṃ prajayā samāyuṣā |
(9,1.15 ) vidyurme asya devā indro vidyātsaha ṛṣibhiḥ ||15||

(9,1.16 ) yathā madhu madhukṛtaḥ saṃbharanti madhāvadhi |
(9,1.16 ) evā me aśvinā varca ātmani dhriyatām ||16||

(9,1.17 ) yathā makṣāḥ idaṃ madhu nyañjanti madhāvadhi |
(9,1.17 ) evā me aśvinā varcastejo balamojaśca dhriyatām ||17||

(9,1.18 ) yadgiriṣu parvateṣu goṣvaśveṣu yan madhu |
(9,1.18 ) surāyāṃ sicyamānāyāṃ yattatra madhu tan mayi ||18||

(9,1.19 ) aśvinā sāragheṇa mā madhunāṅktaṃ śubhaspatī |
(9,1.19 ) yathā varcasvatīṃ vācamāvadāni janāmanu ||19||

(9,1.20 ) stanayitnuste vākprajāpate vṛṣā śuṣmaṃ kṣipasi bhūmyāṃ divi |
(9,1.20 ) tāṃ paśava upa jīvanti sarve teno seṣamūrjaṃ piparti ||20||

(9,1.21 ) pṛthivī daṇḍo'ntarikṣaṃ garbho dyauḥ kaśā vidyutprakaśo hiraṇyayo binduḥ ||21||

(9,1.22 ) yo vai kaśāyāḥ sapta madhūni veda madhumān bhavati |
(9,1.22 ) brāhmaṇaśca rājā ca dhenuścānaḍvāṃśca vrīhiśca yavaśca madhu saptamam ||22||

(9,1.23 ) madhumān bhavati madhumadasyāhāryaṃ bhavati |
(9,1.23 ) madhumato lokān jayati ya evaṃ veda ||23||

(9,1.24 ) yadvīdhre stanayati prajāpatireva tatprajābhyaḥ prādurbhavati |
(9,1.24 ) tasmātprācīnopavītastiṣṭhe prajāpate'nu mā budhyasveti |
(9,1.24 ) anvenaṃ prajā anu prajāpatirbudhyate ya evaṃ veda ||24|| {2}



(9,2.1 ) sapatnahanamṛṣabhaṃ ghṛtena kāmaṃ śikṣāmi haviṣājyena |
(9,2.1 ) nīcaiḥ sapatnān mama padaya tvamabhiṣṭuto mahatā vīryeṇa ||1||

(9,2.2 ) yan me manaso na priyaṃ cakṣuṣo yan me babhasti nābhinandati |
(9,2.2 ) tadduṣvapnyaṃ prati muñcāmi sapatne kāmaṃ stutvodahaṃ bhideyam ||2||

(9,2.3 ) duṣvapnyaṃ kāma duritaṃ ca kamāprajastāmasvagatāmavartim |
(9,2.3 ) ugra īśānaḥ prati muñca tasmin yo asmabhyamaṃhūraṇā cikitsāt||3||

(9,2.4 ) nudasva kāma pra ṇudasva kāmāvartiṃ yantu mama ye sapatnāḥ |
(9,2.4 ) teṣāṃ nuttānāmadhamā tamāṃsyagne vāstūni nirdaha tvam ||4||

(9,2.5 ) sā te kāma duhitā dhenurucyate yāmāhurvācaṃ kavayo virājam |
(9,2.5 ) tayā sapatnān pari vṛṅgdhi ye mama paryenān prāṇaḥ paśavo jīvanaṃ vṛṇaktu ||5||

(9,2.6 ) kāmasyendrasya varuṇasya rājño viṣṇorbalena savituḥ savena |
(9,2.6 ) agnerhotreṇa pra ṇude sapatnāṃ chambīva nāvamudakeṣu dhīraḥ ||6||

(9,2.7 ) adhyakṣo vājī mama kāma ugraḥ kṛṇotu mahyamasapatnameva |
(9,2.7 ) viśve devā mama nāthaṃ bhavantu sarve devā havamā yantu ma imam ||7||

(9,2.8 ) idamājyaṃ ghṛtavajjuṣāṇāḥ kāmajyeṣṭhā iha mādayadhvam |
(9,2.8 ) kṛṇvanto mahyamasapatnameva ||8||

(9,2.9 ) indrāgnī kāma sarathaṃ hi bhūtvā nīcaiḥ sapatnān mama pādayāthaḥ |
(9,2.9 ) teṣāṃ pannānāmadhamā tamāṃsyagne vāstūnyanunirdaha tvam ||9||

(9,2.10 ) jahi tvaṃ kāma mama ye sapatnā andhā tamāṃsyava pādayainān |
(9,2.10 ) nirindriyā arasāḥ santu sarve mā te jīviṣuḥ katamaccanāhaḥ ||10|| {3}

(9,2.11 ) avadhītkāmo mama ye sapatnā uruṃ lokamakaran mahyamedhatum |
(9,2.11 ) mahyaṃ namantāṃ pradiśaścatasro mahyaṃ ṣaḍurvīrghṛtamā vahantu ||11||

(9,2.12 ) te'dharāñcaḥ pra plavantāṃ chinnā nauriva bandhanāt|
(9,2.12 ) na sāyakapraṇuttānāṃ punarasti nivartanam ||12||

(9,2.13 ) agniryava indro yavaḥ somo yavaḥ |
(9,2.13 ) yavayāvāno devā yavayantvenam ||13||

(9,2.14 ) asarvavīraścaratu praṇutto dveṣyo mitrānāṃ parivargyaḥ svānām |
(9,2.14 ) uta pṛthivyāmava syanti vidyuta ugro vo devaḥ pra mṛṇatsapatnān ||14||

(9,2.15 ) cyutā ceyaṃ bṛhatyacyutā ca vidyudbibharti stanayitnūṃśca sarvān |
(9,2.15 ) udyann ādityo draviṇena tejasā nīcaiḥ sapatnān nudatāṃ me sahasvān ||15||

(9,2.16 ) yatte kāma śarma trivarūthamudbhu brahma varma vitatamanativyādhyaṃ kṛtam |
(9,2.16 ) tena sapatnān pari vṛṅgdhi ye mama paryenān prāṇaḥ paśavo jīvanaṃ vṛṇaktu ||16||

(9,2.17 ) yena devā asurān prāṇudanta yenendro dasyūn adhamaṃ tamo nināya |
(9,2.17 ) tena tvaṃ kāma mama ye sapatnāstān asmāllokātpra ṇudasva dūram ||17||

(9,2.18 ) yathā devā asurān prāṇudanta yathendro dasyūn adhamaṃ tamo babādhe |
(9,2.18 ) tathā tvaṃ kāma mama ye sapatnāstān asmāllokātpra ṇudasva dūram ||18||

(9,2.19 ) kāmo jajñe prathamo nainaṃ devā āpuḥ pitaro na martyāḥ |
(9,2.19 ) tatastvamasi jyāyān viśvahā mahāṃstasmai te kāma nama itkṛnomi ||19||

(9,2.20 ) yāvatī dyāvāpṛthivī varimṇā yāvadāpaḥ siṣyaduryāvadagniḥ |
(9,2.20 ) tatastvamasi jyāyān viśvahā mahāṃstasmai te kāma nama itkṛṇomi ||20|| {4}

(9,2.21 ) yāvatīrdiśaḥ pradiśo viṣūcīryāvatīrāśā abhicakṣaṇā divaḥ |
(9,2.21 ) tatastvamasi jyāyān viśvahā mahāṃstasmai te kāma nama itkṛṇomi ||21||

(9,2.22 ) yāvatīrbhṛṅgā jatvaḥ kurūravo yāvatīrvaghā vṛkṣasarpyo babhūvuḥ |
(9,2.22 ) tatastvamasi jyāyān viśvahā mahāṃstasmai te kāma nama itkṛṇomi ||22||

(9,2.23 ) jyāyān nimiṣato'si tiṣṭhato jyāyāntsamudrādasi kāma manyo |
(9,2.23 ) tatastvamasi jyāyān viśvahā mahāṃstasmai te kāma nama itkṛnomi ||23||

(9,2.24 ) na vai vātaścana kāmamāpnoti nāgniḥ sūryo nota candramāḥ |
(9,2.24 ) tatastvamasi jyāyān viśvahā mahāṃstasmai te kāma nama itkṛṇomi ||24||

(9,2.25 ) yāste śivāstanvaḥ kāma bhadrā yābhiḥ satyaṃ bhavati yadvṛṇiṣe |
(9,2.25 ) tābhiṣṭvamasmāmabhisaṃviśasvānyatra pāpīrapa veśayā dhiyaḥ ||25|| {5}


(9,3.1 ) upamitāṃ pratimitāmatho parimitāmuta |
(9,3.1 ) śālāyā viśvavārāyā naddhāni vi cṛtāmasi ||1||

(9,3.2 ) yatte naddhaṃ viśvavāre pāśo granthiśca yaḥ kṛtaḥ |
(9,3.2 ) bṛhaspatirivāhaṃ balaṃ vācā vi sraṃsayāmi tat||2||

(9,3.3 ) ā yayāma saṃ babarha granthīṃścakāra te dṛḍhān |
(9,3.3 ) parūṃṣi vidvāṃ chastevendreṇa vi cṛtāmasi ||3||

(9,3.4 ) vaṃśānāṃ te nahanānāṃ prāṇāhasya tṛṇasya ca |
(9,3.4 ) pakṣāṇāṃ viśvavāre te naddhāni vi cṛtāmasi ||4||

(9,3.5 ) saṃdaṃśānāṃ paladānāṃ pariṣvañjalyasya ca |
(9,3.5 ) idaṃ mānasya patnyā naddhāni vi cṛtāmasi ||5||

(9,3.6 ) yāni te'ntaḥ śikyānyābedhū raṇyāya kam |
(9,3.6 ) pra te tāni cṛtāmasi śivā mānasya patni na uddhitā tanve bhava ||6||

(9,3.7 ) havirdhānamagniśālaṃ patnīnāṃ sadanaṃ sadaḥ |
(9,3.7 ) sado devānāmasi devi śāle ||7||

(9,3.8 ) akṣumopaśaṃ vitataṃ sahasrākṣaṃ viṣūvati |
(9,3.8 ) avanaddhamabhihitaṃ brahmaṇā vi cṛtāmasi ||8||

(9,3.9 ) yastvā śāle pratigṛhṇāti yena cāsi mitā tvam |
(9,3.9 ) ubhau mānasya patni tau jīvatāṃ jaradaṣṭī ||9||

(9,3.10 ) amutrainamā gachatāddṛḍhā naddhā pariṣkṛtā |
(9,3.10 ) yasyāste vicṛtāmasyaṅgamaṅgaṃ paruṣparuḥ ||10|| {6}

(9,3.11 ) yastvā śāle nimimāya saṃjabhāra vanaspatīn |
(9,3.11 ) prajāyai cakre tvā śāle parameṣṭhī prajāpatiḥ ||11||

(9,3.12 ) namastasmai namo dātre śālāpataye ca kṛṇmaḥ |
(9,3.12 ) namo'gnaye pracarate puruṣāya ca te namaḥ ||12||

(9,3.13 ) gobhyo aśvebhyo namo yacchālāyāṃ vijāyate |
(9,3.13 ) vijāvati prajāvati vi te pāśāṃścṛtāmasi ||13||

(9,3.14 ) agnimantaśchādayasi puruṣān paśubhiḥ saha |
(9,3.14 ) vijāvati prajāvati vi te pāśāṃścṛtāmasi ||14||

(9,3.15 ) antarā dyāṃ ca pṛthivīṃ ca yadvyacastena śālāṃ prati gṛhṇāmi ta imām |
(9,3.15 ) yadantarikṣaṃ rajaso vimānaṃ tatkṛṇve'hamudaraṃ śevadhibhyaḥ |
(9,3.15 ) tena śālāṃ prati gṛhṇāmi tasmai ||15||

(9,3.16 ) ūrjasvatī payasvatī pṛthivyāṃ nimitā mitā |
(9,3.16 ) viśvānnaṃ bibhratī śāle mā hiṃsīḥ pratigṛhṇataḥ ||16||

(9,3.17 ) tṛṇairāvṛtā paladān vasānā rātrīva śālā jagato niveśanī |
(9,3.17 ) mitā pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhasi hastinīva padvatī ||17||

(9,3.18 ) iṭasya te vi cṛtāmyapinaddhamaporṇuvan |
(9,3.18 ) varuṇena samubjitāṃ mitraḥ prātarvyubjatu ||18||

(9,3.19 ) brahmaṇā śālāṃ nimitāṃ kavibhirnimitāṃ mitām |
(9,3.19 ) indrāgnī rakṣatāṃ śālāmamṛtau somyaṃ sadaḥ ||19||

(9,3.20 ) kulāye'dhi kulāyaṃ kośe kośaḥ samubjitaḥ |
(9,3.20 ) tatra marto vi jāyate yasmādviśvaṃ prajāyate ||20|| {7}

(9,3.21 ) yā dvipakṣā catuṣpakṣā ṣaṭpakṣā yā nimīyate |
(9,3.21 ) aṣṭāpakṣāṃ daśapakṣāṃ śālāṃ mānasya patnīmagnirgarbha ivā śaye ||21||

(9,3.22 ) pratīcīṃ tvā pratīcīnaḥ śāle praimyahiṃsatīm |
(9,3.22 ) agnirhyantarāpaśca ṛtasya prathamā dvāḥ ||22||

(9,3.23 ) imā āpaḥ pra bharāmyayakṣmā yakṣmanāśanīḥ |
(9,3.23 ) gṛhān upa pra sīdāmyamṛtena sahāgninā ||23||

(9,3.24 ) mā naḥ pāśaṃ prati muco gururbhāro laghurbhava |
(9,3.24 ) vadhūmiva tvā śāle yatrakāmaṃ bharāmasi ||24||

(9,3.25 ) prācyā diśaḥ śālāyā namo mahimne svāhā devebhyaḥ svāhyebhyaḥ ||25||

(9,3.26 ) dakṣiṇāyā diśaḥ śālāyā namo mahimne svāhā devebhyaḥ svāhyebhyaḥ ||26||

(9,3.27 ) pratīcyā diśaḥ śālāyā namo mahimne svāhā devebhyaḥ svāhyebhyaḥ ||27||

(9,3.28 ) udīcyā diśaḥ śālāyā namo mahimne svāhā devebhyaḥ svāhyebhyaḥ ||28||

(9,3.29 ) dhruvāyā diśaḥ śālāyā namo mahimne svāhā devebhyaḥ svāhyebhyaḥ ||29||

(9,3.30 ) ūrdhvāyā diśaḥ śālāyā namo mahimne svāhā devebhyaḥ svāhyebhyaḥ ||30||

(9,3.31 ) diśodiśaḥ śālāyā namo mahimne svāhā devebhyaḥ svāhyebhyaḥ ||31|| {8}



(9,4.1 ) sāhasrastveṣa ṛṣabhaḥ payasvān viśvā rūpāṇi vakṣaṇāsu bibhrat|
(9,4.1 ) bhadraṃ dātre yajamānāya śīkṣan bārhaspatya usriyastantumātān ||1||

(9,4.2 ) apāṃ yo agne pratimā babhūva prabhūḥ sarvasmai pṛthivīva devī |
(9,4.2 ) pitā vatsānāṃ patiraghnyānāṃ sāhasre poṣe api naḥ kṛṇotu ||2||

(9,4.3 ) pumān antarvāntsthaviraḥ payasvān vasoḥ kabandhamṛṣabho bibharti |
(9,4.3 ) tamindrāya pathibhirdevayānairhutamagnirvahatu jātavedāḥ ||3||

(9,4.4 ) pitā vatsānāṃ patiraghnyānāmatho pitā mahatāṃ gargarāṇām |
(9,4.4 ) vatso jarāyu pratidhukpīyūṣa āmikṣā ghṛtaṃ tadvasya retaḥ ||4||

(9,4.5 ) devānāṃ bhāga upanāha eṣo'pāṃ rasa oṣadhīnāṃ ghṛtasya |
(9,4.5 ) somasya bhakṣamavṛṇīta śakro bṛhann adrirabhavadyaccharīram ||5||

(9,4.6 ) somena pūrṇaṃ kalaśaṃ bibharṣi tvastā rupāṇāṃ janitā paśūnām |
(9,4.6 ) śivāste santu prajanva iha yā imā nyasmabhyaṃ svadhite yacha yā amūḥ ||6||

(9,4.7 ) ājaṃ bibharti ghṛtamasya retaḥ sāhasraḥ poṣastamu yajñamāhuḥ |
(9,4.7 ) indrasya rūpamṛṣabho vasānaḥ so asmān devāḥ śiva aitu dattaḥ ||7||

(9,4.8 ) indrasyaujo varuṇasya bāhū aśvinoraṃsau marutāmiyaṃ kakut|
(9,4.8 ) bṛhaspatiṃ saṃbhṛtametamāhurye dhīrāsaḥ kavayo ye manīṣiṇaḥ ||8||

(9,4.9 ) daivīrviśaḥ payasvān ā tanoṣi tvāmindraṃ tvāṃ sarasvantamāhuḥ |
(9,4.9 ) sahasraṃ sa ekamukhā dadāti yo brāhmaṇa ṛṣabhamājuhoti ||9||
(9,4.10 ) bṛhaspatiḥ savitā te vayo dadhau tvaṣṭurvāyoḥ paryātmā ta ābhṛtaḥ |
(9,4.10 ) antarikṣe manasā tvā juhomi barhiṣṭe dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām ||10|| {9}

(9,4.11 ) ya indra iva deveṣu goṣveti vivāvadat|
(9,4.11 ) tasya ṛṣabhasyāṅgāni brahmā saṃ stautu bhadrayā ||11||

(9,4.12 ) pārśve āstāmanumatyā bhagasyāstāmanūvṛjau |
(9,4.12 ) aṣṭhīvantāvabravīn mitro mamaitau kevalāviti ||12||

(9,4.13 ) bhasadāsīdādityānāṃ śroṇī āstāṃ bṛhaspateḥ |
(9,4.13 ) puchaṃ vātasya devasya tena dhūnotyoṣadhīḥ ||13||

(9,4.14 ) gudā āsantsinīvālyāḥ sūryāyāstvacamabruvan |
(9,4.14 ) utthāturabruvan pada ṛṣabhaṃ yadakalpayan ||14||

(9,4.15 ) kroḍa āsījjāmiśaṃsasya somasya klaśo dhṛtaḥ |
(9,4.15 ) devāḥ saṃgatya yatsarva ṛṣabhaṃ vyakalpayan ||15||

(9,4.16 ) te kuṣṭhikāḥ saramāyai kurmebhyo adadhuḥ śaphān |
(9,4.16 ) ūbadhyamasya kītebhyaḥ śvavartebhyo adhārayan ||16||

(9,4.17 ) śṛṅgābhyāṃ rakṣa ṛṣatyavartiṃ hanti cakṣuṣā |
(9,4.17 ) śṛṇoti bhadraṃ karṇābhyāṃ gavāṃ yaḥ patiraghnyaḥ ||17||

(9,4.18 ) śatayājaṃ sa yajate nainaṃ dunvantyagnayaḥ |
(9,4.18 ) jinvanti viśve taṃ devā yo brāhmaṇa ṛṣabhamājuhoti ||18||

(9,4.19 ) brāhmaṇebhya ṛṣabhaṃ dattvā varīyaḥ kṛṇute manaḥ |
(9,4.19 ) puṣṭiṃ so aghnyānāṃ sve goṣṭhe'va paśyate ||19||

(9,4.20 ) gāvaḥ santu prajāḥ santvatho astu tanūbalam |
(9,4.20 ) tatsarvamanu manyantāṃ devā ṛṣabhadāyine ||20||

(9,4.21 ) ayaṃ pipāna indra idrayiṃ dadhātu cetanīm |
(9,4.21 ) ayaṃ dhenuṃ sudughāṃ nityavatsāṃ vaśaṃ duhāṃ vipaścitaṃ paro divaḥ ||21||

(9,4.22 ) piśaṅgarūpo nabhaso vayodhā aindraḥ śuṣmo viśvarūpo na āgan |
(9,4.22 ) āyurasmabhyaṃ dadhatprajāṃ ca rāyaśca poṣairabhi naḥ sacatām ||22||

(9,4.23 ) upehopaparcanāsmin goṣṭha upa pṛñca naḥ |
(9,4.23 ) upa ṛṣabhasya yadreta upendra tava vīryam ||23||

(9,4.24 ) etaṃ vo yuvānaṃ prati dadhmo atra tena krīḍantīścarata vaśāmanu |
(9,4.24 ) mā no hāsiṣṭa januṣā subhāgā rāyaśca poṣairabhi naḥ sacadhvam ||24|| {10}



(9,5.1 ) ā nayaitamā rabhasva sukṛtāṃ lokamapi gachatu prajānan |
(9,5.1 ) tīrtvā tamāṃsi bahudhā mahāntyajo nākamā kramatāṃ tṛtīyam ||1||

(9,5.2 ) indrāya bhāgaṃ pari tvā nayāmyasmin yajñe yajamānāya sūrim |
(9,5.2 ) ye no dviṣantyanu tān rabhasvānāgaso yajamānasya vīrāḥ ||2||

(9,5.3 ) pra pado'va nenigdhi duścaritaṃ yaccacāra śuddhaiḥ śaphairā kramatāṃ prajānan |
(9,5.3 ) tīrtvā tamāṃsi bahudhā vipaśyann ajo nākamā kramatāṃ tṛtīyam ||3||

(9,5.4 ) anuchya śyāmena tvacametāṃ viśastaryathāparvasinā mābhi maṃsthāḥ |
(9,5.4 ) mābhi druhaḥ paruśaḥ kalpayainaṃ tṛtīye nāke adhi vi śrayainam ||4||

(9,5.5 ) ṛcā kumbhīmadhyagnau śrayāmyā siñcodakamava dhehyenam |
(9,5.5 ) paryādhattāgninā śamitāraḥ śṛto gachatu sukṛtāṃ yatra lokaḥ ||5||

(9,5.6 ) utkrāmātaḥ pari cedataptastaptāccaroradhi nākaṃ tṛtīyam |
(9,5.6 ) agneragniradhi saṃ babhūvitha jyotiṣmantamabhi lokaṃ jayaitam ||6||

(9,5.7 ) ajo agnirajamu jyotirāhurajaṃ jīvatā brahmaṇe deyamāhuḥ |
(9,5.7 ) ajastamāṃsyapa hanti dūramasmiṃlloke śraddadhānena dattaḥ ||7||

(9,5.8 ) pañcaudanaḥ pañcadhā vi kramatāmākraṃsyamānastrīṇi jyotīṃṣi |
(9,5.8 ) ījānānāṃ sukṛtāṃ prehi madhyaṃ tṛtīye nāke adhi vi śrayasva ||8||

(9,5.9 ) ajā roha sukṛtāṃ yatra lokaḥ śarabho na catto'ti durgānyeṣaḥ |
(9,5.9 ) pañcaudano brahmaṇe dīyamānaḥ sa dātāraṃ tṛptyā tarpayāti ||9||

(9,5.10 ) ajastrināke tridive tripṛṣṭhe nākasya pṛṣṭhe dadivāṃsaṃ dadhāti |
(9,5.10 ) pañcaudano brahmaṇe dīyamāno viśvarūpā dhenuḥ kāmadughāsyekā ||10|| {11}

(9,5.11 ) etadvo jyotiḥ pitarastṛtīyaṃ pañcaudanaṃ brahmaṇe'jaṃ dadāti |
(9,5.11 ) ajastamāṃsyapa hanti dūramasmiṃlloke śraddadhānena dattaḥ ||11||

(9,5.12 ) ījānānāṃ sukṛtāṃ lokamīpsan pañcaudanaṃ brahmaṇe'jaṃ dadāti |
(9,5.12 ) sa vyāptimabhi lokaṃ jayaitaṃ śivo'smabhyaṃ pratigṛhīto astu ||12||

(9,5.13 ) ajo hyagnerajaniṣṭa śokādvipro viprasya sahaso vipaścit|
(9,5.13 ) iṣṭaṃ pūrtamabhipūrtaṃ vaṣaṭkṛtaṃ taddevā ṛtuśaḥ kalpayantu ||13||

(9,5.14 ) amotaṃ vāso dadyāddhiraṇyamapi dakṣiṇām |
(9,5.14 ) tathā lokāntsamāpnoti ye divyā ye ca pārthivāḥ ||14||

(9,5.15 ) etāstvājopa yantu dhārāḥ somyā devīrghṛtapṛṣṭhā madhuścutaḥ |
(9,5.15 ) stabhān pṛthivīmuta dyāṃ nākasya pṛṣṭhe'dhi saptaraśmau ||15||

(9,5.16 ) ajo'syaja svargo'si tvayā lokamaṅgirasaḥ prājānan |
(9,5.16 ) taṃ lokaṃ puṇyaṃ pra jñeṣam ||16||

(9,5.17 ) yenā sahasraṃ vahasi yenāgne sarvavedasam |
(9,5.17 ) tenemaṃ yajñaṃ no vaha svardeveṣu gantave ||17||

(9,5.18 ) ajaḥ pakvaḥ svarge loke dadhāti pañcaudano nirṛtiṃ bādhamānaḥ |
(9,5.18 ) tena lokāntsūryavato jayema ||18||

(9,5.19 ) yaṃ brāhmaṇe nidadhe yaṃ ca vikṣu yā vipruṣa odanānāmajasya |
(9,5.19 ) sarvaṃ tadagne sukṛtasya loke jānītān naḥ saṃgamane pathīnām ||19||

(9,5.20 ) ajo vā idamagne vyakramata tasyora iyamabhavaddyauḥ pṛṣṭiham |
(9,5.20 ) antarikṣaṃ madhyaṃ diśaḥ pārśve samudrau kukṣī ||20|| {12}

(9,5.21 ) satyaṃ ca rtaṃ ca cakṣuṣī viśvaṃ satyaṃ śraddhā prāṇo virāṭśiraḥ |
(9,5.21 ) eṣa vā aparimito yajño yadajaḥ pañcaudanaḥ ||21||

(9,5.22 ) aparimitameva yajñamāpnotyaparimitaṃ lokamava runddhe |
(9,5.22 ) yo'jaṃ pañcaudanaṃ dakṣiṇājyotiṣaṃ dadāti ||22||

(9,5.23 ) nāsyāsthīni bhindyān na majjño nirdhayet|
(9,5.23 ) sarvamenaṃ samādāyedamidaṃ pra veśayet||23||

(9,5.24 ) idamidamevāsya rūpaṃ bhavati tenainaṃ saṃ gamayati |
(9,5.24 ) iṣaṃ maha ūrjamasmai duhe yo'jaṃ pañcaudanaṃ dakṣiṇājyotiṣaṃ dadāti ||24||

(9,5.25 ) pañca rukmā pañca navāni vastrā pañcāsmai dhenavaḥ kāmadughā bhavanti |
(9,5.25 ) yo'jaṃ pañcaudanaṃ dakṣiṇājyotiṣaṃ dadāti ||25||

(9,5.26 ) pañca rukmā jyotirasmai bhavanti varma vāsāṃsi tanve bhavanti |
(9,5.26 ) svargaṃ lokamaśnute yo'jaṃ pañcaudanaṃ dakṣiṇājyotiṣaṃ dadāti ||26||

(9,5.27 ) yā pūrvaṃ patiṃ vittvā'thānyaṃ vindate'param |
(9,5.27 ) pañcaudanaṃ ca tāvajaṃ dadāto na vi yoṣataḥ ||27||

(9,5.28 ) samānaloko bhavati punarbhuvāparaḥ patiḥ |
(9,5.28 ) yo'jaṃ pañcaudanaṃ dakṣiṇājyotiṣaṃ dadāti ||28||

(9,5.29 ) anupūrvavatsāṃ dhenumanaḍvāhamupabarhaṇam |
(9,5.29 ) vāso hiraṇyaṃ dattvā te yanti divamuttamām ||29||

(9,5.30 ) ātmānaṃ pitaraṃ putraṃ pautraṃ pitāmaham |
(9,5.30 ) jāyāṃ janitrīṃ mātaraṃ ye priyāstān upa hvaye ||30|| {13}

(9,5.31 ) yo vai naidāghaṃ nāma rtuṃ veda |
(9,5.31 ) eṣa vai naidāgho nāma rturyadajaḥ pañcaudanaḥ |
(9,5.31 ) nirevāpriyasya bhrātṛvyasya śriyaṃ dahati bhavatyātmanā |

(9,5.32 ) yo vai kurvantaṃ nāma rtuṃ veda |
(9,5.32 ) kurvatīṃkurvatīmevāpriyasya bhrātṛvyasya śriyamā datte |
(9,5.32 ) eṣa vai kurvan nāma rturyadajaḥ pañcaudanaḥ |

(9,5.33 ) yo vai saṃyantaṃ nāma rtuṃ veda |
(9,5.33 ) saṃyatīṃsaṃyatīmevāpriyasya bhrātṛvyasya śriyamā datte |
(9,5.33 ) eṣa vai saṃyan nāma rturyadajaḥ pañcaudanaḥ |

(9,5.34 ) yo vai pinvantaṃ nāma rtuṃ veda |
(9,5.34 ) pinvatīṃpinvatīmevāpriyasya bhrātṛvyasya śriyamā datte |
(9,5.34 ) eṣa vai pinvan nāma rturyadajaḥ pañcaudanaḥ |

(9,5.35 ) yo vā udyantaṃ nāma rtuṃ veda |
(9,5.35 ) udyatīṃudyatīmevāpriyasya bhrātṛvyasya śriyamā datte |
(9,5.35 ) eṣa vā udyann nāma rturyadajaḥ pañcaudanaḥ |

(9,5.36 ) yo vā abhibhuvaṃ nāma rtuṃ veda |
(9,5.36 ) abhibhavantīmabhibhavantīmevāpriyasya bhrātṛvyasya śriyamā datte |
(9,5.36 ) eṣa vā abhibhūrnāma rturyadajaḥ pañcaudanaḥ |

(9,5.37 ) ajaṃ ca pacata pañca caudanān |
(9,5.37 ) sarvā diśaḥ saṃmanasaḥ sadhrīcīḥ sāntardeśāḥ prati gṛhnantu ta etam ||37||

(9,5.38 ) tāste rakṣantu tava tubhyametaṃ tābhya ājyaṃ haviridaṃ juhomi ||38|| {14}



(9,6.1 ) yo vidyādbrahma pratyakṣaṃ parūṃṣi yasya saṃbhārā ṛco yasyānūkyam ||1||

(9,6.2 ) sāmāni yasya lomāni yajurhṛdayamucyate paristaraṇamiddhaviḥ ||2||

(9,6.3 ) yadvā atithipatiratithīn pratipaśyati devayajanaṃ prekṣate ||3||

(9,6.4 ) yadabhivadati dīkṣāmupaiti yadudakaṃ yācatyapaḥ pra ṇayati ||4||

(9,6.5 ) yā eva yajña āpaḥ praṇīyante tā eva tāḥ ||5||

(9,6.6 ) yattarpaṇamāharanti ya evāgnīṣomīyaḥ paśurbadhyate sa eva saḥ ||6||

(9,6.7 ) yadāvasathān kalpayanti sadohavirdhānānyeva tatkalpayanti ||7||

(9,6.8 ) yadupastṛṇanti barhireva tat||8||

(9,6.9 ) yadupariśayanamāharanti svargameva tena lokamava runddhe ||9||

(9,6.10 ) yatkaśipūpabarhaṇamāharanti paridhaya eva te ||10||

(9,6.11 ) yadāñjanābhyañjanamāharantyājyameva tat||11||

(9,6.12 ) yatpurā pariveṣātsvādamāharanti purodāśāveva tau ||12||

(9,6.13 ) yadaśanakṛtaṃ hvayanti haviṣkṛtameva taddhvayanti ||13||

(9,6.14 ) ye vrīhayo yavā nirupyante'ṃśava eva te ||14||

(9,6.15 ) yānyulūkhalamusalāni grāvāṇa eva te ||15||

(9,6.16 ) śūrpaṃ pavitraṃ tuṣā ṛjīṣābhiṣavaṇīrāpaḥ ||16||

(9,6.17 ) srugdarvirnekṣaṇamāyavanaṃ droṇakalaśāḥ kumbhyo vāyavyāni pātrāṇīyameva kṛṣṇājinam ||17|| {15}

(9,6.18 ) yajamānabrāhmaṇaṃ vā etadatithipatiḥ kurute yadāhāryāṇi prekṣata idaṃ bhūyā3 idā3 iti ||18||

(9,6.19 ) yadāha bhūya uddhareti prāṇameva tena varṣīyāṃsaṃ kurute ||19||

(9,6.20 ) upa harati havīṃṣyā sādayati ||20||

(9,6.21 ) teṣāmāsannānāmatithirātman juhoti ||21||

(9,6.22 ) srucā hastena prāṇe yūpe srukkāreṇa vaṣaṭkāreṇa ||22||

(9,6.23 ) ete vai priyāścāpriyāśca rtvijaḥ svargaṃ lokaṃ gamayanti yadatithayaḥ ||23||

(9,6.24 ) sa ya evaṃ vidvān na dviṣann aśnīyān na dviṣato'nnamaśnīyān na mīmāṃsitasya na mīmāṃsamānasya ||24||

(9,6.25 ) sarvo vā eṣa jagdhapāpmā yasyānnamaśnanti ||25||

(9,6.26 ) sarvo vā eso'jagdhapāpmā yasyānnaṃ nāśnanti ||26||

(9,6.27 ) sarvadā vā eṣa yuktagrāvārdrapavitro vitatādhvara āhṛtayajñakraturya upaharati ||27||

(9,6.28 ) prājāpatyo vā etasya yajño vitato ya upaharati ||28||

(9,6.29 ) prajāpatervā eṣa vikramān anuvikramate ya upaharati ||29||

(9,6.30 ) yo'tithīnāṃ sa āhavanīyo yo veśmani sa gārhapatyo yasmin pacanti sa dakṣiṇāgniḥ ||30|| {16}

(9,6.31 ) iṣṭaṃ ca vā eṣa pūrtaṃ ca gṛhāṇāmaśnāti yaḥ pūrvo'titheraśnāti ||31||

(9,6.32 ) payaśca vā eṣa rasaṃ ca gṛhāṇāmaśnāti yaḥ pūrvo'titheraśnāti ||32||

(9,6.33 ) ūrjāṃ ca vā eṣa sphātiṃ ca gṛhāṇāmaśnāti yaḥ pūrvo'titheraśnāti ||33||

(9,6.34 ) prajāṃ vā eṣa paśūṃśca gṛhāṇāmaśnāti yaḥ pūrvo'titheraśnāti ||34||

(9,6.35 ) kīrtiṃ vā eṣa yaśaśca gṛhāṇāmaśnāti yaḥ pūrvo'titheraśnāti ||35||

(9,6.36 ) śriyaṃ vā eṣa saṃvidaṃ ca gṛhāṇāmaśnāti yaḥ pūrvo'titheraśnāti ||36||

(9,6.37 ) eṣa vā atithiryacchrotriyastasmātpūrvo nāśnīyāt||37||

(9,6.38 ) aśitāvatyatithāvaśnīyādyajñasya sātmatvāya yajñasyāvichedāya tadvratam ||38||

(9,6.39 ) etadvā u svādīyo yadadhigavaṃ kṣīraṃ vā māṃsaṃ vā tadeva nāśnīyāt||39|| {17}

(9,6.40 ) sa ya evaṃ vidvān kṣīramupasicyopaharati |
(9,6.40 ) yāvadagniṣṭomeneṣṭvā susamṛddhenāvarunddhe tāvadenenāva runddhe ||40||

(9,6.41 ) sa ya evaṃ vidvāntsarpirupasicyopaharati |
(9,6.41 ) yāvadatirātreṇeṣṭvā susamṛddhenāvarunddhe tāvadenenāva runddhe ||41||

(9,6.42 ) sa ya evaṃ vidvān madhūpasicyopaharati |
(9,6.42 ) yāvadsattrasadyeneṣṭvā susamṛddhenāvarunddhe tāvadenenāva runddhe ||42||

(9,6.43 ) sa ya evaṃ vidvān māṃsamupasicyopaharati |
(9,6.43 ) yāvaddvādaśāheneṣṭvā susamṛddhenāvarunddhe tāvadenenāva runddhe ||43||

(9,6.44 ) sa ya evaṃ vidvān udakamupasicyopaharati |
(9,6.44 ) prajānāṃ prajananāya gachati pratiṣṭhāṃ priyaḥ prajānāṃ bhavati ya evaṃ vidvān upasicyopaharati ||44|| {18}

(9,6.45 ) tasmā uṣā hiṅkṛṇoti savitā pra stauti |
(9,6.45 ) bṛhaspatirūrjayodgāyati tvaṣṭā puṣṭyā prati harati viśve devā nidhanam ||2||
(9,6.45 ) nidhanaṃ bhūtyāḥ prajāyāḥ paśūnāṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||45||

(9,6.46 ) tasmā udyantsūryo hiṅkṛṇoti saṃgavaḥ pra stauti |
(9,6.46 ) madhyandina udgāyatyaparāhṇaḥ prati haratyastaṃyan nidhanam |
(9,6.46 ) nidhanaṃ bhūtyāḥ prajāyāḥ paśūnāṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||46||

(9,6.47 ) tasmā abhro bhavan hiṅkṛṇoti stanayan pra stauti |
(9,6.47 ) vidyotamānaḥ prati harati varṣann udgāyatyudgṛhṇan nidhanam |
(9,6.47 ) nidhanaṃ bhūtyāḥ prajāyāḥ paśūnāṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||47||

(9,6.48 ) atithīn prati paśyati hiṅkṛṇotyabhi vadati pra stautyudakaṃ yācatyudgāyati |
(9,6.48 ) upa harati prati haratyucchiṣṭaṃ nidhanam |
(9,6.48 ) nidhanaṃ bhūtyāḥ prajāyāḥ paśūnāṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||48|| {19}
(9,6.48 ) yatkṣattāraṃ hvayatyā śrāvayatyeva tat||49||

(9,6.50 ) yatpratiśṛṇoti pratyāśrāvayatyeva tat||50||

(9,6.51 ) yatpariveṣṭāraḥ pātrahastāḥ pūrve cāpare ca prapadyante camasādhvaryava eva te ||51||

(9,6.52 ) teṣāṃ na kaścanāhotā ||52||

(9,6.53 ) yadvā atithipatiratithīn pariviṣya gṛhān upodaityavabhṛthameva tadupāvaiti ||53||

(9,6.54 ) yatsabhāgayati dakṣiṇāḥ sabhāgayati yadanutiṣṭhata udavasyatyeva tat||54||

(9,6.55 ) sa upahūtaḥ pṛthivyāṃ bhakṣayatyupahūtastasmin yatpṛthivyāṃ viśvarūpam ||55||

(9,6.56 ) sa upahūto'ntarikṣe bhakṣayatyupahūtastasmin yaddivi viśvarūpam ||56||

(9,6.57 ) sa upahūto divi bhakṣayatyupahūtastasmin yaddivi viśvarūpam ||57||

(9,6.58 ) sa upahūto deveṣu bhakṣayatyupahūtastasmin yaddivi viśvarūpam ||58||

(9,6.59 ) sa upahūto lokeṣu bhakṣayatyupahūtastasmin yaddivi viśvarūpam ||59||

(9,6.60 ) sa upahūta upahūtaḥ ||60||

(9,6.61 ) āpnotīmaṃ lokamāpnotyamum ||61||

(9,6.62 ) jyotiṣmato lokān jayati ya evaṃ veda ||62|| {20}



(9,7.1 ) prajāpatiśca parameṣṭhī ca śṛṅge indraḥ śiro agnirlalāṭaṃ yamaḥ kṛkāṭam ||1||

(9,7.2 ) somo rājā mastiṣko dyauruttarahanuḥ pṛthivyadharahanuḥ ||2||
(9,7.3 ) vidyujjihvā maruto dantā revatirgrīvāḥ kṛttikā skandhā gharmo vahaḥ ||3||

(9,7.4 ) viśvaṃ vāyuḥ svargo lokaḥ kṛṣṇadraṃ vidharaṇī niveṣyaḥ ||4||

(9,7.5 ) śyenaḥ kroto'ntarikṣaṃ pājasyaṃ bṛhaspatiḥ kakudbṛhatīḥ kīkasāḥ ||5||

(9,7.6 ) devānāṃ patnīḥ pṛṣṭaya upasadaḥ parśavaḥ ||6||

(9,7.7 ) mitraśca varuṇaścāṃsau tvaṣṭā cāryamā ca doṣaṇī mahādevo bāhū ||7||

(9,7.8 ) indrāṇī bhasadvāyuḥ puchaṃ pavamāno bālāḥ ||8||

(9,7.9 ) brahma ca kṣatraṃ ca śroṇī balamūrū ||9||

(9,7.10 ) dhātā ca savitā cāṣṭhīvantau jaṅghā gandharvā apsarasaḥ kuṣṭhikā aditiḥ śaphāḥ ||10||

(9,7.11 ) ceto hṛdayaṃ yakṛn medhā vrataṃ purītat||11||

(9,7.12 ) kṣutkukṣirirā vaniṣṭhuḥ parvatāḥ plāśayaḥ ||12||

(9,7.13 ) krodho vṛkkau manyurāṇḍau prajā śepaḥ ||13||

(9,7.14 ) nadī sūtrī varṣasya pataya stanā stanayitnurūdhaḥ ||14||

(9,7.15 ) viśvavyacāscarmauṣadhayo lomāni nakṣatrāṇi rūpam ||15||

(9,7.16 ) devajanā gudā manuṣyā āntrāṇyatrā udaram ||16||

(9,7.17 ) rakṣāṃsi lohitamitarajanā ūbadhyam ||17||

(9,7.18 ) abhraṃ pībo majjā nidhanam ||18||

(9,7.19 ) agnirāsīna utthito'śvinā ||19||

(9,7.20 ) indraḥ prāṅtiṣṭhan dakṣiṇā tiṣṭhan yamaḥ ||20||

(9,7.21 ) pratyaṅtiṣṭhan dhātodaṅtiṣṭhantsavitā ||21||

(9,7.22 ) tṛṇāni prāptaḥ somo rājā ||22||

(9,7.23 ) mitra īkṣamāṇa āvṛtta ānandaḥ ||23||

(9,7.24 ) yujyamāno vaiśvadevo yuktaḥ prajāpatirvimuktaḥ sarvam ||24||

(9,7.25 ) etadvai viśvarūpaṃ sarvarūpaṃ gorūpam ||25||

(9,7.26 ) upainaṃ viśvarūpāḥ sarvarūpāḥ paśavastiṣṭhanti ya evaṃ veda ||26|| {21}



(9,8.1 ) śīrṣaktiṃ śīrṣāmayaṃ karṇaśūlaṃ vilohitam |
(9,8.1 ) sarvaṃ śīrṣanyaṃ te rogaṃ bahirnirmantrayāmahe ||1||

(9,8.2 ) karṇābhyāṃ te kaṅkūṣebhyaḥ karṇaśūlaṃ visalpakam |
(9,8.2 ) sarvaṃ śīrṣanyaṃ te rogaṃ bahirnirmantrayāmahe ||2||

(9,8.3 ) yasya hetoḥ pracyavate yakṣmaḥ karṇato āsyataḥ |
(9,8.3 ) sarvaṃ śīrṣanyaṃ te rogaṃ bahirnirmantrayāmahe ||3||
(9,8.4 ) yaḥ kṛṇoti pramotamandhaṃ kṛṇoti pūruṣam |
(9,8.4 ) sarvaṃ śīrṣanyaṃ te rogaṃ bahirnirmantrayāmahe ||4||

(9,8.5 ) aṅgabhedamaṅgajvaraṃ viśvāṅgyaṃ visalpakam |
(9,8.5 ) sarvaṃ śīrṣanyaṃ te rogaṃ bahirnirmantrayāmahe ||5||

(9,8.6 ) yasya bhīmaḥ pratīkāśa udvepayati pūruṣam |
(9,8.6 ) takmānaṃ viśvaśāradaṃ bahirnirmantrayāmahe ||6||

(9,8.7 ) ya ūrū anusarpatyatho eti gavīnike |
(9,8.7 ) yakṣmaṃ te antaraṅgebhyo bahirnirmantrayāmahe ||7||

(9,8.8 ) yadi kāmādapakāmāddhṛdayājjāyate pari |
(9,8.8 ) hṛdo balāsamaṅgebhyo bahirnirmantrayāmahe ||8||

(9,8.9 ) harimāṇaṃ te aṅgebhyo'pvāmantarodarāt|
(9,8.9 ) yakṣmodhāmantarātmano bahirnirmantrayāmahe ||9||

(9,8.10 ) āso balāso bhavatu mūtraṃ bhavatvāmayat|
(9,8.10 ) yakṣmāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ viṣaṃ niravocamahaṃ tvat||10|| {22}

(9,8.11 ) bahirbilaṃ nirdravatu kāhābāhaṃ tavodarāt|
(9,8.11 ) yakṣmāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ viṣaṃ niravocamahaṃ tvat||11||

(9,8.12 ) udarātte klomno nābhyā hṛdayādadhi |
(9,8.12 ) yakṣmāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ viṣaṃ niravocamahaṃ tvat||12||

(9,8.13 ) yāḥ sīmānaṃ virujanti mūrdhānaṃ pratyarṣanīḥ |
(9,8.13 ) ahiṃsantīranāmayā nirdravantu bahirbilam ||13||

(9,8.14 ) yā hṛdayamuparṣantyanutanvanti kīkasāḥ |
(9,8.14 ) ahiṃsantīranāmayā nirdravantu bahirbilam ||14||

(9,8.15 ) yāḥ pārśve uparṣantyanunikṣanti pṛṣṭīḥ |
(9,8.15 ) ahiṃsantīranāmayā nirdravantu bahirbilam ||15||

(9,8.16 ) yāstiraścīḥ uparṣantyarṣaṇīrvakṣaṇāsu te |
(9,8.16 ) ahiṃsantīranāmayā nirdravantu bahirbilam ||16||

(9,8.17 ) yā gudā anusarpantyāntrāṇi mohayanti ca |
(9,8.17 ) ahiṃsantīranāmayā nirdravantu bahirbilam ||17||
