(9,8.18 ) yā majjño nirdhayanti parūṃṣi virujanti ca |
(9,8.18 ) ahiṃsantīranāmayā nirdravantu bahirbilam ||18||

(9,8.19 ) ye aṅgāni madayanti yakṣmāso ropaṇāstava |
(9,8.19 ) yakṣmāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ viṣaṃ niravocamahaṃ tvat||19||

(9,8.20 ) visalpasya vidradhasya vātīkārasya vālajeḥ |
(9,8.20 ) yakṣmāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ viṣaṃ niravocamahaṃ tvat||20||

(9,8.21 ) pādābhyāṃ te jānubhyāṃ śroṇibhyāṃ pari bhaṃsasaḥ |
(9,8.21 ) anūkādarṣaṇīruṣṇihābhyaḥ śīrṣṇo rogamanīnaśam ||21||

(9,8.22 ) saṃ te śīrṣṇaḥ kapālāni hṛdayasya ca yo vidhuḥ |
(9,8.22 ) udyann āditya raśmibhiḥ śīrṣṇo rogamanīnaśo'ṅgabhedamaśīśamaḥ ||22|| {23}



(9,9.1 ) asya vāmasya palitasya hotustasya bhrātā madhyamo astyaśnaḥ |
(9,9.1 ) tṛtīyo bhrātā ghṛtapṛṣṭho asyātrāpaśyaṃ viśpatiṃ saptaputram ||1||

(9,9.2 ) sapta yuñjanti rathamekacakrameko aśvo vahati saptanāmā |
(9,9.2 ) trinābhi cakramajaramanarvaṃ yatremā viśvā bhuvanādhi tasthuḥ ||2||

(9,9.3 ) imaṃ rathamadhi ye sapta tasthuḥ saptacakraṃ sapta vahantyaśvāḥ |
(9,9.3 ) sapta svasāro abhi saṃ navanta yatra gavāṃ nihitā sapta nāma ||3||

(9,9.4 ) ko dadarśa prathamaṃ jāyamānamasthanvantaṃ yadanasthā bibharti |
(9,9.4 ) bhūmyā asurasṛgātmā kva svitko vidvāṃsamupa gātpraṣṭumetat||4||

(9,9.5 ) iha bravītu ya īmaṅga vedāsya vāmasya nihitaṃ padaṃ veḥ |
(9,9.5 ) śīrṣṇaḥ kṣīraṃ duhrate gāvo asya vavriṃ vasānā udakaṃ padā'puḥ ||5||

(9,9.6 ) pākaḥ pṛchāmi manasā'vijānan devānāmenā nihitā padāni |
(9,9.6 ) vatse baṣkaye'dhi sapta tantūn vi tatnire kavaya otavā u ||6||

(9,9.7 ) acikitvāṃscikituṣaścidatra kavīn pṛchāmi vidvano na vidvān |
(9,9.7 ) vi yastastambha ṣaṭimā rajāṃsyajasya rūpe kimapi svidekam ||7||

(9,9.8 ) mātā pitaramṛta ā babhāja'dhītyagre manasā saṃ hi jagme |
(9,9.8 ) sā bībhatsurgarbharasā nividdhā namasvanta idupavākamīyuḥ ||8||

(9,9.9 ) yuktā mātāsiddhuri dakṣiṇāyā atiṣṭhadgarbho vṛjanīṣvantaḥ |
(9,9.9 ) amīmedvatso anu gāmapaśyadviśvarūpyaṃ triṣu yoganeṣu ||9||

(9,9.10 ) tisro matṝstrīn pitṝn bibhradeka urdhvastasthau nemava glāpayanta |
(9,9.10 ) mantrayante divo amuṣya pṛṣṭhe viśvavido vācamaviśvavinnām ||10|| {24}

(9,9.11 ) pañcāre cakre parivartamāne yasminn ātasthurbhuvanāni viśvā |
(9,9.11 ) tasya nākṣastapyate bhūribhāraḥ sanādeva na chidyate sanābhiḥ ||11||

(9,9.12 ) pañcapādaṃ pitaraṃ dvādaśākṛtiṃ diva āhuḥ pare ardhe purīṣiṇam |
(9,9.12 ) atheme anya upare vicakṣaṇe saptacakre ṣaḍara āhurarpitam ||12||

(9,9.13 ) dvādaśāraṃ nahi tajjarāya varvarti cakraṃ pari dyāmṛtasya |
(9,9.13 ) ā putrā agne mithunāso atra sapta śatāni viṃśatiśca tasthuḥ ||13||

(9,9.14 ) sanemi cakramajaraṃ vi vavṛta uttānāyāṃ daśa yuktā vahanti |
(9,9.14 ) sūryasya cakṣū rajasaityāvṛtaṃ yasminn ātasthurbhuvanāni viśvā ||14||

(9,9.15 ) striyaḥ satīstāmu me puṃsaḥ āhuḥ paśyadakṣaṇvānn vi cetadandhaḥ |
(9,9.15 ) kaviryaḥ putraḥ sa īmā ciketa yastā vijānātsa pituṣpitāsat||15||

(9,9.16 ) sākaṃjānāṃ saptathamāhurekajaṃ ṣaḍidyamā ṛṣayo devajā iti |
(9,9.16 ) teṣāmiṣṭāni vihitāni dhāmaśa sthātre rejante vikṛtāni rūpaśaḥ ||16||

(9,9.17 ) avaḥ pareṇa para enā avareṇa padā vatsaṃ bibhratī gaurudasthāt|
(9,9.17 ) sā kadrīcī kaṃ svidardhaṃ parāgātkva svitsūte nahi yūthe asmin ||17||

(9,9.18 ) avaḥ pareṇa pitaraṃ yo asya vedāvaḥ pareṇa para enāvareṇa |
(9,9.18 ) kavīyamānaḥ ka iha pra vocaddevaṃ manaḥ kuto adhi prajātam ||18||

(9,9.19 ) ye arvāñcastāmu parāca āhurye parāñcastāmu arvāca āhuḥ |
(9,9.19 ) indraśca yā cakrathuḥ soma tāni dhurā na yuktā rajaso vahanti ||19||

(9,9.20 ) dvā suparṇā sayujā sakhāyā samānaṃ vṛkṣaṃ pari ṣasvajāte |
(9,9.20 ) tayoranyaḥ pippalaṃ svādvattyanaśnann anyo abhi cākaśīti ||20||

(9,9.21 ) yasmin vṛkṣe madhvadaḥ suparṇā niviśante suvate cādhi viśve |
(9,9.21 ) tasya yadāhuḥ pippalaṃ svādvagre tan non naśadyaḥ pitaraṃ na veda ||21||

(9,9.22 ) yatrā suparṇā amṛtasya bhakṣamanimeṣaṃ vidathābhisvaranti |
(9,9.22 ) enā viśvasya bhuvanasya gopāḥ sa mā dhīraḥ pākamatrā viveśa ||22|| {25}



(9,10.1 ) yadgāyatre adhi gāyatramāhitaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ vā traiṣṭubhān niratakṣata |
(9,10.1 ) yadvā jagajjagatyāhitaṃ padaṃ ya ittadviduste amṛtatvamānuśuḥ ||1||

(9,10.2 ) gāyatreṇa prati mimīte arkamarkeṇa sāma traiṣṭubhena vākam |
(9,10.2 ) vākena vākaṃ dvipadā catuṣpadākṣareṇa mimate sapta vāṇīḥ ||2||

(9,10.3 ) jagatā sindhuṃ divyaskabhāyadrathaṃtare sūryaṃ paryapaśyat|
(9,10.3 ) gāyatrasya samidhastisra āhustato mahnā pra ririce mahitvā ||3||

(9,10.4 ) upa hvaye sudughāṃ dhenumetāṃ suhasto godhuguta dohadenām |
(9,10.4 ) śreṣṭhaṃ savaṃ savitā sāviṣan no'bhīddho gharmastadu ṣu pra vocat||4||

(9,10.5 ) hiṅkṛṇvatī vasupatnī vasūnāṃ vatsamichantī manasābhyāgāt|
(9,10.5 ) duhāmaśvibhyāṃ payo aghnyeyaṃ sā vardhatāṃ mahate saubhagāya ||5||

(9,10.6 ) gauramīmedabhi vatsaṃ miṣantaṃ mūrdhānaṃ hiṅṅakṛṇon mātavā u |
(9,10.6 ) sṛkvāṇaṃ gharmamabhi vāvaśānā mimāti māyuṃ payate payobhiḥ ||6||

(9,10.7 ) ayaṃ sa śiṅkte yena gaurabhivṛtā mimāti mayuṃ dhvasanāvadhi śritā |
(9,10.7 ) sā cittibhirni hi cakāra martyān vidyudbhavantī prati vavrimauhata ||7||

(9,10.8 ) anacchaye turagātu jīvamejaddhruvaṃ madhya ā pastyānām |
(9,10.8 ) jīvo mṛtasya carati svadhābhiramartyo martyenā sayoniḥ ||8||

(9,10.9 ) vidhuṃ dadrāṇaṃ salilasya pṛṣṭhe yuvānaṃ santaṃ palito jagāra |
(9,10.9 ) devasya paśya kāvyaṃ mahitvādya mamāra sa hyaḥ samāna ||9||

(9,10.10 ) ya īṃ cakāra na so asya veda ya īṃ dadarśa hirugin nu tasmāt|
(9,10.10 ) sa māturyonā parivīto antarbahuprajā nirṛtirā viveśa ||10|| {26}

(9,10.11 ) apaśyaṃ gopāmanipadyamānamā ca parā ca pathibhiścarantam |
(9,10.11 ) sa sadhrīcīḥ sa viṣūcīrvasāna ā varīvarti bhuvaneṣvantaḥ ||11||

(9,10.12 ) dyaurnaḥ pitā janitā nābhiratra bandhurno mātā pṛthivī mahīyam |
(9,10.12 ) uttānayoścamvoryonirantaratrā pitā duhiturgarbhamādhāt||12||

(9,10.13 ) pṛchāmi tvā paramantaṃ pṛthivyāḥ pṛchāmi vṛṣṇo aśvasya retaḥ |
(9,10.13 ) pṛchāmi viśvasya bhuvanasya nābhiṃ pṛchāmi vācaḥ paramaṃ vyoma ||13||
(9,10.14 ) iyaṃ vediḥ paro antaḥ pṛthivyā ayaṃ somo vṛṣṇo aśvasya retaḥ |
(9,10.14 ) ayaṃ yajño viśvasya bhuvanasya nābhirbrahmāyaṃ vācaḥ paramaṃ vyoma ||14||
(9,10.15 ) na vi jānāmi yadivedamasmi niṇyaḥ saṃnaddho manasā carāmi |
(9,10.15 ) yadā māgan prathamajā ṛtasyādidvāco aśnuve bhāgamasyāḥ ||15||

(9,10.16 ) apāṅprāṅeti svadhayā gṛbhīto'martyo martyenā sayoniḥ |
(9,10.16 ) tā śaśvantā viṣūcīnā viyantā nyanyaṃ cikyurna ni cikyuranyam ||16||

(9,10.17 ) saptārdhagarbhā bhuvanasya reto viṣṇostiṣṭhanti pradiśā vidharmaṇi |
(9,10.17 ) te dhītibhirmanasā te vipaścitaḥ paribhuvaḥ pari bhavanti viśvataḥ ||17||

(9,10.18 ) ṛco akṣare parame vyoman yasmin devā adhi viśve niṣeduḥ |
(9,10.18 ) yastan na veda kimṛcā kariṣyati ya ittadviduste amī samāsate ||18||

(9,10.19 ) ṛcaḥ padaṃ mātrayā kalpayanto'rdharcena cakḷpurviśvamejat|
(9,10.19 ) tripādbrahma pururūpaṃ vi taṣṭhe tena jīvanti pradiśaścatasraḥ ||19||

(9,10.20 ) sūyavasādbhagavatī hi bhūyā adhā vayaṃ bhagavantaḥ syāma |
(9,10.20 ) addhi tṛṇamaghnye viśvadānīṃ piba śuddhamudakamācarantī ||20|| {27}

(9,10.21 ) gaurin mimāya salilāni takṣatī ekapadī dvipadī sā catuṣpadī |
(9,10.21 ) aṣṭāpadī navapadī babhūvuṣī sahasrākṣarā bhuvanasya paṅktistasyāḥ samudrā adhi vi kṣaranti ||21||

(9,10.22 ) kṛṣṇaṃ niyānaṃ harayaḥ suparṇā apo vasānā divamutpatanti |
(9,10.22 ) tamāvavṛtrantsadanādṛtasyādidghṛtena pṛthivīṃ vyūduḥ ||22||

(9,10.23 ) apādeti prathamā padvatīnāṃ kastadvāṃ mitrāvaruṇā ciketa |
(9,10.23 ) garbho bhāraṃ bharatyā cidasyā ṛtaṃ piparti anṛtaṃ ni pāti ||23||

(9,10.24 ) virāḍvāgvirāṭpṛthivī virāḍantarikṣaṃ virāṭprajāpatiḥ |
(9,10.24 ) virāṇmṛtyuḥ sādhyānāmadhirājo babhūva tasya bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ vaśe sa me bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ vaśe kṛṇotu ||24||

(9,10.25 ) śakamayaṃ dhūmamārādapaśyaṃ viṣūvatā para enāvareṇa |
(9,10.25 ) ukṣāṇaṃ pṛśnimapacanta vīrāstāni dharmāṇi prathamānyāsan ||25||

(9,10.26 ) trayaḥ keśina ṛtuthā vi cakṣate saṃvatsare vapata eka eṣām |
(9,10.26 ) viśvamanyo abhicaṣṭe śacībhirdhrājirekasya dadṛśe na rūpam ||26||

(9,10.27 ) catvāri vākparimitā padāni tāni vidurbrāhmaṇā ye manīṣiṇaḥ |
(9,10.27 ) guhā trīṇi nihitā neṅgayanti turīyaṃ vāco manuṣyā vadanti ||27||

(9,10.28 ) indraṃ mitraṃ varuṇamagnimāhuratho divyaḥ sa suparṇo garutmān |
(9,10.28 ) ekaṃ sadviprā bahudhā vadantyagniṃ yamaṃ mātariśvānamāhuḥ ||28|| {28}


(10,1.1 ) yāṃ kalpayanti vahatau vadhūmiva viśvarūpāṃ hastakṛtāṃ cikitsavaḥ |
(10,1.1 ) sārādetvapa nudāma enām ||1||

(10,1.2 ) śīrṣaṇvatī nasvatī karṇiṇī kṛtyākṛtā saṃbhṛtā viśvarūpā |
(10,1.2 ) sārādetvapa nudāma enām ||2||

(10,1.3 ) śūdrakṛtā rājakṛtā strīkṛtā brahmabhiḥ kṛtā |
(10,1.3 ) jāyā patyā nutteva kartāraṃ bandhvṛchatu ||3||

(10,1.4 ) anayāhamoṣadhyā sarvāḥ kṛtyā adūduṣam |
(10,1.4 ) yāṃ kṣetre cakruryāṃ goṣu yāṃ vā te puruṣeṣu ||4||

(10,1.5 ) aghamastvaghakṛte śapathaḥ śapathīyate |
(10,1.5 ) pratyakpratiprahiṇmo yathā kṛtyākṛtaṃ hanat||5||

(10,1.6 ) pratīcīna āṅgiraso'dhyakṣo naḥ purohitaḥ |
(10,1.6 ) pratīcīḥ kṛtyā ākṛtyāmūn kṛtyākṛto jahi ||6||

(10,1.7 ) yastvovāca parehīti pratikūlamudāyyam |
(10,1.7 ) taṃ kṛtye'bhinivartasva māsmān icho anāgasaḥ ||7||

(10,1.8 ) yaste parūṃṣi saṃdadhau rathasyeva rbhurdhiyā |
(10,1.8 ) taṃ gacha tatra te'yanamajñātaste'yaṃ janaḥ ||8||

(10,1.9 ) ye tvā kṛtvālebhire vidvalā abhicāriṇaḥ |
(10,1.9 ) śaṃbhvidaṃ kṛtyādūṣaṇaṃ prativartma punaḥsaraṃ tena tvā snapayāmasi ||9||

(10,1.10 ) yaddurbhagāṃ prasnapitāṃ mṛtavatsāmupeyima |
(10,1.10 ) apaitu sarvaṃ matpāpaṃ draviṇaṃ mopa tiṣṭhatu ||10|| {1}

(10,1.11 ) yatte pitṛbhyo dadato yajñe vā nāma jagṛhuḥ |
(10,1.11 ) saṃdeśyātsarvasmātpāpādimā muñcantu tvauṣadhīḥ ||11||

(10,1.12 ) devainasātpitryān nāmagrāhātsaṃdeśyādabhiniṣkṛtāt|
(10,1.12 ) muñcantu tvā vīrudho vīryeṇa brahmaṇā ṛgbhiḥ payasā ṛṣīṇām ||12||

(10,1.13 ) yathā vātaścyāvayati bhūmyā reṇumantarikṣāccābhram |
(10,1.13 ) evā matsarvaṃ durbhūtaṃ brahmanuttamapāyati ||13||

(10,1.14 ) apa krāma nānadatī vinaddhā gardabhīva |
(10,1.14 ) kartṝn nakṣasveto nuttā brahmaṇā vīryāvatā ||14||

(10,1.15 ) ayaṃ panthāḥ kṛtyeti tvā nayāmo'bhiprahitāṃ prati tvā pra hiṇmaḥ |
(10,1.15 ) tenābhi yāhi bhañjatyanasvatīva vāhinī viśvarūpā kurūtinī ||15||

(10,1.16 ) parākte jyotirapathaṃ te arvāganyatrāsmadayanā kṛṇuṣva |
(10,1.16 ) pareṇehi navatiṃ nāvyā ati durgāḥ srotyā mā kṣaṇiṣṭhāḥ parehi ||16||

(10,1.17 ) vāta iva vṛkṣān ni mṛṇīhi pādaya mā gāmaśvaṃ puruṣamucchiṣa eṣām |
(10,1.17 ) kartṝn nivṛtyetaḥ kṛtye'prajāstvāya bodhaya ||17||

(10,1.18 ) yāṃ te barhiṣi yāṃ śmaśāne kṣetre kṛtyāṃ valagaṃ vā nicakhnuḥ |
(10,1.18 ) agnau vā tvā gārhapatye'bhiceruḥ pākaṃ santaṃ dhīratarā anāgasam ||18||

(10,1.19 ) upāhṛtamanubuddhaṃ nikhātaṃ vairaṃ tsāryanvavidāma kartram |
(10,1.19 ) tadetu yata ābhṛtaṃ tatrāśva iva vi vartatāṃ hantu kṛtyākṛtaḥ prajām ||19||

(10,1.20 ) svāyasā asayaḥ santi no gṛhe vidmā te kṛtye yatidhā parūṃṣi |
(10,1.20 ) uttiṣṭhaiva parehīto'jñāte kimihechasi ||20|| {2}

(10,1.21 ) grīvāste kṛtye pādau cāpi kartsyāmi nirdrava |
(10,1.21 ) indrāgnī asmān rakṣatāṃ yau prajānāṃ prajāvatī ||21||

(10,1.22 ) somo rājādhipā mṛḍitā ca bhūtasya naḥ patayo mṛḍayantu ||22||

(10,1.23 ) bhavāśarvāvasyatāṃ pāpakṛte kṛtyākṛte |
(10,1.23 ) duṣkṛte vidyutaṃ devahetim ||23||

(10,1.24 ) yadyeyatha dvipadī catuṣpadī kṛtyākṛtā saṃbhṛtā viśvarūpā |
(10,1.24 ) seto'ṣṭāpadī bhūtvā punaḥ parehi duchune ||24||

(10,1.25 ) abhyaktāktā svaraṃkṛtā sarvaṃ bharantī duritaṃ parehi |
(10,1.25 ) jānīhi kṛtye kartāraṃ duhiteva pitaraṃ svam ||25||

(10,1.26 ) parehi kṛtye mā tiṣṭho viddhasyeva padaṃ naya |
(10,1.26 ) mṛgaḥ sa mṛgayustvaṃ na tvā nikartumarhati ||26||

(10,1.27 ) uta hanti pūrvāsinaṃ pratyādāyāpara iṣvā |
(10,1.27 ) uta pūrvasya nighnato ni hantyaparaḥ prati ||27||

(10,1.28 ) etaddhi śṛṇu me vaco'thehi yata eyatha |
(10,1.28 ) yastvā cakāra taṃ prati ||28||

(10,1.29 ) anāgohatyā vai bhīmā kṛtye mā no gāmaśvaṃ puruṣaṃ vadhīḥ |
(10,1.29 ) yatrayatrāsi nihitā tatastvotthāpayāmasi parṇāllaghīyasī bhava ||29||

(10,1.30 ) yadi stha tamasāvṛtā jālenabhihitā iva |
(10,1.30 ) sarvāḥ saṃlupyetaḥ kṛtyāḥ punaḥ kartre pra hiṇmasi ||30||

(10,1.31 ) kṛtyākṛto valagino'bhiniṣkāriṇaḥ prajām |
(10,1.31 ) mṛṇīhi kṛtye mocchiṣo'mūn kṛtyākṛto jahi ||31||

(10,1.32 ) yathā sūryo mucyate tamasaspari rātriṃ jahātyuṣasaśca ketūn |
(10,1.32 ) evāhaṃ sarvaṃ durbhūtaṃ kartraṃ kṛtyākṛtā kṛtaṃ hastīva rajo duritaṃ jahāmi ||32|| {3}



(10,2.1 ) kena pārṣṇī ābhṛte pūruṣasya kena māṃsaṃ saṃbhṛtaṃ kena gulphau |
(10,2.1 ) kenāṅgulīḥ peśanīḥ kena khāni kenocchlaṅkhau madhyataḥ kaḥ pratiṣṭhām ||1||

(10,2.2 ) kasmān nu gulphāvadharāvakṛṇvann aṣṭhīvantāvuttarau puruṣasya |
(10,2.2 ) jaṅghe nirṛtya nyadadhuḥ kva svijjānunoḥ saṃdhī ka u tacciketa ||2||

(10,2.3 ) catuṣṭayaṃ yujate saṃhitāntaṃ jānubhyāmūrdhvaṃ śithiraṃ kabandham |
(10,2.3 ) śroṇī yadūrū ka u tajjajāna yābhyāṃ kusindhaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ babhūva ||3||

(10,2.4 ) kati devāḥ katame ta āsan ya uro grīvāścikyuḥ puruṣasya |
(10,2.4 ) kati stanau vyadadhuḥ kaḥ kaphodau kati skandhān kati pṛṣṭīracinvan ||4||

(10,2.5 ) ko asya bāhū samabharadvīryāṃ karavāditi |
(10,2.5 ) aṃsau ko asya taddevaḥ kusindhe adhyā dadhau ||5||

(10,2.6 ) kaḥ sapta khāni vi tatarda śīrṣaṇi karṇāvimau nāsike cakṣaṇī mukham |
(10,2.6 ) yeṣāṃ purutrā vijayasya mahnani catuṣpādo dvipado yanti yāmam ||6||

(10,2.7 ) hanvorhi jihvāmadadhātpurūcīmadhā mahīmadhi śiśrāya vācam |
(10,2.7 ) sa ā varīvarti bhuvaneṣvantarapo vasānaḥ ka u tacciketa ||7||

(10,2.8 ) mastiṣkamasya yatamo lalātaṃ kakāṭikāṃ prathamo yaḥ kapālam |
(10,2.8 ) citvā cityaṃ hanvoḥ pūruṣasya divaṃ ruroha katamaḥ sa devaḥ ||8||

(10,2.9 ) priyāpriyāṇi bahulā svapnaṃ saṃbādhatandyaḥ |
(10,2.9 ) ānandān ugro nandāṃśca kasmādvahati pūruṣaḥ ||9||

(10,2.10 ) ārtiravartirnirṛtiḥ kuto nu puruṣe'matiḥ |
(10,2.10 ) rāddhiḥ samṛddhiravyṛddhirmatiruditayaḥ kutaḥ ||10|| {4}

(10,2.11 ) ko asminn āpo vyadadhātviṣūvṛtaḥ purūvṛtaḥ sindhusṛtyāya jātāḥ |
(10,2.11 ) tīvrā aruṇā lohinīstāmradhūmrā ūrdhvā avācīḥ puruṣe tiraścīḥ ||11||

(10,2.12 ) ko asmin rūpamadadhātko mahmānaṃ ca nāma ca |
(10,2.12 ) gātuṃ ko asmin kaḥ ketuṃ kaścaritrāni puruṣe ||12||

(10,2.13 ) ko asmin prāṇamavayatko apānaṃ vyānamu |
(10,2.13 ) samānamasmin ko devo'dhi śiśrāya puruṣe ||13||

(10,2.14 ) ko asmin yajñamadadhādeko devo'dhi puruṣe |
(10,2.14 ) ko asmintsatyaṃ ko'nṛtaṃ kuto mṛtyuḥ kuto'mṛtam ||14||

(10,2.15 ) ko asmai vāsaḥ paryadadhātko asyāyurakalpayat|
(10,2.15 ) balaṃ ko asmai prāyachatko asyākalpayajjavam ||15||

(10,2.16 ) kenāpo anvatanuta kenāharakarodruce |
(10,2.16 ) uṣasaṃ kenānvainddha kena sāyaṃbhavaṃ dade ||16||

(10,2.17 ) ko asmin reto nyadadhāttanturā tāyatāmiti |
(10,2.17 ) medhāṃ ko asminn adhyauhatko bāṇaṃ ko nṛto dadhau ||17||

(10,2.18 ) kenemāṃ bhūmimaurṇotkena paryabhavaddivam |
(10,2.18 ) kenābhi mahnā parvatān kena karmāṇi puruṣaḥ ||18||

(10,2.19 ) kena parjanyamanveti kena somaṃ vicakṣaṇam |
(10,2.19 ) kena yajñaṃ ca śraddhāṃ ca kenāsmin nihitaṃ manaḥ ||19||

(10,2.20 ) kena śrotriyamāpnoti kenemaṃ parameṣṭhinam |
(10,2.20 ) kenemamagniṃ pūruṣaḥ kena saṃvatsaraṃ mame ||20|| {5}

(10,2.21 ) brahma śrotriyamāpnoti brahmemaṃ parameṣṭhinam |
(10,2.21 ) brahmemamagniṃ pūruṣo brahma saṃvatsaraṃ mame ||21||

(10,2.22 ) kena devāmanu kṣiyati kena daivajanīrviśaḥ |
(10,2.22 ) kenedamanyan nakṣatraṃ kena satkṣatramucyate ||22||

(10,2.23 ) brahma devāmanu kṣiyati brahma daivajanīrviśaḥ |
(10,2.23 ) brahmedamanyan nakṣatraṃ brahma satkṣatramucyate ||23||

(10,2.24 ) keneyaṃ bhūmirvihitā kena dyauruttarā hitā |
(10,2.24 ) kenedamūrdhvaṃ tiryakcāntarikṣaṃ vyaco hitam ||24||

(10,2.25 ) brahmaṇā bhūmirvihitā brahma dyauruttarā hitā |
(10,2.25 ) brahmedamūrdhvaṃ tiryakcāntarikṣaṃ vyaco hitam ||25||

(10,2.26 ) mūrdhānamasya saṃsīvyātharvā hṛdayaṃ ca yat|
(10,2.26 ) mastiṣkādūrdhvaḥ prairayatpavamāno'dhi śīrṣataḥ ||26||

(10,2.27 ) tadvā atharvaṇaḥ śiro devakośaḥ samubjitaḥ |
(10,2.27 ) tatprāṇo abhi rakṣati śiro annamatho manaḥ ||27||

(10,2.28 ) ūrdhvo nu sṛṣṭā3 tiryaṅnu sṛṣṭā3 sarvā diśaḥ puruṣa ā babhūvā3  |
(10,2.28 ) puraṃ yo brahmaṇo veda yasyāḥ puruṣa ucyate ||28||

(10,2.29 ) yo vai tāṃ brahmaṇo vedāmṛtenāvṛtāṃ puram |
(10,2.29 ) tasmai brahma ca brāhmāśca cakṣuḥ prāṇaṃ prajāṃ daduḥ ||29||

(10,2.30 ) na vai taṃ cakṣurjahāti na prāṇo jarasaḥ purā |
(10,2.30 ) puraṃ yo brahmaṇo veda yasyāḥ puruṣa ucyate ||30||

(10,2.31 ) aṣṭācakrā navadvārā devānāṃ pūrayodhyā |
(10,2.31 ) tasyāṃ hiraṇyayaḥ kośaḥ svargo jyotiṣāvṛtaḥ ||31||

(10,2.32 ) tasmin hiraṇyaye kośe tryare tripratiṣṭhite |
(10,2.32 ) tasmin yadyakṣamātmanvattadvai brahmavido viduḥ ||32||

(10,2.33 ) prabhrājamānāṃ hariṇīṃ yaśasā saṃparīvṛtām |
(10,2.33 ) puraṃ hiraṇyayīṃ brahmā viveśāparājitām ||33|| {6}



(10,3.1 ) ayaṃ me varaṇo maṇiḥ sapatnakṣayaṇo vṛṣā |
(10,3.1 ) tenā rabhasva tvaṃ śatrūn pra mṛṇīhi durasyataḥ ||1||

(10,3.2 ) praiṇān chṛṇīhi pra mṛṇā rabhasva maṇiste astu puraetā purastāt|
(10,3.2 ) avārayanta varaṇena devā abhyācāramasurāṇāṃ śvaḥśvaḥ ||2||

(10,3.3 ) ayaṃ maṇirvaraṇo viśvabheṣajaḥ sahasrākṣo harito hiraṇyayaḥ |
(10,3.3 ) sa te śatrūn adharān pādayāti pūrvastān dabhnuhi ye tvā dviṣanti ||3||

(10,3.4 ) ayaṃ te kṛtyāṃ vitatāṃ pauruṣeyādayaṃ bhayāt|
(10,3.4 ) ayaṃ tvā sarvasmātpāpādvaraṇo vārayiṣyate ||4||

(10,3.5 ) varaṇo vārayātā ayaṃ devo vanaspatiḥ |
(10,3.5 ) yakṣmo yo asminn āviṣṭastamu devā avīvaran ||5||

(10,3.6 ) svapnaṃ suptvā yadi paśyāsi pāpaṃ mṛgaḥ sṛtiṃ yati dhāvādajuṣṭām |
(10,3.6 ) parikṣavācchakuneḥ pāpavādādayaṃ maṇirvaraṇo vārayiṣyate ||6||

(10,3.7 ) arātyāstvā nirṛtyā abhicārādatho bhayāt|
(10,3.7 ) mṛtyorojīyaso vadhādvaraṇo vārayiṣyate ||7||

(10,3.8 ) yan me mātā yan me pitā bhrātaro yacca me svā yadenaścakṛmā vayam |
(10,3.8 ) tato no vārayiṣyate'yaṃ devo vanaspatiḥ ||8||

(10,3.9 ) varaṇena pravyathitā bhrātṛvyā me sabandhavaḥ |
(10,3.9 ) asūrtaṃ rajo apyaguste yantvadhamaṃ tamaḥ ||9||

(10,3.10 ) ariṣṭo'hamariṣṭagurāyuṣmāntsarvapūruṣaḥ |
(10,3.10 ) taṃ māyaṃ varaṇo maṇiḥ pari pātu diśodiśaḥ ||10|| {7}

(10,3.11 ) ayaṃ me varaṇa urasi rājā devo vanaspatiḥ |
(10,3.11 ) sa me śatrūn vi bādhatāmindro dasyūn ivāsurān ||11||

(10,3.12 ) imaṃ bibharmi varaṇamāyuṣmān chataśāradaḥ |
(10,3.12 ) sa me rāṣṭraṃ ca kṣatraṃ ca paśūn ojaśca me dadhat||12||

(10,3.13 ) yathā vāto vanaspatīn vṛkṣān bhanaktyojasā |
(10,3.13 ) evā sapatnān me bhaṅgdhi pūrvān jātāmutāparān varaṇastvābhi rakṣatu ||13||

(10,3.14 ) yathā vātaścāgniśca vṛkṣān psāto vanaspatīn |
(10,3.14 ) evā sapatnān me psāhi pūrvān jātāmutāparān varaṇastvābhi rakṣatu ||14||

(10,3.15 ) yathā vātena prakṣīṇā vṛkṣāḥ śere nyarpitāḥ |
(10,3.15 ) evā sapatnāṃstvaṃ mama pra kṣiṇīhi nyarpaya |
(10,3.15 ) pūrvān jātāmutāparān varaṇastvābhi rakṣatu ||15||

(10,3.16 ) tāṃstvaṃ pra chinddhi varaṇa purā diṣṭātpurāyuṣaḥ |
(10,3.16 ) ya enaṃ paśuṣu dipsanti ye cāsya rāṣṭradipsavaḥ ||16||

(10,3.17 ) yathā sūryo atibhāti yathāsmin teja āhitam |
(10,3.17 ) evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtiṃ bhūtiṃ ni yachatu |
(10,3.17 ) tejasā mā samukṣatu yaśasā samanaktu mā ||17||

(10,3.18 ) yathā yaśaścandramasyāditye ca nṛcakṣasi |
(10,3.18 ) evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtiṃ bhūtiṃ ni yachatu |
(10,3.18 ) tejasā mā samukṣatu yaśasā samanaktu mā ||18||

(10,3.19 ) yathā yaśaḥ pṛthivyāṃ yathāsmin jātavedasi |
(10,3.19 ) evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtiṃ bhūtiṃ ni yachatu |
(10,3.19 ) tejasā mā samukṣatu yaśasā samanaktu mā ||19||

(10,3.20 ) yathā yaśaḥ kanyāyāṃ yathāsmintsaṃbhṛte rathe |
(10,3.20 ) evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtiṃ bhūtiṃ ni yachatu |
(10,3.20 ) tejasā mā samukṣatu yaśasā samanaktu mā ||20|| {8}

(10,3.21 ) yathā yaśaḥ somapīthe madhuparke yathā yaśaḥ |
(10,3.21 ) evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtiṃ bhūtiṃ ni yachatu |
(10,3.21 ) tejasā mā samukṣatu yaśasā samanaktu mā ||21||

(10,3.22 ) yathā yaśo'gnihotre vaṣaṭkāre yathā yaśaḥ |
(10,3.22 ) evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtiṃ bhūtiṃ ni yachatu |
(10,3.22 ) tejasā mā samukṣatu yaśasā samanaktu mā ||22||

(10,3.23 ) yathā yaśo yajamāne yathāsmin yajña āhitam |
(10,3.23 ) evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtiṃ bhūtiṃ ni yachatu |
(10,3.23 ) tejasā mā samukṣatu yaśasā samanaktu mā ||23||

(10,3.24 ) yathā yaśaḥ prajāpatau yathāsmin parameṣṭhini |
(10,3.24 ) evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtiṃ bhūtiṃ ni yachatu |
(10,3.24 ) tejasā mā samukṣatu yaśasā samanaktu mā ||24||

(10,3.25 ) yathā deveṣvamṛtaṃ yathaiṣu satyamāhitam |
(10,3.25 ) evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtiṃ bhūtiṃ ni yachatu |
(10,3.25 ) tejasā mā samukṣatu yaśasā samanaktu mā ||25|| {9}



(10,4.1 ) indrasya prathamo ratho devānāmaparo ratho varuṇasya tṛtīya it|
(10,4.1 ) ahīnāmapamā ratha sthānumāradathārṣat||1||

(10,4.2 ) darbhaḥ śocistarūṇakamaśvasya vāraḥ paruṣasya vāraḥ |
(10,4.2 ) rathasya bandhuram ||2||

(10,4.3 ) ava śveta padā jahi pūrveṇa cāpareṇa ca |
(10,4.3 ) udaplutamiva dārvahīnāmarasaṃ viṣaṃ vārugram ||3||

(10,4.4 ) araṃghuṣo nimajyonmaja punarabravīt|
(10,4.4 ) udaplutamiva dārvahīnāmarasaṃ viṣaṃ vārugram ||4||

(10,4.5 ) paidvo hanti kasarṇīlaṃ paidvaḥ śvitramutāsitam |
(10,4.5 ) paidvo ratharvyāḥ śiraḥ saṃ bibheda pṛdākvāḥ ||5||

(10,4.6 ) paidva prehi prathamo'nu tvā vayamemasi |
(10,4.6 ) ahīn vyasyatātpatho yena smā vayamemasi ||6||

(10,4.7 ) idaṃ paidvo ajāyatedamasya parāyaṇam |
(10,4.7 ) imānyarvataḥ padāhighnyo vājinīvataḥ ||7||

(10,4.8 ) saṃyataṃ na vi ṣparadvyāttaṃ na saṃ yamat|
(10,4.8 ) asmin kṣetre dvāvahī strī ca pumāṃśca tāvubhāvarasā ||8||

(10,4.9 ) arasāsa ihāhayo ye anti ye ca dūrake |
(10,4.9 ) ghanena hanmi vṛścikamahiṃ daṇḍenāgatam ||9||

(10,4.10 ) aghāśvasyedaṃ bheṣajamubhayo svajasya ca |
(10,4.10 ) indro me'himaghāyantamahiṃ paidvo arandhayat||10|| {10}

(10,4.11 ) paidvasya manmahe vayaṃ sthirasya sthiradhāmnaḥ |
(10,4.11 ) ime paścā pṛdākavaḥ pradīdhyata āsate ||11||

(10,4.12 ) naṣṭāsavo naṣṭaviṣā hatā indreṇa vajriṇā |
(10,4.12 ) jaghānendro jaghnimā vayam ||12||

(10,4.13 ) hatāstiraścirājayo nipiṣṭāsaḥ pṛdākavaḥ |
(10,4.13 ) darviṃ karikrataṃ śvitraṃ darbheṣvasitaṃ jahi ||13||

(10,4.14 ) kairātikā kumārikā sakā khanati bheṣajam |
(10,4.14 ) hiraṇyayībhirabhribhirgirīnāmupa sānuṣu ||14||

(10,4.15 ) āyamagan yuvā bhiṣakpṛśnihāparājitaḥ |
(10,4.15 ) sa vai svajasya jambhana ubhayorvṛścikasya ca ||15||

(10,4.16 ) indro me'himarandhayan mitraśca varuṇaśca |
(10,4.16 ) vātāparjanyobhā ||16||

(10,4.17 ) indro me'himarandhayatpṛdākuṃ ca pṛdākvam |
(10,4.17 ) svajaṃ tiraścirājiṃ kasarṇīlaṃ daśonasim ||17||

(10,4.18 ) indro jaghāna prathamaṃ janitāramahe tava |
(10,4.18 ) teṣāmu tṛhyamāṇānāṃ kaḥ svitteṣāmasadrasaḥ ||18||

(10,4.19 ) saṃ hi śīrṣāṇyagrabhaṃ pauñjiṣṭha iva karvaram |
(10,4.19 ) sindhormadhyaṃ paretya vyanijamaherviṣam ||19||

(10,4.20 ) ahīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ viṣaṃ parā vahantu sindhavaḥ |
(10,4.20 ) hatāstiraścirājayo nipiṣṭāsaḥ pṛdākavaḥ ||20|| {11}

(10,4.21 ) oṣadhīnāmahaṃ vṛṇa urvarīriva sādhuyā |
(10,4.21 ) nayāmyarvatīrivāhe niraitu viṣam ||21||

(10,4.22 ) yadagnau sūrye viṣaṃ pṛthivyāmoṣadhīṣu yat|
(10,4.22 ) kāndāviṣaṃ kanaknakaṃ niraitvaitu te viṣam ||22||

(10,4.23 ) ye agnijā oṣadhijā ahīnāṃ ye apsujā vidyuta ābabhūvuḥ |
(10,4.23 ) yeṣāṃ jātāni bahudhā mahānti tebhyaḥ sarpebhyo namasā vidhema ||23||

(10,4.24 ) taudī nāmāsi kanyā ghṛtācī nāma vā asi |
(10,4.24 ) adhaspadena te padamā dade viṣadūṣaṇam ||24||

(10,4.25 ) aṅgādaṅgātpra cyāvaya hṛdayaṃ pari varjaya |
(10,4.25 ) adhā viṣasya yattejo'vācīnaṃ tadetu te ||25||

(10,4.26 ) āre abhūdviṣamaraudviṣe viṣamaprāgapi |
(10,4.26 ) agnirviṣamaherniradhātsomo niraṇayīt|
(10,4.26 ) daṃṣṭāramanvagādviṣamahiramṛta ||26|| {12}



(10,5.1 ) indrasyauja sthendrasya saha sthendrasya balaṃ sthendrasya vīryaṃ sthendrasya nṛmṇaṃ stha |
(10,5.1 ) jiṣṇave yogāya brahmayogairvo yunajmi ||1||

(10,5.2 ) indrasyauja sthendrasya saha sthendrasya balaṃ sthendrasya vīryaṃ sthendrasya nṛmṇaṃ stha |
(10,5.2 ) jiṣṇave yogāya kṣatrayogairvo yunajmi ||2||

(10,5.3 ) indrasyauja sthendrasya saha sthendrasya balaṃ sthendrasya vīryaṃ sthendrasya nṛmṇaṃ stha |
(10,5.3 ) jiṣṇave yogāyendrayogairvo yunajmi ||3||

(10,5.4 ) indrasyauja sthendrasya saha sthendrasya balaṃ sthendrasya vīryaṃ sthendrasya nṛmṇaṃ stha |
(10,5.4 ) jiṣṇave yogāya somayogairvo yunajmi ||4||

(10,5.5 ) indrasyauja sthendrasya saha sthendrasya balaṃ sthendrasya vīryaṃ sthendrasya nṛmṇaṃ stha |
(10,5.5 ) jiṣṇave yogāyāpsuyogairvo yunajmi ||5||

(10,5.6 ) indrasyauja sthendrasya saha sthendrasya balaṃ sthendrasya vīryaṃ sthendrasya nṛmṇaṃ stha |
(10,5.6 ) jiṣṇave yogāya viśvāni mā bhūtānyupa tiṣṭhantu yuktā ma āpa stha ||6||

(10,5.7 ) agnerbhāga stha apāṃ śukramāpo devīrvarco asmāsu dhatta |
(10,5.7 ) prajāpatervo dhāmnāsmai lokāya sādaye ||7||

(10,5.8 ) indrasya bhāga stha apāṃ śukramāpo devīrvarco asmāsu dhatta |
(10,5.8 ) prajāpatervo dhāmnāsmai lokāya sādaye ||8||

(10,5.9 ) somasya bhāga stha apāṃ śukramāpo devīrvarco asmāsu dhatta |
(10,5.9 ) prajāpatervo dhāmnāsmai lokāya sādaye ||9||

(10,5.10 ) varuṇasya bhāga stha apāṃ śukramāpo devīrvarco asmāsu dhatta |
(10,5.10 ) prajāpatervo dhāmnāsmai lokāya sādaye ||10|| {13}

(10,5.11 ) mitrāvaruṇayorbhāga stha apāṃ śukramāpo devīrvarco asmāsu dhatta |
(10,5.11 ) prajāpatervo dhāmnāsmai lokāya sādaye ||11||

(10,5.12 ) yamasya bhāga stha apāṃ śukramāpo devīrvarco asmāsu dhatta |
(10,5.12 ) prajāpatervo dhāmnāsmai lokāya sādaye ||12||

(10,5.13 ) pitṝṇāṃ bhāga stha apāṃ śukramāpo devīrvarco asmāsu dhatta |
(10,5.13 ) prajāpatervo dhāmnāsmai lokāya sādaye ||13||

(10,5.14 ) devasya saviturbhāga stha apāṃ śukramāpo devīrvarco asmāsu dhatta |
(10,5.14 ) prajāpatervo dhāmnāsmai lokāya sādaye ||14||

(10,5.15 ) yo va āpo'pāṃ bhāgo'psvantaryajuṣyo devayajanaḥ |
(10,5.15 ) idaṃ tamati sṛjāmi taṃ mābhyavanikṣi |
(10,5.15 ) tena tamabhyatisṛjāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ |
(10,5.15 ) taṃ vadheyaṃ taṃ stṛṣīyānena brahmaṇānena karmaṇānayā menyā || 15 ||

(10,5.16 ) yo va āpo'pāmūrmirapsvantaryajuṣyo devayajanaḥ |
(10,5.16 ) idaṃ tamati sṛjāmi taṃ mābhyavanikṣi |
(10,5.16 ) tena tamabhyatisṛjāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ |
(10,5.16 ) taṃ vadheyaṃ taṃ stṛṣīyānena brahmaṇānena karmaṇānayā menyā || 16 ||

(10,5.17 ) yo va āpo'pāṃ vatso'psvantaryajuṣyo devayajanaḥ |
(10,5.17 ) idaṃ tamati sṛjāmi taṃ mābhyavanikṣi |
(10,5.17 ) tena tamabhyatisṛjāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ |
(10,5.17 ) taṃ vadheyaṃ taṃ stṛṣīyānena brahmaṇānena karmaṇānayā menyā || 17 ||

(10,5.18 ) yo va āpo'pāṃ vṛṣabho'psvantaryajuṣyo devayajanaḥ ||
(10,5.18 ) idaṃ tamati sṛjāmi taṃ mābhyavanikṣi |
(10,5.18 ) tena tamabhyatisṛjāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||
(10,5.18 ) taṃ vadheyaṃ taṃ stṛṣīyānena brahmaṇānena karmaṇānayā menyā || 18 ||

(10,5.19 ) yo va āpo'pāṃ hiraṇyagarbho'psvantaryajuṣyo devayajanaḥ |
(10,5.19 ) idaṃ tamati sṛjāmi taṃ mābhyavanikṣi |
(10,5.19 ) tena tamabhyatisṛjāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ |
(10,5.19 ) taṃ vadheyaṃ taṃ stṛṣīyānena brahmaṇānena karmaṇānayā menyā || 19 ||

(10,5.20 ) yo va āpo'pāmaśmā pṛśnirdivyo'psvantaryajuṣyo devayajanaḥ |
(10,5.20 ) idaṃ tamati sṛjāmi taṃ mābhyavanikṣi |
(10,5.20 ) tena tamabhyatisṛjāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ |
(10,5.20 ) taṃ vadheyaṃ taṃ stṛṣīyānena brahmaṇānena karmaṇānayā menyā || 20 ||

(10,5.21 ) yo va āpo'pāmagnayo'psvantaryajuṣyo devayajanaḥ |
(10,5.21 ) idaṃ tamati sṛjāmi taṃ mābhyavanikṣi |
(10,5.21 ) tena tamabhyatisṛjāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ |
(10,5.21 ) taṃ vadheyaṃ taṃ stṛṣīyānena brahmaṇānena karmaṇānayā menyā || 21 ||

(10,5.22 ) yadarvācīnaṃ traihāyaṇādanṛtaṃ kiṃ codima |
(10,5.22 ) āpo mā tasmātsarvasmādduritātpāntvaṃhasaḥ ||22||

(10,5.23 ) samudraṃ vaḥ pra hiṇomi svāṃ yonimapītana |
(10,5.23 ) ariṣṭāḥ sarvahāyaso mā ca naḥ kiṃ canāmamat||23||

(10,5.24 ) ariprā āpo apa ripramasmat|
(10,5.24 ) prāsmadeno duritaṃ supratīkāḥ pra duṣvapnyaṃ pra malaṃ vahantu ||24||

(10,5.25 ) viṣṇoḥ kramo'si sapatnahā pṛthivīsaṃśito'gnitejāḥ |
(10,5.25 ) pṛthivīmanu vi krame'haṃ pṛthivyāstaṃ nirbhajāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ |
(10,5.25 ) sa mā jīvīttaṃ prāno jahātu ||25||

(10,5.26 ) viṣṇoḥ kramo'si sapatnahāntarikṣasaṃśito vāyutejāḥ |
(10,5.26 ) antarikṣamanu vi krame'hamantarikṣāttaṃ nirbhajāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ |
(10,5.26 ) sa mā jīvīttaṃ prāno jahātu ||26||

(10,5.27 ) viṣṇoḥ kramo'si sapatnahā dyausaṃśitaḥ sūryatejāḥ |
(10,5.27 ) divamanu vi krame'haṃ divastaṃ nirbhajāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ |
(10,5.27 ) sa mā jivīttaṃ prāno jahātu ||27||

(10,5.28 ) viṣṇoḥ kramo'si sapatnahā diksaṃśito manastejāḥ |
(10,5.28 ) diśo anu vi krame'haṃ digbhyastaṃ nirbhajāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ |
(10,5.28 ) sa mā jīvīttaṃ prāno jahātu ||28||

(10,5.29 ) viṣṇoḥ kramo'si sapatnahāśāsaṃśito vātatejāḥ |
(10,5.29 ) āśā anu vi krame'hamāśābhyastaṃ nirbhajāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ |
(10,5.29 ) sa mā jīvīttaṃ prāno jahātu ||29||

(10,5.30 ) viṣṇoḥ kramo'si sapatnahā ṛksaṃśito sāmatejāḥ |
(10,5.30 ) ṛco'nu vi krame'hamṛgbhyastaṃ nirbhajāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ |
(10,5.30 ) sa mā jīvīttaṃ prāno jahātu ||30|| {15}

(10,5.31 ) viṣṇoḥ kramo'si sapatnahā yajñasaṃśito brahmatejāḥ |
(10,5.31 ) yajñamanu vi krame'haṃ yajñāttaṃ nirbhajāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ |
(10,5.31 ) sa mā jīvīttaṃ prāno jahātu ||31||

(10,5.32 ) viṣṇoḥ kramo'si sapatnahauṣadhīsaṃśito somatejāḥ |
(10,5.32 ) oṣadhīranu vi krame'hamoṣadhībhyastaṃ nirbhajāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ |
(10,5.32 ) sa mā jīvīttaṃ prāno jahātu ||32||

(10,5.33 ) viṣṇoḥ kramo'si sapatnahāpsusaṃśito varuṇatejāḥ |
(10,5.33 ) apo'nu vi krame'hamadbhyastaṃ nirbhajāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ |
(10,5.33 ) sa mā jīvīttaṃ prāno jahātu ||33||

(10,5.34 ) viṣṇoḥ kramo'si sapatnahā kṛṣisaṃśito'nnatejāḥ |
(10,5.34 ) kṛṣimanu vi krame'haṃ kṛṣyāstaṃ nirbhajāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ |
(10,5.34 ) sa mā jīvīttaṃ prāno jahātu ||34||

(10,5.35 ) visṇoḥ kramo'si sapatnahā prāṇasaṃśitaḥ puruṣatejāḥ |
(10,5.35 ) prāṇamanu vi krame'haṃ prāṇāttaṃ nirbhajāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ |
(10,5.35 ) sa mā jīvīttaṃ prāno jahātu ||35||
(10,5.36 ) jitamasmākamudbhinnamasmākamabhyaṣṭhāṃ viśvāḥ pṛtanā arātīḥ |
(10,5.36 ) idamahamāmuṣyāyaṇasyāmuṣyāḥ putrasya varcastejaḥ prāṇamāyurni veṣṭayāmīdamenamadharāñcaṃ pādayāmi ||36||

(10,5.37 ) sūryasyāvṛtamanvāvarte dakṣiṇāmanvāvṛtam |
(10,5.37 ) sā me draviṇaṃ yachatu sā me brāhmaṇavarcasam ||37||

(10,5.38 ) diśo jyotiṣmatīrabhyāvarte |
(10,5.38 ) tā me draviṇaṃ yachantu tā me brāhmaṇavarcasam ||38||

(10,5.39 ) saptaṛṣīn abhyāvarte |
(10,5.39 ) te me draviṇaṃ yachantu te me brāhmaṇavarcasam ||39||

(10,5.40 ) brahmābhyāvarte |
(10,5.40 ) tan me draviṇaṃ yachantu tan me brāhmaṇavarcasam ||40|| {16}

(10,5.41 ) brāhmaṇāmabhyāvarte |
(10,5.41 ) te me draviṇaṃ yachantu te me brāhmaṇavarcasam ||41||

(10,5.42 ) yaṃ vayaṃ mṛgayāmahe taṃ vadhai stṛṇavāmahai |
(10,5.42 ) vyātte parameṣṭhino brahmaṇāpīpadāma tam ||42||

(10,5.43 ) vaiśvānarasya daṃṣṭrābhyāṃ hetistaṃ samadhādabhi |
(10,5.43 ) iyaṃ taṃ psātvāhutiḥ samiddevī sahīyasī ||43||

(10,5.44 ) rājño varuṇasya bandho'si |
(10,5.44 ) so'mumāmuṣyāyaṇamamuṣyāḥ putramanne prāṇe badhāna ||44||

(10,5.45 ) yatte annaṃ bhuvaspata ākṣiyati pṛthivīmanu |
(10,5.45 ) tasya nastvaṃ bhuvaspate saṃprayacha prajāpate ||45||

(10,5.46 ) apo divyā acāyiṣaṃ rasena samapṛkṣmahi |
(10,5.46 ) payasvān agna āgamaṃ taṃ mā saṃ sṛja varcasā ||46||

(10,5.47 ) saṃ māgne varcasā sṛja saṃ prajayā samāyuṣā |
(10,5.47 ) vidyurme asya devā indro vidyātsaha ṛṣibhiḥ ||47||
(10,5.48 ) yadagne adya mithunā śapato yadvācastṛṣṭaṃ janayanta rebhāḥ |
(10,5.48 ) manyormanasaḥ śaravyā jāyate yā tayā vidhya hṛdaye yātudhānān ||48||

(10,5.49 ) parā śṛṇīhi tapasā yātudhānān parāgne rakṣo harasā śṛṇīhi |
(10,5.49 ) parārciṣā mūradevāṃ chṛṇīhi parāsutṛpaḥ śośucataḥ śṛṇīhi ||49||

(10,5.50 ) apāmasmai vajraṃ pra harāmi caturbhṛṣṭiṃ śīrṣabhidyāya vidvān |
(10,5.50 ) so asyāṅgāni pra śṛṇātu sarvā tan me devā anu jānantu viśve ||50|| {17}



(10,6.1 ) arātīyorbhrātṛvyasya durhārdo dviṣataḥ śiraḥ |
(10,6.1 ) api vṛścāmyojasā ||1||

(10,6.2 ) varma mahyamayaṃ maṇiḥ phālājjātaḥ kariṣyati |
(10,6.2 ) pūrṇo manthena māgamadrasena saha varcasā ||2||

(10,6.3 ) yattvā śikvaḥ parāvadhīttakṣā hastena vāsyā |
(10,6.3 ) āpastvā tasmajjīvalāḥ punantu śucayaḥ śucim ||3||

(10,6.4 ) hiraṇyasragayaṃ maṇiḥ śraddhāṃ yajñaṃ maho dadhat|
(10,6.4 ) gṛhe vasatu no'tithiḥ ||4||

(10,6.5 ) tasmai ghṛtaṃ suraṃ madhvannamannaṃ kṣadāmahe |
(10,6.5 ) sa naḥ piteva putrebhyaḥ śreyaḥśreyaścikitsatu bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvo devebhyo maṇiretya ||5||

(10,6.6 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirmaṇiṃ phālaṃ ghṛtaścutamugraṃ kadhiramojase |
(10,6.6 ) tamagniḥ pratyamuñcata so asmai duha ājyaṃ bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvastena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ||6||

(10,6.7 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirmaṇiṃ phālaṃ ghṛtaścutamugraṃ kadhiramojase |
(10,6.7 ) tamindraḥ pratyamuñcataujase vīryāya kam |
(10,6.7 ) so asmai balamidduhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvastena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ||7||

(10,6.8 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirmaṇiṃ phālaṃ ghṛtaścutamugraṃ kadhiramojase |
(10,6.8 ) taṃ somaḥ pratyamuñcata mahe śrotrāya cakṣase |
(10,6.8 ) so asmai varca idduhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvastena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ||8||

(10,6.9 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirmaṇiṃ phālaṃ ghṛtaścutamugraṃ khadiramojase |
(10,6.9 ) taṃ sūryaḥ pratyamuñcata tenemā ajayaddiśaḥ |
(10,6.9 ) so asmai bhūtimidduhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvastena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ||9||

(10,6.10 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirmaṇiṃ phālaṃ ghṛtaścutamugraṃ khadiramojase |
(10,6.10 ) taṃ bibhraccandramā maṇimasurāṇāṃ puro'jayaddānavānāṃ hiraṇyayīḥ |
(10,6.10 ) so asmai śriyamidduhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvastena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ||10|| {18}

(10,6.11 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirvātāya maṇimāśave |
(10,6.11 ) so asmai vājinamidduhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvastena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ||11||

(10,6.12 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirvātāya maṇimāśave |
(10,6.12 ) tenemāṃ maṇinā kṛṣimaśvināvabhi rakṣataḥ |
(10,6.12 ) sa bhiṣagbhyāṃ maho duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvastena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ||12||

(10,6.13 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirvātāya maṇimāśave |
(10,6.13 ) taṃ bibhratsavitā maṇiṃ tenedamajayatsvaḥ |
(10,6.13 ) so asmai sūnṛtāṃ duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvastena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ||13||

(10,6.14 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirvātāya maṇimāśave |
(10,6.14 ) tamāpo bibhratīrmaṇiṃ sadā dhāvantyakṣitāḥ |
(10,6.14 ) sa ābhyo'mṛtamidduhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvastena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ||14||

(10,6.15 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirvātāya maṇimāśave |
(10,6.15 ) taṃ rājā varuṇo maṇiṃ pratyamuñcata śaṃbhuvam |
(10,6.15 ) so asmai satyamidduhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvastena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ||15||

(10,6.16 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirvātāya maṇimāśave |
(10,6.16 ) taṃ devā bibhrato maṇiṃ sarvāṃllokān yudhājayan |
(10,6.16 ) sa ebhyo jitimidduhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvastena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ||16||

(10,6.17 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirvātāya maṇimāśave |
(10,6.17 ) tamimaṃ devatā maṇiṃ pratyamuñcanta śambhuvam |
(10,6.17 ) sa ābhyo viśvamidduhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvastena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ||17||

(10,6.18 ) ṛtavastamabadhnatārtavāstamabadhnata |
(10,6.18 ) saṃvatsarastaṃ baddhvā sarvaṃ bhūtaṃ vi rakṣati ||18||

(10,6.19 ) antardeśā abadhnata pradiśastamabadhnata |
(10,6.19 ) prajāpatisṛṣṭo maṇirdviṣato me'dharāmakaḥ ||19||

(10,6.20 ) atharvāṇo abadhnatātharvaṇā abadhnata |
(10,6.20 ) tairmedino aṅgiraso dasyūnāṃ bibhiduḥ purastena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ||20|| {19}

(10,6.21 ) taṃ dhātā pratyamuñcata sa bhūtaṃ vyakalpayat|
(10,6.21 ) tena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ||21||

(10,6.22 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirdevebhyo asurakṣitim |
(10,6.22 ) sa māyaṃ maṇirāgamadrasena saha varcasā ||22||

(10,6.23 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirdevebhyo asurakṣitim |
(10,6.23 ) sa māyaṃ maṇirāgamatsaha gobhirajāvibhirannena prajayā saha ||23||

(10,6.24 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirdevebhyo asurakṣitim |
(10,6.24 ) sa māyaṃ maṇirāgamatsaha vrīhiyavābhyāṃ mahasā bhūtyā saha ||24||

(10,6.25 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirdevebhyo asurakṣitim |
(10,6.25 ) sa māyaṃ maṇirāgaman madhorghṛtasya dhārayā kīlālena maṇiḥ saha ||25||

(10,6.26 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirdevebhyo asurakṣitim |
(10,6.26 ) sa māyaṃ maṇirāgamadūrjayā payasā saha draviṇena śriyā saha ||26||

(10,6.27 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirdevebhyo asurakṣitim |
(10,6.27 ) sa māyaṃ maṇirāgamattejasā tviṣyā saha yaśasā kīrtyā saha ||27||

(10,6.28 ) yamabadhnādbṛhaspatirdevebhyo asurakṣitim |
(10,6.28 ) sa māyaṃ maṇirāgamatsarvābhirbhūtibhiḥ saha ||28||

(10,6.29 ) tamimaṃ devatā maṇiṃ mahyaṃ dadatu puṣṭaye |
(10,6.29 ) abhibhuṃ kṣatravardhanaṃ sapatnadambhanaṃ maṇim ||29||

(10,6.30 ) brahmaṇā tejasā saha prati muñcāmi me śivam |
(10,6.30 ) asapatnaḥ sapatnahā sapatnān me'dharāmakaḥ ||30|| {20}

(10,6.31 ) uttaraṃ dviṣato māmayaṃ maṇiḥ kṛṇotu devajāḥ |
(10,6.31 ) yasya lokā ime trayaḥ payo dugdhamupāsate |
(10,6.31 ) sa māyamadhi rohatu maṇiḥ śraiṣṭhyāya mūrdhataḥ ||31||

(10,6.32 ) yaṃ devāḥ pitaro manuṣyā upajīvanti sarvadā |
(10,6.32 ) sa māyamadhi rohatu maṇiḥ śraiṣṭhyāya mūrdhataḥ ||32||

(10,6.33 ) yathā bījamurvarāyāṃ kṛṣṭe phālena rohati |
(10,6.33 ) evā mayi prajā paśavo'nnamannaṃ vi rohatu ||33||

(10,6.34 ) yasmai tvā yajñavardhana maṇe pratyamucaṃ śivam |
(10,6.34 ) taṃ tvaṃ śatadakṣiṇa maṇe śraiṣṭhyāya jinvatāt||34||

(10,6.35 ) etamidhmaṃ samāhitaṃ juṣaṇo agne prati harya homaiḥ |
(10,6.35 ) tasmin vidhema sumatiṃ svasti prajāṃ cakṣuḥ paśūntsamiddhe jātavedasi brahmaṇā ||35|| {21}



(10,7.1 ) kasminn aṅge tapo asyādhi tiṣṭhati kasminn aṅga ṛtamasyādhyāhitam |
(10,7.1 ) kva vrataṃ kva śraddhāsya tiṣṭhati kasminn aṅge satyamasya pratiṣṭhitam ||1||

(10,7.2 ) kasmādaṅgāddīpyate agnirasya kasmādaṅgātpavate mātariśva |
(10,7.2 ) kasmādaṅgādvi mimīte'dhi candramā maha skambhasya mimāno aṅgam ||2||

(10,7.3 ) kasminn aṅge tiṣṭhati bhūmirasya kasminn aṅge tiṣṭhatyantarikṣam |
(10,7.3 ) kasminn aṅge tiṣṭhatyāhitā dyauḥ kasminn aṅge tiṣṭhatyuttaraṃ divaḥ ||3||

(10,7.4 ) kva prepsan dīpyata ūrdhvo agniḥ kva prepsan pavate mātariśvā |
(10,7.4 ) yatra prepsantīrabhiyantyāvṛtaḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svideva saḥ ||4||

(10,7.5 ) kvārdhamāsāḥ kva yanti māsāḥ saṃvatsareṇa saha saṃvidānāḥ |
(10,7.5 ) yatra yantyṛtavo yatrārtavāḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svideva saḥ ||5||

(10,7.6 ) kva prepsantī yuvatī virūpe ahorātre dravataḥ saṃvidāne |
(10,7.6 ) yatra prepsantīrabhiyantyāpaḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svideva saḥ ||6||

(10,7.7 ) yasmintstabdhvā prajāpatirlokāntsarvāmadhārayat|
(10,7.7 ) skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svideva saḥ ||7||

(10,7.8 ) yatparamamavamaṃ yacca madhyamaṃ prajāpatiḥ sasṛje viśvarūpam |
(10,7.8 ) kiyatā skambhaḥ pra viveśa tatra yan na prāviśatkiyattadbabhūva ||8||

(10,7.9 ) kiyatā skambhaḥ pra viveśa bhūtaṃ kiyadbhaviṣyadanvāśaye'sya |
(10,7.9 ) ekaṃ yadaṅgamakṛṇotsahasradhā kiyatā skambhaḥ pra viveśa tatra ||9||

(10,7.10 ) yatra lokāmśca kośāṃścāpo brahma janā viduḥ |
(10,7.10 ) asacca yatra saccānta skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svideva saḥ ||10|| {22}

(10,7.11 ) yatra tapaḥ parākramya vrataṃ dhārayatyuttaram |
(10,7.11 ) ṛtaṃ ca yatra śraddhā cāpo brahma samāhitāḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svideva saḥ ||11||

(10,7.12 ) yasmin bhūmirantarikṣaṃ dyauryasminn adhyāhitā |
(10,7.12 ) yatrāgniścandramāḥ sūryo vātastiṣṭhantyārpitāḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svideva saḥ ||12||

(10,7.13 ) yasya trayastriṃśaddevā aṅge sarve samāhitāḥ |
(10,7.13 ) skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svideva saḥ ||13||

(10,7.14 ) yatra ṛṣayaḥ prathamajā ṛcaḥ sāma yajurmahī |
(10,7.14 ) ekarṣiryasminn ārpitaḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svideva saḥ ||14||

(10,7.15 ) yatrāmṛtaṃ ca mṛtyuśca puruṣe'dhi samāhite |
(10,7.15 ) samudro yasya nāḍyaḥ puruṣe'dhi samāhitāḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svideva saḥ ||15||

(10,7.16 ) yasya catasraḥ pradiśo nāḍyastiṣṭhanti prathamāḥ |
(10,7.16 ) yajño yatra parākrāntaḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svideva saḥ ||16||

(10,7.17 ) ye puruṣe brahma viduste viduḥ parameṣṭhinam |
(10,7.17 ) yo veda parameṣṭhinaṃ yaśca veda prajāpatim |
(10,7.17 ) jyeṣṭhaṃ ye brāhmaṇaṃ viduste skambhamanusaṃviduḥ ||17||

(10,7.18 ) yasya śiro vaiśvānaraścakṣuraṅgiraso'bhavan |
(10,7.18 ) aṅgāni yasya yātavaḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svideva saḥ ||18||

(10,7.19 ) yasya brahma mukhamāhurjihvāṃ madhukaśāmuta |
(10,7.19 ) virājamūdho yasyāhuḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svideva saḥ ||19||

(10,7.20 ) yasmādṛco apātakṣan yajuryasmādapākaṣan |
(10,7.20 ) sāmāni yasya lomānyatharvāṅgiraso mukhaṃ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svideva saḥ ||20|| {23}

(10,7.21 ) asaccākhāṃ pratiṣṭhantīṃ paramamiva janā viduḥ |
(10,7.21 ) uto san manyante'vare ye te śākhāmupāsate ||21||

(10,7.22 ) yatrādityāśca rudrāśca vasavaśca samāhitāḥ |
(10,7.22 ) bhūtaṃ ca yatra bhavyaṃ ca sarve lokāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svideva saḥ ||22||

(10,7.23 ) yasya trayastriṃśaddevā nidhiṃ rakṣanti sarvadā |
(10,7.23 ) nidhiṃ tamadya ko veda yaṃ devā abhirakṣatha ||23||

(10,7.24 ) yatra devā brahmavido brahma jyeṣṭhamupāsate |
(10,7.24 ) yo vai tān vidyātpratyakṣaṃ sa brahmā veditā syāt||24||

(10,7.25 ) bṛhanto nāma te devā ye'sataḥ pari jajñire |
(10,7.25 ) ekaṃ tadaṅgaṃ skambhasyāsadāhuḥ paro janāḥ ||25||

(10,7.26 ) yatra skambhaḥ prajanayan purāṇaṃ vyavartayat|
(10,7.26 ) ekaṃ tadaṅgaṃ skambhasya purāṇamanusaṃviduḥ ||26||

(10,7.27 ) yasya trayastriṃśaddevā aṅge gātrā vibhejire |
(10,7.27 ) tān vai trayastriṃśaddevān eke brahamvido viduḥ ||27||

(10,7.28 ) hiraṇyagarbhaṃ paramamanatyudyaṃ janā viduḥ |
(10,7.28 ) skambhastadagre prāsiñcaddhiraṇyaṃ loke antarā ||28||

(10,7.29 ) skambhe lokāḥ skambhe tapaḥ skambhe'dhyṛtamāhitam |
(10,7.29 ) skambha tvā veda pratyakṣamindre sarvaṃ samāhitam ||29||

(10,7.30 ) indre lokā indre tapa indre'dhyṛtamāhitam |
(10,7.30 ) indraṃ tvā veda pratyakṣaṃ skambhe sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ||30|| {24}

(10,7.31 ) nāma nāmnā johavīti purā sūryātpuroṣasaḥ |
(10,7.31 ) yadajaḥ prathamaṃ saṃbabhūva sa ha tatsvarājyamiyāya yasmān nānyatparamasti bhūtam ||31||

(10,7.32 ) yasya bhūmiḥ pramāntarikṣamutodaram |
(10,7.32 ) divaṃ yaścakre mūrdhānaṃ tasmai jyeṣṭhāya brahmaṇe namaḥ ||32||

(10,7.33 ) yasya sūryaścakṣuścandramāśca punarṇavaḥ |
(10,7.33 ) agniṃ yaścakra āsyaṃ tasmai jyeṣṭhāya brahmaṇe namaḥ ||33||

(10,7.34 ) yasya vātaḥ prāṇāpānau cakṣuraṅgiraso'bhavan |
(10,7.34 ) diśo yaścakre prajñānīstasmai jyeṣṭhāya brahmaṇe namaḥ ||34||
(10,7.35 ) skambho dādhāra dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe ime skambho dādhārorvantarikṣam |
(10,7.35 ) skambho dādhāra pradiśaḥ ṣaḍurvīḥ skambha idaṃ viśvaṃ bhuvanamā viveśa ||35||

(10,7.36 ) yaḥ śramāttapaso jāto lokāntsarvāntsamānaśe |
(10,7.36 ) somaṃ yaścakre kevalaṃ tasmai jyeṣṭhāya brahmaṇe namaḥ ||36||

(10,7.37 ) kathaṃ vāto nelayati kathaṃ na ramate manaḥ |
(10,7.37 ) kimāpaḥ satyaṃ prepsantīrnelayanti kadā cana ||37||

(10,7.38 ) mahadyakṣaṃ bhuvanasya madhye tapasi krāntaṃ salilasya pṛṣṭhe |
(10,7.38 ) tasmin chrayante ya u ke ca devā vṛkṣasya skandhaḥ parita iva śākhāḥ ||38||
(10,7.39 ) yasmai hastābhyāṃ pādābhyāṃ vācā śrotreṇa cakṣuṣā |
(10,7.39 ) yasmai devāḥ sadā baliṃ prayachanti vimite'mitaṃ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svideva saḥ ||39||

(10,7.40 ) apa tasya hataṃ tamo vyāvṛttaḥ sa pāpmanā |
(10,7.40 ) sarvāṇi tasmin jyotīṃṣi yāni trīṇi prajāpatau ||40||

(10,7.41 ) yo vetasaṃ hiraṇyayaṃ tiṣṭhantaṃ salile veda |
(10,7.41 ) sa vai guhyaḥ prajāpatiḥ ||41||

(10,7.42 ) tantrameke yuvatī virūpe abhyākrāmaṃ vayataḥ ṣaṇmayūkham |
(10,7.42 ) prānyā tantūṃstirate dhatte anyā nāpa vṛñjāte na gamāto antam ||42||

(10,7.43 ) tayorahaṃ parinṛtyantyoriva na vi jānāmi yatarā parastāt|
(10,7.43 ) pumān enadvayatyudgṛṇanti pumān enadvi jabhārādhi nāke ||43||

(10,7.44 ) ime mayūkhā upa tastabhurdivaṃ sāmāni cakrustasarāṇi vātave ||44|| {25}



(10,8.1 ) yo bhūtaṃ ca bhavyaṃ ca sarvaṃ yaścādhitiṣṭhati |
(10,8.1 ) svaryasya ca kevalaṃ tasmai jyeṣṭhāya brahmaṇe namaḥ ||1||

(10,8.2 ) skambheneme viṣṭabhite dyauśca bhūmiśca tiṣṭhataḥ |
(10,8.2 ) skambha idaṃ sarvamātmanvadyatprāṇan nimiṣacca yat||2||
(10,8.3 ) tisro ha prajā atyāyamāyan nyanyā arkamabhito'viśanta |
(10,8.3 ) bṛhan ha tasthau rajaso vimāno harito hariṇīrā viveśa ||3||

(10,8.4 ) dvādaśa pradhayaścakramekaṃ trīṇi nabhyāni ka u tacciketa |
(10,8.4 ) tatrāhatāstrīṇi śatāni śaṅkavaḥ ṣaṣṭiśca khīlā avicācalā ye ||4||

(10,8.5 ) idaṃ savitarvi jānīhi ṣaḍyamā eka ekajaḥ |
(10,8.5 ) tasmin hāpitvamichante ya eṣāmeka ekajaḥ ||5||

(10,8.6 ) āviḥ san nihitaṃ guhā jaran nāma mahatpadam |
(10,8.6 ) tatredaṃ sarvamārpitamejatprāṇatpratiṣṭhitam ||6||

(10,8.7 ) ekacakraṃ vartata ekanemi sahasrākṣaraṃ pra puro ni paścā |
(10,8.7 ) ardhena viśvaṃ bhuvanaṃ jajāna yadasyārdhaṃ kva tadbabhūva ||7||

(10,8.8 ) pañcavāhī vahatyagrameṣāṃ praṣṭayo yuktā anusaṃvahanti |
(10,8.8 ) ayātamasya dadṛśe na yātaṃ paraṃ nedīyo'varaṃ davīyaḥ ||8||

(10,8.9 ) tiryagbilaścamasa ūrdhvabudhnastasmin yaśo nihitaṃ viśvarūpam |
(10,8.9 ) tadāsata ṛṣayaḥ sapta sākaṃ ye asya gopā mahato babhūvuḥ ||9||

(10,8.10 ) yā purastādyujyate yā ca paścādyā viśvato yujyate yā ca sarvataḥ |
(10,8.10 ) yayā yajñaḥ prāṅtāyate tāṃ tvā pṛchāmi katamā sā ṛcām ||10|| {26}

(10,8.11 ) yadejati patati yacca tiṣṭhati prāṇadaprāṇan nimiṣacca yadbhuvat|
(10,8.11 ) taddādhāra pṛthivīṃ viśvarūpaṃ tatsaṃbhūya bhavatyekameva ||11||

(10,8.12 ) anantaṃ vitataṃ purutrānantamantavaccā samante |
(10,8.12 ) te nākapālaścarati vicinvan vidvān bhūtamuta bhavyamasya ||12||

(10,8.13 ) prajāpatiścarati garbhe antaradṛśyamāno bahudhā vi jāyate |
(10,8.13 ) ardhena viśvaṃ bhuvanaṃ jajāna yadasyārdhaṃ katamaḥ sa ketuḥ ||13||

(10,8.14 ) ūrdhvaṃ bharantamudakaṃ kumbhenevodahāryam |
(10,8.14 ) paśyanti sarve cakṣuṣā na sarve manasā viduḥ ||14||

(10,8.15 ) dūre pūrṇena vasati dūra ūnena hīyate |
(10,8.15 ) mahadyakṣaṃ bhuvanasya madhye tasmai baliṃ rāṣṭrabhṛto bharanti ||15||

(10,8.16 ) yataḥ sūryaḥ udetyastaṃ yatra ca gachati |
(10,8.16 ) tadeva manye'haṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ tadu nātyeti kiṃ cana ||16||

(10,8.17 ) ye arvāṅmadhya uta vā purāṇaṃ vedaṃ vidvāṃsamabhito vadanti |
(10,8.17 ) ādityameva te pari vadanti sarve agniṃ dvitīyaṃ trivṛtaṃ ca haṃsam ||17||

(10,8.18 ) sahasrāhṇyaṃ viyatāvasya pakṣau harerhaṃsasya patataḥ svargam |
(10,8.18 ) sa devāntsarvān urasyupadadya saṃpaśyan yāti bhuvanāni viśvā ||18||

(10,8.19 ) satyenordhvastapati brahmaṇārvāṅvi paśyati |
(10,8.19 ) prāṇena tiryaṅprāṇati yasmin jyeṣṭhamadhi śritam ||19||

(10,8.20 ) yo vai te vidyādaraṇī yābhyāṃ nirmathyate vasu |
(10,8.20 ) sa vidvān jyeṣṭhaṃ manyeta sa vidyādbrāhmaṇaṃ mahat||20|| {27}

(10,8.21 ) apādagre samabhavatso agre svarābharat|
(10,8.21 ) catuṣpādbhūtvā bhogyaḥ sarvamādatta bhojanam ||21||

(10,8.22 ) bhogyo bhavadatho annamadadbahu |
(10,8.22 ) yo devamuttarāvantamupāsātai sanātanam ||22||

(10,8.23 ) sanātanamenamāhurutādya syātpunarṇavaḥ |
(10,8.23 ) ahorātre pra jāyete anyo anyasya rūpayoḥ ||23||

(10,8.24 ) śataṃ sahasramayutaṃ nyarbudamasaṃkhyeyaṃ svamasmin niviṣṭam |
(10,8.24 ) tadasya ghnantyabhipaśyata eva tasmāddevo rocateṣa etat||24||

(10,8.25 ) bālādekamaṇīyaskamutaikaṃ neva dṛśyate |
(10,8.25 ) tataḥ pariṣvajīyasī devatā sā mama priyā ||25||

(10,8.26 ) iyaṃ kalyāṇyajarā martyasyāmṛtā gṛhe |
(10,8.26 ) yasmai kṛtā śaye sa yaścakāra jajāra saḥ ||26||

(10,8.27 ) tvaṃ strī tvaṃ pumān asi tvaṃ kumāra uta vā kumārī||
(10,8.27 ) tvaṃ jīrṇo daṇḍena vañcasi tvaṃ jāto bhavasi viśvatomukhaḥ ||27||

(10,8.28 ) utaiṣāṃ pitota vā putra eṣāmutaiṣāṃ jyeṣṭha uta vā kaniṣṭhaḥ |
(10,8.28 ) eko ha devo manasi praviṣṭaḥ prathamo jātaḥ sa u garbhe antaḥ ||28||

(10,8.29 ) pūrṇātpūrṇamudacati pūrṇaṃ pūrṇena sicyate |
(10,8.29 ) uto tadadya vidyāma yatastatpariṣicyate ||29||

(10,8.30 ) eṣā sanatnī sanameva jātaiṣā purāṇī pari sarvaṃ babhūva |
(10,8.30 ) mahī devyuṣaso vibhātī saikenaikena miṣatā vi caṣṭe ||30|| {28}

(10,8.31 ) avirvai nāma devatartenāste parīvṛtā |
(10,8.31 ) tasyā rūpeṇeme vṛkṣā haritā haritasrajaḥ ||31||

(10,8.32 ) anti santaṃ na jahātyanti santaṃ na paśyati |
(10,8.32 ) devasya paśya kāvyaṃ na mamāra na jīryati ||32||

(10,8.33 ) apūrveṇeṣitā vācastā vadanti yathāyatham |
(10,8.33 ) vadantīryatra gachanti tadāhurbrāhmaṇaṃ mahat||33||

(10,8.34 ) yatra devāśca manuṣyāścārā nābhāviva śritāḥ |
(10,8.34 ) apāṃ tvā puṣpaṃ pṛchāmi yatra tan māyayā hitam ||34||

(10,8.35 ) yebhirvāta iṣitaḥ pravāti ye dadante pañca diśaḥ sadhrīcīḥ |
(10,8.35 ) ya āhutimatyamanyanta devā apāṃ netāraḥ katame ta āsan ||35||

(10,8.36 ) imāmeṣāṃ pṛthivīṃ vasta eko'ntarikṣaṃ paryeko babhūva |
(10,8.36 ) divameṣāṃ dadate yo vidhartā viśvā āśāḥ prati rakṣantyeke ||36||

(10,8.37 ) yo vidyātsūtraṃ vitataṃ yasminn otāḥ prajā imāḥ |
(10,8.37 ) sūtraṃ sūtrasya yo vidyādsa vidyādbrāhmaṇaṃ mahat||37||

(10,8.38 ) vedāhaṃ sūtraṃ vitataṃ yasminn otāḥ prajā imāḥ |
(10,8.38 ) sūtraṃ sūtrasyāhaṃ vedātho yadbrāhmaṇaṃ mahad||38||

(10,8.39 ) yadantarā dyāvāpṛthivī agniraitpradahan viśvadāvyaḥ |
(10,8.39 ) yatrātiṣṭhann ekapatnīḥ parastātkvevāsīn mātariśvā tadānīm ||39||

(10,8.40 ) apsvāsīn mātariśvā praviṣṭaḥ praviṣṭā devāḥ salilānyāsan||
(10,8.40 ) bṛhan ha tasthau rajaso vimānaḥ pavamāno harita ā viveśa ||40||

(10,8.41 ) uttareṇeva gayatrīmamṛte'dhi vi cakrame |
(10,8.41 ) sāmnā ye sāma saṃvidurajastaddadṛśe kva ||41||

(10,8.42 ) niveśanaḥ saṃgamano vasūnāṃ deva iva savitā satyadharmā |
(10,8.42 ) indro na tasthau samare dhanānām ||42||

(10,8.43 ) puṇḍarīkaṃ navadvāraṃ tribhirguṇebhirāvṛtam |
(10,8.43 ) tasmin yadyakṣamātmanvattadvai brahmavido viduḥ ||43||

(10,8.44 ) akāmo dhīro amṛtaḥ svayaṃbhū rasena tṛpto na kutaścanonaḥ |
(10,8.44 ) tameva vidvān na bibhāya mṛtyorātmānaṃ dhīramajaraṃ yuvānam ||44|| {29}



(10,9.1 ) aghāyatāmapi nahyā mukhāni sapatneṣu vajramarpayaitam |
(10,9.1 ) indreṇa dattā prathamā śataudanā bhrātṛvyaghnī yajamānasya gātuḥ ||1||

(10,9.2 ) vediṣṭe carma bhavatu barhirlomāni yāni te |
(10,9.2 ) eṣā tvā raśanāgrabhīdgrāvā tvaiṣo'dhi nṛtyatu ||2||

(10,9.3 ) bālāste prokṣaṇīḥ santu jīhvā saṃ mārṣṭu aghnye |
(10,9.3 ) śuddhā tvaṃ yajñiyā bhūtvā divaṃ prehi śataudane ||3||

(10,9.4 ) yaḥ śataudanāṃ pacati kāmapreṇa sa kalpate |
(10,9.4 ) prītā hyasya ṛtvijaḥ sarve yanti yathāyatham ||4||

(10,9.5 ) sa svargamā rohati yatrādastridivaṃ divaḥ |
(10,9.5 ) apūpanābhiṃ kṛtvā yo dadāti śataudanām ||5||
(10,9.6 ) sa tāṃllokāntsamāpnoti ye divyā ye ca pārthivāḥ |
(10,9.6 ) hiraṇyajyotiṣaṃ kṛtvā yo dadāti śataudanām ||6||

(10,9.7 ) ye te devi śamitāraḥ paktāro ye ca te janāḥ |
(10,9.7 ) te tvā sarve gopsyanti maibhyo bhaiṣīḥ śataudane ||7||

(10,9.8 ) vasavastvā dakṣiṇata uttarān marutastvā |
(10,9.8 ) ādityāḥ paścādgopsyanti sāgniṣṭomamati drava ||8||

(10,9.9 ) devāḥ pitaro manuṣyā gandharvāpsarasaśca ye |
(10,9.9 ) te tvā sarve gopsyanti sātirātramati drava ||9||

(10,9.10 ) antarikṣaṃ divaṃ bhūmimādityān maruto diśaḥ |
(10,9.10 ) lokāntsa sarvān āpnoti yo dadāti śataudanām ||10|| {30}

(10,9.11 ) ghṛtaṃ prokṣantī subhagā devī devān gamiṣyati |
(10,9.11 ) paktāramaghnye mā hiṃsīrdivaṃ prehi śataudane ||11||

(10,9.12 ) ye devā diviṣado antarikṣasadaśca ye ye ceme bhūmyāmadhi |
(10,9.12 ) tebhyastvaṃ dhukṣva sarvadā kṣīraṃ sarpiratho madhu ||12||

(10,9.13 ) yatte śiro yatte mukhaṃ yau karṇau ye ca te hanū |
(10,9.13 ) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpiratho madhu ||13||

(10,9.14 ) yau ta oṣṭhau ye nāsike ye śṛṅge ye ca te'kṣiṇī |
(10,9.14 ) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpiratho madhu ||14||

(10,9.15 ) yatte klomā yaddhṛdayaṃ purītatsahakaṇṭhikā |
(10,9.15 ) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpiratho madhu ||15||

(10,9.16 ) yatte yakṛdye matasne yadāntraṃ yāśca te gudāḥ |
(10,9.16 ) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpiratho madhu ||16||

(10,9.17 ) yaste plāśiryo vaniṣṭhuryau kukṣī yacca carma te |
(10,9.17 ) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpiratho madhu ||17||

(10,9.18 ) yatte majjā yadasthi yan maṃsaṃ yacca lohitam |
(10,9.18 ) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpiratho madhu ||18||

(10,9.19 ) yau te bāhū ye doṣaṇī yāvaṃsau yā ca te kakut|
(10,9.19 ) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpiratho madhu ||19||

(10,9.20 ) yāste grīvā ye skandhā yāḥ pṛṣṭīryāśca parśavaḥ |
(10,9.20 ) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpiratho madhu ||20|| {31}

(10,9.21 ) yau ta urū aṣṭhīvantau ye śroṇī yā ca te bhasat|
(10,9.21 ) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpiratho madhu ||21||

(10,9.22 ) yatte puchaṃ ye te bālā yadūdho ye ca te stanāḥ |
(10,9.22 ) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpiratho madhu ||22||

(10,9.23 ) yāste jaṅghāḥ yāḥ kuṣṭhikā ṛcharā ye ca te śaphāḥ |
(10,9.23 ) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpiratho madhu ||23||

(10,9.24 ) yatte carma śataudane yāni lomānyaghnye |
(10,9.24 ) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpiratho madhu ||24||

(10,9.25 ) kroḍau te stāṃ purodāśāvājyenābhighāritau |
(10,9.25 ) tau pakṣau devi kṛtvā sā paktāraṃ divaṃ vaha ||25||

(10,9.26 ) ulūkhale musale yaśca carmaṇi yo vā śūrpe taṇḍulaḥ kaṇaḥ |
(10,9.26 ) yaṃ vā vāto mātariśvā pavamāno mamāthāgniṣṭaddhotā suhutaṃ kṛṇotu ||26||

(10,9.27 ) apo devīrmadhumatīrghṛtaścuto brahmaṇāṃ hasteṣu prapṛthaksādayāmi |
(10,9.27 ) yatkāma idamabhiṣiñcāmi vo'haṃ tan me sarvaṃ saṃ padyatāṃ vayaṃ syāma patayo rayīṇām ||27|| {32}



(10,10.1 ) namaste jāyamānāyai jātāyā uta te namaḥ |
(10,10.1 ) bālebhyaḥ śaphebhyo rūpāyāghnye te namaḥ ||1||

(10,10.2 ) yo vidyātsapta pravataḥ sapta vidyātparāvataḥ |
(10,10.2 ) śiro yajñasya yo vidyātsa vaśāṃ prati gṛhṇīyāt||2||

(10,10.3 ) vedāhaṃ sapta pravataḥ sapta veda parāvataḥ |
(10,10.3 ) śiro yajñasyāhaṃ veda somaṃ cāsyāṃ vicakṣaṇam ||3||

(10,10.4 ) yayā dyauryayā pṛthivī yayāpo gupitā imāḥ |
(10,10.4 ) vaśāṃ sahasradhārāṃ brahmaṇāchāvadāmasi ||4||

(10,10.5 ) śataṃ kaṃsāḥ śataṃ dogdhāraḥ śataṃ goptāro adhi pṛṣṭhe asyāḥ |
(10,10.5 ) ye devāstasyāṃ prāṇanti te vaśāṃ vidurekadhā ||5||

(10,10.6 ) yajñapadīrākṣīrā svadhāprāṇā mahīlukā |
(10,10.6 ) vaśā parjanyapatnī devāmapyeti brahmaṇā ||6||

(10,10.7 ) anu tvāgniḥ prāviśadanu somo vaśe tvā |
(10,10.7 ) ūdhaste bhadre parjanyo vidyutaste stanā vaśe ||7||

(10,10.8 ) apastvaṃ dhukṣe prathamā urvarā aparā vaśe |
(10,10.8 ) tṛtīyaṃ rāṣṭraṃ dhukṣe'nnaṃ kṣīraṃ vaśe tvam ||8||

(10,10.9 ) yadādityairhūyamānopātiṣṭha ṛtavari |
(10,10.9 ) indraḥ sahasraṃ pātrāntsomaṃ tvāpāyayadvaśe ||9||

(10,10.10 ) yadanūcīndramairāttvā ṛṣabho'hvayat|
(10,10.10 ) tasmātte vṛtrahā payaḥ kṣīraṃ kruddho'haradvaśe ||10|| {33}

(10,10.11 ) yatte kruddho dhanapatirā kṣīramaharadvaśe |
(10,10.11 ) idaṃ tadadya nākastriṣu pātreṣu rakṣati ||11||

(10,10.12 ) triṣu pātreṣu taṃ somamā devyaharadvaśā |
(10,10.12 ) atharvā yatra dīkṣito barhiṣyāsta hiraṇyaye ||12||

(10,10.13 ) saṃ hi somenāgata samu sarveṇa padvatā |
(10,10.13 ) vaśā samudramadhyaṣṭhadgandharvaiḥ kalibhiḥ saha ||13||

(10,10.14 ) saṃ hi vātenāgata samu sarvaiḥ patatribhiḥ |
(10,10.14 ) vaśā samudre prānṛtyadṛcaḥ sāmāni bibhratī ||14||

(10,10.15 ) saṃ hi sūryeṇāgata samu sarveṇa cakṣuṣā |
(10,10.15 ) vaśā samudramatyakhyadbhadrā jyotīṃṣi bibhratī ||15||

(10,10.16 ) abhīvṛtā hiraṇyena yadatiṣṭha ṛtāvari |
(10,10.16 ) aśvaḥ samudro bhūtvādhyaskandadvaśe tvā ||16||

(10,10.17 ) tadbhadrāḥ samagachanta vaśā deṣṭryatho svadhā |
(10,10.17 ) atharvā yatra dīkṣito barhiṣyāsta hiraṇyaye ||17||

(10,10.18 ) vaśā mātā rājanyasya vaśā mātā svadhe tava |
(10,10.18 ) vaśāyā yajña āyudhaṃ tataścittamajāyata ||18||

(10,10.19 ) ūrdhvo bindurudacaradbrahmaṇaḥ kakudādadhi |
(10,10.19 ) tatastvaṃ jajñiṣe vaśe tato hotājāyata ||19||
(10,10.20 ) āsnaste gāthā abhavann uṣṇihābhyo balaṃ vaśe |
(10,10.20 ) pājasyājjajñe yajña stanebhyo raśmayastava ||20|| {34}

(10,10.21 ) īrmābhyāmayanaṃ jātaṃ sakthibhyāṃ ca vaśe tava |
(10,10.21 ) āntrebhyo jajñire atrā udarādadhi vīrudhaḥ ||21||

(10,10.22 ) yadudaraṃ varuṇasyānuprāviśathā vaśe |
(10,10.22 ) tatastvā brahmodahvayatsa hi netramavettava ||22||

(10,10.23 ) sarve garbhādavepanta jāyamānādasūsvaḥ |
(10,10.23 ) sasūva hi tāmāhu vaśeti brahmabhiḥ kḷptaḥ sa hyasyā bandhuḥ ||23||

(10,10.24 ) yudha ekaḥ saṃ sṛjati yo asyā eka idvaśī |
(10,10.24 ) tarāṃsi yajñā abhavan tarasāṃ cakṣurabhavadvaśā ||24||

(10,10.25 ) vaśā yajñaṃ pratyagṛhṇādvaśā sūryamadhārayat|
(10,10.25 ) vaśāyāmantaraviśadodano brahmaṇā saha ||25||

(10,10.26 ) vaśāmevāmṛtamāhurvaśāṃ mṛtyumupāsate |
(10,10.26 ) vaśedaṃ sarvamabhavaddevā manuṣyā asurāḥ pitara ṛṣayaḥ ||26||

(10,10.27 ) ya evaṃ vidyātsa vaśāṃ prati gṛhṇīyāt|
(10,10.27 ) tathā hi yajñaḥ sarvapādduhe dātre'napasphuran ||27||

(10,10.28 ) tisro jihvā varuṇasyāntardīdyatyāsani |
(10,10.28 ) tāsāṃ yā madhye rājati sā vaśā duṣpratigrahā ||28||

(10,10.29 ) caturdhā reto abhavadvaśāyāḥ |
(10,10.29 ) āpasturīyamamṛtaṃ turīyaṃ yajñasturīyaṃ paśavasturīyam ||29||

(10,10.30 ) vaśā dyaurvaśā pṛthivī vaśā viṣṇuḥ prajāpatiḥ |
(10,10.30 ) vaśāyā dugdhamapibantsādhyā vasavaśca ye ||30||

(10,10.31 ) vaśāyā dugdhaṃ pītvā sādhyā vasavaśca ye |
(10,10.31 ) te vai bradhnasya viṣṭapi payo asyā upāsate ||31||

(10,10.32 ) somamenāmeke duhre ghṛtameka upāsate |
(10,10.32 ) ya evaṃ viduṣe vaśāṃ daduste gatāstridivaṃ divaḥ ||32||

(10,10.33 ) brāhmaṇebhyo vaśāṃ dattvā sarvāṃllokāntsamaśnute |
(10,10.33 ) ṛtaṃ hyasyāmārpitamapi brahmātho tapaḥ ||33||

(10,10.34 ) vaśāṃ devā upa jīvanti vaśāṃ manuṣyā uta |
(10,10.34 ) vaśedaṃ sarvamabhavadyāvatsūryo vipaśyati ||34|| {35}




(11,1.1 ) agne jāyasvāditirnāthiteyaṃ brahmaudanaṃ pacati putrakāmā |
(11,1.1 ) saptaṛṣayo bhūtakṛtaste tvā manthantu prajayā saheha ||1||

(11,1.2 ) kṛṇuta dhūmaṃ vṛṣaṇaḥ sakhāyo'droghāvitā vācamacha |
(11,1.2 ) ayamagniḥ pṛtanāṣāṭsuvīro yena devā asahanta dasyūn ||2||

(11,1.3 ) agne'janiṣṭhā mahate vīryāya brahmaudanāya paktave jātavedaḥ |
(11,1.3 ) saptaṛṣayo bhūtakṛtaste tvājījanann asyai rayiṃ sarvavīraṃ ni yacha ||3||

(11,1.4 ) samiddho agne samidhā samidhyasva vidvān devān yajñiyāmeha vakṣaḥ |
(11,1.4 ) tebhyo haviḥ śrapayaṃ jātaveda uttamaṃ nākamadhi rohayemam ||4||

(11,1.5 ) tredhā bhāgo nihito yaḥ purā vo devānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ martyānām |
(11,1.5 ) aṃśāṃ jānīdhvaṃ vi bhajāmi tān vo yo devānāṃ sa imāṃ pārayāti ||5||

(11,1.6 ) agne sahasvān abhibhūrabhīdasi nīco nyubja dviṣataḥ sapatnān |
(11,1.6 ) iyaṃ mātrā mīyamānā mitā ca sajātāṃste balihṛtaḥ kṛṇotu ||6||

(11,1.7 ) sākaṃ sajātaiḥ payasā sahaidhyudubjaināṃ mahate vīryāya |
(11,1.7 ) ūrdhvo nākasyādhi roha viṣṭapaṃ svargo loka iti yaṃ vadanti ||7||

(11,1.8 ) iyaṃ mahī prati gṛhṇātu carma pṛthivī devī sumanasyamānā |
(11,1.8 ) atha gachema sukṛtasya lokam ||8||

(11,1.9 ) etau grāvāṇau sayujā yuṅdhi carmaṇi nirbindhyaṃśūn yajamānāya sādhu |
(11,1.9 ) avaghnatī ni jahi ya imāṃ pṛtanyava ūrdhvaṃ prajāmudbharantyudūha ||9||

(11,1.10 ) gṛhāṇa grāvāṇau sakṛtau vīra hasta ā te devā yajñiyā yajñamaguḥ |
(11,1.10 ) trayo varā yatamāṃstvaṃ vṛṇīṣe tāste samṛddhīriha rādhayāmi ||10|| {1}

(11,1.11 ) iyaṃ te dhītiridamu te janitraṃ gṛhṇātu tvāmaditiḥ śūraputrā |
(11,1.11 ) parā punīhi ya imāṃ pṛtanyavo'syai rayiṃ sarvavīraṃ ni yacha ||11||

(11,1.12 ) upaśvase druvaye sīdatā yūyaṃ vi vicyadhvaṃ yajñiyāsastuṣaiḥ |
(11,1.12 ) śriyā samānān ati sarvāntsyāmādhaspadaṃ dviṣataspādayāmi ||12||

(11,1.13 ) parehi nāri punarehi kṣipramapāṃ tvā goṣṭho adhyarukṣadbharāya |
(11,1.13 ) tāsāṃ gṛhṇītādyatamā yajñiyā asan vibhājya dhīrītarā jahītāt||13||

(11,1.14 ) emā aguryoṣitaḥ śumbhamānā uttiṣṭha nāri tavasaṃ rabhasva |
(11,1.14 ) supatnī patyā prajayā prajāvatyā tvāgan yajñaḥ prati kumbhaṃ gṛbhāya ||14||

(11,1.15 ) ūrjo bhāgo nihito yaḥ purā va ṛṣipraśiṣṭāpa ā bharaitāḥ |
(11,1.15 ) ayaṃ yajño gātuvin nāthavitprajāvidugraḥ paśuvidvīravidvo astu ||15||

(11,1.16 ) agne caruryajñiyastvādhyarukṣacchucistapiṣṭhastapasā tapainam |
(11,1.16 ) ārṣeyā daivā abhisaṃgatya bhāgamimaṃ tapiṣṭhā ṛtubhistapantu ||16||

(11,1.17 ) śuddhāḥ pūtā yoṣito yajñiyā imā āpaścarumava sarpantu śubhrāḥ |
(11,1.17 ) aduḥ prajāṃ bahulāṃ paśūn naḥ paktaudanasya sukṛtāmetu lokam ||17||

(11,1.18 ) brahmaṇā śuddhā uta pūtā ghṛtena somasyāṃśavastaṇḍulā yajñiyā ime |
(11,1.18 ) apaḥ pra viśata prati gṛhṇātu vaścarurimaṃ paktvā sukṛtāmeta lokam ||18||

(11,1.19 ) uruḥ prathasva mahatā mahimnā sahasrapṛṣṭhaḥ sukṛtasya loke |
(11,1.19 ) pitāmahāḥ pitaraḥ prajopajāhaṃ paktā pañcadaśaste asmi ||19||

(11,1.20 ) sahasrapṛṣṭhaḥ śatadhāro akṣito brahmaudano devayānaḥ svargaḥ |
(11,1.20 ) amūṃsta ā dadhāmi prajayā reṣayainān balihārāya mṛḍatān mahyameva ||20|| {2}

(11,1.21 ) udehi vediṃ prajayā vardhayaināṃ nudasva rakṣaḥ prataraṃ dhehyenām |
(11,1.21 ) śriyā samānān ati sarvāntsyāmādhaspadaṃ dviṣataspādayāmi ||21||

(11,1.22 ) abhyāvartasva paśubhiḥ sahaināṃ pratyaṅṅenāṃ devatābhiḥ sahaidhi |
(11,1.22 ) mā tvā prāpacchapatho mābhicāraḥ sve kṣetre anamīvā vi rāja ||22||

(11,1.23 ) ṛtena taṣṭā manasā hitaiṣā brahmaudanasya vihitā vediragre |
(11,1.23 ) aṃsadhrīṃ śuddhāmupa dhehi nāri tatraudanaṃ sādaya daivānām ||23||

(11,1.24 ) aditerhastāṃ srucametāṃ dvitīyāṃ saptaṛṣayo bhūtakṛto yāmakṛṇvan |
(11,1.24 ) sā gātrāṇi viduṣyodanasya darvirvedyāmadhyenaṃ cinotu ||24||

(11,1.25 ) śṛtaṃ tvā havyamupa sīdantu daivā niḥsṛpyāgneḥ punarenān pra sīda |
(11,1.25 ) somena pūto jaṭhare sīda brahmaṇāmārṣeyāste mā riṣan prāśitāraḥ ||25||

(11,1.26 ) soma rājantsaṃjñānamā vapaibhyaḥ subrāhmaṇā yatame tvopasīdān |
(11,1.26 ) ṛṣīn ārṣeyāṃstapaso'dhi jātān brahmaudane suhavā johavīmi ||26||

(11,1.27 ) śuddhāḥ pūtā yoṣito yajñiyā imā brahmaṇāṃ hasteṣu prapṛthaksādayāmi |
(11,1.27 ) yatkāma idamabhiṣiñcāmi vo'hamindro marutvāntsa dadādidaṃ me ||27||

(11,1.28 ) idaṃ me jyotiramṛtaṃ hiraṇyaṃ pakvaṃ kṣetrātkāmadughā ma eṣā |
(11,1.28 ) idaṃ dhanaṃ ni dadhe brāhmaṇeṣu kṛṇve panthāṃ pitṛṣu yaḥ svargaḥ ||28||

(11,1.29 ) agnau tuṣān ā vapa jātavedasi paraḥ kambūkāmapa mṛḍḍhi dūram |
(11,1.29 ) etaṃ śuśruma gṛharājasya bhāgamatho vidma nirṛterbhāgadheyam ||29||

(11,1.30 ) śrāmyataḥ pacato viddhi sunvataḥ panthāṃ svargamadhi rohayainam |
(11,1.30 ) yena rohātparamāpadya yadvaya uttamaṃ nākaṃ paramaṃ vyoma ||30|| {3}

(11,1.31 ) babhreradhvaryo mukhametadvi mṛḍḍhyājyāya lokaṃ kṛṇuhi pravidvān |
(11,1.31 ) ghṛtena gātrānu sarvā vi mṛḍḍhi kṛṇve panthāṃ pitṛṣu yaḥ svargaḥ ||31||

(11,1.32 ) babhre rakṣaḥ samadamā vapaibhyo'brāhmaṇā yatame tvopasīdān |
(11,1.32 ) purīṣiṇaḥ prathamānāḥ purastādārṣeyāste mā riṣan prāśitāraḥ ||32||

(11,1.33 ) ārṣeyeṣu ni dadha odana tvā nānārṣeyāṇāmapyastyatra |
(11,1.33 ) agnirme goptā marutaśca sarve viśve devā abhi rakṣantu pakvam ||33||

(11,1.34 ) yajñaṃ duhānaṃ sadamitprapīnaṃ pumāṃsaṃ dhenuṃ sadanaṃ rayīṇām |
(11,1.34 ) prajāmṛtatvamuta dīrghamāyū rāyaśca poṣairupa tvā sadema ||34||

(11,1.35 ) vṛṣabho'si svarga ṛṣīn ārṣeyān gacha |
(11,1.35 ) sukṛtāṃ loke sīda tatra nau saṃskṛtam ||35||

(11,1.36 ) samācinuṣvānusaṃprayāhyagne pathaḥ kalpaya devayānān |
(11,1.36 ) etaiḥ sukṛtairanu gachema yajñaṃ nāke tiṣṭhantamadhi saptaraśmau ||36||

(11,1.37 ) yena devā jyotiṣā dyāmudāyan brahmaudanaṃ paktvā sukṛtasya lokam |
(11,1.37 ) tena geṣma sukṛtasya lokaṃ svarārohanto abhi nākamuttamam ||37|| {4}



(11,2.1 ) bhavāśarvau mṛḍataṃ mābhi yātaṃ bhūtapatī paśupatī namo vām |
(11,2.1 ) pratihitāmāyatāṃ mā vi srāṣṭaṃ mā no hiṃsiṣṭaṃ dvipado mā catuṣpadaḥ ||1||

(11,2.2 ) śune kroṣṭre mā śarīrāṇi kartamaliklavebhyo gṛdhrebhyo ye ca kṛṣṇā aviṣyavaḥ |
(11,2.2 ) makṣikāste paśupate vayāṃsi te vighase mā vidanta ||2||

(11,2.3 ) krandāya te prāṇāya yāśca te bhava ropayaḥ |
(11,2.3 ) namaste rudra kṛṇmaḥ sahasrākṣāyāmartya ||3||

(11,2.4 ) purastātte namaḥ kṛṇma uttarādadharāduta |
(11,2.4 ) abhīvargāddivasparyantarikṣāya te namaḥ ||4||

(11,2.5 ) mukhāya te paśupate yāni cakṣūṃṣi te bhava |
(11,2.5 ) tvace rūpāya saṃdṛśe pratīcīnāya te namaḥ ||5||

(11,2.6 ) aṅgebhyasta udarāya jihvāyā āsyāya te |
(11,2.6 ) dadbhyo gandhāya te namaḥ ||6||

(11,2.7 ) astrā nīlaśikhaṇḍena sahasrākṣeṇa vājinā |
(11,2.7 ) rudreṇārdhakaghātinā tena mā samarāmahi ||7||

(11,2.8 ) sa no bhavaḥ pari vṛṇaktu viśvata āpa ivāgniḥ pari vṛṇaktu no bhavaḥ |
(11,2.8 ) mā no'bhi māṃsta namo astvasmai ||8||

(11,2.9 ) caturnamo aṣṭakṛtvo bhavāya daśa kṛtvaḥ paśupate namaste |
(11,2.9 ) taveme pañca paśavo vibhaktā gāvo aśvāḥ puruṣā ajāvayaḥ ||9||

(11,2.10 ) tava catasraḥ pradiśastava dyaustava pṛthivī tavedamugrorvantarikṣam |
(11,2.10 ) tavedaṃ sarvamātmanvadyatprāṇatpṛthivīmanu ||10|| {5}

(11,2.11 ) uruḥ kośo vasudhānastavāyaṃ yasminn imā viśvā bhuvanānyantaḥ |
(11,2.11 ) sa no mṛḍa paśupate namaste paraḥ kroṣṭāro abhibhāḥ śvānaḥ paro yantvagharudo vikeśyaḥ ||11||

(11,2.12 ) dhanurbibharṣi haritaṃ hiraṇyayaṃ sahasraghniṃ śatavadhaṃ śikhaṇḍin |
(11,2.12 ) rudrasyeṣuścarati devahetistasyai namo yatamasyāṃ diśītaḥ ||12||

(11,2.13 ) yo'bhiyāto nilayate tvāṃ rudra nicikīrṣati |
(11,2.13 ) paścādanuprayuṅkṣe taṃ viddhasya padanīriva ||13||

(11,2.14 ) bhavārudrau sayujā saṃvidānāvubhāvugrau carato vīryāya |
(11,2.14 ) tābhyāṃ namo yatamasyāṃ diśītaḥ ||14||

(11,2.15 ) namaste astvāyate namo astu parāyate |
(11,2.15 ) namaste rudra tiṣṭhata āsīnāyota te namaḥ ||15||

(11,2.16 ) namaḥ sāyaṃ namaḥ prātarnamo rātryā namo divā |
(11,2.16 ) bhavāya ca śarvāya cobhābhyāmakaraṃ namaḥ ||16||

(11,2.17 ) sahasrākṣamatipaśyaṃ purastādrudramasyantaṃ bahudhā vipaścitam |
(11,2.17 ) mopārāma jihvayeyamānam ||17||

(11,2.18 ) śyāvāśvaṃ kṛṣṇamasitaṃ mṛṇantaṃ bhīmaṃ rathaṃ keśinaḥ pādayantam |
(11,2.18 ) pūrve pratīmo namo astvasmai ||18||

(11,2.19 ) mā no'bhi srā matyaṃ devahetiṃ mā naḥ krudhaḥ paśupate namaste |
(11,2.19 ) anyatrāsmaddivyāṃ śākhāṃ vi dhūnu ||19||

(11,2.20 ) mā no hiṃsīradhi no brūhi pari ṇo vṛṅdhi mā krudhaḥ |
(11,2.20 ) mā tvayā samarāmahi ||20|| {6}

(11,2.21 ) mā no goṣu puruṣeṣu mā gṛdho no ajāviṣu |
(11,2.21 ) anyatrogra vi vartaya piyārūṇāṃ prajāṃ jahi ||21||

(11,2.22 ) yasya takmā kāsikā hetirekamaśvasyeva vṛṣaṇaḥ kranda eti |
(11,2.22 ) abhipūrvaṃ nirṇayate namo astvasmai ||22||

(11,2.23 ) yo'ntarikṣe tiṣṭhati viṣṭabhito'yajvanaḥ pramṛṇan devapīyūn |
(11,2.23 ) tasmai namo daśabhiḥ śakvarībhiḥ ||23||

(11,2.24 ) tubhyamāraṇyāḥ paśavo mṛgā vane hitā haṃsāḥ suparṇāḥ śakunā vayāṃsi |
(11,2.24 ) tava yakṣaṃ paśupate apsvantastubhyaṃ kṣaranti divyā āpo vṛdhe ||24||

(11,2.25 ) śiṃśumārā ajagarāḥ purīkayā jaṣā matsyā rajasā yebhyo asyasi |
(11,2.25 ) na te dūraṃ na pariṣṭhāsti te bhava sadyaḥ sarvāṃ pari paśyasi bhūmiṃ pūrvasmāddhaṃsyuttarasmintsamudre ||25||

(11,2.26 ) mā no rudra takmanā mā viṣeṇa mā naḥ saṃ srā divyenāgninā |
(11,2.26 ) anyatrāsmadvidyutaṃ pātayaitām ||26||

(11,2.27 ) bhavo divo bhava īśe pṛthivyā bhava ā papra urvantarikṣam |
(11,2.27 ) tasyai namo yatamasyāṃ diśītaḥ ||27||

(11,2.28 ) bhava rājan yajamānāya mṛḍa paśūnāṃ hi paśupatirbabhūtha |
(11,2.28 ) yaḥ śraddadhāti santi devā iti catuṣpade dvipade'sya mṛḍa ||28||

(11,2.29 ) mā no mahāntamuta mā no arbhakaṃ mā no vahantamuta mā no vakṣyataḥ |
(11,2.29 ) mā no hiṃsīḥ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ ca svāṃ tanvaṃ rudra mā rīriṣo naḥ ||29||

(11,2.30 ) rudrasyailabakārebhyo'saṃsūktagilebhyaḥ |
(11,2.30 ) idaṃ mahāsyebhyaḥ śvabhyo akaraṃ namaḥ ||30||

(11,2.31 ) namaste ghoṣiṇībhyo namaste keśinībhyaḥ |
(11,2.31 ) namo namaskṛtābhyo namaḥ saṃbhuñjatībhyaḥ |
(11,2.31 ) namaste deva senābhyaḥ svasti no abhayaṃ ca naḥ ||31|| {7}


(11,3.1 ) tasyaudanasya bṛhaspatiḥ śiro brahma mukham ||1||

(11,3.2 ) dyāvāpṛthivī śrotre sūryācandramasāvakṣiṇī saptaṛṣayaḥ prāṇāpānāḥ ||2||

(11,3.3 ) cakṣurmusalaṃ kāma ulūkhalam ||3||

(11,3.4 ) ditiḥ śūrpamaditiḥ śūrpagrāhī vāto'pāvinak||4||

(11,3.5 ) aśvāḥ kaṇā gāvastaṇḍulā maśakāstuṣāḥ ||5||

(11,3.6 ) kabru phalīkaraṇāḥ śaro'bhram ||6||

(11,3.7 ) śyāmamayo'sya māṃsāni lohitamasya lohitam ||7||

(11,3.8 ) trapu bhasma haritaṃ varṇaḥ puṣkaramasya gandhaḥ ||8||

(11,3.9 ) khalaḥ pātraṃ sphyāvaṃsāvīṣe anūkye ||9||

(11,3.10 ) āntrāṇi jatravo gudā varatrāḥ ||10||

(11,3.11 ) iyameva pṛthivī kumbhī bhavati rādhyamānasyaudanasya dyaurapidhānam ||11||

(11,3.12 ) sītāḥ parśavaḥ sikatā ūbadhyam ||12||

(11,3.13 ) ṛtaṃ hastāvanejanaṃ kulyopasecanam ||13||

(11,3.14 ) ṛcā kumbhyadhihitārtvijyena preṣitā ||14||

(11,3.15 ) brahmaṇā parigṛhītā sāmnā paryūḍhā ||15||

(11,3.16 ) bṛhadāyavanaṃ rathantaraṃ darviḥ ||16||

(11,3.17 ) ṛtavaḥ paktāra ārtavāḥ samindhate ||17||

(11,3.18 ) caruṃ pañcabilamukhaṃ gharmo'bhīndhe ||18|| [note COṛṛīṅEṇḍā ed. śড়ড়]
(11,3.19 ) odanena yajñavataḥ sarve lokāḥ samāpyāḥ ||19||

(11,3.20 ) yasmintsamudro dyaurbhūmistrayo'varaparaṃ śritāḥ ||20||

(11,3.21 ) yasya devā akalpantocchiṣṭe ṣaḍaśītayaḥ ||21||

(11,3.22 ) taṃ tvaudanasya pṛchāmi yo asya mahimā mahān ||22||

(11,3.23 ) sa ya odanasya mahimānaṃ vidyāt||23||

(11,3.24 ) nālpa iti brūyān nānupasecana iti nedaṃ ca kiṃ ceti ||24||

(11,3.25 ) yāvaddātābhimanasyeta tan nāti vadet||25||

(11,3.26 ) brahmavādino vadanti parāñcamodanaṃ prāśī3ḥ pratyañcā3 iti ||26||

(11,3.27 ) tvamodanaṃ prāśī3 tvāmodanā3 iti ||27||

(11,3.28 ) parāñcaṃ cainaṃ prāśīḥ prāṇāstvā hāsyantītyenamāha ||28||

(11,3.29 ) pratyañcaṃ cainaṃ prāśīrapānāstvā hāsyantītyenamāha ||29||

(11,3.30 ) naivāhamodanaṃ na māmodanaḥ ||30||

(11,3.31 ) odana evaudanaṃ prāśīt||31|| {8}



(11,3.32[4.1]a) tataścainamanyena śīrṣṇā prāśīryena caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11,3.32[4.1]b) jyeṣṭhataste prajā mariṣyatītyenamāha |
(11,3.32[4.1]c) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11,3.32[4.1]d) bṛhaspatinā śīrṣṇā |
(11,3.32[4.1]e) tenainaṃ prāśiṣaṃ tenainamajīgamam |
(11,3.32[4.1]f) eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ |
(11,3.32[4.1]g) sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ saṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||32|| [1]

(11,3.33[4.2]a) tataścainamanyābhyāṃ śrotrābhyāṃ prāśīryābhyāṃ caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11,3.33[4.2]b) badhiro bhaviṣyasītyenamāha |
(11,3.33[4.2]c) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11,3.33[4.2]d) dyāvāpṛthivībhyāṃ śrotrābhyām |
(11,3.33[4.2]e) tābhyāmenaṃ prāśiṣaṃ tābhyāmenamajīgamam |
(11,3.33[4.2]f) eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ |
(11,3.33[4.2]g) sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ saṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||33|| [2]

(11,3.34[4.3]a) tataścainamanyābhyāmakṣībhyāṃ prāśīryābhyāṃ caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11,3.34[4.3]b) andho bhaviṣyasītyenamāha |
(11,3.34[4.3]c) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11,3.34[4.3]d) sūryācandramasābhyāmakṣībhyām |
(11,3.34[4.3]e) tābhyāmenaṃ prāśiṣaṃ tābhyāmenamajīgamam |
(11,3.34[4.3]f) eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ |
(11,3.34[4.3]g) sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ saṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||34|| [3]

(11,3.35[4.4]a) tataścainamanyena mukhena prāśīryena caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11,3.35[4.4]b) mukhataste prajā mariṣyatītyenamāha |
(11,3.35[4.4]c) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11,3.35[4.4]d) brahmaṇā mukhena |
(11,3.35[4.4]e) tenainaṃ prāśiṣaṃ tenainamajīgamam |
(11,3.35[4.4]f) eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ |
(11,3.35[4.4]g) sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ saṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||35|| [4]

(11,3.36[4.5]a) tataścainamanyayā jihvayā prāśīryayā caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11,3.36[4.5]b) jihvā te mariṣyatītyenamāha |
(11,3.36[4.5]c) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11,3.36[4.5]d) agnerjihvayā |
(11,3.36[4.5]e) tayainaṃ prāśiṣaṃ tayainamajīgamam |
(11,3.36[4.5]f) eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ |
(11,3.36[4.5]g) sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ saṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||36|| [5]

(11,3.37[4.6]a) tataścainamanyairdantaiḥ prāśīryaiścaitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11,3.37[4.6]b) dantāste śatsyantītyenamāha |
(11,3.37[4.6]c) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11,3.37[4.6]d) ṛtubhirdantaiḥ |
(11,3.37[4.6]e) tairenaṃ prāśiṣaṃ tairenamajīgamam |
(11,3.37[4.6]f) eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ |
(11,3.37[4.6]g) sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ saṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||37|| [6]

(11,3.38[4.7]a) tataścainamanyaiḥ prāṇāpānaiḥ prāśīryaiścaitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11,3.38[4.7]b) prāṇāpānāstvā hāsyantītyenamāha |
(11,3.38[4.7]c) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11,3.38[4.7]d) saptaṛṣibhiḥ prāṇāpānaiḥ |
(11,3.38[4.7]e) tairenaṃ prāśiṣaṃ tairenamajīgamam |
(11,3.38[4.7]f) eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ |
(11,3.38[4.7]g) sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ saṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||38|| [7]

(11,3.39[4.8]a) tataścainamanyena vyacasā prāśīryena caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11,3.39[4.8]b) rājayakṣmastvā haniṣyatītyenamāha |
(11,3.39[4.8]c) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11,3.39[4.8]d) antarikṣeṇa vyacasā |
(11,3.39[4.8]e) tenainaṃ prāśiṣaṃ tenainamajīgamam |
(11,3.39[4.8]f) eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ |
(11,3.39[4.8]g) sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ saṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||39|| [8]

(11,3.40[4.9]a) tataścainamanyena pṛṣṭhena prāśīryena caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11,3.40[4.9]b) vidyuttvā haniṣyatītyenamāha |
(11,3.40[4.9]c) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11,3.40[4.9]d) divā pṛṣṭhena |
(11,3.40[4.9]e) tenainaṃ prāśiṣaṃ tenainamajīgamam |
(11,3.40[4.9]f) eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ |
(11,3.40[4.9]g) sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ saṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||40|| [9]

(11,3.41[4.10]a) tataścainamanyenorasā prāśīryena caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11,3.41[4.10]b) kṛṣyā na rātsyasītyenamāha |
(11,3.41[4.10]c) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11,3.41[4.10]d) pṛthivyorasā |
(11,3.41[4.10]e) tenainaṃ prāśiṣaṃ tenainamajīgamam |
(11,3.41[4.10]f) eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ |
(11,3.41[4.10]g) sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ saṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||41|| [10]

(11,3.42[4.11]a) tataścainamanyenodareṇa prāśīryena caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11,3.42[4.11]b) udaradārastvā haniṣyatītyenamāha |
(11,3.42[4.11]c) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11,3.42[4.11]d) satyenodareṇa |
(11,3.42[4.11]e) tenainaṃ prāśiṣaṃ tenainamajīgamam |
(11,3.42[4.11]f) eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ |
(11,3.42[4.11]g) sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ saṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||42|| [11]

(11,3.43[4.12]a) tataścainamanyena vastinā prāśīryena caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11,3.43[4.12]b) apsu mariṣyasītyenamāha |
(11,3.43[4.12]c) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11,3.43[4.12]d) samudreṇa vastinā |
(11,3.43[4.12]e) tenainaṃ prāśiṣaṃ tenainamajīgamam |
(11,3.43[4.12]f) eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ |
(11,3.43[4.12]g) sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ saṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||43|| [12]

(11,3.44[4.13]a) tataścainamanyābhyāmūrubhyāṃ prāśīryābhyāṃ caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11,3.44[4.13]b) ūrū te mariṣyata ityenamāha |
(11,3.44[4.13]c) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11,3.44[4.13]d) mitrāvaruṇayorūrubhyām |
(11,3.44[4.13]e) tābhyāmenaṃ prāśiṣaṃ tābhyāmenamajīgamam |
(11,3.44[4.13]f) eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ |
(11,3.44[4.13]g) sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ saṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||44|| [13]

(11,3.45[4.14]a) tataścainamanyābhyāmaṣṭhīvadbhyāṃ prāśīryābhyāṃ caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11,3.45[4.14]b) srāmo bhaviṣyasītyenamāha |
(11,3.45[4.14]c) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11,3.45[4.14]d) tvaṣṭuraṣṭhīvadbhyām |
(11,3.45[4.14]e) tābhyāmenaṃ prāśiṣaṃ tābhyāmenamajīgamam |
(11,3.45[4.14]f) eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ |
(11,3.45[4.14]g) sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ saṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||45|| [14]

(11,3.46[4.15]a) tataścainamanyābhyāṃ pādābhyāṃ prāśīryābhyāṃ caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11,3.46[4.15]b) bahucārī bhaviṣyasītyenamāha |
(11,3.46[4.15]c) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11,3.46[4.15]d) aśvinoḥ pādābhyām |
(11,3.46[4.15]e) tābhyāmenaṃ prāśiṣaṃ tābhyāmenamajīgamam |
(11,3.46[4.15]f) eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ |
(11,3.46[4.15]g) sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ saṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||46|| [15]

(11,3.47[4.16]a) tataścainamanyābhyāṃ prapadābhyāṃ prāśīryābhyāṃ caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11,3.47[4.16]b) sarpastvā haniṣyatītyenamāha |
(11,3.47[4.16]c) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11,3.47[4.16]d) savituḥ prapadābhyām |
(11,3.47[4.16]e) tābhyāmenaṃ prāśiṣaṃ tābhyāmenamajīgamam |
(11,3.47[4.16]f) eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ |
(11,3.47[4.16]g) sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ saṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||47|| [16]

(11,3.48[4.17]a) tataścainamanyābhyāṃ hastābhyāṃ prāśīryābhyāṃ caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11,3.48[4.17]b) brāhmaṇaṃ haniṣyasītyenamāha |
(11,3.48[4.17]c) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11,3.48[4.17]d) ṛtasya hastābhyām |
(11,3.48[4.17]e) tābhyāmenaṃ prāśiṣaṃ tābhyāmenamajīgamam |
(11,3.48[4.17]f) eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ |
(11,3.48[4.17]g) sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ saṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||48|| [17]

(11,3.49[4.18]a) tataścainamanyayā pratiṣṭhayā prāśīryayā caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11,3.49[4.18]b) apratiṣṭhāno'nāyatano mariṣyasītyenamāha |
(11,3.49[4.18]c) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11,3.49[4.18]d) satye pratiṣṭhāya |
(11,3.49[4.18]e) tayainaṃ prāśiṣaṃ tayainamajīgamam |
(11,3.49[4.18]f) eṣa vā odanaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ |
(11,3.49[4.18]g) sarvāṅga eva sarvaparuḥ sarvatanūḥ saṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||49|| [18] {9}



(11,3.50[5.1]a) etadvai bradhnasya viṣṭapaṃ yadodanaḥ ||50|| [1]

(11,3.51[5.2]a) bradhnaloko bhavati bradhnasya viṣṭapi śrayate ya evaṃ veda ||51|| [2]

(11,3.52[5.3]a) etasmādvā odanāttrayastriṃśataṃ lokān niramimīta prajāpatiḥ ||52|| [3]

(11,3.53[5.4]a) teṣāṃ prajñānāya yajñamasṛjata ||53|| [4]

(11,3.54[5.5]a) sa ya evaṃ viduṣa upadraṣṭā bhavati prāṇaṃ ruṇaddhi ||54|| [5]

(11,3.55[5.6]a) na ca prāṇaṃ ruṇaddhi sarvajyāniṃ jīyate ||55|| [6]

(11,3.56[5.7]a) na ca sarvajyāniṃ jīyate purainaṃ jarasaḥ prāṇo jahāti ||56|| [7] {10}



(11,4[6].1 ) prāṇāya namo yasya sarvamidaṃ vaśe |
(11,4[6].1 ) yo bhūtaḥ sarvasyeśvaro yasmintsarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ||1||

(11,4[6].2 ) namaste prāṇa krandāya namaste stanayitnave |
(11,4[6].2 ) namaste prāṇa vidyute namaste prāṇa varṣate ||2||

(11,4[6].3 ) yatprāṇa stanayitnunābhikrandatyoṣadhīḥ |
(11,4[6].3 ) pra vīyante garbhān dadhate'tho bahvīrvi jāyante ||3||

(11,4[6].4 ) yatprāṇa ṛtāvāgate'bhikrandatyoṣadhīḥ |
(11,4[6].4 ) sarvaṃ tadā pra modate yatkiṃ ca bhūmyāmadhi ||4||
(11,4[6].5 ) yadā prāṇo abhyavarṣīdvarṣeṇa pṛthivīṃ mahīm |
(11,4[6].5 ) paśavastatpra modante maho vai no bhaviṣyati ||5||

(11,4[6].6 ) abhivṛṣṭā oṣadhayaḥ prāṇena samavādiran |
(11,4[6].6 ) āyurvai naḥ prātītaraḥ sarvā naḥ surabhīrakaḥ ||6||

(11,4[6].7 ) namaste astvāyate namo astu parāyate |
(11,4[6].7 ) namaste prāṇa tiṣṭhata āsīnāyota te namaḥ ||7||

(11,4[6].8 ) namaste prāṇa prāṇate namo astvapānate |
(11,4[6].8 ) parācīnāya te namaḥ pratīcīnāya te namaḥ sarvasmai ta idaṃ namaḥ ||8||

(11,4[6].9 ) yā te prāṇa priyā tanūryo te prāṇa preyasī |
(11,4[6].9 ) atho yadbheṣajaṃ tava tasya no dhehi jīvase ||9||
(11,4[6].10 ) prāṇaḥ prajā anu vaste pitā putramiva priyam |
(11,4[6].10 ) prāṇo ha sarvasyeśvaro yacca prāṇati yacca na ||10|| {11}

(11,4[6].11 ) prāṇo mṛtyuḥ prāṇastakmā prāṇaṃ devā upāsate |
(11,4[6].11 ) prāṇo ha satyavādinamuttame loka ā dadhat||11||

(11,4[6].12 ) prāṇo virāṭprāṇo deṣṭrī prāṇaṃ sarva upāsate |
(11,4[6].12 ) prāṇo ha sūryaścandramāḥ prāṇamāhuḥ prajāpatim ||12||

(11,4[6].13 ) prāṇāpānau vrīhiyavāvanaḍvān prāṇa ucyate |
(11,4[6].13 ) yave ha prāṇa āhito'pāno vrīhirucyate ||13||

(11,4[6].14 ) apānati prāṇati puruṣo garbhe antarā |
(11,4[6].14 ) yadā tvaṃ prāṇa jinvasyatha sa jāyate punaḥ ||14||

(11,4[6].15 ) prāṇamāhurmātariśvānaṃ vāto ha prāṇa ucyate |
(11,4[6].15 ) prāṇe ha bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ ca prāṇe sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ||15||

(11,4[6].16 ) ātharvaṇīrāṅgirasīrdaivīrmanuṣyajā uta |
(11,4[6].16 ) oṣadhayaḥ pra jāyante yadā tvaṃ prāṇa jinvasi ||16||

(11,4[6].17 ) yadā prāṇo abhyavarṣīdvarṣeṇa pṛthivīṃ mahīm |
(11,4[6].17 ) oṣadhayaḥ pra jāyante'tho yāḥ kāśca vīrudhaḥ ||17||

(11,4[6].18 ) yaste prāṇedaṃ veda yasmiṃścāsi pratiṣṭhitaḥ |
(11,4[6].18 ) sarve tasmai baliṃ harān amuṣmiṃlloka uttame ||18||
(11,4[6].19 ) yathā prāṇa balihṛtastubhyaṃ sarvāḥ prajā imāḥ |
(11,4[6].19 ) evā tasmai baliṃ harān yastvā śṛṇavatsuśravaḥ ||19||

(11,4[6].20 ) antargarbhaścarati devatāsvābhūto bhūtaḥ sa u jāyate punaḥ |
(11,4[6].20 ) sa bhūto bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyatpitā putraṃ pra viveśā śacībhiḥ ||20|| {12}

(11,4[6].21 ) ekaṃ pādaṃ notkhidati salilāddhaṃsa uccaran |
(11,4[6].21 ) yadaṅga sa tamutkhiden naivādya na śvaḥ syāt|
(11,4[6].21 ) na rātrī nāhaḥ syān na vyuchetkadā cana ||21||

(11,4[6].22 ) aṣṭācakraṃ vartata ekanemi sahasrākṣaraṃ pra puro ni paścā |
(11,4[6].22 ) ardhena viśvaṃ bhuvanaṃ jajāna yadasyārdhaṃ katamaḥ sa ketuḥ ||22||

(11,4[6].23 ) yo asya viśvajanmana īśe viśvasya ceṣṭataḥ |
(11,4[6].23 ) anyeṣu kṣipradhanvane tasmai prāṇa namo'stu te ||23||

(11,4[6].24 ) yo asya sarvajanmana īśe sarvasya ceṣṭataḥ |
(11,4[6].24 ) atandro brahmaṇā dhīraḥ prāṇo mānu tiṣṭhatu ||24||

(11,4[6].25 ) ūrdhvaḥ supteṣu jāgāra nanu tiryaṅni padyate |
(11,4[6].25 ) na suptamasya supteṣvanu śuśrāva kaścana ||25||

(11,4[6].26 ) prāṇa mā matparyāvṛto na madanyo bhaviṣyasi |
(11,4[6].26 ) apāṃ garbhamiva jīvase prāṇa badhnāmi tvā mayi ||26|| {13}



(11,5[7].1 ) brahmacārīṣṇaṃścarati rodasī ubhe tasmin devāḥ saṃmanaso bhavanti |
