(11,5[7].1 ) sa dādhāra pṛthivīṃ divaṃ ca sa ācāryaṃ tapasā piparti ||1||

(11,5[7].2 ) brahmacāriṇaṃ pitaro devajanāḥ pṛthagdevā anusaṃyanti sarve |
(11,5[7].2 ) gandharvā enamanvāyan trayastriṃśattriśatāḥ ṣaṭsahasrāḥ sarvāntsa devāṃstapasā piparti ||2||

(11,5[7].3 ) ācārya upanayamāno brahmacāriṇaṃ kṛṇute garbhamantaḥ |
(11,5[7].3 ) taṃ rātrīstisra udare bibharti taṃ jātaṃ draṣṭumabhisaṃyanti devāḥ ||3||

(11,5[7].4 ) iyaṃ samitpṛthivī dyaurdvitīyotāntarikṣaṃ samidhā pṛṇāti |
(11,5[7].4 ) brahmacārī samidhā mekhalayā śrameṇa lokāṃstapasā piparti ||4||

(11,5[7].5 ) pūrvo jāto brahmaṇo brahmacārī gharmaṃ vasānastapasodatiṣṭhat|
(11,5[7].5 ) tasmājjātaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ brahma jyeṣṭhaṃ devāśca sarve amṛtena sākam ||5||

(11,5[7].6 ) brahmacāryeti samidhā samiddhaḥ kārṣṇaṃ vasāno dīkṣito dīrghaśmaśruḥ |
(11,5[7].6 ) sa sadya eti pūrvasmāduttaraṃ samudraṃ lokāntsaṃgṛbhya muhurācarikrat||6||

(11,5[7].7 ) brahmacārī janayan brahmāpo lokaṃ prajāpatiṃ parameṣṭhinaṃ virājam |
(11,5[7].7 ) garbho bhūtvāmṛtasya yonāvindro ha bhūtvāsurāṃstatarha ||7||

(11,5[7].8 ) ācāryastatakṣa nabhasī ubhe ime urvī gambhīre pṛthivīṃ divaṃ ca |
(11,5[7].8 ) te rakṣati tapasā brahmacārī tasmin devāḥ saṃmanaso bhavanti ||8||

(11,5[7].9 ) imāṃ bhūmiṃ pṛthivīṃ brahmacārī bhikṣāmā jabhāra prathamo divaṃ ca |
(11,5[7].9 ) te kṛtvā samidhāvupāste tayorārpitā bhuvanāni viśvā ||9||

(11,5[7].10 ) arvāganyaḥ paro anyo divaspṛṣṭhādguhā nidhī nihitau brāhmaṇasya |
(11,5[7].10 ) tau rakṣati tapasā brahmacārī tatkevalaṃ kṛṇute brahma vidvān ||10|| {14}

(11,5[7].11 ) arvāganya ito anyaḥ pṛthivyā agnī sameto nabhasī antareme |
(11,5[7].11 ) tayoḥ śrayante raśmayo'dhi dṛḍhāstān ā tiṣṭhati tapasā brahmacārī ||11||

(11,5[7].12 ) abhikrandan stanayann aruṇaḥ śitiṅgo bṛhacchepo'nu bhūmau jabhāra |
(11,5[7].12 ) brahmacārī siñcati sānau retaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tena jīvanti pradiśaścatasraḥ ||12||

(11,5[7].13 ) agnau sūrye candramasi mātariśvan brahmacāryapsu samidhamā dadhāti |
(11,5[7].13 ) tāsāmarcīṃṣi pṛthagabhre caranti tāsāmājyaṃ puruṣo varṣamāpaḥ ||13||

(11,5[7].14 ) ācāryo mṛtyurvaruṇaḥ soma oṣadhayaḥ payaḥ |
(11,5[7].14 ) jīmūtā āsantsatvānastairidaṃ svarābhṛtam ||14||

(11,5[7].15 ) amā ghṛtaṃ kṛṇute kevalamācāryo bhūtvā varuṇaḥ |
(11,5[7].15 ) yadyadaichatprajāpatau tadbrahmacārī prāyachatsvān mitro adhyātmanaḥ ||15||

(11,5[7].16 ) ācāryo brahmacārī brahmacārī prajāpatiḥ |
(11,5[7].16 ) prajāpatirvi rājati virāḍindro'bhavadvaśī ||16||

(11,5[7].17 ) brahmacaryeṇa tapasā rājā rāṣṭraṃ vi rakṣati |
(11,5[7].17 ) ācāryo brahmacaryeṇa brahmacāriṇamichate ||17||

(11,5[7].18 ) brahmacaryeṇa kanyā yuvānaṃ vindate patim |
(11,5[7].18 ) anaḍvān brahmacaryeṇāśvo ghāsaṃ jigīṣati ||18||

(11,5[7].19 ) brahmacaryeṇa tapasā devā mṛtyumapāghnata |
(11,5[7].19 ) indro ha brahmacaryeṇa devebhyaḥ svarābharat||19||

(11,5[7].20 ) oṣadhayo bhūtabhavyamahorātre vanaspatiḥ |
(11,5[7].20 ) saṃvatsaraḥ saha ṛtubhiste jātā brahmacāriṇaḥ ||20|| {15}

(11,5[7].21 ) pārthivā divyāḥ paśava āraṇyā grāmyāśca ye |
(11,5[7].21 ) apakṣāḥ pakṣiṇaśca ye te jātā brahmacāriṇaḥ ||21||

(11,5[7].22 ) pṛthaksarve prājāpatyāḥ prāṇān ātmasu bibhrati |
(11,5[7].22 ) tāntsarvān brahma rakṣati brahmacāriṇyābhṛtam ||22||

(11,5[7].23 ) devānāmetatpariṣūtamanabhyārūḍhaṃ carati rocamānam |
(11,5[7].23 ) tasmājjātaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ brahma jyeṣṭhaṃ devāśca sarve amṛtena sākam ||23||

(11,5[7].24 ) brahmacārī brahma bhrājadbibharti tasmin devā adhi viśve samotāḥ |
(11,5[7].24 ) prāṇāpānau janayann ādvyānaṃ vācaṃ mano hṛdayaṃ brahma medhām ||24||

(11,5[7].25 ) cakṣuḥ śrotraṃ yaśo asmāsu dhehyannaṃ reto lohitamudaram ||25||

(11,5[7].26 ) tāni kalpan brahmacārī salilasya pṛṣṭhe tapo'tiṣṭhattapyamānaḥ samudre |
(11,5[7].26 ) sa snāto babhruḥ piṅgalaḥ pṛthivyāṃ bahu rocate ||26|| {16}



(11,6[8].1 ) agniṃ brūmo vanaspatīn oṣadhīruta vīrudhaḥ |
(11,6[8].1 ) indraṃ bṛhaspatiṃ sūryaṃ te no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||1||

(11,6[8].2 ) brūmo rājānaṃ varuṇaṃ mitraṃ viṣṇumatho bhagam |
(11,6[8].2 ) aṃśaṃ vivasvantaṃ brūmaste no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||2||

(11,6[8].3 ) brūmo devaṃ savitāraṃ dhātāramuta pūṣaṇam |
(11,6[8].3 ) tvaṣṭāramagriyaṃ brūmaste no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||3||

(11,6[8].4 ) gandharvāpsaraso brūmo aśvinā brahmaṇaspatim |
(11,6[8].4 ) aryamā nāma yo devaste no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||4||

(11,6[8].5 ) ahorātre idaṃ brūmaḥ sūryācandramasāvubhā |
(11,6[8].5 ) viśvān ādityān brūmaste no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||5||

(11,6[8].6 ) vātaṃ brūmaḥ parjanyamantarikṣamatho diśaḥ |
(11,6[8].6 ) āśāśca sarvā brūmaste no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||6||

(11,6[8].7 ) muñcantu mā śapathyādahorātre atho uṣāḥ |
(11,6[8].7 ) somo mā devo muñcatu yamāhuścandramā iti ||7||

(11,6[8].8 ) pārthivā divyāḥ paśava āraṇyā uta ye mṛgāḥ |
(11,6[8].8 ) śakuntān pakṣiṇo brūmaste no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||8||

(11,6[8].9 ) bhavāśarvāvidaṃ brūmo rudraṃ paśupatiśca yaḥ |
(11,6[8].9 ) iṣūryā eṣāṃ saṃvidma tā naḥ santu sadā śivāḥ ||9||

(11,6[8].10 ) divaṃ brūmo nakṣatrāṇi bhūmiṃ yakṣāṇi parvatān |
(11,6[8].10 ) samudrā nadyo veśantāste no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||10|| {17}

(11,6[8].11 ) saptaṛṣīn vā idaṃ brūmo'po devīḥ prajāpatim |
(11,6[8].11 ) pitṝn yamaśreṣṭhān brūmaste no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ |11||

(11,6[8].12 ) ye devā diviṣado antarikṣasadaśca ye |
(11,6[8].12 ) pṛthivyāṃ śakrā ye śritāste no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||12||

(11,6[8].13 ) ādityā rudrā vasavo divi devā atharvāṇaḥ |
(11,6[8].13 ) aṅgiraso manīṣiṇaste no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||13||

(11,6[8].14 ) yajñaṃ brūmo yajamānamṛcaḥ sāmāni bheṣajā |
(11,6[8].14 ) yajūṃṣi hotrā brūmaste no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||14||

(11,6[8].15 ) pañca rājyāni vīrudhāṃ somaśreṣṭhāni brūmaḥ |
(11,6[8].15 ) darbho bhaṅgo yavaḥ sahaste no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||15||

(11,6[8].16 ) arāyān brūmo rakṣāṃsi sarpān puṇyajanān pitṝn |
(11,6[8].16 ) mṛtyūn ekaśataṃ brūmaste no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||16||

(11,6[8].17 ) ṛtūn brūma ṛtupatīn ārtavān uta hāyanān |
(11,6[8].17 ) samāḥ saṃvatsarān māsāṃste no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||17||

(11,6[8].18 ) eta devā dakṣiṇataḥ paścātprāñca udeta |
(11,6[8].18 ) purastāduttarācchakrā viśve devāḥ sametya te no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||18||

(11,6[8].19 ) viśvān devān idaṃ brūmaḥ satyasandhān ṛtāvṛdhaḥ |
(11,6[8].19 ) viśvābhiḥ patnībhiḥ saha te no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||19||

(11,6[8].20 ) sarvān devān idaṃ brūmaḥ satyasandhān ṛtāvṛdhaḥ |
(11,6[8].20 ) sarvābhiḥ patnībhiḥ saha te no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||20||

(11,6[8].21 ) bhūtaṃ brūmo bhūtapatiṃ bhūtānāmuta yo vaśī |
(11,6[8].21 ) bhūtāni sarvā saṃgatya te no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||21||

(11,6[8].22 ) yā devīḥ pañca pradiśo ye devā dvādaśa ṛtavaḥ |
(11,6[8].22 ) saṃvatsarasya ye daṃṣṭrāste naḥ santu sadā śivāḥ ||22||

(11,6[8].23 ) yan mātalī rathakrītamamṛtaṃ veda bheṣajam |
(11,6[8].23 ) tadindro apsu prāveśayattadāpo datta bheṣajam ||23|| {18}



(11,7[9].1 ) ucchiṣṭe nāma rūpaṃ cocchiṣṭe loka āhitaḥ |
(11,7[9].1 ) ucchiṣṭa indraścāgniśca viśvamantaḥ samāhitam ||1||

(11,7[9].2 ) ucchiṣṭe dyāvāpṛthivī viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ samāhitam |
(11,7[9].2 ) āpaḥ samudra ucchiṣṭe candramā vāta āhitaḥ ||2||

(11,7[9].3 ) sann ucchiṣṭe asaṃścobhau mṛtyurvājaḥ prajāpatiḥ |
(11,7[9].3 ) laukyā ucchiṣṭa āyattā vraśca draścāpi śrīrmayi ||3||

(11,7[9].4 ) dṛḍho dṛṃha sthiro nyo brahma viśvasṛjo daśa |
(11,7[9].4 ) nābhimiva sarvataścakramucchiṣṭe devatāḥ śritāḥ ||4||

(11,7[9].5 ) ṛksāma yajurucchiṣṭa udgīthaḥ prastutaṃ stutam |
(11,7[9].5 ) hiṅkāra ucchiṣṭe svaraḥ sāmno meḍiśca tan mayi ||5||

(11,7[9].6 ) aindrāgnaṃ pāvamānaṃ mahānāmnīrmahāvratam |
(11,7[9].6 ) ucchiṣṭe yajñasyāṅgānyantargarbha iva mātari ||6||

(11,7[9].7 ) rājasūyaṃ vājapeyamagniṣṭomaṣtadadhvaraḥ |
(11,7[9].7 ) arkāśvamedhāvucchiṣṭe jīvabarhirmadintamaḥ ||7||

(11,7[9].8 ) agnyādheyamatho dīkṣā kāmapraśchandasā saha |
(11,7[9].8 ) utsannā yajñāḥ satrāṇyucchiṣṭe'dhi samāhitāḥ ||8||

(11,7[9].9 ) agnihotraṃ ca śraddhā ca vaṣaṭkāro vrataṃ tapaḥ |
(11,7[9].9 ) dakṣiṇeṣṭaṃ pūrtaṃ cocchiṣṭe'dhi samāhitāḥ ||9||

(11,7[9].10 ) ekarātro dvirātraḥ sadyaḥkrīḥ prakrīrukthyaḥ |
(11,7[9].10 ) otaṃ nihitamucchiṣṭe yajñasyāṇūni vidyayā ||10|| {19}

(11,7[9].11 ) catūrātraḥ pañcarātraḥ ṣaḍrātraścobhayaḥ saha |
(11,7[9].11 ) ṣoḍaśī saptarātraścocchiṣṭājjajñire sarve ye yajñā amṛte hitāḥ ||11||

(11,7[9].12 ) pratīhāro nidhanaṃ viśvajiccābhijicca yaḥ |
(11,7[9].12 ) sāhnātirātrāvucchiṣṭe dvādaśāho'pi tan mayi ||12||

(11,7[9].13 ) sūnṛtā saṃnatiḥ kṣemaḥ svadhorjāmṛtaṃ sahaḥ |
(11,7[9].13 ) ucchiṣṭe sarve pratyañcaḥ kāmāḥ kāmena tātṛpuḥ ||13||

(11,7[9].14 ) nava bhūmīḥ samudrā ucchiṣṭe'dhi śritā divaḥ |
(11,7[9].14 ) ā sūryo bhātyucchiṣṭe'horātre api tan mayi ||14||
(11,7[9].15 ) upahavyaṃ viṣūvantaṃ ye ca yajñā guhā hitāḥ |
(11,7[9].15 ) bibharti bhartā viśvasyocchiṣṭo janituḥ pitā ||15||

(11,7[9].16 ) pitā janiturucchiṣṭo'soḥ pautraḥ pitāmahaḥ |
(11,7[9].16 ) sa kṣiyati viśvasyeśāno vṛṣā bhūmyāmatighnyaḥ ||16||

(11,7[9].17 ) ṛtaṃ satyaṃ tapo rāṣṭraṃ śramo dharmaśca karma ca |
(11,7[9].17 ) bhūtaṃ bhaviṣyaducchiṣṭe vīryaṃ lakṣmīrbalaṃ bale ||17||

(11,7[9].18 ) samṛddhiroja ākūtiḥ kṣatraṃ rāṣṭraṃ ṣaḍurvyaḥ |
(11,7[9].18 ) saṃvatsaro'dhyucchiṣṭa iḍā praiṣā grahā haviḥ ||18||

(11,7[9].19 ) caturhotāra āpriyaścāturmāsyāni nīvidaḥ |
(11,7[9].19 ) ucchiṣṭe yajñā hotrāḥ paśubandhāstadiṣṭayaḥ ||19||

(11,7[9].20 ) ardhamāsāśca māsāścārtavā ṛtubhiḥ saha |
(11,7[9].20 ) ucchiṣṭe ghoṣiṇīrāpa stanayitnuḥ śrutirmahī ||20|| {20}

(11,7[9].21 ) śarkarāḥ sikatā aśmāna oṣadhayo vīrudhastṛṇā |
(11,7[9].21 ) abhrāṇi vidyuto varṣamucchiṣṭe saṃśritā śritā ||21||

(11,7[9].22 ) rāddhiḥ prāptiḥ samāptirvyāptirmaha edhatuḥ |
(11,7[9].22 ) atyāptirucchiṣṭe bhūtiścāhitā nihitā hitā ||22||

(11,7[9].23 ) yacca prāṇati prāṇena yacca paśyati cakṣuṣā |
(11,7[9].23 ) ucchiṣṭājjajñire sarve divi devā diviśritaḥ ||23||

(11,7[9].24 ) ṛcaḥ sāmāni chandāṃsi purāṇaṃ yajuṣā saha |
(11,7[9].24 ) ucchiṣṭājjajñire sarve divi devā diviśritaḥ ||24||

(11,7[9].25 ) prāṇāpānau cakṣuḥ śrotramakṣitiśca kṣitiśca yā |
(11,7[9].25 ) ucchiṣṭājjajñire sarve divi devā diviśritaḥ ||25||

(11,7[9].26 ) ānandā modāḥ pramudo'bhīmodamudaśca ye |
(11,7[9].26 ) ucchiṣṭājjajñire sarve divi devā diviśritaḥ ||26||

(11,7[9].27 ) devāḥ pitaro manuṣyā gandharvāpsarasaśca ye |
(11,7[9].27 ) ucchiṣṭājjajñire sarve divi devā diviśritaḥ ||27|| {21}



(11,8[10].1 ) yan manyurjāyāmāvahatsaṃkalpasya gṛhādadhi |
(11,8[10].1 ) ka āsaṃ janyāḥ ke varāḥ ka u jyeṣṭhavaro'bhavat||1||

(11,8[10].2 ) tapaścaivāstāṃ karma cāntarmahatyarṇave |
(11,8[10].2 ) ta āsaṃ janyāste varā brahma jyeṣṭhavaro'bhavat||2||

(11,8[10].3 ) daśa sākamajāyanta devā devebhyaḥ purā |
(11,8[10].3 ) yo vai tān vidyātpratyakṣaṃ sa vā adya mahadvadet||3||

(11,8[10].4 ) prāṇāpānau cakṣuḥ śrotramakṣitiśca kṣitiśca yā |
(11,8[10].4 ) vyānodānau vāṅmanaste vā ākūtimāvahan ||4||

(11,8[10].5 ) ajātā āsann ṛtavo'tho dhātā bṛhaspatiḥ |
(11,8[10].5 ) indrāgnī aśvinā tarhi kaṃ te jyeṣṭhamupāsata ||5||

(11,8[10].6 ) tapaścaivāstāṃ karma cāntarmahatyarṇave |
(11,8[10].6 ) tapo ha jajñe karmaṇastatte jyeṣṭhamupāsata ||6||

(11,8[10].7 ) yeta āsīdbhūmiḥ pūrvā yāmaddhātaya idviduḥ |
(11,8[10].7 ) yo vai tāṃ vidyān nāmathā sa manyeta purāṇavit||7||

(11,8[10].8 ) kuta indraḥ kutaḥ somaḥ kuto agnirajāyata |
(11,8[10].8 ) kutastvaṣṭā samabhavatkuto dhātājāyata ||8||

(11,8[10].9 ) indrādindraḥ somātsomo agneragnirajāyata |
(11,8[10].9 ) tvaṣṭā ha jajñe tvaṣṭurdhāturdhātājāyata ||9||

(11,8[10].10 ) ye ta āsan daśa jātā devā devebhyaḥ purā |
(11,8[10].10 ) putrebhyo lokaṃ dattvā kasmiṃste loka āsate ||10|| {22}

(11,8[10].11 ) yadā keśān asthi snāva māṃsaṃ majjānamābharat|
(11,8[10].11 ) śarīraṃ kṛtvā pādavatkaṃ lokamanu prāviśat||11||

(11,8[10].12 ) kutaḥ keśān kutaḥ snāva kuto asthīnyābharat|
(11,8[10].12 ) aṅgā parvāṇi majjānaṃ ko māṃsaṃ kuta ābharat||12||

(11,8[10].13 ) saṃsico nāma te devā ye saṃbhārāntsamabharan |
(11,8[10].13 ) sarvaṃ saṃsicya martyaṃ devāḥ puruṣamāviśan ||13||

(11,8[10].14 ) ūrū pādāvaṣṭhīvantau śiro hastāvatho mukham |
(11,8[10].14 ) pṛṣṭīrbarjahye pārśve kastatsamadadhādṛṣiḥ ||14||

(11,8[10].15 ) śiro hastāvatho mukhaṃ jihvāṃ grīvāśca kīkasāḥ |
(11,8[10].15 ) tvacā prāvṛtya sarvaṃ tatsaṃdhā samadadhān mahī ||15||

(11,8[10].16 ) yattaccharīramaśayatsaṃdhayā saṃhitaṃ mahat|
(11,8[10].16 ) yenedamadya rocate ko asmin varṇamābharat||16||

(11,8[10].17 ) sarve devā upāśikṣan tadajānādvadhūḥ satī |
(11,8[10].17 ) īśā vaśasya yā jāyā sāsmin varṇamābharat||17||

(11,8[10].18 ) yadā tvaṣṭā vyatṛṇatpitā tvaṣṭurya uttaraḥ |
(11,8[10].18 ) gṛhaṃ kṛtvā martyaṃ devāḥ puruṣamāviśan ||18||

(11,8[10].19 ) svapno vai tandrīrnirṛtiḥ pāpmāno nāma devatāḥ |
(11,8[10].19 ) jarā khālatyaṃ pālityaṃ śarīramanu prāviśan ||19||

(11,8[10].20 ) steyaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ vṛjinaṃ satyaṃ yajño yaśo bṛhat|
(11,8[10].20 ) balaṃ ca kṣatramojaśca śarīramanu prāviśan ||20|| {23}

(11,8[10].21 ) bhūtiśca vā abhūtiśca rātayo'rātayaśca yāḥ |
(11,8[10].21 ) kṣudhaśca sarvāstṛṣṇāśca śarīramanu prāviśan ||21||

(11,8[10].22 ) nindāśca vā anindāśca yacca hanteti neti ca |
(11,8[10].22 ) śarīraṃ śraddhā dakṣiṇāśraddhā cānu prāviśan ||22||

(11,8[10].23 ) vidyāśca vā avidyāśca yaccānyadupadeśyam |
(11,8[10].23 ) śarīraṃ brahma prāviśadṛcaḥ sāmātho yajuḥ ||23||

(11,8[10].24 ) ānandā modāḥ pramudo'bhīmodamudaśca ye |
(11,8[10].24 ) haso nariṣṭā nṛttāni śarīramanu prāviśan ||24||

(11,8[10].25 ) ālāpāśca pralāpāścābhīlāpalapaśca ye |
(11,8[10].25 ) śarīraṃ sarve prāviśann āyujaḥ prayujo yujaḥ ||25||

(11,8[10].26 ) prāṇāpānau cakṣuḥ śrotramakṣitiśca kṣitiśca yā |
(11,8[10].26 ) vyānodānau vāṅmanaḥ śarīreṇa ta īyante ||26||

(11,8[10].27 ) āśiṣaśca praśiṣaśca saṃśiṣo viśiṣaśca yāḥ |
(11,8[10].27 ) cittāni sarve saṃkalpāḥ śarīramanu prāviśan ||27||

(11,8[10].28 ) āsteyīśca vāsteyīśca tvaraṇāḥ kṛpaṇāśca yāḥ |
(11,8[10].28 ) guhyāḥ śukrā sthūlā apastā bībhatsāvasādayan ||28||

(11,8[10].29 ) asthi kṛtvā samidhaṃ tadaṣṭāpo asādayan |
(11,8[10].29 ) retaḥ kṛtvājyaṃ devāḥ puruṣamāviśan ||29||

(11,8[10].30 ) yā āpo yāśca devatā yā virāḍbrahmaṇā saha |
(11,8[10].30 ) śarīraṃ brahma prāviśaccharīre'dhi prajāpatiḥ ||30||

(11,8[10].31 ) sūryaścakṣurvātaḥ prāṇaṃ puruṣasya vi bhejire |
(11,8[10].31 ) athāsyetaramātmānaṃ devāḥ prāyachann agnaye ||31||

(11,8[10].32 ) tasmādvai vidvān puruṣamidaṃ brahmeti manyate |
(11,8[10].32 ) sarvā hyasmin devatā gāvo goṣṭha ivāsate ||32||

(11,8[10].33 ) prathamena pramāreṇa tredhā viṣvaṅvi gachati |
(11,8[10].33 ) ada ekena gachatyada ekena gachatīhaikena ni ṣevate ||33||

(11,8[10].34 ) apsu stīmāsu vṛddhāsu śarīramantarā hitam |
(11,8[10].34 ) tasmiṃ chavo'dhyantarā tasmācchavo'dhyucyate ||34|| {24}



(11,9[11].1 ) ye bāhavo yā iṣavo dhanvanāṃ vīryāṇi ca |
(11,9[11].1 ) asīn paraśūn āyudhaṃ cittākūtaṃ ca yaddhṛdi |
(11,9[11].1 ) sarvaṃ tadarbude tvamamitrebhyo dṛśe kurūdārāṃśca pra darśaya ||1||

(11,9[11].2 ) uttiṣṭhata saṃ nahyadhvaṃ mitrā devajanā yūyam |
(11,9[11].2 ) saṃdṛṣṭā guptā vaḥ santu yā no mitrāṇyarbude ||2||

(11,9[11].3 ) uttiṣṭhatamā rabhetāmādānasaṃdānābhyām |
(11,9[11].3 ) amitrāṇāṃ senā abhi dhattamarbude ||3||

(11,9[11].4 ) arbudirnāma yo deva īśānaśca nyarbudiḥ |
(11,9[11].4 ) yābhyāmantarikṣamāvṛtamiyaṃ ca pṛthivī mahī |
(11,9[11].4 ) tābhyāmindramedibhyāmahaṃ jitamanvemi senayā ||4||

(11,9[11].5 ) uttiṣṭha tvaṃ devajanārbude senayā saha |
(11,9[11].5 ) bhañjann amitrāṇāṃ senāṃ bhogebhiḥ pari vāraya ||5||

(11,9[11].6 ) sapta jātān nyarbuda udārāṇāṃ samīkṣayan |
(11,9[11].6 ) tebhiṣṭvamājye hute sarvairuttiṣṭha senayā ||6||

(11,9[11].7 ) pratighnānāśrumukhī kṛdhukarṇī ca krośatu |
(11,9[11].7 ) vikeśī puruṣe hate radite arbude tava ||7||

(11,9[11].8 ) saṃkarṣantī karūkaraṃ manasā putramichantī |
(11,9[11].8 ) patiṃ bhrātaramātsvān radite arbude tava ||8||

(11,9[11].9 ) aliklavā jāṣkamadā gṛdhrāḥ śyenāḥ patatriṇaḥ |
(11,9[11].9 ) dhvāṅkṣāḥ śakunayastṛpyantvamitreṣu samīkṣayan radite arbude tava ||9||

(11,9[11].10 ) atho sarvaṃ śvāpadaṃ makṣikā tṛpyatu krimiḥ |
(11,9[11].10 ) pauruṣeye'dhi kuṇape radite arbude tava ||10|| {25}

(11,9[11].11 ) ā gṛhṇītaṃ saṃ bṛhataṃ prāṇāpānān nyarbude |
(11,9[11].11 ) nivāśā ghoṣāḥ saṃ yantvamitreṣu samīkṣayan radite arbude tava ||11||

(11,9[11].12 ) udvepaya saṃ vijantāṃ bhiyāmitrāntsaṃ sṛja |
(11,9[11].12 ) urugrāhairbāhvaṅkairvidhyāmitrān nyarbude ||12||

(11,9[11].13 ) muhyantveṣāṃ bāhavaścittākūtaṃ ca yaddhṛdi |
(11,9[11].13 ) maiṣāmuccheṣi kiṃ cana radite arbude tava ||13||

(11,9[11].14 ) pratighnānāḥ saṃ dhāvantūraḥ paṭaurāvāghnānāḥ |
(11,9[11].14 ) aghāriṇīrvikeśyo rudatyaḥ puruṣe hate radite arbude tava ||14||

(11,9[11].15 ) śvanvatīrapsaraso rūpakā utārbude |
(11,9[11].15 ) antaḥpātre rerihatīṃ riśāṃ durṇihitaiṣiṇīm |
(11,9[11].15 ) sarvāstā arbude tvamamitrebhyo dṛśe kurūdārāṃśca pra darśaya ||15||

(11,9[11].16 ) khaḍūre'dhicaṅkramāṃ kharvikāṃ kharvavāsinīm |
(11,9[11].16 ) ya udārā antarhitā gandharvāpsarasaśca ye |
(11,9[11].16 ) sarpā itarajanā rakṣāṃsi ||16||

(11,9[11].17 ) caturdaṃṣṭrāṃ chyāvadataḥ kumbhamuṣkāmasṛṅmukhān |
(11,9[11].17 ) svabhyasā ye codbhyasāḥ ||17||

(11,9[11].18 ) udvepaya tvamarbude'mitrāṇāmamūḥ sicaḥ |
(11,9[11].18 ) jayaṃśca jiṣṇuścāmitrāṃ jayatāmindramedinau ||18||

(11,9[11].19 ) prablīno mṛditaḥ śayāṃ hato'mitro nyarbude |
(11,9[11].19 ) agnijihvā dhūmaśikhā jayantīryantu senayā ||19||

(11,9[11].20 ) tayārbude praṇuttānāmindro hantu varaṃvaram |
(11,9[11].20 ) amitrāṇāṃ śacīpatirmāmīṣāṃ moci kaścana ||20|| {26}

(11,9[11].21 ) utkasantu hṛdayānyūrdhvaḥ prāṇa udīṣatu |
(11,9[11].21 ) śauṣkāsyamanu vartatāmamitrān mota mitriṇaḥ ||21||

(11,9[11].22 ) ye ca dhīrā ye cādhīrāḥ parāñco badhirāśca ye |
(11,9[11].22 ) tamasā ye ca tūparā atho bastābhivāsinaḥ |
(11,9[11].22 ) sarvāṃstāmarbude tvamamitrebhyo dṛśe kurūdārāṃśca pra darśaya ||22||

(11,9[11].23 ) arbudiśca triṣandhiścāmitrān no vi vidhyatām |
(11,9[11].23 ) yathaiṣāmindra vṛtrahan hanāma śacīpate'mitrāṇāṃ sahasraśaḥ ||23||

(11,9[11].24 ) vanaspatīn vānaspatyān oṣadhīruta vīrudhaḥ |
(11,9[11].24 ) gandharvāpsarasaḥ sarpān devān puṇyajanān pitṝn |
(11,9[11].24 ) sarvāṃstāmarbude tvamamitrebhyo dṛśe kurūdārāṃśca pra darśaya ||24||

(11,9[11].25 ) īśāṃ vo maruto deva ādityo brahmaṇaspatiḥ |
(11,9[11].25 ) īśāṃ va indraścāgniśca dhātā mitraḥ prajāpatiḥ |
(11,9[11].25 ) īśāṃ va ṛṣayaścakruramitreṣu samīkṣayan radite arbude tava ||25||

(11,9[11].26 ) teṣāṃ sarveṣāmīśānā uttiṣṭhata saṃ nahyadhvam |
(11,9[11].26 ) mitrā devajanā yūyamimaṃ saṃgrāmaṃ saṃjitya yathālokaṃ vi tiṣṭhadhvam ||26|| {27}



(11,10[12].1 ) uttiṣṭhata saṃ nahyadhvamudārāḥ ketubhiḥ saha |
(11,10[12].1 ) sarpā itarajanā rakṣāṃsyamitrān anu dhāvata ||1||

(11,10[12].2 ) īśāṃ vo veda rājyaṃ triṣandhe aruṇaiḥ ketubhiḥ saha |
(11,10[12].2 ) ye antarikṣe ye divi pṛthivyāṃ ye ca mānavāḥ |
(11,10[12].2 ) triṣandheste cetasi durṇāmāna upāsatām ||2||

(11,10[12].3 ) ayomukhāḥ sūcīmukhā atho vikaṅkatīmukhāḥ |
(11,10[12].3 ) kravyādo vātaraṃhasa ā sajantvamitrān vajreṇa triṣandhinā ||3||

(11,10[12].4 ) antardhehi jātaveda āditya kuṇapaṃ bahu |
(11,10[12].4 ) triṣandheriyaṃ senā suhitāstu me vaśe ||4||

(11,10[12].5 ) uttiṣṭha tvaṃ devajanārbude senayā saha |
(11,10[12].5 ) ayaṃ balirva āhutastriṣandherāhutiḥ priyā ||5||

(11,10[12].6 ) śitipadī saṃ dyatu śaravyeyaṃ catuṣpadī |
(11,10[12].6 ) kṛtye'mitrebhyo bhava triṣandheḥ saha senayā ||6||

(11,10[12].7 ) dhūmākṣī saṃ patatu kṛdhukarṇī ca krośatu |
(11,10[12].7 ) triṣandheḥ senayā jite aruṇāḥ santu ketavaḥ ||7||

(11,10[12].8 ) avāyantāṃ pakṣiṇo ye vayāṃsyantarikṣe divi ye caranti |
(11,10[12].8 ) śvāpado makṣikāḥ saṃ rabhantāmāmādo gṛdhrāḥ kuṇape radantām ||8||

(11,10[12].9 ) yāmindreṇa saṃdhāṃ samadhatthā brahmaṇā ca bṛhaspate |
(11,10[12].9 ) tayāhamindrasaṃdhayā sarvān devān iha huva ito jayata māmutaḥ ||9||

(11,10[12].10 ) bṛhaspatirāṅgirasa ṛṣayo brahmasaṃśitāḥ |
(11,10[12].10 ) asurakṣayaṇaṃ vadhaṃ triṣandhiṃ divyāśrayan ||10|| {28}

(11,10[12].11 ) yenāsau gupta āditya ubhāvindraśca tiṣṭhataḥ |
(11,10[12].11 ) triṣandhiṃ devā abhajantaujase ca balāya ca ||11||

(11,10[12].12 ) sarvāṃllokāntsamajayan devā āhutyānayā |
(11,10[12].12 ) bṛhaspatirāṅgiraso vajraṃ yamasiñcatāsurakṣayaṇaṃ vadham ||12||

(11,10[12].13 ) bṛhaspatirāṅgiraso vajraṃ yamasiñcatāsurakṣayaṇaṃ vadham |
(11,10[12].13 ) tenāhamamūṃ senāṃ ni limpāmi bṛhaspate'mitrān hanmyojasā ||13||

(11,10[12].14 ) sarve devā atyāyanti ye aśnanti vaṣaṭkṛtam |
(11,10[12].14 ) imāṃ juṣadhvamāhutimito jayata māmutaḥ ||14||

(11,10[12].15 ) sarve devā atyāyantu triṣandherāhutiḥ priyā |
(11,10[12].15 ) saṃdhāṃ mahatīṃ rakṣata yayāgre asurā jitāḥ ||15||

(11,10[12].16 ) vāyuramitrāṇāmiṣvagrāṇyāñcatu |
(11,10[12].16 ) indra eṣāṃ bāhūn prati bhanaktu mā śakan pratidhāmiṣum |
(11,10[12].16 ) āditya eṣāmastraṃ vi nāśayatu candramā yutāmagatasya panthām ||16||

(11,10[12].17 ) yadi preyurdevapurā brahma varmāṇi cakrire |
(11,10[12].17 ) tanūpānaṃ paripāṇaṃ kṛṇvānā yadupocire sarvaṃ tadarasaṃ kṛdhi ||17||

(11,10[12].18 ) kravyādānuvartayan mṛtyunā ca purohitam |
(11,10[12].18 ) triṣandhe prehi senayā jayāmitrān pra padyasva ||18||
(11,10[12].19 ) triṣandhe tamasā tvamamitrān pari vāraya |
(11,10[12].19 ) pṛṣadājyapraṇuttānāṃ māmīṣāṃ moci kaścana ||19||

(11,10[12].20 ) śitipadī saṃ patatvamitrāṇāmamūḥ sicaḥ |
(11,10[12].20 ) muhyantvadyāmūḥ senā amitrāṇāṃ nyarbude ||20|| {29}

(11,10[12].21 ) mūḍhā amitrā nyarbude jahyeṣāṃ varaṃvaram |
(11,10[12].21 ) anayā jahi senayā ||21||

(11,10[12].22 ) yaśca kavacī yaścākavaco'mitro yaścājmani |
(11,10[12].22 ) jyāpāśaiḥ kavacapāśairajmanābhihataḥ śayām ||22||

(11,10[12].23 ) ye varmiṇo ye'varmāṇo amitrā ye ca varmiṇaḥ |
(11,10[12].23 ) sarvāṃstāmarbude hatāṃ chvāno'dantu bhūmyām ||23||

(11,10[12].24 ) ye rathino ye arathā asādā ye ca sādinaḥ |
(11,10[12].24 ) sarvān adantu tān hatān gṛdhrāḥ śyenāḥ patatriṇaḥ ||24||

(11,10[12].25 ) sahasrakuṇapā śetāmāmitrī senā samare vadhānām |
(11,10[12].25 ) vividdhā kakajākṛtā ||25||

(11,10[12].26 ) marmāvidhaṃ roruvataṃ suparṇairadantu duścitaṃ mṛditaṃ śayānam |
(11,10[12].26 ) ya imāṃ pratīcīmāhutimamitro no yuyutsati ||26||

(11,10[12].27 ) yāṃ devā anutiṣṭhanti yasyā nāsti virādhanam |
(11,10[12].27 ) tayendro hantu vṛtrahā vajreṇa triṣandhinā ||27|| {30}


(12,1.1 ) satyaṃ bṛhadṛtamugraṃ dīkṣā tapo brahma yajñaḥ pṛthivīṃ dhārayanti |
(12,1.1 ) sā no bhūtasya bhavyasya patnyuruṃ lokaṃ pṛthivī naḥ kṛṇotu ||1||

(12,1.2 ) asaṃbādhaṃ madhyato mānavānāṃ yasyā udvataḥ pravataḥ samaṃ bahu |
(12,1.2 ) nānāvīryā oṣadhīryā bibharti pṛthivī naḥ prathatāṃ rādhyatāṃ naḥ ||2||

(12,1.3 ) yasyāṃ samudra uta sindhurāpo yasyāmannaṃ kṛṣṭayaḥ saṃbabhūvuḥ |
(12,1.3 ) yasyāmidaṃ jinvati prāṇadejatsā no bhūmiḥ pūrvapeye dadhātu ||3||

(12,1.4 ) yasyāścatasraḥ pradiśaḥ pṛthivyā yasyāmannaṃ kṛṣṭayaḥ saṃbabhūvuḥ |
(12,1.4 ) yā bibharti bahudhā prāṇadejatsā no bhūmirgoṣvapyanne dadhātu ||4||

(12,1.5 ) yasyāṃ pūrve pūrvajanā vicakrire yasyāṃ devā asurān abhyavartayan |
(12,1.5 ) gavāmaśvānāṃ vayasaśca viṣṭhā bhagaṃ varcaḥ pṛthivī no dadhātu ||5||

(12,1.6 ) viśvaṃbharā vasudhānī pratiṣṭhā hiraṇyavakṣā jagato niveśanī |
(12,1.6 ) vaiśvānaraṃ bibhratī bhūmiragnimindraṛṣabhā draviṇe no dadhātu ||6||

(12,1.7 ) yāṃ rakṣantyasvapnā viśvadānīṃ devā bhūmiṃ pṛthivīmapramādam |
(12,1.7 ) sā no madhu priyaṃ duhāmatho ukṣatu varcasā ||7||

(12,1.8 ) yārṇave'dhi salilamagra āsītyāṃ māyābhiranvacaran manīṣiṇaḥ |
(12,1.8 ) yasyā hṛdayaṃ parame vyomantsatyenāvṛtamamṛtaṃ pṛthivyāḥ |
(12,1.8 ) sā no bhūmistviṣiṃ balaṃ rāṣṭre dadhātūttame ||8||

(12,1.9 ) yasyāmāpaḥ paricarāḥ samānīrahorātre apramādaṃ kṣaranti |
(12,1.9 ) sā no bhūmirbhūridhārā payo duhāmatho ukṣatu varcasā ||9||

(12,1.10 ) yāmaśvināvamimātāṃ viṣṇuryasyāṃ vicakrame |
(12,1.10 ) indro yāṃ cakra ātmane'namitrāṃ śacīpatiḥ |
(12,1.10 ) sā no bhūmirvi sṛjatāṃ mātā putrāya me payaḥ ||10|| {1}

(12,1.11 ) girayaste parvatā himavanto'raṇyaṃ te pṛthivi syonamastu |
(12,1.11 ) babhruṃ kṛṣṇāṃ rohiṇīṃ viśvarūpāṃ dhruvāṃ bhūmiṃ pṛthivīmindraguptām |
(12,1.11 ) ajīto'hato akṣato'dhyaṣṭhāṃ pṛthivīmaham ||11||

(12,1.12 ) yatte madhyaṃ pṛthivi yacca nabhyaṃ yāsta ūrjastanvaḥ saṃbabhūvuḥ |
(12,1.12 ) tāsu no dhehyabhi naḥ pavasva mātā bhūmiḥ putro ahaṃ pṛthivyāḥ parjanyaḥ pitā sa u naḥ pipartu ||12||

(12,1.13 ) yasyāṃ vediṃ parigṛhṇanti bhūmyāṃ yasyāṃ yajñaṃ tanvate viśvakarmāṇaḥ |
(12,1.13 ) yasyāṃ mīyante svaravaḥ pṛthivyāmūrdhvāḥ śukrā āhutyāḥ purastāt|
(12,1.13 ) sā no bhūmirvardhayadvardhamānā ||13||

(12,1.14 ) yo no dveṣatpṛthivi yaḥ pṛtanyādyo'bhidāsān manasā yo vadhena |
(12,1.14 ) taṃ no bhūme randhaya pūrvakṛtvari ||14||

(12,1.15 ) tvajjātāstvayi caranti martyāstvaṃ bibharṣi dvipadastvaṃ catuṣpadaḥ |
(12,1.15 ) taveme pṛthivi pañca mānavā yebhyo jyotiramṛtaṃ martyebhya udyantsūryo raśmibhirātanoti ||15||

(12,1.16 ) tā naḥ prajāḥ saṃ duhratāṃ samagrā vāco madhu pṛthivi dhehi mahyam ||16||

(12,1.17 ) viśvasvaṃ mātaramoṣadhīnāṃ dhruvāṃ bhūmiṃ pṛthivīṃ dharmaṇā dhṛtām |
(12,1.17 ) śivāṃ syonāmanu carema viśvahā ||17||

(12,1.18 ) mahatsadhasthaṃ mahatī babhūvitha mahān vega ejathurvepathuṣṭe |
(12,1.18 ) mahāṃstvendro rakṣatyapramādam |
(12,1.18 ) sā no bhūme pra rocaya hiraṇyasyeva saṃdṛśi mā no dvikṣata kaścana ||18||

(12,1.19 ) agnirbhūmyāmoṣadhīṣvagnimāpo bibhratyagniraśmasu |
(12,1.19 ) agnirantaḥ puruṣeṣu goṣvaśveṣvagnayaḥ ||19||

(12,1.20 ) agnirdiva ā tapatyagnerdevasyorvantarikṣam |
(12,1.20 ) agniṃ martāsa indhate havyavāhaṃ ghṛtapriyam ||20|| {2}

(12,1.21 ) agnivāsāḥ pṛthivyasitajñūstviṣīmantaṃ saṃśitaṃ mā kṛṇotu ||21||

(12,1.22 ) bhūmyāṃ devebhyo dadati yajñaṃ havyamaraṃkṛtam |
(12,1.22 ) bhūmyāṃ manuṣyā jīvanti svadhayānnena martyāḥ |
(12,1.22 ) sā no bhūmiḥ prāṇamāyurdadhātu jaradaṣṭiṃ mā pṛthivī kṛṇotu ||22||

(12,1.23 ) yaste gandhaḥ pṛthivi saṃbabhūva yaṃ bibhratyoṣadhayo yamāpaḥ |
(12,1.23 ) yaṃ gandharvā apsarasaśca bhejire tena mā surabhiṃ kṛṇu mā no dvikṣata kaścana ||23||

(12,1.24 ) yaste gandhaḥ puṣkaramāviveśa yaṃ saṃjabhruḥ sūryāyā vivāhe |
(12,1.24 ) amartyāḥ pṛthivi gandhamagre tena mā surabhiṃ kṛṇu mā no dvikṣata kaścana ||24||

(12,1.25 ) yaste gandhaḥ puruṣeṣu strīṣu puṃsu bhago ruciḥ |
(12,1.25 ) yo aśveṣu vīreṣu yo mṛgeṣūta hastiṣu |
(12,1.25 ) kanyāyāṃ varco yadbhūme tenāsmāmapi saṃ sṛja mā no dvikṣata kaścana ||25||

(12,1.26 ) śilā bhūmiraśmā pāṃsuḥ sā bhūmiḥ saṃdhṛtā dhṛtā |
(12,1.26 ) tasyai hiraṇyavakṣase pṛthivyā akaraṃ namaḥ ||26||

(12,1.27 ) yasyāṃ vṛkṣā vānaspatyā dhruvāstiṣṭhanti viśvahā |
(12,1.27 ) pṛthivīṃ viśvadhāyasaṃ dhṛtāmachāvadāmasi ||27||

(12,1.28 ) udīrāṇā utāsīnāstiṣṭhantaḥ prakrāmantaḥ |
(12,1.28 ) padbhyāṃ dakṣiṇasavyābhyāṃ mā vyathiṣmahi bhūmyām ||28||

(12,1.29 ) vimṛgvarīṃ pṛthivīmā vadāmi kṣamāṃ bhūmiṃ brahmaṇā vāvṛdhānām |
(12,1.29 ) ūrjaṃ puṣṭaṃ bibhratīmannabhāgaṃ ghṛtaṃ tvābhi ni ṣīdema bhūme ||29||

(12,1.30 ) śuddhā na āpastanve kṣarantu yo naḥ sedurapriye taṃ ni dadhmaḥ |
(12,1.30 ) pavitreṇa pṛthivi motpunāmi ||30|| {3}

(12,1.31 ) yāste prācīḥ pradiśo yā udīcīryāste bhūme adharādyāśca paścāt|
(12,1.31 ) syonāstā mahyaṃ carate bhavantu mā ni paptaṃ bhuvane śiśriyāṇaḥ ||31||

(12,1.32 ) mā naḥ paścān mā purastān nudiṣṭhā mottarādadharāduta |
(12,1.32 ) svasti bhūme no bhava mā vidan paripanthino varīyo yāvayā vadham ||32||

(12,1.33 ) yāvatte'bhi vipaśyāmi bhūme sūryeṇa medinā |
(12,1.33 ) tāvan me cakṣurmā meṣṭottarāmuttarāṃ samām ||33||

(12,1.34 ) yacchayānaḥ paryāvarte dakṣiṇaṃ sakhyamabhi bhūme pārśvamuttānāstvā pratīcīṃ yatpṛṣṭībhiradhiśemahe |
(12,1.34 ) mā hiṃsīstatra no bhūme sarvasya pratiśīvari ||34||

(12,1.35 ) yatte bhūme vikhanāmi kṣipraṃ tadapi rohatu |
(12,1.35 ) mā te marma vimṛgvari mā te hṛdayamarpipam ||35||

(12,1.36 ) grīṣmaste bhūme varṣāṇi śaraddhemantaḥ śiśiro vasantaḥ |
(12,1.36 ) ṛtavaste vihitā hāyanīrahorātre pṛthivi no duhātām ||36||

(12,1.37 ) yāpa sarpaṃ vijamānā vimṛgvarī yasyāmāsann agnayo ye apsvantaḥ |
(12,1.37 ) parā dasyūn dadatī devapīyūn indraṃ vṛṇānā pṛthivī na vṛtraṃ śakrāya dadhre vṛṣabhāya vṛṣṇe ||37||

(12,1.38 ) yasyāṃ sadohavirdhāne yūpo yasyāṃ nimīyate |
(12,1.38 ) brahmāṇo yasyāmarcantyṛgbhiḥ sāmnā yajurvidaḥ yujyante yasyāmṛtvijaḥ somamindrāya pātave ||38||

(12,1.39 ) yasyāṃ pūrve bhūtakṛta ṛṣayo gā udānṛcuḥ |
(12,1.39 ) sapta satreṇa vedhaso yajñena tapasā saha ||39||

(12,1.40 ) sā no bhūmirā diśatu yaddhanaṃ kāmayāmahe |
(12,1.40 ) bhago anuprayuṅktāmindra etu purogavaḥ ||40|| {4}

(12,1.41 ) yasyāṃ gāyanti nṛtyanti bhūmyāṃ martyā vyailabāḥ |
(12,1.41 ) yudhyante yasyāmākrando yasyāṃ vadati dundubhiḥ |
(12,1.41 ) sā no bhūmiḥ pra ṇudatāṃ sapatnān asapatnaṃ mā pṛthivī kṛṇotu ||41||

(12,1.42 ) yasyāmannaṃ vrīhiyavau yasyā imāḥ pañca kṛṣṭayaḥ |
(12,1.42 ) bhūmyai parjanyapatnyai namo'stu varṣamedase ||42||

(12,1.43 ) yasyāḥ puro devakṛtāḥ kṣetre yasyā vikurvate |
(12,1.43 ) prajāpatiḥ pṛthivīṃ viśvagarbhāmāśāmāśāṃ raṇyāṃ naḥ kṛṇotu ||43||

(12,1.44 ) nidhiṃ bibhratī bahudhā guhā vasu maṇiṃ hiraṇyaṃ pṛthivī dadātu me |
(12,1.44 ) vasūni no vasudā rāsamānā devī dadhātu sumanasyamānā ||44||

(12,1.45 ) janaṃ bibhratī bahudhā vivācasaṃ nānādharmāṇaṃ pṛthivī yathaukasam |
(12,1.45 ) sahasraṃ dhārā draviṇasya me duhāṃ dhruveva dhenuranapasphurantī ||45||

(12,1.46 ) yaste sarpo vṛścikastṛṣṭadaṃśmā hemantajabdho bhṛmalo guhā śaye |
(12,1.46 ) krimirjinvatpṛthivi yadyadejati prāvṛṣi tan naḥ sarpan mopa sṛpadyacchivaṃ tena no mṛḍa ||46||

(12,1.47 ) ye te panthāno bahavo janāyanā rathasya vartmānasaśca yātave |
(12,1.47 ) yaiḥ saṃcarantyubhaye bhadrapāpāstaṃ panthānaṃ jayemānamitramataskaraṃ yacchivaṃ tena no mṛḍa ||47||

(12,1.48 ) malvaṃ bibhratī gurubhṛdbhadrapāpasya nidhanaṃ titikṣuḥ |
(12,1.48 ) varāheṇa pṛthivī saṃvidānā sūkarāya vi jihīte mṛgāya ||48||

(12,1.49 ) ye ta āraṇyāḥ paśavo mṛgā vane hitāḥ siṃhā vyāghrāḥ puruṣādaścaranti |
(12,1.49 ) ulaṃ vṛkaṃ pṛthivi duchunāmita ṛkṣīkāṃ rakṣo apa bādhayāsmat||49||

(12,1.50 ) ye gandharvā apsaraso ye cārāyāḥ kimīdinaḥ |
(12,1.50 ) piśācāntsarvā rakṣāṃsi tān asmadbhūme yāvaya ||50|| {5}

(12,1.51 ) yāṃ dvipādaḥ pakṣiṇaḥ saṃpatanti haṃsāḥ suparṇāḥ śakunā vayāṃsi |
(12,1.51 ) yasyāṃ vāto mātariśveyate rajāṃsi kṛṇvaṃścyāvayaṃśca vṛkṣān |
(12,1.51 ) vātasya pravāmupavāmanu vātyarciḥ ||51||

(12,1.52 ) yasyāṃ kṛṣṇamaruṇaṃ ca saṃhite ahorātre vihite bhūmyāmadhi |
(12,1.52 ) varṣeṇa bhūmiḥ pṛthivī vṛtāvṛtā sā no dadhātu bhadrayā priye dhāmanidhāmani ||52||

(12,1.53 ) dyauśca ma idaṃ pṛthivī cāntarikṣaṃ ca me vyacaḥ |
(12,1.53 ) agniḥ sūrya āpo medhāṃ viśve devāśca saṃ daduḥ ||53||

(12,1.54 ) ahamasmi sahamāna uttaro nāma bhūmyām |
(12,1.54 ) abhīṣāḍasmi viśvāṣāḍāśāmāśāṃ viṣāsahiḥ ||54||

(12,1.55 ) ado yaddevi prathamānā purastāddevairuktā vyasarpo mahitvam |
(12,1.55 ) ā tvā subhūtamaviśattadānīmakalpayathāḥ pradiśaścatasraḥ ||55||

(12,1.56 ) ye grāmā yadaraṇyaṃ yāḥ sabhā adhi bhūmyām |
(12,1.56 ) ye saṃgrāmāḥ samitayasteṣu cāru vadema te ||56||

(12,1.57 ) aśva iva rajo dudhuve vi tān janān ya ākṣiyan pṛthivīṃ yādajāyata |
(12,1.57 ) mandrāgretvarī bhuvanasya gopā vanaspatīnāṃ gṛbhiroṣadhīnām ||57||

(12,1.58 ) yadvadāmi madhumattadvadāmi yadīkṣe tadvananti mā |
(12,1.58 ) tviṣīmān asmi jūtimān avānyān hanmi dodhataḥ ||58||

(12,1.59 ) śantivā surabhiḥ syonā kīlālodhnī payasvatī |
(12,1.59 ) bhūmiradhi bravītu me pṛthivī payasā saha ||59||

(12,1.60 ) yāmanvaichaddhaviṣā viśvakarmāntararṇave rajasi praviṣṭām |
(12,1.60 ) bhujiṣyaṃ pātraṃ nihitaṃ guhā yadāvirbhoge abhavan mātṛmadbhyaḥ ||60||

(12,1.61 ) tvamasyāvapanī janānāmaditiḥ kāmadughā paprathānā |
(12,1.61 ) yatta ūnaṃ tatta ā pūrayāti prajāpatiḥ prathamajā ṛtasya ||61||

(12,1.62 ) upasthāste anamīvā ayakṣmā asmabhyaṃ santu pṛthivi prasūtāḥ |
(12,1.62 ) dīrghaṃ na āyuḥ pratibudhyamānā vayaṃ tubhyaṃ balihṛtaḥ syāma ||62||

(12,1.63 ) bhūme mātarni dhehi mā bhadrayā supratiṣṭhitam |
(12,1.63 ) saṃvidānā divā kave śriyāṃ mā dhehi bhūtyām ||63|| {6}



(12,2.1 ) naḍamā roha na te atra loka idaṃ sīsaṃ bhāgadheyaṃ ta ehi |
(12,2.1 ) yo goṣu yakṣmaḥ puruṣeṣu yakṣmastena tvaṃ sākamadharāṅparehi ||1||

(12,2.2 ) aghaśaṃsaduḥśaṃsābhyāṃ kareṇānukareṇa ca |
(12,2.2 ) yakṣmaṃ ca sarvaṃ teneto mṛtyuṃ ca nirajāmasi ||2||

(12,2.3 ) nirito mṛtyuṃ nirṛtiṃ nirarātimajāmasi |
(12,2.3 ) yo no dveṣṭi tamaddhyagne akravyādyamu dviṣmastamu te pra suvāmasi ||3||

(12,2.4 ) yadyagniḥ kravyādyadi vā vyāghra imaṃ goṣṭhaṃ praviveśānyokāḥ |
(12,2.4 ) taṃ māṣājyaṃ kṛtvā pra hiṇomi dūraṃ sa gachatvapsuṣado'pyagnīn ||4||

(12,2.5 ) yattvā kruddhāḥ pracakrurmanyunā puruṣe mṛte |
(12,2.5 ) sukalpamagne tattvayā punastvoddīpayāmasi ||5||

(12,2.6 ) punastvādityā rudrā vasavaḥ punarbrahmā vasunītiragne |
(12,2.6 ) punastvā brahmaṇaspatirādhāddīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya ||6||
(12,2.7 ) yo agniḥ kravyātpraviveśa no gṛhamimaṃ paśyann itaraṃ jātavedasam |
(12,2.7 ) taṃ harāmi pitṛyajñāya dūraṃ sa gharmamindhāṃ parame sadhasthe ||7||

(12,2.8 ) kravyādamagniṃ pra hiṇomi dūraṃ yamarājño gachatu ripravāhaḥ |
(12,2.8 ) ihāyamitaro jātavedā devo devebhyo havyaṃ vahatu prajānan ||8||

(12,2.9 ) kravyādamagnimiṣito harāmi janān dṛṃhantaṃ vajreṇa mṛtyum |
(12,2.9 ) ni taṃ śāsmi gārhapatyena vidvān pitṝṇāṃ loke'pi bhāgo astu ||9||

(12,2.10 ) kravyādamagniṃ śaśamānamukthyaṃ pra hiṇomi pathibhiḥ pitṛyāṇaiḥ |
(12,2.10 ) mā devayānaiḥ punarā gā atraivaidhi pitṛṣu jāgṛhi tvam ||10|| {7}

(12,2.11 ) samindhate saṃkasukaṃ svastaye śuddhā bhavantaḥ śucayaḥ pāvakāḥ |
(12,2.11 ) jahāti ripramatyena eti samiddho agniḥ supunā punāti ||11||

(12,2.12 ) devo agniḥ saṃkasuko divaspṛṣṭhānyāruhat|
(12,2.12 ) mucyamāno nireṇaso'mogasmāmaśastyāḥ ||12||

(12,2.13 ) asmin vayaṃ saṃkasuke agnau riprāṇi mṛjmahe |
(12,2.13 ) abhūma yajñiyāḥ śuddhāḥ pra ṇa āyūṃṣi tāriṣat||13||

(12,2.14 ) saṃkasuko vikasuko nirṛtho yaśca nisvaraḥ |
(12,2.14 ) te te yakṣmaṃ savedaso dūrāddūramanīnaśan ||14||

(12,2.15 ) yo no aśveṣu vīreṣu yo no goṣvajāviṣu |
(12,2.15 ) kravyādaṃ nirṇudāmasi yo agnirjanayopanaḥ ||15||

(12,2.16 ) anyebhyastvā puruṣebhyo gobhyo aśvebhyastvā |
(12,2.16 ) niḥ kravyādaṃ nudāmasi yo agnirjīvitayopanaḥ ||16||

(12,2.17 ) yasmin devā amṛjata yasmin manuṣyā uta |
(12,2.17 ) tasmin ghṛtastāvo mṛṣṭvā tvamagne divaṃ ruha ||17||

(12,2.18 ) samiddho agna āhuta sa no mābhyapakramīḥ |
(12,2.18 ) atraiva dīdihi dyavi jyokca sūryaṃ dṛśe ||18||

(12,2.19 ) sīse mṛḍḍhvaṃ naḍe mṛḍḍhvamagnau saṃkasuke ca yat|
(12,2.19 ) atho avyāṃ rāmāyāṃ śīrṣaktimupabarhaṇe ||19||

(12,2.20 ) sīse malaṃ sādayitvā śīrṣaktimupabarhaṇe |
(12,2.20 ) avyāmasiknyāṃ mṛṣṭvā śuddhā bhavata yajñiyāḥ ||20|| {8}

(12,2.21 ) paraṃ mṛtyo anu parehi panthāṃ yasta eṣa itaro devayānāt|
(12,2.21 ) cakṣuṣmate śṛṇvate te bravīmīheme vīrā bahavo bhavantu ||21||

(12,2.22 ) ime jīvā vi mṛtairāvavṛtrann abhūdbhadrā devahutirno adya |
(12,2.22 ) prāñco agāma nṛtaye hasāya suvīrāso vidathamā vadema ||22||

(12,2.23 ) imaṃ jīvebhyaḥ paridhiṃ dadhāmi maiṣāṃ nu gādaparo arthametam |
(12,2.23 ) śataṃ jīvantaḥ śaradaḥ purūcīstiro mṛtyuṃ dadhatāṃ parvatena ||23||
(12,2.24 ) ā rohatāyurjarasaṃ vṛṇānā anupūrvaṃ yatamānā yati stha |
(12,2.24 ) tān vastvaṣṭā sujanimā sajoṣāḥ sarvamāyurnayatu jīvanāya ||24||

(12,2.25 ) yathāhānyanupūrvaṃ bhavanti yatha rtava ṛtubhiryanti sākam |
(12,2.25 ) yathā na pūrvamaparo jahātyevā dhātarāyūṃṣi kalpayaiṣām ||25||

(12,2.26 ) aśmanvatī rīyate saṃ rabhadhvaṃ vīrayadhvaṃ pra taratā sakhāyaḥ |
(12,2.26 ) atrā jahīta ye asan durevā anamīvān uttaremābhi vājān ||26||

(12,2.27 ) uttiṣṭhatā pra taratā sakhāyo'śmanvatī nadī syandata iyam |
(12,2.27 ) atrā jahīta ye asann aśivāḥ śivāntsyonān uttaremābhi vājān ||27||

(12,2.28 ) vaiśvadevīṃ varcasā ā rabhadhvaṃ śuddhā bhavantaḥ śucayaḥ pāvakāḥ |
(12,2.28 ) atikrāmanto duritā padāni śataṃ himāḥ sarvavīrā madema ||28||

(12,2.29 ) udīcīnaiḥ pathibhirvāyumadbhiratikrāmanto'varān parebhiḥ |
(12,2.29 ) triḥ sapta kṛtva ṛṣayaḥ paretā mṛtyuṃ pratyauhan padayopanena ||29||

(12,2.30 ) mṛtyoḥ padaṃ yopayanta eta drāghīya āyuḥ prataraṃ dadhānāḥ |
(12,2.30 ) āsīnā mṛtyuṃ nudatā sadhasthe'tha jīvāso vidathamā vadema ||30|| {9}

(12,2.31 ) imā nārīravidhavāḥ supatnīrāñjanena sarpiṣā saṃ spṛśantām |
(12,2.31 ) anaśravo anamīvāḥ suratnā ā rohantu janayo yonimagre ||31||

(12,2.32 ) vyākaromi haviṣāhametau tau brahmaṇā vyahaṃ kalpayāmi |
(12,2.32 ) svadhāṃ pitṛbhyo ajarāṃ kṛṇomi dīrgheṇāyuṣā samimāntsṛjāmi ||32||

(12,2.33 ) yo no agniḥ pitaro hṛtsvantarāviveśāmṛto martyeṣu |
(12,2.33 ) mayyahaṃ taṃ pari gṛhṇāmi devaṃ mā so asmān dvikṣata mā vayaṃ tam ||33||

(12,2.34 ) apāvṛtya gārhapatyātkravyādā preta dakṣiṇā |
(12,2.34 ) priyaṃ pitṛbhya ātmane brahmabhyaḥ kṛṇutā priyam ||34||

(12,2.35 ) dvibhāgadhanamādāya pra kṣiṇātyavartyā |
(12,2.35 ) agniḥ putrasya jyeṣṭhasya yaḥ kravyādanirāhitaḥ ||35||

(12,2.36 ) yatkṛṣate yadvanute yacca vasnena vindate |
(12,2.36 ) sarvaṃ martyasya tan nāsti kravyāccedanirāhitaḥ ||36||

(12,2.37 ) ayajñiyo hatavarcā bhavati nainena havirattave |
(12,2.37 ) chinatti kṛṣyā gordhanādyaṃ kravyādanuvartate ||37||

(12,2.38 ) muhurgṛdhyaiḥ pra vadatyārtiṃ martyo nītya |
(12,2.38 ) kravyādyān agnirantikādanuvidvān vitāvati ||38||

(12,2.39 ) grāhyā gṛhāḥ saṃ sṛjyante striyā yan mriyate patiḥ |
(12,2.39 ) brahmaiva vidvān eṣyo yaḥ kravyādaṃ nirādadhat||39||
(12,2.40 ) yadripraṃ śamalaṃ cakṛma yacca duṣkṛtam |
(12,2.40 ) āpo mā tasmācchumbhantvagneḥ saṃkasukācca yat||40|| {10}

(12,2.41 ) tā adharādudīcīrāvavṛtran prajānaitīḥ pathibhirdevayānaiḥ |
(12,2.41 ) parvatasya vṛṣabhasyādhi pṛṣṭhe navāścaranti saritaḥ purāṇīḥ ||41||

(12,2.42 ) agne akravyān niḥ kravyādaṃ nudā devayajanaṃ vaha ||42||

(12,2.43 ) imaṃ kravyādā viveśāyaṃ kravyādamanvagāt|
(12,2.43 ) vyāghrau kṛtvā nānānaṃ taṃ harāmi śivāparam ||43||

(12,2.44 ) antardhirdevānāṃ paridhirmanuṣyāṇāmagnirgārhapatya ubhayān antarā śritaḥ ||44||

(12,2.45 ) jīvānāmāyuḥ pra tira tvamagne pitṝṇāṃ lokamapi gachantu ye mṛtāḥ |
(12,2.45 ) sugārhapatyo vitapann arātimuṣāmuṣāṃ śreyasīṃ dhehyasmai ||45||

(12,2.46 ) sarvān agne sahamānaḥ sapatnān aiṣāmūrjaṃ rayimasmāsu dhehi ||46||

(12,2.47 ) imamindraṃ vahniṃ paprimanvārabhadhvaṃ sa vo nirvakṣadduritādavadyāt|
(12,2.47 ) tenāpa hata śarumāpatantaṃ tena rudrasya pari pātāstām ||47||

(12,2.48 ) anaḍvāhaṃ plavamanvārabhadhvaṃ sa vo nirvakṣadduritādavadyāt|
(12,2.48 ) ā rohata saviturnāvametāṃ ṣaḍbhirurvībhiramatiṃ tarema ||48||

(12,2.49 ) ahorātre anveṣi bibhratkṣemyastiṣṭhan prataraṇaḥ suvīraḥ |
(12,2.49 ) anāturāntsumanasastalpa bibhrajjyogeva naḥ puruṣagandhiredhi ||49||

(12,2.50 ) te devebhya ā vṛścante pāpaṃ jīvanti sarvadā |
(12,2.50 ) kravyādyān agnirantikādaśva ivānuvapate naḍam ||50|| {11}

(12,2.51 ) ye'śraddhā dhanakāmyā kravyādā samāsate |
(12,2.51 ) te vā anyeṣāṃ kumbhīṃ paryādadhati sarvadā ||51||

(12,2.52 ) preva pipatiṣati manasā muhurā vartate punaḥ |
(12,2.52 ) kravyādyān agnirantikādanuvidvān vitāvati ||52||

(12,2.53 ) aviḥ kṛṣṇā bhāgadheyaṃ paśūnāṃ sīsaṃ kravyādapi candraṃ ta āhuḥ |
(12,2.53 ) māṣāḥ piṣṭā bhāgadheyaṃ te havyamaraṇyānyā gahvaraṃ sacasva ||53||

(12,2.54 ) iṣīkāṃ jaratīmiṣṭvā tilpiñjaṃ daṇḍanaṃ naḍam |
(12,2.54 ) tamindra idhmaṃ kṛtvā yamasyāgniṃ nirādadhau ||54||

(12,2.55 ) pratyañcamarkaṃ pratyarpayitvā pravidvān panthāṃ vi hyāviveśa |
(12,2.55 ) parāmīṣāmasūn dideśa dīrgheṇāyuṣā samimāntsṛjāmi ||55|| {12}



(12,3.1 ) pumān puṃso'dhi tiṣṭha carmehi tatra hvayasva yatamā priyā te |
(12,3.1 ) yāvantāvagre prathamaṃ sameyathustadvāṃ vayo yamarājye samānam ||1||

(12,3.2 ) tāvadvāṃ cakṣustati vīryāṇi tāvattejastatidhā vājināni |
(12,3.2 ) agniḥ śarīraṃ sacate yadaidho'dhā pakvān mithunā saṃ bhavāthaḥ ||2||

(12,3.3 ) samasmiṃlloke samu devayāne saṃ smā sametaṃ yamarājyeṣu |
(12,3.3 ) pūtau pavitrairupa taddhvayethāṃ yadyadreto adhi vāṃ saṃbabhūva ||3||

(12,3.4 ) āpasputrāso abhi saṃ viśadhvamimaṃ jīvaṃ jīvadhanyāḥ sametya |
(12,3.4 ) tāsāṃ bhajadhvamamṛtaṃ yamāhurodanaṃ pacati vāṃ janitrī ||4||

(12,3.5 ) yaṃ vāṃ pitā pacati yaṃ ca mātā riprān nirmuktyai śamalācca vācaḥ |
(12,3.5 ) sa odanaḥ śatadhāraḥ svarga ubhe vyāpa nabhasī mahitvā ||5||

(12,3.6 ) ubhe nabhasī ubhayāṃśca lokān ye yajvanāmabhijitāḥ svargāḥ |
(12,3.6 ) teṣāṃ jyotiṣmān madhumān yo agre tasmin putrairjarasi saṃ śrayethām ||6||

(12,3.7 ) prācīṃprācīṃ pradiśamā rabhethāmetaṃ lokaṃ śraddadhānāḥ sacante |
(12,3.7 ) yadvāṃ pakvaṃ pariviṣṭamagnau tasya guptaye daṃpatī saṃ śrayethām ||7||

(12,3.8 ) dakṣiṇāṃ diśamabhi nakṣamāṇau paryāvartethāmabhi pātrametat|
(12,3.8 ) tasmin vāṃ yamaḥ pitṛbhiḥ saṃvidānaḥ pakvāya śarma bahulaṃ ni yachāt||8||

(12,3.9 ) pratīcī diśāmiyamidvaraṃ yasyāṃ somo adhipā mṛḍitā ca |
(12,3.9 ) tasyāṃ śrayethāṃ sukṛtaḥ sacethāmadhā pakvān mithunā saṃ bhavāthaḥ ||9||

(12,3.10 ) uttaraṃ rāṣṭraṃ prajayottarāvaddiśāmudīcī kṛṇavan no agram |
(12,3.10 ) pāṅktaṃ chandaḥ puruṣo babhūva viśvairviśvāṅgaiḥ saha saṃ bhavema ||10|| {13}

(12,3.11 ) dhruveyaṃ virāṇnamo astvasyai śivā putrebhya uta mahyamastu |
(12,3.11 ) sā no devyadite viśvavāra irya iva gopā abhi rakṣa pakvam ||11||

(12,3.12 ) piteva putrān abhi saṃ svajasva naḥ śivā no vātā iha vāntu bhūmau |
(12,3.12 ) yamodanaṃ pacato devate iha taṃ nastapa uta satyaṃ ca vettu ||12||

(12,3.13 ) yadyadkṛṣṇaḥ śakuna eha gatvā tsaran viṣaktaṃ bila āsasāda |
(12,3.13 ) yadvā dāsyārdrahastā samaṅkta ulūkhalaṃ musalaṃ śumbhatāpaḥ ||13||

(12,3.14 ) ayaṃ grāvā pṛthubudhno vayodhāḥ pūtaḥ pavitrairapa hantu rakṣaḥ |
(12,3.14 ) ā roha carma mahi śarma yacha mā daṃpatī pautramaghaṃ ni gātām ||14||

(12,3.15 ) vanaspatiḥ saha devairna āgan rakṣaḥ piśācāmapabādhamānaḥ |
(12,3.15 ) sa ucchrayātai pra vadāti vācaṃ tena lokāmabhi sarvān jayema ||15||

(12,3.16 ) sapta medhān paśavaḥ paryagṛhṇan ya eṣāṃ jyotiṣmāmuta yaścakarśa |
(12,3.16 ) trayastriṃśaddevatāstāntsacante sa naḥ svargamabhi neṣa lokam ||16||

(12,3.17 ) svargaṃ lokamabhi no nayāsi saṃ jāyayā saha putraiḥ syāma |
(12,3.17 ) gṛhṇāmi hastamanu maitvatra mā nastārīn nirṛtirmo arātiḥ ||17||

(12,3.18 ) grāhiṃ pāpmānamati tāmayāma tamo vyasya pra vadāsi valgu |
(12,3.18 ) vānaspatya udyato mā jihiṃsīrmā taṇḍulaṃ vi śarīrdevayantam ||18||

(12,3.19 ) viśvavyacā ghṛtapṛṣṭho bhaviṣyantsayonirlokamupa yāhyetam |
(12,3.19 ) varṣavṛddhamupa yacha śūrpaṃ tuṣaṃ palāvān apa tadvinaktu ||19||

(12,3.20 ) trayo lokāḥ saṃmitā brāhmaṇena dyaurevāsau pṛthivyantarikṣam |
(12,3.20 ) aṃśūn gṛbhītvānvārabhethāmā pyāyantāṃ punarā yantu śūrpam ||20|| {14}

(12,3.21 ) pṛthagrūpāṇi bahudhā paśūnāmekarūpo bhavasi saṃ samṛddhyā |
(12,3.21 ) etāṃ tvacaṃ lohinīṃ tāṃ nudasva grāvā śumbhāti malaga iva vastrā ||21||

(12,3.22 ) pṛthivīṃ tvā pṛthivyāmā veśayāmi tanūḥ samānī vikṛtā ta eṣā |
(12,3.22 ) yadyaddyuttaṃ likhitamarpaṇena tena mā susrorbrahmaṇāpi tadvapāmi ||22||

(12,3.23 ) janitrīva prati haryāsi sūnuṃ saṃ tvā dadhāmi pṛthivīṃ pṛthivyā |
(12,3.23 ) ukhā kumbhī vedyāṃ mā vyathiṣṭhā yajñāyudhairājyenātiṣaktā ||23||

(12,3.24 ) agniḥ pacan rakṣatu tvā purastādindro rakṣatu dakṣiṇato marutvān |
(12,3.24 ) varuṇastvā dṛṃhāddharuṇe pratīcyā uttarāttvā somaḥ saṃ dadātai ||24||

(12,3.25 ) pūtāḥ pavitraiḥ pavante abhrāddivaṃ ca yanti pṛthivīṃ ca lokān |
(12,3.25 ) tā jīvalā jīvadhanyāḥ pratiṣṭhāḥ pātra āsiktāḥ paryagnirindhām ||25||

(12,3.26 ) ā yanti divaḥ pṛthivīṃ sacante bhūmyāḥ sacante adhyantarikṣam |
(12,3.26 ) śuddhāḥ satīstā u śumbhanta eva tā naḥ svargamabhi lokaṃ nayantu ||26||

(12,3.27 ) uteva prabhvīruta saṃmitāsa uta śukrāḥ śucayaścāmṛtāsaḥ |
(12,3.27 ) tā odanaṃ daṃpatibhyāṃ praśiṣṭā āpaḥ śikṣantīḥ pacatā sunāthāḥ ||27||

(12,3.28 ) saṃkhyātā stokāḥ pṛthivīṃ sacante prāṇāpānaiḥ saṃmitā oṣadhībhiḥ |
(12,3.28 ) asaṃkhyātā opyamānāḥ suvarṇāḥ sarvaṃ vyāpuḥ śucayaḥ śucitvam ||28||

(12,3.29 ) udyodhantyabhi valganti taptāḥ phenamasyanti bahulāṃśca bindūn |
(12,3.29 ) yoṣeva dṛṣṭvā patimṛtviyāyaitaistaṇḍulairbhavatā samāpaḥ ||29||

(12,3.30 ) utthāpaya sīdato budhna enān adbhirātmānamabhi saṃ spṛśantām |
(12,3.30 ) amāsi pātrairudakaṃ yadetan mitāstaṇḍulāḥ pradiśo yadīmāḥ ||30|| {15}

(12,3.31 ) pra yacha parśuṃ tvarayā harausamahiṃsanta oṣadhīrdāntu parvan |
(12,3.31 ) vāsāṃ somaḥ pari rājyaṃ babhūvāmanyutā no vīrudho bhavantu ||31||

(12,3.32 ) navaṃ barhirodanāya stṛṇīta priyaṃ hṛdaścakṣuṣo valgvastu |
(12,3.32 ) tasmin devāḥ saha daivīrviśantvimaṃ prāśnantvṛtubhirniṣadya ||32||

(12,3.33 ) vanaspate stīrṇamā sīda barhiragniṣṭobhaiḥ saṃmito devatābhiḥ |
(12,3.33 ) tvaṣṭreva rūpaṃ sukṛtaṃ svadhityainā ehāḥ pari pātre dadṛśrām ||33||

(12,3.34 ) ṣaṣṭyāṃ śaratsu nidhipā abhīchātsvaḥ pakvenābhyaśnavātai |
(12,3.34 ) upainaṃ jīvān pitaraśca putrā etaṃ svargaṃ gamayāntamagneḥ ||34||

(12,3.35 ) dhartā dhriyasva dharuṇe pṛthivyā acyutaṃ tvā devatāścyāvayantu |
(12,3.35 ) taṃ tvā daṃpatī jīvantau jīvaputrāvudvāsayātaḥ paryagnidhānāt||35||

(12,3.36 ) sarvāntsamāgā abhijitya lokān yāvantaḥ kāmāḥ samatītṛpastān |
(12,3.36 ) vi gāhethāmāyavanaṃ ca darvirekasmin pātre adhyuddharainam ||36||

(12,3.37 ) upa stṛṇīhi prathaya purastādghṛtena pātramabhi ghārayaitat|
(12,3.37 ) vāśrevosrā taruṇaṃ stanasyumimaṃ devāso abhihiṅkṛṇota ||37||

(12,3.38 ) upāstarīrakaro lokametamuruḥ prathatāmasamaḥ svargaḥ |
(12,3.38 ) tasmiṃ chrayātai mahiṣaḥ suparṇo devā enaṃ devatābhyaḥ pra yachān ||38||

(12,3.39 ) yadyajjāyā pacati tvatparaḥparaḥ patirvā jāye tvattiraḥ |
(12,3.39 ) saṃ tatsṛjethāṃ saha vāṃ tadastu saṃpādayantau saha lokamekam ||39||

(12,3.40 ) yāvanto asyāḥ pṛthivīṃ sacante asmatputrāḥ pari ye saṃbabhūvuḥ |
(12,3.40 ) sarvāṃstāmupa pātre hvayethāṃ nābhiṃ jānānāḥ śiśavaḥ samāyān ||40|| {16}

(12,3.41 ) vasoryā dhārā madhunā prapīnā ghṛtena miśrā amṛtasya nābhayaḥ |
(12,3.41 ) sarvāstā ava rundhe svargaḥ ṣaṣṭyāṃ śaratsu nidhipā abhīchāt||41||

(12,3.42 ) nidhiṃ nidhipā abhyenamichādanīśvarā abhitaḥ santu ye'nye |
(12,3.42 ) asmābhirdatto nihitaḥ svargastribhiḥ kāṇḍaistrīntsvargān arukṣat||42||

(12,3.43 ) agnī rakṣastapatu yadvidevaṃ kravyādpiśāca iha mā pra pāsta |
(12,3.43 ) nudāma enamapa rudhmo asmadādityā enamaṅgirasaḥ sacantām ||43||
(12,3.44 ) ādityebhyo aṅgirobhyo madhvidaṃ ghṛtena miśraṃ prati vedayāmi |
(12,3.44 ) śuddhahastau brāhmaṇasyānihatyaitaṃ svargaṃ sukṛtāvapītam ||44||

(12,3.45 ) idaṃ prāpamuttamaṃ kāṇḍamasya yasmāllokātparameṣṭhī samāpa |
(12,3.45 ) ā siñca sarpirghṛtavatsamaṅgdhyeṣa bhāgo aṅgiraso no atra ||45||

(12,3.46 ) satyāya ca tapase devatābhyo nidhiṃ śevadhiṃ pari dadma etam |
(12,3.46 ) mā no dyūte'va gān mā samityāṃ mā smānyasmā utsṛjatā purā mat||46||

(12,3.47 ) ahaṃ pacāmyahaṃ dadāmi mamedu karman karuṇe'dhi jāyā |
(12,3.47 ) kaumāro loko ajaniṣṭa putro'nvārabhethāṃ vaya uttarāvat||47||

(12,3.48 ) na kilbiṣamatra nādhāro asti na yan mitraiḥ samamamāna eti |
(12,3.48 ) anūnaṃ pātraṃ nihitaṃ na etatpaktāraṃ pakvaḥ punarā viśāti ||48||

(12,3.49 ) priyaṃ priyāṇāṃ kṛṇavāma tamaste yantu yatame dviṣanti |
(12,3.49 ) dhenuranaḍvān vayovaya āyadeva pauruṣeyamapa mṛtyuṃ nudantu ||49||

(12,3.50 ) samagnayaḥ viduranyo anyaṃ ya oṣadhīḥ sacate yaśca sindhūn |
(12,3.50 ) yāvanto devā divyātapanti hiraṇyaṃ jyotiḥ pacato babhūva ||50|| {17}

(12,3.51 ) eṣā tvacāṃ puruṣe saṃ babhūvānagnāḥ sarve paśavo ye anye |
(12,3.51 ) kṣatreṇātmānaṃ pari dhāpayātho'motaṃ vāso mukhamodanasya ||51||

(12,3.52 ) yadakṣeṣu vadā yatsamityāṃ yadvā vadā anṛtaṃ vittakāmyā |
(12,3.52 ) samānaṃ tantumabhi samvasānau tasmintsarvaṃ śamalaṃ sādayāthaḥ ||52||

(12,3.53 ) varṣaṃ vanuṣvāpi gacha devāṃstvaco dhūmaṃ paryutpātayāsi |
(12,3.53 ) viśvavyacā ghṛtapṛṣṭho bhaviṣyantsayonirlokamupa yāhyetam ||53||

(12,3.54 ) tanvaṃ svargo bahudhā vi cakre yathā vida ātmann anyavarṇām |
(12,3.54 ) apājaitkṛṣṇāṃ ruśatīṃ punāno yā lohinī tāṃ te agnau juhomi ||54||

(12,3.55 ) prācyai tvā diśe'gnaye'dhipataye'sitāya rakṣitra ādityāyeṣumate |
(12,3.55 ) etaṃ pari dadmastaṃ no gopāyatāsmākamaitoḥ |
(12,3.55 ) diṣṭaṃ no atra jarase ni neṣajjarā mṛtyave pari ṇo dadātvatha pakvena saha saṃ bhavema ||55||

(12,3.56 ) dakṣiṇāyai tvā diśa indrāyādhipataye tiraścirājaye rakṣitre yamāyeṣumate |
(12,3.56 ) etaṃ pari dadmastaṃ no gopāyatāsmākamaitoḥ |
(12,3.56 ) diṣṭaṃ no atra jarase ni neṣajjarā mṛtyave pari ṇo dadātvatha pakvena saha saṃ bhavema ||56||

(12,3.57 ) pratīcyai tvā diśe varuṇāyādhipataye pṛdākave rakṣitre'nnāyeṣumate |
(12,3.57 ) etaṃ pari dadmastaṃ no gopāyatāsmākamaitoḥ |
(12,3.57 ) diṣṭaṃ no atra jarase ni neṣajjarā mṛtyave pari ṇo dadātvatha pakvena saha saṃ bhavema ||57||
(12,3.58 ) udīcyai tvā diśe somāyādhipataye svajāya rakṣitre'śanyā iṣumatyai |
(12,3.58 ) etaṃ pari dadmastaṃ no gopāyatāsmākamaitoḥ |
(12,3.58 ) diṣṭaṃ no atra jarase ni neṣajjarā mṛtyave pari ṇo dadātvatha pakvena saha saṃ bhavema ||58||

(12,3.59 ) dhruvāyai tvā diśe viṣṇave'dhipataye kalmāṣagrīvāya rakṣitra oṣadhībhya iṣumatībhyaḥ |
(12,3.59 ) etaṃ pari dadmastaṃ no gopāyatāsmākamaitoḥ |
(12,3.59 ) diṣṭaṃ no atra jarase ni neṣajjarā mṛtyave pari ṇo dadātvatha pakvena saha saṃ bhavema ||59||

(12,3.60 ) ūrdhvāyai tvā diśe bṛhaspataye'dhipataye śvitrāya rakṣitre varṣāyeṣumate |
(12,3.60 ) etaṃ pari dadmastaṃ no gopāyatāsmākamaitoḥ |
(12,3.60 ) diṣṭaṃ no atra jarase ni neṣajjarā mṛtyave pari ṇo dadātvatha pakvena saha saṃ bhavema ||60|| {18}



(12,4.1 ) dadāmītyeva brūyādanu caināmabhutsata |
(12,4.1 ) vaśāṃ brahmabhyo yācadbhyastatprajāvadapatyavat||1||

(12,4.2 ) prajayā sa vi krīṇīte paśubhiścopa dasyati |
(12,4.2 ) ya ārṣeyebhyo yācadbhyo devānāṃ gāṃ na ditsati ||2||

(12,4.3 ) kūṭayāsya saṃ śīryante śloṇayā kāṭamardati |
(12,4.3 ) baṇḍayā dahyante gṛhāḥ kāṇayā dīyate svam ||3||

(12,4.4 ) vilohito adhiṣṭhānācchakno vindati gopatim |
(12,4.4 ) tathā vaśāyāḥ saṃvidyaṃ duradabhnā hyucyase ||4||

(12,4.5 ) padorasyā adhiṣṭhānādviklindurnāma vindati |
(12,4.5 ) anāmanātsaṃ śīryante yā mukhenopajighrati ||5||

(12,4.6 ) yo asyāḥ karṇāvāskunotyā sa deveṣu vṛścate |
(12,4.6 ) lakṣma kurva iti manyate kanīyaḥ kṛṇute svam ||6||

(12,4.7 ) yadasyāḥ kasmai cidbhogāya bālān kaścitprakṛntati |
(12,4.7 ) tataḥ kiśorā mriyante vatsāṃśca ghātuko vṛkaḥ ||7||

(12,4.8 ) yadasyā gopatau satyā loma dhvāṅkṣo ajīhiḍat|
(12,4.8 ) tataḥ kumārā mriyante yakṣmo vindatyanāmanāt||8||

(12,4.9 ) yadasyāḥ palpūlanaṃ śakṛddāsī samasyati |
(12,4.9 ) tato'parūpaṃ jāyate tasmādavyeṣyadenasaḥ ||9||

(12,4.10 ) jāyamānābhi jāyate devāntsabrāhmaṇān vaśā |
(12,4.10 ) tasmādbrahmabhyo deyaiṣā tadāhuḥ svasya gopanam ||10|| {19}

(12,4.11 ) ya enāṃ vanimāyanti teṣāṃ devakṛtā vaśā |
(12,4.11 ) brahmajyeyaṃ tadabruvan ya enāṃ nipriyāyate ||11||

(12,4.12 ) ya ārṣeyebhyo yācadbhyo devānāṃ gāṃ na ditsati |
(12,4.12 ) ā sa deveṣu vṛścate brāhmaṇānāṃ ca manyave ||12||

(12,4.13 ) yo asya syādvaśābhogo anyāmicheta tarhi saḥ |
(12,4.13 ) hiṃste adattā puruṣaṃ yācitāṃ ca na ditsati ||13||

(12,4.14 ) yathā śevadhirnihito brāhmaṇānāṃ tathā vaśā |
(12,4.14 ) tāmetadachāyanti yasmin kasmiṃśca jāyate ||14||

(12,4.15 ) svametadachāyanti yadvaśāṃ brāhmaṇā abhi |
(12,4.15 ) yathainān anyasmin jinīyādevāsyā nirodhanam ||15||

(12,4.16 ) caredevā traihāyaṇādavijñātagadā satī |
(12,4.16 ) vaśāṃ ca vidyān nārada brāhmaṇāstarhyeṣyāḥ ||16||

(12,4.17 ) ya enāmavaśāmāha devānāṃ nihitaṃ nidhim |
(12,4.17 ) ubhau tasmai bhavāśarvau parikramyeṣumasyataḥ ||17||

(12,4.18 ) yo asyā ūdho na vedātho asyā stanān uta |
(12,4.18 ) ubhayenaivāsmai duhe dātuṃ cedaśakadvaśām ||18||

(12,4.19 ) duradabhnainamā śaye yācitāṃ ca na ditsati |
(12,4.19 ) nāsmai kāmāḥ samṛdhyante yāmadattvā cikīrṣati ||19||

(12,4.20 ) devā vaśāmayācan mukhaṃ kṛtvā brāhmaṇam |
(12,4.20 ) teṣāṃ sarveṣāmadadaddheḍaṃ nyeti mānuṣaḥ ||20|| {20}

(12,4.21 ) heḍaṃ paśūnāṃ nyeti brāhmaṇebhyo'dadadvaśām |
(12,4.21 ) devānāṃ nihitaṃ bhāgaṃ martyaścen nipriyāyate ||21||

(12,4.22 ) yadanye śataṃ yāceyurbrāhmaṇā gopatiṃ vaśām |
(12,4.22 ) athaināṃ devā abruvann evaṃ ha viduṣo vaśā ||22||

(12,4.23 ) ya evaṃ viduṣe'dattvāthānyebhyo dadadvaśām |
(12,4.23 ) durgā tasmā adhiṣṭhāne pṛthivī sahadevatā ||23||

(12,4.24 ) devā vaśāmayācan yasminn agre ajāyata |
(12,4.24 ) tāmetāṃ vidyān nāradaḥ saha devairudājata ||24||

(12,4.25 ) anapatyamalpapaśuṃ vaśā kṛṇoti pūruṣam |
(12,4.25 ) brāhmaṇaiśca yācitāmathaināṃ nipriyāyate ||25||

(12,4.26 ) agnīṣomābhyāṃ kāmāya mitrāya varuṇāya ca |
(12,4.26 ) tebhyo yācanti brāhmaṇāsteṣvā vṛścate'dadat||26||

(12,4.27 ) yāvadasyā gopatirnopaśṛṇuyādṛcaḥ svayam |
(12,4.27 ) caredasya tāvadgoṣu nāsya śrutvā gṛhe vaset||27||

(12,4.28 ) yo asyā ṛca upaśrutyātha goṣvacīcarat|
(12,4.28 ) āyuśca tasya bhūtiṃ ca devā vṛścanti hīḍitāḥ ||28||

(12,4.29 ) vaśā carantī bahudhā devānāṃ nihito nidhiḥ |
(12,4.29 ) āviṣkṛṇuṣva rūpāṇi yadā sthāma jighāṃsati ||29||

(12,4.30 ) āvirātmānaṃ kṛṇute yadā sthāma jighāṃsati |
(12,4.30 ) atho ha brahmabhyo vaśā yāñcyāya kṛṇute manaḥ ||30|| {21}

(12,4.31 ) manasā saṃ kalpayati taddevāmapi gachati |
(12,4.31 ) tato ha brahmāṇo vaśāmupaprayanti yācitum ||31||

(12,4.32 ) svadhākāreṇa pitṛbhyo yajñena devatābhyaḥ |
(12,4.32 ) dānena rājanyo vaśāyā māturheḍaṃ na gachati ||32||

(12,4.33 ) vaśā mātā rājanyasya tathā saṃbhūtamagraśaḥ |
(12,4.33 ) tasyā āhuranarpaṇaṃ yadbrahmabhyaḥ pradīyate ||33||

(12,4.34 ) yathājyaṃ pragṛhītamālumpetsruco agnaye |
(12,4.34 ) evā ha brahmabhyo vaśāmagnaya ā vṛścate'dadat||34||

(12,4.35 ) puroḍāśavatsā sudughā loke'smā upa tiṣṭhati |
(12,4.35 ) sāsmai sarvān kāmān vaśā pradaduṣe duhe ||35||

(12,4.36 ) sarvān kāmān yamarājye vaśā pradaduṣe duhe |
(12,4.36 ) athāhurnārakaṃ lokaṃ nirundhānasya yācitām ||36||

(12,4.37 ) pravīyamānā carati kruddhā gopataye vaśā |
(12,4.37 ) vehataṃ mā manyamāno mṛtyoḥ pāśeṣu badhyatām ||37||

(12,4.38 ) yo vehataṃ manyamāno'mā ca pacate vaśām |
(12,4.38 ) apyasya putrān pautrāṃśca yācayate bṛhaspatiḥ ||38||

(12,4.39 ) mahadeṣāva tapati carantī goṣu gaurapi |
(12,4.39 ) atho ha gopataye vaśādaduṣe viṣaṃ duhe ||39||

(12,4.40 ) priyaṃ paśūnāṃ bhavati yadbrahmabhyaḥ pradīyate |
(12,4.40 ) atho vaśāyāstatpriyaṃ yaddevatrā haviḥ syāt||40|| {22}

(12,4.41 ) yā vaśā udakalpayan devā yajñādudetya |
(12,4.41 ) tāsāṃ viliptyaṃ bhīmāmudākuruta nāradaḥ ||41||

(12,4.42 ) tāṃ devā amīmāṃsanta vaśeyā3 avaśeti |
(12,4.42 ) tāmabravīn nārada eṣā vaśānāṃ vaśatameti ||42||

(12,4.43 ) kati nu vaśā nārada yāstvaṃ vettha manuṣyajāḥ |
(12,4.43 ) tāstvā pṛchāmi vidvāṃsaṃ kasyā nāśnīyādabrāhmaṇaḥ ||43||

(12,4.44 ) viliptyā bṛhaspate yā ca sūtavaśā vaśā |
(12,4.44 ) tasyā nāśnīyādabrāhmaṇo ya āśaṃseta bhūtyām ||44||

(12,4.45 ) namaste astu nāradānuṣṭhu viduṣe vaśā |
(12,4.45 ) katamāsāṃ bhīmatamā yāmadattvā parābhavet||45||

(12,4.46 ) viliptī yā bṛhaspate'tho sūtavaśā vaśā |
(12,4.46 ) tasyā nāśnīyādabrāhmaṇo ya āśaṃseta bhūtyām ||46||

(12,4.47 ) trīṇi vai vaśājātāni viliptī sūtavaśā vaśā |
(12,4.47 ) tāḥ pra yachedbrahmabhyaḥ so'nāvraskaḥ prajāpatau ||47||

(12,4.48 ) etadvo brāhmaṇā haviriti manvīta yācitaḥ |
(12,4.48 ) vaśāṃ cedenaṃ yāceyuryā bhīmādaduṣo gṛhe ||48||

(12,4.49 ) devā vaśāṃ paryavadan na no'dāditi hīḍitāḥ |
(12,4.49 ) etābhirṛgbhirbhedaṃ tasmādvai sa parābhavat||49||

(12,4.50 ) utaināṃ bhedo nādadādvaśāmindreṇa yācitaḥ |
(12,4.50 ) tasmāttaṃ devā āgaso'vṛścann ahamuttare ||50||

(12,4.51 ) ye vaśāyā adānāya vadanti parirāpiṇaḥ |
(12,4.51 ) indrasya manyave jālmā ā vṛścante acittyā ||51||

(12,4.52 ) ye gopatiṃ parāṇīyāthāhurmā dadā iti |
(12,4.52 ) rudrasyāstāṃ te hetīṃ pari yantyacittyā ||52||

(12,4.53 ) yadi hutaṃ yadyahutāmamā ca pacate vaśām |
(12,4.53 ) devāntsabrāhmaṇān ṛtvā jihmo lokān nirṛchati ||53|| {23}


(12,5.53 ) śrameṇa tapasā sṛṣṭā brahmaṇā vittā rte śritā ||1||

(12,5.2 ) satyenāvṛtā śriyā prāvṛtā yaśasā parīvṛtā ||2||

(12,5.3 ) svadhayā parihitā śraddhayā paryūḍhā dīkṣayā guptā yajñe pratiṣṭhitā loko nidhanam ||3||

(12,5.4 ) brahma padavāyaṃ brāhmaṇo'dhipatiḥ ||4||

(12,5.5 ) tāmādadānasya brahmagavīṃ jinato brāhmaṇaṃ kṣatriyasya ||5||

(12,5.6 ) apa krāmati sūnṛtā vīryaṃ punyā lakṣmīḥ ||6|| {24}

(12,5.7 ) ojaśca tejaśca sahaśca balaṃ ca vākcendriyaṃ ca śrīśca dharmaśca ||7||

(12,5.8 ) brahma ca kṣatraṃ ca rāṣṭraṃ ca viśaśca tviṣiśca yaśaśca varcaśca draviṇaṃ ca ||8||

(12,5.9 ) āyuśca rūpaṃ ca nāma ca kīrtiśca prāṇaścāpānaśca cakṣuśca śrotraṃ ca ||9||

(12,5.10 ) payaśca rasaścānnaṃ cānnādyaṃ ca rtaṃ ca satyaṃ ceṣṭaṃ ca pūrtaṃ ca prajā ca paśavaśca ||10||

(12,5.11 ) tāni sarvāṇyapa krāmanti brahmagavīmādadānasya jinato brāhmaṇaṃ kṣatriyasya ||11|| {25}

(12,5.12 ) saiṣā bhīmā brahmagavyaghaviṣā sākṣātkṛtyā kūlbajamāvṛtā ||12||

(12,5.13 ) sarvāṇyasyāṃ ghorāṇi sarve ca mṛtyavaḥ ||13||

(12,5.14 ) sarvāṇyasyāṃ krūrāṇi sarve puruṣavadhāḥ ||14||

(12,5.15 ) sā brahmajyaṃ devapīyuṃ brahmagavyādīyamānā mṛtyoḥ padvīṣa ā dyati ||15||

(12,5.16 ) meniḥ śatavadhā hi sā brahmajyasya kṣitirhi sā ||16||

(12,5.17 ) tasmādvai brāhmaṇānāṃ gaurdurādharṣā vijānatā ||17||

(12,5.18 ) vajro dhāvantī vaiśvānara udvītā ||18||

(12,5.19 ) hetiḥ śaphān utkhidantī mahādevo'pekṣamāṇā ||19||

(12,5.20 ) kṣurapavirīkṣamāṇā vāśyamānābhi sphūrjati ||20||

(12,5.21 ) mṛtyurhiṅkṛṇvatyugro devaḥ puchaṃ paryasyantī ||21||

(12,5.22 ) sarvajyāniḥ karṇau varīvarjayantī rājayakṣmo mehantī ||22||

(12,5.23 ) menirduhyamānā śīrṣaktirdugdhā ||23||

(12,5.24 ) sedirupatiṣṭhantī mithoyodhaḥ parāmṛṣṭā ||24||
(12,5.25 ) śaravyā mukhe'pinahyamāna ṛtirhanyamānā ||25||

(12,5.26 ) aghaviṣā nipatantī tamo nipatitā ||26||

(12,5.27 ) anugachantī prāṇān upa dāsayati brahmagavī brahmajyasya ||27|| {26}

(12,5.28 ) vairaṃ vikṛtyamānā pautrādyaṃ vibhājyamānā ||28||

(12,5.29 ) devahetirhriyamāṇā vyṛddhirhṛtā ||29||

(12,5.30 ) pāpmādhidhīyamānā pāruṣyamavadhīyamānā ||30||

(12,5.31 ) viṣaṃ prayasyantī takmā prayastā ||31||

(12,5.32 ) aghaṃ pracyamānā duṣvapnyaṃ pakvā ||32||
(12,5.33 ) mūlabarhaṇī paryākriyamāṇā kṣitiḥ paryākṛtā ||33||

(12,5.34 ) asaṃjñā gandhena śuguddhriyamāṇāśīviṣa uddhṛtā ||34||

(12,5.35 ) abhūtirupahriyamāṇā parābhūtirupahṛtā ||35||

(12,5.36 ) śarvaḥ kruddhaḥ piśyamānā śimidā piśitā ||36||

(12,5.37 ) avartiraśyamānā nirṛtiraśitā ||37||

(12,5.38 ) aśitā lokācchinatti brahmagavī brahmajyamasmāccāmuṣmācca ||38|| {27}

(12,5.39 ) tasyā āhananaṃ kṛtyā menirāśasanaṃ valaga ūbadhyam ||39||

(12,5.40 ) asvagatā parihṇutā ||40||

(12,5.41 ) agniḥ kravyādbhūtvā brahmagavī brahmajyaṃ praviśyātti ||41||

(12,5.42 ) sarvāsyāṅgā parvā mūlāni vṛścati ||42||

(12,5.43 ) chinattyasya pitṛbandhu parā bhāvayati mātṛbandhu ||43||

(12,5.44 ) vivāhāṃ jñātīntsarvān api kṣāpayati brahmagavī brahmajyasya kṣatriyeṇāpunardīyamānā ||44||

(12,5.45 ) avāstumenamasvagamaprajasaṃ karotyaparāparaṇo bhavati kṣīyate ||45||

(12,5.46 ) ya evaṃ viduṣo brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyo gāmādatte ||46|| {28}

(12,5.47 ) kṣipraṃ vai tasyāhanane gṛdhrāḥ kurvata ailabam ||47||

(12,5.48 ) kṣipraṃ vai tasyādahanaṃ pari nṛtyanti keśinīrāghnānāḥ pāṇinorasi kurvāṇāḥ pāpamailabam ||48||

(12,5.49 ) kṣipraṃ vai tasya vāstuṣu vṛkāḥ kurvata ailabam ||49||

(12,5.50 ) kṣipraṃ vai tasya pṛchanti yattadāsī3 idaṃ nu tā3 iti ||50||

(12,5.51 ) chindhyā chindhi pra chindhyapi kṣāpaya kṣāpaya ||51||

(12,5.52 ) ādadānamāṅgirasi brahmajyamupa dāsaya ||52||

(12,5.53 ) vaiśvadevī hyucyase kṛtyā kūlbajamāvṛtā ||53||

(12,5.54 ) oṣantī samoṣantī brahmaṇo vajraḥ ||54||

(12,5.55 ) kṣurapavirmṛtyurbhūtvā vi dhāva tvam ||55||

(12,5.56 ) ā datse jinatāṃ varca iṣṭaṃ pūrtaṃ cāśiṣaḥ ||56||

(12,5.57 ) ādāya jītaṃ jītāya loke'muṣmin pra yachasi ||57||

(12,5.58 ) aghnye padavīrbhava brāhmaṇasyābhiśastyā ||58||

(12,5.59 ) meniḥ śaravyā bhavāghādaghaviṣā bhava ||59||

(12,5.60 ) aghnye pra śiro jahi brahmajyasya kṛtāgaso devapīyorarādhasaḥ ||60||

(12,5.61 ) tvayā pramūrṇaṃ mṛditamagnirdahatu duścitam ||61|| {29}

(12,5.62 ) vṛśca pra vṛśca saṃ vṛśca daha pra daha saṃ daha ||62||

(12,5.63 ) brahmajyaṃ devyaghnya ā mūlādanusaṃdaha ||63||

(12,5.64 ) yathāyādyamasādanātpāpalokān parāvataḥ ||64||

(12,5.65 ) evā tvaṃ devyaghnye brahmajyasya kṛtāgaso devapīyorarādhasaḥ ||65||

(12,5.66 ) vajreṇa śataparvaṇā tīkṣṇena kṣurabhṛṣṭinā ||66||

(12,5.67 ) pra skandhān pra śiro jahi ||67||

(12,5.68 ) lomānyasya saṃ chindhi tvacamasya vi veṣṭaya ||68||

(12,5.69 ) māṃsānyasya śātaya snāvānyasya saṃ vṛha ||69||

(12,5.70 ) asthīnyasya pīḍaya majjānamasya nirjahi ||70||

(12,5.71 ) sarvāsyāṅgā parvāṇi vi śrathaya ||71||

(12,5.72 ) agnirenaṃ kravyātpṛthivyā nudatāmudoṣatu vāyurantarikṣān mahato varimṇaḥ ||72||

(12,5.73 ) sūrya enaṃ divaḥ pra ṇudatāṃ nyoṣatu ||73|| {30}



(13,1.1 ) udehi vājin yo apsvantaridaṃ rāṣṭraṃ pra viśa sūnṛtāvat|
(13,1.1 ) yo rohito viśvamidaṃ jajāna sa tvā rāṣṭrāya subhṛtaṃ bibhartu ||1||

(13,1.2 ) udvāja ā gan yo apsvantarviśa ā roha tvadyonayo yāḥ |
(13,1.2 ) somaṃ dadhāno'pa oṣadhīrgāścatuṣpado dvipada ā veśayeha ||2||

(13,1.3 ) yūyamugrā marutaḥ pṛśnimātara indreṇa yujā pra mṛṇīta śatrūn |
(13,1.3 ) ā vo rohitaḥ śṛṇavatsudānavastriṣaptāso marutaḥ svādusaṃmudaḥ ||3||

(13,1.4 ) ruho ruroha rohita ā ruroha garbho janīnāṃ januṣāmupastham |
(13,1.4 ) tābhiḥ saṃrabdhamanvavindan ṣaḍurvīrgātuṃ prapaśyann iha rāṣṭramāhāḥ ||4||

(13,1.5 ) ā te rāṣṭramiha rohito'hārṣīdvyāsthan mṛdho abhayaṃ te abhūt|
(13,1.5 ) tasmai te dyāvāpṛthivī revatībhiḥ kāmaṃ duhātāmiha śakvarībhiḥ ||5||

(13,1.6 ) rohito dyāvāpṛthivī jajāna tatra tantuṃ parameṣṭhī tatāna |
(13,1.6 ) tatra śiśriye'ja ekapādo'dṛṃhaddyāvāpṛthivī balena ||6||

(13,1.7 ) rohito dyāvāpṛthivī adṛṃhattena sva stabhitaṃ tena nākaḥ |
(13,1.7 ) tenāntarikṣaṃ vimitā rajāṃsi tena devā amṛtamanvavindan ||7||

(13,1.8 ) vi rohito amṛśadviśvarūpaṃ samākurvāṇaḥ praruho ruhaśca |
(13,1.8 ) divaṃ rūḍhvā mahatā mahimnā saṃ te rāṣṭramanaktu payasā ghṛtena ||8||

(13,1.9 ) yāste ruhaḥ praruho yāsta āruho yābhirāpṛṇāsi divamantarikṣam |
(13,1.9 ) tāsāṃ brahmaṇā payasā vavṛdhāno viśi rāṣṭre jāgṛhi rohitasya ||9||

(13,1.10 ) yaste viśastapasaḥ saṃbabhūvurvatsaṃ gāyatrīmanu tā ihāguḥ |
(13,1.10 ) tāstvā viśantu manasā śivena saṃmātā vatso abhyetu rohitaḥ ||10|| {1}

(13,1.11 ) ūrdhvo rohito adhi nāke asthādviśvā rūpāṇi janayan yuvā kaviḥ |
(13,1.11 ) tigmenāgnirjyotiṣā vi bhāti tṛtīye cakre rajasi priyāṇi ||11||

(13,1.12 ) sahasraśṛṅgo vṛṣabho jātavedā ghṛtāhutaḥ somapṛṣṭhaḥ suvīraḥ |
(13,1.12 ) mā mā hāsīn nāthito nettvā jahāni gopoṣaṃ ca me vīrapoṣaṃ ca dhehi ||12||

(13,1.13 ) rohito yajñasya janitā mukhaṃ ca rohitāya vācā śrotreṇa manasā juhomi |
(13,1.13 ) rohitaṃ devā yanti sumanasyamānāḥ sa mā rohaiḥ sāmityai rohayatu ||13||

(13,1.14 ) rohito yajñaṃ vyadadhādviśvakarmaṇe tasmāttejāṃsyupa memānyāguḥ |
(13,1.14 ) voceyaṃ te nābhiṃ bhuvanasyādhi majmani ||14||

(13,1.15 ) ā tvā ruroha bṛhatyuta paṅktirā kakubvarcasā jātavedaḥ |
(13,1.15 ) ā tvā rurohoṣṇihākṣaro vaṣaṭkāra ā tvā ruroha rohito retasā saha ||15||

(13,1.16 ) ayaṃ vaste garbhaṃ pṛthivyā divaṃ vaste'yamantarikṣam |
(13,1.16 ) ayaṃ bradhnasya viṣṭapi svarlokān vyānaśe ||16||

(13,1.17 ) vācaspate pṛthivī naḥ syonā syonā yonistalpā naḥ suśevā |
(13,1.17 ) ihaiva prāṇaḥ sakhye no astu taṃ tvā parameṣṭhin paryagnirāyuṣā varcasā dadhātu ||17||

(13,1.18 ) vācaspata ṛtavaḥ pañca ye nau vaiśvakarmaṇāḥ pari ye saṃbabhūvuḥ |
(13,1.18 ) ihaiva prāṇaḥ sakhye no astu taṃ tvā parameṣṭhin pari rohita āyuṣā varcasā dadhātu ||18||

(13,1.19 ) vācaspate saumanasaṃ manaśca goṣṭhe no gā janaya yoniṣu prajāḥ |
(13,1.19 ) ihaiva prāṇaḥ sakhye no astu taṃ tvā parameṣṭhin paryahamāyuṣā varcasā dadhātu ||19||

(13,1.20 ) pari tvā dhātsavitā devo agnirvarcasā mitrāvaruṇāvabhi tvā |
(13,1.20 ) sarvā arātīravakrāmann ehīdaṃ rāṣṭramakaraḥ sunṛtāvat||20|| {2}

(13,1.21 ) yaṃ tvā pṛṣatī rathe praṣṭirvahati rohita |
(13,1.21 ) śubhā yāsi riṇann apaḥ ||21||

(13,1.22 ) anuvratā rohiṇī rohitasya sūriḥ suvarṇā bṛhatī suvarcāḥ |
(13,1.22 ) tayā vājān viśvarūpāṃ jayema tayā viśvāḥ pṛtanā abhi ṣyāma ||22||

(13,1.23 ) idaṃ sado rohiṇī rohitasyāsau panthāḥ pṛṣatī yena yāti |
(13,1.23 ) tāṃ gandharvāḥ kaśyapā un nayanti tāṃ rakṣanti kavayo'pramādam ||23||

(13,1.24 ) sūryasyāśvā harayaḥ ketumantaḥ sadā vahantyamṛtāḥ sukhaṃ ratham |
(13,1.24 ) ghṛtapāvā rohito bhrājamāno divaṃ devaḥ pṛṣatīmā viveśa ||24||

(13,1.25 ) yo rohito vṛṣabhastigmaśṛṅgaḥ paryagniṃ pari sūryaṃ babhūva |
(13,1.25 ) yo viṣṭabhnāti pṛthivīṃ divaṃ ca tasmāddevā adhi sṛṣṭīḥ sṛjante ||25||

(13,1.26 ) rohito divamāruhan mahataḥ paryarṇavāt|
(13,1.26 ) sarvo ruroha rohito ruhaḥ ||26||

(13,1.27 ) vi mimīṣva payasvatīṃ ghṛtācīṃ devānāṃ dhenuranapaspṛgeṣā |
(13,1.27 ) indraḥ somaṃ pibatu kṣemo astvagniḥ pra stautu vi mṛdho nudasva ||27||

(13,1.28 ) samiddho agniḥ samidhāno ghṛtavṛddho ghṛtāhutaḥ |
(13,1.28 ) abhīṣāṭviśvāṣāḍagniḥ sapatnān hantu ye mama ||28||

(13,1.29 ) hantvenān pra dahatvariryo naḥ pṛtanyati |
(13,1.29 ) kravyādāgninā vayaṃ sapatnān pra dahāmasi ||29||

(13,1.30 ) avācīnān ava jahīndra vajreṇa bāhumān |
(13,1.30 ) adhā sapatnān māmakān agnestejobhirādiṣi ||30|| {3}

(13,1.31 ) agne sapatnān adharān pādayāsmadvyathayā sajātamutpipānaṃ bṛhaspate |
(13,1.31 ) indrāgnī mitrāvaruṇāvadhare padyantāmapratimanyūyamānāḥ ||31||

(13,1.32 ) udyaṃstvaṃ deva sūrya sapatnān ava me jahi |
(13,1.32 ) avainān aśmanā jahi te yantvadhamaṃ tamaḥ ||32||

(13,1.33 ) vatso virājo vṛṣabho matīnāmā ruroha śukrapṛṣṭho'ntarikṣam |
(13,1.33 ) ghṛtenārkamabhyarcanti vatsaṃ brahma santaṃ brahmaṇā vardhayanti ||33||

(13,1.34 ) divaṃ ca roha pṛthivīṃ ca roha rāṣṭraṃ ca roha draviṇaṃ ca roha |
(13,1.34 ) prajāṃ ca rohāmṛtaṃ ca roha rohitena tanvaṃ saṃ spṛṣasva ||34||

(13,1.35 ) ye devā rāṣṭrabhṛto'bhito yanti sūryam |
(13,1.35 ) taiṣṭe rohitaḥ samvidāno rāṣṭraṃ dadhātu sumanasyamānaḥ ||35||

(13,1.36 ) uttvā yajñā brahmapūtā vahantyadhvagato harayastvā vahanti |
(13,1.36 ) tiraḥ samudramati rocase'rṇavam ||36||

(13,1.37 ) rohite dyāvāpṛthivī adhi śrite vasujiti gojiti saṃdhanājiti |
(13,1.37 ) sahasraṃ yasya janimāni sapta ca voceyaṃ te nābhiṃ bhuvanasyādhi majmani ||37||

(13,1.38 ) yaśā yāsi pradiśo diśaśca yaśāḥ paśūnāmuta carṣaṇīnām |
(13,1.38 ) yaśāḥ pṛthivyā adityā upasthe'haṃ bhūyāsaṃ saviteva cāruḥ ||38||

(13,1.39 ) amutra sann iha vetthetaḥ saṃstāni paśyasi |
(13,1.39 ) itaḥ paśyanti rocanaṃ divi sūryaṃ vipaścitam ||39||

(13,1.40 ) devo devān marcayasyantaścarasyarṇave |
(13,1.40 ) samānamagnimindhate taṃ viduḥ kavayaḥ pare ||40|| {4}

(13,1.41 ) avaḥ pareṇa para enāvareṇa padā vatsaṃ bibratī gaurudasthāt|
(13,1.41 ) sā kadrīcī kaṃ svidardhaṃ parāgātkva svitsūte nahi yūthe asmin ||41||

(13,1.42 ) ekapadī dvipadī sā catuṣpadyaṣṭāpadī navapadī babhūvuṣī |
(13,1.42 ) sahasrākṣarā bhuvanasya paṅktistasyāḥ samudrā adhi vi kṣaranti ||42||

(13,1.43 ) ārohan dyāmamṛtaḥ prāva me vacaḥ |
(13,1.43 ) uttvā yajñā brahmapūtā vahantyadhvagato harayastvā vahanti ||43||

(13,1.44 ) veda tatte amartya yatta ākramaṇaṃ divi |
(13,1.44 ) yatte sadhasthaṃ parame vyoman ||44||

(13,1.45 ) sūryo dyāṃ sūryaḥ pṛṭhivīṃ sūrya āpo'ti paśyati |
(13,1.45 ) sūryo bhūtasyaikaṃ cakṣurā ruroha divaṃ mahīm ||45||

(13,1.46 ) urvīrāsan paridhayo vedirbhūmirakalpata |
(13,1.46 ) tatraitāvagnī ādhatta himaṃ ghraṃsaṃ ca rohitaḥ ||46||

(13,1.47 ) himaṃ ghraṃsaṃ cādhāya yūpān kṛtvā parvatān |
(13,1.47 ) varṣājyāvagnī ījāte rohitasya svarvidaḥ ||47||

(13,1.48 ) svarvido rohitasya brahmaṇāgniḥ samidhyate |
(13,1.48 ) tasmādghraṃsastasmāddhimastasmādyajño'jāyata ||48||

(13,1.49 ) brahmaṇāgnī vāvṛdhānau brahmavṛddhau brahmāhutau |
(13,1.49 ) brahmeddhāvagnī ījāte rohitasya svarvidaḥ ||49||

(13,1.50 ) satye anyaḥ samāhito'psvanyaḥ samidhyate |
(13,1.50 ) brahmeddhāvagnī ījāte rohitasya svarvidaḥ ||50|| {5}

(13,1.51 ) yaṃ vātaḥ pariśumbhati yaṃ vendro brahmaṇaspatiḥ |
(13,1.51 ) brahmeddhāvagnī ījāte rohitasya svarvidaḥ ||51||

(13,1.52 ) vediṃ bhūmiṃ kalpayitvā divaṃ kṛtvā dakṣiṇām |
(13,1.52 ) ghraṃsaṃ tadagniṃ kṛtvā cakāra viśvamātmanvadvarṣeṇājyena rohitaḥ ||52||

(13,1.53 ) varṣamājaṃ ghraṃso agnirvedirbhūmirakalpata |
(13,1.53 ) tatraitān parvatān agnirgīrbhirūrdhvāmakalpayat||53||

(13,1.54 ) gīrbhirūrdhvān kalpayitvā rohito bhūmimabravīt|
(13,1.54 ) tvayīdaṃ sarvaṃ jāyatāṃ yadbhūtaṃ yacca bhāvyam ||54||

(13,1.55 ) sa yajñaḥ prathamo bhūto bhavyo ajāyata |
(13,1.55 ) tasmāddha jajña idaṃ sarvaṃ yatkiṃ cedaṃ virocate rohitena ṛṣiṇābhṛtam ||55||

(13,1.56 ) yaśca gāṃ padā sphurati pratyaṅsūryaṃ ca mehati |
(13,1.56 ) tasya vṛścāmi te mūlaṃ na chāyāṃ karavo'param ||56||

(13,1.57 ) yo mābhichāyamatyeṣi māṃ cāgniṃ cāntarā |
(13,1.57 ) tasya vṛścāmi te mūlaṃ na chāyāṃ karavo'param ||57||

(13,1.58 ) yo adya deva sūrya tvāṃ ca māṃ cāntarāyati |
(13,1.58 ) duṣvapnyaṃ tasmiṃ chamalaṃ duritāni ca mṛjmahe ||58||

(13,1.59 ) mā pra gāma patho vayaṃ mā yajñādindra sominaḥ |
(13,1.59 ) mānta sthurno arātayaḥ ||59||

(13,1.60 ) yo yajñasya prasādhanastanturdeveṣvātataḥ |
(13,1.60 ) tamāhutamaśīmahi ||60|| {6}



(13,2.1 ) udasya ketavo divi śukrā bhrājanta īrate |
(13,2.1 ) ādityasya nṛcakṣaso mahivratasya mīḍhuṣaḥ ||1||

(13,2.2 ) diśāṃ prajñānāṃ svarayantamarciṣā supakṣamāśuṃ patayantamarṇave |
(13,2.2 ) stavāma sūryaṃ bhuvanasya gopāṃ yo raśmibhirdiśa ābhāti sarvāḥ ||2||

(13,2.3 ) yatprāṅpratyaṅsvadhayā yāsi śībhaṃ nānārūpe ahanī karṣi māyayā |
(13,2.3 ) tadāditya mahi tatte mahi śravo yadeko viśvaṃ pari bhūma jāyase ||3||

(13,2.4 ) vipaścitaṃ taraṇiṃ bhrājamānaṃ vahanti yaṃ haritaḥ sapta bahvīḥ |
(13,2.4 ) srutādyamattrirdivamunnināya taṃ tvā paśyanti pariyāntamājim ||4||

(13,2.5 ) mā tvā dabhan pariyāntamājiṃ svasti durgāmati yāhi śībham |
(13,2.5 ) divaṃ ca sūrya pṛthivīṃ ca devīmahorātre vimimāno yadeṣi ||5||

(13,2.6 ) svasti te sūrya carase rathāya yenobhāvantau pariyāsi sadyaḥ |
(13,2.6 ) yaṃ te vahanti harito vahiṣṭhāḥ śatamaśvā yadi vā sapta bahvīḥ ||6||

(13,2.7 ) sukhaṃ sūrya rathamaṃśumantaṃ syonaṃ suvahnimadhi tiṣṭha vājinam |
(13,2.7 ) yaṃ te vahanti harito vahiṣṭhāḥ śatamaśvā yadi vā sapta bahvīḥ ||7||

(13,2.8 ) sapta sūryo harito yātave rathe hiraṇyatvacaso bṛhatīrayukta |
(13,2.8 ) amoci śukro rajasaḥ parastādvidhūya devastamo divamāruhat||8||

(13,2.9 ) utketunā bṛhatā deva āgann apāvṛktamo'bhi jyotiraśrait|
(13,2.9 ) divyaḥ suparṇaḥ sa vīro vyakhyadaditeḥ putro bhuvanāni viśvā ||9||

(13,2.10 ) udyan raśmīn ā tanuṣe viśvā rupāṇi puṣyasi |
(13,2.10 ) ubhā samudrau kratunā vi bhāsi sarvāṃllokān paribhūrbhrājamānaḥ ||10|| {7}

(13,2.11 ) pūrvāparaṃ carato māyayaitau śiśū krīḍantau pari yāto'rṇavam |
(13,2.11 ) viśvānyo bhuvanā vicaṣṭe hairaṇyairanyaṃ harito vahanti ||11||

(13,2.12 ) divi tvāttriradhārayatsūryā māsāya kartave |
(13,2.12 ) sa eṣi sudhṛtastapan viśvā bhūtāvacākaśat||12||

(13,2.13 ) ubhāvantau samarṣasi vatsaḥ saṃmātarāviva |
(13,2.13 ) nanvetaditaḥ purā brahma devā amī viduḥ ||13||

(13,2.14 ) yatsamudramanu śritaṃ tatsiṣāsati sūryaḥ |
(13,2.14 ) adhvāsya vitato mahān pūrvaścāparaśca yaḥ ||14||

(13,2.15 ) taṃ samāpnoti jūtibhistato nāpa cikitsati |
(13,2.15 ) tenāmṛtasya bhakṣaṃ devānāṃ nāva rundhate ||15||

(13,2.16 ) udu tyaṃ jātavedasaṃ devaṃ vahanti ketavaḥ |
(13,2.16 ) dṛśe viśvāya sūryam ||16||

(13,2.17 ) apa tye tāyavo yathā nakṣatrā yantyaktubhiḥ |
(13,2.17 ) sūrāya viśvacakṣase ||17||

(13,2.18 ) adṛśrann asya ketavo vi raśmayo janāmanu |
(13,2.18 ) bhrājanto agnayo yathā ||18||

(13,2.19 ) taraṇirviśvadarśato jyotiṣkṛdasi sūrya |
(13,2.19 ) viśvamā bhāsi rocana ||19||

(13,2.20 ) pratyaṅdevānāṃ viśaḥ pratyaṅṅudeṣi mānuṣīḥ |
(13,2.20 ) pratyaṅviśvaṃ svardṛśe ||20|| {8}

(13,2.21 ) yenā pāvaka cakṣasā bhuraṇyantaṃ janāmanu |
(13,2.21 ) tvaṃ varuṇa paśyasi ||21||

(13,2.22 ) vi dyāmeṣi rajaspṛthvaharmimāno aktubhiḥ |
(13,2.22 ) paśyan janmāni sūrya ||22||

(13,2.23 ) sapta tvā harito rathe vahanti deva sūrya |
(13,2.23 ) śociṣkeśaṃ vicakṣaṇam ||23||

(13,2.24 ) ayukta sapta śundhyuvaḥ sūro rathasya naptyaḥ |
(13,2.24 ) tābhiryāti svayuktibhiḥ ||24||

(13,2.25 ) rohito divamāruhattapasā tapasvī |
(13,2.25 ) sa yonimaiti sa u jāyate punaḥ sa devānāmadhipatirbabhūva ||25||

(13,2.26 ) yo viśvacarṣaṇiruta viśvatomukho yo viśvataspāṇiruta viśvataspṛthaḥ |
(13,2.26 ) saṃ bāhubhyāṃ bharati saṃ patatrairdyāvāpṛthivī janayan deva ekaḥ ||26||

(13,2.27 ) ekapāddvipado bhūyo vi cakrame dvipāttripādamabhyeti paścāt|
(13,2.27 ) dvipāddha ṣaṭpado bhūyo vi cakrame ta ekapadastanvaṃ samāsate ||27||

(13,2.28 ) atandro yāsyan harito yadāsthāddve rūpe kṛṇute rocamānaḥ |
(13,2.28 ) ketumān udyantsahamāno rajāṃsi viśvā āditya pravato vi bhāsi ||28||

(13,2.29 ) baṇmahāmasi sūrya baḍāditya mahāmasi |
(13,2.29 ) mahāṃste mahato mahimā tvamāditya mahāmasi ||29||

(13,2.30 ) rocase divi rocase antarikṣe pataṅga pṛthivyāṃ rocase rocase apsvantaḥ |
(13,2.30 ) ubhā samudrau rucyā vyāpitha devo devāsi mahiṣaḥ svarjit||30|| {9}

(13,2.31 ) arvāṅparastātprayato vyadhva āśurvipaścitpatayan pataṅgaḥ |
(13,2.31 ) viṣṇurvicittaḥ śavasādhitiṣṭhan pra ketunā sahate viśvamejat||31||

(13,2.32 ) citrāścikitvān mahiṣaḥ suparṇa ārocayan rodasī antarikṣam |
(13,2.32 ) ahorātre pari sūryaṃ vasāne prāsya viśvā tirato vīryāṇi ||32||

(13,2.33 ) tigmo vibhrājan tanvaṃ śiśāno'raṃgamāsaḥ pravato rarāṇaḥ |
(13,2.33 ) jyotiṣmān pakṣī mahiṣo vayodhā viśvā āsthātpradiśaḥ kalpamānaḥ ||33||

(13,2.34 ) citraṃ devānāṃ keturanīkaṃ jyotiṣmān pradiśaḥ sūrya udyan |
(13,2.34 ) divākaro'ti dyumnaistamāṃsi viśvātārīdduritāni śukraḥ ||34||

(13,2.35 ) citraṃ devānāmudagādanīkaṃ cakṣurmitrasya varuṇasyāgneḥ |
(13,2.35 ) āprāddyāvāpṛthivī antarikṣaṃ sūrya ātmā jagatastasthuṣaśca ||35||

(13,2.36 ) uccā patantamaruṇaṃ suparṇaṃ madhye divastaraṇiṃ bhrājamānam |
(13,2.36 ) paśyama tvā savitāraṃ yamāhurajasraṃ jyotiryadavindadattriḥ ||36||

(13,2.37 ) divaspṛṣṭhe dhāvamānaṃ suparṇamadityāḥ putraṃ nāthakāma upa yāmi bhītaḥ |
(13,2.37 ) sa naḥ sūrya pra tira dīrghamāyurmā riṣāma sumatau te syāma ||37||

(13,2.38 ) sahasrāhṇyaṃ viyatāvasya pakṣau harerhaṃsasya patataḥ svargam |
(13,2.38 ) sa devāntsarvān urasyupadadya saṃpaśyan yāti bhuvanāni viśvā ||38||

(13,2.39 ) rohitaḥ kālo abhavadrohito'gre prajāpatiḥ |
(13,2.39 ) rohito yajñānāṃ mukhaṃ rohitaḥ svarābharat||39||

(13,2.40 ) rohito loko abhavadrohito'tyatapaddivam |
(13,2.40 ) rohito raśmibhirbhūmiṃ samudramanu saṃ carat||40|| {10}

(13,2.41 ) sarvā diśaḥ samacaradrohito'dhipatirdivaḥ |
(13,2.41 ) divaṃ samudramādbhūmiṃ sarvaṃ bhūtaṃ vi rakṣati ||41||

(13,2.42 ) ārohan chukro bṛhatīratandro dve rūpe kṛṇute rocamānaḥ |
(13,2.42 ) citraścikitvān mahiṣo vātamāyā yāvato lokān abhi yadvibhāti ||42||

(13,2.43 ) abhyanyadeti paryanyadasyate'horātrābhyāṃ mahiṣaḥ kalpamānaḥ |
(13,2.43 ) sūryaṃ vayaṃ rajasi kṣiyantaṃ gātuvidaṃ havāmahe nādhamānāḥ ||43||

(13,2.44 ) pṛthivīpro mahiṣo nādhamānasya gāturadabdhacakṣuḥ pari viśvaṃ babhūva |
(13,2.44 ) viśvaṃ saṃpaśyantsuvidatro yajatra idaṃ śṛṇotu yadahaṃ bravīmi ||44||

(13,2.45 ) paryasya mahimā pṛthivīṃ samudraṃ jyotiṣā vibhrājan pari dyāmantarikṣam |
(13,2.45 ) sarvaṃ saṃpaśyantsuvidatro yajatra idaṃ śṛṇotu yadahaṃ bravīmi ||45||

(13,2.46 ) abodhyagniḥ samidhā janānāṃ prati dhenumivāyatīmuṣasam |
(13,2.46 ) yahvā iva pra vayāmujjihānāḥ pra bhānavaḥ sisrate nākamacha ||46|| {11}



(13,3.1 ) ya ime dyāvāpṛthivī jajāna yo drāpiṃ kṛtvā bhuvanāni vaste |
(13,3.1 ) yasmin kṣiyanti pradiśaḥ ṣaḍurvīryāḥ pataṅgo anu vicākaśīti |
(13,3.1 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13,3.2 ) yasmādvātā ṛtuthā pavante yasmātsamudrā adhi vikṣaranti |
(13,3.2 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13,3.2 ) udvepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān ||2||

(13,3.3 ) yo mārayati prāṇayati yasmātprāṇanti bhuvanāni viśvā |
(13,3.3 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13,3.3 ) udvepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān ||3||

(13,3.4 ) yaḥ prāṇena dyāvāpṛthivī tarpayatyapānena samudrasya jaṭharaṃ yaḥ piparti |
(13,3.4 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13,3.4 ) udvepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān ||4||

(13,3.5 ) yasmin virāṭparameṣṭhī prajāpatiragnirvaiśvānaraḥ saha paṅktyā śritaḥ |
(13,3.5 ) yaḥ parasya prāṇaṃ paramasya teja ādade |
(13,3.5 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13,3.6 ) yasmin ṣaḍurvīḥ pañca diśo adhiśritāścatasra āpo yajñasya trayo'kṣarāḥ |
(13,3.6 ) yo antarā rodasī kruddhaścakṣuṣaikṣata |
(13,3.6 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13,3.7 ) yo annādo annapatirbabhūva brahmaṇaspatiruta yaḥ |
(13,3.7 ) bhūto bhaviṣyatbhuvanasya yaspatiḥ |
(13,3.7 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13,3.8 ) ahorātrairvimitaṃ triṃśadaṅgaṃ trayodaśaṃ māsaṃ yo nirmimīte |
(13,3.8 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13,3.8 ) udvepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān ||8||

(13,3.9 ) kṛsṇaṃ niyānaṃ harayaḥ suparṇā apo vasānā divamutpatanti |
(13,3.9 ) ta āvavṛtrantsadanādṛtasya |
(13,3.9 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13,3.10 ) yatte candraṃ kaśyapa rocanāvadyatsaṃhitaṃ puṣkalaṃ citrabhānu yasmintsūryā ārpitāḥ sapta sākam |
(13,3.10 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13,3.10 ) udvepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān ||10|| {12}

(13,3.11 ) bṛhadenamanu vaste purastādrathaṃtaraṃ prati gṛhṇāti paścāt|
(13,3.11 ) jyotirvasāne sadamapramādam |
(13,3.11 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13,3.12 ) bṛhadanyataḥ pakṣa āsīdrathaṃtaramanyataḥ sabale sadhrīcī |
(13,3.12 ) yadrohitamajanayanta devāḥ |
(13,3.12 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13,3.13 ) sa varuṇaḥ sāyamagnirbhavati sa mitro bhavati prātarudyan |
(13,3.13 ) sa savitā bhūtvāntarikṣeṇa yāti sa indro bhūtvā tapati madhyato divam |
(13,3.13 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13,3.14 ) sahasrāhṇyaṃ viyatāvasya pakṣau harerhaṃsasya patataḥ svargam |
(13,3.14 ) sa devāntsarvān urasyupadadya saṃpaśyan yāti bhuvanāni viśvā |
(13,3.14 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13,3.15 ) ayaṃ sa devo apsvantaḥ sahasramūlaḥ paruśāko attriḥ |
(13,3.15 ) ya idaṃ viśvaṃ bhuvanaṃ jajāna |
(13,3.15 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13,3.16 ) śukraṃ vahanti harayo raghuṣyado devaṃ divi varcasā bhrājamānam |
(13,3.16 ) yasyordhvā divaṃ tanvastapantyarvāṅsuvarṇaiḥ paṭarairvi bhāti |
(13,3.16 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13,3.17 ) yenādityān haritaḥ samvahanti yena yajñena bahavo yanti prajānantaḥ |
(13,3.17 ) yadekaṃ jyotirbahudhā vibhāti |
(13,3.17 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13,3.18 ) sapta yuñjanti rathamekacakrameko aśvo vahati saptanāmā |
(13,3.18 ) trinābhi cakramajaramanarvaṃ yatremā viśvā bhuvanādhi tasthuḥ |
(13,3.18 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13,3.19 ) aṣṭadhā yukto vahati vahnirugraḥ pitā devānāṃ janitā matīnām |
(13,3.19 ) ṛtasya tantuṃ manasā mimānaḥ sarvā diśaḥ pavate mātariśvā |
(13,3.19 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13,3.20 ) saṃyañcaṃ tantuṃ pradiśo'nu sarvā antargāyatryāmamṛtasya garbhe |
(13,3.20 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13,3.20 ) udvepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān ||20|| {13}

(13,3.21 ) nimrucastisro vyuṣo ha tisrastrīṇi rajāṃsi divo aṅga tisraḥ |
(13,3.21 ) vidmā te agne tredhā janitraṃ tredhā devānāṃ janimāni vidma |
(13,3.21 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13,3.22 ) vi ya aurṇotpṛthivīṃ jāyamāna ā samudramadadhātantarikṣe |
(13,3.22 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13,3.22 ) udvepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān ||22||

(13,3.23 ) tvamagne kratubhiḥ ketubhirhito'rkaḥ samiddha udarocathā divi |
(13,3.23 ) kimabhyārcan marutaḥ pṛśnimātaro yadrohitamajanayanta devāḥ |
(13,3.23 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13,3.24 ) ya ātmadā baladā yasya viśva upāsate praśiṣaṃ yasya devāḥ |
(13,3.24 ) yo'syeśe dvipado yaścatuṣpadaḥ |
(13,3.24 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13,3.25 ) ekapāddvipado bhūyo vi cakrame dvipāttripādamabhyeti paścāt|
(13,3.25 ) catuṣpāccakre dvipadāmabhisvare saṃpaśyan paṅktimupatiṣṭhamānaḥ |
(13,3.25 ) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitadāgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13,3.26 ) kṛṣṇāyaḥ putro arjuno rātryā vatso'jāyata |
(13,3.26 ) sa ha dyāmadhi rohati ruho ruroha rohitaḥ ||26|| {14}


(13,4.26 ) sa eti savitā svardivaspṛṣṭhe'vacākaśat||1||

(13,4.2 ) raśmibhirnabha ābhṛtaṃ mahendra etyāvṛtaḥ ||2||

(13,4.3 ) sa dhātā sa vidhartā sa vāyurnabha ucchritam ||3||

(13,4.4 ) so'ryamā sa varuṇaḥ sa rudraḥ sa mahādevaḥ ||4||

(13,4.5 ) so agniḥ sa u sūryaḥ sa u eva mahāyamaḥ ||5||

(13,4.6 ) taṃ vatsā upa tiṣṭhantyekaśīrṣāṇo yutā daśa ||6||

(13,4.7 ) paścātprāñca ā tanvanti yadudeti vi bhāsati ||7||

(13,4.8 ) tasyaiṣa māruto gaṇaḥ sa eti śikyākṛtaḥ ||8||
