(13,4.9 ) raśmibhirnabha ābhṛtaṃ mahendra etyāvṛtaḥ ||9||

(13,4.10 ) tasyeme nava kośā viṣṭambhā navadhā hitāḥ ||10||

(13,4.11 ) sa prajābhyo vi paśyati yacca prāṇati yacca na ||11||

(13,4.12 ) tamidaṃ nigataṃ sahaḥ sa eṣa eka ekavṛdeka eva ||12||

(13,4.13 ) ete asmin devā ekavṛto bhavanti ||13|| {15}

(13,4.14 ) kīrtiśca yaśaścāmbhaśca nabhaśca brāhmaṇavarcasaṃ cānnaṃ cānnādyaṃ ca ||14||

(13,4.15 ) ya etaṃ devamekavṛtaṃ veda ||15||

(13,4.16 ) na dvitīyo na tṛtīyaścaturtho nāpyucyate ||16||

(13,4.17 ) na pañcamo na ṣaṣṭhaḥ saptamo nāpyucyate ||17||

(13,4.18 ) nāṣṭamo na navamo daśamo nāpyucyate ||18||

(13,4.19 ) sa sarvasmai vi paśyati yacca prāṇati yacca na ||19||

(13,4.20 ) tamidaṃ nigataṃ sahaḥ sa eṣa eka ekavṛdeka eva ||20||

(13,4.21 ) sarve asmin devā ekavṛto bhavanti ||21|| {16}

(13,4.22 ) brahma ca tapaśca kīrtiśca yaśaścāmbhaśca nabhaśca brāhmaṇavarcasaṃ cānnaṃ cānnādyaṃ ca ||2||

(13,4.23 ) bhūtaṃ ca bhavyaṃ ca śraddhā ca ruciśca svargaśca svadhā ca ||23||

(13,4.24 ) ya etaṃ devamekavṛtaṃ veda ||24||

(13,4.25 ) sa eva mṛtyuḥ so'mṛtaṃ so'bhvaṃ sa rakṣaḥ ||25||

(13,4.26 ) sa rudro vasuvanirvasudeye namovāke vaṣaṭkāro'nu saṃhitaḥ ||26||

(13,4.27 ) tasyeme sarve yātava upa praśiṣamāsate ||27||

(13,4.28 ) tasyāmū sarvā nakṣatrā vaśe candramasā saha ||28|| {17}

(13,4.29 ) sa vā ahno'jāyata tasmādaharajāyata ||29||

(13,4.30 ) sa vai rātryā ajāyata tasmādrātrirajāyata ||30||

(13,4.31 ) sa vā antarikṣādajāyata tasmādantarikṣamajāyata ||31||

(13,4.32 ) sa vai vāyorajāyata tasmādvāyurajāyata ||32||

(13,4.33 ) sa vai divo'jāyata tasmāddyauradhi ajāyata ||33||

(13,4.34 ) sa vai digbhyo'jāyata tasmāddiśo'jāyanta ||34||

(13,4.35 ) sa vai bhūmerajāyata tasmādbhūmirajāyata ||35||

(13,4.36 ) sa vā agnerajāyata tasmādagnirajāyata ||36||

(13,4.37 ) sa vā adbhyo'jāyata tasmādāpo'jāyanta ||37||

(13,4.38 ) sa vā ṛgbhyo'jāyata tasmādṛco'jāyanta ||38||

(13,4.39 ) sa vai yajñādajāyata tasmādyajño'jāyata ||39||

(13,4.40 ) sa yajñastasya yajñaḥ sa yajñasya śiraskṛtam ||40||

(13,4.41 ) sa stanayati sa vi dyotate sa u aśmānamasyati ||41||

(13,4.42 ) pāpāya vā bhadrāya vā puruṣāyāsurāya vā ||42||

(13,4.43 ) yadvā kṛṇoṣyoṣadhīryadvā varṣasi bhadrayā yadvā janyamavīvṛdhaḥ ||43||

(13,4.44 ) tāvāṃste maghavan mahimopo te tanvaḥ śatam ||44||

(13,4.45 ) upo te badhve baddhāni yadi vāsi nyarbudam ||45|| {18}

(13,4.46 ) bhūyān indro namurādbhūyān indrāsi mṛtyubhyaḥ ||46||

(13,4.47 ) bhūyān arātyāḥ śacyāḥ patistvamindrāsi vibhūḥ prabhūriti tvopāsmahe vayam ||47||

(13,4.48 ) namaste astu paśyata paśya mā paśyata ||48||

(13,4.49 ) annādyena yaśasā tejasā brāhmaṇavarcasena ||49||

(13,4.50 ) ambho amo mahaḥ saha iti tvopāsmahe vayam ||50||

(13,4.51 ) ambho aruṇaṃ rajataṃ rajaḥ saha iti tvopāsmahe vayam ||51|| {19}

(13,4.52 ) uruḥ pṛthuḥ subhūrbhuva iti tvopāsmahe vayam ||52||

(13,4.53 ) pratho varo vyaco loka iti tvopāsmahe vayam ||53||

(13,4.54 ) bhavadvasuridadvasuḥ saṃyadvasurāyadvasuriti tvopāsmahe vayam ||54||

(13,4.55 ) namaste astu paśyata paśya mā paśyata ||55||

(13,4.56 ) annādyena yaśasā tejasā brāhmaṇavarcasena ||56|| {20}



(14,1.1 ) satyenottabhitā bhūmiḥ sūryeṇottabhitā dyauḥ |
(14,1.1 ) ṛtenādityāstiṣṭhanti divi somo adhi śritaḥ ||1||

(14,1.2 ) somenādityā balinaḥ somena pṛthivī mahī |
(14,1.2 ) atho nakṣatrāṇāmeṣāmupasthe soma āhitaḥ ||2||

(14,1.3 ) somaṃ manyate papivān yatsaṃpiṃṣantyoṣadhim |
(14,1.3 ) somaṃ yaṃ brahmāṇo vidurna tasyāśnāti pārthivaḥ ||3||

(14,1.4 ) yattvā soma prapibanti tata ā pyāyase punaḥ |
(14,1.4 ) vāyuḥ somasya rakṣitā samānāṃ māsa ākṛtiḥ ||4||

(14,1.5 ) āchadvidhānairgupito bārhataiḥ soma rakṣitaḥ |
(14,1.5 ) grāvṇāmicchṛṇvan tiṣṭhasi na te aśnāti pārthivaḥ ||5||

(14,1.6 ) cittirā upabarhaṇaṃ cakṣurā abhyañjanam |
(14,1.6 ) dyaurbhūmiḥ kośa āsīdyadayātsūryā patim ||6||

(14,1.7 ) raibhyāsīdanudeyī nārāśaṃsī nyocanī |
(14,1.7 ) suryāyā bhadramidvāso gāthayati pariṣkṛtā ||7||

(14,1.8 ) stomā āsan pratidhayaḥ kurīraṃ chanda opaśaḥ |
(14,1.8 ) sūryāyā aśvinā varāgnirāsītpurogavaḥ ||8||

(14,1.9 ) somo vadhūyurabhavadaśvināstāmubhā varā |
(14,1.9 ) sūryāṃ yatpatye śaṃsantīṃ manasā savitādadāt||9||

(14,1.10 ) mano asyā ana āsīddyaurāsīduta chadiḥ |
(14,1.10 ) śukrāvanaḍvāhāvāstāṃ yadayātsūryā patim ||10|| {1}

(14,1.11 ) ṛksāmābhyāmabhihitau gāvau te sāmanāvaitām |
(14,1.11 ) śrotre te cakre āstāṃ divi panthāścarācaraḥ ||11||

(14,1.12 ) śucī te cakre yātyā vyāno akṣa āhataḥ |
(14,1.12 ) ano manasmayaṃ sūryārohatprayatī patim ||12||

(14,1.13 ) sūryāyā vahatuḥ prāgātsavitā yamavāsṛjat|
(14,1.13 ) maghāsu hanyante gāvaḥ phalgunīṣu vyuhyate ||13||

(14,1.14 ) yadaśvinā pṛchamānāvayātaṃ tricakreṇa vahatuṃ sūryāyāḥ |
(14,1.14 ) kvaikaṃ cakraṃ vāmāsītkva deṣṭrāya tasthathuḥ ||14||

(14,1.15 ) yadayātaṃ śubhaspatī vareyaṃ sūryāmupa |
(14,1.15 ) viśve devā anu tadvāmajānan putraḥ pitaramavṛṇīta pūṣā ||15||

(14,1.16 ) dve te cakre sūrye brahmāṇa ṛtuthā viduḥ |
(14,1.16 ) athaikaṃ cakraṃ yadguhā tadaddhātaya idviduḥ ||16||

(14,1.17 ) aryamaṇaṃ yajāmahe subandhuṃ pativedanam |
(14,1.17 ) urvārukamiva bandhanātpreto muñcāmi nāmutaḥ ||17||

(14,1.18 ) preto muñcāmi nāmutaḥ subaddhāmamutaskaram |
(14,1.18 ) yatheyamindra mīḍhvaḥ suputrā subhagāsati ||18||

(14,1.19 ) pra tvā muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśādyena tvābadhnātsavitā suśevāḥ |
(14,1.19 ) ṛtasya yonau sukṛtasya loke syonaṃ te astu sahasaṃbhalāyai ||19||
(14,1.20 ) bhagastveto nayatu hastagṛhyāśvinā tvā pra vahatāṃ rathena |
(14,1.20 ) gṛhān gacha gṛhapatnī yathāso vaśinī tvaṃ vidathamā vadāsi ||20|| {2}

(14,1.21 ) iha priyaṃ prajāyai te samṛdhyatāmasmin gṛhe gārhapatyāya jāgṛhi |
(14,1.21 ) enā patyā tanvaṃ saṃ spṛśasvātha jirvirvidathamā vadāsi ||21||

(14,1.22 ) ihaiva staṃ mā vi yauṣṭaṃ viśvamāyurvyaśnutam |
(14,1.22 ) krīḍantau putrairnaptṛbhirmodamānau svastakau ||22||

(14,1.23 ) pūrvāparaṃ carato māyaitau śiśū krīḍantau pari yāto'rṇavam |
(14,1.23 ) viśvānyo bhuvanā vicaṣṭa ṛtūṃranyo vidadhajjāyase navaḥ ||23||

(14,1.24 ) navonavo bhavasi jāyamāno'hnāṃ keturuṣasāmeṣyagram |
(14,1.24 ) bhāgaṃ devebhyo vi dadhāsyāyan pra candramastirase dīrghamāyuḥ ||24||
(14,1.25 ) parā dehi śāmulyaṃ brahmabhyo vi bhajā vasu |
(14,1.25 ) kṛtyaiṣā padvatī bhūtvā jāyā viśate patim ||25||

(14,1.26 ) nīlalohitaṃ bhavati kṛtyāsaktirvyajyate |
(14,1.26 ) edhante asyā jñātayaḥ patirbandheṣu badhyate ||26||

(14,1.27 ) aślīlā tanūrbhavati ruśatī pāpayāmuyā |
(14,1.27 ) patiryadvadhvo vāsasaḥ svamaṅgamabhyūrṇute ||27||

(14,1.28 ) āśasanaṃ viśasanamatho adhivikartanam |
(14,1.28 ) sūryāyāḥ paśya rūpāṇi tāni brahmota śumbhati ||28||

(14,1.29 ) tṛṣṭametatkaṭukamapāṣṭhavadviṣavan naitadattave |
(14,1.29 ) sūryāṃ yo brahmā veda sa idvādhūyamarhati ||29||

(14,1.30 ) sa ittatsyonaṃ harati brahmā vāsaḥ sumaṅgalam |
(14,1.30 ) prāyaścittiṃ yo adhyeti yena jāyā na riṣyati ||30|| {3}

(14,1.31 ) yuvaṃ bhagaṃ saṃ bharataṃ samṛddhamṛtaṃ vadantāvṛtodyeṣu |
(14,1.31 ) brahmaṇaspate patimasyai rocaya cāru saṃbhalo vadatu vācametām ||31||

(14,1.32 ) ihedasātha na paro gamāthemaṃ gāvaḥ prajayā vardhayātha |
(14,1.32 ) śubhaṃ yatīrusriyāḥ somavarcaso viśve devāḥ krann iha vo manāṃsi ||32||

(14,1.33 ) imaṃ gāvaḥ prajayā saṃ viśāthāyaṃ devānāṃ na mināti bhāgam |
(14,1.33 ) asmai vaḥ pūṣā marutaśca sarve asmai vo dhātā savitā suvāti ||33||

(14,1.34 ) anṛkṣarā ṛjavaḥ santu panthano yebhiḥ sakhāyo yanti no vareyam |
(14,1.34 ) saṃ bhagena samaryamṇā saṃ dhātā sṛjatu varcasā ||34||

(14,1.35 ) yacca varco akṣeṣu surāyāṃ ca yadāhitam |
(14,1.35 ) yadgoṣvaśvinā varcastenemāṃ varcasāvatam ||35||

(14,1.36 ) yena mahānaghnyā jaghanamaśvinā yena vā surā |
(14,1.36 ) yenākṣā abhyaṣicyanta tenemāṃ varcasāvatam ||36||

(14,1.37 ) yo anidhmo dīdayadapsvantaryaṃ viprāsa īḍate adhvareṣu |
(14,1.37 ) apāṃ napān madhumatīrapo dā yābhirindro vāvṛdhe vīryāvān ||37||

(14,1.38 ) idamahaṃ ruśantaṃ grābhaṃ tanūdūṣimapohāmi |
(14,1.38 ) yo bhadro rocanastamudacāmi ||38||

(14,1.39 ) āsyai brāhmaṇāḥ snapanīrharantvavīraghnīrudajantvāpaḥ |
(14,1.39 ) aryamṇo agniṃ paryetu pūṣan pratīkṣante śvaśuro devaraśca ||39||

(14,1.40 ) śaṃ te hiraṇyaṃ śamu santvāpaḥ śaṃ methirbhavatu śaṃ yugasya tardma |
(14,1.40 ) śaṃ ta āpaḥ śatapavitrā bhavantu śamu patyā tanvaṃ saṃ spṛśasva ||40|| {4}

(14,1.41 ) khe rathasya khe'nasaḥ khe yugasya śatakrato |
(14,1.41 ) apālāmindra triṣpūtvākṛṇoḥ sūryatvacam ||41||

(14,1.42 ) āśāsānā saumanasaṃ prajāṃ saubhāgyaṃ rayim |
(14,1.42 ) patyuranuvratā bhūtvā saṃ nahyasvāmṛtāya kam ||42||

(14,1.43 ) yathā sindhurnadīnāṃ sāmrājyaṃ suṣuve vṛṣā |
(14,1.43 ) evā tvaṃ samrājñyedhi patyurastaṃ paretya ||43||

(14,1.44 ) samrājñyedhi śvaśureṣu samrājñyuta devṛṣu |
(14,1.44 ) nanānduḥ samrājñyedhi samrājñyuta śvaśrvāḥ ||44||

(14,1.45 ) yā akṛntann avayan yāśca tatnire yā devīrantāmabhito'dadanta |
(14,1.45 ) tāstvā jarase saṃ vyayantvāyuṣmatīdaṃ pari dhatsva vāsaḥ ||45||

(14,1.46 ) jīvaṃ rudanti vi nayantyadhvaraṃ dīrghāmanu prasitiṃ dīdhyurnaraḥ |
(14,1.46 ) vāmaṃ pitṛbhyo ya idaṃ samīrire mayaḥ patibhyo janaye pariṣvaje ||46||

(14,1.47 ) syonaṃ dhruvaṃ prajāyai dhārayāmi te'śmānaṃ devyāḥ pṛthivyā upasthe |
(14,1.47 ) tamā tiṣṭhānumādyā suvarcā dīrghaṃ ta āyuḥ savitā kṛṇotu ||47||

(14,1.48 ) yenāgnirasyā bhūmyā hastaṃ jagrāha dakṣiṇam |
(14,1.48 ) tena gṛhṇāmi te hastaṃ mā vyathiṣṭhā mayā saha prajayā ca dhanena ca ||48||

(14,1.49 ) devaste savitā hastaṃ gṛhṇātu somo rājā suprajasaṃ kṛṇotu |
(14,1.49 ) agniḥ subhagāṃ jatavedāḥ patye patnīṃ jaradaṣṭiṃ kṛṇotu ||49||

(14,1.50 ) gṛhṇāmi te saubhagatvāya hastaṃ mayā patyā jaradaṣṭiryathāsaḥ |
(14,1.50 ) bhago aryamā savitā puraṃdhirmahyaṃ tvādurgārhapatyāya devāḥ ||50|| {5}
(14,1.51 ) bhagaste hastamagrahītsavitā hastamagrahīt|
(14,1.51 ) patnī tvamasi dharmaṇāhaṃ gṛhapatistava ||51||

(14,1.52 ) mameyamastu poṣyā mahyaṃ tvādādbṛhaspatiḥ |
(14,1.52 ) mayā patyā prajāvati saṃ jīva śaradaḥ śatam ||52||

(14,1.53 ) tvaṣṭā vāso vyadadhācchubhe kaṃ bṛhaspateḥ praśiṣā kavīnām |
(14,1.53 ) tenemāṃ nārīṃ savitā bhagaśca sūryāmiva pari dhattāṃ prajayā ||53||

(14,1.54 ) indrāgnī dyāvāpṛthivī mātariśvā mitrāvaruṇā bhago aśvinobhā |
(14,1.54 ) bṛhaspatirmaruto brahma soma imāṃ nāriṃ prajayā vardhayantu ||54||
(14,1.55 ) bṛhaspatiḥ prathamaḥ sūryāyāḥ śīrṣe keśāmakalpayat|
(14,1.55 ) tenemāmaśvinā nārīṃ patye saṃ śobhayāmasi ||55||

(14,1.56 ) idaṃ tadrūpaṃ yadavasta yoṣā jāyāṃ jijñāse manasā carantīm |
(14,1.56 ) tāmanvartiṣye sakhibhirnavagvaiḥ ka imān vidvān vi cacarta pāśān ||56||

(14,1.57 ) ahaṃ vi ṣyāmi mayi rūpamasyā vedaditpaśyan manasaḥ kulāyam |
(14,1.57 ) na steyamadmi manasodamucye svayaṃ śrathnāno varuṇasya pāśān ||57||

(14,1.58 ) pra tvā muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśādyena tvābadhnātsavitā suśevāḥ |
(14,1.58 ) uruṃ lokaṃ sugamatra panthāṃ kṛṇomi tubhyaṃ sahapatnyai vadhu ||58||

(14,1.59 ) udyachadhvamapa rakṣo hanāthemaṃ nārīṃ sukṛte dadhāta |
(14,1.59 ) dhātā vipaścitpatimasyai viveda bhago rājā pura etu prajānan ||59||

(14,1.60 ) bhagastatakṣa caturaḥ pādān bhagastatakṣa catvāryuṣpalāni |
(14,1.60 ) tvaṣṭā pipeśa madhyato'nu vardhrāntsā no astu sumaṅgalī ||60||

(14,1.61 ) sukiṃśukaṃ vahatuṃ viśvarūpaṃ hiraṇyavarṇaṃ suvṛtaṃ sucakram |
(14,1.61 ) ā roha sūrye amṛtasya lokaṃ syonaṃ patibhyo vahatuṃ kṛṇu tvam ||61||

(14,1.62 ) abhrātṛghnīṃ varuṇāpaśughnīṃ bṛhaspate |
(14,1.62 ) indrāpatighnīṃ putriṇīmāsmabhyaṃ savitarvaha ||62||

(14,1.63 ) mā hiṃsiṣṭaṃ kumāryaṃ sthūṇe devakṛte pathi |
(14,1.63 ) śālāyā devyā dvāraṃ syonaṃ kṛṇmo vadhūpatham ||63||

(14,1.64 ) brahmāparaṃ yujyatāṃ brahma pūrvaṃ brahmāntato madhyato brahma sarvataḥ |
(14,1.64 ) anāvyādhāṃ devapurāṃ prapadya śivā syonā patiloke vi rāja ||64|| {6}



(14,2.1 ) tubhyamagre paryavahantsūryāṃ vahatunā saha |
(14,2.1 ) sa naḥ patibhyo jāyāṃ dā agne prajayā saha ||1||

(14,2.2 ) punaḥ patnīmagniradādāyuṣā saha varcasā |
(14,2.2 ) dīrghāyurasyā yaḥ patirjīvāti śaradaḥ śatam ||2||

(14,2.3 ) somasya jāyā prathamaṃ gandharvaste'paraḥ patiḥ |
(14,2.3 ) tṛtīyo agniṣṭe patisturīyaste manuṣyajāḥ ||3||

(14,2.4 ) somo dadadgandharvāya gandharvo dadadagnaye |
(14,2.4 ) rayiṃ ca putrāṃscādādagnirmahyamatho imām ||4||

(14,2.5 ) ā vāmagantsumatirvājinīvasū nyaśvinā hṛtsu kāmā araṃsata |
(14,2.5 ) abhūtaṃ gopā mithunā śubhaspatī priyā aryamṇo duryāmaśīmahi ||5||
(14,2.6 ) sā mandasānā manasā śivena rayiṃ dhehi sarvavīraṃ vacasyam |
(14,2.6 ) sugaṃ tīrthaṃ suprapāṇaṃ śubhaspatī sthāṇuṃ pathiṣṭhāmapa durmatiṃ hatam ||6||

(14,2.7 ) yā oṣadhayo yā nadyo yāni kṣetrāṇi yā vanā |
(14,2.7 ) tāstvā vadhu prajāvatīṃ patye rakṣantu rakṣasaḥ ||7||

(14,2.8 ) emaṃ panthāmarukṣāma sugaṃ svastivāhanam |
(14,2.8 ) yasmin vīro na riṣyatyanyeṣāṃ vindate vasu ||8||

(14,2.9 ) idaṃ su me naraḥ śṛṇuta yayāśiṣā daṃpatī vāmamaśnutaḥ |
(14,2.9 ) ye gandharvā apsarasaśca devīreṣu vānaspatyeṣu ye'dhi tasthuḥ |
(14,2.9 ) syonāste asyai vadhvai bhavantu mā hiṃsiṣurvahatumuhyamānam ||9||

(14,2.10 ) ye vadhvaścandraṃ vahatuṃ yakṣmā yanti janāmanu |
(14,2.10 ) punastān yajñiyā devā nayantu yata āgatāḥ ||10|| {7}

(14,2.11 ) mā vidan paripanthino ya āsīdanti daṃpatī |
(14,2.11 ) sugena durgamatītāmapa drāntvarātayaḥ ||11||

(14,2.12 ) saṃ kāśayāmi vahatuṃ brahmaṇā gṛhairaghoreṇa cakṣuṣā mitriyeṇa |
(14,2.12 ) paryāṇaddhaṃ viśvarūpaṃ yadasti syonaṃ patibhyaḥ savitā tatkṛṇotu ||12||

(14,2.13 ) śivā nārīyamastamāgann imaṃ dhātā lokamasyai dideśa |
(14,2.13 ) tāmaryamā bhago aśvinobhā prajāpatiḥ prajayā vardhayantu ||13||

(14,2.14 ) ātmanvatyurvarā nārīyamāgan tasyāṃ naro vapata bījamasyām |
(14,2.14 ) sā vaḥ prajāṃ janayadvakṣaṇābhyo bibhratī dugdhamṛṣabhasya retaḥ ||14||

(14,2.15 ) prati tiṣṭha virāḍasi viṣṇuriveha sarasvati |
(14,2.15 ) sinīvāli pra jāyatāṃ bhagasya sumatāvasat||15||

(14,2.16 ) udva ūrmiḥ śamyā hantvāpo yoktrāṇi muñcata |
(14,2.16 ) māduṣkṛtau vyenasāvaghnyāvaśunamāratām ||16||

(14,2.17 ) aghoracakṣurapatighnī syonā śagmā suśevā suyamā gṛhebhyaḥ |
(14,2.17 ) vīrasūrdevṛkāmā saṃ tvayaidhiṣīmahi sumasyamānā ||17||

(14,2.18 ) adevṛghnyapatighnīhaidhi śivā paśubhyaḥ suyamā suvarcāḥ |
(14,2.18 ) prajāvatī vīrasūrdevṛkāmā syonemamagniṃ gārhapatyaṃ saparya ||18||

(14,2.19 ) uttiṣṭhetaḥ kimichantīdamāgā ahaṃ tveḍe abhibhūḥ svādgṛhāt|
(14,2.19 ) śūnyaiṣī nirṛte yājaganthottiṣṭhārāte pra pata meha raṃsthāḥ ||19||

(14,2.20 ) yadā gārhapatyamasaparyaitpūrvamagniṃ vadhūriyam |
(14,2.20 ) adhā sarasvatyai nāri pitṛbhyaśca namaskuru ||20|| {8}

(14,2.21 ) śarma varmaitadā harāsyai nāryā upastire |
(14,2.21 ) sinīvāli pra jāyatāṃ bhagasya sumatāvasat||21||

(14,2.22 ) yaṃ balbajaṃ nyasyatha carma copastṛṇīthana |
(14,2.22 ) tadā rohatu suprajā yā kanyā vindate patim ||22||

(14,2.23 ) upa stṛṇīhi balbajamadhi carmaṇi rohite |
(14,2.23 ) tatropaviśya suprajā imamagniṃ saparyatu ||23||

(14,2.24 ) ā roha carmopa sīdāgnimeṣa devo hanti rakṣāṃsi sarvā |
(14,2.24 ) iha prajāṃ janaya patye asmai sujyaiṣṭhyo bhavatputrasta eṣaḥ ||24||

(14,2.25 ) vi tiṣṭhantāṃ māturasyā upasthān nānārūpāḥ paśavo jāyamānāḥ |
(14,2.25 ) sumaṅgalyupa sīdemamagniṃ saṃpatnī prati bhūṣeha devān ||25||

(14,2.26 ) sumaṅgalī prataraṇī gṛhāṇāṃ suśevā patye śvaśurāya śaṃbhūḥ |
(14,2.26 ) syonā śvaśrvai pra gṛhān viśemān ||26||

(14,2.27 ) syonā bhava śvaśurebhyaḥ syonā patye gṛhebhyaḥ |
(14,2.27 ) syonāsyai sarvasyai viśe syonā puṣṭāyaiṣāṃ bhava ||27||

(14,2.28 ) sumaṅgalīriyaṃ vadhūrimāṃ sameta paśyata |
(14,2.28 ) saubhāgyamasyai dattvā daurbhāgyairviparetana ||28||

(14,2.29 ) yā durhārdo yuvatayo yāśceha jaratīrapi |
(14,2.29 ) varco nvasyai saṃ dattāthāstaṃ viparetana ||29||

(14,2.30 ) rukmaprastaraṇaṃ vahyaṃ viśvā rūpāṇi bibhratam |
(14,2.30 ) ārohatsūryā sāvitrī bṛhate saubhagāya kam ||30|| {9}

(14,2.31 ) ā roha talpaṃ sumanasyamāneha prajāṃ janaya patye asmai |
(14,2.31 ) indrāṇīva subudhā budhyamānā jyotiragrā uṣasaḥ prati jāgarāsi ||31||

(14,2.32 ) devā agre nyapadyanta patnīḥ samaspṛśanta tanvastanūbhiḥ |
(14,2.32 ) sūryeva nāri viśvarūpā mahitvā prajāvatī patyā saṃ bhaveha ||32||

(14,2.33 ) uttiṣṭheto viśvāvaso namaseḍāmahe tvā |
(14,2.33 ) jāmimicha pitṛṣadaṃ nyaktāṃ sa te bhāgo januṣā tasya viddhi ||33||

(14,2.34 ) apsarasaḥ sadhamādaṃ madanti havirdhānamantarā sūryaṃ ca |
(14,2.34 ) tāste janitramabhi tāḥ parehi namaste gandharvartunā kṛṇomi ||34||

(14,2.35 ) namo gandharvasya namase namo bhāmāya cakṣuṣe ca kṛṇmaḥ |
(14,2.35 ) viśvāvaso brahmaṇā te namo'bhi jāyā apsarasaḥ parehi ||35||

(14,2.36 ) rāyā vayaṃ sumanasaḥ syāmodito gandharvamāvīvṛtāma |
(14,2.36 ) agantsa devaḥ paramaṃ sadhasthamaganma yatra pratiranta āyuḥ ||36||

(14,2.37 ) saṃ pitarāvṛtviye sṛjethāṃ mātā pitā ca retaso bhavāthaḥ |
(14,2.37 ) marya iva yoṣāmadhi rohayaināṃ prajāṃ kṛṇvāthāmiha puṣyataṃ rayim ||37||

(14,2.38 ) tāṃ pūṣaṃ chivatamāmerayasva yasyāṃ bījaṃ manuṣyā vapanti |
(14,2.38 ) yā na ūrū uśatī viśrayāti yasyāmuśantaḥ praharema śepaḥ ||38||

(14,2.39 ) ā rohorumupa dhatsva hastaṃ pari ṣvajasva jāyāṃ sumanasyamānaḥ |
(14,2.39 ) prajāṃ kṛṇvāthāmiha modamānau dīrghaṃ vāmāyuḥ savitā kṛṇotu ||39||

(14,2.40 ) ā vāṃ prajāṃ janayatu prajāpatirahorātrābhyāṃ samanaktvaryamā |
(14,2.40 ) adurmaṅgalī patilokamā viśemaṃ śaṃ no bhava dvipade śaṃ catuṣpade ||40|| {10}

(14,2.41 ) devairdattaṃ manunā sākametadvādhūyaṃ vāso vadhvaśca vastram |
(14,2.41 ) yo brahmaṇe cikituṣe dadāti sa idrakṣāṃsi talpāni hanti ||41||

(14,2.42 ) yaṃ me datto brahmabhāgaṃ vadhūyorvādhūyaṃ vāso vadhvaśca vastram |
(14,2.42 ) yuvaṃ brahmaṇe'numanyamānau bṛhaspate sākamindraśca dattam ||42||

(14,2.43 ) syonādyoneradhi badhyamānau hasāmudau mahasā modamānau |
(14,2.43 ) sugū suputrau sugṛhau tarātho jīvāvuṣaso vibhātīḥ ||43||

(14,2.44 ) navaṃ vasānaḥ surabhiḥ suvāsā udāgāṃ jīva uṣaso vibhātīḥ |
(14,2.44 ) āṇḍātpatatrīvāmukṣi viśvasmādenasaspari ||44||

(14,2.45 ) śumbhanī dyāvāpṛthivī antisumne mahivrate |
(14,2.45 ) āpaḥ sapta susruvurdevīstā no muñcantvaṃhasaḥ ||45||

(14,2.46 ) sūryāyai devebhyo mitrāya varuṇāya ca |
(14,2.46 ) ye bhūtasya pracetasastebhya idamakaraṃ namaḥ ||46||

(14,2.47 ) ya ṛte cidabhiśriṣaḥ purā jatrubhya ātṛdaḥ |
(14,2.47 ) saṃdhātā saṃdhiṃ maghavā purūvasurniṣkartā vihrutaṃ punaḥ ||47||

(14,2.48 ) apāsmattama uchatu nīlaṃ piśaṅgamuta lohitaṃ yat|
(14,2.48 ) nirdahanī yā pṛṣātakyasmin tāṃ sthāṇāvadhyā sajāmi ||48||

(14,2.49 ) yāvatīḥ kṛtyā upavāsane yāvanto rājño varuṇasya pāśāḥ |
(14,2.49 ) vyṛddhayo yā asamṛddhayo yā asmin tā sthāṇāvadhi sādayāmi ||49||

(14,2.50 ) yā me priyatamā tanūḥ sā me bibhāya vāsasaḥ |
(14,2.50 ) tasyāgre tvaṃ vanaspate nīviṃ kṛṇuṣva mā vayaṃ riṣāma ||50|| {11}

(14,2.51 ) ye antā yāvatīḥ sico ya otavo ye ca tantavaḥ |
(14,2.51 ) vāso yatpatnībhirutaṃ tan na syonamupa spṛśāt||51||

(14,2.52 ) uśatīḥ kanyalā imāḥ pitṛlokātpatiṃ yatīḥ |
(14,2.52 ) ava dīkṣāmasṛkṣata svāhā ||52||

(14,2.53 ) bṛhaspatināvasṛṣṭāṃ viśve devā adhārayan |
(14,2.53 ) varco goṣu praviṣṭaṃ yattenemāṃ saṃ sṛjāmasi ||53||

(14,2.54 ) bṛhaspatināvasṛṣṭāṃ viśve devā adhārayan |
(14,2.54 ) tejo goṣu praviṣṭaṃ yattenemāṃ saṃ sṛjāmasi ||54||

(14,2.55 ) bṛhaspatināvasṛṣṭāṃ viśve devā adhārayan |
(14,2.55 ) bhajo goṣu praviṣṭo yastenemāṃ saṃ sṛjāmasi ||55||

(14,2.56 ) bṛhaspatināvasṛṣṭāṃ viśve devā adhārayan |
(14,2.56 ) yaśo goṣu praviṣṭaṃ yattenemāṃ saṃ sṛjāmasi ||56||

(14,2.57 ) bṛhaspatināvasṛṣṭāṃ viśve devā adhārayan |
(14,2.57 ) payo goṣu praviṣṭaṃ yattenemāṃ saṃ sṛjāmasi ||57||

(14,2.58 ) bṛhaspatināvasṛṣṭāṃ viśve devā adhārayan |
(14,2.58 ) raso goṣu praviṣṭo yastenemāṃ saṃ sṛjāmasi ||58||

(14,2.59 ) yadīme keśino janā gṛhe te samanartiṣū rodena kṛṇvanto'gham |
(14,2.59 ) agniṣṭvā tasmādenasaḥ savitā ca pra muñcatām ||59||

(14,2.60 ) yadīyaṃ duhitā tava vikeśyarudadgṛhe rodena kṛṇvatyagham |
(14,2.60 ) agniṣṭvā tasmādenasaḥ savitā ca pra muñcatām ||60|| {12}

(14,2.61 ) yajjāmayo yadyuvatayo gṛhe te samanartiṣū rodena kṛṇvatīragham |
(14,2.61 ) agniṣṭvā tasmādenasaḥ savitā ca pra muñcatām ||61||

(14,2.62 ) yatte prajāyāṃ paśuṣu yadvā gṛheṣu niṣṭhitamaghakṛdbhiraghaṃ kṛtam |
(14,2.62 ) agniṣṭvā tasmādenasaḥ savitā ca pra muñcatām ||62||

(14,2.63 ) iyaṃ nāryupa brūte pūlyānyāvapantikā |
(14,2.63 ) dīrghāyurastu me patirjīvāti śaradaḥ śatam ||63||

(14,2.64 ) ihemāvindra saṃ nuda cakravākeva daṃpatī |
(14,2.64 ) prajayainau svastakau viśvamāyurvyaśnutām ||64||

(14,2.65 ) yadāsandyāmupadhāne yadvopavāsane kṛtam |
(14,2.65 ) vivāhe kṛtyāṃ yāṃ cakrurāsnāne tāṃ ni dadhmasi ||65||

(14,2.66 ) yadduṣkṛtaṃ yacchamalaṃ vivāhe vahatau ca yat|
(14,2.66 ) tatsaṃbhalasya kambale mṛjmahe duritaṃ vayam ||66||

(14,2.67 ) saṃbhale malaṃ sādayitvā kambale duritaṃ vayam |
(14,2.67 ) abhūma yajñiyāḥ śuddhāḥ pra ṇa āyūṃṣi tāriṣat||67||

(14,2.68 ) kṛtrimaḥ kaṇṭakaḥ śatadan ya eṣaḥ |
(14,2.68 ) apāsyāḥ keśyaṃ malamapa śīrṣaṇyaṃ likhāt||68||

(14,2.69 ) aṅgādaṅgādvayamasyā apa yakṣmaṃ ni dadhmasi |
(14,2.69 ) tan mā prāpatpṛthivīṃ mota devān divaṃ mā prāpadurvantarikṣam |
(14,2.69 ) apo mā prāpan malametadagne yamaṃ mā prāpatpitṝṃśca sarvān ||69||

(14,2.70 ) saṃ tvā nahyāmi payasā pṛthivyāḥ saṃ tvā nahyāmi payasauṣadhīnām |
(14,2.70 ) saṃ tvā nahyāmi prajayā dhanena sā saṃnaddhā sanuhi vājamemam ||70|| {13}

(14,2.71 ) amo'hamasmi sā tvaṃ sāmāhamasmyṛktvaṃ dyaurahaṃ pṛthivī tvam |
(14,2.71 ) tāviha saṃ bhavāva prajāmā janayāvahai ||71||

(14,2.72 ) janiyanti nāvagravaḥ putriyanti sudānavaḥ |
(14,2.72 ) ariṣṭāsū sacevahi bṛhate vājasātaye ||72||

(14,2.73 ) ye pitaro vadhūdarśā imaṃ vahatumāgaman |
(14,2.73 ) te asyai vadhvai saṃpatnyai prajāvaccharma yachantu ||73||

(14,2.74 ) yedaṃ pūrvāgan raśanāyamānā prajāmasyai draviṇaṃ ceha dattvā |
(14,2.74 ) tāṃ vahantvagatasyānu panthāṃ virāḍiyaṃ suprajā atyajaiṣīt||74||

(14,2.75 ) pra budhyasva subudhā budhyamānā dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya |
(14,2.75 ) gṛhān gacha gṛhapatnī yathāso dīrghaṃ ta āyuḥ savitā kṛṇotu ||75|| {14}


(15,1.1 ) vrātya āsīdīyamāna eva sa prajāpatiṃ samairayat||1||

(15,1.2 ) sa prajāpatiḥ suvarṇamātmann apaśyattatprājanayat||2||

(15,1.3 ) tadekamabhavattallalāmamabhavattan mahadabhavattajjyeṣṭhamabhavattadbrahmābhavattattapo'bhavattatsatyamabhavattena prājāyata ||3||

(15,1.4 ) so'vardhata sa mahān abhavatsa mahādevo'bhavat||4||

(15,1.5 ) sa devānāmīśāṃ paryaitsa īśāno'bhavat||5||

(15,1.6 ) sa ekavrātyo'bhavatsa dhanurādatta tadevendradhanuḥ ||6||

(15,1.7 ) nīlamasyodaraṃ lohitaṃ pṛṣṭham ||7||

(15,1.8 ) nīlenaivāpriyaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ prorṇoti lohitena dviṣantaṃ vidhyatīti brahmavādino vadanti ||8||



(15,2.1[2.1]a) sa udatiṣṭhatsa prācīṃ diśamanu vyacalat| [1]
(15,2.1[2.2]b) taṃ bṛhacca rathantaraṃ cādityāśca viśve ca devā anuvyacalan | [2]
(15,2.1[2.3]c) bṛhate ca vai sa rathantarāya cādityebhyaśca viśvebhyaśca devebhya ā vṛścate ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ vrātyamupavadati | [3]
(15,2.1[2.4]d) bṛhataśca vai sa rathantarasya cādityānāṃ ca viśveṣāṃ ca devānāṃ priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda | [4]
(15,2.1[2.5]e) tasya prācyāṃ diśi śraddhā puṃścalī mitro māgadho vijñānaṃ vāso'haruṣṇīṣaṃ rātrī keśā haritau pravartau kalmalirmaṇiḥ | [5]
(15,2.1[2.67]f) bhūtaṃ ca bhaviṣyacca pariṣkandau mano vipathaṃ mātariśvā ca pavamānaśca vipathavāhau vātaḥ sārathī reṣmā pratodaḥ kīrtiśca yaśaśca puraḥsarau | [67]
(15,2.1[2.8]g) ainaṃ kīrtirgachatyā yaśo gachati ya evaṃ veda ||1|| [8]

(15,2.2[2.9]a) sa udatiṣṭhatsa dakṣiṇāṃ diśamanu vyacalat| [9]
(15,2.2[2.10]b) taṃ yajñāyajñiyaṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca yajñaśca yajamānaśca paśavaścānuvyacalan | [10]
(15,2.2[2.11]c) yajñāyajñiyāya ca vai sa vāmadevyāya ca yajñāya ca yajamānāya ca paśubhyaścā vṛścate ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ vrātyamupavadati | [11]
(15,2.2[2.12]d) yajñāyajñiyasya ca vai sa vāmadevyasya ca yajñasya ca yajamānasya ca paśūnāṃ ca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda | [12]
(15,2.2[2.13]e) tasya dakṣiṇāyāṃ diśyuṣāḥ puṃścalī mantro māgadho vijñānaṃ vāso'haruṣṇīṣaṃ rātrī keśā haritau pravartau kalmalirmaṇiḥ | [13]
(15,2.2[2.14]f) amāvāsyā ca paurṇamāsī ca pariṣkandau mano vipathaṃ mātariśvā ca pavamānaśca vipathavāhau vātaḥ sārathī reṣmā pratodaḥ kīrtiśca yaśaśca puraḥsarau |
(15,2.2[2.14]g) ainaṃ kīrtirgachatyā yaśo gachati ya evaṃ veda ||2|| [14]

(15,2.3[2.15]a) sa udatiṣṭhatsa pratīcīṃ diśamanu vyacalat| [15]
(15,2.3[2.16]b) taṃ vairūpaṃ ca vairājaṃ cāpaśca varuṇaśca rājānuvyacalan | [16]
(15,2.3[2.17]c) vairūpāya ca vai sa vairājāya cādbhyaśca varuṇāya ca rājña ā vṛścate ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ vrātyamupavadati | [17]
(15,2.3[2.18]d) vairūpasya ca vai sa vairājasya cāpāṃ ca varuṇasya ca rājñaḥ priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda | [18]
(15,2.3[2.19]e) tasya pratīcyāṃ diśīrā puṃścalī haso māgadho vijñānaṃ vāso'haruṣṇīṣaṃ rātrī keśā haritau pravartau kalmalirmaṇiḥ | [19]
(15,2.3[2.20]f) ahaśca rātrī ca pariṣkandau mano vipathaṃ mātariśvā ca pavamānaśca vipathavāhau vātaḥ sārathī reṣmā pratodaḥ kīrtiśca yaśaśca puraḥsarau |
(15,2.3[2.20]g) ainaṃ kīrtirgachatyā yaśo gachati ya evaṃ veda ||3|| [20]

(15,2.4[2.21]a) sa udatiṣṭhatsa udīcīṃ diśamanu vyacalat| [21]
(15,2.4[2.22]b) taṃ śyaitaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca saptarṣayaśca somaśca rājānuvyacalan | [22]
(15,2.4[2.23]c) śyaitāya ca vai sa naudhasāya ca saptarṣibhyaśca somāya ca rājña ā vṛścate ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ vrātyamupavadati | [23]
(15,2.4[2.24]d) śyaitasya ca vai sa naudhasasya ca saptarṣīṇāṃ ca somasya ca rājñaḥ priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda | [24]
(15,2.4[2.25]e) tasyodīcyāṃ diśi vidyutpuṃścalī stanayitnurmāgadho vijñānaṃ vāso'haruṣṇīṣaṃ rātrī keśā haritau pravartau kalmalirmaṇiḥ | [25]
(15,2.4[2.2627]f) śrutaṃ ca viśrutaṃ ca pariṣkandau mano vipathaṃ mātariśvā ca pavamānaśca vipathavāhau vātaḥ sārathī reṣmā pratodaḥ kīrtiśca yaśaśca puraḥsarau | [2627]
(15,2.4[2.28]g) ainaṃ kīrtirgachatyā yaśo gachati ya evaṃ veda ||4|| [28]



(15,3.1 ) sa saṃvatsaramūrdhvo'tiṣṭhattaṃ devā abruvan vrātya kiṃ nu tiṣṭhasīti ||1||
(15,3.2 ) so'bravīdāsandīṃ me saṃ bharantviti ||2||

(15,3.3 ) tasmai vrātyāyāsandīṃ samabharan ||3||

(15,3.4 ) tasyā grīṣmaśca vasantaśca dvau pādāvāstāṃ śaracca varṣāśca dvau ||4||

(15,3.5 ) bṛhacca rathantaraṃ cānūcye āstāṃ yajñāyajñiyaṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca tiraścye ||5||

(15,3.6 ) ṛcaḥ prāñcastantavo yajūṃṣi tiryañcaḥ ||6||

(15,3.7 ) veda āstaraṇaṃ brahmopabarhaṇam ||7||

(15,3.8 ) sāmāsāda udgītho'paśrayaḥ ||8||

(15,3.9 ) tāmāsandīṃ vrātya ārohat||9||

(15,3.10 ) tasya devajanāḥ pariṣkandā āsantsaṃkalpāḥ prahāyyā viśvāni bhūtānyupasadaḥ ||10||

(15,3.11 ) viśvānyevāsya bhūtānyupasado bhavanti ya evaṃ veda ||11||



(15,4.1[4.1]a) tasmai prācyā diśaḥ | [1]
(15,4.1[4.2]b) vāsantau māsau goptārāvakurvan bṛhacca rathantaraṃ cānuṣṭhātārau | [2]
(15,4.1[4.3]c) vāsantāvenaṃ māsau prācyā diśo gopāyato bṛhacca rathantaraṃ cānu tiṣṭhato ya evaṃ veda ||1|| [3]

(15,4.2[4.4]a) tasmai dakṣiṇāyā diśaḥ | [4]
(15,4.2[4.5]b) graiṣmau māsau goptārāvakurvan yajñāyajñiyaṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ cānuṣṭhātārau | [5]
(15,4.2[4.6]c) graiṣmāvenaṃ māsau dakṣiṇāyā diśo gopāyato yajñāyajñiyaṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ cānu tiṣṭhato ya evaṃ veda ||2|| [6]

(15,4.3[4.7]a) tasmai pratīcyā diśaḥ | [7]
(15,4.3[4.8]b) vārṣikau māsau goptārāvakurvan vairūpaṃ ca vairājaṃ cānuṣṭhātārau | [8]
(15,4.3[4.9]c) vārṣikāvenaṃ māsau pratīcyā diśo gopāyato vairūpaṃ ca vairājaṃ cānu tiṣṭhato ya evaṃ veda ||3|| [9]

(15,4.4[4.10]a) tasmā udīcyā diśaḥ | [10]
(15,4.4[4.11]b) śāradau māsau goptārāvakurvaṃ chyaitaṃ ca naudhasaṃ cānuṣṭhātārau | [11]
(15,4.4[4.12]c) śāradāvenaṃ māsāvudīcyā diśo gopāyataḥ śyaitaṃ ca naudhasaṃ cānu tiṣṭhato ya evaṃ veda ||4|| [12]

(15,4.5[4.13]a) tasmai dhruvāyā diśaḥ | [13]
(15,4.5[4.14]b) haimanau māsau goptārāvakurvan bhūmiṃ cāgniṃ cānuṣṭhātārau | [14]
(15,4.5[4.15]c) haimanāvenaṃ māsau dhruvāyā diśo gopāyato bhūmiścāgniścānu tiṣṭhato ya evaṃ veda ||5|| [15]

(15,4.6[4.16]a) tasmā ūrdhvāyā diśaḥ | [16]
(15,4.6[4.17]b) śaiśirau māsau goptārāvakurvan divaṃ cādityaṃ cānuṣṭhātārau | [17]
(15,4.6[4.18]c) śaiśirāvenaṃ māsāvūrdhvāyā diśo gopāyato dyauścādityaścānu tiṣṭhato ya evaṃ veda ||6|| [18]



(15,5.1[5.1]a) tasmai prācyā diśo antardeśādbhavamiṣvāsamanuṣṭhātāramakurvan | [1]
(15,5.1[5.23]b) bhava enamiṣvāsaḥ prācyā diśo antardeśādanuṣṭhātānu tiṣṭhati nainaṃ śarvo na bhavo neśāno nāsya paśūn na samānān hinasti ya evaṃ veda ||1|| [23]

(15,5.2[5.4]a) tasmai dakṣiṇāyā diśo antardeśāccharvamiṣvāsamanuṣṭhātāramakurvan | [4]
(15,5.2[5.5]b) śarva enamiśvāso dakṣiṇāyā diśo antardeśādanuṣṭhātānu tiṣṭhati nainaṃ śarvo na bhavo neśāno nāsya paśūn na samānān hinasti ya evaṃ veda ||2|| [5]

(15,5.3[5.6]a) tasmai pratīcyā diśo antardeśātpaśupatimiṣvāsamanuṣṭhātāramakurvan | [6]
(15,5.3[5.7]b) paśupatirenamiṣvāsaḥ pratīcyā diśo antardeśādanuṣṭhātānu tiṣṭhati nainaṃ śarvo na bhavo neśāno nāsya paśūn na samānān hinasti ya evaṃ veda ||3|| [7]

(15,5.4[5.8]a) tasmā udīcyā diśo antardeśādugraṃ devamiṣvāsamanuṣṭhātāramakurvan | [8]
(15,5.4[5.9]b) ugra enaṃ deva iṣvāsa udīcyā diśo antardeśādanuṣṭhātānu tiṣṭhati nainaṃ śarvo na bhavo neśāno nāsya paśūn na samānān hinasti ya evaṃ veda ||4|| [9]

(15,5.5[5.10]a) tasmai dhruvāyā diśo antardeśādrudramiṣvāsamanuṣṭhātāramakurvan | [10]
(15,5.5[5.11]b) rudra enamiṣvāso dhruvāyā diśo antardeśādanuṣṭhātānu tiṣṭhati nainaṃ śarvo na bhavo neśāno nāsya paśūn na samānān hinasti ya evaṃ veda ||5|| [11]

(15,5.6[5.12]a) tasmā ūrdhvāyā diśo antardeśān mahādevamiṣvāsamanuṣṭhātāramakurvan | [12]
(15,5.6[5.13]b) mahādeva enamiṣvāsa ūrdhvāyā diśo antardeśādanuṣṭhātānu tiṣṭhati nainaṃ śarvo na bhavo neśāno nāsya paśūn na samānān hinasti ya evaṃ veda ||6|| [13]

(15,5.7[5.14]a) tasmai sarvebhyo antardeśebhya īśānamiṣvāsamanuṣṭhātāramakurvan | [14]
(15,5.7[5.1516]b) īśāna enamiṣvāsaḥ sarvebhyo antardeśebhyo'nuṣṭhātānu tiṣṭhati nainaṃ śarvo na bhavo neśāno nāsya paśūn na samānān hinasti ya evaṃ veda ||7|| [1516]



(15,6.1[6.1]a) sa dhruvāṃ diśamanu vyacalat| [1]
(15,6.1[6.2]b) taṃ bhūmiścāgniścauṣadhayaśca vanaspatayaśca vānaspatyāśca vīrudhaścānuvyacalan | [2]
(15,6.1[6.3]c) bhūmeśca vai so'gneścauṣadhīnāṃ ca vanaspatīnāṃ ca vānaspatyānāṃ ca vīrudhāṃ ca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||1|| [3]

(15,6.2[6.4]a) sa ūrdhvāṃ diśamanu vyacalat| [4]
(15,6.2[6.5]b) tamṛtaṃ ca satyaṃ ca sūryaśca candraśca nakṣatrāṇi cānuvyacalan | [5]
(15,6.2[6.6]c) ṛtasya ca vai sa satyasya ca sūryasya ca candrasya ca nakṣatrāṇāṃ ca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||2|| [6]

(15,6.3[6.7]a) sa uttamāṃ diśamanu vyacalat| [7]
(15,6.3[6.8]b) tamṛcaśca sāmāni ca yajūṃṣi ca brahma cānuvyacalan | [8]
(15,6.3[6.9]c) ṛcāṃ ca vai sa sāmnāṃ ca yajuṣāṃ ca brahmaṇaśca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||3|| [9]

(15,6.4[6.10]a) sa bṛhatīṃ diśamanu vyacalat| [10]
(15,6.4[6.11]b) tamitihāsaśca purāṇaṃ ca gāthāśca nārāśaṃsīścānuvyacalan | [11]
(15,6.4[6.12]c) itihāsasya ca vai sa purāṇasya ca gāthānāṃ ca nārāśaṃsīnāṃ ca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||4|| [12]

(15,6.5[6.13]a) sa paramāṃ diśamanu vyacalat| [13]
(15,6.5[6.14]b) tamāhavanīyaśca gārhapatyaśca dakṣiṇāgniśca yajñaśca yajamānaśca paśavaścānuvyacalan | [14]
(15,6.5[6.15]c) āhavanīyasya ca vai sa gārhapatyasya ca dakṣiṇāgneśca yajñasya ca yajamānasya ca paśūnāṃ ca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||5|| [15]

(15,6.6[6.16]a) so'nādiṣṭāṃ diśamanu vyacalat| [16]
(15,6.6[6.17]b) tamṛtavaścārtavāśca lokāśca laukyāśca māsāścārdhamāsāścāhorātre cānuvyacalan | [17]
(15,6.6[6.18]c) ṛtūnāṃ ca vai sa ārtavānāṃ ca lokānāṃ ca laukyānāṃ ca māsānāṃ cārdhamāsānāṃ cāhorātrayośca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||6|| [18]

(15,6.7[6.19]a) so'nāvṛttāṃ diśamanu vyacalattato nāvartsyann amanyata | [19]
(15,6.7[6.20]b) taṃ ditiścāditiśceḍā cendrāṇī cānuvyacalan | [20]
(15,6.7[6.21]c) diteśca vai so'diteśceḍāyāścendrāṇyāśca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||7|| [21]

(15,6.8[6.22]a) sa diśo'nu vyacalattaṃ virāḍanu vyacalatsarve ca devāḥ sarvāśca devatāḥ | [22]
(15,6.8[6.23]b) virājaśca vai sa sarveṣāṃ ca devānāṃ sarvāsāṃ ca devatānāṃ priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||8|| [23]

(15,6.9[6.24]a) sa sarvān antardeśān anu vyacalat| [24]
(15,6.9[6.25]b) taṃ prajāpatiśca parameṣṭhī ca pitā ca pitāmahaścānuvyacalan | [25]
(15,6.9[6.26]c) prajāpateśca vai sa parameṣṭhinaśca pituśca pitāmahasya ca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||9|| [26]



(15,7.1 ) sa mahimā sadrurbhūtvāntaṃ pṛthivyā agachatsamudro'bhavat||1||

(15,7.2 ) taṃ prajāpatiśca parameṣṭhī ca pitā ca pitāmahaścāpaśca śraddhā ca varṣaṃ bhūtvānuvyavartayanta ||2||

(15,7.3 ) ainamāpo gachantyainaṃ śraddhā gachatyainaṃ varṣaṃ gachati ya evaṃ veda ||3||

(15,7.4 ) taṃ śraddhā ca yajñaśca lokaścānnaṃ cānnādyaṃ ca bhūtvābhiparyāvartanta ||4||

(15,7.5 ) ainaṃ śraddhā gachatyainaṃ yajño gachatyainaṃ loko gachatyainamannaṃ gachatyainamannādyaṃ gachati ya evaṃ veda ||5||



(15,8.1 ) so'rajyata tato rājanyo'jāyata ||1||

(15,8.2 ) sa viśaḥ sabandhūn annamannādyamabhyudatiṣṭhat||2||

(15,8.3 ) viśāṃ ca vai sa sabandhūnāṃ cānnasya cānnādyasya ca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||3||



(15,9.1 ) sa viśo'nu vyacalat||1||

(15,9.2 ) taṃ sabhā ca samitiśca senā ca surā cānuvyacalan ||2||

(15,9.3 ) sabhāyāśca vai sa samiteśca senāyāśca surāyāśca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||3||



(15,10.1 ) tadyasyaivaṃ vidvān vrātyo rājño'tithirgṛhān āgachet||1||

(15,10.2 ) śreyāṃsamenamātmano mānayettathā kṣatrāya nā vṛścate tathā rāṣṭrāya nā vṛścate ||2||

(15,10.3 ) ato vai brahma ca kṣatraṃ codatiṣṭhatāṃ te abrūtāṃ kaṃ pra viśāveti ||3||

(15,10.4 ) bṛhaspatimeva brahma praviśatvindraṃ kṣatraṃ tathā vā iti ||4||

(15,10.5 ) ato vai bṛhaspatimeva brahma prāviśadindraṃ kṣatram ||5||

(15,10.6 ) iyaṃ vā u pṛthivī bṛhaspatirdyaurevendraḥ ||6||

(15,10.7 ) ayaṃ vā u agnirbrahmāsāvādityaḥ kṣatram ||7||

(15,10.8 ) ainaṃ brahma gachati brahmavarcasī bhavati ||8||

(15,10.9 ) yaḥ pṛthivīṃ bṛhaspatimagniṃ brahma veda ||9||

(15,10.10 ) ainamindriyaṃ gachatīndriyavān bhavati ||10||

(15,10.11 ) ya ādityaṃ kṣatraṃ divamindraṃ veda ||11||



(15,11.1 ) tadyasyaivaṃ vidvān vrātyo'tithirgṛhān āgachet||1||

(15,11.2 ) svayamenamabhyudetya brūyādvrātya kvāvātsīrvrātyodakaṃ vrātya tarpayantu vrātya yathā te priyaṃ tathāstu vrātya yathā te vaśastathāstu vrātya yathā te nikāmastathāstviti ||2||

(15,11.3 ) yadenamāha vrātya kvāvātsīriti patha eva tena devayānān ava rundhe ||3||

(15,11.4 ) yadenamāha vrātyodakamityapa eva tenāva rundhe ||4||

(15,11.5 ) yadenamāha vrātya tarpayantviti prāṇameva tena varṣīyāṃsaṃ kurute ||5||

(15,11.6 ) yadenamāha vrātya yathā te priyaṃ tathāstviti priyameva tenāva rundhe ||6||

(15,11.7 ) ainaṃ priyaṃ gachati priyaḥ priyasya bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||7||

(15,11.8 ) yadenamāha vrātya yathā te vaśastathāstviti vaśameva tenāva rundhe ||8||

(15,11.9 ) ainaṃ vaśo gachati vaśī vaśināṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||9||

(15,11.10 ) yadenamāha vrātya yathā te nikāmastathāstviti nikāmameva tenāva rundhe ||10||

(15,11.11 ) ainaṃ nikāmo gachati nikāme nikāmasya bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||11||



(15,12.1 ) tadyasyaivaṃ vidvān vrātya uddhṛteṣvagniṣvadhiśrite'gnihotre'tithirgṛhān āgachet||1||

(15,12.2 ) svayamenamabhyudetya brūyādvrātyāti sṛja hoṣyāmīti ||2||

(15,12.3 ) sa cātisṛjejjuhuyān na cātisṛjen na juhuyāt||3||

(15,12.4 ) sa ya evaṃ viduṣā vrātyenātisṛṣṭo juhoti ||4||

(15,12.5 ) pra pitṛyāṇaṃ panthāṃ jānāti pra devayānam ||5||

(15,12.6 ) na deveṣvā vṛścate hutamasya bhavati ||6||

(15,12.7 ) paryasyāsmiṃlloka āyatanaṃ śiṣyate ya evaṃ viduṣā vrātyenātisṛṣṭo juhoti ||7||

(15,12.8 ) atha ya evaṃ viduṣā vrātyenānatisṛṣṭo juhoti ||8||

(15,12.9 ) na pitṛyāṇaṃ panthāṃ jānāti na devayānam ||9||

(15,12.10 ) ā deveṣu vṛścate ahutamasya bhavati ||10||

(15,12.11 ) nāsyāsmiṃlloka āyatanaṃ śiṣyate ya evaṃ viduṣā vrātyenānatisṛṣṭo juhoti ||11||


(15,13.1[13.1]a) tadyasyaivaṃ vidvān vrātya ekāṃ rātrimatithirgṛhe vasati | [1]
(15,13.1[13.2]b) ye pṛthivyāṃ puṇyā lokāstān eva tenāva rundhe ||1|| [2]

(15,13.2[13.3]a) tadyasyaivaṃ vidvān vrātyo dvitīyāṃ rātrimatithirgṛhe vasati | [3]
(15,13.2[13.4]b) ye'ntarikṣe puṇyā lokāstān eva tenāva rundhe ||2|| [4]
(15,13.3[13.5]a) tadyasyaivaṃ vidvān vrātyastṛtīyāṃ rātrimatithirgṛhe vasati | [5]
(15,13.3[13.6]b) ye divi puṇyā lokāstān eva tenāva rundhe ||3|| [6]

(15,13.4[13.7]a) tadyasyaivaṃ vidvān vrātyaścaturthīṃ rātrimatithirgṛhe vasati | [7]
(15,13.4[13.8]b) ye puṇyānāṃ puṇyā lokāstān eva tenāva rundhe ||4|| [8]

(15,13.5[13.9]a) tadyasyaivaṃ vidvān vrātyo'parimitā rātrīratithirgṛhe vasati | [9]
(15,13.5[13.10]b) ya evāparimitāḥ puṇyā lokāstān eva tenāva rundhe ||5|| [10]

(15,13.6[13.11]a) atha yasyāvrātyo vrātyabruvo nāmabibhratyatithirgṛhān āgachet||6|| [11]

(15,13.7[13.12]a) karṣedenaṃ na cainaṃ karṣet||7|| [12]

(15,13.8[13.13]a) asyai devatāyā udakaṃ yācāmīmāṃ devatāṃ vāsaya imāmimāṃ devatāṃ pari veveṣmītyenaṃ pari veviṣyāt||8|| [13]

(15,13.9[13.14]a) tasyāmevāsya taddevatāyāṃ hutaṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ||9|| [14]



(15,14.1[14.1]a) sa yatprācīṃ diśamanu vyacalan mārutaṃ śardho bhūtvānuvyacalan mano'nnādaṃ kṛtvā | [1]
(15,14.1[14.2]b) manasānnādenānnamatti ya evaṃ veda ||1|| [2]

(15,14.2[14.3]a) sa yaddakṣiṇāṃ diśamanu vyacaladindro bhūtvānuvyacaladbalamannādaṃ kṛtvā | [3]
(15,14.2[14.4]b) balenānnādenānnamatti ya evaṃ veda ||2|| [4]

(15,14.3[14.5]a) sa yatpratīcīṃ diśamanu vyacaladvaruṇo rājā bhūtvānuvyacaladapo'nnādīḥ kṛtvā | [5]
(15,14.3[14.6]b) adbhirannādībhirannamatti ya evaṃ veda ||3|| [6]

(15,14.4[14.7]a) sa yadudīcīṃ diśamanu vyacalatsomo rājā bhūtvānuvyacalatsaptarṣibhirhuta āhutimannādīṃ kṛtvā | [7]
(15,14.4[14.8]b) āhutyānnādyānnamatti ya evaṃ veda ||4|| [8]

(15,14.5[14.9]a) sa yaddhruvāṃ diśamanu vyacaladviṣṇurbhūtvānuvyacaladvirājamannādīṃ kṛtvā | [9]
(15,14.5[14.10]b) virājānnādyānnamatti ya evaṃ veda ||5|| [10]

(15,14.6[14.11]a) sa yatpaśūn anu vyacaladrudro bhūtvānuvyacaladoṣadhīrannādīḥ kṛtvā | [11]
(15,14.6[14.12]b) oṣadhībhirannādībhirannamatti ya evaṃ veda ||6|| [12]

(15,14.7[14.13]a) sa yatpitṝn anu vyacaladyamo rājā bhūtvānuvyacalatsvadhākāramannādaṃ kṛtvā | [13]
(15,14.7[14.14]b) svadhākāreṇānnādenānnamatti ya evaṃ veda ||7|| [14]

(15,14.8[14.15]a) sa yan manuṣyān anu vyacaladagnirbhūtvānuvyacalatsvāhākāramannādaṃ kṛtvā | [15]
(15,14.8[14.16]b) svāhākāreṇānnādenānnamatti ya evaṃ veda ||8|| [16]

(15,14.9[14.17]a) sa yadūrdhvāṃ diśamanu vyacaladbṛhaspatirbhūtvānuvyacaladvaṣaṭkāramannādaṃ kṛtvā | [17]
(15,14.9[14.18]b) vaṣaṭkāreṇānnādenānnamatti ya evaṃ veda ||9|| [18]

(15,14.10[14.19]a) sa yaddevān anu vyacaladīśāno bhūtvānuvyacalan manyumannādaṃ kṛtvā | [19]
(15,14.10[14.20]b) manyunānnādenānnamatti ya evaṃ veda ||10|| [20]

(15,14.11[14.21]a) sa yatprajā anu vyacalatprajāpatirbhūtvānuvyacalatprāṇamannādaṃ kṛtvā | [21]
(15,14.11[14.22]b) prāṇenānnādenānnamatti ya evaṃ veda ||11|| [22]

(15,14.12[14.23]a) sa yatsarvān antardeśān anu vyacalatparameṣṭhī bhūtvānuvyacaladbrahmānnādaṃ kṛtvā | [23]
(15,14.12[14.24]b) brahmaṇānnādenānnamatti ya evaṃ veda ||12|| [24]


(15,15.1 ) tasya vrātyasya ||1||

(15,15.2 ) sapta prāṇāḥ saptāpānāḥ sapta vyānāḥ ||2||

(15,15.3 ) yo'sya prathamaḥ prāṇa ūrdhvo nāmāyaṃ so agniḥ ||3||

(15,15.4 ) yo'sya dvitīyaḥ prāṇaḥ prauḍho nāmāsau sa ādityaḥ ||4||

(15,15.5 ) yo'sya tṛtīyaḥ prāṇo'bhyūḍho nāmāsau sa candramāḥ ||5||

(15,15.6 ) yo'sya caturthaḥ prāṇo vibhūrnāmāyaṃ sa pavamānaḥ ||6||

(15,15.7 ) yo'sya pañcamaḥ prāṇo yonirnāma tā imā āpaḥ ||7||

(15,15.8 ) yo'sya ṣaṣṭhaḥ prāṇaḥ priyo nāma ta ime paśavaḥ ||8||

(15,15.9 ) yo'sya saptamaḥ prāṇo'parimito nāma tā imāḥ prajāḥ ||9||



(15,16.1 ) yo'sya prathamo'pānaḥ sā paurṇamāsī ||1||

(15,16.2 ) yo'sya dvitīyo'pānaḥ sāṣṭakā ||2||

(15,16.3 ) yo'sya tṛtīyo'pānaḥ sāmāvāsyā ||3||

(15,16.4 ) yo'sya caturtho'pānaḥ sā śraddhā ||4||

(15,16.5 ) yo'sya pañcamo'pānaḥ sā dīkṣā ||5||

(15,16.6 ) yo'sya ṣaṣṭho'pānaḥ sa yajñaḥ ||6||

(15,16.7 ) yo'sya saptamo'pānastā imā dakṣiṇāḥ ||7||



(15,17.1 ) yo'sya prathamo vyānaḥ seyaṃ bhūmiḥ ||1||

(15,17.2 ) yo'sya dvitīyo vyānastadantarikṣam ||2||

(15,17.3 ) yo'sya tṛtīyo vyānaḥ sā dyauḥ ||3||

(15,17.4 ) yo'sya caturtho vyānastāni nakṣatrāṇi ||4||

(15,17.5 ) yo'sya pañcamo vyānasta ṛtavaḥ ||5||

(15,17.6 ) yo'sya ṣaṣṭho vyānasta ārtavāḥ ||6||

(15,17.7 ) yo'sya saptamo vyānaḥ sa saṃvatsaraḥ ||7||

(15,17.8 ) samānamarthaṃ pari yanti devāḥ saṃvatsaraṃ vā etadṛtavo'nupariyanti vrātyaṃ ca ||8||

(15,17.9 ) yadādityamabhisaṃviśantyamāvāsyāṃ caiva tatpaurṇamāsīṃ ca ||9||

(15,17.10 ) ekaṃ tadeṣāmamṛtatvamityāhutireva ||10||



(15,18.1 ) tasya vrātyasya ||1||

(15,18.2 ) yadasya dakṣiṇamakṣyasau sa ādityo yadasya savyamakṣyasau sa candramāḥ ||2||

(15,18.3 ) yo'sya dakṣiṇaḥ karṇo'yaṃ so agniryo'sya savyaḥ karṇo'yaṃ sa pavamānaḥ ||3||

(15,18.4 ) ahorātre nāsike ditiścāditiśca śīrṣakapāle saṃvatsaraḥ śiraḥ ||4||

(15,18.5 ) ahnā pratyaṅvrātyo rātryā prāṅnamo vrātyāya ||5||


(16,1.1 ) atisṛṣṭo apāṃ vṛṣabho'tisṛṣṭā agnayo divyāḥ ||1||

(16,1.2 ) rujan parirujan mṛṇan pramṛṇan ||2||

(16,1.3 ) mroko manohā khano nirdāha ātmadūṣistanūdūṣiḥ ||3||

(16,1.4 ) idaṃ tamati sṛjāmi taṃ mābhyavanikṣi ||4||

(16,1.5 ) tena tamabhyatisṛjāmo yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ||5||

(16,1.6 ) apāmagramasi samudraṃ vo'bhyavasṛjāmi ||6||

(16,1.7 ) yo'psvagnirati taṃ sṛjāmi mrokaṃ khaniṃ tanūdūṣim ||7||

(16,1.8 ) yo va āpo'gnirāviveśa sa eṣa yadvo ghoraṃ tadetat||8||

(16,1.9 ) indrasya va indriyeṇābhi ṣiñcet||9||

(16,1.10 ) ariprā āpo apa ripramasmat||10||
(16,1.11 ) prāsmadeno vahantu pra duṣvapnyaṃ vahantu ||11||

(16,1.12 ) śivena mā cakṣuṣā paśyatāpaḥ śivayā tanvopa spṛśata tvacaṃ me ||12||

(16,1.13 ) śivān agnīn apsuṣado havāmahe mayi kṣatraṃ varca ā dhatta devīḥ ||13||



(16,2.1 ) nirdurarmaṇya ūrjā madhumatī vāk||1||

(16,2.2 ) madhumatī stha madhumatīṃ vācamudeyam ||2||

(16,2.3 ) upahūto me gopāḥ upahūto gopīthaḥ ||3||

(16,2.4 ) suśrutau karṇau bhadraśrutau karṇau bhadraṃ ślokaṃ śrūyāsam ||4||

(16,2.5 ) suśrutiśca mopaśrutiśca mā hāsiṣṭāṃ sauparṇaṃ cakṣurajasraṃ jyotiḥ ||5||

(16,2.6 ) ṛṣīṇāṃ prastaro'si namo'stu daivāya prastarāya ||6||



(16,3.1 ) mūrdhāhaṃ rayīṇāṃ mūrdhā samānānāṃ bhūyāsam ||1||

(16,3.2 ) rujaśca mā venaśca mā hāsiṣṭāṃ mūrdhā ca mā vidharmā ca mā hāsiṣṭām ||2||

(16,3.3 ) urvaśca mā camasaśca mā hāsiṣṭāṃ dhartā ca mā dharuṇaśca mā hāsiṣṭām ||3||

(16,3.4 ) vimokaśca mārdrapaviśca mā hāsiṣṭāmārdradānuśca mā mātariśvā ca mā hāsiṣṭām ||4||

(16,3.5 ) bṛhaspatirma ātmā nṛmaṇā nāma hṛdyaḥ ||5||

(16,3.6 ) asaṃtāpaṃ me hṛdayamurvī gavyūtiḥ samudro asmi vidharmaṇā ||6||



(16,4.1 ) nābhirahaṃ rayīṇāṃ nābhiḥ samānānāṃ bhūyāsam ||1||

(16,4.2 ) svāsadasi sūṣā amṛto martyeśvā ||2||

(16,4.3 ) mā māṃ prāṇo hāsīn mo apāno'vahāya parā gāt||3||

(16,4.4 ) sūryo māhnaḥ pātvagniḥ pṛthivyā vāyurantarikṣādyamo manuṣyebhyaḥ sarasvatī pārthivebhyaḥ ||4||

(16,4.5 ) prāṇāpanau mā mā hāsiṣṭaṃ mā jane pra meṣi ||5||

(16,4.6 ) svastyadyoṣaso doṣasaśca sarva āpaḥ sarvagaṇo aśīya ||6||

(16,4.7 ) śakvarī stha paśavo mopa stheṣurmitrāvaruṇau me prāṇāpānāvagnirme dakṣaṃ dadhātu ||7||



(16,5.1 ) vidma te svapna janitraṃ grāhyāḥ putro'si yamasya karaṇaḥ |
(16,5.1 ) antako'si mṛtyurasi |
(16,5.1 ) taṃ tvā svapna tathā saṃ vidma sa naḥ svapna duṣvapnyātpāhi ||1||

(16,5.2 ) vidma te svapna janitraṃ nirṛtyāḥ putro'si yamasya karaṇaḥ | [...] || 2 ||

(16,5.3 ) vidma te svapna janitramabhūtyāḥ putro'si yamasya [...] || 3 ||

(16,5.4 ) vidma te svapna janitraṃ nirbhūtyāḥ putro'si [...] || 4 ||

(16,5.5 ) vidma te svapna janitraṃ parābhūtyāḥ putro'si [...] || 5 ||

(16,5.6 ) vidma te svapna janitraṃ devajāmīnāṃ putro'si yamasya karaṇaḥ |
(16,5.6 ) antako'si mṛtyurasi |
(16,5.6 ) taṃ tvā svapna tathā saṃ vidma sa naḥ svapna duṣvapnyātpāhi ||6||



(16,6.1 ) ajaiṣmādyāsanāmadyāmūmanāgaso vayam ||1||

(16,6.2 ) uṣo yasmādduṣvapnyādabhaiṣmāpa taduchatu ||2||

(16,6.3 ) dviṣate tatparā vaha śapate tatparā vaha ||3||

(16,6.4 ) yaṃ dviṣmo yaśca no dveṣṭi tasmā enadgamayāmaḥ ||4||

(16,6.5 ) uṣā devī vācā saṃvidānā vāgdevyuṣasā saṃvidānā ||5||

(16,6.6 ) uṣaspatirvācaspatinā saṃvidāno vācaspatinā saṃvidānaḥ ||6||

(16,6.7 ) te'muṣmai parā vahantvarāyān durṇāmnaḥ sadānvāḥ ||7||

(16,6.8 ) kumbhīkāḥ dūṣīkāḥ pīyakān ||8||

(16,6.9 ) jāgradduṣvapnyaṃ svapneduṣvapnyam ||9||

(16,6.10 ) anāgamiṣyato varān avitteḥ saṃkalpān amucyā druhaḥ pāśān ||10||

(16,6.11 ) tadamuṣmā agne devāḥ parā vahantu vaghriryathāsadvithuro na sādhuḥ ||11||



(16,7.1 ) tenainaṃ vidhyāmyabhūtyainaṃ vidhyāmi nirbhūtyainaṃ vidhyāmi parābhūtyainaṃ vidhyāmi grāhyainaṃ vidhyāmi tamasainaṃ vidhyāmi ||1||

(16,7.2 ) devānāmenaṃ ghoraiḥ krūraiḥ praiṣairabhipreṣyāmi ||2||

(16,7.3 ) vaiśvānarasyainaṃ daṃṣṭrayorapi dadhāmi ||3||

(16,7.4 ) evānevāva sā garat||4||

(16,7.5 ) yo'smān dveṣṭi tamātmā dveṣṭu yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ sa ātmānaṃ dveṣṭu ||5||

(16,7.6 ) nirdviṣantaṃ divo niḥ pṛthivyā nirantarikṣādbhajāma ||6||

(16,7.7 ) suyāmaṃścākṣuṣa ||7||

(16,7.8 ) idamahamāmuṣyāyaṇe'muṣyāḥ putre duṣvapnyaṃ mṛje ||8||

(16,7.9 ) yadadoado abhyagachaṃ yaddoṣā yatpūrvāṃ rātrim ||9||

(16,7.10 ) yajjāgradyatsupto yaddivā yan naktam ||10||

(16,7.11 ) yadaharaharabhigachāmi tasmādenamava daye ||11||

(16,7.12 ) taṃ jahi tena mandasva tasya pṛṣṭīrapi śṛṇīhi ||12||

(16,7.13 ) sa mā jīvīttaṃ prāṇo jahātu ||13||

(16,8.1 ) jitamasmākamudbhinnamasmākamṛtamasmākaṃ tejasasmākaṃ brahmāsmākaṃ svarasmākaṃ yajño'smākaṃ paśavo'smākaṃ prajā asmākaṃ vīrā asmākam ||1||

(16,9.1 ) jitamasmākamudbhinnamasmākamabhyaṣṭhāṃ viśvāḥ pṛtanā arātīḥ ||1||

(16,9.2 ) tadagnirāha tadu soma āha pūṣā mā dhātsukṛtasya loke ||2||

(16,9.3 ) aganma svaḥ svaraganma saṃ sūryasya jyotiṣāganma ||3||

(16,9.4 ) vasyobhūyāya vasumān yajño vasu vaṃsiṣīya vasumān bhūyāsaṃ vasu mayi dhehi ||4||



(17,1.1 ) viṣāsahiṃ sahamānaṃ sāsahānaṃ sahīyāṃsam |
(17,1.1 ) sahamānaṃ sahojitaṃ svarjitaṃ gojitaṃ saṃdhanājitam |
(17,1.1 ) īḍyaṃ nāma hva indramāyuṣmān bhūyāsam ||1||

(17,1.2 ) viṣāsahiṃ sahamānaṃ sāsahānaṃ sahīyāṃsam |
(17,1.2 ) sahamānaṃ sahojitaṃ svarjitaṃ gojitaṃ saṃdhanājitam |
(17,1.2 ) īḍyaṃ nāma hva indraṃ priyo devānāṃ bhūyāsam ||2||

(17,1.3 ) viṣāsahiṃ sahamānaṃ sāsahānaṃ sahīyāṃsam |
(17,1.3 ) sahamānaṃ sahojitaṃ svarjitaṃ gojitaṃ saṃdhanājitam |
(17,1.3 ) īḍyaṃ nāma hva indraṃ priyaḥ prajānāṃ bhūyāsam ||3||

(17,1.4 ) viṣāsahiṃ sahamānaṃ sāsahānaṃ sahīyāṃsam |
(17,1.4 ) sahamānaṃ sahojitaṃ svarjitaṃ gojitaṃ saṃdhanājitam |
(17,1.4 ) īḍyaṃ nāma hva indraṃ priyaḥ paśūnāṃ bhūyāsam ||4||

(17,1.5 ) viṣāsahiṃ sahamānaṃ sāsahānaṃ sahīyāṃsam |
(17,1.5 ) sahamānaṃ sahojitaṃ svarjitaṃ gojitaṃ saṃdhanājitam |
(17,1.5 ) īḍyaṃ nāma hva indraṃ priyaḥ samānānāṃ bhūyāsam ||5||

(17,1.6 ) udihyudihi sūrya varcasā mābhyudihi |
(17,1.6 ) dviṣaṃśca mahyaṃ radhyatu mā cāhaṃ dviṣate radhaṃ tavedviṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāni |
(17,1.6 ) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhirviśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ||6||

(17,1.7 ) udihyudihi sūrya varcasā mābhyudihi |
(17,1.7 ) āṃśca paśyāmi yāṃśca na teṣu mā sumatiṃ kṛdhi tavedviṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāni |
(17,1.7 ) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhirviśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ||7||

(17,1.8 ) mā tvā dabhantsalile apsvantarye pāśina upatiṣṭhantyatra |
(17,1.8 ) hitvāśastiṃ divamārukṣa etāṃ sa no mṛḍa sumatau te syāma tavedviṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāni |
(17,1.8 ) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhirviśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ||8||

(17,1.9 ) tvaṃ na indra mahate saubhagāyādabdhebhiḥ pari pāhyaktubhistavedviṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāni |
(17,1.9 ) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhirviśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ||9||

(17,1.10 ) tvaṃ na indrotibhiḥ śivābhiḥ śaṃtamo bhava |
(17,1.10 ) ārohaṃstridivaṃ divo gṛṇānaḥ somapītaye priyadhāmā svastaye tavedviṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāni |
(17,1.10 ) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhirviśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ||10|| {1}

(17,1.11 ) tvamindrāsi viśvajitsarvavitpuruhūtastvamindra |
(17,1.11 ) tvamindremaṃ suhavaṃ stomamerayasva sa no mṛḍa sumatau te syāma tavedviṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi |
(17,1.11 ) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhirviśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ||11||

(17,1.12 ) adabdho divi pṛthivyāmutāsi na ta āpurmahimānamantarikṣe |
(17,1.12 ) adabdhena brahmaṇā vāvṛdhānaḥ sa tvaṃ na indra divi saṃ charma yacha tavedviṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi |
(17,1.12 ) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhirviśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ||12||

(17,1.13 ) yā ta indra tanūrapsu yā pṛthivyāṃ yāntaragnau yā te indra pavamāne svarvidi |
(17,1.13 ) yayendra tanvā'ntarikṣaṃ vyāpitha tayā na indra tanvā śarma yacha tavedviṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi |
(17,1.13 ) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhirviśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ||13||

(17,1.14 ) tvāmindra brahmaṇā vardhayantaḥ sattraṃ ni ṣedurṛṣayo nādhamānāstavedviṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi |
(17,1.14 ) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhirviśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ||14||

(17,1.15 ) tvaṃ tṛtaṃ tvaṃ paryeṣyutsaṃ sahasradhāraṃ vidathaṃ svarvidaṃ tavedviṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi |
(17,1.15 ) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhirviśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ||15||

(17,1.16 ) tvaṃ rakṣase pradiśaścatasrastvaṃ śociṣā nabhasī vi bhāsi |
(17,1.16 ) tvamimā viśvā bhuvanānu tiṣṭhasa ṛtasya panthāmanveṣi vidvāṃstavedviṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi |
(17,1.16 ) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhirviśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ||16||

(17,1.17 ) pañcabhiḥ parāṅtapasyekayārvāṅaśastimeṣi sudine bādhamānastavedviṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi |
(17,1.17 ) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhirviśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ||17||

(17,1.18 ) tvamindrastvaṃ mahendrastvaṃ lokastvaṃ prajāpatiḥ |
(17,1.18 ) tubhyaṃ yajño vi tāyate tubhyaṃ juhvati juhvatastavedviṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi |
(17,1.18 ) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhirviśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ||18||

(17,1.19 ) asati satpratiṣṭhitaṃ sati bhūtaṃ pratiṣṭhitam |
(17,1.19 ) bhūtaṃ ha bhavya āhitaṃ bhavyaṃ bhūte pratiṣṭhitaṃ tavedviṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi |
(17,1.19 ) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhirviśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ||19||

(17,1.20 ) śukro'si bhrājo'si |
(17,1.20 ) sa yathā tvaṃ bhrājatā bhrājo'syevāhaṃ bhrājatā bhrājyāsam ||20|| {2}

(17,1.21 ) rucirasi roco'si |
(17,1.21 ) sa yathā tvaṃ rucyā roco'syevāhaṃ paśubhiśca brāhmaṇavarcasena ca ruciṣīya ||21||

(17,1.22 ) udyate nama udāyate nama uditāya namaḥ |
(17,1.22 ) virāje namaḥ svarāje namaḥ samrāje namaḥ ||22||

(17,1.23 ) astaṃyate namo'stameṣyate namo'stamitāya namaḥ |
(17,1.23 ) virāje namaḥ svarāje namaḥ samrāje namaḥ ||23||

(17,1.24 ) udagādayamādityo viśvena tapasā saha |
(17,1.24 ) sapatnān mahyaṃ randhayan mā cāhaṃ dviṣate radhaṃ tavedviṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi |
(17,1.24 ) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhirviśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ||24||

(17,1.25 ) āditya nāvamārukṣaḥ śatāritrāṃ svastaye |
(17,1.25 ) aharmātyapīparo rātriṃ satrāti pāraya ||25||

(17,1.26 ) sūrya nāvamārukṣaḥ śatāritrāṃ svastaye |
(17,1.26 ) rātriṃ mātyapīparo'haḥ satrāti pāraya ||26||

(17,1.27 ) prajāpaterāvṛto brahmaṇā varmaṇāhaṃ kaśyapasya jyotiṣā varcasā ca |
(17,1.27 ) jaradaṣṭiḥ kṛtavīryo vihāyāḥ sahasrāyuḥ sukṛtaścareyam ||27||

(17,1.28 ) parivṛto brahmaṇā varmaṇāhaṃ kaśyapasya jyotiṣā varcasā ca |
(17,1.28 ) mā mā prāpann iṣavo daivyā yā mā mānuṣīravasṛṣṭāḥ vadhāya ||28||

(17,1.29 ) ṛtena gupta ṛtubhiśca sarvairbhūtena gupto bhavyena cāham |
(17,1.29 ) mā mā prāpatpāpmā mota mṛtyurantardadhe'haṃ salilena vācaḥ ||29||

(17,1.30 ) agnirmā goptā pari pātu viśvataḥ udyantsūryo nudatāṃ mṛtyupāśān |
(17,1.30 ) vyuchantīruṣasaḥ parvatā dhruvāḥ sahasraṃ prāṇā mayyā yatantām ||30|| {3}



(18,1.1 ) o citsakhāyaṃ sakhyā vavṛtyāṃ tiraḥ puru cidarṇavaṃ jaganvān |
(18,1.1 ) piturnapātamā dadhīta vedhā adhi kṣami prataraṃ dīdhyānaḥ ||1||

(18,1.2 ) na te sakhā sakhyaṃ vaṣṭyetatsalakṣmā yadviṣurūpā bhavati |
(18,1.2 ) mahasputrāso asurasya vīrā divo dhartāra urviyā pari khyan ||2||

(18,1.3 ) uśanti ghā te amṛtāsa etadekasya cittyajasaṃ martyasya |
(18,1.3 ) ni te mano manasi dhāyyasme janyuḥ patistanvamā viviṣyāḥ ||3||

(18,1.4 ) na yatpurā cakṛmā kaddha nūnamṛtaṃ vadanto anṛtaṃ rapema |
(18,1.4 ) gandharvo apsvapyā ca yoṣā sā nau nābhiḥ paramaṃ jāmi tan nau ||4||

(18,1.5 ) garbhe nu nau janitā dampatī kardevastvaṣṭā savitā viśvarūpaḥ |
(18,1.5 ) nakirasya pra minanti vratāni veda nāvasya pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||5||

(18,1.6 ) ko adya yuṅkte dhuri gā ṛtasya śimīvato bhāmino durhṛṇāyūn |
(18,1.6 ) āsanniṣūn hṛtsvaso mayobhūn ya eṣāṃ bhṛtyāmṛṇadhatsa jīvāt||6||

(18,1.7 ) ko asya veda prathamasyāhnaḥ ka īṃ dadarśa ka iha pra vocat|
(18,1.7 ) bṛhan mitrasya varuṇasya dhāma kadu brava āhano vīcyā nṝn ||7||

(18,1.8 ) yamasya mā yamyaṃ kāma āgantsamāne yonau sahaśeyyāya |
(18,1.8 ) jāyeva patye tanvaṃ riricyāṃ vi cidvṛheva rathyeva cakrā ||8||

(18,1.9 ) na tiṣṭhanti na ni miṣantyete devānāṃ spaśa iha ye caranti |
(18,1.9 ) anyena madāhano yāhi tūyaṃ tena vi vṛha rathyeva cakrā ||9||

(18,1.10 ) rātrībhirasmā ahabhirdaśasyetsūryasya cakṣurmuhurun mimīyāt|
(18,1.10 ) divā pṛthivyā mithunā sabandhū yamīryamasya vivṛhādajāmi ||10|| {1}

(18,1.11 ) ā ghā tā gachān uttarā yugāni yatra jāmayaḥ kṛṇavann ajāmi |
(18,1.11 ) upa barbṛhi vṛṣabhāya bāhumanyamichasva subhage patiṃ mat||11||
(18,1.12 ) kiṃ bhrātāsadyadanāthaṃ bhavāti kimu svasā yan nirṛtirnigachāt|
(18,1.12 ) kāmamūtā bahvetadrapāmi tanvā me tanvaṃ saṃ pipṛgdhi ||12||

(18,1.13 ) na te nāthaṃ yamyatrāhamasmi na te tanūṃ tanvā saṃ papṛcyām |
(18,1.13 ) anyena matpramudaḥ kalpayasva na te bhrātā subhage vaṣṭyetat||13||

(18,1.14 ) na vā u te tanūṃ tanvā saṃ pipṛcyāṃ pāpamāhuryaḥ svasāraṃ nigachāt|
(18,1.14 ) asaṃyadetan manaso hṛdo me bhrātā svasuḥ śayane yacchayīya ||14||

(18,1.15 ) bato batāsi yama naiva te mano hṛdayaṃ cāvidāmā |
(18,1.15 ) anyā kila tvāṃ kakṣyeva yuktaṃ pari ṣvajātau libujeva vṛkṣam ||15||

(18,1.16 ) anyamū ṣu yamyanya u tvāṃ pari ṣvajātau libujeva vṛkṣam |
(18,1.16 ) tasya vā tvaṃ mana ichā sa vā tavādhā kṛṇuṣva saṃvidaṃ subhadrām ||16||

(18,1.17 ) trīṇi chandāṃsi kavayo vi yetire pururūpaṃ darśataṃ viśvacakṣaṇam |
(18,1.17 ) āpo vātā oṣadhayastānyekasmin bhuvana ārpitāni ||17||
(18,1.18 ) vṛṣā vṛṣṇe duduhe dohasā divaḥ payāṃsi yahvo aditeradābhyaḥ |
(18,1.18 ) viśvaṃ sa veda varuṇo yathā dhiyā sa yajñiyo yajati yajñiyāmṛtūn ||18||

(18,1.19 ) rapadgandharvīrapyā ca yoṣaṇā nadasya nāde pari pātu no manaḥ |
(18,1.19 ) iṣṭasya madhye aditirni dhātu no bhrātā no jyeṣṭhaḥ prathamo vi vocati ||19||

(18,1.20 ) so citnu bhadrā kṣumatī yaśasvatyuṣā uvāsa manave svarvatī |
(18,1.20 ) yadīmuśantamuśatāmanu kratumagniṃ hotāraṃ vidathāya jījanan ||20|| {2}

(18,1.21 ) adha tyaṃ drapsaṃ vibhvaṃ vicakṣanaṃ virābharadiṣiraḥ śyeno adhvare |
(18,1.21 ) yadī viśo vṛṇate dasmamāryā agniṃ hotāramadha dhīrajāyata ||21||

(18,1.22 ) sadāsi raṇvo yavaseva puṣyate hotrābhiragne manuṣaḥ svadhvaraḥ |
(18,1.22 ) viprasya vā yacchaśamāna ukthyo vājaṃ sasavāmupayāsi bhūribhiḥ ||22||

(18,1.23 ) udīraya pitarā jāra ā bhagamiyakṣati haryato hṛtta iṣyati |
(18,1.23 ) vivakti vahniḥ svapasyate makhastaviṣyate asuro vepate matī ||23||

(18,1.24 ) yaste agne sumatiṃ marto akhyatsahasaḥ sūno ati sa pra śṛṇve |
(18,1.24 ) iṣaṃ dadhāno vahamāno aśvairā sa dyumāmamavān bhūṣati dyūn ||24||

(18,1.25 ) śrudhī no agne sadane sadhasthe yukṣvā rathamamṛtasya dravitnum |
(18,1.25 ) ā no vaha rodasī devaputre mākirdevānāmapa bhūriha syāḥ ||25||

(18,1.26 ) yadagna eṣā samitirbhavāti devī deveṣu yajatā yajatra |
(18,1.26 ) ratnā ca yadvibhajāsi svadhāvo bhāgaṃ no atra vasumantaṃ vītāt||26||

(18,1.27 ) anvagniruṣasāmagramakhyadanvahāni prathamo jātavedāḥ |
(18,1.27 ) anu sūrya uṣaso anu raśmīn dyāvāpṛthivī ā viveśa ||27||

(18,1.28 ) pratyagniruṣasāmagramakhyatpratyahāni prathamo jātavedāḥ |
(18,1.28 ) prati sūryasya purudhā ca raśmīn prati dyāvāpṛthivī ā tatāna ||28||

(18,1.29 ) dyāvā ha kṣāmā prathame ṛtenābhiśrāve bhavataḥ satyavācā |
(18,1.29 ) devo yan martān yajathāya kṛṇvantsīdaddhotā pratyaṅsvamasuṃ yan ||29||

(18,1.30 ) devo devān paribhūrṛtena vahā no havyaṃ prathamaścikitvān |
(18,1.30 ) dhūmaketuḥ samidhā bhāṛjīko mandro hotā nityo vācā yajīyān ||30|| {3}
(18,1.31 ) arcāmi vāṃ vardhāyāpo ghṛtasnū dyāvābhūmī śṛṇutaṃ rodasī me |
(18,1.31 ) ahā yaddevā asunītimāyan madhvā no atra pitarā śiśītām ||31||

(18,1.32 ) svāvṛgdevasyāmṛtaṃ yadī gorato jātāso dhārayanta urvī |
(18,1.32 ) viśve devā anu tatte yajurgurduhe yadenī divyaṃ ghṛtaṃ vāḥ ||32||

(18,1.33 ) kiṃ svin no rājā jagṛhe kadasyāti vrataṃ cakṛmā ko vi veda |
(18,1.33 ) mitrasciddhi ṣmā juhurāṇo devāṃ chloko na yātāmapi vājo asti ||33||

(18,1.34 ) durmantvatrāmṛtasya nāma salakṣmā yadviṣurūpā bhavāti |
(18,1.34 ) yamasya yo manavate sumantvagne tamṛṣva pāhyaprayuchan ||34||
(18,1.35 ) yasmin devā vidathe mādayante vivasvataḥ sadane dhārayante |
(18,1.35 ) sūrye jyotiradadhurmāsyaktūn pari dyotaniṃ carato ajasrā ||35||

(18,1.36 ) yasmin devā manmani saṃcarantyapīcye na vayamasya vidma |
(18,1.36 ) mitro no atrāditiranāgāntsavitā devo varuṇāya vocat||36||

(18,1.37 ) sakhāya ā śiṣāmahe brahmendrāya vajriṇe |
(18,1.37 ) stuṣa ū ṣu nṛtamāya dhṛṣṇave ||37||

(18,1.38 ) śavasā hyasi śruto vṛtrahatyena vṛtrahā |
(18,1.38 ) maghairmaghono ati śūra dāśasi ||38||

(18,1.39 ) stego na kṣāmatyeṣi pṛthivīṃ mahī no vātā iha vāntu bhūmau |
(18,1.39 ) mitro no atra varuṇo yujamāno agnirvane na vyasṛṣṭa śokam ||39||

(18,1.40 ) stuhi śrutaṃ gartasadaṃ janānāṃ rājānaṃ bhīmamupahatnumugram |
(18,1.40 ) mṛḍā jaritre rudra stavāno anyamasmatte ni vapantu senyam ||40|| {4}

(18,1.41 ) sarasvatīṃ devayanto havante sarasvatīmadhvare tāyamāne |
(18,1.41 ) sarasvatīṃ sukṛto havante sarasvatī dāśuṣe vāryaṃ dāt||41||

(18,1.42 ) sarasvatīṃ pitaro havante dakṣinā yajñamabhinakṣamāṇāḥ |
(18,1.42 ) āsadyāsmin barhiṣi mādayadhvamanamīvā iṣa ā dhehyasme ||42||

(18,1.43 ) sarasvati yā sarathaṃ yayāthokthaiḥ svadhābhirdevi pitṛbhirmadantī |
(18,1.43 ) sahasrārghamiḍo atra bhāgaṃ rāyaspoṣaṃ yajamānāya dhehi ||43||

(18,1.44 ) udīratāmavara utparāsa un madhyamāḥ pitaraḥ somyāsaḥ |
(18,1.44 ) asuṃ ya īyuravṛkā ṛtajñāste no'vantu pitaro haveṣu ||44||

(18,1.45 ) āhaṃ pitṝntsuvidatrāmavitsi napātaṃ ca vikramaṇaṃ ca viṣṇoḥ |
(18,1.45 ) barhiṣado ye svadhayā sutasya bhajanta pitvasta ihāgamiṣṭhāḥ ||45||

(18,1.46 ) idaṃ pitṛbhyo namo astvadya ye pūrvāso ye aparāsa īyuḥ |
(18,1.46 ) ye pārthive rajasyā niṣaktā ye vā nūnaṃ suvṛjanāsu dikṣu ||46||

(18,1.47 ) mātalī kavyairyamo aṅgirobhirbṛhaspatirṛkvabhirvāvṛdhānaḥ |
(18,1.47 ) yāṃśca devā vāvṛdhurye ca devāṃste no'vantu pitaro haveṣu ||47||

(18,1.48 ) svāduṣkilāyaṃ madhumāmutāyaṃ tīvraḥ kilāyaṃ rasavāmutāyam |
(18,1.48 ) uto nvasya papivāṃsamindraṃ na kaścana sahata āhaveṣu ||48||

(18,1.49 ) pareyivāṃsaṃ pravato mahīriti bahubhyaḥ panthāmanupaspaśānam |
(18,1.49 ) vaivasvataṃ saṃgamanaṃ janānāṃ yamaṃ rājānaṃ haviṣā saparyata ||49||

(18,1.50 ) yamo no gātuṃ prathamo viveda naiṣā gavyūtirapabhartavā u |
(18,1.50 ) yatrā naḥ pūrve pitaraḥ paretā enā jajñānāḥ pathyā anu svāḥ ||50|| {5}

(18,1.51 ) barhiṣadaḥ pitara ūtyarvāgimā vo havyā cakṛmā juṣadhvam |
(18,1.51 ) ta ā gatāvasā śaṃtamenādhā naḥ śaṃ yorarapo dadhāta ||51||

(18,1.52 ) ācyā jānu dakṣiṇato niṣadyedaṃ no havirabhi gṛṇantu viśve |
(18,1.52 ) mā hiṃsiṣṭa pitaraḥ kena cin no yadva āgaḥ puruṣatā karāma ||52||

(18,1.53 ) tvaṣṭā duhitre vahatuṃ kṛṇoti tenedaṃ viśvaṃ bhuvanaṃ sameti |
(18,1.53 ) yamasya mātā paryuhyamānā maho jāyā vivasvato nanāśa ||53||

(18,1.54 ) prehi prehi pathibhiḥ pūryāṇairyenā te pūrve pitaraḥ paretāḥ |
(18,1.54 ) ubhā rājānau svadhayā madantau yamaṃ paśyāsi varuṇaṃ ca devam ||54||

(18,1.55 ) apeta vīta vi ca sarpatāto'smā etaṃ pitaro lokamakran |
(18,1.55 ) ahobhiradbhiraktubhirvyaktaṃ yamo dadātyavasānamasmai ||55||

(18,1.56 ) uśantastvedhīmahyuśantaḥ samidhīmahi |
(18,1.56 ) uśann uśata ā vaha pitṝn haviṣe attave ||56||

(18,1.57 ) dyumantastvedhīmahi dyumantaḥ samidhīmahi |
(18,1.57 ) dyumān dyumata ā vaha pitṝn haviṣe attave ||57||

(18,1.58 ) aṅgiraso naḥ pitaro navagvā atharvāṇo bhṛgavaḥ somyāsaḥ |
(18,1.58 ) teṣāṃ vayaṃ sumatau yajñiyānāmapi bhadre saumanase syāma ||58||

(18,1.59 ) aṅgirobhiryajñiyairā gahīha yama vairūpairiha mādayasva |
(18,1.59 ) vivasvantaṃ huve yaḥ pitā te'smin barhiṣyā niṣadya ||59||

(18,1.60 ) imaṃ yama prastaramā hi rohāṅgirobhiḥ pitṛbhiḥ saṃvidānaḥ |
(18,1.60 ) ā tvā mantrāḥ kaviśastā vahantvenā rājan haviṣo mādayasva ||60||

(18,1.61 ) ita eta udāruhan divaspṛṣṭhānvāruhan |
(18,1.61 ) pra bhūrjayo yathā pathā dyāmaṅgiraso yayuḥ ||61|| {6}



(18,2.1 ) yamāya somaḥ pavate yamāya kriyate haviḥ |
(18,2.1 ) yamaṃ ha yajño gachatyagnidūto araṃkṛtaḥ ||1||

(18,2.2 ) yamāya madhumattamaṃ juhotā pra ca tiṣṭhata |
(18,2.2 ) idaṃ nama ṛṣibhyaḥ pūrvajebhyaḥ pūrvebhyaḥ pathikṛdbhyaḥ ||2||

(18,2.3 ) yamāya ghṛtavatpayo rājñe havirjuhotana |
(18,2.3 ) sa no jīveṣvā yameddīrghamāyuḥ pra jīvase ||3||

(18,2.4 ) mainamagne vi daho mābhi śūśuco māsya tvacaṃ cikṣipo mā śarīram |
(18,2.4 ) śṛtaṃ yadā karasi jātavedo'themenaṃ pra hiṇutātpitṝṃrupa ||4||

(18,2.5 ) yadā śṛtaṃ kṛṇavo jātavedo'themamenaṃ pari dattātpitṛbhyaḥ |
(18,2.5 ) yado gachātyasunītimetāmatha devānāṃ vaśanīrbhavāti ||5||

(18,2.6 ) trikadrukebhiḥ pavate ṣaḍurvīrekamidbṛhat|
(18,2.6 ) triṣṭubgāyatrī chandāṃsi sarvā tā yama ārpitā ||6||

(18,2.7 ) sūryaṃ cakṣuṣā gacha vātamātmanā divaṃ ca gacha pṛthivīṃ ca dharmabhiḥ |
(18,2.7 ) apo vā gacha yadi tatra te hitamoṣadhīṣu prati tiṣṭhā śarīraiḥ ||7||

(18,2.8 ) ajo bhāgastapasastaṃ tapasva taṃ te śocistapatu taṃ te arciḥ |
(18,2.8 ) yāste śivāstanvo jātavedastābhirvahainaṃ sukṛtāmu lokam ||8||

(18,2.9 ) yāste śocayo raṃhayo jātavedo yābhirāpṛṇāsi divamantarikṣam |
(18,2.9 ) ajaṃ yantamanu tāḥ samṛṇvatāmathetarābhiḥ śivatamābhiḥ śṛtaṃ kṛdhi ||9||

(18,2.10 ) ava sṛja punaragne pitṛbhyo yasta āhutaścarati svadhāvān |
(18,2.10 ) āyurvasāna upa yātu śeṣaḥ saṃ gachatāṃ tanvā suvarcāḥ ||10|| {7}

(18,2.11 ) ati drava śvānau sārameyau caturakṣau śabalau sādhunā pathā |
(18,2.11 ) adhā pitṝntsuvidatrāmapīhi yamena ye sadhamādaṃ madanti ||11||

(18,2.12 ) yau te śvānau yama rakṣitārau caturakṣau pathiṣadī nṛcakṣasā |
(18,2.12 ) tābhyāṃ rājan pari dhehyenaṃ svastyasmā anamīvaṃ ca dhehi ||12||

(18,2.13 ) urūṇasāvasutṛpāvudumbalau yamasya dūtau carato janāmanu |
(18,2.13 ) tāvasmabhyaṃ dṛśaye sūryāya punardātāmasumadyeha bhadram ||13||

(18,2.14 ) soma ekebhyaḥ pavate ghṛtameka upāsate |
(18,2.14 ) yebhyo madhu pradhāvati tāṃścidevāpi gachatāt||14||

(18,2.15 ) ye citpūrva ṛtasātā ṛtajātā ṛtāvṛdhaḥ |
(18,2.15 ) ṛṣīn tapasvato yama tapojāmapi gachatāt||15||

(18,2.16 ) tapasā ye anādhṛṣyāstapasā ye svaryayuḥ |
(18,2.16 ) tapo ye cakrire mahastāṃścidevāpi gachatāt||16||

(18,2.17 ) ye yudhyante pradhaneṣu śūrāso ye svartanūtyajaḥ |
(18,2.17 ) ye vā sahasradakṣiṇāstāṃ cidevāpi gachatāt||17||

(18,2.18 ) sahasraṇīthāḥ kavayo ye gopāyanti sūryam |
(18,2.18 ) ṛṣīn tapasvato yama tapojāmapi gachatāt||18||

(18,2.19 ) syonāsmai bhava pṛthivyanṛkṣarā niveśanī |
(18,2.19 ) yachāsmai śarma saprathāḥ ||19||

(18,2.20 ) asaṃbādhe pṛthivyā urau loke ni dhīyasva |
(18,2.20 ) svadhā yāścakṛṣe jīvan tāste santu madhuścutaḥ ||20|| {8}

(18,2.21 ) hvayāmi te manasā mana ihemān gṛhāmupa jujuṣāṇa ehi |
(18,2.21 ) saṃ gachasva pitṛbhiḥ saṃ yamena syonāstvā vātā upa vāntu śagmāḥ ||21||

(18,2.22 ) uttvā vahantu maruta udavāhā udaprutaḥ |
(18,2.22 ) ajena kṛṇvantaḥ śītaṃ varṣeṇokṣantu bāliti ||22||

(18,2.23 ) udahvamāyurāyuṣe kratve dakṣāya jīvase |
(18,2.23 ) svān gachatu te mano adhā pitṝṃrupa drava ||23||

(18,2.24 ) mā te mano māsormāṅgānāṃ mā rasasya te |
(18,2.24 ) mā te hāsta tanvaḥ kiṃ caneha ||24||

(18,2.25 ) mā tvā vṛkṣaḥ saṃ bādhiṣṭa mā devī pṛthivī mahī |
(18,2.25 ) lokaṃ pitṛṣu vittvaidhasva yamarājasu ||25||

(18,2.26 ) yatte aṅgamatihitaṃ parācairapānaḥ prāṇo ya u vā te paretaḥ |
(18,2.26 ) tatte saṃgatya pitaraḥ sanīḍā ghāsādghāsaṃ punarā veśayantu ||26||

(18,2.27 ) apemaṃ jīvā arudhan gṛhebhyastaṃ nirvahata pari grāmāditaḥ |
(18,2.27 ) mṛtyuryamasyāsīddūtaḥ pracetā asūn pitṛbhyo gamayāṃ cakāra ||27||

(18,2.28 ) ye dasyavaḥ pitṛṣu praviṣṭā jñātimukhā ahutādaścaranti |
(18,2.28 ) parāpuro nipuro ye bharantyagniṣṭān asmātpra dhamāti yajñāt||28||

(18,2.29 ) saṃ viśantviha pitaraḥ svā naḥ syonaṃ kṛṇvantaḥ pratiranta āyuḥ |
(18,2.29 ) tebhyaḥ śakema haviṣā nakṣamāṇā jyogjīvantaḥ śaradaḥ purūcīḥ ||29||

(18,2.30 ) yāṃ te dhenuṃ nipṛṇāmi yamu kṣīra odanam |
(18,2.30 ) tenā janasyāso bhartā yo'trāsadajīvanaḥ ||30|| {9}

(18,2.31 ) aśvāvatīṃ pra tara yā suśevā rkṣākaṃ vā prataraṃ navīyaḥ |
(18,2.31 ) yastvā jaghāna vadhyaḥ so astu mā so anyadvidata bhāgadheyam ||31||

(18,2.32 ) yamaḥ paro'varo vivasvān tataḥ paraṃ nāti paśyāmi kiṃ cana |
(18,2.32 ) yame adhvaro adhi me niviṣṭo bhuvo vivasvān anvātatāna ||32||

(18,2.33 ) apāgūhann amṛtāṃ martyebhyaḥ kṛtvā savarṇāmadadhurvivasvate |
(18,2.33 ) utāśvināvabharadyattadāsīdajahādu dvā mithunā saraṇyūḥ ||33||

(18,2.34 ) ye nikhātā ye paroptā ye dagdhā ye coddhitāḥ |
(18,2.34 ) sarvāṃstān agna ā vaha pitṝn haviṣe attave ||34||

(18,2.35 ) ye agnidagdhā ye anagnidagdhā madhye divaḥ svadhayā mādayante |
(18,2.35 ) tvaṃ tān vettha yadi te jātavedaḥ svadhayā yajñaṃ svadhitiṃ juṣantām ||35||

(18,2.36 ) śaṃ tapa māti tapo agne mā tanvaṃ tapaḥ |
(18,2.36 ) vaṇeṣu śuṣmo astu te pṛthivyāmastu yaddharaḥ ||36||

(18,2.37 ) dadāmyasmā avasānametadya eṣa āgan mama cedabhūdiha |
(18,2.37 ) yamaścikitvān pratyetadāha mamaiṣa rāya upa tiṣṭhatāmiha ||37||

(18,2.38 ) imāṃ mātrāṃ mimīmahe yathāparaṃ na māsātai |
(18,2.38 ) śate śaratsu no purā ||38||

(18,2.39 ) premāṃ mātrāṃ mimīmahe yathāparaṃ na māsātai |
(18,2.39 ) śate śaratsu no purā ||39||

(18,2.40 ) apemāṃ mātrāṃ mimīmahe yathāparaṃ na māsātai |
(18,2.40 ) śate śaratsu no purā ||40|| {10}

(18,2.41 ) vīmāṃ mātrāṃ mimīmahe yathāparaṃ na māsātai |
(18,2.41 ) śate śaratsu no purā ||41||

(18,2.42 ) nirimāṃ mātrāṃ mimīmahe yathāparaṃ na māsātai |
(18,2.42 ) śate śaratsu no purā ||42||
(18,2.43 ) udimāṃ mātrāṃ mimīmahe yathāparaṃ na māsātai |
(18,2.43 ) śate śaratsu no purā ||43||

(18,2.44 ) samimāṃ mātrāṃ mimīmahe yathāparaṃ na māsātai |
(18,2.44 ) śate śaratsu no purā ||44||

(18,2.45 ) amāsi mātrāṃ svaragāmāyuṣmān bhūyāsam |
(18,2.45 ) yathāparaṃ na māsātai śate śaratsu no purā ||45||
(18,2.46 ) prāṇo apāno vyāna āyuścakṣurdṛśaye sūryāya |
(18,2.46 ) aparipareṇa pathā yamarājñaḥ pitṝn gacha ||46||

(18,2.47 ) ye agravaḥ śaśamānāḥ pareyurhitvā dveṣāṃsyanapatyavantaḥ |
(18,2.47 ) te dyāmudityāvidanta lokaṃ nākasya pṛṣṭhe adhi dīdhyānāḥ ||47||

(18,2.48 ) udanvatī dyauravamā pīlumatīti madhyamā |
(18,2.48 ) tṛtīyā ha pradyauriti yasyāṃ pitara āsate ||48||

(18,2.49 ) ye na pituḥ pitaro ye pitāmahā ya āviviśururvantarikṣam |
(18,2.49 ) ya ākṣiyanti pṛthivīmuta dyāṃ tebhyaḥ pitṛbhyo namasā vidhema ||49||

(18,2.50 ) idamidvā u nāparaṃ divi paśyasi sūryam |
(18,2.50 ) mātā putraṃ yathā sicābhyenaṃ bhūma ūrṇuhi ||50|| {11}

(18,2.51 ) idamidvā u nāparaṃ jarasyanyadito'param |
(18,2.51 ) jāyā patimiva vāsasābhyenaṃ bhūma ūrṇuhi ||51||

(18,2.52 ) abhi tvorṇomi pṛthivyā māturvastreṇa bhadrayā |
(18,2.52 ) jīveṣu bhadraṃ tan mayi svadhā pitṛṣu sā tvayi ||52||

(18,2.53 ) agnīṣomā pathikṛtā syonaṃ devebhyo ratnaṃ dadhathurvi lokam |
(18,2.53 ) upa preṣyantaṃ pūṣaṇaṃ yo vahātyañjoyānaiḥ pathibhistatra gachatam ||53||

(18,2.54 ) pūṣā tvetaścyāvayatu pra vidvān anaṣṭapaśurbhuvanasya gopāḥ |
(18,2.54 ) sa tvaitebhyaḥ pari dadatpitṛbhyo'gnirdevebhyaḥ suvidatriyebhyaḥ ||54||

(18,2.55 ) āyurviśvāyuḥ pari pātu tvā pūṣā tvā pātu prapathe purastāt|
(18,2.55 ) yatrāsate sukṛto yatra ta īyustatra tvā devaḥ savitā dadhātu ||55||

(18,2.56 ) imau yunajmi te vahnī asunītāya voḍhave |
(18,2.56 ) tābhyāṃ yamasya sādanaṃ samitiścāva gachatāt||56||

(18,2.57 ) etattvā vāsaḥ prathamaṃ nvāgann apaitadūha yadihābibhaḥ purā |
(18,2.57 ) iṣṭāpūrtamanusaṃkrāma vidvān yatra te dattaṃ bahudhā vibandhuṣu ||57||

(18,2.58 ) agnervarma pari gobhirvyayasva saṃ prorṇuṣva medasā pīvasā ca |
(18,2.58 ) nettvā dhṛṣṇurharasā jarhṛṣāṇo dadhṛgvidhakṣan parīṅkhayātai ||58||

(18,2.59 ) daṇḍaṃ hastādādadāno gatāsoḥ saha śrotreṇa varcasā balena |
(18,2.59 ) atraiva tvamiha vayaṃ suvīrā viśvā mṛdho abhimātīrjayema ||59||

(18,2.60 ) dhanurhastādādadāno mṛtasya saha kṣatreṇa varcasā balena |
(18,2.60 ) samāgṛbhāya vasu bhūri puṣṭamarvāṅtvamehyupa jīvalokam ||60|| {12}



(18,3.1 ) iyaṃ nārī patilokaṃ vṛṇānā ni padyata upa tvā martya pretam |
(18,3.1 ) dharmaṃ purāṇamanupālayantī tasyai prajāṃ draviṇaṃ ceha dhehi ||1||

(18,3.2 ) udīrṣva nāryabhi jīvalokaṃ gatāsumetamupa śeṣa ehi |
(18,3.2 ) hastagrābhasya dadhiṣostavedaṃ patyurjanitvamabhi saṃ babhūtha ||2||

(18,3.3 ) apaśyaṃ yuvatiṃ nīyamānāṃ jīvāṃ mṛtebhyaḥ pariṇīyamānām |
(18,3.3 ) andhena yattamasā prāvṛtāsītprākto apācīmanayaṃ tadenām ||3||

(18,3.4 ) prajānatyaghnye jīvalokaṃ devānāṃ panthāmanusaṃcarantī |
(18,3.4 ) ayaṃ te gopatistaṃ juṣasva svargaṃ lokamadhi rohayainam ||4||

(18,3.5 ) upa dyāmupa vetasamavattaro nadīnām |
(18,3.5 ) agne pittamapāmasi ||5||

(18,3.6 ) yaṃ tvamagne samadahastamu nirvāpaya punaḥ |
(18,3.6 ) kyāmbūratra rohatu śāṇḍadūrvā vyalkaśā ||6||

(18,3.7 ) idaṃ ta ekaṃ pura ū ta ekaṃ tṛtīyena jyotiṣā saṃ viśasva |
(18,3.7 ) saṃveśane tanvā cāruredhi priyo devānāṃ parame sadhasthe ||7||

(18,3.8 ) uttiṣṭha prehi pra dravaukaḥ kṛṇuṣva salile sadhasthe |
(18,3.8 ) tatra tvaṃ pitṛbhiḥ saṃvidānaḥ saṃ somena madasva saṃ svadhābhiḥ ||8||

(18,3.9 ) pra cyavasva tanvaṃ saṃ bharasva mā te gātrā vi hāyi mo śarīram |
(18,3.9 ) mano niviṣṭamanusaṃviśasva yatra bhūmerjuṣase tatra gacha ||9||

(18,3.10 ) varcasā māṃ pitaraḥ somyāso añjantu devā madhunā ghṛtena |
(18,3.10 ) cakṣuṣe mā prataraṃ tārayanto jarase mā jaradaṣṭiṃ vardhantu ||10|| {13}

(18,3.11 ) varcasā māṃ samanaktvagnirmedhāṃ me viṣṇurnyanaktvāsan |
(18,3.11 ) rayiṃ me viśve ni yachantu devāḥ syonā māpaḥ pavanaiḥ punantu ||11||

(18,3.12 ) mitrāvaruṇā pari māmadhātāmādityā mā svaravo vardhayantu |
(18,3.12 ) varco ma indro nyanaktu hastayorjaradaṣṭiṃ mā savitā kṛṇotu ||12||

(18,3.13 ) yo mamāra prathamo martyānāṃ yaḥ preyāya prathamo lokametam |
(18,3.13 ) vaivasvataṃ saṃgamanaṃ janānāṃ yamaṃ rājānaṃ haviṣā saparyata ||13||

(18,3.14 ) parā yāta pitara ā ca yātāyaṃ vo yajño madhunā samaktaḥ |
(18,3.14 ) datto asmabhyaṃ draviṇeha bhadraṃ rayiṃ ca naḥ sarvavīraṃ dadhāta ||14||

(18,3.15 ) kaṇvaḥ kakṣīvān purumīḍho agastyaḥ śyāvāśvaḥ sobharyarcanānāḥ |
(18,3.15 ) viśvāmitro'yaṃ jamadagniratriravantu naḥ kaśyapo vāmadevaḥ ||15||

(18,3.16 ) viśvāmitra jamadagne vasiṣṭha bharadvāja gotama vāmadeva |
(18,3.16 ) śardirno atriragrabhīn namobhiḥ susaṃśāsaḥ pitaro mṛḍatā naḥ ||16||

(18,3.17 ) kasye mṛjānā ati yanti ripramāyurdadhānāḥ prataraṃ navīyaḥ |
(18,3.17 ) āpyāyamānāḥ prajayā dhanenādha syāma surabhayo gṛheṣu ||17||

(18,3.18 ) añjate vyañjate samañjate kratuṃ rihanti madhunābhyañjate |
(18,3.18 ) sindhorucchvāse patayantamukṣaṇaṃ hiraṇyapāvāḥ paśumāsu gṛhnate ||18||

(18,3.19 ) yadvo mudraṃ pitaraḥ somyaṃ ca teno sacadhvaṃ svayaśaso hi bhūta |
(18,3.19 ) te arvāṇaḥ kavaya ā śṛṇota suvidatrā vidathe huyamānāḥ ||19||

(18,3.20 ) ye atrayo aṅgiraso navagvā iṣṭāvanto rātiṣāco dadhānāḥ |
(18,3.20 ) dakṣiṇāvantaḥ sukṛto ya u sthāsadyāsmin barhiṣi mādayadhvam ||20|| {14}

(18,3.21 ) adhā yathā naḥ pitaraḥ parāsaḥ pratnāso agna ṛtamāśaśānāḥ |
(18,3.21 ) śucīdayan dīdhyata ukthaśasaḥ kṣāmā bhindanto aruṇīrapa vran ||21||

(18,3.22 ) sukarmānaḥ suruco devayanto ayo na devā janimā dhamantaḥ |
(18,3.22 ) śucanto agniṃ vāvṛdhanta indramurvīṃ gavyāṃ pariṣadaṃ no akran ||22||

(18,3.23 ) ā yūtheva kṣumati paśvo akhyaddevānāṃ janimāntyugraḥ |
(18,3.23 ) martāsaścidurvaśīrakṛpran vṛdhe cidarya uparasyāyoḥ ||23||

(18,3.24 ) akarma te svapaso abhūma ṛtamavasrann uṣaso vibhātīḥ |
(18,3.24 ) viśvaṃ tadbhadraṃ yadavanti devā bṛhadvadema vidathe suvīrāḥ ||24||

(18,3.25 ) indro mā marutvān prācyā diśaḥ pātu bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyāmivopari |
(18,3.25 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||25||

(18,3.26 ) dhātā mā nirṛtyā dakṣiṇāyā diśaḥ pātu bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyāmivopari |
(18,3.26 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||26||

(18,3.27 ) aditirmādityaiḥ pratīcyā diśaḥ pātu bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyāmivopari |
(18,3.27 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||27||

(18,3.28 ) somo mā viśvairdevairudīcyā diśaḥ pātu bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyāmivopari |
(18,3.28 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||28||

(18,3.29 ) dhartā ha tvā dharuṇo dhārayātā ūrdhvaṃ bhānuṃ savitā dyāmivopari |
(18,3.29 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||29||

(18,3.30 ) prācyāṃ tvā diśi purā samvṛtaḥ svadhāyāmā dadhāmi bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyāmivopari |
(18,3.30 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||30|| {15}

(18,3.31 ) dakṣiṇāyāṃ tvā diśi purā samvṛtaḥ svadhāyāmā dadhāmi bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyāmivopari |
(18,3.31 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||31||

(18,3.32 ) pratīcyāṃ tvā diśi purā saṃvṛtaḥ svadhāyāmā dadhāmi bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyāmivopari |
(18,3.32 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||32||

(18,3.33 ) udīcyāṃ tvā diśi purā samvṛtaḥ svadhāyāmā dadhāmi bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyāmivopari |
(18,3.33 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||33||

(18,3.34 ) dhruvāyāṃ tvā diśi purā saṃvṛtaḥ svadhāyāmā dadhāmi bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyāmivopari |
(18,3.34 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||34||

(18,3.35 ) ūrdhvāyāṃ tvā diśi purā samvṛtaḥ svadhāyāmā dadhāmi bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyāmivopari |
(18,3.35 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||35||

(18,3.36 ) dhartāsi dharuno'si vaṃsago'si ||36||

(18,3.37 ) udapūrasi madhupūrasi vātapūrasi ||37||

(18,3.38 ) itaśca māmutaścāvatāṃ yame iva yatamāne yadaitam |
(18,3.38 ) pra vāṃ bharan mānuṣā devayanto ā sīdatāṃ svamu lokaṃ vidāne ||38||

(18,3.39 ) svāsasthe bhavatamindave no yuje vāṃ brahma pūrvyaṃ namobhiḥ |
(18,3.39 ) vi śloka eti pathyeva sūriḥ śṛṇvantu viśve amṛtāsa etat||39||

(18,3.40 ) trīṇi padāni rupo anvarohaccatuṣpadīmanvetadvratena |
(18,3.40 ) akṣareṇa prati mimīte arkamṛtasya nābhāvabhi saṃ punāti ||40|| {16}

(18,3.41 ) devebhyaḥ kamavṛṇīta mṛtyuṃ prajāyai kimamṛtaṃ nāvṛṇīta |
(18,3.41 ) bṛhaspatiryajñamatanuta ṛṣiḥ priyāṃ yamastanvamā rireca ||41||

(18,3.42 ) tvamagna īḍito jātavedo'vāḍḍhavyāni surabhīṇi kṛtvā |
(18,3.42 ) prādāḥ pitṛbhyaḥ svadhayā te akṣann addhi tvaṃ deva prayatā havīṃṣi ||42||

(18,3.43 ) āsīnāso aruṇīnāmupasthe rayiṃ dhatta dāśuṣe martyāya |
(18,3.43 ) putrebhyaḥ pitarastasya vasvaḥ pra yachata ta ihorjaṃ dadhāta ||43||

(18,3.44 ) agniṣvāttāḥ pitara eha gachata sadaḥsadaḥ sadata supraṇītayaḥ |
(18,3.44 ) atto havīṃṣi prayatāni barhiṣi rayiṃ ca naḥ sarvavīraṃ dadhāta ||44||

(18,3.45 ) upahūtā naḥ pitaraḥ somyāso barhiṣyeṣu nidhiṣu priyeṣu |
(18,3.45 ) ta ā gamantu ta iha śruvantvadhi bruvantu te'vantvasmān ||45||

(18,3.46 ) ye naḥ pituḥ pitaro ye pitāmahā anūjahire somapīthaṃ vasiṣṭhāḥ |
(18,3.46 ) tebhiryamaḥ samrarāṇo havīṃṣyuśann uśadbhiḥ pratikāmamattu ||46||

(18,3.47 ) ye tātṛṣurdevatrā jehamānā hotrāvidaḥ stomataṣṭāso arkaiḥ |
(18,3.47 ) āgne yāhi sahasraṃ devavandaiḥ satyaiḥ kavibhirṛṣibhirgharmasadbhiḥ ||47||

(18,3.48 ) ye satyāso havirado haviṣpā indreṇa devaiḥ sarathaṃ tureṇa |
(18,3.48 ) āgne yāhi suvidatrebhirarvāṅparaiḥ pūrvairṛṣibhirgharmasadbhiḥ ||48||

(18,3.49 ) upa sarpa mātaraṃ bhūmimetāmuruvyacasaṃ pṛthivīṃ suśevām |
(18,3.49 ) ūrṇamradāḥ pṛthivī dakṣiṇāvata eṣā tvā pātu prapathe purastāt||49||

(18,3.50 ) ucchvañcasva pṛthivi mā ni bādhathāḥ sūpāyanāsmai bhava sūpasarpaṇā |
(18,3.50 ) mātā putraṃ yathā sicābhyenaṃ bhūma ūrṇuhi ||50|| {17}

(18,3.51 ) ucchvañcamānā pṛthivī su tiṣṭhatu sahasraṃ mita upa hi śrayantām |
(18,3.51 ) te gṛhāso ghṛtaścutaḥ syonā viśvāhāsmai śaraṇāḥ santvatra ||51||

(18,3.52 ) utte stabhnāmi pṛthivīṃ tvatparīmaṃ logaṃ nidadhan mo ahaṃ riṣam |
(18,3.52 ) etāṃ sthūṇāṃ pitaro dhārayanti te tatra yamaḥ sādanā te kṛṇotu ||52||

(18,3.53 ) imamagne camasaṃ mā vi jihvaraḥ priyo devānāmuta somyānām |
(18,3.53 ) ayaṃ yaścamaso devapānastasmin devā amṛtā mādayantām ||53||

(18,3.54 ) atharvā pūrṇaṃ camasaṃ yamindrāyābibharvājinīvate |
(18,3.54 ) tasmin kṛṇoti sukṛtasya bhakṣaṃ tasmin induḥ pavate viśvadānim ||54||

(18,3.55 ) yatte kṛṣṇaḥ śakuna ātutoda pipīlaḥ sarpa uta vā śvāpadaḥ |
(18,3.55 ) agniṣṭadviśvādagadaṃ kṛṇotu somaśca yo brāhmaṇāmāviveśa ||55||

(18,3.56 ) payasvatīroṣadhayaḥ payasvan māmakaṃ payaḥ |
(18,3.56 ) apāṃ payaso yatpayastena mā saha śumbhatu ||56||

(18,3.57 ) imā nārīravidhavāḥ supatnīrāñjanena sarpiṣā saṃ spṛśantām |
(18,3.57 ) anaśravo anamīvāḥ suratnā ā rohantu janayo yonimagre ||57||

(18,3.58 ) saṃ gachasva pitṛbhiḥ saṃ yameneṣṭāpūrtena parame vyoman |
(18,3.58 ) hitvāvadyaṃ punarastamehi saṃ gachatāṃ tanvā suvarcāḥ ||58||

(18,3.59 ) ye naḥ pituḥ pitaro ye pitāmahā ya āviviśururvantarikṣam |
(18,3.59 ) tebhyaḥ svarādasunītirno adya vathāvaśaṃ tanvaḥ kalpayāti ||59||

(18,3.60 ) śaṃ te nīhāro bhavatu śaṃ te pruṣvāva śīyatām |
(18,3.60 ) śītike śītikāvati hlādike hlādikāvati |
(18,3.60 ) maṇḍūkyapsu śaṃ bhuva imaṃ svagniṃ śamaya ||60|| {18}

(18,3.61 ) vivasvān no abhayaṃ kṛṇotu yaḥ sutrāmā jīradānuḥ sudānuḥ |
(18,3.61 ) iheme vīrā bahavo bhavantu gomadaśvavan mayyastu puṣṭam ||61||

(18,3.62 ) vivasvān no amṛtatve dadhātu paraitu mṛtyuramṛtaṃ na aitu |
(18,3.62 ) imān rakṣatu puruṣān ā jarimṇo mo sveṣāmasavo yamaṃ guḥ ||62||

(18,3.63 ) yo dadhre antarikṣe na mahnā pitṝṇāṃ kaviḥ pramatirmatīnām |
(18,3.63 ) tamarcata viśvamitrā havirbhiḥ sa no yamaḥ prataraṃ jīvase dhāt||63||

(18,3.64 ) ā rohata divamuttamāmṛṣayo mā bibhītana |
(18,3.64 ) somapāḥ somapāyina idaṃ vaḥ kriyate haviraganma jvotiruttamam ||64||

(18,3.65 ) pra ketunā bṛhatā bhātyagnirā rodasī vṛṣabho roravīti |
(18,3.65 ) divaścidantādupamāmudānaḍapāmupasthe mahiṣo vavardha ||65||

(18,3.66 ) nāke suparṇamupa yatpatantaṃ hrdā venanto abhyacakṣata tvā |
(18,3.66 ) hiraṇyapakṣaṃ varuṇasya dūtaṃ yamasya yonau śakunaṃ bhuranyum ||66||

(18,3.67 ) indra kratuṃ na ā bhara pitā putrebhyo yathā |
(18,3.67 ) śikṣā ṇo asmin puruhūta yāmani jīvā jyotiraśīmahi ||67||
(18,3.68 ) apūpāpihitān kumbhān yāṃste devā adhārayan |
(18,3.68 ) te te santu svadhāvanto madhumanto ghṛtaścutaḥ ||68||

(18,3.69 ) yāste dhānā anukirāmi tilamiśrā svadhāvatīḥ |
(18,3.69 ) tāste santu vibhvīḥ prabhvīstāste yamo rājānu manyatām ||69||

(18,3.70 ) punardehi vanaspate ya eṣa nihitastvayi |
(18,3.70 ) yathā yamasya sādana āsātau vidathā vadan ||70||

(18,3.71 ) ā rabhasva jātavedastejasvaddharo astu te |
(18,3.71 ) śarīramasya saṃ dahāthainaṃ dehi sukṛtāmu loke ||71||
(18,3.72 ) ye te pūrve parāgatā apare pitaraśca ye |
(18,3.72 ) tebhyo ghṛtasya kulyaitu śatadhārā vyundatī ||72||

(18,3.73 ) etadā roha vaya unmṛjānaḥ svā iha bṛhadu dīdayante |
(18,3.73 ) abhi prehi madhyato māpa hāsthāḥ pitṝnāṃ lokaṃ prathamo yo atra ||73|| {19}



(18,4.1 ) ā rohata janitrīṃ jātavedasaḥ pitṛyānaiḥ saṃ va ā rohayāmi |
(18,4.1 ) avāḍḍhavyeṣito havyavāha ījānaṃ yuktāḥ sukṛtāṃ dhatta loke ||1||

(18,4.2 ) devā yajñamṛtavaḥ kalpayanti haviḥ puroḍāśaṃ sruco yajñāyudhāni |
(18,4.2 ) tebhiryāhi pathibhirdevayānairyairījānāḥ svargaṃ yanti lokam ||2||

(18,4.3 ) ṛtasya panthāmanu paśya sādhvaṅgirasaḥ sukṛto yena yanti |
(18,4.3 ) tebhiryāhi pathibhiḥ svargaṃ yatrādityā madhu bhakṣayanti tṛtīye nāke adhi vi śrayasva ||3||

(18,4.4 ) trayaḥ suparṇā uparasya māyū nākasya pṛṣṭhe adhi viṣṭapi śritāḥ |
(18,4.4 ) svargā lokā amṛtena viṣṭhā iṣamūrjaṃ yajamānāya duhrām ||4||

(18,4.5 ) juhūrdādhāra dyāmupabhṛdantarikṣaṃ dhruvā dādhāra pṛthivīṃ pratiṣṭhām |
(18,4.5 ) pratīmāṃ lokā ghṛtapṛṣṭhāḥ svargāḥ kāmaṃkāmaṃ yajamānāya duhrām ||5||

(18,4.6 ) dhruva ā roha pṛthivīṃ viśvabhojasamantarikṣamupabhṛdā kramasva |
(18,4.6 ) juhu dyāṃ gacha yajamānena sākaṃ sruveṇa vatsena diśaḥ prapīnāḥ sarvā dhukṣvāhṛṇyamānaḥ ||6||
(18,4.7 ) tīrthaistaranti pravato mahīriti yajñakṛtaḥ sukṛto yena yanti |
(18,4.7 ) atrādadhuryajamānāya lokaṃ diśo bhūtāni yadakalpayanta ||7||

(18,4.8 ) aṅgirasāmayanaṃ pūrvo agnirādityānāmayanaṃ gārhapatyo dakṣiṇānāmayanaṃ dakṣiṇāgniḥ |
(18,4.8 ) mahimānamagnervihitasya brahmaṇā samaṅgaḥ sarva upa yāhi śagmaḥ ||8||

(18,4.9 ) pūrvo agniṣṭvā tapatu śaṃ purastācchaṃ paścāttapatu gārhapatyaḥ |
(18,4.9 ) dakṣiṇāgniṣṭe tapatu śarma varmottarato madhyato antarikṣāddiśodiśo agne pari pāhi ghorāt||9||

(18,4.10 ) yūyamagne śaṃtamābhistanūbhirījānamabhi lokaṃ svargam |
(18,4.10 ) aśvā bhūtvā pṛṣṭivāho vahātha yatra devaiḥ sadhamādaṃ madanti ||10|| {20}

(18,4.11 ) śamagne paścāttapa śaṃ purastācchamuttarācchamadharāttapainam |
(18,4.11 ) ekastredhā vihito jātavedaḥ samyagenaṃ dhehi sukṛtāmu loke ||11||

(18,4.12 ) śamagnayaḥ samiddhā ā rabhantāṃ prājāpatyaṃ medhyaṃ jātavedasaḥ |
(18,4.12 ) śṛtaṃ kṛṇvanta iha māva cikṣipan ||12||

(18,4.13 ) yajña eti vitataḥ kalpamāna ījānamabhi lokaṃ svargam |
(18,4.13 ) tamagnayaḥ sarvahutaṃ juṣantāṃ prājāpatyaṃ medhyaṃ jātavedasaḥ ||13||

(18,4.14 ) ījānaścitamārukṣadagniṃ nākasya pṛṣṭhāddivamutpatiṣyan |
(18,4.14 ) tasmai pra bhāti nabhaso jyotiṣīmāntsvargaḥ panthāḥ sukṛte devayānaḥ ||14||
