(18,4.15 ) agnirhotādhvaryuṣṭe bṛhaspatirindro brahmā dakṣiṇataste astu |
(18,4.15 ) huto'yaṃ saṃsthito yajña eti yatra pūrvamayanaṃ hutānām ||15||

(18,4.16 ) apūpavān kṣīravāṃścarureha sīdatu |
(18,4.16 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||16||

(18,4.17 ) apūpavān dadhivāṃścarureha sīdatu |
(18,4.17 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||17||

(18,4.18 ) apūpavān drapsavāṃścarureha sīdatu |
(18,4.18 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||18||

(18,4.19 ) apūpavān ghṛtavāṃścarureha sīdatu |
(18,4.19 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||19||

(18,4.20 ) apūpavān māṃsavāṃścarureha sīdatu |
(18,4.20 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||20|| {21}

(18,4.21 ) apūpavān annavāṃścarureha sīdatu |
(18,4.21 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||21||

(18,4.22 ) apūpavān madhumāṃścarureha sīdatu |
(18,4.22 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||22||

(18,4.23 ) apūpavān rasavāṃścarureha sīdatu |
(18,4.23 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||23||

(18,4.24 ) apūpavān apavāṃścarureha sīdatu |
(18,4.24 ) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ||24||

(18,4.25 ) apūpāpihitān kumbhān yāṃste devā adhārayan |
(18,4.25 ) te te santu svadhāvanto madhumanto ghṛtaścutaḥ ||25||

(18,4.26 ) yāste dhānā anukirāmi tilamiśrāḥ svadhāvatīḥ |
(18,4.26 ) tāste santūdbhvīḥ prabhvīstāste yamo rājānu manyatām ||26||

(18,4.27 ) akṣitiṃ bhūyasīm ||27||

(18,4.28 ) drapsaścaskanda pṛthivīmanu dyāmimaṃ ca yonimanu yaśca pūrvaḥ |
(18,4.28 ) samānaṃ yonimanu samcarantaṃ drapsaṃ juhomyanu sapta hotrāḥ ||28||

(18,4.29 ) śatadhāraṃ vāyumarkaṃ svarvidaṃ nṛcakṣasaste abhi cakṣate rayim |
(18,4.29 ) ye pṛnanti pra ca yachanti sarvadā te duhrate dakṣiṇāṃ saptamātaram ||29||

(18,4.30 ) kośaṃ duhanti kalaśaṃ caturbilamiḍāṃ dhenuṃ madhumatīṃ svastaye |
(18,4.30 ) ūrjaṃ madantīmaditiṃ janeṣvagne mā hiṃsīḥ parame vyoman ||30|| {22}

(18,4.31 ) etatte devaḥ savitā vāso dadāti bhartave |
(18,4.31 ) tattvaṃ yamasya rājye vasānastārpyaṃ cara ||31||

(18,4.32 ) dhānā dhenurabhavadvatso asyāstilo'bhavat|
(18,4.32 ) tāṃ vai yamasya rājye akṣitāmupa jīvati ||32||

(18,4.33 ) etāste asau dhenavaḥ kāmadughā bhavantu |
(18,4.33 ) enīḥ śyenīḥ sarūpā virūpāstilavatsā upa tiṣṭhantu tvātra ||33||

(18,4.34 ) enīrdhānā hariṇīḥ śyenīrasya kṛṣṇā dhānā rohiṇīrdhenavaste |
(18,4.34 ) tilavatsā ūrjamasmai duhānā viśvāhā santvanapasphurantīḥ ||34||

(18,4.35 ) vaiśvānare haviridaṃ juhomi sāhasraṃ śatadhāramutsam |
(18,4.35 ) sa bibharti pitaraṃ pitāmahān prapitāmahān bibharti pinvamānaḥ ||35||

(18,4.36 ) sahasradhāraṃ śatadhāramutsamakṣitaṃ vyacyamānaṃ salilasya pṛṣṭhe |
(18,4.36 ) ūrjaṃ duhānamanapasphurantamupāsate pitaraḥ svadhābhiḥ ||36||

(18,4.37 ) idaṃ kasāmbu cayanena citaṃ tatsajātā ava paśyateta |
(18,4.37 ) martyo'yamamṛtatvameti tasmai gṛhān kṛṇuta yāvatsabandhu ||37||

(18,4.38 ) ihaivaidhi dhanasanirihacitta ihakratuḥ |
(18,4.38 ) ihaidhi vīryavattaro vayodhā aparāhataḥ ||38||

(18,4.39 ) putraṃ pautramabhitarpayantīrāpo madhumatīrimāḥ |
(18,4.39 ) svadhāṃ pitṛbhyo amṛtaṃ duhānā āpo devīrubhayāṃstarpayantu ||39||

(18,4.40 ) āpo agniṃ pra hiṇuta pitṝṃrupemaṃ yajñaṃ pitaro me juṣantām |
(18,4.40 ) āsīnāmūrjamupa ye sacante te no rayiṃ sarvavīraṃ ni yachān ||40|| {23}

(18,4.41 ) samindhate amartyaṃ havyavāhaṃ ghṛtapriyam |
(18,4.41 ) sa veda nihitān nidhīn pitṝn parāvato gatān ||41||

(18,4.42 ) yaṃ te manthaṃ yamodananaṃ yan māṃsaṃ nipṛṇāmi te |
(18,4.42 ) te te santu svadhāvanto madhumanto ghṛtaścutaḥ ||42||

(18,4.43 ) yāste dhānā anukirāmi tilamiśrāḥ svadhāvatīḥ |
(18,4.43 ) tāste santūdbhvīḥ prabhvīstāste yamo rājānu manyatām ||43||

(18,4.44 ) idaṃ pūrvamaparaṃ niyānaṃ yenā te pūrve pitaraḥ paretāḥ |
(18,4.44 ) purogavā ye abhisāco asya te tvā vahanti sukṛtāmu lokam ||44||

(18,4.45 ) sarasvatīṃ devayanto havante sarasvatīmadhvare tāyamāne |
(18,4.45 ) sarasvatīṃ sukṛto havante sarasvatī dāśuṣe vāryaṃ dāt||45||

(18,4.46 ) sarasvatīṃ pitaro havante dakṣiṇā yajñamabhinakṣamāṇāḥ |
(18,4.46 ) āsadyāsmin barhiṣi mādayadhvamanamīvā iṣa ā dhehyasme ||46||

(18,4.47 ) sarasvati yā sarathaṃ yayāthokthaiḥ svadhābhirdevi pitṛbhirmadantī |
(18,4.47 ) sahasrārghamiḍo atra bhāgaṃ rāyaspoṣaṃ yajamānāya dhehi ||47||

(18,4.48 ) pṛthivīṃ tvā pṛthivyāmā veśayāmi devo no dhātā pra tirātyāyuḥ |
(18,4.48 ) parāparaitā vasuvidvo astvadhā mṛtāḥ pitṛṣu saṃ bhavantu ||48||

(18,4.49 ) ā pra cyavethāmapa tan mṛjethāṃ yadvāmabhibhā atrocuḥ |
(18,4.49 ) asmādetamaghnyau tadvaśīyo dātuḥ pitṛṣvihabhojanau mama ||49||

(18,4.50 ) eyamagan dakṣiṇā bhadrato nā anena dattā sudughā vayodhāḥ |
(18,4.50 ) yauvane jīvān upapṛñcatī jarā pitṛbhya upasaṃparāṇayādimān ||50|| {24}

(18,4.51 ) idaṃ pitṛbhyaḥ pra bharāmi barhirjīvaṃ devebhya uttaraṃ stṛṇāmi |
(18,4.51 ) tadā roha puruṣa medhyo bhavan prati tvā jānantu pitaraḥ paretam ||51||

(18,4.52 ) edaṃ barhirasado medhyo'bhūḥ prati tvā jānantu pitaraḥ paretam |
(18,4.52 ) yathāparu tanvaṃ saṃ bharasva gātrāṇi te brahmaṇā kalpayāmi ||52||

(18,4.53 ) parṇo rājāpidhānaṃ carūṇāmūrjo balaṃ saha ojo na āgan |
(18,4.53 ) āyurjīvebhyo vidadhaddīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya ||53||

(18,4.54 ) ūrjo bhāgo ya imaṃ jajānāśmānnānāmādhipatyaṃ jagāma |
(18,4.54 ) tamarcata viśvamitrā havirbhiḥ sa no yamaḥ prataraṃ jīvase dhāt||54||

(18,4.55 ) yathā yamāya harmyamavapan pañca mānavāḥ |
(18,4.55 ) evā vapāmi harmyaṃ yathā me bhūrayo'sata ||55||

(18,4.56 ) idaṃ hiraṇyaṃ bibhṛhi yatte pitābibhaḥ purā |
(18,4.56 ) svargaṃ yataḥ piturhastaṃ nirmṛḍḍhi dakṣiṇam ||56||

(18,4.57 ) ye ca jīvā ye ca mṛtā ye jātā ye ca yajñiyāḥ |
(18,4.57 ) tebhyo ghṛtasya kulyaitu madhudhārā vyundatī ||57||

(18,4.58 ) vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavate vicakṣaṇaḥ sūro ahnāṃ pratarītoṣasāṃ divaḥ |
(18,4.58 ) prāṇaḥ sindhūnāṃ kalaśāmacikradadindrasya hārdimāviśan manīṣayā ||58||

(18,4.59 ) tveṣaste dhūma ūrṇotu divi ṣaṃ chukra ātataḥ |
(18,4.59 ) sūro na hi dyutā tvaṃ kṛpā pāvaka rocase ||59||

(18,4.60 ) pra vā etīndurindrasya niṣkṛtiṃ sakhā sakhyurna pra mināti saṃgiraḥ |
(18,4.60 ) marya iva yoṣāḥ samarṣase somaḥ kalaśe śatayāmanā pathā ||60|| {25}

(18,4.61 ) akṣann amīmadanta hyava priyāmadhūṣata |
(18,4.61 ) astoṣata svabhānavo viprā yaviṣṭhā īmahe ||61||

(18,4.62 ) ā yāta pitaraḥ somyāso gambhīraiḥ pathibhiḥ pitṛyāṇaiḥ |
(18,4.62 ) āyurasmabhyaṃ dadhataḥ prajāṃ ca rāyaśca poṣairabhi naḥ sacadhvam ||62||

(18,4.63 ) parā yāta pitaraḥ somyāso gambhīraiḥ pathibhiḥ pūryāṇaiḥ |
(18,4.63 ) adhā māsi punarā yāta no gṛhān havirattuṃ suprajasaḥ suvīrāḥ ||63||

(18,4.64 ) yadvo agnirajahādekamaṅgaṃ pitṛlokaṃ gamayaṃ jātavedāḥ |
(18,4.64 ) tadva etatpunarā pyāyayāmi sāṅgāḥ svarge pitaro mādayadhvam ||64||

(18,4.65 ) abhūddūtaḥ prahito jātavedāḥ sāyaṃ nyahna upavandyo nṛbhiḥ |
(18,4.65 ) prādāḥ pitṛbhyaḥ svadhayā te akṣann addhi tvaṃ deva prayatā havīṃṣi ||65||

(18,4.66 ) asau hā iha te manaḥ kakutsalamiva jāmayaḥ |
(18,4.66 ) abhyenaṃ bhūma ūrṇuhi ||66||

(18,4.67 ) śumbhantāṃ lokāḥ pitṛṣadanāḥ pitṛṣadane tvā loka ā sādayāmi ||67||

(18,4.68 ) ye asmākaṃ pitarasteṣāṃ barhirasi ||68||

(18,4.69 ) uduttamaṃ varuṇa pāśamasmadavādhamaṃ śrathāya |
(18,4.69 ) adhā vayamāditya vrate tavānāgaso aditaye syāma ||69||

(18,4.70 ) prāsmatpāśān varuṇa muñca sarvān yaiḥ samāme badhyate yairvyāme |
(18,4.70 ) adhā jīvema śaradaṃ śatāni tvayā rājan gupitā rakṣamāṇāḥ ||70|| {26}

(18,4.71 ) agnaye kavyavāhanāya svadhā namaḥ ||71||

(18,4.72 ) somāya pitṛmate svadhā namaḥ ||72||

(18,4.73 ) pitṛbhyaḥ somavadbhyaḥ svadhā namaḥ ||73||

(18,4.74 ) yamāya pitṛmate svadhā namaḥ ||74||
(18,4.75 ) etatte pratatāmaha svadhā ye ca tvāmanu ||75||

(18,4.76 ) etatte tatāmaha svadhā ye ca tvāmanu ||76||

(18,4.77 ) etatte tata svadhā ||77||

(18,4.78 ) svadhā pitṛbhyaḥ pṛthiviṣadbhyaḥ ||78||

(18,4.79 ) svadhā pitṛbhyo antarikṣasadbhyaḥ ||79||

(18,4.80 ) svadhā pitṛbhyo diviṣadbhyaḥ ||80|| {27}

(18,4.81 ) namo vaḥ pitara ūrje namo vaḥ pitaro rasāya ||81||

(18,4.82 ) namo vaḥ pitaro bhāmāya namo vaḥ pitaro manyave ||82||

(18,4.83 ) namo vaḥ pitaro yadghoraṃ tasmai namo vaḥ pitaro yatkrūraṃ tasmai ||83||

(18,4.84 ) namo vaḥ pitaro yacchivaṃ tasmai namo vaḥ pitaro yatsyonaṃ tasmai ||84||

(18,4.85 ) namo vaḥ pitaraḥ svadhā vaḥ pitaraḥ ||85||

(18,4.86 ) ye'tra pitaraḥ pitaro ye'tra yūyaṃ stha yuṣmāṃste'nu yūyaṃ teṣāṃ śreṣṭhā bhūyāstha ||86||

(18,4.87 ) ya iha pitaro jīvā iha vayaṃ smaḥ |
(18,4.87 ) asmāṃste'nu vayaṃ teṣāṃ śreṣṭhā bhūyāsma ||87||

(18,4.88 ) ā tvāgna idhīmahi dyumantaṃ devājaram |
(18,4.88 ) yadgha sā te panīyasī samiddīdayati dyavi |
(18,4.88 ) iṣaṃ stotṛbhya ā bhara ||88||

(18,4.89 ) candramā apsvantarā suparṇo dhāvate divi |
(18,4.89 ) na vo hiraṇyanemayaḥ padaṃ vindanti vidyuto vittaṃ me asya rodasī ||89|| {28}



(19,1.1 ) saṃsaṃ sravantu nadyaḥ saṃ vātāḥ saṃ patatriṇaḥ |
(19,1.1 ) yajñamimaṃ vardhayatā giraḥ saṃsrāvyeṇa haviṣā juhomi ||1||

(19,1.2 ) imaṃ homā yajñamavatemaṃ saṃsrāvaṇā uta yajñamimaṃ vardhayatā giraḥ saṃsrāvyeṇa haviṣā juhomi ||2||

(19,1.3 ) rūpaṃrūpaṃ vayovayaḥ saṃrabhyainaṃ pari ṣvaje |
(19,1.3 ) yajñamimaṃ catasraḥ pradiśo vardhayantu saṃsrāvyeṇa haviṣā juhomi ||3||


(19,2.1 ) śaṃ ta āpo haimavatīḥ śamu te santūtsyāḥ |
(19,2.1 ) śaṃ te saniṣyadā āpaḥ śamu te santu varṣyāḥ ||1||

(19,2.2 ) śaṃ ta āpo dhanvanyāḥ śaṃ te santvanūpyāḥ |
(19,2.2 ) śaṃ te khanitrimā āpaḥ śaṃ yāḥ kumbhebhirābhṛtāḥ ||2||

(19,2.3 ) anabhrayaḥ khanamānā viprā gambhīre apasaḥ |
(19,2.3 ) bhiṣagbhyo bhiṣaktarā āpo achā vadāmasi ||3||
(19,2.4 ) apāmaha divyānāmapāṃ srotasyānām |
(19,2.4 ) apāmaha praṇejane'śvā bhavatha vājinaḥ ||4||

(19,2.5 ) tā apaḥ śivā apo'yakṣmaṃkaraṇīrapaḥ |
(19,2.5 ) yathaiva tṛpyate mayastāsta ā datta bhesajīḥ ||5||



(19,3.1 ) divaspṛthivyāḥ paryantarikṣādvanaspatibhyo adhyoṣadhībhyaḥ |
(19,3.1 ) yatrayatra vibhṛto jātavedāstata stuto juṣamāṇo na ehi ||1||

(19,3.2 ) yaste apsu mahimā yo vaneṣu ya oṣadhīṣu paśuṣvapsvantaḥ |
(19,3.2 ) agne sarvāstanvaḥ saṃ rabhasva tābhirna ehi draviṇodā ajasraḥ ||2||

(19,3.3 ) yaste deveṣu mahimā svargo yā te tanuḥ pitṛṣvāviveśa |
(19,3.3 ) puṣṭiryā te manuṣyeṣu paprathe'gne tayā rayimasmāsu dhehi ||3||

(19,3.4 ) śrutkarṇāya kavaye vedyāya vacobhirvākairupa yāmi rātim |
(19,3.4 ) yato bhayamabhayaṃ tan no astvava devānāṃ yaja heḍo agne ||4||



(19,4.1 ) yāmāhutiṃ prathamāmatharvā yā jātā yā havyamakṛṇojjātavedāḥ |
(19,4.1 ) tāṃ ta etāṃ prathamo johavīmi tābhiṣṭupto vahatu havyamagniragnaye svāha ||1||

(19,4.2 ) ākūtiṃ devīṃ subhagāṃ puro dadhe cittasya mātā suhavā no astu |
(19,4.2 ) yāmāśāmemi kevalī sā me astu videyamenāṃ manasi praviṣṭām ||2||

(19,4.3 ) ākūtyā no bṛhaspata ākūtyā na upā gahi |
(19,4.3 ) atho bhagasya no dhehyatho naḥ suhavo bhava ||3||

(19,4.4 ) bṛhaspatirma ākūtimāṅgirasaḥ prati jānātu vācametām |
(19,4.4 ) yasya devā devatāḥ saṃbabhūvuḥ sa supraṇītāḥ kāmo anvetvasmān ||4||



(19,5.1 ) indro rājā jagataścarṣaṇīnāmadhi kṣami viṣurūpaṃ yadasti |
(19,5.1 ) tato dadāti dāśuṣe vasūni codadrādha upastutaścidarvāk||1||



(19,6.1 ) sahasrabāhuḥ puruṣaḥ sahasrākṣaḥ sahasrapāt|
(19,6.1 ) sa bhūmiṃ viśvato vṛtvātyatiṣṭhaddaśāṅgulam ||1||

(19,6.2 ) tribhiḥ padbhirdyāmarohatpādasyehābhavatpunaḥ |
(19,6.2 ) tathā vyakrāmadviṣvaṅaśanānaśane anu ||2||

(19,6.3 ) tāvanto asya mahimānastato jyāyāṃśca pūruṣaḥ |
(19,6.3 ) pādo'sya viśvā bhūtāni tripādasyāmṛtaṃ divi ||3||

(19,6.4 ) puruṣa evedaṃ sarvaṃ yadbhūtaṃ yacca bhāvyam |
(19,6.4 ) utāmṛtatvasyeśvaro yadanyenābhavatsaha ||4||

(19,6.5 ) yatpuruṣaṃ vyadadhuḥ katidhā vyakalpayan |
(19,6.5 ) mukhaṃ kimasya kiṃ bāhū kimūrū pādā ucyate ||5||

(19,6.6 ) brāhmaṇo'sya mukhamāsīdbāhū rājanyo'bhavat|
(19,6.6 ) madhyaṃ tadasya yadvaiśyaḥ padbhyāṃ śūdro ajāyata ||6||

(19,6.7 ) candramā manaso jātaścakṣoḥ sūryo ajāyata |
(19,6.7 ) mukhādindraścāgniśca prāṇādvāyurajāyata ||7||

(19,6.8 ) nābhyā āsīdantarikṣaṃ śīrṣṇo dyauḥ samavartata |
(19,6.8 ) padbhyāṃ bhūmirdiśaḥ śrotrāttathā lokāmakalpayan ||8||

(19,6.9 ) virāḍagre samabhavadvirājo adhi pūruṣaḥ |
(19,6.9 ) sa jāto atyaricyata paścādbhūmimatho puraḥ ||9||

(19,6.10 ) yatpuruṣeṇa haviṣā devā yajñamatanvata |
(19,6.10 ) vasanto asyāsīdājyaṃ grīṣma idhmaḥ śaraddhaviḥ ||10||

(19,6.11 ) taṃ yajñaṃ prāvṛṣā praukṣan puruṣaṃ jātamagraśaḥ |
(19,6.11 ) tena devā ayajanta sādhyā vasavaśca ye ||11||

(19,6.12 ) tasmādaśvā ajāyanta ye ca ke cobhayādataḥ |
(19,6.12 ) gāvo ha jajñire tasmāttasmājjātā ajāvayaḥ ||12||

(19,6.13 ) tasmādyajñātsarvahuta ṛcaḥ sāmāni jajñire |
(19,6.13 ) chando ha jajñire tasmādyajustasmādajāyata ||13||

(19,6.14 ) tasmādyajñātsarvahutaḥ saṃbhṛtaṃ pṛṣadājyam |
(19,6.14 ) paśūṃstāṃścakre vāyavyān āraṇyā grāmyāśca ye ||14||

(19,6.15 ) saptāsyāsan paridhayastriḥ sapta samidhaḥ kṛtāḥ |
(19,6.15 ) devā yadyajñaṃ tanvānā abadhnan puruṣaṃ paśum ||15||

(19,6.16 ) mūrdhno devasya bṛhato aṃśavaḥ sapta saptatīḥ |
(19,6.16 ) rājñaḥ somasyājāyanta jātasya puruṣādadhi ||16||



(19,7.1 ) citrāṇi sākaṃ divi rocanāni sarīsṛpāṇi bhuvane javāni |
(19,7.1 ) turmiśaṃ sumatimichamāno ahāni gīrbhiḥ saparyāmi nākam ||1||

(19,7.2 ) suhavamagne kṛttikā rohiṇī cāstu bhadraṃ mṛgaśiraḥ śamārdrā |
(19,7.2 ) punarvasū sūnṛtā cāru puṣyo bhānurāśleṣā ayanaṃ maghā me ||2||

(19,7.3 ) puṇyaṃ pūrvā phalgunyau cātra hastaścitrā śivā svāti sukho me astu |
(19,7.3 ) rādhe viśākhe suhavānurādhā jyeṣṭhā sunakṣatramariṣṭa mūlam ||3||

(19,7.4 ) annaṃ pūrvā rāsatāṃ me aṣādhā ūrjaṃ devyuttarā ā vahantu |
(19,7.4 ) abhijin me rāsatāṃ puṇyameva śravaṇaḥ śraviṣṭhāḥ kurvatāṃ supuṣṭim ||4||

(19,7.5 ) ā me mahacchatabhiṣagvarīya ā me dvayā proṣṭhapadā suśarma |
(19,7.5 ) ā revatī cāśvayujau bhagaṃ ma ā me rayiṃ bharaṇya ā vahantu ||5||



(19,8.1 ) yāni nakṣatrāṇi divyantarikṣe apsu bhūmau yāni nageṣu dikṣu |
(19,8.1 ) prakalpayaṃścandramā yānyeti sarvāṇi mamaitāni śivāni santu ||1||

(19,8.2 ) aṣṭāviṃśāni śivāni śagmāni saha yogaṃ bhajantu me |
(19,8.2 ) yogaṃ pra padye kṣemaṃ ca kṣemaṃ pra padye yogaṃ ca namo'horātrābhyāmastu ||2||

(19,8.3 ) svastitaṃ me suprātaḥ susāyaṃ sudivaṃ sumṛgaṃ suśakunaṃ me astu |
(19,8.3 ) suhavamagne svastyamartyaṃ gatvā punarāyābhinandan ||3||

(19,8.4 ) anuhavaṃ parihavaṃ parivādaṃ parikṣavam |
(19,8.4 ) sarvairme riktakumbhān parā tāntsavitaḥ suva ||4||

(19,8.5 ) apapāpaṃ parikṣavaṃ puṇyaṃ bhakṣīmahi kṣavam |
(19,8.5 ) śivā te pāpa nāsikāṃ puṇyagaścābhi mehatām ||5||

(19,8.6 ) imā yā brahmaṇaspate viṣucīrvāta īrate |
(19,8.6 ) sadhrīcīrindra tāḥ kṛtvā mahyaṃ śivatamāskṛdhi ||6||

(19,8.7 ) svasti no astvabhayaṃ no astu namo'horatrābhyāmastu ||7||



(19,9.1 ) śāntā dyauḥ śāntā pṛthivī śāntamidamurvantarikṣam |
(19,9.1 ) śāntā udanvatīrāpaḥ śāntā naḥ santvoṣadhīḥ ||1||

(19,9.2 ) śāntāni pūrvarūpāṇi śāntaṃ no astu kṛtākṛtam |
(19,9.2 ) śāntaṃ bhūtaṃ ca bhavyaṃ ca sarvameva śamastu naḥ ||2||

(19,9.3 ) iyaṃ yā parameṣṭhinī vāgdevī brahmasaṃśitā |
(19,9.3 ) yayaiva sasṛje ghoraṃ tayaiva śāntirastu naḥ ||3||

(19,9.4 ) idaṃ yatparameṣṭhinaṃ mano vāṃ brahmasaṃśitam |
(19,9.4 ) yenaiva sasṛje ghoraṃ tenaiva śāntirastu naḥ ||4||

(19,9.5 ) imāni yāni pañcendriyāni manaḥṣaṣṭhāni me hṛdi brahmaṇā saṃśitāni |
(19,9.5 ) yaireva sasṛje ghoraṃ taireva śāntirastu naḥ ||5||

(19,9.6 ) śaṃ no mitraḥ śaṃ varuṇaḥ śaṃ viṣṇuḥ śaṃ prajāpatiḥ |
(19,9.6 ) śaṃ na indro bṛhaspatiḥ śaṃ no bhavatvaryamā ||6||

(19,9.7 ) śaṃ no mitraḥ śaṃ varuṇaḥ śaṃ vivasvāṃ chamantakaḥ |
(19,9.7 ) utpātāḥ pārthivāntarikṣāḥ śaṃ no divicarā grahāḥ ||7||

(19,9.8 ) śaṃ no bhūmirvepyamānā śamulkā nirhataṃ ca yat|
(19,9.8 ) śaṃ gāvo lohitakṣīrāḥ śaṃ bhūmirava tīryatīḥ ||8||

(19,9.9 ) nakṣatramulkābhihataṃ śamastu naḥ śaṃ no'bhicārāḥ śamu santu kṛtyāḥ |
(19,9.9 ) śaṃ no nikhātā valgāḥ śamulkā deśopasargāḥ śamu no bhavantu ||9||

(19,9.10 ) śaṃ no grahāścāndramasāḥ śamādityaśca rāhuṇā |
(19,9.10 ) śaṃ no mṛtyurdhūmaketuḥ śaṃ rudrāstigmatejasaḥ ||10||

(19,9.11 ) śaṃ rudrāḥ śaṃ vasavaḥ śamādityāḥ śamagnayaḥ |
(19,9.11 ) śaṃ no maharṣayo devāḥ śaṃ devāḥ śaṃ bṛhaspatiḥ ||11||

(19,9.12 ) brahma prajāpatirdhātā lokā vedāḥ saptaṛṣayo'gnayaḥ |
(19,9.12 ) tairme kṛtaṃ svastyayanamindro me śarma yachatu brahmā me śarma yachatu |
(19,9.12 ) viśve me devāḥ śarma yachantu sarve me devāḥ śarma yachantu ||12||

(19,9.13 ) yāni kāni cicchāntāni loke saptaṛṣayo viduḥ |
(19,9.13 ) sarvāṇi śaṃ bhavantu me śaṃ me astvabhayaṃ me astu ||13||

(19,9.14 ) pṛthivī śāntirantarikṣaṃ śāntirdyauḥ śāntirāpaḥ śāntiroṣadhayaḥ śāntirvanaspatayaḥ śāntirviśve me devāḥ śāntiḥ sarve me devāḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntibhiḥ |
(19,9.14 ) yadiha ghoraṃ yadiha krūraṃ yadiha pāpaṃ tacchāntaṃ tacchivaṃ sarvameva śamastu naḥ ||14||



(19,10.1 ) śaṃ na indrāgnī bhavatāmavobhiḥ śaṃ na indrāvaruṇā rātahavyā |
(19,10.1 ) śamindrāsomā suvitāya śaṃ yoḥ śaṃ na indrāpūṣaṇā vājasātau ||1||

(19,10.2 ) śaṃ no bhagaḥ śamu naḥ śaṃso astu śaṃ naḥ puraṃdhiḥ śamu santu rāyaḥ |
(19,10.2 ) śaṃ naḥ satyasya suyamasya śaṃsaḥ śaṃ no aryamā purujāto astu ||2||

(19,10.3 ) śaṃ no dhātā śamu dhartā no astu śaṃ na urūcī bhavatu svadhābhiḥ |
(19,10.3 ) śaṃ rodasī bṛhatī śaṃ no adriḥ śaṃ no devānāṃ suhavāni santu ||3||

(19,10.4 ) śaṃ no agnirjyotiranīko astu śaṃ no mitrāvaruṇāvaśvinā śam |
(19,10.4 ) śaṃ naḥ sukṛtāṃ sukṛtāni santu śaṃ na iṣiro abhi vātu vātaḥ ||4||

(19,10.5 ) śaṃ no dyāvāpṛthivī pūrvahūtau śamantarikṣaṃ dṛśaye no astu |
(19,10.5 ) śaṃ na oṣadhīrvanino bhavantu śaṃ no rajasaspatirastu jiṣṇuḥ ||5||

(19,10.6 ) śaṃ na indro vasubhirdevo astu śamādityebhirvaruṇaḥ suśaṃsaḥ |
(19,10.6 ) śaṃ no rudro rudrebhirjalāṣaḥ śaṃ nastvaṣṭā gnābhiriha śṛṇotu ||6||

(19,10.7 ) śaṃ naḥ somo bhavatu brahma śaṃ naḥ śaṃ no grāvāṇaḥ śamu santu yajñāḥ |
(19,10.7 ) śaṃ naḥ svarūnāṃ mitayo bhavantu śaṃ naḥ prasvaḥ śaṃ vastu vediḥ ||7||

(19,10.8 ) śaṃ naḥ sūrya urucakṣā udetu śaṃ no bhavantu pradiśaścatasraḥ |
(19,10.8 ) śaṃ naḥ parvatā dhruvayo bhavantu śaṃ naḥ sindhavaḥ śamu santvāpaḥ ||8||

(19,10.9 ) śaṃ no aditirbhavatu vratebhiḥ śaṃ no bhavantu marutaḥ svarkāḥ |
(19,10.9 ) śaṃ no viṣṇuḥ śamu pūṣā no astu śaṃ no bhavitraṃ śaṃ vastu vāyuḥ ||9||

(19,10.10 ) śaṃ no devaḥ savitā trāyamāṇaḥ śaṃ no bhavantūṣaso vibhātīḥ |
(19,10.10 ) śaṃ naḥ parjanyo bhavatu prajābhyaḥ śaṃ naḥ kṣetrasya patirastu śaṃbhuḥ ||10||



(19,11.1 ) śaṃ naḥ satyasya patayo bhavantu śaṃ no arvantaḥ śamu santu gāvaḥ |
(19,11.1 ) śaṃ na ṛbhavaḥ sukṛtaḥ suhastāḥ śaṃ no bhavatu pitaro haveṣu ||1||

(19,11.2 ) śaṃ no devā viśvadevā bhavantu śaṃ sarasvatī saha dhībhirastu |
(19,11.2 ) śamabhiṣācaḥ śamu rātiṣācaḥ śaṃ no divyāḥ pārthivāḥ śaṃ no apyāḥ ||2||

(19,11.3 ) śaṃ no aja ekapāddevo astu śamahirbudhnyaḥ śaṃ samudraḥ |
(19,11.3 ) śaṃ no apāṃ napātperurastu śaṃ naḥ pṛṣṇirbhavatu devagopā ||3||

(19,11.4 ) ādityā rudrā vasavo juṣantāmidaṃ brahma kriyamāṇaṃ navīyaḥ |
(19,11.4 ) sṛṇvantu no divyāḥ pārthivāso gojātā uta ye yajñiyāsaḥ ||4||

(19,11.5 ) ye devānāmṛtvijo yajñiyāso manoryajatrā amṛtā ṛtajñāḥ |
(19,11.5 ) te no rāsantāmurugāyamadya yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||5||

(19,11.6 ) tadastu mitrāvaruṇā tadagne śaṃ yorasmabhyamidamastu śastam |
(19,11.6 ) aśīmahi gādhamuta pratiṣṭhāṃ namo dive bṛhate sādanāya ||6||



(19,12.1 ) uṣā apa svasustamaḥ saṃ vartayati vartaniṃ sujātatā |
(19,12.1 ) ayā vājaṃ devahitaṃ sanema madema śatahimāḥ suvīrāḥ ||1||



(19,13.1 ) indrasya bāhū sthavirau vṛṣāṇau citrā imā vṛṣabhau pārayiṣṇū |
(19,13.1 ) tau yokṣe prathamo yoga āgate yābhyāṃ jitamasurāṇāṃ svaryat||1||

(19,13.2 ) āśuḥ śiśāno vṛṣabho na bhīmo ghanāghanaḥ kṣobhaṇaścarṣaṇīnām |
(19,13.2 ) saṃkrandano'nimiṣa ekavīraḥ śataṃ senā ajayatsākamindraḥ ||2||

(19,13.3 ) saṃkrandanenānimiṣeṇa jiṣṇunāyodhyena duścyavanena dhṛṣṇunā |
(19,13.3 ) tadindreṇa jayata tatsahadhvaṃ yudho nara iṣuhastena vṛṣṇā ||3||

(19,13.4 ) sa iṣuhastaiḥ sa niṣaṅgibhirvaśī saṃsraṣṭā sa yudha indro gaṇena |
(19,13.4 ) saṃsṛṣṭajitsomapā bāhuśardhyugradhanvā pratihitābhirastā ||4||
(19,13.5 ) balavijñāyaḥ sthaviraḥ pravīraḥ sahasvān vājī sahamāna ugraḥ |
(19,13.5 ) abhivīro abhiṣatvā sahojijjaitramindra rathamā tiṣṭha govidam ||5||

(19,13.6 ) imaṃ vīramanu harṣadhvamugramindraṃ sakhāyo anu saṃ rabhadhvam |
(19,13.6 ) grāmajitaṃ gojitaṃ vajrabāhuṃ jayantamajma pramṛṇantamojasā ||6||

(19,13.7 ) abhi gotrāṇi sahasā gāhamāno'dāya ugraḥ śatamanyurindraḥ |
(19,13.7 ) duścyavanaḥ pṛtanāṣāḍayodhyo'smākaṃ senā avatu pra yutsu ||7||

(19,13.8 ) bṛhaspate pari dīyā rathena rakṣohāmitrāmapabādhamānaḥ |
(19,13.8 ) prabhañjaṃ chatrūn pramṛṇann amitrān asmākamedhyavitā tanūnām ||8||

(19,13.9 ) indra eṣāṃ netā bṛhaspatirdakṣiṇā yajñaḥ pura etu somaḥ |
(19,13.9 ) devasenānāmabhibhañjatīnāṃ jayantīnāṃ maruto yantu madhye ||9||

(19,13.10 ) indrasya vṛṣṇo varuṇasya rājña ādityānāṃ marutāṃ śardha ugram |
(19,13.10 ) mahāmanasāṃ bhuvanacyavānāṃ ghoṣo devānāṃ jayatāmudasthāt||10||

(19,13.11 ) asmākamindraḥ samṛteṣu dhvajeṣvasmākaṃ yā iṣavastā jayantu |
(19,13.11 ) asmākaṃ vīrā uttare bhavantvasmān devāso'vatā haveṣu ||11||



(19,14.1 ) idamucchreyo'vasānamāgāṃ śive me dyāvāpṛthivī abhūtām |
(19,14.1 ) asapatnāḥ pradiśo me bhavantu na vai tvā dviṣmo abhayaṃ no astu ||1||



(19,15.1 ) yata indra bhayāmahe tato no abhayaṃ kṛdhi |
(19,15.1 ) maghavaṃ chagdhi tava tvaṃ na ūtibhirvi dviṣo vi mṛdho jahi ||1||

(19,15.2 ) indraṃ vayamanūrādhaṃ havāmahe'nu rādhyāsma dvipadā catuṣpadā |
(19,15.2 ) mā naḥ senā araruṣīrupa gurviṣūcirindra druho vi nāśaya ||2||

(19,15.3 ) indrastrātota vṛtrahā parasphāno vareṇyaḥ |
(19,15.3 ) sa rakṣitā caramataḥ sa madhyataḥ sa paścātsa purastān no astu ||3||

(19,15.4 ) uruṃ no lokamanu neṣi vidvāntsvaryajjyotirabhayaṃ svasti |
(19,15.4 ) ugrā ta indra sthavirasya bāhū upa kṣayema śaraṇā bṛhantā ||4||

(19,15.5 ) abhayaṃ naḥ karatyantarikṣamabhayaṃ dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe ime |
(19,15.5 ) abhayaṃ paścādabhayaṃ purastāduttarādadharādabhayaṃ no astu ||5||

(19,15.6 ) abhayaṃ mitrādabhayamamitrādabhayaṃ jñātādabhayaṃ puro yaḥ |
(19,15.6 ) abhayaṃ naktamabhayaṃ divā naḥ sarvā āśā mama mitraṃ bhavantu ||6||



(19,16.1 ) asapatnaṃ purastātpaścān no abhayaṃ kṛtam |
(19,16.1 ) savitā mā dakṣiṇata uttarān mā śacīpatiḥ ||1||

(19,16.2 ) divo mādityā rakṣatu bhūmyā rakṣantvagnayaḥ |
(19,16.2 ) indrāgnī rakṣatāṃ mā purastādaśvināvabhitaḥ śarma yachatām |
(19,16.2 ) tiraścīn aghnyā rakṣatu jātavedā bhūtakṛto me sarvataḥ santu varma ||2||



(19,17.1 ) agnirmā pātu vasubhiḥ purastāttasmin krame tasmiṃ chraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19,17.1 ) sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānaṃ pari dade svāhā ||1||

(19,17.2 ) vāyurmāntarikṣeṇaitasyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmiṃ chraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19,17.2 ) sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānaṃ pari dade svāhā ||2||

(19,17.3 ) somo mā rudrairdakṣiṇāyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmiṃ chraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19,17.3 ) sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānaṃ pari dade svāhā ||3||

(19,17.4 ) varuṇo mādityairetasyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmiṃ chraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19,17.4 ) sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānaṃ pari dade svāhā ||4||

(19,17.5 ) sūryo mā dyāvāpṛthivībhyāṃ pratīcyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmiṃ chraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19,17.5 ) sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānaṃ pari dade svāhā ||5||

(19,17.6 ) āpo mauṣadhīmatīretasyā diśaḥ pāntu tāsu krame tāsu śraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19,17.6 ) tā mā rakṣantu tā mā gopāyantu tābhya ātmānaṃ pari dade svāhā ||6||
(19,17.7 ) viśvakarmā mā saptaṛṣibhirudīcyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmiṃ chraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19,17.7 ) sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānaṃ pari dade svāhā ||7||

(19,17.8 ) indro mā marutvān etasyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmiṃ chraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19,17.8 ) sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānaṃ pari dade svāhā ||8||

(19,17.9 ) prajāpatirmā prajananavāntsaha pratiṣṭhayā dhruvāyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmiṃ chraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19,17.9 ) sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānaṃ pari dade svāhā ||9||
(19,17.10 ) bṛhaspatirmā viśvairdevairūrdhvāyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmiṃ chraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19,17.10 ) sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānaṃ pari dade svāhā ||10||



(19,18.1 ) agniṃ te vasuvantamṛchantu |
(19,18.1 ) ye māghāyavaḥ prācyā diśo'bhidāsān ||1||

(19,18.2 ) vāyuṃ te'ntarikṣavantamṛchantu |
(19,18.2 ) ye māghāyava etasyā diśo'bhidāsān ||2||

(19,18.3 ) somaṃ te rudravantamṛchantu |
(19,18.3 ) ye māghāyavo dakṣiṇāyā diśo'bhidāsān ||3||

(19,18.4 ) varuṇaṃ ta ādityavantamṛchantu |
(19,18.4 ) ye māghāyava etasyā diśo'bhidāsān ||4||

(19,18.5 ) sūryaṃ te dyāvāpṛthivīvantamṛchantu |
(19,18.5 ) ye māghāyava pratīcyā diśo'bhidāsān ||5||

(19,18.6 ) apasta oṣadhīmatīrṛchantu |
(19,18.6 ) ye māghāyava etasyā diśo'bhidāsān ||6||

(19,18.7 ) viśvakarmāṇaṃ te saptaṛṣivantamṛchantu |
(19,18.7 ) ye māghāyava udīcyā diśo'bhidāsān ||7||

(19,18.8 ) indraṃ te marutvantamṛchantu |
(19,18.8 ) ye māghāyava etasyā diśo'bhidāsān ||8||

(19,18.9 ) prajāpatiṃ te prajananavantamṛchantu |
(19,18.9 ) ye māghāyavo dhruvāyā diśo'bhidāsān ||9||

(19,18.10 ) bṛhaspatiṃ te viśvadevavantamṛchantu |
(19,18.10 ) ye māghāyava ūrdhvāyā diśo'bhidāsān ||10||



(19,19.1 ) mitraḥ pṛthivyodakrāmattāṃ puraṃ pra ṇayāmi vaḥ |
(19,19.1 ) tāmā viśata tāṃ pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu ||1||

(19,19.2 ) vāyurantarikṣeṇodakrāmattāṃ puraṃ pra ṇayāmi vaḥ |
(19,19.2 ) tāmā viśata tāṃ pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu ||2||

(19,19.3 ) sūryo divodakrāmattāṃ puraṃ pra ṇayāmi vaḥ |
(19,19.3 ) tāmā viśata tāṃ pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu ||3||

(19,19.4 ) candramā nakṣatrairudakrāmattāṃ puraṃ pra ṇayāmi vaḥ |
(19,19.4 ) tāmā viśata tāṃ pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu ||4||

(19,19.5 ) soma oṣadhībhirudakrāmattāṃ puraṃ pra ṇayāmi vaḥ |
(19,19.5 ) tāmā viśata tāṃ pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu ||5||

(19,19.6 ) yajño dakṣiṇābhirudakrāmattāṃ puraṃ pra ṇayāmi vaḥ |
(19,19.6 ) tāmā viśata tāṃ pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu ||6||

(19,19.7 ) samudro nadībhirudakrāmattāṃ puraṃ pra ṇayāmi vaḥ |
(19,19.7 ) tāmā viśata tāṃ pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu ||7||

(19,19.8 ) brahma brahmacāribhirudakrāmattāṃ puraṃ pra ṇayāmi vaḥ |
(19,19.8 ) tāmā viśata tāṃ pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu ||8||

(19,19.9 ) indro vīryeṇodakrāmattāṃ puraṃ pra ṇayāmi vaḥ |
(19,19.9 ) tāmā viśata tāṃ pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu ||9||

(19,19.10 ) devā amṛtenodakrāmaṃstāṃ puraṃ pra ṇayāmi vaḥ |
(19,19.10 ) tāmā viśata tāṃ pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu ||10||

(19,19.11 ) prajāpatiḥ prajābhirudakrāmattāṃ puraṃ pra ṇayāmi vaḥ |
(19,19.11 ) tāmā viśata tāṃ pra viśata sā vaḥ śarma ca varma ca yachatu ||11||



(19,20.1 ) apa nyadhuḥ pauruṣeyaṃ vadhaṃ yamindrāgnī dhātā savitā bṛhaspatiḥ |
(19,20.1 ) somo rājā varuṇo aśvinā yamaḥ pūṣāsmān pari pātu mṛtyoḥ ||1||

(19,20.2 ) yāni cakāra bhuvanasya yaspatiḥ prajāpatirmātariśvā prajābhyaḥ |
(19,20.2 ) pradiśo yāni vasate diśaśca tāni me varmāṇi bahulāni santu ||2||

(19,20.3 ) yatte tanūṣvanahyanta devā dyurājayo dehinaḥ |
(19,20.3 ) indro yaccakre varma tadasmān pātu viśvataḥ ||3||

(19,20.4 ) varma me dyāvāpṛthivī varmāharvarma sūryaḥ |
(19,20.4 ) varma me viśve devāḥ kran mā mā prāpatpratīcikā ||4||



(19,21.1 ) gāyatryuṣṇiganuṣṭubbṛhatī paṅktistriṣṭubjagatyai ||1||



(19,22.1 ) āṅgirasānāmādyaiḥ pañcānuvākaiḥ svāhā ||1||

(19,22.2 ) ṣaṣṭhāya svāhā ||2||

(19,22.3 ) saptamāṣṭamābhyāṃ svāhā ||3||

(19,22.4 ) nīlanakhebhyaḥ svāhā ||4||

(19,22.5 ) haritebhyaḥ svāhā ||5||

(19,22.6 ) kṣudrebhyaḥ svāhā ||6||

(19,22.7 ) paryāyikebhyaḥ svāhā ||7||

(19,22.8 ) prathamebhyaḥ śaṅkhebhyaḥ svāhā ||8||

(19,22.9 ) dvitīyebhyaḥ śaṅkhebhyaḥ svāhā ||9||

(19,22.10 ) tṛtīyebhyaḥ śaṅkhebhyaḥ svāhā ||10||

(19,22.11 ) upottamebhyaḥ svāhā ||11||

(19,22.12 ) uttamebhyaḥ svāhā ||12||

(19,22.13 ) uttarebhyaḥ svāhā ||13||

(19,22.14 ) ṛṣibhyaḥ svāhā ||14||

(19,22.15 ) śikhibhyaḥ svāhā ||15||

(19,22.16 ) gaṇebhyaḥ svāhā ||16||

(19,22.17 ) mahāgaṇebhyaḥ svāhā ||17||

(19,22.18 ) sarvebhyo'ṅgirobhyo vidagaṇebhyaḥ svāhā ||18||

(19,22.19 ) pṛthaksahasrābhyāṃ svāhā ||19||

(19,22.20 ) brahmaṇe svāhā ||20||

(19,22.21 ) brahmajyeṣṭhā sambhṛtā viryāṇi brahmāgre jyeṣṭhaṃ divamā tatāna |
(19,22.21 ) bhūtānāṃ brahmā prathamota jajñe tenārhati brahmaṇā spardhituṃ kaḥ ||21||



(19,23.1 ) ātharvaṇānāṃ caturṛcebhyaḥ svāhā ||1||

(19,23.2 ) pañcarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||2||

(19,23.3 ) ṣaḻṛcebhyaḥ svāhā ||3||

(19,23.4 ) saptarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||4||

(19,23.5 ) aṣṭarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||5||

(19,23.6 ) navarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||6||

(19,23.7 ) daśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||7||

(19,23.8 ) ekādaśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||8||

(19,23.9 ) dvādaśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||9||

(19,23.10 ) trayodaśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||10||

(19,23.11 ) caturdaśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||11||

(19,23.12 ) pañcadaśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||12||

(19,23.13 ) ṣoḍaśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||13||

(19,23.14 ) saptadaśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||14||

(19,23.15 ) aṣṭādaśarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||15||

(19,23.16 ) ekonaviṃśatiḥ svāhā ||16||

(19,23.17 ) viṃśatiḥ svāhā ||17||

(19,23.18 ) mahatkāṇḍāya svāhā ||18||

(19,23.19 ) tṛcebhyaḥ svāhā ||19||

(19,23.20 ) ekarcebhyaḥ svāhā ||20||

(19,23.21 ) kṣudrebhyaḥ svāhā ||21||

(19,23.22 ) ekānṛcebhyaḥ svāhā ||22||

(19,23.23 ) rohitebhyaḥ svāhā ||23||

(19,23.24 ) sūryābhyāṃ svāhā ||24||
(19,23.25 ) vrātyābhyāṃ svāhā ||25||

(19,23.26 ) prājāpatyābhyāṃ svāhā ||26||

(19,23.27 ) viṣāsahyai svāhā ||27||

(19,23.28 ) maṅgalikebhyaḥ svāhā ||28||

(19,23.29 ) brahmaṇe svāhā ||29||
(19,23.30 ) brahmajyeṣṭhā saṃbhṛtā vīryāṇi brahmāgre jyeṣṭhaṃ divamā tatāna |
(19,23.30 ) bhūtānāṃ brahmā prathamota jajñe tenārhati brahmaṇā spardhituṃ kaḥ ||30||


(19,24.1 ) yena devaṃ savitāraṃ pari devā adhārayan |
(19,24.1 ) tenemaṃ brahmaṇaspate pari rāṣṭrāya dhattana ||1||

(19,24.2 ) parīmamindramāyuṣe mahe kṣatrāya dhattana |
(19,24.2 ) yathainaṃ jarase nayājjyokkṣatre'dhi jāgarat||2||

(19,24.3 ) parīmamindramāyuṣe mahe śrotrāya dhattana |
(19,24.3 ) yathainaṃ jarase nayājjyokśrotre'dhi jāgarat||3||

(19,24.4 ) pari dhatta dhatta no varcasemaṃ jarāmṛtyuṃ kṛṇuta dīrghamāyuḥ |
(19,24.4 ) bṛhaspatiḥ prāyachadvāsa etatsomāya rājñe paridhātavā u ||4||
(19,24.5 ) jarāṃ su gacha pari dhatsva vāso bhavā gṛṣṭīnāmabhiśastipā u |
(19,24.5 ) śataṃ ca jīva śaradaḥ purūcī rāyaśca poṣamupasaṃvyayasva ||5||

(19,24.6 ) parīdaṃ vāso adhithāḥ svastaye'bhūrvāpīnāmabhiśastipā u |
(19,24.6 ) śataṃ ca jīva śaradaḥ purūcīrvasūni cārurvi bhajāsi jīvan ||6||

(19,24.7 ) yogeyoge tavastaraṃ vājevāje havāmahe |
(19,24.7 ) sakhāya indramūtaye ||7||

(19,24.8 ) hiraṇyavarṇo ajaraḥ suvīro jarāmṛtyuḥ prajayā saṃ viśasva |
(19,24.8 ) tadagnirāha tadu soma āha bṛhaspatiḥ savitā tadindraḥ ||8||



(19,25.1 ) aśrāntasya tvā manasā yunajmi prathamasya ca |
(19,25.1 ) utkūlamudvaho bhavoduhya prati dhāvatāt||1||


(19,26.1 ) agneḥ prajātaṃ pari yaddhiraṇyamamṛtaṃ dadhre adhi martyeṣu |
(19,26.1 ) ya enadveda sa idenamarhati jarāmṛtyurbhavati yo bibharti ||1||

(19,26.2 ) yaddhiraṇyaṃ sūryeṇa suvarṇaṃ prajāvanto manavaḥ pūrva īṣire |
(19,26.2 ) tattvā candraṃ varcasā saṃ sṛjatyāyuṣmān bhavati yo bibharti ||2||

(19,26.3 ) āyuṣe tvā varcase tvaujase ca balāya ca |
(19,26.3 ) yathā hiraṇyatejasā vibhāsāsi janāmanu ||3||

(19,26.4 ) yadveda rājā varuṇo veda devo bṛhaspatiḥ |
(19,26.4 ) indro yadvṛtrahā veda tatta āyuṣyaṃ bhuvattatte varcasyaṃ bhuvat||4||


(19,27.1 ) gobhiṣṭvā pātvṛṣabho vṛṣā tvā pātu vājibhiḥ |
(19,27.1 ) vāyuṣṭvā brahmaṇā pātvindrastvā pātvindriyaiḥ ||1||

(19,27.2 ) somastvā pātvoṣadhībhirnakṣatraiḥ pātu sūryaḥ |
(19,27.2 ) mādbhyastvā candro vṛtrahā vātaḥ prāṇena rakṣatu ||2||

(19,27.3 ) tisro divastisraḥ pṛthivīstrīṇyantarikṣāṇi caturaḥ samudrān |
(19,27.3 ) trivṛtaṃ stomaṃ trivṛta āpa āhustāstvā rakṣantu trivṛtā trivṛdbhiḥ ||3||

(19,27.4 ) trīn nākāṃstrīn samudrāṃstrīn bradhnāṃstrīn vaiṣṭapān |
(19,27.4 ) trīn mātariśvanastrīntsūryān goptṝn kalpayāmi te ||4||

(19,27.5 ) ghṛtena tvā samukṣāmyagne ājyena vardhayan |
(19,27.5 ) agneścandrasya sūryasya mā prāṇaṃ māyino dabhan ||5||

(19,27.6 ) mā vaḥ prāṇaṃ mā vo'pānaṃ mā haro māyino dabhan |
(19,27.6 ) bhrājanto viśvavedaso devā daivyena dhāvata ||6||

(19,27.7 ) prāṇenāgniṃ saṃ sṛjati vātaḥ prāṇena saṃhitaḥ |
(19,27.7 ) prāṇena viśvatomukhaṃ sūryaṃ devā ajanayan ||7||

(19,27.8 ) āyuṣāyuḥkṛtāṃ jīvāyuṣmān jīva mā mṛthāḥ |
(19,27.8 ) prāṇenātmanvatāṃ jīva mā mṛtyorudagā vaśam ||8||

(19,27.9 ) devānāṃ nihitaṃ nidhiṃ yamindro'nvavindatpathibhirdevayānaiḥ |
(19,27.9 ) āpo hiraṇyaṃ jugupustrivṛdbhistāstvā rakṣantu trivṛtā trivṛdbhiḥ ||9||

(19,27.10 ) trayastriṃśaddevatāstrīṇi ca vīryāṇi priyāyamāṇā jugupurapsvantaḥ |
(19,27.10 ) asmiṃścandre adhi yaddhiraṇyaṃ tenāyaṃ kṛṇavadvīryāṇi ||10||

(19,27.11 ) ye devā divyekādaśa stha te devāso haviridaṃ juṣadhvam ||11||

(19,27.12 ) ye devā antarikṣa ekādaśa stha te devāso haviridaṃ juṣadhvam ||12||

(19,27.13 ) ye devā pṛthivyāmekādaśa stha te devāso haviridaṃ juṣadhvam ||13||

(19,27.14 ) asapatnaṃ purastātpaścān no abhayaṃ kṛtam |
(19,27.14 ) savitā mā dakṣiṇata uttarān mā śacīpatiḥ ||14||

(19,27.15 ) divo mādityā rakṣantu bhūmyā rakṣantvagnayaḥ |
(19,27.15 ) indrāgnī rakṣatāṃ mā purastādaśvināvabhitaḥ śarma yachatām |
(19,27.15 ) tiraścīn aghnyā rakṣatu jātavedā bhūtakṛto me sarvataḥ santu varma ||15||



(19,28.1 ) imaṃ badhnāmi te maṇiṃ dīrghāyutvāya tejase |
(19,28.1 ) darbhaṃ sapatnadambhanaṃ dviṣatastapanaṃ hṛdaḥ ||1||

(19,28.2 ) dviṣatastāpayan hṛdaḥ śatrūṇāṃ tāpayan manaḥ |
(19,28.2 ) durhārdaḥ sarvāṃstvaṃ darbha gharma ivābhīntsaṃtāpayan ||2||

(19,28.3 ) gharma ivābhitapan darbha dviṣato nitapan maṇe |
(19,28.3 ) hṛdaḥ sapatnānāṃ bhinddhīndra iva virujaṃ balam ||3||

(19,28.4 ) bhinddhi darbha sapatnānāṃ hṛdayaṃ dviṣatāṃ maṇe |
(19,28.4 ) udyan tvacamiva bhūmyāḥ śira eṣāṃ vi pātaya ||4||

(19,28.5 ) bhinddhi darbha sapatnān me bhinddhi me pṛtanāyataḥ |
(19,28.5 ) bhinddhi me sarvān durhārdo bhinddhi me dviṣato maṇe ||5||

(19,28.6 ) chinddhi darbha sapatnān me chinddhi me pṛtanāyataḥ |
(19,28.6 ) chinddhi me sarvān durhārdo chinddhi me dviṣato maṇe ||6||

(19,28.7 ) vṛśca darbha sapatnān me vṛśca me pṛtanāyataḥ |
(19,28.7 ) vṛśca me sarvān durhārdo vṛśca me dviṣato maṇe ||7||

(19,28.8 ) kṛnta darbha sapatnān me kṛnta me pṛtanāyataḥ |
(19,28.8 ) kṛnta me sarvān durhārdo kṛnta me dviṣato maṇe ||8||

(19,28.9 ) piṃśa darbha sapatnān me piṃśa me pṛtanāyataḥ |
(19,28.9 ) piṃśa me sarvān durhārdo piṃśa me dviṣato maṇe ||9||

(19,28.10 ) vidhya darbha sapatnān me vidhya me pṛtanāyataḥ |
(19,28.10 ) vidhya me sarvān durhārdo vidhya me dviṣato maṇe ||10||



(19,29.1 ) nikṣa darbha sapatnān me nikṣa me pṛtanāyataḥ |
(19,29.1 ) nikṣa me sarvān durhārdo nikṣa me dviṣato maṇe ||1||

(19,29.2 ) tṛnddhi darbha sapatnān me tṛnddhi me pṛtanāyataḥ |
(19,29.2 ) tṛnddhi me sarvān durhārdo tṛnddhi me dviṣato maṇe ||2||

(19,29.3 ) runddhi darbha sapatnān me runddhi me pṛtanāyataḥ |
(19,29.3 ) runddhi me sarvān durhārdo runddhi me dviṣato maṇe ||3||

(19,29.4 ) mṛṇa darbha sapatnān me mṛṇa me pṛtanāyataḥ |
(19,29.4 ) mṛṇa me sarvān durhārdo mṛṇa me dviṣato maṇe ||4||

(19,29.5 ) mantha darbha sapatnān me mantha me pṛtanāyataḥ |
(19,29.5 ) mantha me sarvān durhārdo mantha me dviṣato maṇe ||5||

(19,29.6 ) piṇḍḍhi darbha sapatnān me piṇḍḍhi me pṛtanāyataḥ |
(19,29.6 ) piṇḍḍhi me sarvān durhārdo piṇḍḍhi me dviṣato maṇe ||6||

(19,29.7 ) oṣa darbha sapatnān me oṣa me pṛtanāyataḥ |
(19,29.7 ) oṣa me sarvān durhārdo oṣa me dviṣato maṇe ||7||

(19,29.8 ) daha darbha sapatnān me daha me pṛtanāyataḥ |
(19,29.8 ) daha me sarvān durhārdo daha me dviṣato maṇe ||8||

(19,29.9 ) jahi darbha sapatnān me jahi me pṛtanāyataḥ |
(19,29.9 ) jahi me sarvān durhārdo jahi me dviṣato maṇe ||9||



(19,30.1 ) yatte darbha jarāmṛtyu śataṃ varmasu varma te |
(19,30.1 ) tenemaṃ varmiṇaṃ kṛtvā sapatnāṃ jahi vīryaiḥ ||1||

(19,30.2 ) śataṃ te darbha varmāṇi sahasraṃ vīryāṇi te |
(19,30.2 ) tamasmai viśve tvāṃ devā jarase bhartavā aduḥ ||2||

(19,30.3 ) tvāmāhurdevavarma tvāṃ darbha brahmaṇaspatim |
(19,30.3 ) tvāmindrasyāhurvarma tvaṃ rāṣṭrāṇi rakṣasi ||3||

(19,30.4 ) sapatnakṣayaṇaṃ darbha dviṣatastapanaṃ hṛdaḥ |
(19,30.4 ) maṇiṃ kṣatrasya vardhanaṃ tanūpānaṃ kṛṇomi te ||4||

(19,30.5 ) yatsamudro abhyakrandatparjanyo vidyutā saha |
(19,30.5 ) tato hiranyayo bindustato darbho ajāyata ||5||



(19,31.1 ) audumbareṇa maṇinā puṣṭikāmāya vedhasā |
(19,31.1 ) paśūṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ sphātiṃ goṣṭhe me savitā karat||1||

(19,31.2 ) yo no agnirgārhapatyaḥ paśūnāmadhipā asat|
(19,31.2 ) audumbaro vṛṣā maṇiḥ saṃ mā sṛjatu puṣṭyā ||2||

(19,31.3 ) karīṣiṇīṃ phalavatīṃ svadhāmirāṃ ca no gṛhe |
(19,31.3 ) audumbarasya tejasā dhātā puṣṭiṃ dadhātu me ||3||

(19,31.4 ) yaddvipācca catuṣpācca yānyannāni ye rasāḥ |
(19,31.4 ) gṛhṇe'haṃ tveṣāṃ bhūmānaṃ bibhradaudumbaraṃ maṇim ||4||
(19,31.5 ) puṣṭiṃ paśūnāṃ pari jagrabhāhaṃ catuṣpadāṃ dvipadāṃ yacca dhānyam |
(19,31.5 ) payaḥ paśūnāṃ rasamoṣadhīnāṃ bṛhaspatiḥ savitā me ni yachāt||5||

(19,31.6 ) ahaṃ paśūnāmadhipā asāni mayi puṣṭaṃ puṣṭapatirdadhātu |
(19,31.6 ) mahyamaudumbaro maṇirdraviṇāni ni yachatu ||6||

(19,31.7 ) upa maudumbaro maṇiḥ prajayā ca dhanena ca |
(19,31.7 ) indreṇa jinvito maṇirā māgantsaha varcasā ||7||

(19,31.8 ) devo maṇiḥ sapatnahā dhanasā dhanasātaye |
(19,31.8 ) paśorannasya bhūmānaṃ gavāṃ sphātiṃ ni yachatu ||8||

(19,31.9 ) yathāgre tvaṃ vanaspate puṣṭhyā saha jajñiṣe |
(19,31.9 ) evā dhanasya me sphātimā dadhātu sarasvatī ||9||

(19,31.10 ) ā me dhanaṃ sarasvatī payasphātiṃ ca dhānyam |
(19,31.10 ) sinīvālyupā vahādayaṃ caudumbaro maṇiḥ ||10||

(19,31.11 ) tvaṃ maṇīṇāmadhipā vṛṣāsi tvayi puṣṭaṃ puṣṭapatirjajāna |
(19,31.11 ) tvayīme vājā draviṇāni sarvaudumbaraḥ sa tvamasmatsahasvārādārādarātimamatiṃ kṣudhaṃ ca ||11||

(19,31.12 ) grāmaṇīrasi grāmaṇīrutthāya abhiṣikto'bhi mā siñca varcasā |
(19,31.12 ) tejo'si tejo mayi dhārayādhi rayirasi rayiṃ me dhehi ||12||

(19,31.13 ) puṣṭirasi puṣṭyā mā samaṅgdhi gṛhamedhī gṛhapatiṃ mā kṛṇu |
(19,31.13 ) audumbaraḥ sa tvamasmāsu dhehi rayiṃ ca naḥ sarvavīraṃ ni yacha rāyaspoṣāya prati muñce ahaṃ tvām ||13||

(19,31.14 ) ayamaudumbaro maṇirvīro vīrāya badhyate |
(19,31.14 ) sa naḥ saniṃ madhumatīṃ kṛṇotu rayiṃ ca naḥ sarvavīraṃ ni yachāt||14||



(19,32.1 ) śatakāṇḍo duścyavanaḥ sahasraparṇa uttiraḥ |
(19,32.1 ) darbho ya ugra oṣadhistaṃ te badhnāmyāyuṣe ||1||

(19,32.2 ) nāsya keśān pra vapanti norasi tāḍamā ghnate |
(19,32.2 ) yasmā achinnaparṇena darbhena śarma yachati ||2||

(19,32.3 ) divi te tūlamoṣadhe pṛthivyāmasi niṣṭhitaḥ |
(19,32.3 ) tvayā sahasrakāṇḍenāyuḥ pra vardhayāmahe ||3||

(19,32.4 ) tisro divo atyatṛṇattisra imāḥ pṛthivīruta |
(19,32.4 ) tvayāhaṃ durhārdo jihvāṃ ni tṛṇadmi vacāṃsi ||4||

(19,32.5 ) tvamasi sahamāno'hamasmi sahasvān |
(19,32.5 ) ubhau sahasvantau bhūtvā sapatnān sahiṣīvahi ||5||

(19,32.6 ) sahasva no abhimātiṃ sahasva pṛtanāyataḥ |
(19,32.6 ) sahasva sarvān durhārdaḥ suhārdo me bahūn kṛdhi ||6||

(19,32.7 ) darbheṇa devajātena divi ṣṭambhena śaśvadit|
(19,32.7 ) tenāhaṃ śaśvato janāmasanaṃ sanavāni ca ||7||

(19,32.8 ) priyaṃ mā darbha kṛṇu brahmarājanyābhyāṃ śūdrāya cāryāya ca |
(19,32.8 ) yasmai ca kāmayāmahe sarvasmai ca vipaśyate ||8||

(19,32.9 ) yo jāyamānaḥ pṛthivīmadṛṃhadyo astabhnādantarikṣaṃ divaṃ ca |
(19,32.9 ) yaṃ bibhrataṃ nanu pāpmā viveda sa no'yaṃ darbho varuṇo divā kaḥ ||9||

(19,32.10 ) sapatnahā śatakāṇḍaḥ sahasvān oṣadhīnāṃ prathamaḥ saṃ babhūva |
(19,32.10 ) sa no'yaṃ darbhaḥ pari pātu viśvatastena sākṣīya pṛtanāḥ pṛtanyataḥ ||10||



(19,33.1 ) sahasrārghaḥ śatakāṇḍaḥ payasvān apāmagnirvīrudhāṃ rājasūyam |
(19,33.1 ) sa no'yaṃ darbhaḥ pari pātu viśvato devo maṇirāyuṣā saṃ sṛjāti naḥ ||1||

(19,33.2 ) ghṛtādullupto madhumān payasvān bhūmidṛṃho'cyutaścyāvayiṣṇuḥ |
(19,33.2 ) nudantsapatnān adharāṃśca kṛṇvan darbhā roha mahatāmindriyeṇa ||2||

(19,33.3 ) tvaṃ bhūmimatyeṣyojasā tvaṃ vedyāṃ sīdasi cāruradhvare |
(19,33.3 ) tvāṃ pavitramṛṣayo'bharanta tvaṃ punīhi duritānyasmat||3||
(19,33.4 ) tīkṣṇo rājā viṣāsahī rakṣohā viśvacarṣaṇiḥ |
(19,33.4 ) ojo devānāṃ balamugrametattaṃ te badhnāmi jarase svastaye ||4||

(19,33.5 ) darbheṇa tvaṃ kṛṇavadvīryāṇi darbhaṃ bibhradātmanā mā vyathiṣṭhāḥ |
(19,33.5 ) atiṣṭhāya varcasādhānyāntsūrya ivā bhāhi pradiśaścatasraḥ ||5||



(19,34.1 ) jāṅgiḍo'si jaṅgiḍo rakṣitāsi jaṅgidaḥ |
(19,34.1 ) dvipāccatuṣpādasmākaṃ sarvaṃ rakṣatu jaṅgidaḥ ||1||
(19,34.2 ) yā gṛtsyastripañcāśīḥ śataṃ kṛtyākṛtaśca ye |
(19,34.2 ) sarvān vinaktu tejaso'rasāṃ jaṅgidaskarat||2||

(19,34.3 ) arasaṃ kṛtrimaṃ nādamarasāḥ sapta visrasaḥ |
(19,34.3 ) apeto jaṅgiḍāmatimiṣumasteva śātaya ||3||
(19,34.4 ) kṛtyādūṣaṇa evāyamatho arātidūṣaṇaḥ |
(19,34.4 ) atho sahasvāñjaṅgiḍaḥ pra na āyumṣi tāriṣat||4||

(19,34.5 ) sa jaṅgiḍasya mahimā pari ṇaḥ pātu viśvataḥ |
(19,34.5 ) viṣkandhaṃ yena sāsaha saṃskandhamoja ojasā ||5||

(19,34.6 ) triṣṭvā devā ajanayan niṣṭhitaṃ bhūmyāmadhi |
(19,34.6 ) tamu tvāṅgirā iti brāhmaṇāḥ pūrvyā viduḥ ||6||

(19,34.7 ) na tvā pūrvā oṣadhayo na tvā taranti yā navāḥ |
(19,34.7 ) vibādha ugro jaṅgiḍaḥ paripāṇaḥ sumaṅgalaḥ ||7||

(19,34.8 ) athopadāna bhagavo jāṅgiḍāmitavīrya |
(19,34.8 ) purā ta ugrā grasata upendro vīryaṃ dadau ||8||

(19,34.9 ) ugra itte vanaspata indra ojmānamā dadhau |
(19,34.9 ) amīvāḥ sarvāścātayaṃ jahi rakṣāṃsyoṣadhe ||9||

(19,34.10 ) āśarīkaṃ viśarīkaṃ balāsaṃ pṛṣṭyāmayam |
(19,34.10 ) takmānaṃ viśvaśāradamarasāṃ jaṅgiḍaskarat||10||



(19,35.1 ) indrasya nāma gṛhṇanta ṛsayo jaṅgidaṃ daduḥ |
(19,35.1 ) devā yaṃ cakrurbheṣajamagre viṣkandhadūṣaṇam ||1||

(19,35.2 ) sa no rakṣatu jaṅgiḍo dhanapālo dhaneva |
(19,35.2 ) devā yaṃ cakrurbrāhmaṇāḥ paripāṇamarātiham ||2||

(19,35.3 ) durhārdaḥ saṃghoraṃ cakṣuḥ pāpakṛtvānamāgamam |
(19,35.3 ) tāṃstvaṃ sahasracakṣo pratībodhena nāśaya paripāṇo'si jaṅgiḍaḥ ||3||

(19,35.4 ) pari mā divaḥ pari mā pṛthivyāḥ paryantarikṣātpari mā vīrudbhyaḥ |
(19,35.4 ) pari mā bhūtātpari mota bhavyāddiśodiśo jaṅgiḍaḥ pātvasmān ||4||

(19,35.5 ) ya ṛṣṇavo devakṛtā ya uto vavṛte'nyaḥ |
(19,35.5 ) sarvāṃ stān viśvabheṣajo'rasāṃ jaṅgiḍaskarat||5||



(19,36.1 ) śatavāro anīnaśadyakṣmān rakṣāṃsi tejasā |
(19,36.1 ) ārohan varcasā saha maṇirdurṇāmacātanaḥ ||1||

(19,36.2 ) śṛṅgābhyāṃ rakṣo nudate mūlena yātudhānyaḥ |
(19,36.2 ) madhyena yakṣmaṃ bādhate nainaṃ pāpmāti tatrati ||2||

(19,36.3 ) ye yakṣmāso arbhakā mahānto ye ca śabdinaḥ |
(19,36.3 ) sarvāṃ durṇāmahā maṇiḥ śatavāro anīnaśat||3||

(19,36.4 ) śataṃ vīrān ajanayacchataṃ yakṣmān apāvapat|
(19,36.4 ) durṇāmnaḥ sarvān hatvāva rakṣāṃsi dhūnute ||4||

(19,36.5 ) hiraṇyaśṛṅga ṛṣabhaḥ śātavāro ayaṃ maṇiḥ |
(19,36.5 ) durṇāmnaḥ sarvāṃstṛdhvāva rakṣāṃsyakramīt||5||

(19,36.6 ) śatamahaṃ durṇāmnīnāṃ gandharvāpsarasāṃ śatam |
(19,36.6 ) śataṃ śaśvanvatīnāṃ śatavāreṇa vāraye ||6||


(19,37.1 ) idaṃ varco agninā dattamāgan bhargo yaśaḥ saha ojo vayo balam |
(19,37.1 ) trayastriṃśadyāni ca vīryāṇi tānyagniḥ pra dadātu me ||1||

(19,37.2 ) varca ā dhehi me tanvāṃ saha ojo vayo balam |
(19,37.2 ) indriyāya tvā karmaṇe vīryāya prati gṛhṇāmi śataśāradāya ||2||

(19,37.3 ) ūrje tvā balāya tvaujase sahase tvā |
(19,37.3 ) abhibhūyāya tvā rāṣṭrabhṛtyāya paryūhāmi śataśāradāya ||3||

(19,37.4 ) ṛtubhyaṣṭvārtavebhyo mādbhyaḥ saṃvatsarebhyaḥ |
(19,37.4 ) dhātre vidhātre samṛdhe bhūtasya pataye yaje ||4||



(19,38.1 ) na taṃ yakṣmā arundhate nainaṃ śapatho aśnute |
(19,38.1 ) yaṃ bheṣajasya gulguloḥ surabhirgandho aśnute ||1||

(19,38.2 ) viṣvañcastasmādyakṣmā mṛgā aśvā iverate |
(19,38.2 ) yadgulgulu saindhavaṃ yadvāpyasi samudriyam ||2||

(19,38.3 ) ubhayoragrabhaṃ nāmāsmā ariṣṭatātaye ||3||



(19,39.1 ) aitu devastrāyamāṇaḥ kuṣṭho himavataspari |
(19,39.1 ) takmānaṃ sarvaṃ nāśaya sarvāśca yātudhānyaḥ ||1||

(19,39.2 ) trīṇi te kuṣṭha nāmāni nadyamāro nadyāriṣaḥ |
(19,39.2 ) nadyāyaṃ puruṣo riṣat|
(19,39.2 ) yasmai paribravīmi tvā sāyaṃprātaratho divā ||2||

(19,39.3 ) jīvalā nāma te mātā jīvanto nāma te pitā |
(19,39.3 ) nadyāyaṃ puruṣo riṣat|
(19,39.3 ) yasmai paribravīmi tvā sāyaṃprātaratho divā ||3||

(19,39.4 ) uttamo asyoṣadhīnāmanaḍvān jagatāmiva vyāghraḥ śvapadāmiva |
(19,39.4 ) nadyāyaṃ puruṣo riṣat|
(19,39.4 ) yasmai paribravīmi tvā sāyaṃprātaratho divā ||4||

(19,39.5 ) triḥ śāmbubhyo aṅgirebhyastrirādityebhyaspari |
(19,39.5 ) trirjāto viśvadevebhyaḥ |
(19,39.5 ) sa kuṣṭho viśvabheṣajaḥ sākaṃ somena tiṣṭhati |

(19,39.6 ) aśvattho devasadanastṛtīyasyāmito divi |
(19,39.6 ) tatrāmṛtasya cakṣaṇaṃ tataḥ kuṣṭho ajāyata |
(19,39.6 ) sa kuṣṭho viśvabheṣajaḥ sākaṃ somena tiṣṭhati |

(19,39.7 ) hiraṇyayī nauracaraddhiraṇyabandhanā divi |
(19,39.7 ) tatrāmṛtasya cakṣaṇaṃ tataḥ kuṣṭho ajāyata |
(19,39.7 ) sa kuṣṭho viśvabheṣajaḥ sākaṃ somena tiṣṭhati |

(19,39.8 ) yatra nāvaprabhraṃśanaṃ yatra himavataḥ śiraḥ |
(19,39.8 ) tatrāmṛtasya cakṣaṇaṃ tataḥ kuṣṭho ajāyata |
(19,39.8 ) sa kuṣṭho viśvabheṣajaḥ sākaṃ somena tiṣṭhati |

(19,39.9 ) yaṃ tvā veda pūrva ikṣvāko yaṃ vā tvā kuṣṭha kāmyaḥ |
(19,39.9 ) yaṃ vā vaso yamātsyastenāsi viśvabheṣajaḥ ||9||

(19,39.10 ) śīrṣaśokaṃ tṛtīyakaṃ sadaṃdiryaśca hāyanaḥ |
(19,39.10 ) takmānaṃ viśvadhāvīryādharāñcaṃ parā suva ||10||


(19,40.1 ) yan me chidraṃ manaso yacca vācaḥ sarasvatī manyumantaṃ jagāma |
(19,40.1 ) viśvaistaddevaiḥ saha saṃvidānaḥ saṃ dadhātu bṛhaspatiḥ ||1||

(19,40.2 ) mā na āpo medhāṃ mā brahma pra mathiṣṭana |
(19,40.2 ) suṣyadā yūyaṃ syandadhvamupahūto'haṃ sumedhā varcasvī ||2||

(19,40.3 ) mā no medhāṃ mā no dīkṣāṃ mā no hiṃsiṣṭaṃ yattapaḥ |
(19,40.3 ) śivā naḥ śaṃ santvāyuṣe śivā bhavantu mātaraḥ ||3||

(19,40.4 ) yā naḥ pīparadaśvinā jyotiṣmatī tamastiraḥ |
(19,40.4 ) tāmasme rāsatāmiṣam ||4||



(19,41.1 ) bhadramichanta ṛṣayaḥ svarvidastapo dīkṣāmupaniṣeduragre |
(19,41.1 ) tato rāṣṭraṃ balamojaśca jātaṃ tadasmai devā upasaṃnamantu ||1||



(19,42.1 ) brahma hotā brahma yajñā brahmaṇā svaravo mitāḥ |
(19,42.1 ) adhvaryurbrahmaṇo jāto brahmaṇo'ntarhitaṃ haviḥ ||1||

(19,42.2 ) brahma sruco ghṛtavatīrbrahmaṇā vediruddhitā |
(19,42.2 ) brahma yajñasya tattvaṃ ca ṛtvijo ye haviṣkṛtaḥ |
(19,42.2 ) śamitāya svāhā ||2||

(19,42.3 ) aṃhomuce pra bhare manīṣāmā sutrāvṇe sumatimāvṛṇānaḥ |
(19,42.3 ) imamindra prati havyaṃ gṛbhāya satyāḥ santu yajamānasya kāmāḥ ||3||

(19,42.4 ) aṃhomucaṃ vrṣabhaṃ yajñiyānāṃ virājantaṃ prathamamadhvarāṇam |
(19,42.4 ) apāṃ napātamaśvinā huve dhiya indriyeṇa ta indriyaṃ dattamojaḥ ||4||



(19,43.1 ) yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha |
(19,43.1 ) agnirmā tatra nayatvagnirmedhā dadhātu me |
(19,43.1 ) agnaye svāhā ||1||

(19,43.2 ) yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha |
(19,43.2 ) vāyurmā tatra nayatu vāyuḥ praṇān dadhātu me vāyave svāhā ||2||

(19,43.3 ) yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha |
(19,43.3 ) sūryo mā tatra nayatu cakṣuḥ sūryo dadhātu me |
(19,43.3 ) sūryāya svāhā ||3||

(19,43.4 ) yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha |
(19,43.4 ) candro mā tatra nayatu manaścandro dadhātu me |
(19,43.4 ) candrāya svāhā ||4||

(19,43.5 ) yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha |
(19,43.5 ) somo mā tatra nayatu payaḥ somo dadhātu me |
(19,43.5 ) somāya svāhā ||5||

(19,43.6 ) yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha |
(19,43.6 ) indro mā tatra nayatu balamindro dadhātu me |
(19,43.6 ) indrāya svāhā ||6||

(19,43.7 ) yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha |
(19,43.7 ) āpo mā tatra nayatvamṛtaṃ mopa tiṣṭhatu |
(19,43.7 ) adbhyaḥ svāhā ||7||

(19,43.8 ) yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha |
(19,43.8 ) brahmā mā tatra nayatu brahmā brahma dadhātu me |
(19,43.8 ) brahmaṇe svāhā ||8||



(19,44.1 ) āyuṣo'si prataraṇaṃ vipraṃ bheṣajamucyase |
(19,44.1 ) tadāñjana tvaṃ śaṃtāte śamāpo abhayaṃ kṛtam ||1||

(19,44.2 ) yo harimā jāyānyo'ṅgabhedo viṣalpakaḥ |
(19,44.2 ) sarvaṃ te yakṣmamaṅgebhyo bahirnirhantvāñjanam ||2||

(19,44.3 ) āñjanaṃ pṛthivyāṃ jātaṃ bhadraṃ puruṣajīvanam |
(19,44.3 ) kṛṇotvapramāyukaṃ rathajūtimanāgasam ||3||

(19,44.4 ) prāṇa prāṇaṃ trāyasvāso asave mṛḍa |
(19,44.4 ) nirṛte nirṛtyā naḥ pāśebhyo muñca ||4||

(19,44.5 ) sindhorgarbho'si vidyutāṃ puṣpam |
(19,44.5 ) vātaḥ prāṇaḥ sūryaścakṣurdivaspayaḥ ||5||

(19,44.6 ) devāñjana traikakuda pari mā pāhi viśvataḥ |
(19,44.6 ) na tvā tarantyoṣadhayo bāhyāḥ parvatīyā uta ||6||

(19,44.7 ) vīdaṃ madhyamavāsṛpadrakṣohāmīvacātanaḥ |
(19,44.7 ) amīvāḥ sarvāścātayan nāśayadabhibhā itaḥ ||7||

(19,44.8 ) bahvidaṃ rājan varuṇānṛtamāha pūruṣaḥ |
(19,44.8 ) tasmātsahasravīrya muñca naḥ paryaṃhasaḥ ||8||

(19,44.9 ) yadāpo aghnyā iti varuṇeti yadūcima |
(19,44.9 ) tasmātsahasravīrya muñca naḥ paryaṃhasaḥ ||9||

(19,44.10 ) mitraśca tvā varuṇaścānupreyaturāñjana |
(19,44.10 ) tau tvānugatya dūraṃ bhogāya punarohatuḥ ||10||



(19,45.1 ) ṛṇādṛṇamiva saṃ naya kṛtyāṃ kṛtyākṛto gṛham |
(19,45.1 ) cakṣurmantrasya durhārdaḥ pṛṣṭīrapi śṛṇāñjana ||1||

(19,45.2 ) yadasmāsu duṣvapnyaṃ yadgoṣu yacca no gṛhe |
(19,45.2 ) anāmagastaṃ ca durhārdaḥ priyaḥ prati muñcatām ||2||

(19,45.3 ) apāmūrja ojaso vāvṛdhānamagnerjātamadhi jātavedasaḥ |
(19,45.3 ) caturvīraṃ parvatīyaṃ yadāñjanaṃ diśaḥ pradiśaḥ karadicchivāste ||3||

(19,45.4 ) caturvīraṃ badhyata āñjanaṃ te sarvā diśo abhayāste bhavantu |
(19,45.4 ) dhruvastiṣṭhāsi saviteva cārya imā viśo abhi harantu te balim ||4||

(19,45.5 ) ākṣvaikaṃ maṇimekaṃ krṇuṣva snāhyekenā pibaikameṣām |
(19,45.5 ) caturvīraṃ nairṛtebhyaścaturbhyo grāhyā bandhebhyaḥ pari pātvasmān ||5||

(19,45.6 ) agnirmāgnināvatu prāṇāyāpānāyāyuṣe varcasa ojase |
(19,45.6 ) tejase svastaye subhūtaye svāhā ||6||

(19,45.7 ) indro mendriyeṇāvatu prāṇāyāpānāyāyuṣe varcasa ojase |
(19,45.7 ) tejase svastaye subhūtaye svāhā ||7||

(19,45.8 ) somo mā saumyenāvatu prāṇāyāpānāyāyuṣe varcasa ojase |
(19,45.8 ) tejase svastaye subhūtaye svāhā ||8||

(19,45.9 ) bhago ma bhagenāvatu prāṇāyāpānāyāyuṣe varcasa ojase |
(19,45.9 ) tejase svastaye subhūtaye svāhā ||9||

(19,45.10 ) maruto mā gaṇairavantu prāṇāyāpānāyuṣe varcasa ojase tejase |
(19,45.10 ) svastaye subhūtaye svāhā ||10||


(19,46.1 ) prajāpatiṣṭvā badhnātprathamamastṛtaṃ vīryāya kam |
(19,46.1 ) tatte badhnāmyāyuṣe varcasa ojase ca balāya cāstṛtastvābhi rakṣatu ||1||

(19,46.2 ) ūrdhvastiṣṭhatu rakṣann apramādamastṛtemaṃ mā tvā dabhan paṇayo yātudhānāḥ |
(19,46.2 ) indra iva dasyūn ava dhūnuṣva pṛtanyataḥ sarvāṃ chatrūn vi ṣahasvāstṛtastvābhi rakṣatu ||2||

(19,46.3 ) śataṃ ca na praharanto nighnanto na tastire |
(19,46.3 ) tasminn indraḥ paryadatta cakṣuḥ prāṇamatho balamastṛtastvābhi rakṣatu ||3||

(19,46.4 ) indrasya tvā varmaṇā pari dhāpayāmo yo devānāmadhirājo babhūva |
(19,46.4 ) punastvā devāḥ pra ṇayantu sarve'stṛtastvābhi rakṣatu ||4||

(19,46.5 ) asmin maṇāvekaśataṃ vīryāṇi sahasraṃ prāṇā asminn astṛte |
(19,46.5 ) vyāghraḥ śatrūn abhi tiṣṭha sarvān yastvā pṛtanyādadharaḥ so astvastṛtastvābhi rakṣatu ||5||

(19,46.6 ) ghṛtādullupto madhumān payasvāntsahasraprāṇaḥ śatayonirvayodhāḥ |
(19,46.6 ) śambhūśca mayobhūścorjasvāṃśca payasvāṃścāstṛtastvābhi rakṣatu ||6||

(19,46.7 ) yathā tvamuttaro'so asapatnaḥ sapatnahā |
(19,46.7 ) sajātānāmasadvaśī tathā tvā savitā karadastṛtastvābhi rakṣatu ||7||



(19,47.1 ) ā rātri pārthivaṃ rajaḥ pituraprāyi dhāmabhiḥ |
(19,47.1 ) divaḥ sadāṃsi bṛhatī vi tiṣṭhasa ā tveṣaṃ vartate tamaḥ ||1||

(19,47.2 ) na yasyāḥ pāraṃ dadṛśe na yoyuvadviśvamasyāṃ ni viśate yadejati |
(19,47.2 ) ariṣṭāsasta urvi tamasvati rātri pāramaśīmahi bhadre pāramaśīmahi ||2||

(19,47.3 ) ye te rātri nṛcakṣaso draṣṭāro navatirnava |
(19,47.3 ) aśītiḥ santyaṣṭā uto te sapta saptatiḥ ||3||

(19,47.4 ) ṣaṣṭiśca ṣaṭca revati pañcāśatpañca sumnayi |
(19,47.4 ) catvāraścatvāriṃśacca trayastriṃśacca vājini ||4||

(19,47.5 ) dvau ca te viṃśatiśca te rātryekādaśāvamāḥ |
(19,47.5 ) tebhirno adya pāyubhirnu pāhi duhitardivaḥ ||5||

(19,47.6 ) rakṣā mākirno aghaśaṃsa īśata mā no duḥśaṃsa īśata |
(19,47.6 ) mā no adya gavāṃ steno māvīnāṃ vṛka īśata ||6||

(19,47.7 ) māśvānāṃ bhadre taskaro mā nṛṇāṃ yātudhānyaḥ |
(19,47.7 ) paramebhiḥ pathibhi steno dhāvatu taskaraḥ |
(19,47.7 ) pareṇa datvatī rajjuḥ pareṇāghāyurarṣatu ||7||

(19,47.8 ) adha rātri tṛṣṭadhūmamaśīrṣāṇamahiṃ kṛṇu |
(19,47.8 ) hanū vṛkasya jambhayā stenaṃ drupade jahi ||8||

(19,47.9 ) tvayi rātri vasāmasi svapiṣyāmasi jāgṛhi |
(19,47.9 ) gobhyo naḥ śarma yachāśvebhyaḥ puruṣebhyaḥ ||9||



(19,48.1 ) atho yāni ca yasmā ha yāni cāntaḥ parīṇahi |
(19,48.1 ) tāni te pari dadmasi ||1||

(19,48.2 ) rātri mātaruṣase naḥ pari dehi |
(19,48.2 ) uṣā no ahne pari dadātvahastubhyaṃ vibhāvari ||2||
(19,48.3 ) yatkiṃ cedaṃ patayati yatkiṃ cedaṃ sarīsṛpam |
(19,48.3 ) yatkiṃ ca parvatāyāsatvaṃ tasmāttvaṃ rātri pāhi naḥ ||3||

(19,48.4 ) sā paścātpāhi sā puraḥ sottarādadharāduta |
(19,48.4 ) gopāya no vibhāvari stotārasta iha smasi ||4||

(19,48.5 ) ye rātrimanutiṣṭhanti ye ca bhūteṣu jāgrati |
(19,48.5 ) paśūn ye sarvān rakṣanti te na ātmasu jāgrati te naḥ paśuṣu jāgrati ||5||

(19,48.6 ) veda vai rātri te nāma ghṛtācī nāma vā asi |
(19,48.6 ) tāṃ tvāṃ bharadvājo veda sā no vitte'dhi jāgrati ||6||



(19,49.1 ) iṣirā yoṣā yuvatirdamūnā rātrī devasya saviturbhagasya |
(19,49.1 ) aśvakṣabhā suhavā saṃbhṛtaśrīrā paprau dyāvāpṛthivī mahitvā ||1||

(19,49.2 ) ati viśvānyaruhadgambhiro varṣiṣṭhamaruhanta śraviṣṭhāḥ |
(19,49.2 ) uśatī rātryanu sā bhadrābhi tiṣṭhate mitra iva svadhābhiḥ ||2||

(19,49.3 ) varye vande subhage sujāta ājagan rātri sumanā iha syām |
(19,49.3 ) asmāṃstrāyasva naryāṇi jātā atho yāni gavyāni puṣṭyā ||3||

(19,49.4 ) siṃhasya rātryuśatī pīṃṣasya vyāghrasya dvīpino varca ā dade |
(19,49.4 ) aśvasya bradhnaṃ puruṣasya māyuṃ puru rūpāṇi kṛṇuṣe vibhātī ||4||

(19,49.5 ) śivāṃ rātrimanusūryaṃ ca himasya mātā suhavā no astu |
(19,49.5 ) asya stomasya subhage ni bodha yena tvā vande viśvāsu dikṣu ||5||

(19,49.6 ) stomasya no vibhāvari rātri rājeva joṣase |
(19,49.6 ) asāma sarvavīrā bhavāma sarvavedaso vyuchantīranūṣasaḥ ||6||

(19,49.7 ) śamyā ha nāma dadhiṣe mama dipsanti ye dhanā |
(19,49.7 ) rātrīhi tān asutapā ya steno na vidyate yatpunarna vidyate ||7||

(19,49.8 ) bhadrāsi rātri camaso na viṣṭo viṣvaṅgorūpaṃ yuvatirbibharṣi |
(19,49.8 ) cakṣuṣmatī me uśatī vapūmṣi prati tvaṃ divyā na kṣāmamukthāḥ ||8||

(19,49.9 ) yo adya stena āyatyaghāyurmartyo ripuḥ |
(19,49.9 ) rātrī tasya pratītya pra grīvāḥ pra śiro hanat||9||

(19,49.10 ) pra pādau na yathāyati pra hastau na yathāśiṣat|
(19,49.10 ) yo malimlurupāyati sa saṃpiṣṭo apāyati |
(19,49.10 ) apāyati svapāyati śuṣke sthāṇāvapāyati ||10||



(19,50.1 ) adha rātri tṛṣṭadhūmamaśīrṣāṇamahiṃ kṛṇu |
(19,50.1 ) akṣau vṛkasya nirjahyāstena taṃ drupade jahi ||1||

(19,50.2 ) ye te rātryanaḍvāhastīkṣṇaśṛṅgāḥ svāśavaḥ |
(19,50.2 ) tebhirno adya pārayāti durgāṇi viśvahā ||2||

(19,50.3 ) rātriṃrātrimariṣyantastarema tanvā vayam |
(19,50.3 ) gambhīramaplavā iva na tareyurarātayaḥ ||3||

(19,50.4 ) yathā śāmyākaḥ prapatann apavān nānuvidyate |
(19,50.4 ) evā rātri pra pātaya yo asmāmabhyaghāyati ||4||

(19,50.5 ) apa stenaṃ vāsayo goajamuta taskaram |
(19,50.5 ) atho yo arvataḥ śiro'bhidhāya ninīṣati ||5||

(19,50.6 ) yadadya rātri subhage vibhajantyayo vasu |
(19,50.6 ) yadetadasmān bhojaya yathedanyān upāyasi ||6||

(19,50.7 ) uṣase naḥ pari dehi sarvān rātryanāgasaḥ |
(19,50.7 ) uṣā no ahne ā bhajādahastubhyaṃ vibhāvari ||7||



(19,51.1 ) ayuto'hamayuto ma ātmāyutaṃ me cakṣurayutaṃ me śrotram |
(19,51.1 ) ayuto me prāṇo'yuto me'pāno'yuto me vyāno'yuto'haṃ sarvaḥ ||1||

(19,51.2 ) devasya tvā savituḥ prasave'śvinorbāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo hastābhyāṃ prasūta ā rabhe ||2||



(19,52.1 ) kāmastadagre samavartata manaso retaḥ prathamaṃ yadāsīt|
(19,52.1 ) sa kāma kāmena bṛhatā sayonī rāyaspoṣaṃ yajamānāya dhehi ||1||

(19,52.2 ) tvaṃ kāma sahasāsi pratiṣṭhito vibhurvibhāvā sakha ā sakhīyate |
(19,52.2 ) tvamugraḥ pṛtanāsu sasahiḥ saha ojo yajamānāya dhehi ||2||

(19,52.3 ) dūrāccakamānāya pratipāṇāyākṣaye |
(19,52.3 ) āsmā aśṛṇvann āśāḥ kāmenājanayantsvaḥ ||3||

(19,52.4 ) kāmena mā kāma āgan hṛdayāddhṛdayaṃ pari |
(19,52.4 ) yadamīṣāmado manastadaitūpa māmiha ||4||

(19,52.5 ) yatkāma kāmayamānā idaṃ kṛṇmasi te haviḥ |
(19,52.5 ) tan naḥ sarvaṃ samṛdhyatāmathaitasya haviṣo vīhi svāhā ||5||



(19,53.1 ) kālo aśvo vahati saptaraśmiḥ sahasrākṣo ajaro bhūriretāḥ |
(19,53.1 ) tamā rohanti kavayo vipaścitastasya cakrā bhuvanāni viśvā ||1||

(19,53.2 ) sapta cakrān vahati kāla eṣa saptāsya nābhīramṛtaṃ nvakṣaḥ |
(19,53.2 ) sa imā viśvā bhuvanānyañjatkālaḥ sa īyate prathamo nu devaḥ ||2||

(19,53.3 ) pūrṇaḥ kumbho'dhi kāla āhitastaṃ vai paśyāmo bahudhā nu santam |
(19,53.3 ) sa imā viśvā bhuvanāni pratyaṅkālaṃ tamāhuḥ parame vyoman ||3||

(19,53.4 ) sa eva saṃ bhuvanānyābharatsa eva saṃ bhuvanāni paryait|
(19,53.4 ) pitā sann abhavatputra eṣāṃ tasmādvai nānyatparamasti tejaḥ ||4||

(19,53.5 ) kālo'mūṃ divamajanayatkāla imāḥ pṛthivīruta |
(19,53.5 ) kāle ha bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ ceṣitaṃ ha vi tiṣṭhate ||5||

(19,53.6 ) kālo bhūtimasṛjata kāle tapati sūryaḥ |
(19,53.6 ) kāle ha viśvā bhūtāni kāle cakṣurvi paśyati ||6||

(19,53.7 ) kāle manaḥ kāle prāṇaḥ kāle nāma samāhitam |
(19,53.7 ) kālena sarvā nandantyāgatena prajā imāḥ ||7||

(19,53.8 ) kāle tapaḥ kāle jyeṣṭhaṃ kāle brahma samāhitam |
(19,53.8 ) kālo ha sarvasyeśvaro yaḥ pitāsītprajāpateḥ ||8||

(19,53.9 ) teneṣitaṃ tena jātaṃ tadu tasmin pratiṣṭhitam |
(19,53.9 ) kālo ha brahma bhūtvā bibharti parameṣṭhinam ||9||

(19,53.10 ) kālaḥ prajā asṛjata kālo agre prajāpatim |
(19,53.10 ) svayaṃbhūḥ kaśyapaḥ kālāttapaḥ kālādajāyata ||10||



(19,54.1 ) kālādāpaḥ samabhavan kālādbrahma tapo diśaḥ |
(19,54.1 ) kālenodeti sūryaḥ kāle ni viśate punaḥ ||1||

(19,54.2 ) kālena vātaḥ pavate kālena pṛthivī mahī |
(19,54.2 ) dyaurmahī kāla āhitā ||2||

(19,54.3 ) kālo ha bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ ca putro ajanayatpurā |
(19,54.3 ) kālādṛcaḥ samabhavan yajuḥ kālādajāyata ||3||

(19,54.4 ) kālo yajñaṃ samairayaddevebhyo bhāgamakṣitam |
(19,54.4 ) kāle gandharvāpsarasaḥ kāle lokāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ ||4||

(19,54.5 ) kāle'yamaṅgirā devo'tharvā cādhi tiṣṭhataḥ |
(19,54.5 ) imaṃ ca lokaṃ paramaṃ ca lokaṃ puṇyāṃśca lokān vidhṛtīśca puṇyāḥ |
(19,54.5 ) sarvāṃllokān abhijitya brahmaṇā kālaḥ sa īyate paramo nu devaḥ ||5||



(19,55.1 ) rātriṃrātrimaprayātaṃ bharanto'śvāyeva tiṣṭhate ghāsamasmai |
(19,55.1 ) rāyaspoṣeṇa samiṣā madanto mā te agne prativeśā riṣāma ||1||

(19,55.2 ) yā te vasorvāta iṣuḥ sā ta eṣā tayā no mṛḍa |
(19,55.2 ) rāyaspoṣeṇa samiṣā madanto mā te agne prativeśā riṣāma ||2||

(19,55.3 ) sāyaṃsāyaṃ gṛhapatirno agniḥ prātaḥprātaḥ saumanasasya dātā |
(19,55.3 ) vasorvasorvasudāna edhi vayaṃ tvendhānāstanvaṃ puṣema ||3||

(19,55.4 ) prātaḥprātargṛhapatirno agniḥ sāyaṃsāyaṃ saumanasasya dātā |
(19,55.4 ) vasorvasorvasudāna edhīndhānāstvā śataṃhimā ṛdhema ||4||

(19,55.5 ) apaścā dagdhānnasya bhūyāsam |
(19,55.5 ) annādāyānnapataye rudrāya namo agnaye |
(19,55.5 ) sabhyaḥ sabhāṃ me pāhi ye ca sabhyāḥ sabhāsadaḥ ||5||

(19,55.6 ) tvāmindrā puruhūta viśvamāyurvyaśnavan |
(19,55.6 ) aharaharbalimitte haranto'śvāyeva tiṣṭhate ghāsamagne ||6||



(19,56.1 ) yamasya lokādadhyā babhūvitha pramadā martyān pra yunakṣi dhīraḥ |
(19,56.1 ) ekākinā sarathaṃ yāsi vidvāntsvapnaṃ mimāno asurasya yonau ||1||

(19,56.2 ) bandhastvāgre viśvacayā apaśyatpurā rātryā janitoreke ahni |
(19,56.2 ) tataḥ svapnedamadhyā babhūvitha bhiṣagbhyo rūpamapagūhamānaḥ ||2||

(19,56.3 ) bṛhadgāvāsurebhyo'dhi devān upāvartata mahimānamichan |
(19,56.3 ) tasmai svapnāya dadhurādhipatyaṃ trayastriṃśāsaḥ svarānaśānāḥ ||3||

(19,56.4 ) naitāṃ viduḥ pitaro nota devā yeṣāṃ jalpiścaratyantaredam |
(19,56.4 ) trite svapnamadadhurāptye nara ādityāso varuṇenānuśiṣṭāḥ ||4||

(19,56.5 ) yasya krūramabhajanta duṣkṛto'svapnena sukṛtaḥ puṇyamāyuḥ |
(19,56.5 ) svarmadasi parameṇa bandhunā tapyamānasya manaso'dhi jajñiṣe ||5||

(19,56.6 ) vidma te sarvāḥ parijāḥ purastādvidma svapna yo adhipā ihā te |
(19,56.6 ) yaśaśvino no yaśaseha pāhyārāddviṣebhirapa yāhi dūram ||6||


(19,57.1 ) yathā kalāṃ yathā śaphaṃ yathā rṇaṃ sannayanti |
(19,57.1 ) evā duṣvapnyaṃ sarvamapriye saṃ nayāmasi ||1||

(19,57.2 ) saṃ rājāno aguḥ samṛṇāmyaguḥ saṃ kuṣṭhā aguḥ saṃ kalā aguḥ |
(19,57.2 ) samasmāsu yadduṣvapnyaṃ nirdviṣate duṣvapnyaṃ suvāma ||2||

(19,57.3 ) devānāṃ patnīnāṃ garbha yamasya kara yo bhadraḥ svapna |
(19,57.3 ) sa mama yaḥ pāpastaddviṣate pra hiṇmaḥ |
(19,57.3 ) mā tṛṣṭānāmasi kṛṣṇaśakunermukham ||3||

(19,57.4 ) taṃ tvā svapna tathā saṃ vidma sa tvaṃ svapnāśva iva kāyamaśva iva nīnāham |
(19,57.4 ) anāsmākaṃ devapīyuṃ piyāruṃ vapa yadasmāsu duṣvapnyaṃ yadgoṣu yacca no gṛhe ||4||

(19,57.5 ) anāsmākastaddevapīyuḥ piyārurniṣkamiva prati muñcatām |
(19,57.5 ) navāratnīn apamayā asmākaṃ tataḥ pari |
(19,57.5 ) duṣvapnyaṃ sarvaṃ dviṣate nirdayāmasi ||5||



(19,58.1 ) ghṛtasya jūtiḥ samanā sadevā saṃvatsaraṃ haviṣā vardhayantī |
(19,58.1 ) śrotraṃ cakṣuḥ prāṇo'chinno no astvachinnā vayamāyuṣo varcasaḥ ||1||
(19,58.2 ) upāsmān prāṇo hvayatāmupa prāṇaṃ havāmahe |
(19,58.2 ) varco jagrāha pṛthivyantarikṣaṃ varcaḥ somo bṛhaspatirvidhattā ||2||

(19,58.3 ) varcaso dyāvāpṛthivī saṃgrahaṇī babhūvathurvarco gṛhītvā pṛthivīmanu saṃ carema |
(19,58.3 ) yaśasaṃ gāvo gopatimupa tiṣṭhantyāyatīryaśo gṛhītvā pṛthivīmanu saṃ carema ||3||

(19,58.4 ) vrajaṃ kṛṇudhvaṃ sa hi vo nṛpāṇo varmā sīvyadhvaṃ bahulā pṛthūni |
(19,58.4 ) puraḥ kṛṇudhvamāyasīradhṛṣṭā mā vaḥ susroccamaso dṛṃhata tam ||4||

(19,58.5 ) yajñasya cakṣuḥ prabhṛtirmukhaṃ ca vācā śrotreṇa manasā juhomi |
(19,58.5 ) imaṃ yajñaṃ vitataṃ viśvakarmaṇā devā yantu sumanasyamānāḥ ||5||
(19,58.6 ) ye devānāmṛtvijo ye ca yajñiyā yebhyo havyaṃ kriyate bhāgadheyam |
(19,58.6 ) imaṃ yajñaṃ saha patnībhiretya yāvanto devāstaviṣā mādayantām ||6||



(19,59.1 ) tvamagne vratapā asi deva ā martyeṣvā |
(19,59.1 ) tvaṃ yajñeṣvīḍyaḥ ||1||

(19,59.2 ) yadvo vayaṃ pramināma vratāni viduṣāṃ devā aviduṣṭarāsaḥ |
(19,59.2 ) agniṣṭadviśvādā pṛṇātu vidvāntsomasya yo brāhmaṇāmāviveśa ||2||

(19,59.3 ) ā devānāmapi panthāmaganma yacchaknavāma tadanupravoḍhum |
(19,59.3 ) agnirvidvāntsa yajātsa iddhotā so'dhvarāntsa ṛtūn kalpayāti ||3||



(19,60.1 ) vāṅma āsan nasoḥ prāṇaścakṣurakṣṇoḥ śrotraṃ karṇayoḥ |
(19,60.1 ) apalitāḥ keśā aśoṇā dantā bahu bāhvorbalam ||1||
(19,60.2 ) ūrvorojo jaṅghayorjavaḥ pādayoḥ |
(19,60.2 ) pratiṣṭhā ariṣṭāni me sarvātmānibhṛṣṭaḥ ||2||



(19,61.1 ) tanūstanvā me sahe dataḥ sarvamāyuraśīya |
(19,61.1 ) syonaṃ me sīda puruḥ pṛṇasva pavamānaḥ svarge ||1||


(19,62.1 ) priyaṃ mā kṛṇu deveṣu priyaṃ rājasu mā kṛṇu |
(19,62.1 ) priyaṃ sarvasya paśyata uta śūdra utārye ||1||



(19,63.1 ) uttiṣṭha brahmaṇaspate devān yajñena bodhaya |
(19,63.1 ) āyuḥ prāṇaṃ prajāṃ paśūn kīrtiṃ yajamānaṃ ca vardhaya ||1||


(19,64.1 ) agne samidhamāhārṣaṃ bṛhate jātavedase |
(19,64.1 ) sa me śraddhāṃ ca medhāṃ ca jātavedāḥ pra yachatu ||1||

(19,64.2 ) idhmena tvā jātavedaḥ samidhā vardhayāmasi |
(19,64.2 ) tathā tvamasmān vardhaya prajayā ca dhanena ca ||2||

(19,64.3 ) yadagne yāni kāni cidā te dārūṇi dadhmasi |
(19,64.3 ) sarvaṃ tadastu me śivaṃ tajjuṣasva yaviṣṭhya ||3||

(19,64.4 ) etāste agne samidhastvamiddhaḥ samidbhava |
(19,64.4 ) āyurasmāsu dhehyamṛtatvamācāryāya ||4||



(19,65.1 ) hariḥ suparṇo divamāruho'rciṣā ye tvā dipsanti divamutpatantam |
(19,65.1 ) ava tāṃ jahi harasā jātavedo'bibhyadugro'rciṣā divamā roha sūrya ||1||



(19,66.1 ) ayojālā asurā māyino'yasmayaiḥ pāśairaṅkino ye caranti |
(19,66.1 ) tāṃste randhayāmi harasā jātavedaḥ sahasraṛṣṭiḥ sapatnān pramṛṇan pāhi vajraḥ ||1||



(19,67.1 ) paśyema śaradaḥ śatam ||1||

(19,67.2 ) jīvema śaradaḥ śatam ||2||

(19,67.3 ) budhyema śaradaḥ śatam ||3||

(19,67.4 ) rohema śaradaḥ śatam ||4||

(19,67.5 ) pūṣema śaradaḥ śatam ||5||

(19,67.6 ) bhavema śaradaḥ śatam ||6||

(19,67.7 ) bhūṣema śaradaḥ śatam ||7||
(19,67.8 ) bhūyasīḥ śaradaḥ śatam ||8||



(19,68.1 ) avyasaśca vyacasaśca bilaṃ vi ṣyāmi māyayā |
(19,68.1 ) tābhyāmuddhṛtya vedamatha karmāṇi kṛṇmahe ||1||



(19,69.1 ) jīvā stha jīvyāsaṃ sarvamāyurjīvyāsam ||1||

(19,69.2 ) upajīvā sthopa jīvyāsaṃ sarvamāyurjīvyāsam ||2||

(19,69.3 ) saṃjīvā stha saṃ jīvyāsaṃ sarvamāyurjīvyāsam ||3||

(19,69.4 ) jīvalā stha jīvyāsaṃ sarvamāyurjīvyāsam ||4||



(19,70.1 ) indra jīva sūrya jīva devā jīvā jīvyāsamaham |
(19,70.1 ) sarvamāyurjīvyāsam ||1||



(19,71.1 ) stutā mayā varadā vedamātā pra codayantāṃ pāvamānī dvijānām |
(19,71.1 ) āyuḥ prāṇaṃ prajāṃ paśuṃ kīrtiṃ draviṇaṃ brahmavarcasam |
(19,71.1 ) mahyaṃ dattvā vrajata brahmalokam ||1||



(19,72.1 ) yasmātkośādudabharāma vedaṃ tasminn antarava dadhma enam |
(19,72.1 ) kṛtamiṣṭaṃ brahmaṇo vīryeṇa tena mā devāstapasāvateha ||1||



(20,1.1 ) indra tvā vṛṣabhaṃ vayaṃ sute some havāmahe |
(20,1.1 ) sa pāhi madhvo andhasaḥ ||1||

(20,1.2 ) maruto yasya hi kṣaye pāthā divo vimahasaḥ |
(20,1.2 ) sa sugopātamo janaḥ ||2||

(20,1.3 ) ukṣānnāya vaśānnāya somapṛṣṭhāya vedhase |
(20,1.3 ) stomairvidhemāgnaye ||3||



(20,2.1 ) marutaḥ potrātsuṣṭubhaḥ svarkādṛtunā somaṃ pibantu ||1||

(20,2.2 ) agnirāgnīdhrātsuṣṭubhaḥ svarkādṛtunā somaṃ pibatu ||2||

(20,2.3 ) indro brahmā brāhmaṇātsuṣṭubhaḥ svarkādṛtunā somaṃ pibatu ||3||

(20,2.4 ) devo draviṇodāḥ potrātsuṣṭubhaḥ svarkādṛtunā somaṃ pibatu ||4||



(20,3.1 ) ā yāhi suṣumā hi ta indra somaṃ pibā imam |
(20,3.1 ) edaṃ barhiḥ sado mama ||1||

(20,3.2 ) ā tvā brahmayujā harī vahatāmindra keśinā |
(20,3.2 ) upa brahmāṇi naḥ śṛṇu ||2||

(20,3.3 ) brahmāṇastvā vayaṃ yujā somapāmindra sominaḥ |
(20,3.3 ) sutāvanto havāmahe ||3||


(20,4.1 ) ā no yāhi sutāvato'smākaṃ suṣṭutīrupa |
(20,4.1 ) pibā su śiprinn andhasaḥ ||1||

(20,4.2 ) ā te siñcāmi kukṣyoranu gātrā vi dhāvatu |
(20,4.2 ) gṛbhāya jihvayā madhu ||2||

(20,4.3 ) svāduṣṭe astu saṃsude madhumān tanve tava |
(20,4.3 ) somaḥ śamastu te hṛde ||3||


(20,5.1 ) ayamu tvā vicarṣaṇe janīrivābhi saṃvṛtaḥ |
(20,5.1 ) pra soma indra sarpatu ||1||

(20,5.2 ) tuvigrīvo vapodaraḥ subāhurandhaso sade |
(20,5.2 ) indro vṛtrāṇi jighnate ||2||

(20,5.3 ) indra prehi purastvaṃ viśvasyeśāna ojasā |
(20,5.3 ) vṛtrāṇi vṛtrahaṃ jahi ||3||

(20,5.4 ) dīrghaste astvaṅkuśo yenā vasu prayachasi |
(20,5.4 ) yajamānāya sunvate ||4||

(20,5.5 ) ayaṃ ta indra somo nipūto adhi barhiṣi |
(20,5.5 ) ehīmasya dravā piba ||5||

(20,5.6 ) śācigo śācipūjanāyaṃ raṇāya te sutaḥ |
(20,5.6 ) ākhaṇḍala pra hūyase ||6||

(20,5.7 ) yaste śṛṅgavṛṣo napātpraṇapātkuṇḍapāyyaḥ |
(20,5.7 ) nyasmin dadhra ā manaḥ ||7||



(20,6.1 ) indra tvā vṛṣabhaṃ vayaṃ sute some havāmahe |
(20,6.1 ) sa pāhi madhvo andhasaḥ ||1||

(20,6.2 ) indra kratuvidaṃ sutaṃ somaṃ harya puruṣṭuta |
(20,6.2 ) pibā vṛṣasva tātṛpim ||2||

(20,6.3 ) indra pra ṇo dhitāvānaṃ yajñaṃ viśvebhirdevebhir|
(20,6.3 ) tira stavāna viśpate ||3||

(20,6.4 ) indra somāḥ sutā ime tava pra yanti satpate |
(20,6.4 ) kṣayaṃ candrāsa indavaḥ ||4||

(20,6.5 ) dadhiṣvā jaṭhare sutaṃ somamindra vareṇyam |
(20,6.5 ) tava dyukṣāsa indavaḥ ||5||

(20,6.6 ) girvaṇaḥ pāhi naḥ sutaṃ madhordhārābhirajyase |
(20,6.6 ) indra tvādātamidyaśaḥ ||6||

(20,6.7 ) abhi dyumnāni vanina indraṃ sacante akṣitā |
(20,6.7 ) pītvī somasya vāvṛdhe ||7||

(20,6.8 ) arvāvato na ā gahi parāvataśca vṛtrahan |
(20,6.8 ) imā juṣasva no giraḥ ||8||

(20,6.9 ) yadantarā parāvatamarvāvataṃ ca hūyase |
(20,6.9 ) indreha tata ā gahi ||9||



(20,7.1 ) udghedabhi śrutāmaghaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ naryāpasam |
(20,7.1 ) astārameṣi sūrya ||1||

(20,7.2 ) nava yo navatiṃ puro bibheda bāhvojasā |
(20,7.2 ) ahiṃ ca vṛtrahāvadhīt||2||

(20,7.3 ) sa na indraḥ śivaḥ sakhāśvāvadgomadyavamat|
(20,7.3 ) urudhāreva dohate ||3||

(20,7.4 ) indra kratuvidaṃ sutaṃ somaṃ harya puruṣṭuta |
(20,7.4 ) pibā vṛṣasva tātṛpim ||4||



(20,8.1 ) evā pāhi pratnathā mandatu tvā śrudhi brahma vāvṛdhasvota gīrbhiḥ |
(20,8.1 ) āviḥ sūryaṃ kṛṇuhi pīpihīṣo jahi śatrūṃrabhi gā indra tṛndhi ||1||

(20,8.2 ) arvāṅehi somakāmaṃ tvāhurayaṃ sutastasya pibā madāya |
(20,8.2 ) uruvyacā jaṭhara ā vṛṣasva piteva naḥ śṛṇuhi hūyamānaḥ ||2||

(20,8.3 ) āpūrṇo asya kalaśaḥ svāhā sekteva kośaṃ siṣice pibadhyai |
(20,8.3 ) samu priyā āvavṛtran madāya pradakṣiṇidabhi somāsa indram ||3||



(20,9.1 ) taṃ vo dasmamṛtīṣahaṃ vasormandānamandhasaḥ |
(20,9.1 ) abhi vatsaṃ na svasareṣu dhenava indraṃ gīrbhirnavāmahe ||1||

(20,9.2 ) dyukṣaṃ sudānuṃ taviṣībhirāvṛtaṃ giriṃ na purubhojasam |
(20,9.2 ) kṣumantaṃ vājaṃ śatinaṃ sahasriṇaṃ makṣū gomantamīmahe ||2||

(20,9.3 ) tattvā yāmi suvīryaṃ tadbrahma pūrvacittaye |
(20,9.3 ) yenā yatibhyo bhṛgave dhane hite yena praskaṇvamāvitha ||3||

(20,9.4 ) yenā samudramasṛjo mahīrapastadindra vṛṣṇi te śavaḥ |
(20,9.4 ) sadyaḥ so asya mahimā na saṃnaśe yaṃ kṣoṇīranucakrade ||4||



(20,10.1 ) udu te madhumattamā gira stomāsa īrate |
(20,10.1 ) satrājito dhanasā akṣitotayo vājayanto rathā iva ||1||

(20,10.2 ) kaṇvā iva bhṛgavaḥ sūryā iva viśvamiddhītamānaśuḥ |
(20,10.2 ) indraṃ stomebhirmahayanta āyavaḥ priyamedhāso asvaran ||2||



(20,11.1 ) indraḥ pūrbhidātiraddāsamarkairvidadvasurdayamāno vi śatrūn |
(20,11.1 ) brahmajūtastanvā vāvṛdhāno bhūridātra āpṛṇadrodasī ubhe ||1||

(20,11.2 ) makhasya te taviṣasya pra jūtimiyarmi vācamamṛtāya bhūṣan |
(20,11.2 ) indra kṣitīnāmasi mānuṣīṇāṃ viśāṃ daivīnāmuta pūrvayāvā ||2||

(20,11.3 ) indro vṛtramavṛṇocchardhanītiḥ pra māyināmaminādvarpaṇītiḥ |
(20,11.3 ) ahan vyaṃsamuśadhagvaneṣvāvirdhenā akṛṇodrāmyāṇām ||3||

(20,11.4 ) indraḥ svarṣā janayann ahāni jigāyośigbhiḥ pṛtanā abhiṣṭiḥ |
(20,11.4 ) prārocayan manave ketumahnāmavindajjyotirbṛhate raṇāya ||4||

(20,11.5 ) indrastujo barhaṇā ā viveśa nṛvaddadhāno naryā purūṇi |
(20,11.5 ) acetayaddhiya imā jaritre premaṃ varṇamatiracchukramāsām ||5||

(20,11.6 ) maho mahāni panayantyasyendrasya karma sukṛtā purūṇi |
(20,11.6 ) vṛjanena vṛjināntsaṃ pipeṣa māyābhirdasyūṃrabhibhūtyojāḥ ||6||

(20,11.7 ) yudhendro mahnā varivaścakāra devebhyaḥ satpatiścarṣaṇiprāḥ |
(20,11.7 ) vivasvataḥ sadane asya tāni viprā ukthebhiḥ kavayo gṛṇanti ||7||

(20,11.8 ) satrāsāhaṃ vareṇyaṃ sahodāṃ sasavāṃsaṃ svarapaśca devīḥ |
(20,11.8 ) sasāna yaḥ pṛthivīṃ dyāmutemāmindraṃ madantyanu dhīraṇāsaḥ ||8||

(20,11.9 ) sasānātyāmuta sūryaṃ sasānendraḥ sasāna purubhojasaṃ gām |
(20,11.9 ) hiraṇyayamuta bhogaṃ sasāna hatvī dasyūn prāryaṃ varṇamāvat||9||

(20,11.10 ) indra oṣadhīrasanodahāni vanaspatīṃrasanodantarikṣam |
(20,11.10 ) bibheda balaṃ nunude vivāco'thābhavaddamitābhikratūnām ||10||

(20,11.11 ) śunaṃ huvema maghavānamindramasmin bhare nṛtamaṃ vājasātau |
(20,11.11 ) śṛṇvantamugramūtaye samatsu ghnantaṃ vṛtrāṇi saṃjitaṃ dhanānām ||11||


(20,12.1 ) udu brahmāṇyairata śravasyendraṃ samarye mahayā vasiṣṭha |
(20,12.1 ) ā yo viśvāni śavasā tatānopaśrotā ma īvato vacāṃsi ||1||

(20,12.2 ) ayāmi ghoṣa indra devajāmirirajyanta yacchurudho vivāci |
(20,12.2 ) nahi svamāyuścikite janeṣu tānīdaṃhāṃsyati parṣyasmān ||2||

(20,12.3 ) yuje rathaṃ gaveṣaṇaṃ haribhyāmupa brahmāṇi jujuṣāṇamasthuḥ |
(20,12.3 ) vi bādhiṣṭa sya rodasī mahitvendro vṛtrāṇyapratī jaghanvān ||3||

(20,12.4 ) āpaścitpipyu staryo na gāvo nakṣann ṛtaṃ jaritārasta indra |
(20,12.4 ) yāhi vāyurna niyuto no achā tvaṃ hi dhībhirdayase vi vājān ||4||

(20,12.5 ) te tvā madā indra mādayantu śuṣmiṇaṃ tuvirādhasaṃ jaritre |
(20,12.5 ) eko devatrā dayase hi martān asmin chūra savane mādayasva ||5||

(20,12.6 ) evedindraṃ vṛṣaṇaṃ vajrabāhuṃ vasiṣṭhāso abhyarcantyarkaiḥ |
(20,12.6 ) sa na stuto vīravaddhātu gomadyūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||6||

(20,12.7 ) ṛjīṣī vajrī vṛṣabhasturāṣāṭchuṣmī rājā vṛtrahā somapāvā |
(20,12.7 ) yuktvā haribhyāmupa yāsadarvāṅmādhyaṃdine savane matsadindraḥ ||7||



(20,13.1 ) indraśca somaṃ pibataṃ bṛhaspate'smin yajñe mandasānā vṛṣaṇvasū |
(20,13.1 ) ā vāṃ viśantvindavaḥ svābhuvo'sme rayiṃ sarvavīraṃ ni yachatam ||1||

(20,13.2 ) ā vo vahantu saptayo raghuṣyado raghupatvānaḥ pra jigāta bāhubhiḥ |
(20,13.2 ) sīdatā barhiruru vaḥ sadaskṛtaṃ mādayadhvaṃ maruto madhvo andhasaḥ ||2||

(20,13.3 ) imaṃ stomamarhate jātavedase rathamiva saṃ mahemā manīṣayā |
(20,13.3 ) bhadrā hi naḥ pramatirasya saṃsadyagne sakhye mā riṣāmā vayaṃ tava ||3||

(20,13.4 ) aibhiragne sarathaṃ yāhyarvāṅnānārathaṃ vā vibhavo hyaśvāḥ |
(20,13.4 ) patnīvatastriṃśataṃ trīṃśca devān anuṣvadhamā vaha mādayasva ||4||



(20,14.1 ) vayamu tvāmapūrvya sthūraṃ na kaccidbharanto'vasyavaḥ |
(20,14.1 ) vāje citraṃ havāmahe ||1||

(20,14.2 ) upa tvā karmann ūtaye sa no yuvograścakrāma yo dhṛṣat|
(20,14.2 ) tvāmiddhyavitāraṃ vavṛmahe sakhāya indra sānasim ||2||

(20,14.3 ) yo na idamidaṃ purā pra vasya ānināya tamu va stuṣe |
(20,14.3 ) sakhāya indramūtaye ||3||

(20,14.4 ) haryaśvaṃ satpatiṃ carṣaṇīsahaṃ sa hi ṣmā yo amandata |
(20,14.4 ) ā tu naḥ sa vayati gavyamaśvyaṃ stotṛbhyo maghavā śatam ||4||



(20,15.1 ) pra maṃhiṣṭhāya bṛhate bṛhadraye satyaśuṣmāya tavase matiṃ bhare |
(20,15.1 ) apāmiva pravaṇe yasya durdharaṃ rādho viśvāyu śavase apāvṛtam ||1||

(20,15.2 ) adha te viśvamanu hāsadiṣṭaya āpo nimneva savanā haviṣmataḥ |
(20,15.2 ) yatparvate na samaśīta haryata indrasya vajraḥ śnathitā hiraṇyayaḥ ||2||

(20,15.3 ) asmai bhīmāya namasā samadhvara uṣo na śubhra ā bharā panīyase |
(20,15.3 ) yasya dhāma śravase nāmendriyaṃ jyotirakāri harito nāyase ||3||

(20,15.4 ) ime ta indra te vayaṃ puruṣṭuta ye tvārabhya carāmasi prabhūvaso |
(20,15.4 ) nahi tvadanyo girvaṇo giraḥ sadhatkṣoṇīriva prati no harya tadvacaḥ ||4||

(20,15.5 ) bhūri ta indra vīryaṃ tava smasyasya stoturmaghavan kāmamā pṛṇa |
(20,15.5 ) anu te dyaurbṛhatī vīryaṃ mama iyaṃ ca te pṛthivī nema ojase ||5||

(20,15.6 ) tvaṃ tamindra parvataṃ mahāmuruṃ vajreṇa vajrin parvaśaścakartitha |
(20,15.6 ) avāsṛjo nivṛtāḥ sartavā apaḥ satrā viśvaṃ dadhiṣe kevalaṃ sahaḥ ||6||



(20,16.1 ) udapruto na vayo rakṣamāṇā vāvadato abhriyasyeva ghoṣāḥ |
(20,16.1 ) giribhrajo normayo madanto bṛhaspatimabhyarkā anāvan ||1||

(20,16.2 ) saṃ gobhiraṅgiraso nakṣamāṇo bhaga ivedaryamaṇaṃ nināya |
(20,16.2 ) jane mitro na dampatī anakti bṛhaspate vājayāśūṃrivājau ||2||

(20,16.3 ) sādhvaryā atithinīriṣirā spārhāḥ suvarṇā anavadyarūpāḥ |
(20,16.3 ) bṛhaspatiḥ parvatebhyo vitūryā nirgā ūpe yavamiva sthivibhyaḥ ||3||

(20,16.4 ) āpruṣāyan madhunā ṛtasya yonimavakṣipann arka ulkāmiva dyoḥ |
(20,16.4 ) bṛhaspatiruddharann aśmano gā bhūmyā udneva vi tvacaṃ bibheda ||4||

(20,16.5 ) apa jyotiṣā tamo antarikṣadudnaḥ śīpālamiva vāta ājat|
(20,16.5 ) bṛhaspatiranumṛśyā valasyābhramiva vāta ā cakra ā gāḥ ||5||

(20,16.6 ) yadā valasya pīyato jasuṃ bhedbṛhaspatiragnitapobhirarkaiḥ |
(20,16.6 ) dadbhirna jihvā pariviṣṭamādadāvirnidhīṃrakṛṇodusriyāṇām ||6||

(20,16.7 ) bṛhaspatiramata hi tyadāsāṃ nāma svarīṇāṃ sadane guhā yat|
(20,16.7 ) āṇḍeva bhitvā śakunasya garbhamudusriyāḥ parvatasya tmanājat||7||

(20,16.8 ) aśnāpinaddhaṃ madhu paryapaśyan matsyaṃ na dīna udani kṣiyantam |
(20,16.8 ) niṣṭajjabhāra camasaṃ na vṛkṣādbṛhaspatirviraveṇā vikṛtya ||8||

(20,16.9 ) soṣāmavindatsa svaḥ so agniṃ so arkeṇa vi babādhe tamāṃsi |
(20,16.9 ) bṛhaspatirgovapuṣo valasya nirmajjānaṃ na parvaṇo jabhāra ||9||

(20,16.10 ) himeva parṇā muṣitā vanāni bṛhaspatinākṛpayadvalo gāḥ |
(20,16.10 ) anānukṛtyamapunaścakāra yātsūryāmāsā mitha uccarātaḥ ||10||

(20,16.11 ) abhi śyāvaṃ na kṛśanebhiraśvaṃ nakṣatrebhiḥ pitaro dyāmapiṃśan |
(20,16.11 ) rātryāṃ tamo adadhurjyotirahan bṛhaspatirbhinadadriṃ vidadgāḥ ||11||

(20,16.12 ) idamakarma namo abhriyāya yaḥ pūrvīranvānonavīti |
(20,16.12 ) bṛhaspatiḥ sa hi gobhiḥ so aśvaiḥ sa vīrebhiḥ sa nṛbhirno vayo dhāt||12||



(20,17.1 ) achā ma indraṃ matayaḥ svarvidaḥ sadhrīcīrviśvā uśatīranūṣata |
(20,17.1 ) pari ṣvajante janayo yathā patiṃ maryaṃ na śundhyuṃ maghavānamūtaye ||1||

(20,17.2 ) na ghā tvadrigapa veti me manastve itkāmaṃ puruhūta śiśraya |
(20,17.2 ) rājeva dasma ni ṣado'dhi barhiṣyasmintsu some'vapānamastu te ||2||

(20,17.3 ) viṣūvṛdindro amuteruta kṣudhaḥ sa idrāyo maghavā vasva īśate |
(20,17.3 ) tasyedime pravaṇe sapta sindhavo vayo vardhanti vṛṣabhasya śuṣmiṇaḥ ||3||

(20,17.4 ) vayo na vṛkṣaṃ supalāśamāsadantsomāsa indraṃ mandinaścamūṣadaḥ |
(20,17.4 ) praiṣāmanīkaṃ śavasā davidyutadvidatsvarmanave jyotirāryam ||4||

(20,17.5 ) kṛtaṃ na śvaghnī vi cinoti devane saṃvargaṃ yan maghavā sūryaṃ jayat|
(20,17.5 ) na tatte anyo anu vīryaṃ śakan na purāṇo maghavan nota nūtanaḥ ||5||

(20,17.6 ) viśaṃviśaṃ maghavā paryaśāyata janānāṃ dhenā avacākaśadvṛṣā |
(20,17.6 ) yasyāha śakraḥ savaneṣu raṇyati sa tīvraiḥ somaiḥ sahate pṛtanyataḥ ||6||

(20,17.7 ) āpo na sindhumabhi yatsamakṣarantsomāsa indraṃ kulyā iva hradam |
(20,17.7 ) vardhanti viprā maho asya sādane yavaṃ na vṛṣṭirdivyena dānunā ||7||

(20,17.8 ) vṛṣā na kruddhaḥ patayadrajaḥsvā yo aryapatnīrakṛṇodimā apaḥ |
(20,17.8 ) sa sunvate maghavā jīradānave'vindajjyotirmanave haviṣmate ||8||

(20,17.9 ) ujjāyatāṃ paraśu jyotiṣā saha bhūyā ṛtasya sudughā purāṇavat|
(20,17.9 ) vi rocatāmaruṣo bhānunā śuciḥ svarna śukraṃ śuśucīta satpatiḥ ||9||

(20,17.10 ) gobhiṣṭaremāmatiṃ durevāṃ yavena kṣudhaṃ puruhūta viśvām |
(20,17.10 ) vayaṃ rājabhiḥ prathamā dhanānyasmākena vṛjanenā jayema ||10||

(20,17.11 ) bṛhaspatirnaḥ pari pātu paścādutottarasmādadharādaghāyoḥ |
(20,17.11 ) indraḥ purastāduta madhyato naḥ sakhā sakhibhyaḥ varivaḥ kṛṇotu ||11||

(20,17.12 ) bṛhaspate yuvamindraśca vasvo divyasyeśāthe uta pārthivasya |
(20,17.12 ) dhattaṃ rayiṃ stuvate kīraye cidyūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||12||


(20,18.1 ) vayamu tvā taditarthā indra tvāyantaḥ sakhāyaḥ |
(20,18.1 ) kaṇvā ukthebhirjarante ||1||

(20,18.2 ) na ghemanyadā papana vajrinn apaso naviṣṭau |
(20,18.2 ) tavedu stomaṃ ciketa ||2||

(20,18.3 ) ichanti devāḥ sunvantaṃ na svapnāya spṛhayanti |
(20,18.3 ) yanti pramādamatandrāḥ ||3||

(20,18.4 ) vayamindra tvāyavo'bhi pra ṇonumo vṛṣan |
(20,18.4 ) viddhi tvasya no vaso ||4||

(20,18.5 ) mā no nide ca vaktave'ryo randhīrarāvne |
(20,18.5 ) tve api kraturmama ||5||

(20,18.6 ) tvaṃ varmāsi saprathaḥ puroyodhaśca vṛtrahan |
(20,18.6 ) tvayā prati bruve yujā ||6||



(20,19.1 ) vārtrahatyāya śavase pṛtanāṣāhyāya ca |
(20,19.1 ) indra tvā vartayāmasi ||1||

(20,19.2 ) arvācīnaṃ su te mana uta cakṣuḥ śatakrato |
(20,19.2 ) indra kṛṇvantu vāghataḥ ||2||

(20,19.3 ) nāmāni te śatakrato viśvābhirgīrbhirīmahe |
(20,19.3 ) indrābhimātiṣāhye ||3||

(20,19.4 ) puruṣṭutasya dhāmabhiḥ śatena mahayāmasi |
(20,19.4 ) indrasya carṣaṇīdhṛtaḥ ||4||

(20,19.5 ) indraṃ vṛtrāya hantave puruhūtamupa bruve |
(20,19.5 ) bhareṣu vājasātaye ||5||

(20,19.6 ) vājeṣu sāsahirbhava tvāmīmahe śatakrato |
(20,19.6 ) indra vṛtrāya hantave ||6||

(20,19.7 ) dyumneṣu pṛtanājye pṛtsutūrṣu śravaḥsu ca |
(20,19.7 ) indra sākṣvābhimātiṣu ||7||



(20,20.1 ) śuṣmintamaṃ na ūtaye dyumninaṃ pāhi jāgṛvim |
(20,20.1 ) indra somaṃ śatakrato ||1||

(20,20.2 ) indriyāṇi śatakrato yā te janeṣu pañcasu |
(20,20.2 ) indra tāni ta ā vṛṇe ||2||

(20,20.3 ) agann indra śravo bṛhaddyumnaṃ dadhiṣva duṣṭaram |
(20,20.3 ) utte śuṣmaṃ tirāmasi ||3||

(20,20.4 ) arvāvato na ā gahyatho śakra parāvataḥ |
(20,20.4 ) u loko yaste adriva indreha tata ā gahi ||4||

(20,20.5 ) indro aṅgaṃ mahadbhayamabhi ṣadapa cucyavat|
(20,20.5 ) sa hi sthiro vicarṣaṇiḥ ||5||

(20,20.6 ) indraśca mṛlayāti no na naḥ paścādaghaṃ naśat|
(20,20.6 ) bhadraṃ bhavāti naḥ puraḥ ||6||

(20,20.7 ) indra āśābhyaspari sarvābhyo abhayaṃ karat|
(20,20.7 ) jetā śatrūn vicarṣaṇiḥ ||7||



(20,21.1 ) nyū ṣu vācaṃ pra mahe bharāmahe gira indrāya sadane vivasvataḥ |
(20,21.1 ) nū ciddhi ratnaṃ sasatāmivāvidan na duṣṭutirdraviṇodeṣu śasyate ||1||

(20,21.2 ) duro aśvasya dura indra gorasi duro yavasya vasuna inaspatiḥ |
(20,21.2 ) śikṣānaraḥ pradivo akāmakarśanaḥ sakhā sakhibhyastamidaṃ gṛṇīmasi ||2||

(20,21.3 ) śacīva indra purukṛddyumattama tavedidamabhitaścekite vasu |
(20,21.3 ) ataḥ saṃgṛbhyābhibhūta ā bhara mā tvāyato jarituḥ kāmamūnayīḥ ||3||

(20,21.4 ) ebhirdyubhirsumanā ebhirindubhirnirundhāno amatiṃ gobhiraśvinā |
(20,21.4 ) indreṇa dasyuṃ darayanta indubhiryutadveṣasaḥ samiṣā rabhemahi ||4||

(20,21.5 ) samindra rāyā samiṣā rabhemahi saṃ vājebhiḥ puruścandrairabhidyubhiḥ |
(20,21.5 ) saṃ devyā pramatyā vīraśuṣmayā goagrayāśvāvatyā rabhemahi ||5||

(20,21.6 ) te tvā madā amadan tāni vṛṣṇyā te somāso vṛtrahatyeṣu satpate |
(20,21.6 ) yatkārave daśa vṛtrāṇyaprati barhiṣmate ni sahasrāṇi barhayaḥ ||6||

(20,21.7 ) yudhā yudhamupa ghedeṣi dhṛṣṇuyā purā puraṃ samidaṃ haṃsyojasā |
(20,21.7 ) namyā yadindra sakhyā parāvati nibarhayo namuciṃ nāma māyinam ||7||

(20,21.8 ) tvaṃ karañjamuta parṇayaṃ vadhīstejiṣṭhayātithigvasya vartanī |
(20,21.8 ) tvaṃ śatā vaṅgṛdasyābhinatpuro'nānudaḥ pariṣūtā ṛjiśvanā ||8||
(20,21.9 ) tvametāṃ janarājño dvirdaśābandhunā suśravasopajagmuṣaḥ |
(20,21.9 ) ṣaṣṭiṃ sahasrā navatiṃ nava śruto ni cakreṇa rathyā duṣpadāvṛṇak||9||

(20,21.10 ) tvamāvitha suśravasaṃ tavotibhistava trāmabhirindra tūrvayāṇam |
(20,21.10 ) tva asmai kutsamatithigvamāyuṃ mahe rājñe yūne arandhanāyaḥ ||10||

(20,21.11 ) ya udṛcīndra devagopāḥ sakhāyaste śivatamā asāma |
