(20,21.11 ) tvāṃ stoṣāma tvayā suvīrā drāghīya āyuḥ prataraṃ dadhānāḥ ||11||



(20,22.1 ) abhi tvā vṛṣabhā sute sutaṃ sṛjāmi pītaye |
(20,22.1 ) tṛmpā vyaśnuhī madam ||1||

(20,22.2 ) mā tvā mūrā aviṣyavo mopahasvāna ā dabhan |
(20,22.2 ) mākīṃ brahmadviṣo vanaḥ ||2||

(20,22.3 ) iha tvā goparīṇasā mahe mandantu rādhase |
(20,22.3 ) saro gauro yathā piba ||3||

(20,22.4 ) abhi pra gopatiṃ girendramarca yathā vide |
(20,22.4 ) sūnuṃ satyasya satpatim ||4||

(20,22.5 ) ā harayaḥ sasṛjrire'ruṣīradhi barhiṣi |
(20,22.5 ) yatrābhi saṃnavāmahe ||5||

(20,22.6 ) indrāya gāva āśiraṃ duduhre vajriṇe madhu |
(20,22.6 ) yatsīmupahvare vidat||6||



(20,23.1 ) ā tū na indra madryagghuvānaḥ somapītaye |
(20,23.1 ) haribhyāṃ yāhyadrivaḥ ||1||

(20,23.2 ) satto hotā na ṛtviyastistire barhirānuṣak|
(20,23.2 ) ayujran prātaradrayaḥ ||2||

(20,23.3 ) imā brahma brahmavāhaḥ kriyanta ā barhiḥ sīda |
(20,23.3 ) vīhi śūra purolāśam ||3||

(20,23.4 ) rārandhi savaneṣu ṇa eṣu stomeṣu vṛtrahan |
(20,23.4 ) uktheṣvindra girvaṇaḥ ||4||

(20,23.5 ) matayaḥ somapāmuruṃ rihanti śavasaspatim |
(20,23.5 ) indraṃ vatsaṃ na mātaraḥ ||5||

(20,23.6 ) sa mandasvā hyandhaso rādhase tanvā mahe |
(20,23.6 ) na stotāraṃ nide karaḥ ||6||

(20,23.7 ) vayamindra tvāyavo haviṣmanto jarāmahe |
(20,23.7 ) uta tvamasmayurvaso ||7||

(20,23.8 ) māre asmadvi mumuco haripriyārvāṅyāhi |
(20,23.8 ) indra svadhāvo matsveha ||8||

(20,23.9 ) arvāñcaṃ tvā sukhe rathe vahatāmindra keśinā |
(20,23.9 ) ghṛtasnū barhirāsade ||9||



(20,24.1 ) upa naḥ sutamā gahi somamindra gavāśiram |
(20,24.1 ) haribhyāṃ yaste asmayuḥ ||1||

(20,24.2 ) tamindra madamā gahi barhiṣṭhāṃ grāvabhiḥ sutam |
(20,24.2 ) kuvin nvasya tṛpṇavaḥ ||2||

(20,24.3 ) indramitthā giro mamāchāguriṣitā itaḥ |
(20,24.3 ) āvṛte somapītaye ||3||

(20,24.4 ) indraṃ somasya pītaye stomairiha havāmahe |
(20,24.4 ) ukthebhiḥ kuvidāgamat||4||

(20,24.5 ) indra somāḥ sutā ime tān dadhiṣva śatakrato |
(20,24.5 ) jathare vājinīvaso ||5||

(20,24.6 ) vidmā hi tvā dhanaṃjayaṃ vājeṣu dadhṛṣaṃ kave |
(20,24.6 ) adhā te sumnamīmahe ||6||

(20,24.7 ) imamindra gavāśiraṃ yavāśiraṃ ca naḥ piba |
(20,24.7 ) āgatyā vṛṣabhiḥ sutam ||7||

(20,24.8 ) tubhyedindra sva okye somaṃ codāmi pītaye |
(20,24.8 ) eṣa rārantu te hṛdi ||8||

(20,24.9 ) tvāṃ sutasya pītaye pratnamindra havāmahe |
(20,24.9 ) kuśikāso avasyavaḥ ||9||


(20,25.1 ) aśvāvati prathamo goṣu gachati suprāvīrindra martyastavotibhiḥ |
(20,25.1 ) tamitpṛṇakṣi vasunā bhavīyasā sindhumāpo yathābhito vicetasaḥ ||1||

(20,25.2 ) āpo na devīrupa yanti hotriyamavaḥ paśyanti vitataṃ yathā rajaḥ |
(20,25.2 ) prācairdevāsaḥ pra ṇayanti devayuṃ brahmapriyaṃ joṣayante varā iva ||2||

(20,25.3 ) adhi dvayoradadhā ukthyaṃ vaco yatasrucā mithunā yā saparyataḥ |
(20,25.3 ) asaṃyatto vrate te kṣeti puṣyati bhadrā śaktiryajamānāya sunvate ||3||

(20,25.4 ) ādaṅgirāḥ prathamaṃ dadhire vaya iddhāgnayaḥ śamyā ye sukṛtyayā |
(20,25.4 ) sarvaṃ paṇeḥ samavindanta bhojanamaśvāvantaṃ gomantamā paśuṃ naraḥ ||4||

(20,25.5 ) yajñairatharvā prathamaḥ pathastate tataḥ sūryo vratapā vena ājani |
(20,25.5 ) ā gā ājaduśanā kāvyaḥ sacā yamasya jātamamṛtaṃ yajāmahe ||5||

(20,25.6 ) barhirvā yatsvapatyāya vṛjyate'rko vā ślokamāghoṣate divi |
(20,25.6 ) grāvā yatra vadati karurukthyastasyedindro abhipitveṣu raṇyati ||6||

(20,25.7 ) progrāṃ pītiṃ vṛṣṇa iyarmi satyāṃ prayai sutasya haryaśva tubhyam |
(20,25.7 ) indra dhenābhiriha mādayasva dhībhirviśvābhiḥ śacyā gṛṇānaḥ ||7||



(20,26.1 ) yogeyoge tavastaraṃ vājevāje havāmahe |
(20,26.1 ) sakhāya indramūtaye ||1||

(20,26.2 ) ā ghā gamadyadi śravatsahasriṇībhirūtibhiḥ |
(20,26.2 ) vājebhirupa no havam ||2||

(20,26.3 ) anu pratnasyaukaso huve tuvipratiṃ naram |
(20,26.3 ) yaṃ te pūrvaṃ pitā huve ||3||

(20,26.4 ) yuñjanti bradhnamaruṣaṃ carantaṃ pari tasthuṣaḥ |
(20,26.4 ) rocante rocanā divi ||4||

(20,26.5 ) yuñjanti asya kāmyā harī vipakṣasā rathe |
(20,26.5 ) śoṇā dhṛṣṇū nṛvāhasā ||5||

(20,26.6 ) ketuṃ kṛṇvann aketave peśo maryā apeśase |
(20,26.6 ) samuṣadbhirajāyathāḥ ||6||



(20,27.1 ) yadindrāhaṃ yathā tvamīśīya vasva eka it|
(20,27.1 ) stotā me goṣakhā syāt||1||

(20,27.2 ) śikṣeyamasmai ditseyaṃ śacīpate manīṣiṇe |
(20,27.2 ) yadahaṃ gopatiḥ syām ||2||

(20,27.3 ) dhenuṣṭa indra sūnṛtā yajamānāya sunvate |
(20,27.3 ) gāmaśvaṃ pipyuṣī duhe ||3||

(20,27.4 ) na te vartāsti rādhasa indra devo na martyaḥ |
(20,27.4 ) yadditsasi stuto magham ||4||

(20,27.5 ) yajña indramavardhayadyadbhūmiṃ vyavartayat|
(20,27.5 ) cakrāṇa opaśaṃ divi ||5||

(20,27.6 ) vāvṛdhānasya te vayaṃ viśvā dhanāni jigyuṣaḥ |
(20,27.6 ) ūtimindrā vṛṇīmahe ||6||



(20,28.1 ) vyantarikṣamatiran made somasya rocanā |
(20,28.1 ) indro yadabhinadvalam ||1||

(20,28.2 ) udgā ājadaṅgirobhya āviṣkrṇvan guhā satīḥ |
(20,28.2 ) arvāñcaṃ nunude valam ||2||

(20,28.3 ) indreṇa rocanā divo dṛlhāni dṛṃhitāni ca |
(20,28.3 ) sthirāṇi na parāṇude ||3||

(20,28.4 ) apāmūrmirmadann iva stoma indrājirāyate |
(20,28.4 ) vi te madā arājiṣuḥ ||4||



(20,29.1 ) tvaṃ hi stomavardhana indrāsyukthavardhanaḥ |
(20,29.1 ) stotṝṇāmuta bhadrakṛt||1||

(20,29.2 ) indramitkeśinā harī somapeyāya vakṣataḥ |
(20,29.2 ) upa yajñaṃ surādhasam ||2||

(20,29.3 ) apāṃ phenena namuceḥ śira indrodavartayaḥ |
(20,29.3 ) viśvā yadajaya spṛdhaḥ ||3||

(20,29.4 ) māyābhirutsisṛpsata indra dyāmārurukṣataḥ |
(20,29.4 ) ava dasyūṃradhūnuthāḥ ||4||

(20,29.5 ) asunvāmindra saṃsadaṃ viṣūcīṃ vyanāśayaḥ |
(20,29.5 ) somapā uttaro bhavan ||5||



(20,30.1 ) pra te mahe vidathe śaṃsiṣaṃ harī pra te vanve vanuṣo haryataṃ madam |
(20,30.1 ) ghṛtaṃ na yo haribhiścāru secata ā tvā viśantu harivarpasaṃ giraḥ ||1||

(20,30.2 ) hariṃ hi yonimabhi ye samasvaran hinvanto harī divyaṃ yathā sadaḥ |
(20,30.2 ) ā yaṃ pṛṇanti haribhirna dhenava indrāya śūśaṃ harivantamarcata ||2||

(20,30.3 ) so asya vajro harito ya āyaso harirnikāmo harirā gabhastyoḥ |
(20,30.3 ) dyumnī suśipro harimanyusāyaka indre ni rūpā haritā mimikṣire ||3||

(20,30.4 ) divi na keturadhi dhāyi haryato vivyacadvajro harito na raṃhyā |
(20,30.4 ) tudadahiṃ hariśipro ya āyasaḥ sahasraśokā abhavaddharimbharaḥ ||4||

(20,30.5 ) tvaṃtvamaharyathā upastutaḥ pūrvebhirindra harikeśa yajvabhiḥ |
(20,30.5 ) tvaṃ haryasi tava viśvamukthyamasāmi rādho harijāta haryatam ||5||



(20,31.1 ) tā vajriṇaṃ mandinaṃ stomyaṃ mada indraṃ rathe vahato haryatā harī |
(20,31.1 ) purūṇyasmai savanāni haryata indrāya somā harayo dadhanvire ||1||

(20,31.2 ) araṃ kāmāya harayo dadhamire sthirāya hinvan harayo harī turā |
(20,31.2 ) arvadbhiryo haribhirjoṣamīyate so asya kāmaṃ harivantamānaśe ||2||

(20,31.3 ) hariśmaśārurharikeśa āyasasturaspeye yo haripā avardhata |
(20,31.3 ) arvadbhiryo haribhirvājinīvasurati viśvā duritā pāriṣaddharī ||3||

(20,31.4 ) śruveva yasya hariṇī vipetatuḥ śipre vājāya hariṇī davidhvataḥ |
(20,31.4 ) pra yatkṛte camase marmṛjaddharī pītvā madasya haryatasyāndhasaḥ ||4||

(20,31.5 ) uta sma sadna haryatasya pastyoratyo na vājaṃ harivāmacikradat|
(20,31.5 ) mahī ciddhi dhiṣaṇāharyadojasā bṛhadvayo dadhiṣe haryatascidā ||5||



(20,32.1 ) ā rodasī haryamāṇo mahitvā navyaṃnavyaṃ haryasi manma nu priyam |
(20,32.1 ) pra pastyamasura haryataṃ gorāviṣkṛdhi haraye sūryāya ||1||

(20,32.2 ) ā tvā haryantaṃ prayujo janānāṃ rathe vahantu hariśipramindra |
(20,32.2 ) pibā yathā pratibhṛtasya madhvo haryan yajñaṃ sadhamāde daśoṇim ||2||

(20,32.3 ) apāḥ pūrveṣāṃ harivaḥ sutānāmatho idaṃ savanaṃ kevalaṃ te |
(20,32.3 ) mamaddhi somaṃ madhumantamindra satrā vṛṣaṃ jathara ā vṛṣasva ||3||



(20,33.1 ) apsu dhūtasya harivaḥ pibeha nṛbhiḥ sutasya jaṭharaṃ pṛṇasva |
(20,33.1 ) mimikṣuryamadraya indra tubhyaṃ tebhirvardhasva madamukthavāhaḥ ||1||

(20,33.2 ) progrāṃ pītiṃ vṛṣṇa iyarmi satyāṃ prayai sutasya haryaśva tubhyam |
(20,33.2 ) indra dhenābhiriha mādayasva dhībhirviśvābhiḥ śacyā gṛṇānaḥ ||2||

(20,33.3 ) ūtī śacīvastava vīryeṇa vayo dadhānā uśija ṛtajñāḥ |
(20,33.3 ) prajāvadindra maṃso duroṇe tasthurgṛṇantaḥ sadhamādyāsaḥ ||3||



(20,34.1 ) yo jāta eva prathamo manasvān devo devān kratunā paryamūṣat|
(20,34.1 ) yasya śuṣmādrodasī abhyasetāṃ nṛmṇasya mahnā sa janāsa indraḥ ||1||

(20,34.2 ) yaḥ pṛthivīṃ vyathamānāmadṛṃhadyaḥ parvatān prakupitāmaramṇāt|
(20,34.2 ) yo antarikṣaṃ vimame varīyo yo dyāmastabhnātsa janāsa indraḥ ||2||

(20,34.3 ) yo hatvāhimariṇātsapta sindhūn yo gā udājadapadhā valasya |
(20,34.3 ) yo aśmanorantaragniṃ jajāna saṃvṛksamatsu sa janāsa indraḥ ||3||

(20,34.4 ) yenemā viśvā cyavanā kṛtāni yo dāsaṃ varṇamadharaṃ guhākaḥ |
(20,34.4 ) śvaghnīva yo jigīvāṃ lakṣamādadaryaḥ puṣṭāni sa janāsa indraḥ ||4||

(20,34.5 ) yaṃ smā pṛchanti kuha seti ghoramutemāhurnaiṣo astītyenam |
(20,34.5 ) so aryaḥ puṣṭīrvija ivā mināti śradasmai dhatta sa janāsa indraḥ ||5||

(20,34.6 ) yo radhrasya coditā yaḥ kṛśasya yo brahmaṇo nādhamānasya kīreḥ |
(20,34.6 ) yuktagrāvṇo yo'vitā suśipraḥ sutasomasya sa janāsa indraḥ ||6||

(20,34.7 ) yasyāśvāsaḥ pradiśi yasya gāvo yasya grāmā yasya viśve rathāsaḥ |
(20,34.7 ) yaḥ sūryaṃ ya uṣasaṃ jajāna yo apāṃ netā sa janāsa indraḥ ||7||

(20,34.8 ) yaṃ krandasī saṃyatī vihvayete pare'vare ubhayā amitrāḥ |
(20,34.8 ) samānaṃ cidrathamātasthivāṃsā nānā havete sa janāsa indraḥ ||8||

(20,34.9 ) yasmān na ṛte vijayante janāso yaṃ yudhyamānā avase havante |
(20,34.9 ) yo viśvasya pratimānaṃ babhūva yo acyutacyutsa janāsa indraḥ ||9||
(20,34.10 ) yaḥ śasvato mahyeno dadhānān amanyamānāṃ charvā jaghāna |
(20,34.10 ) yaḥ śardhate nānudadāti śṛdhyāṃ yo dasyorhantā sa janāsa indraḥ ||10||

(20,34.11 ) yaḥ śambharaṃ parvateṣu kṣiyantaṃ catvāriṃśyāṃ śaradyanvavindat|
(20,34.11 ) ojāyamānaṃ yo ahiṃ jaghāna dānuṃ śayānaṃ sa janāsa indraḥ ||11||

(20,34.12 ) yaḥ śambharaṃ paryataratkasībhiryo'cārukāsnāpibatsutasya |
(20,34.12 ) antargirau yajamānaṃ bahuṃ janaṃ yasminn āmūrchatsa janāsa indraḥ ||12||

(20,34.13 ) yaḥ saptaraśmirvṛṣabhastuviṣmān avāsṛjatsartave sapta sindhūn |
(20,34.13 ) yo rauhiṇamasphuradvajrabāhurdyāmārohantaṃ sa janāsa indraḥ ||13||

(20,34.14 ) dyāvā cidasmai pṛthivī mamete śuṣmāccidasya parvatā bhayante |
(20,34.14 ) yaḥ somapā nicito vajrabāhuryo vajrahastaḥ sa janāsa indraḥ ||14||

(20,34.15 ) yaḥ sunvantamavati yaḥ pacantaṃ yaḥ śaṃsantaṃ yaḥ śaśamānamūtī |
(20,34.15 ) yasya brahma vardhanaṃ yasya somo yasyedaṃ rādhaḥ sa janāsa indraḥ ||15||

(20,34.16 ) jāto vyakhyatpitrorupasthe bhuvo na veda janituḥ parasya |
(20,34.16 ) staviṣyamāṇo no yo asmadvratā devānāṃ sa janāsa indraḥ ||16||

(20,34.17 ) yaḥ somakāmo haryaśvaḥ sūriryasmādrejante bhuvanāni viśvā |
(20,34.17 ) yo jaghāna śambaraṃ yaśca śuṣṇaṃ ya ekavīraḥ sa janāsa indraḥ ||17||

(20,34.18 ) yaḥ sunvate pacate dudhra ā cidvājaṃ dardarṣi sa kilāsi satyaḥ |
(20,34.18 ) vayaṃ ta indra viśvaha priyāsaḥ suvīrāso vidathamā vadema ||18||



(20,35.1 ) asmā idu pra tavase turāya prayo na harmi stomaṃ māhināya |
(20,35.1 ) ṛcīṣamāyādhrigava ohamindrāya brahmāṇi rātatamā ||1||

(20,35.2 ) asmā idu praya iva pra yaṃsi bharāmyāngūṣaṃ bādhe suvṛkti |
(20,35.2 ) indrāya hṛdā manasā manīṣā pratnāya patye dhiyo marjayanta ||2||

(20,35.3 ) asmā idu tyamupamaṃ svarṣāṃ bharāmyāṅgūṣamāsyena |
(20,35.3 ) maṃhiṣṭhamachoktibhirmatīnāṃ suvṛktibhiḥ sūriṃ vāvṛdhadhyai ||3||

(20,35.4 ) asmā idu stomaṃ saṃ hinomi rathaṃ na taṣṭeva tatsināya |
(20,35.4 ) giraśca girvāhase suvṛktīndrāya viśvaminvaṃ medhirāya ||4||

(20,35.5 ) asmā idu saptimiva śravasyendrāyārkaṃ juhvā samañje |
(20,35.5 ) vīraṃ dānaukasaṃ vandadhyai purāṃ gūrtaśravasaṃ darmāṇam ||5||

(20,35.6 ) asmā idu tvaṣṭā takṣadvajraṃ svapastamaṃ svaryaṃ raṇāya |
(20,35.6 ) vṛtrasya cidvidadyena marma tujann īśānastujatā kiyedhāḥ ||6||

(20,35.7 ) asyedu mātuḥ savaneṣu sadyo mahaḥ pituṃ papivāṃ cārvannā |
(20,35.7 ) muṣāyadviṣṇuḥ pacataṃ sahīyān vidhyadvarāhaṃ tiro adrimastā ||7||

(20,35.8 ) asmā idu gnāściddevapatnīrindrāyārkamahihatya ūvuḥ |
(20,35.8 ) pari dyāvāpṛthivī jabhra urvī nāsya te mahimānaṃ pari ṣṭaḥ ||8||

(20,35.9 ) asyedeva pra ririce mahitvaṃ divaspṛthivyāḥ paryantarikṣāt|
(20,35.9 ) svarālindro dama ā viśvagūrtaḥ svariramatro vavakṣe raṇāya ||9||

(20,35.10 ) asyedeva śavasā śuśantaṃ vi vṛścadvajreṇa vṛtramindraḥ |
(20,35.10 ) gā na vrāṇā avanīramuñcadabhi śravo dāvane sacetāḥ ||10||

(20,35.11 ) asyedu tveṣasā ranta sindhavaḥ pari yadvajreṇa sīmayachat|
(20,35.11 ) īśānakṛddāśuṣe daśasyan turvītaye gādhaṃ turvaṇiḥ kaḥ ||11||

(20,35.12 ) asmā idu pra bharā tūtujāno vṛtrāya vajramīśānaḥ kiyedhāḥ |
(20,35.12 ) gorna parva vi radā tiraśceṣyann arṇāṃsyapāṃ caradhyai ||12||

(20,35.13 ) asyedu pra brūhi pūrvyāṇi turasya karmāṇi navya ukthaiḥ |
(20,35.13 ) yudhe yadiṣṇāna āyudhānyṛghāyamāṇo niriṇāti śatrūn ||13||

(20,35.14 ) asyedu bhiyā girayaśca dṛlhā dyāvā ca bhūmā januṣastujete |
(20,35.14 ) upo venasya joguvāna oṇiṃ sadyo bhuvadvīryāya nodhāḥ ||14||

(20,35.15 ) asmā idu tyadanu dāyyeṣāmeko yadvavne bhūrerīśānaḥ |
(20,35.15 ) praitaśaṃ sūrye paspṛdhānaṃ sauvaśvye suṣvimāvadindraḥ ||15||

(20,35.16 ) evā te hāriyojanā suvṛktīndra brahmāṇi gotamāso akran |
(20,35.16 ) aiṣu viśvapeśasaṃ dhiyaṃ dhāḥ prātarmakṣū dhiyāvasurjagamyāt||16||



(20,36.1 ) ya eka iddhavyaścarṣaṇīnāmindraṃ taṃ gīrbhirabhyarca ābhiḥ |
(20,36.1 ) yaḥ patyate vṛṣabho vṛṣṇyāvāntsatyaḥ satvā purumāyaḥ sahasvān ||1||

(20,36.2 ) tamu naḥ pūrve pitaro navagvāḥ sapta viprāso abhi vājayantaḥ |
(20,36.2 ) nakṣaddābhaṃ taturiṃ parvateṣṭhāmadroghavācaṃ matibhiḥ śaviṣṭham ||2||

(20,36.3 ) tamīmahe indramasya rāyaḥ puruvīrasya nṛvataḥ purukṣoḥ |
(20,36.3 ) yo aṣkṛdhoyurajaraḥ svarvān tamā bhara harivo mādayadhyai ||3||

(20,36.4 ) tan no vi voco yadi te purā cijjaritāra ānaśuḥ sumnamindra |
(20,36.4 ) kaste bhāgaḥ kiṃ vayo dudhra khiduḥ puruhūta purūvaso'suraghnaḥ ||4||
(20,36.5 ) taṃ pṛchantī vajrahastaṃ ratheṣṭhāmindraṃ vepī vakvarī yasya nū gīḥ |
(20,36.5 ) tuvigrābhaṃ tuvikūrmiṃ rabhodāṃ gātumiṣe nakṣate tumramacha ||5||

(20,36.6 ) ayā ha tyaṃ māyayā vāvṛdhānaṃ manojuvā svatavaḥ parvatena |
(20,36.6 ) acyutā cidvīlitā svojo rujo vi dṛlhā dhṛṣatā virapśin ||6||

(20,36.7 ) taṃ vo dhiyā navyasyā śaviṣṭhaṃ pratnaṃ pratnavatparitaṃsayadhyai |
(20,36.7 ) sa no vakṣadanimānaḥ suvahnendro viśvānyati durgahāṇi ||7||

(20,36.8 ) ā janāya druhvaṇe pārthivāni divyāni dīpayo'ntarikṣā |
(20,36.8 ) tapā vṛṣan viśvataḥ śociṣā tān brahmadviṣe śocaya kṣāmapaśca ||8||

(20,36.9 ) bhuvo janasya divyasya rājā pārthivasya jagatastveṣasaṃdṛk|
(20,36.9 ) dhiṣva vajraṃ dakṣiṇa indra haste viśvā ajurya dayase vi māyāḥ ||9||

(20,36.10 ) ā saṃyatamindra ṇaḥ svastiṃ śatrutūryāya bṛhatīmamṛdhrām |
(20,36.10 ) yayā dāsānyāryāṇi vṛtrā karo vajrintsutukā nāhuṣāṇi ||10||

(20,36.11 ) sa no niyudbhiḥ puruhūta vedho viśvavārābhirā gahi prayajyo |
(20,36.11 ) na yā adevo varate na deva ābhiryāhi tūyamā madryadrik||11||



(20,37.1 ) yastigmaśṛṅgo vṛṣabho na bhīmaḥ ekaḥ kṛṣṭīścyavayati pra viśvāḥ |
(20,37.1 ) yaḥ śaśvato adāśuṣo gayasya prayantāsi suṣvitarāya vedaḥ ||1||

(20,37.2 ) tvaṃ ha tyadindra kutsamāvaḥ śuśrūṣamāṇastanvā samarye |
(20,37.2 ) dāsaṃ yacśuṣṇaṃ kuyavaṃ nyasmā arandhaya ārjuneyāya śikṣan ||2||

(20,37.3 ) tvaṃ dhṛṣṇo dhṛṣatā vītahavyaṃ prāvo viśvābhirūtibhiḥ sudāsam |
(20,37.3 ) pra paurukutsiṃ trasadasyumāvaḥ kṣetrasātā vṛtrahatyeṣu pūrum ||3||

(20,37.4 ) tvaṃ nṛbhirnṛmaṇo devavītau bhūrīṇi vṛtrā haryaśva haṃsi |
(20,37.4 ) tvaṃ ni dasyuṃ cumuriṃ dhuniṃ cāsvāpayo dabhītaye suhantu ||4||

(20,37.5 ) tava cyautnāni vajrahasta tāni nava yatpuro navatiṃ ca sadyaḥ |
(20,37.5 ) niveśane śatatamāviveṣīrahaṃ ca vṛtraṃ namucimutāhan ||5||

(20,37.6 ) sanā tā ta indra bhojanāni rātahavyāya dāśuṣe sudāse |
(20,37.6 ) vṛṣṇe te harī vṛṣaṇā yunajmi vyantu brahmāṇi puruśāka vājam ||6||

(20,37.7 ) mā te asyāṃ sahasāvan pariṣṭāvaghāya bhūma harivaḥ parādau |
(20,37.7 ) trāyasva no'vṛkebhirvarūthaistava priyāsaḥ sūriṣu syāma ||7||

(20,37.8 ) priyāsa itte maghavann abhiṣṭau naro madema śaraṇe sakhāyaḥ |
(20,37.8 ) ni turvaśaṃ ni yādvaṃ śiśīhyatithigvāya śaṃsyaṃ kariṣyan ||8||

(20,37.9 ) sadyaścin nu te maghavann abhiṣṭau naraḥ śaṃsantyukthaśāsa ukthā |
(20,37.9 ) ye te havebhirvi paṇīṃradāśann asmān vṛṇīṣva yujyāya tasmai ||9||

(20,37.10 ) ete stomā narāṃ nṛtama tubhyamasmadryañco dadato maghāni |
(20,37.10 ) teṣāmindra vṛtrahatye śivo bhūḥ sakhā ca śūro'vitā ca nṛṇām ||10||

(20,37.11 ) nū indra śūra stavamāna ūtī brahmajūtastanvā vāvṛdhasva |
(20,37.11 ) upa no vājān mimīhyupa stīn yuyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||11||



(20,38.1 ) ā yāhi suṣumā hi ta indra somaṃ pibā imam |
(20,38.1 ) edaṃ barhiḥ sado mama ||1||

(20,38.2 ) ā tvā brahmayujā harī vahatāmindra keśinā |
(20,38.2 ) upa brahmāṇi naḥ śṛṇu ||2||

(20,38.3 ) brahmāṇastvā vayaṃ yujā somapāmindra sominaḥ |
(20,38.3 ) sutāvanto havāmahe ||3||

(20,38.4 ) indramidgāthino bṛhadindramarkebhirarkiṇaḥ |
(20,38.4 ) indraṃ vāṇīranūṣata ||4||

(20,38.5 ) indra iddharyoḥ sacā saṃmiśla ā vacoyujā |
(20,38.5 ) indro vajrī hiraṇyayaḥ ||5||

(20,38.6 ) indro dīrghāya cakṣasa ā sūryaṃ rohayaddivi |
(20,38.6 ) vi gobhiradrimairayat||6||



(20,39.1 ) indraṃ vo viśvataspari havāmahe janebhyaḥ |
(20,39.1 ) asmākamastu kevalaḥ ||1||

(20,39.2 ) vyantarikṣamatiran made somasya rocanā |
(20,39.2 ) indro yadabhinadvalam ||2||

(20,39.3 ) udgā ājadaṅgirobhya āviṣkṛṇvan guhā satīḥ |
(20,39.3 ) arvāñcaṃ nunude valam ||3||

(20,39.4 ) indreṇa rocanā divo dṛlhāni dṛṃhitāni ca |
(20,39.4 ) sthirāṇi na parāṇude ||4||

(20,39.5 ) apāmūrmirmadann iva stoma indrājirāyate |
(20,39.5 ) vi te madā arājiṣuḥ ||5||



(20,40.1 ) indreṇa saṃ hi dṛkṣase saṃjagmāno abibhyuṣā |
(20,40.1 ) mandū samānavarcasā ||1||

(20,40.2 ) anavadyairabhidyubhirmakhaḥ sahasvadarcati |
(20,40.2 ) gaṇairindrasya kāmyaiḥ ||2||

(20,40.3 ) ādaha svadhāmanu punargarbhatvamerire |
(20,40.3 ) dadhānā nāma yajñiyam ||3||



(20,41.1 ) indro dadhīco asthabhirvṛtrāṇyapratiṣkutaḥ |
(20,41.1 ) jaghāna navatīrnava ||1||

(20,41.2 ) ichan aśvasya yacchiraḥ parvateṣvapaśritam |
(20,41.2 ) tadvidaccharyaṇāvati ||2||

(20,41.3 ) atrāha goramanvata nāma tvaṣṭurapīcyam |
(20,41.3 ) itthā candramaso gṛhe ||3||



(20,42.1 ) vācamaṣṭāpadīmahaṃ navasraktimṛtaspṛśam |
(20,42.1 ) indrātpari tanvaṃ mame ||1||

(20,42.2 ) anu tvā rodasī ubhe krakṣamāṇamakṛpetām |
(20,42.2 ) indra yaddasyuhābhavaḥ ||2||

(20,42.3 ) uttiṣṭhann ojasā saha pītvī śipre avepayaḥ |
(20,42.3 ) somamindra camū sutam ||3||



(20,43.1 ) bhindhi viśvā apa dviṣaḥ bādho jahī mṛdhaḥ |
(20,43.1 ) vasu spārhaṃ tadā bhara ||1||

(20,43.2 ) yadvīlāvindra yatsthire yatparśāne parābhṛtam |
(20,43.2 ) vasu spārhaṃ tadā bhara ||2||

(20,43.3 ) yasya te viśvamānuṣo bhūrerdattasya vedati |
(20,43.3 ) vasu spārhaṃ tadā bhara ||3||



(20,44.1 ) pra samrājaṃ carṣaṇīnāmindraṃ stotā navyaṃ gīrbhiḥ |
(20,44.1 ) naraṃ nṛṣāhaṃ maṃhiṣṭham ||1||

(20,44.2 ) yasminn ukthāni raṇyanti viśvāni ca śravasya |
(20,44.2 ) apāmavo na samudre ||2||

(20,44.3 ) taṃ suṣṭutyā vivāse jyeṣṭharājaṃ bhare kṛtnum |
(20,44.3 ) maho vājinaṃ sanibhyaḥ ||3||



(20,45.1 ) ayamu te samatasi kapota iva garbhadhim |
(20,45.1 ) vacastaccin na ohase ||1||

(20,45.2 ) stotraṃ rādhānāṃ pate girvāho vīra yasya te |
(20,45.2 ) vibhūtirastu sūnṛtā ||2||

(20,45.3 ) ūrdhvastiṣṭhā na ūtaye'smin vāje śatakrato |
(20,45.3 ) samanyeṣu bravāvahai ||3||



(20,46.1 ) praṇetāraṃ vasyo achā kartāraṃ jyotiḥ samatsu |
(20,46.1 ) sāsahvāṃsaṃ yudhāmitrān ||1||

(20,46.2 ) sa naḥ papriḥ pārayāti svasti nāvā puruhūtaḥ |
(20,46.2 ) indro viśvā ati dviṣaḥ ||2||

(20,46.3 ) sa tvaṃ na indra vājobhirdaśasyā ca gātuyā ca |
(20,46.3 ) achā ca naḥ sumnaṃ neṣi ||3||



(20,47.1 ) tamindraṃ vājayāmasi mahe vṛtrāya hantave |
(20,47.1 ) sa vṛṣā vṛṣabho bhuvat||1||

(20,47.2 ) indraḥ sa dāmane kṛta ojiṣṭhaḥ sa made hitaḥ |
(20,47.2 ) dyumnī ślokī sa somyaḥ ||2||

(20,47.3 ) girā vajro na saṃbhṛtaḥ sabalo anapacyutaḥ |
(20,47.3 ) vavakṣa ṛṣvo astṛtaḥ ||3||

(20,47.4 ) indramidgāthino bṛhadindramarkebhirarkiṇaḥ |
(20,47.4 ) indraṃ vāṇīranūṣata ||4||

(20,47.5 ) indra iddharyoḥ sacā saṃmiśla ā vacoyujā |
(20,47.5 ) indro vajrī hiraṇyayaḥ ||5||

(20,47.6 ) indro dīrghāya cakṣasa ā sūryaṃ rohayaddivi |
(20,47.6 ) vi gobhiradrimairayat||6||

(20,47.7 ) ā yāhi suṣumā hi ta indra somaṃ pibā imam |
(20,47.7 ) edaṃ barhiḥ sado mama ||7||

(20,47.8 ) ā tvā brahmayujā harī vahatāmindra keśinā |
(20,47.8 ) upa brahmāṇi naḥ śṛṇu ||8||

(20,47.9 ) brahmāṇastvā vayaṃ yujā somapāmindra sominaḥ |
(20,47.9 ) sutāvanto havāmahe ||9||

(20,47.10 ) yuñjanti bradhnamaruṣaṃ carantaṃ pari tasthuṣaḥ |
(20,47.10 ) rocante rocanā divi ||10||

(20,47.11 ) yuñjantyasya kāmyā harī vipakṣasā rathe |
(20,47.11 ) śoṇā dhṛṣṇū nṛvāhasā ||11||

(20,47.12 ) ketuṃ kṛṇvann aketave peśo maryā apeśase |
(20,47.12 ) samuṣadbhirajāyathāḥ ||12||
(20,47.13 ) udu tyaṃ jātavedasaṃ devaṃ vahanti ketavaḥ |
(20,47.13 ) dṛśe viśvāya sūryam ||13||

(20,47.14 ) apa tye tāyavo yathā nakṣatrā yantyaktubhiḥ |
(20,47.14 ) sūrāya viśvacakṣase ||14||

(20,47.15 ) adṛśrann asya ketavo vi raśmayo janāmanu |
(20,47.15 ) bhrājanto agnayo yathā ||15||

(20,47.16 ) taraṇirviśvadarśato jyotiṣkṛdasi sūrya |
(20,47.16 ) viśvamā bhāsi rocana ||16||

(20,47.17 ) pratyaṅdevānāṃ viśaḥ pratyaṅṅudeṣi mānuṣīḥ |
(20,47.17 ) pratyaṅviśvaṃ svardṛśe ||17||

(20,47.18 ) yenā pāvaka cakṣasā bhuraṇyantaṃ janāmanu |
(20,47.18 ) tvaṃ varuṇa paśyasi ||18||

(20,47.19 ) vi dyāmeṣi rajaspṛthvaharmimāno aktubhiḥ |
(20,47.19 ) paśyaṃ janmāni sūrya ||19||

(20,47.20 ) sapta tvā harito rathe vahanti deva sūrya |
(20,47.20 ) śociṣkeśaṃ vicakṣaṇam ||20||

(20,47.21 ) ayukta sapta śundhyuvaḥ sūro rathasya naptyaḥ |
(20,47.21 ) tābhiryāti svayuktibhiḥ ||21||



(20,48.1 ) abhi tvā varcasā giraḥ siñcantyā caraṇyuvaḥ |
(20,48.1 ) abhi vatsaṃ na dhenavaḥ ||1||

(20,48.2 ) tā arṣanti śubhriyaḥ pṛñcatīrvarcasā payaḥ |
(20,48.2 ) jātaṃ janiryathā hṛdā ||2||

(20,48.3 ) vajrāpavasādhyaḥ kīrtirmriyamāṇamāvahan |
(20,48.3 ) mahyamāyurghṛtaṃ payaḥ ||3||

(20,48.4 ) āyaṃ gauḥ pṛśnirakramīdasadan mātaraṃ puraḥ |
(20,48.4 ) pitaraṃ ca prayantsvaḥ ||4||

(20,48.5 ) antaścarati rocanā asya prāṇādapānataḥ |
(20,48.5 ) vyakhyan mahiṣaḥ svaḥ ||5||

(20,48.6 ) triṃśaddhāmā vi rājati vākpataṅgo aśiśriyat|
(20,48.6 ) prati vastorahardyubhiḥ ||6||



(20,49.1 ) yacchakrā vācamāruhann antarikṣaṃ siṣāsathaḥ |
(20,49.1 ) saṃ devā amadan vṛṣā ||1||

(20,49.2 ) śakro vācamadhṛṣṭāyoruvāco adhṛṣṇuhi |
(20,49.2 ) maṃhiṣṭha ā madardivi ||2||

(20,49.3 ) śakro vācamadhṛṣṇuhi dhāmadharman vi rājati |
(20,49.3 ) vimadan barhirāsaran ||3||

(20,49.4 ) taṃ vo dasmamṛtīṣahaṃ vasormandānamandhasaḥ |
(20,49.4 ) abhi vatsaṃ na svasareṣu dhenava indraṃ gīrbhirnavāmahe ||4||

(20,49.5 ) dyukṣaṃ sudānuṃ taviṣībhirāvṛtaṃ giriṃ na purubhojasam |
(20,49.5 ) kṣumantaṃ vājaṃ śatinaṃ sahasriṇaṃ makṣū gomantamīmahe ||5||

(20,49.6 ) tattvā yāmi suvīryaṃ tadbrahma pūrvacittaye |
(20,49.6 ) yenā yatibhyo bhṛgave dhane hite yena praskaṇvamāvitha ||6||

(20,49.7 ) yenā samudramasṛjo mahīrapastadindra vṛṣṇi te śavaḥ |
(20,49.7 ) sadyaḥ so asya mahimā na saṃnaśe yaṃ kṣoṇīranucakrade ||7||



(20,50.1 ) kan navyo atasīnāṃ turo gṛṇīta martyaḥ |
(20,50.1 ) nahī nvasya mahimānamindriyaṃ svargṛṇanta ānaśuḥ ||1||

(20,50.2 ) kadu stuvanta ṛtayanta devata ṛṣiḥ ko vipra ohate |
(20,50.2 ) kadā havaṃ maghavann indra sunvataḥ kadu stuvata ā gamaḥ ||2||



(20,51.1 ) abhi pra vaḥ surādhasamindramarca yathā vide |
(20,51.1 ) yo jaritṛbhyo maghavā purūvasuḥ sahasreṇeva śikṣati ||1||

(20,51.2 ) śatānīkeva pra jigāti dhṛṣṇuyā hanti vṛtrāṇi dāśuṣe |
(20,51.2 ) gireriva pra rasā asya pinvire datrāṇi purubhojasaḥ ||2||

(20,51.3 ) pra su śrutaṃ surādhasamarcā śakramabhiṣṭaye |
(20,51.3 ) yaḥ sunvate stuvate kāmyaṃ vasu sahasreṇeva maṃhate ||3||

(20,51.4 ) śatānīkā hetayo asya duṣṭarā indrasya samiṣo mahīḥ |
(20,51.4 ) girirna bhujmā maghatsu pinvate yadīṃ sutā amandiṣuḥ ||4||



(20,52.1 ) vayaṃ gha tvā sutāvanta āpo na vṛktabarhiṣaḥ |
(20,52.1 ) pavitrasya prasravaṇeṣu vṛtrahan pari stotāra āsate ||1||

(20,52.2 ) svaranti tvā sute naro vaso nireka ukthinaḥ |
(20,52.2 ) kadā sutaṃ tṛṣāṇa oka ā gama indra svabdīva vaṃsagaḥ ||2||

(20,52.3 ) kaṇvebhirdhṛṣṇavā dhṛsadvājaṃ darṣi sahasriṇam |
(20,52.3 ) piśaṅgarūpaṃ maghavan vicarṣaṇe makṣū gomantamīmahe ||3||



(20,53.1 ) ka īṃ veda sute sacā pibantaṃ kadvayo dadhe |
(20,53.1 ) ayaṃ yaḥ puro vibhinattyojasā mandānaḥ śipryandhasaḥ ||1||

(20,53.2 ) dānā mṛgo na vāraṇaḥ purutrā carathaṃ dadhe |
(20,53.2 ) nakiṣṭvā ni yamadā sute gamo mahāścarasyojasā ||2||

(20,53.3 ) ya ugraḥ sann aniṣṭṛta sthiro raṇāya saṃskṛtaḥ |
(20,53.3 ) yadi stoturmaghavā śṛṇavaddhavaṃ nendro yoṣatyā gamat||3||



(20,54.1 ) viśvāḥ pṛtanā abhibhūtaraṃ naraṃ sajūstatakṣurindraṃ jajanuśca rājase |
(20,54.1 ) kratvā variṣṭhaṃ vara āmurimutogramojiṣṭhaṃ tavasaṃ tarasvinam ||1||

(20,54.2 ) samīṃ rebhāso asvarann indraṃ somasya pītaye |
(20,54.2 ) svarpatiṃ yadīṃ vṛdhe dhṛtavrato hyojasā samūtibhiḥ ||2||

(20,54.3 ) nemiṃ namanti cakṣasā meṣaṃ viprā abhisvarā |
(20,54.3 ) sudītayo vo adruho'pi karṇe tarasvinaḥ samṛkvabhiḥ ||3||



(20,55.1 ) tamindraṃ johavīmi maghavānamugraṃ satrā dadhānamapratiṣkutaṃ śavāṃsi |
(20,55.1 ) maṃhiṣṭho gīrbhirā ca yajñiyo vavartadrāye no viśvā supathā kṛṇotu vajrī ||1||

(20,55.2 ) yā indra bhuja ābharaḥ svarvāmasurebhyaḥ |
(20,55.2 ) stotāramin maghavann asya vardhaya ye ca tve vṛktabarhiṣaḥ ||2||

(20,55.3 ) yamindra dadhiṣe tvamaśvaṃ gāṃ bhāgamavyayam |
(20,55.3 ) yajamāne sunvati dakṣiṇāvati tasmin taṃ dhehi mā paṇau ||3||



(20,56.1 ) indro madāya vāvṛdhe śavase vṛtrahā nṛbhiḥ |
(20,56.1 ) tamin mahatsvājiṣūtemarbhe havāmahe sa vājeṣu pra no'viṣat||1||

(20,56.2 ) asi hi vīra senyo'si bhūri parādadiḥ |
(20,56.2 ) asi dabhrasya cidvṛdho yajamānāya śikṣasi sunvate bhūri te vasu ||2||

(20,56.3 ) yadudīrata ājayo dhṛṣṇave dhīyate dhanā |
(20,56.3 ) yukṣvā madacyutā harī kaṃ hanaḥ kaṃ vasau dadho'smāmindra vasau dadhaḥ ||3||

(20,56.4 ) mademade hi no dadiryūthā gavāmṛjukratuḥ |
(20,56.4 ) saṃ gṛbhāya puru śatobhayāhastyā vasu śiśīhi rāya ā bhara ||4||

(20,56.5 ) mādayasva sute sacā śavase śūra rādhase |
(20,56.5 ) vidmā hi tvā purūvasumupa kāmāntsasṛjmahe'thā no'vitā bhava ||5||

(20,56.6 ) ete ta indra jantavo viśvaṃ puṣyanti vāryam |
(20,56.6 ) antarhi khyo janānāmaryo vedo adāśuṣāṃ teṣāṃ no veda ā bhara ||6||



(20,57.1 ) surūpakṛtnumūtaye sudughāmiva goduhe |
(20,57.1 ) juhūmasi dyavidyavi ||1||

(20,57.2 ) upa naḥ savanā gahi somasya somapāḥ piba |
(20,57.2 ) godā idrevato madaḥ ||2||

(20,57.3 ) athā te antamānāṃ vidyāma sumatīnām |
(20,57.3 ) mā no ati khya ā gahi ||3||

(20,57.4 ) śuṣmintamaṃ na ūtaye dyumninaṃ pāhi jāgṛvim |
(20,57.4 ) indra somaṃ śatakrato ||4||

(20,57.5 ) indriyāṇi śatakrato yā te janeṣu pañcasu |
(20,57.5 ) indra tāni ta ā vṛṇe ||5||

(20,57.6 ) agann indra śravo bṛhaddyumnaṃ dadhiṣva duṣṭaram |
(20,57.6 ) utte śuṣmaṃ tirāmasi ||6||

(20,57.7 ) arvāvato na ā gahyatho śakra parāvataḥ |
(20,57.7 ) u loko yaste adriva indreha tata ā gahi ||7||

(20,57.8 ) indro aṅga mahadbhayamabhī ṣadapa cucyavat|
(20,57.8 ) sa hi sthiro vicarṣaniḥ ||8||

(20,57.9 ) indraśca mṛlayāti no na naḥ paścādaghaṃ naśat|
(20,57.9 ) bhadraṃ bhavāti naḥ puraḥ ||9||

(20,57.10 ) indra āśābhyaspari sarvābhyo abhayaṃ karat|
(20,57.10 ) jetā śatrūn vicarṣaṇiḥ ||10||

(20,57.11 ) ka īṃ veda sute sacā pibantaṃ kadvayo dadhe |
(20,57.11 ) ayaṃ yaḥ puro vibhinattyojasā mandānaḥ śipryandhasaḥ ||11||

(20,57.12 ) dānā mṛgo na vāraṇaḥ purutrā carathaṃ dadhe |
(20,57.12 ) nakiṣṭvā ni yamadā sute gamo mahāṃścarasyojasā ||12||

(20,57.13 ) ya ugraḥ sann aniṣṭṛta sthiro raṇāya saṃskṛtaḥ |
(20,57.13 ) yadi stoturmaghavā śṛṇavaddhavaṃ nendro yoṣatyā gamat||13||

(20,57.14 ) vayaṃ gha tvā sutāvanta āpo na vṛktabarhiṣaḥ |
(20,57.14 ) pavitrasya prasravaṇeṣu vṛtrahan pari stotāra āsate ||14||

(20,57.15 ) svaranti tvā sute naro vaso nireka ukthinaḥ |
(20,57.15 ) kadā sutaṃ tṛṣāṇa oka ā gama indra svabdīva vaṃsagaḥ ||15||

(20,57.16 ) kaṇvebhirdhṛṣṇavā dhṛṣadvājaṃ darṣi sahasriṇam |
(20,57.16 ) piśaṅgarūpaṃ maghavan vicarṣaṇe makṣū gomantamīmahe ||16||



(20,58.1 ) śrāyanta iva sūryaṃ viśvedindrasya bhakṣata |
(20,58.1 ) vasūni jāte janamāna ojasā prati bhāgaṃ na dīdhima ||1||

(20,58.2 ) anarśarātiṃ vasudāmupa stuhi bhadrā indrasya rātayaḥ |
(20,58.2 ) so asya kāmaṃ vidhato na roṣati mano dānāya codayan ||2||

(20,58.3 ) baṇmahāmasi sūrya baḍāditya mahāmasi |
(20,58.3 ) mahaste sato mahimā panasyate'ddhā deva mahāmasi ||3||

(20,58.4 ) baṭsūrya śravasā mahāmasi satrā deva mahāmasi |
(20,58.4 ) mahnā devānāmasuryaḥ purohito vibhu jyotiradābhyam ||4||



(20,59.1 ) udu tye madhu mattamā gira stomāsa īrate |
(20,59.1 ) satrājito dhanasā akṣitotayo vājayanto rathā iva ||1||

(20,59.2 ) kaṇvā iva bhṛgavaḥ sūrya iva viśvamiddhītamānaśuḥ |
(20,59.2 ) indraṃ stomebhirmahayanta āyavaḥ priyamedhāso asvaran ||2||

(20,59.3 ) udin nvasya ricyate'ṃśo dhanaṃ na jigyusaḥ |
(20,59.3 ) ya indro harivān na dabhanti taṃ ripo dakṣaṃ dadhāti somini ||3||

(20,59.4 ) mantramakharvaṃ sudhitaṃ supeśasaṃ dadhāta yajñiyeṣvā |
(20,59.4 ) pūrvīścana prasitayastaranti taṃ ya indre karmaṇā bhuvat||4||


(20,60.1 ) evā hyasi vīrayurevā śūra uta sthiraḥ |
(20,60.1 ) evā te rādhyaṃ manaḥ ||1||

(20,60.2 ) evā rātistuvīmagha viśvebhirdhāyi dhātṛbhiḥ |
(20,60.2 ) aghā cidindra me sacā ||2||

(20,60.3 ) mo ṣu brahmeva tandrayurbhuvo vājānāṃ pate |
(20,60.3 ) matsvā sutasya gomataḥ ||3||

(20,60.4 ) evā hyasya sūnṛtā virapśī gomatī mahī |
(20,60.4 ) pakvā śākhā na dāśuṣe ||4||

(20,60.5 ) evā hi te vibhūtaya ūtaya indra māvate |
(20,60.5 ) sadyaścitsanti dāśuṣe ||5||

(20,60.6 ) evā hyasya kāmyā stoma ukthaṃ ca śaṃsyā |
(20,60.6 ) indrāya somapītaye ||6||



(20,61.1 ) taṃ te madaṃ gṛṇīmasi vṛṣaṇaṃ pṛtsu sāsahim |
(20,61.1 ) u lokakṛtnumadrivo hariśriyam ||1||

(20,61.2 ) yena jyotīmṣyāyave manave ca viveditha |
(20,61.2 ) mandāno asya barhiṣo vi rājasi ||2||

(20,61.3 ) tadadyā citta ukthino'nu ṣṭuvanti pūrvathā |
(20,61.3 ) vṛṣapatnīrapo jayā divedive ||3||

(20,61.4 ) taṃ vabhi pra gāyata puruhūtaṃ puruṣṭutam |
(20,61.4 ) indraṃ gīrbhistaviṣamā vivāsata ||4||
(20,61.5 ) yasya dvibarhaso bṛhatsaho dādhāra rodasī |
(20,61.5 ) girīṃrajrāmapaḥ svarvṛṣatvanā ||5||

(20,61.6 ) sa rājasi puruṣṭutameko vṛtrāṇi jighnase |
(20,61.6 ) indra jaitrā śravasya ca yantave ||6||



(20,62.1 ) vayamu tvāmapūrvya sthūraṃ na kaccidbharanto'vasyavaḥ |
(20,62.1 ) vāje citraṃ havāmahe ||1||

(20,62.2 ) upa tvā karmann ūtaye sa no yuvograścakrāma yo dhṛṣat|
(20,62.2 ) tvāmiddhyavitāraṃ vavṛmahe sakhāya indra sānasim ||2||

(20,62.3 ) yo na idamidaṃ purā pra vasya ānināya tamu va stuṣe |
(20,62.3 ) sakhāya indramūtaye ||3||

(20,62.4 ) haryaśvaṃ satpatiṃ carṣaṇīsahaṃ sa hi ṣmā yo amandata |
(20,62.4 ) ā tu naḥ sa vayati gavyamaśvyaṃ stotṛbhyo maghavā śatam ||4||
(20,62.5 ) indrāya sāma gāyata viprāya bṛhate bṛhat|
(20,62.5 ) dharmakṛte vipaścite panasyave ||5||

(20,62.6 ) tvamindrābhibhūrasi tvaṃ sūryamarocayaḥ |
(20,62.6 ) viśvakarmā viśvadevo mahāmasi ||6||

(20,62.7 ) vibhrājaṃ jyotiṣā svaragacho rocanaṃ divaḥ |
(20,62.7 ) devāsta indra sakhyāya yemire ||7||

(20,62.8 ) taṃ vabhi pra gāyata puruhūtaṃ puruṣṭutam |
(20,62.8 ) indraṃ gīrbhistaviṣamā vivāsata ||8||

(20,62.9 ) yasya dvibarhaso bṛhatsaho dādhāra rodasī |
(20,62.9 ) girīṃrajrāmapaḥ svarvṛṣatvanā ||9||
(20,62.10 ) sa rājasi puruṣṭutameko vṛtrāṇi jighnase |
(20,62.10 ) indra jaitra śravasya ca yantave ||10||


(20,63.1 ) imā nu kaṃ bhuvanā sīṣadhāmendraśca viśve ca devāḥ |
(20,63.1 ) yajñaṃ ca nastanvaṃ ca prajāṃ cādityairindraḥ saha cīkḷpāti ||1||

(20,63.2 ) ādityairindraḥ sagaṇo marudbhirasmākaṃ bhūtvavitā tanūnām |
(20,63.2 ) hatvāya devā asurān yadāyan devā devatvamabhirakṣamāṇāḥ ||2||

(20,63.3 ) pratyañcamarkamanayaṃ chacībhirāditsvadhāmiṣirāṃ paryapaśyan |
(20,63.3 ) ayā vājaṃ devahitaṃ sanema madema śatahimāḥ suvīrāḥ ||3||

(20,63.4 ) ya eka idvidayate vasu martāya dāśuṣe |
(20,63.4 ) īśāno apratiṣkuta indro aṅga ||4||

(20,63.5 ) kadā martamarādhasaṃ padā kṣumpamiva sphurat|
(20,63.5 ) kadā naḥ śuśravadgira indro aṅga ||5||

(20,63.6 ) yaściddhi tvā bahubhya ā sutāvāmāvivāsati |
(20,63.6 ) ugraṃ tatpatyate śava indro aṅga ||6||

(20,63.7 ) ya indra somapātamo madaḥ śaviṣṭha cetati |
(20,63.7 ) yenā haṃsi nyattriṇaṃ tamīmahe ||7||

(20,63.8 ) yenā daśagvamadhriguṃ vepayantaṃ svarṇaram |
(20,63.8 ) yenā samudramāvithā tamīmahe ||8||

(20,63.9 ) yena sindhuṃ mahīrapo rathāmiva pracodayaḥ |
(20,63.9 ) panthāmṛtasya yātave tamīmahe ||9||



(20,64.1 ) endra no gadhi priyaḥ satrājidagohyaḥ |
(20,64.1 ) girirna viśvataspṛthuḥ patirdivaḥ ||1||

(20,64.2 ) abhi hi satya somapā ubhe babhūtha rodasī |
(20,64.2 ) indrāsi sunvato vṛdhaḥ patirdivaḥ ||2||

(20,64.3 ) tvaṃ hi śaśvatīnāmindra dartā purāmasi |
(20,64.3 ) hantā dasyormanorvṛdhaḥ patirdivaḥ ||3||

(20,64.4 ) edu madhvo madintaraṃ siñca vādhvaryo andhasaḥ |
(20,64.4 ) evā hi vīra stavate sadāvṛdhaḥ ||4||

(20,64.5 ) indra sthātarharīṇāṃ nakiṣte pūrvyastutim |
(20,64.5 ) udānaṃśa śavasā na bhandanā ||5||

(20,64.6 ) taṃ vo vājānāṃ patimahūmahi śravasyavaḥ |
(20,64.6 ) aprāyubhiryajñebhirvāvṛdhenyam ||6||



(20,65.1 ) eto nvindraṃ stavāma sakhāya stomyaṃ naram |
(20,65.1 ) kuṣṭīryo viśvā abhyastyeka it||1||

(20,65.2 ) agorudhāya gaviṣe dyukṣāya dasmyaṃ vacaḥ |
(20,65.2 ) ghṛtātsvādīyo madhunaśca vocata ||2||

(20,65.3 ) yasyāmitāni vīryā na rādhaḥ paryetave |
(20,65.3 ) jyotirna viśvamabhyasti dakṣiṇā ||3||



(20,66.1 ) stuhīndraṃ vyaśvavadanūrmiṃ vājinaṃ yamam |
(20,66.1 ) aryo gayaṃ maṃhamānaṃ vi dāśuṣe ||1||

(20,66.2 ) evā nūnamupa stuhi vaiyaśva daśamaṃ navam |
(20,66.2 ) suvidvāṃsaṃ carkṛtyaṃ caraṇīnām ||2||

(20,66.3 ) vetthā hi nirṛtīnāṃ vajrahasta parivṛjam |
(20,66.3 ) aharahaḥ śundhyuḥ paripadāmiva ||3||



(20,67.1 ) vanoti hi sunvan kṣayaṃ parīṇasaḥ sunvāno hi ṣmā yajatyava dviṣo devānāmava dviṣaḥ |
(20,67.1 ) sunvāna itsiṣāsati sahasrā vājyavṛtaḥ |
(20,67.1 ) sunvānāyendro dadātyābhuvaṃ rayiṃ dadātyābhuvam ||1||

(20,67.2 ) mo ṣu vo asmadabhi tāni pauṃsyā sanā bhūvan dyumnāni mota jāriṣurasmatpurota jāriṣuḥ |
(20,67.2 ) yadvaścitraṃ yugeyuge navyaṃ ghoṣādamartyam |
(20,67.2 ) asmāsu tan maruto yacca duṣṭaraṃ didhṛtā yacca duṣṭaram ||2||

(20,67.3 ) agniṃ hotāraṃ manye dāsvantaṃ vasuṃ sūnuṃ sahaso jātavedasaṃ vipraṃ na jātavedasam |
(20,67.3 ) ya ūrdhvayā svadhvaro devo devācyā kṛpā |
(20,67.3 ) ghṛtasya vibhrāṣṭimanu vaṣṭi śociṣājuhvānasya sarpiṣaḥ ||3||

(20,67.4 ) yajñaiḥ saṃmiślāḥ pṛṣatībhirṛṣṭibhiryāmaṃ chubhrāso añjiṣu priyā uta |
(20,67.4 ) āsadyā barhirbharatasya sūnavaḥ potrādā somaṃ pibatā divo naraḥ ||4||

(20,67.5 ) ā vakṣi devāmiha vipra yakṣi cośan hotarni ṣadā yoniṣu triṣu |
(20,67.5 ) prati vīhi prasthitaṃ somyaṃ madhu pibāgnīdhrāttava bhāgasya tṛsṇuhi ||5||

(20,67.6 ) eṣa sya te tanvo nṛmṇavardhanaḥ saha ojaḥ pradivi bāhvorhitaḥ |
(20,67.6 ) tubhyaṃ suto maghavan tubhyamābhṛtastvamasya brāhmaṇādā tṛpatpiba ||6||

(20,67.7 ) yamu pūrvamahuve tamidaṃ huve sedu havyo dadiryo nāma patyate |
(20,67.7 ) adhvaryubhiḥ prasthitaṃ somyaṃ madhu potrātsomaṃ draviṇodaḥ piba ṛtubhiḥ ||7||



(20,68.1 ) surūpakṛtnumūtaye sudughāmiva goduhe |
(20,68.1 ) juhūmasi dyavidyavi ||1||

(20,68.2 ) upa naḥ savanā gahi somasya somapāḥ piba |
(20,68.2 ) godā idrevato madaḥ ||2||

(20,68.3 ) athā te antamānāṃ vidyāma sumatīnām |
(20,68.3 ) mā no ati khya ā gahi ||3||

(20,68.4 ) parehi vigramastṛtamindraṃ pṛchā vipaścitam |
(20,68.4 ) yaste sakhibhya ā varam ||4||

(20,68.5 ) uta bruvantu no nido niranyataścidārata |
(20,68.5 ) dadhānā indra idduvaḥ ||5||

(20,68.6 ) uta naḥ subhagāmarirvoceyurdasma kṛṣṭayaḥ |
(20,68.6 ) syāmedindrasya śarmaṇi ||6||

(20,68.7 ) emāśumāśave bhara yajñaśriyaṃ nṛmādanam |
(20,68.7 ) patayan mandayatsakham ||7||

(20,68.8 ) asya pītvā śatakrato ghano vṛtrāṇāmabhavaḥ |
(20,68.8 ) prāvo vājeṣu vājinam ||8||

(20,68.9 ) taṃ tvā vājeṣu vājinaṃ vājayāmaḥ śatakrato |
(20,68.9 ) dhanānāmindra sātaye ||9||

(20,68.10 ) yo rāyo'vanirmahāntsupāraḥ sunvataḥ sakhā |
(20,68.10 ) tasmā indrāya gāyata ||10||

(20,68.11 ) ā tvetā ni ṣīdatendramabhi pra gāyata |
(20,68.11 ) sakhāya stomavāhasaḥ ||11||

(20,68.12 ) purūtamaṃ purūṇāmīśānaṃ vāryāṇām |
(20,68.12 ) indraṃ some sacā sute ||12||


(20,69.1 ) sa ghā no yoga ā bhuvatsa rāye sa puraṃdhyām |
(20,69.1 ) gamadvājebhirā sa naḥ ||1||

(20,69.2 ) yasya saṃsthe na vṛṇvate harī samatsu śatravaḥ |
(20,69.2 ) tasmā indrāya gāyata ||2||

(20,69.3 ) sutapāvne sutā ime śucayo yanti vītaye |
(20,69.3 ) somāso dadhyāśiraḥ ||3||

(20,69.4 ) tvaṃ sutasya pītaye sadyo vṛddho ajāyathāḥ |
(20,69.4 ) indra jyaiṣṭhyāya sukrato ||4||

(20,69.5 ) ā tvā viśantvāśavaḥ somāsa indra girvaṇaḥ |
(20,69.5 ) śaṃ te santu pracetase ||5||

(20,69.6 ) tvāṃ stomā avīvṛdhan tvāmukthā śatakrato |
(20,69.6 ) tvāṃ vardhantu no giraḥ ||6||

(20,69.7 ) akṣitotiḥ sanedimaṃ vājamindraḥ sahasriṇam |
(20,69.7 ) yasmin viśvāni pauṃsyā ||7||

(20,69.8 ) mā no martā abhi druhan tanūnāmindra girvaṇaḥ |
(20,69.8 ) īśāno yavayā vadham ||8||

(20,69.9 ) yuñjanti bradhnamaruṣaṃ carantaṃ pari tasthuṣaḥ |
(20,69.9 ) rocante rocanā divi ||9||

(20,69.10 ) yuñjantyasya kāmyā harī vipakṣasā rathe |
(20,69.10 ) śoṇā dhṛṣṇū nṛvāhasā ||10||

(20,69.11 ) ketuṃ kṛṇvann aketave peśo maryā apeśase |
(20,69.11 ) samuṣadbhirajāyathāḥ ||11||

(20,69.12 ) ādaha svadhāmanu punargarbhatvamerire |
(20,69.12 ) dadhānā nāma yajñiyam ||12||



(20,70.1 ) vīlu cidārujatnubhirguhā cidindra vahnibhiḥ |
(20,70.1 ) avinda usriyā anu ||1||

(20,70.2 ) devayanto yathā matimachā vidadvasuṃ giraḥ |
(20,70.2 ) mahāmanūṣata śrutam ||2||

(20,70.3 ) indreṇa saṃ hi dṛkṣase saṃjagmāno abibhyuṣā |
(20,70.3 ) mandū samānavarcasā ||3||

(20,70.4 ) anavadyairabhidyubhirmakhaḥ sahasvadarcati |
(20,70.4 ) gaṇairindrasya kāmyaiḥ ||4||

(20,70.5 ) ataḥ parijmann ā gahi divo vā rocanādadhi |
(20,70.5 ) samasminn ṛñjate giraḥ ||5||

(20,70.6 ) ito vā sātimīmahe divo vā pārthivādadhi |
(20,70.6 ) indraṃ maho vā rajasaḥ ||6||

(20,70.7 ) indramidgathino bṛhadindramarkebhirarkiṇaḥ |
(20,70.7 ) indraṃ vāṇīranūṣata ||7||

(20,70.8 ) indra iddharyoḥ sacā saṃmiśla ā vacoyujā |
(20,70.8 ) indro vajrī hiraṇyayaḥ ||8||

(20,70.9 ) indro dīrghāya cakṣasa ā sūryaṃ rohayaddivi |
(20,70.9 ) vi gobhirindramairayat||9||

(20,70.10 ) indra vājeṣu no'va sahasrapradhaneṣu ca |
(20,70.10 ) ugra ugrābhirūtibhiḥ ||10||

(20,70.11 ) indraṃ vayaṃ mahādhana indramarbhe havāmahe |
(20,70.11 ) yujaṃ vṛtreṣu vajriṇam ||11||

(20,70.12 ) sa no vṛṣann amuṃ caruṃ satrādāvann apā vṛdhi |
(20,70.12 ) asmabhyamapratiṣkutaḥ ||12||

(20,70.13 ) tuñjetuñje ya uttare stomā indrasya vajriṇaḥ |
(20,70.13 ) na vindhe asya suṣṭutim ||13||

(20,70.14 ) vṛṣā yūtheva vaṃsagaḥ kṛṣṭīriyartyojasā |
(20,70.14 ) īśāno apratiṣkutaḥ ||14||

(20,70.15 ) ya ekaścarṣaṇīnāṃ vasūnāmirajyati |
(20,70.15 ) indraḥ pañca kṣitīnām ||15||

(20,70.16 ) indraṃ vo viśvataspari havāmahe janebhyaḥ |
(20,70.16 ) asmākamastu kevalaḥ ||16||

(20,70.17 ) endra sānasiṃ rayiṃ sajitvānaṃ sadāsaham |
(20,70.17 ) varṣiṣṭhamūtaye bhara ||17||

(20,70.18 ) ni yena muṣṭihatyayā ni vṛtrā ruṇadhāmahai |
(20,70.18 ) tvotāso nyarvatā ||18||

(20,70.19 ) indra tvotāso ā vayaṃ vajraṃ ghanā dadīmahi |
(20,70.19 ) jayema saṃ yudhi spṛdhaḥ ||19||

(20,70.20 ) vayaṃ śūrebhirastṛbhirindra tvayā yujā vayam |
(20,70.20 ) sāsahyāma pṛtanyataḥ ||20||



(20,71.1 ) mahāmindraḥ paraśca nu mahitvamastu vajriṇe |
(20,71.1 ) dyaurna prathinā śavaḥ ||1||

(20,71.2 ) samohe vā ya āśata narastokasya sanitau |
(20,71.2 ) viprāso vā dhiyāyavaḥ ||2||

(20,71.3 ) yaḥ kukṣiḥ somapātamaḥ samudra iva pinvate |
(20,71.3 ) urvīrāpo na kākudaḥ ||3||

(20,71.4 ) evā hyasya sūnṛtā virapśī gomatī mahī |
(20,71.4 ) pakvā śākhā na dāśuṣe ||4||

(20,71.5 ) evā hi te vibhūtaya ūtaya indra māvate |
(20,71.5 ) sadyaścitsanti dāśuṣe ||5||

(20,71.6 ) evā hyasya kāmyā stoma ukthaṃ ca śaṃsyā |
(20,71.6 ) indrāya somapītaye ||6||

(20,71.7 ) indrehi matsyandhaso viśvebhiḥ somaparvabhiḥ |
(20,71.7 ) mahāmabhiṣṭirojasā ||7||

(20,71.8 ) emenaṃ sṛjatā sute mandimindrāya mandine |
(20,71.8 ) cakriṃ viśvāni cakraye ||8||

(20,71.9 ) matsvā suśipra mandibhi stomebhirviśvacarṣaṇe |
(20,71.9 ) sacaiṣu savaneṣvā ||9||

(20,71.10 ) asṛgramindra te giraḥ prati tvāmudahāsata |
(20,71.10 ) ajoṣā vṛṣabhaṃ patim ||10||

(20,71.11 ) saṃ codaya citramarvāgrādha indra vareṇyam |
(20,71.11 ) asaditte vibhu prabhu ||11||

(20,71.12 ) asmāntsu tatra codayendra rāye rabhasvataḥ |
(20,71.12 ) tuvidyumna yaśasvataḥ ||12||

(20,71.13 ) saṃ gomadindra vājavadasme pṛthu śravo bṛhat|
(20,71.13 ) viśvāyurdhehyakṣitam ||13||

(20,71.14 ) asme dhehi śravo bṛhaddyumnaṃ sahasrasātamam |
(20,71.14 ) indra tā rathinīriṣaḥ ||14||

(20,71.15 ) vasorindraṃ vasupatiṃ gīrbhirgṛṇanta ṛgmiyam |
(20,71.15 ) homa gantāramūtaye ||15||

(20,71.16 ) sutesute nyokase bṛhadbṛhata edariḥ |
(20,71.16 ) indrāya śūṣamarcati ||16||


(20,72.1 ) viśveṣu hi tvā savaneṣu tuñjate samānamekaṃ vṛṣamaṇyavaḥ pṛthaksvaḥ saniṣyavaḥ pṛthak|
(20,72.1 ) taṃ tvā nāvaṃ na parṣaṇiṃ śūṣasya dhuri dhīmahi |
(20,72.1 ) indraṃ na yajñaiścatayanta āyava stomebhirindramāyavaḥ ||1||

(20,72.2 ) vi tvā tatasre mithunā avasyavo vrajasya sātā gavyasya niḥsṛjaḥ sakṣanta indra niḥsṛjaḥ |
(20,72.2 ) yadgavyantā dvā janā svaryantā samūhasi |
(20,72.2 ) āviṣkarikradvṛṣaṇaṃ sacābhuvaṃ vajramindra sacābhuvam ||2||

(20,72.3 ) uto no asyā uṣaso juṣeta hyarkasya bodhi haviṣo havīmabhiḥ svarṣātā havīmabhiḥ |
(20,72.3 ) yadindra hantave mṛgho vṛṣā vajriṃ ciketasi |
(20,72.3 ) ā me asya vedhaso navīyaso manma śrudhi navīyasaḥ ||3||



(20,73.1 ) tubhyedimā savanā śūra viśvā tubhyaṃ brahmāṇi vardhanā kṛṇomi |
(20,73.1 ) tvaṃ nṛbhirhavyo viśvadhāsi ||1||

(20,73.2 ) nū cin nu te manyamānasya dasmodaśnuvanti mahimānamugra |
(20,73.2 ) na vīryamindra te na rādhaḥ ||2||

(20,73.3 ) pra vo mahe mahivṛdhe bharadhvaṃ pracetase pra sumatiṃ kṛṇudhvam |
(20,73.3 ) viśaḥ pūrvīḥ pra carā carṣaṇiprāḥ ||3||

(20,73.4 ) yadā vajraṃ hiraṇyamidathā rathaṃ harī yamasya vahato vi sūribhiḥ |
(20,73.4 ) ā tiṣṭhati maghavā sanaśruta indro vājasya dīrghaśravasaspatiḥ ||4||

(20,73.5 ) so cin nu vṛṣṭiryūthyā svā sacāmindraḥ śmaśrūṇi haritābhi pruṣṇute |
(20,73.5 ) ava veti sukṣayaṃ sute madhūdiddhūṇoti vāto yathā vanam ||5||

(20,73.6 ) yo vācā vivāco mṛdhravācaḥ purū sahasrāśivā jaghāna |
(20,73.6 ) tattadidasya pauṃsyaṃ gṛṇīmasi piteva yastaviṣīṃ vāvṛdhe śavaḥ ||6||



(20,74.1 ) yacciddhi satya somapā anāśastā iva smasi |
(20,74.1 ) ā tū na indra śaṃsaya goṣvaśveṣu śubhriṣu sahasreṣu tuvīmagha ||1||

(20,74.2 ) śiprin vājānāṃ pate śacīvastava daṃsanā |
(20,74.2 ) ā tū na indra śaṃsaya goṣvaśveṣu śubhriṣu sahasreṣu tuvīmagha ||2||

(20,74.3 ) ni ṣvāpayā mithūdṛśā sastāmabudhyamāne |
(20,74.3 ) ā tū na indra śaṃsaya goṣvaśveṣu śubhriṣu sahasreṣu tuvīmagha ||3||

(20,74.4 ) sasantu tyā arātayo bodhantu śūra rātayaḥ |
(20,74.4 ) ā tū na indra śaṃsaya goṣvaśveṣu śubhriṣu sahasreṣu tuvīmagha ||4||

(20,74.5 ) samindra gardabhaṃ mṛṇa nuvantaṃ papayāmuyā |
(20,74.5 ) ā tū na indra śaṃsaya goṣvaśveṣu śubhriṣu sahasreṣu tuvīmagha ||5||

(20,74.6 ) patāti kuṇḍṛṇācyā dūraṃ vāto vanādadhi |
(20,74.6 ) ā tū na indra śaṃsaya goṣvaśveṣu śubhriṣu sahasreṣu tuvīmagha ||6||

(20,74.7 ) sarvaṃ parikrośaṃ jahi jambhayā kṛkadāśvam |
(20,74.7 ) ā tū na indra śaṃsaya goṣvaśveṣu śubhriṣu sahasreṣu tuvīmagha ||7||



(20,75.1 ) vi tvā tatasre mithunā avasyavo vrajasya sātā gavyasya niḥsṛjaḥ sakṣanta indra niḥsṛjaḥ |
(20,75.1 ) yadgavyanta dvā janā svaryantā samūhasi |
(20,75.1 ) āviṣkarikradvṛṣaṇaṃ sacābhuvaṃ vajramindra sacābhuvam ||1||

(20,75.2 ) viduṣṭe asya vīryasya pūravaḥ puro yadindra śāradīravātiraḥ sāsahāno avātiraḥ |
(20,75.2 ) śāsastamindra martyamayajuṃ śavasaspate |
(20,75.2 ) mahīmamuṣṇāḥ pṛthivīmimā apo mandasāna imā apaḥ ||2||

(20,75.3 ) āditte asya vīryasya carkiran madeṣu vṛṣann uśijo yadāvitha sakhīyato yadāvitha |
(20,75.3 ) cakartha kāramebhyaḥ pṛtanāsu pravantave |
(20,75.3 ) te anyāmanyāṃ nadyaṃ saniṣṇata śravasyantaḥ saniṣṇata ||3||



(20,76.1 ) vane na vā yo nyadhāyi cākraṃ chucirvāṃ stomo bhuraṇāvajīgaḥ |
(20,76.1 ) yasyedindraḥ purudineṣu hotā nṛṇāṃ narvo nṛtamaḥ kṣapāvān ||1||

(20,76.2 ) pra te asyā uṣasaḥ prāparasyā nṛtau syāma nṛtamasya nṛṇām |
(20,76.2 ) anu triśokaḥ śatamāvahan nṝn kutsena ratho yo asatsasavān ||2||

(20,76.3 ) kaste mada indra rantyo bhūdduro giro abhyugro vi dhāva |
(20,76.3 ) kadvāho arvāgupa mā manīṣā ā tvā śakyāmupamaṃ rādho annaiḥ ||3||

(20,76.4 ) kadu dyumnamindra tvāvato nṝn kayā dhiyā karase kan na āgan |
(20,76.4 ) mitro na satya urugāya bhṛtyā anne samasya yadasan manīṣāḥ ||4||

(20,76.5 ) preraya sūro arthaṃ na pāraṃ ye asya kāmaṃ janidhā iva gman |
(20,76.5 ) giraśca ye te tuvijāta pūrvīrnara indra pratiśikṣantyannaiḥ ||5||

(20,76.6 ) mātre nu te sumite indra pūrvī dyaurmajmanā pṛthivī kāvyena |
(20,76.6 ) varāya te ghṛtavantaḥ sutāsaḥ svādnan bhavantu pītaye madhūni ||6||

(20,76.7 ) ā madhvo asmā asicann amatramindrāya pūrṇaṃ sa hi satyarādhāḥ |
(20,76.7 ) sa vāvṛdhe varimann ā pṛthivyā abhi kratvā naryaḥ pauṃsyaiśca ||7||

(20,76.8 ) vyānaḻ indraḥ pṛtanāḥ svojā āsmai yatante sakhyāya pūrvīḥ |
(20,76.8 ) ā smā rathaṃ na pṛtanāsu tiṣṭha yaṃ bhadrayā sumatyā codayāse ||8||



(20,77.1 ) ā satyo yātu maghavāmṛjīṣī dravantvasya haraya upa naḥ |
(20,77.1 ) tasmā idandhaḥ suṣumā sudakṣamihābhipitvaṃ karate gṛṇānaḥ ||1||

(20,77.2 ) ava sya śūrādhvano nānte'smin no adya savane mandadhyai |
(20,77.2 ) śaṃsātyukthamuśaneva vedhāścikituṣe asuryāya manma ||2||

(20,77.3 ) kavirna niṇyaṃ vidathāni sādhan vṛṣā yatsekaṃ vipipāno arcāt|
(20,77.3 ) diva itthā jījanatsapta kārūn ahnā ciccakrurvayunā gṛṇantaḥ ||3||

(20,77.4 ) svaryadvedi sudṛśīkamarkairmahi jyotī rurucuryaddha vastoḥ |
(20,77.4 ) andhā tamāṃsi dudhitā vicakṣe nṛbhyaścakāra nṛtamo abhiṣṭau ||4||

(20,77.5 ) vavakṣa indro amitamṛjiṣyubhe ā paprau rodasī mahitvā |
(20,77.5 ) ataścidasya mahimā vi recyabhi yo viśvā bhuvanā babhūva ||5||

(20,77.6 ) viśvāni śakro naryāṇi vidvān apo rireca sakhibhirnikāmaiḥ |
(20,77.6 ) aśmānaṃ cidye bibhidurvacobhirvrajaṃ gomantamuśijo vi vavruḥ ||6||

(20,77.7 ) apo vṛtraṃ vavrivāṃsaṃ parāhan prāvatte vajraṃ pṛthivī sacetāḥ |
(20,77.7 ) prārṇāṃsi samudriyāṇyainoḥ patirbhavaṃ chavasā śūra dhṛṣṇo ||7||

(20,77.8 ) apo yadadriṃ puruhūta dardarāvirbhuvatsaramā pūrvyaṃ te |
(20,77.8 ) sa no netā vājamā darṣi bhūriṃ gotrā rujann aṅgirobhirgṛṇānaḥ ||8||



(20,78.1 ) tadvo gāya sute sacā puruhūtāya satvane |
(20,78.1 ) śaṃ yadgave na śākine ||1||

(20,78.2 ) na ghā vasurni yamate dānaṃ vājasya gomataḥ |
(20,78.2 ) yatsīmupa śravadgiraḥ ||2||

(20,78.3 ) kuvitsasya pra hi vrajaṃ gomantaṃ dasyuhā gamat|
(20,78.3 ) śacībhirapa no varat||3||



(20,79.1 ) indra kratuṃ na ā bhara pitā putrebhyo yathā |
(20,79.1 ) śikṣā ṇo asmin puruhūta yāmani jīvā jyotiraśīmahi ||1||

(20,79.2 ) mā no ajñātā vṛjanā durādhyo māśivāso ava kramuḥ |
(20,79.2 ) tvayā vayaṃ pravataḥ śaśvatīrapo'ti śūra tarāmasi ||2||



(20,80.1 ) indra jyeṣṭhaṃ na ā bharamojiṣṭhaṃ papuri śravaḥ |
(20,80.1 ) yeneme citra vajrahasta rodasī obhe suśipra prāḥ ||1||

(20,80.2 ) tvāmugramavase carṣaṇīsahaṃ rājan deveṣu hūmahe |
(20,80.2 ) viśvā su no vithurā pibdanā vaso'mitrān suṣahān kṛdhi ||2||



(20,81.1 ) yaddyāva indra te śataṃ śataṃ bhūmiruta syuḥ |
(20,81.1 ) na tvā vajrintsahasraṃ sūryā anu na jātamaṣṭa rodasī ||1||

(20,81.2 ) ā paprātha mahinā kṛṣṇyā vṛṣan viśvā śaviṣṭha śavasā |
(20,81.2 ) asmāmava maghavan gomati vraje vajriṃ citrābhirūtibhiḥ ||2||



(20,82.1 ) yadindra yāvatastvametāvadahamīśīya |
(20,82.1 ) stotāramiddidhiṣeya radāvaso na pāpatvāya rāsīya ||1||

(20,82.2 ) śikṣeyamin mahayate divedive rāya ā kuhacidvide |
(20,82.2 ) nahi tvadanyan maghavan na āpyaṃ vasyo asti pitā cana ||2||



(20,83.1 ) indra tridhātu śaraṇaṃ trivarūthaṃ svastimat|
(20,83.1 ) chardiryacha maghavadbhyaśca mahyaṃ ca yāvayā didyumebhyaḥ ||1||

(20,83.2 ) ye gavyatā manasā śatrumādabhurabhipraghnanti dhṛṣṇuyā |
(20,83.2 ) agha smā no maghavann indra girvaṇastanūpā antamo bhava ||2||



(20,84.1 ) indrā yāhi citrabhāno sutā ime tvāyavaḥ |
(20,84.1 ) aṇvībhistanā pūtāsaḥ ||1||

(20,84.2 ) indrā yāhi dhiyeṣito viprajutaḥ sutāvataḥ |
(20,84.2 ) upa brahmāṇi vāghataḥ ||2||

(20,84.3 ) indrā yāhi tūtujāna upa brahmāṇi harivaḥ |
(20,84.3 ) sute dadhiṣva naścanaḥ ||3||



(20,85.1 ) mā cidanyadvi śaṃsata sakhāyo mā riṣaṇyata |
(20,85.1 ) indramitstotā vṛṣaṇaṃ sacā sute muhurukthā ca śaṃsata ||1||
(20,85.2 ) avakrakṣiṇaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ yathājuraṃ gāṃ na carṣaṇīsaham |
(20,85.2 ) vidveṣaṇaṃ saṃvananobhayaṃkaraṃ maṃhiṣṭhamubhayāvinam ||2||

(20,85.3 ) yacciddhi tvā janā ime nānā havanta ūtaye |
(20,85.3 ) asmākaṃ brahmedamindra bhūtu te'hā viśvā ca vardhanam ||3||

(20,85.4 ) vi tartūryante maghavan vipaścito'ryo vipo janānām |
(20,85.4 ) upa kramasva pururūpamā bhara vājaṃ nediṣṭhamūtaye ||4||



(20,86.1 ) brahmaṇā te brahmayujā yunajmi harī sakhāyā sadhamāda āśū |
(20,86.1 ) sthiraṃ rathaṃ sukhamindrādhitiṣṭhan prajānan vidvāmupa yāhi somam ||1||



(20,87.1 ) adhvaryavo'ruṇaṃ dugdhamaṃśuṃ juhotana vṛṣabhāya kṣitīnām |
(20,87.1 ) gaurādvedīyāmavapānamindro viśvāhedyāti sutasomamichan ||1||

(20,87.2 ) yaddadhiṣe pradivi cārvannaṃ divedive pītimidasya vakṣi |
(20,87.2 ) uta hṛdota manasā juṣāṇa uśann indra prasthitān pāhi somān ||2||

(20,87.3 ) jajñānaḥ somaṃ sahase papātha pra te mātā mahimānamuvāca |
(20,87.3 ) endra paprāthorvantarikṣaṃ yudhā devebhyo varivaścakartha ||3||

(20,87.4 ) yadyodhayā mahato manyamānān sākṣāma tān bāhubhiḥ śāśadānān |
(20,87.4 ) yadvā nṛbhirvṛta indrābhiyudhyāstaṃ tvayājiṃ sauśravasaṃ jayema ||4||

(20,87.5 ) prendrasya vocaṃ prathamā kṛtāni pra nūtanā maghavā yā cakāra |
(20,87.5 ) yadedadevīrasahiṣṭa māyā athābhavatkevalaḥ somo asya ||5||

(20,87.6 ) tavedaṃ viśvamabhitaḥ paśavyaṃ yatpaśyasi cakṣasā sūryasya |
(20,87.6 ) gavāmasi gopatireka indra bhakṣīmahi te prayatasya vasvaḥ ||6||

(20,87.7 ) bṛhaspate yuvamindraśca vasvo divyasyeśāthe uta pārthivasya |
(20,87.7 ) dhattaṃ rayiṃ stuvate kīraye cidyūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||7||



(20,88.1 ) yastastambha sahasā vi jmo antān bṛhaspatistriṣadhastho raveṇa |
(20,88.1 ) taṃ pratnāsa ṛṣayo dīdhyānāḥ puro viprā dadhire mandrajihvam ||1||

(20,88.2 ) dhunetayaḥ supraketaṃ madanto bṛhaspate abhi ye nastatasre |
(20,88.2 ) pṛṣantaṃ sṛpramadabdhamūrvaṃ bṛhaspate rakṣatādasya yonim ||2||

(20,88.3 ) bṛhaspate yā paramā parāvadata ā te ṛtaspṛśo ni ṣeduḥ |
(20,88.3 ) tubhyaṃ khātā avatā adridugdhā madhva ścotantyabhito virapśam ||3||

(20,88.4 ) bṛhaspatiḥ prathamaṃ jāyamāno maho jyotiṣaḥ parame vyoman |
(20,88.4 ) saptāsyastuvijāto raveṇa vi saptaraśmiradhamattamāṃsi ||4||

(20,88.5 ) sa suṣṭubhā sa ṛkvatā gaṇena valaṃ ruroja phaligaṃ ravena |
(20,88.5 ) bṛhaspatirusriyā havyasūdaḥ kanikradadvāvaśatīrudājat||5||

(20,88.6 ) evā pitre viśvadevāya vṛṣṇe yajñairvidhema namasā havirbhiḥ |
(20,88.6 ) bṛhaspate suprajā vīravanto vayaṃ syāma patayo rayīṇām ||6||


(20,89.1 ) asteva su prataraṃ lāyamasyan bhūṣann iva pra bharā stomamasmai |
(20,89.1 ) vācā viprāstarata vācamaryo ni rāmaya jaritaḥ soma indram ||1||

(20,89.2 ) dohena gāmupa śikṣā sakhāyaṃ pra bodhaya jaritarjāramindram |
(20,89.2 ) kośaṃ na pūrṇaṃ vasunā nyṛṣṭamā cyāvaya maghadeyāya śūram ||2||

(20,89.3 ) kimaṅga tvā maghavan bhojamāhuḥ śiśīhi mā śiśayaṃ tvā śṛṇomi |
(20,89.3 ) apnasvatī mama dhīrastu śakra vasuvidaṃ bhagamindrā bharā naḥ ||3||

(20,89.4 ) tvāṃ janā mamasatyeṣvindra saṃtasthānā vi hvayante samīke |
(20,89.4 ) atrā yujaṃ kṛṇute yo haviṣmān nāsunvatā sakhyaṃ vaṣṭi śūraḥ ||4||

(20,89.5 ) dhanaṃ na spandraṃ bahulaṃ yo asmai tīvrāntsomāmāsunoti prayasvān |
(20,89.5 ) tasmai śatrūntsutukān prātarahno ni svaṣṭrān yuvati hanti vṛtram ||5||

(20,89.6 ) yasmin vayaṃ dadhimā śaṃsamindre yaḥ śiśrāya maghavā kāmamasme |
(20,89.6 ) ārāccitsan bhayatāmasya śatrurnyasmai dyumnā janyā namantām ||6||

(20,89.7 ) ārācchatrumapa bādhasva dūramugro yaḥ śambaḥ puruhūta tena |
(20,89.7 ) asme dhehi yavamadgomadindra kṛdhī dhiyaṃ jaritre vājaratnām ||7||

(20,89.8 ) pra yamantarvṛṣasavāso ajman tīvrāḥ somā bahulāntāsa indram |
(20,89.8 ) nāha dāmānaṃ maghavā ni yaṃsan ni sunvate vahati bhūri vāmam ||8||

(20,89.9 ) uta prahāmatidīvā jayati kṛtamiva śvaghnī vi cinoti kāle |
(20,89.9 ) yo devakāmo na dhanaṃ ruṇaddhi samittaṃ rāyaḥ sṛjati svadhābhiḥ ||9||
(20,89.10 ) gobhiṣṭaremāmatiṃ durevāṃ yavena vā kṣudhaṃ puruhūta viśve |
(20,89.10 ) vayaṃ rājasu prathamā dhanānyariṣṭāso vṛjanībhirjayema ||10||

(20,89.11 ) bṛhaspatirnaḥ pari pātu paścādutottarasmādadharādaghayoḥ |
(20,89.11 ) indraḥ purastāduta madhyato naḥ sakhā sakhibhyo varīyaḥ kṛṇotu ||11||



(20,90.1 ) yo adribhitprathamajā ṛtāvā bṛhaspatirāṅgiraso haviṣmān |
(20,90.1 ) dvibarhajmā prāgharmasatpitā na ā rodasī vṛṣabho roravīti ||1||

(20,90.2 ) janāya cidya īvate u lokaṃ bṛhaspatirdevahūtau cakāra |
(20,90.2 ) ghnan vṛtrāṇi vi puro dardarīti jayaṃ chatrūṃramitrān pṛtsu sāhan ||2||

(20,90.3 ) bṛhaspatiḥ samajayadvasūni maho vrajān gomate deva eṣaḥ |
(20,90.3 ) apaḥ siṣāsantsvarapratīto bṛhaspatirhantyamitramarkaiḥ ||3||



(20,91.1 ) imāṃ dhiyaṃ saptaśīrṣṇīṃ pitā na ṛtaprajātāṃ bṛhatīmavindat|
(20,91.1 ) turīyaṃ svijjanayadviśvajanyo'yāsya ukthamindrāya śaṃsan ||1||

(20,91.2 ) ṛtaṃ śaṃsanta ṛju dīdhyānā divasputrāso asurasya vīrāḥ |
(20,91.2 ) vipraṃ padamaṅgiraso dadhānā yajñasya dhāma prathamaṃ mananta ||2||

(20,91.3 ) haṃsairiva sakhibhirvāvadadbhiraśmanmayāni nahanā vyasyan |
(20,91.3 ) bṛhaspatirabhikanikradadgā uta prāstauducca vidvāmagāyat||3||

(20,91.4 ) avo dvābhyāṃ para ekayā gā guhā tiṣṭhantīranṛtasya setau |
(20,91.4 ) bṛhaspatistamasi jyotirichan udusrā ākarvi hi tisra āvaḥ ||4||

(20,91.5 ) vibhidyā puraṃ śayathemapācīṃ nistrīṇi sākamudadherakṛntat|
(20,91.5 ) bṛhaspatiruṣasaṃ sūryaṃ gāmarkaṃ viveda stanayann iva dyauḥ ||5||

(20,91.6 ) indro valaṃ rakṣitāraṃ dughānāṃ kareṇeva vi cakartā raveṇa |
(20,91.6 ) svedāñjibhirāśiramichamāno'rodayatpaṇimā gā amuṣṇāt||6||

(20,91.7 ) sa īṃ satyebhiḥ sakhibhiḥ śucadbhirgodhāyasaṃ vi dhanasairadardaḥ |
(20,91.7 ) brahmaṇaspatirvṛṣabhirvarāhairgharmasvedebhirdraviṇaṃ vyānaṭ||7||

(20,91.8 ) te satyena manasā gopatiṃ gā iyānāsa iṣaṇayanta dhībhiḥ |
(20,91.8 ) bṛhaspatirmithoavadyapebhirudusriyā asṛjata svayugbhiḥ ||8||

(20,91.9 ) taṃ vardhayanto matibhiḥ śivābhiḥ siṃhamiva nānadataṃ sadhasthe |
(20,91.9 ) bṛhaspatiṃ vṛṣaṇaṃ śūrasātau bharebhare anu madema jiṣṇum ||9||

(20,91.10 ) yadā vājamasanadviśvarūpamā dyāmarukṣaduttarāṇi sadma |
(20,91.10 ) bṛhaspatiṃ vṛṣaṇaṃ vardhayanto nānā santo bibhrato jyotirāsā ||10||

(20,91.11 ) satyamāśiṣaṃ kṛṇutā vayodhai kīriṃ ciddhyavatha svebhirevaiḥ |
(20,91.11 ) paścā mṛdho apa bhavantu viśvāstadrodasī śṛṇutaṃ viśvaminve ||11||

(20,91.12 ) indro mahnā mahato arṇavasya vi mūrdhānamabhinadarbudasya |
(20,91.12 ) ahann ahimariṇātsapta sindhūn devairdyāvāpṛthivī prāvataṃ naḥ ||12||



(20,92.1 ) abhi pra gopatiṃ girendramarca yathā vide |
(20,92.1 ) sūtuṃ satyasya satpatim ||1||

(20,92.2 ) ā harayaḥ sasṛjrire'ruṣīradhi barhiṣi |
(20,92.2 ) yatrābhi saṃnavāmahe ||2||

(20,92.3 ) indrāya gāva āśiraṃ duduhre vajriṇe madhu |
(20,92.3 ) yatsīmupahvare vidat||3||

(20,92.4 ) udyadbradhnasya viṣṭapaṃ gṛhamindraśca ganvahi |
(20,92.4 ) madhvaḥ pītvā sacevahi triḥ sapta sakhyuḥ pade ||4||

(20,92.5 ) arcata prārcata priyamedhāso arcata |
(20,92.5 ) arcantu putrakā uta puraṃ na dhṛṣṇvarcata ||5||

(20,92.6 ) ava svarāti gargaro godhā pari saniṣvaṇat|
(20,92.6 ) piṅgā pari caniṣkadadindrāya brahmodyatam ||6||

(20,92.7 ) ā yatpatantyenyaḥ sudughā anapasphuraḥ |
(20,92.7 ) apasphuraṃ gṛbhāyata somamindrāya pātave ||7||

(20,92.8 ) apādindro apādagnirviśve devā amatsata |
(20,92.8 ) varuṇa idiha kṣayattamāpo abhyanūṣata vatsaṃ saṃśiśvarīriva ||8||

(20,92.9 ) sudevo asi varuṇa yasya te sapta sindhavaḥ |
(20,92.9 ) anukṣaranti kākudaṃ sūryaṃ suṣirāmiva ||9||

(20,92.10 ) yo vyatīṃraphāṇayatsuyuktāmupa dāśuṣe |
(20,92.10 ) takvo netā tadidvapurupamā yo amucyata ||10||

(20,92.11 ) atīdu śakra ohata indro viśvā ati dviṣaḥ |
(20,92.11 ) bhinatkanīna odanaṃ pacyamānaṃ paro girā ||11||

(20,92.12 ) arbhako na kumārako'dhi tiṣṭhann avaṃ ratham |
(20,92.12 ) sa pakṣan mahiṣaṃ mṛgaṃ pitre mātre vibhukratum ||12||

(20,92.13 ) ā tū suśipra daṃpate rathaṃ tiṣṭhā hiraṇyayam |
(20,92.13 ) adha dyukṣaṃ sacevahi sahasrapādamaruṣaṃ svastigāmanehasam ||13||

(20,92.14 ) taṃ ghemitthā namasvina upa svarājamāsate |
(20,92.14 ) arthaṃ cidasya sudhitaṃ yadetave āvartayanti dāvane ||14||

(20,92.15 ) anu pratnasyaukasaḥ priyamedhāsa eṣām |
(20,92.15 ) pūrvāmanu prayatiṃ vṛktabarhiṣo hitaprayasa āśata ||15||

(20,92.16 ) yo rājā carṣaṇīnāṃ yātā rathebhiradhriguḥ |
(20,92.16 ) viśvāsāṃ tarutā pṛtanānāṃ jyeṣṭho yo vṛtrahā gṛṇe ||16||

(20,92.17 ) indraṃ taṃ śumbha puruhanmann avase yasya dvitā vidhartari |
(20,92.17 ) hastāya vajraḥ prati dhāyi darśato maho dive na sūryaḥ ||17||

(20,92.18 ) nakiṣṭaṃ karmaṇā naśadyaścakāra sadāvṛdham |
(20,92.18 ) indraṃ na yajñairviśvagūrtamṛbhvasamadhṛṣṭaṃ dhṛṣṇvojasam ||18||

(20,92.19 ) aṣālhamugraṃ pṛtanāsu sāsahiṃ yasmin mahīrurujrayaḥ |
(20,92.19 ) saṃ dhenavo jāyamāne anonavurdyāvaḥ kṣāmo anonavuḥ ||19||

(20,92.20 ) yaddyāva indra te śataṃ śataṃ bhūmīruta syuḥ |
(20,92.20 ) na tvā vajrintsahasraṃ sūryā anu na jātamaṣṭa rodasī ||20||

(20,92.21 ) ā paprātha mahinā vṛṣṇyā vṛṣan viśvā śaviṣṭha śavasā |
(20,92.21 ) asmāmava maghavan gomati vraje vajriṃ citrābhirūtibhiḥ ||21||


(20,93.1 ) uttvā mandantu stomāḥ kṛṇuṣva rādho adrivaḥ |
(20,93.1 ) ava brahmadviṣo jahi ||1||

(20,93.2 ) padā paṇīṃrarādhaso ni bādhasva mahāmasi |
(20,93.2 ) nahi tvā kaścana prati ||2||

(20,93.3 ) tvamīśiṣe sutānāmindra tvamasutānām |
(20,93.3 ) tvaṃ rājā janānām ||3||

(20,93.4 ) īṅkhayantīrapasyuva indraṃ jātamupāsate |
(20,93.4 ) bhejānāsaḥ suvīryam ||4||

(20,93.5 ) tvamindra balādadhi sahaso jāta ojasaḥ |
(20,93.5 ) tvaṃ vṛṣan vṛṣedasi ||5||

(20,93.6 ) tvamindrāsi vṛtrahā vyantarikṣamatiraḥ |
(20,93.6 ) uddyāmastabhnā ojasā ||6||

(20,93.7 ) tvamindra sajoṣasamarkaṃ bibharṣi bāhvoḥ |
(20,93.7 ) vajraṃ śiśāna ojasā ||7||

(20,93.8 ) tvamindrābhibhurasi viśvā jātānyojasā |
(20,93.8 ) sa viśvā bhuva ābhavaḥ ||8||



(20,94.1 ) ā yātvindraḥ svapatirmadāya yo dharmaṇā tūtujānastuviṣmān |
(20,94.1 ) pratvakṣāṇo ati viśvā sahāṃsyapāreṇa mahatā vṛṣṇyena ||1||

(20,94.2 ) suṣṭhāmā rathaḥ suyamā harī te mimyakṣa vajro nṛpate gabhastau |
(20,94.2 ) śībhaṃ rājan supathā yāhyarvāṅvardhāma te papuso vṛṣṇyāni ||2||

(20,94.3 ) endravāho nṛpatiṃ vajrabāhumugramugrāsastaviṣāsa enam |
(20,94.3 ) pratvakṣasaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ satyaśuṣmamemasmatrā sadhamādo vahantu ||3||

(20,94.4 ) evā patiṃ droṇasācaṃ sacetasamūrja skambhaṃ dharuṇa ā vṛṣāyase |
(20,94.4 ) ojaḥ kṛṣva saṃ gṛbhāya tve apyaso yathā kenipānāmino vṛdhe ||4||

(20,94.5 ) gamann asme vasūnyā hi śaṃsiṣaṃ svāśiṣaṃ bharamā yāhi sominaḥ |
(20,94.5 ) tvamīśiṣe sāsminn ā satsi barhiṣyanādhṛṣyā tava pātrāṇi dharmaṇā ||5||

(20,94.6 ) pṛthakprāyan prathamā devahūtayo'kṛṇvata śravasyāni duṣṭarā |
(20,94.6 ) na ye śekuryajñiyāṃ nāvamāruhamirmaiva te nyaviśanta kepayaḥ ||6||

(20,94.7 ) evaivāpāgapare santu dūdhyo'śvā yeṣāṃ duryuga āyuyujre |
(20,94.7 ) itthā ye prāgupare santi dāvane purūṇi yatra vayunāni bhojanā ||7||
(20,94.8 ) girīṃrajrān rejamānāmadhārayaddyauḥ krandadantarikṣāṇi kopayat|
(20,94.8 ) samīcīne dhiṣaṇe vi ṣkabhāyati vṛṣṇaḥ pītvā mada ukthāni śaṃsati ||8||

(20,94.9 ) imaṃ bibharmi sukṛtaṃ te aṅkuśaṃ yenārujāsi maghavaṃ chaphārujaḥ |
(20,94.9 ) asmintsu te savane astvoktyaṃ suta iṣṭau maghavan bodhyābhagaḥ ||9||

(20,94.10 ) gobhiṣṭaremāmatiṃ durevāṃ yavena kṣudhaṃ puruhūta viśvām |
(20,94.10 ) vayaṃ rājabhiḥ prathamā dhanānyasmākena vṛjanenā jayema ||10||

(20,94.11 ) bṛhaspatirnaḥ pari pātu paścādutottarasmādadharādaghayoḥ |
(20,94.11 ) indraḥ purastāduta madhyato naḥ sakhā sakhibhyo varivaḥ kṛṇotu ||11||


(20,95.1 ) trikadrukeṣu mahiṣo yavāśiraṃ tuviśuṣmastṛpatsomamapibadviṣṇunā sutaṃ yathāvaśat|
(20,95.1 ) sa īṃ mamāda mahi karma kartave mahāmuruṃ sainaṃ saścaddevo devaṃ satyamindraṃ satya induḥ ||1||

(20,95.2 ) pro ṣvasmai purorathamindrāya śūṣamarcata |
(20,95.2 ) abhīke cidu lokakṛtsaṃge samatsu vṛtrahāsmākaṃ bodhi coditā nabhantāmanyakeṣāṃ jyākā adhi dhanvasu ||2||

(20,95.3 ) tvaṃ sindhūṃravāsṛjo'dharāco ahann ahim |
(20,95.3 ) aśatrurindra jajñiṣe viśvaṃ puṣyasi vāryaṃ taṃ tvā pari ṣvajāmahe nabhantāmanyakeṣāṃ jyākā adhi dhanvasu ||3||

(20,95.4 ) vi ṣu viśvā arātayo'ryo naśanta no dhiyaḥ |
(20,95.4 ) astāsi śatrave vadhaṃ yo na indra jighāṃsati yā te rātirdadirvasu |
(20,95.4 ) nabhantāmanyakeṣāṃ jyakā adhi dhanvasu ||4||



(20,96.1 ) tīvrasyābhivayaso asya pāhi sarvarathā vi harī iha muñca |
(20,96.1 ) indra mā tvā yajamānāso anye ni rīraman tubhyamime sutāsaḥ ||1||

(20,96.2 ) tubhyaṃ sutāstubhyamu sotvāsastvāṃ giraḥ śvātryā ā hvayanti |
(20,96.2 ) indredamadya savanaṃ juṣāṇo viśvasya vidvāmiha pāhi somam ||2||

(20,96.3 ) ya uśatā manasā somamasmai sarvahṛdā devakāmaḥ sunoti |
(20,96.3 ) na gā indrastasya parā dadāti praśastamiccārumasmai kṛṇoti ||3||
(20,96.4 ) anuspaṣṭo bhavatyeṣo asya yo asmai revān na sunoti somam |
(20,96.4 ) niraratnau maghavā taṃ dadhāti brahmadviṣo hantyanānudiṣṭaḥ ||4||

(20,96.5 ) aśvāyanto gavyanto vājayanto havāmahe tvopagantavā u |
(20,96.5 ) ābhūṣantaste sumatau navāyāṃ vayamindra tvā śunaṃ huvema ||5||

(20,96.6 ) muñcāmi tvā haviṣā jīvanāya kamajñātayakṣmāduta rājayakṣmāt|
(20,96.6 ) grāhirjagrāha yadyetadtasyā indrāgnī pra mumuktamenam ||6||

(20,96.7 ) yadi kṣitāyuryadi vā pareto yadi mṛtyorantikaṃ nīta eva |
(20,96.7 ) tamā harāmi nirṛterupasthādaspārśamenaṃ śataśāradāya ||7||

(20,96.8 ) sahasrākṣeṇa śatavīryeṇa śatāyuṣā haviṣāhārṣamenam |
(20,96.8 ) indro yathainaṃ śarado nayātyati viśvasya duritasya pāram ||8||

(20,96.9 ) śataṃ jīva śarado vardhamānaḥ śataṃ hemantān chatamu vasantān |
(20,96.9 ) śataṃ ta indro agniḥ savitā bṛhaspatiḥ śatāyuṣā haviṣāhārṣamenam ||9||

(20,96.10 ) āhārṣamavidaṃ tvā punarāgāḥ punarṇavaḥ |
(20,96.10 ) sarvāṅga sarvaṃ te cakṣuḥ sarvamāyuśca te'vidam ||10||
(20,96.11 ) brahmaṇāgniḥ samvidāno rakṣohā bādhatāmitaḥ |
(20,96.11 ) amīvā yaste garbhaṃ durṇāmā yonimāśaye ||11||

(20,96.12 ) yaste garbhamamīvā durṇāmā yonimāśaye |
(20,96.12 ) agniṣṭaṃ brahmaṇā saha niṣkravyādamanīnaśat||12||

(20,96.13 ) yaste hanti patayantaṃ niṣatsnuṃ yaḥ sarīsṛpam |
(20,96.13 ) jātaṃ yaste jighāṃsati tamito nāśayāmasi ||13||

(20,96.14 ) yasta ūrū viharatyantarā dampatī śaye |
(20,96.14 ) yoniṃ yo antarārelhi tamito nāśayāmasi ||14||

(20,96.15 ) yastvā bhrātā patirbhūtvā jāro bhūtvā nipadyate |
(20,96.15 ) prajāṃ yaste jighāṃsati tamito nāśayāmasi ||15||

(20,96.16 ) yastvā svapnena tamasā mohayitvā nipadyate |
(20,96.16 ) prajāṃ yaste jighāṃsati tamito nāśayāmasi ||16||

(20,96.17 ) akṣībhyāṃ te nāsikābhyāṃ karṇābhyāṃ chubukādadhi |
(20,96.17 ) yakṣmaṃ śīrṣaṇyaṃ mastiṣkājjihvāyā vi vṛhāmi te ||17||

(20,96.18 ) grīvābhyasta uṣṇihābhyaḥ kīkasābhyo anūkyāt|
(20,96.18 ) yakṣmaṃ doṣaṇyamaṃsābhyāṃ bāhubhyāṃ vi vṛhāmi te ||18||

(20,96.19 ) hṛdayātte pari klomno halīkṣṇātpārśvābhyām |
(20,96.19 ) yakṣmaṃ matasnābhyāṃ plīhno yaknaste vi vṛhāmasi ||19||

(20,96.20 ) āntrebhyaste gudābhyo vaniṣṭhorudarādadhi |
(20,96.20 ) yakṣmaṃ kukṣibhyāṃ plāśernābhyā vi vṛhāmi te ||20||

(20,96.21 ) ūrubhyāṃ te aṣṭhīvadbhyāṃ pārṣṇibhyāṃ prapadābhyām |
(20,96.21 ) yakṣmaṃ bhasadyaṃ śroṇibhyāṃ bhāsadaṃ bhāṃsaso vi vṛhāmi te ||21||

(20,96.22[]a) mehanādvanaṃkaraṇāllomabhyaste nakhebhyaḥ |
(20,96.22[]c) yakṣmaṃ sarvasmādātmanastamidaṃ vi vṛhāmi te ||22||

(20,96.[]22 ) asthibhyaste majjabhyaḥ snāvabhyo dhamanibhyaḥ |
(20,96.[]22 ) yakṣmaṃ pāṇibhyāmaṅgulibhyo nakhebhyo vi vṛhāmi te ||22||

(20,96.23 ) aṅgeaṅge lomnilomni yaste parvaṇiparvaṇi |
(20,96.23 ) yakṣaṃ tvacasyaṃ te vayaṃ kaśyapasya vībarheṇa viṣvañcaṃ vi vṛhāmasi ||23||

(20,96.24 ) apehi manasaspate'pa kāma paraścara |
(20,96.24 ) paro nirṛtyā ā cakṣva bahudhā jīvato manaḥ ||24||



(20,97.1 ) vayamenamidā hyopīpemeha vajriṇam |
(20,97.1 ) tasmā u adya samanā sutaṃ bharā nūnaṃ bhūṣata śrute ||1||

(20,97.2 ) vṛkaścidasya vāraṇa urāmathirā vayuneṣu bhūṣati |
(20,97.2 ) semaṃ naḥ stomaṃ jujuṣāṇa ā gahīndra pra citrayā dhiyā ||2||

(20,97.3 ) kadu nvasyākṛtamindrasyāsti pauṃsyam |
(20,97.3 ) keno nu kaṃ śromatena na śuśruve januṣaḥ pari vṛtrahā ||3||



(20,98.1 ) tvāmiddhi havāmahe sātā vājasya kāravaḥ |
(20,98.1 ) tvāṃ vṛtreṣvindra satpatiṃ narastvāṃ kāṣṭhāsvarvataḥ ||1||

(20,98.2 ) sa tvaṃ naścitra vajrahasta dhṛṣṇuyā maha stavāno adrivaḥ |
(20,98.2 ) gāmaśvaṃ rathyamindra saṃ kira satrā vājaṃ na jigyuṣe ||2||



(20,99.1 ) abhi tvā pūrvapītaya indra stomebhirāyavaḥ |
(20,99.1 ) samīcīnāsa ṛbhavaḥ samasvaran rudrā gṛṇanta pūrvyam ||1||

(20,99.2 ) asyedindro vāvṛdhe vṛṣṇyaṃ śavo made sutasya viṣṇavi |
(20,99.2 ) adyā tamasya mahimānamāyavo'nu ṣṭuvanti pūrvathā ||2||



(20,100.1 ) adhā hīndra girvaṇa upa tvā kāmān mahaḥ sasṛjmahe |
(20,100.1 ) udeva yanta udabhiḥ ||1||

(20,100.2 ) vārṇa tvā yavyābhirvardhanti śūra brahmāṇi |
(20,100.2 ) vāvṛdhvāṃsaṃ cidadrivo divedive ||2||

(20,100.3 ) yuñjanti harī iṣirasya gāthayorau ratha uruyuge |
(20,100.3 ) indravāhā vacoyujā ||3||


(20,101.1 ) agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe hotāraṃ viśvavedasam |
(20,101.1 ) asya yajñasya sukratum ||1||

(20,101.2 ) agnimagniṃ havīmabhiḥ sadā havanta viśpatim |
(20,101.2 ) havyavāhaṃ purupriyam ||2||

(20,101.3 ) agne devāmihā vaha jajñāno vṛktabarhiṣe |
(20,101.3 ) asi hotā na īḍyaḥ ||3||



(20,102.1 ) īlenyo namasyastirastamāṃsi darśataḥ |
(20,102.1 ) samagniridhyate vṛṣā ||1||

(20,102.2 ) vṛṣo agniḥ samidhyate'śvo na devavāhanaḥ |
(20,102.2 ) taṃ haviṣmantaḥ īlate ||2||

(20,102.3 ) vṛṣaṇaṃ tvā vayaṃ vṛṣan vṛṣaṇaḥ samidhīmahi |
(20,102.3 ) agne dīdyataṃ bṛhat||3||



(20,103.1 ) agnimīliṣvāvase gāthābhiḥ śīraśociṣam |
(20,103.1 ) agniṃ rāye purumīlha śrutaṃ naro'gniṃ sudītaye chardiḥ ||1||

(20,103.2 ) agna ā yāhyagnibhirhotāraṃ tvā vṛṇīmahe |
(20,103.2 ) ā tvāmanaktu prayatā haviṣmatī yajiṣṭhaṃ barhirāsade ||2||

(20,103.3 ) acha hi tvā sahasaḥ sūno aṅgiraḥ srucaścarantyadhvare |
(20,103.3 ) ūrjo napātaṃ ghṛtakeśamīmahe'gniṃ yajñeṣu pūrvyam ||3||



(20,104.1 ) imā u tvā purūvaso giro vardhantu yā mama |
(20,104.1 ) pāvakavarṇāḥ śucayo vipaścito'bhi stomairanūṣata ||1||

(20,104.2 ) ayaṃ sahasramṛṣibhiḥ sahaskṛtaḥ samudra iva paprathe |
(20,104.2 ) satyaḥ so asya mahimā gṛne śavo yajñeṣu viprarājye ||2||

(20,104.3 ) ā no viśvāsu havya indraḥ samatsu bhūṣatu |
(20,104.3 ) upa brahmāṇi savanāni vṛtrahā paramajyā ṛcīṣamaḥ ||3||

(20,104.4 ) tvaṃ dātā prathamo rāghasāmasyasi satya īśānakṛt|
(20,104.4 ) tuvidyumnasya yujyā vṛṇīmahe putrasya śavaso mahaḥ ||4||



(20,105.1 ) tvamindra pratūrtiṣvabhi viśvā asi spṛdhaḥ |
(20,105.1 ) aśastihā janitā viśvatūrasi tvaṃ tūrya taruṣyataḥ ||1||

(20,105.2 ) anu te śuṣmaṃ turayantamīyatuḥ kṣoṇī śiśuṃ na mātarā |
(20,105.2 ) viśvāste spṛdhaḥ śnathayanta manyave vṛtraṃ yadindra tūrvasi ||2||

(20,105.3 ) ita ūtī vo ajaraṃ prahetāramaprahitam |
(20,105.3 ) āśuṃ jetāraṃ hetāraṃ rathītamamatūrtaṃ tugryāvṛdham ||3||

(20,105.4 ) yo rājā carṣaṇīnāṃ yātā rathebhiradhriguḥ |
(20,105.4 ) viśvāsāṃ tarutā pṛtanānāṃ jyeṣṭho yo vṛtrahā gṛṇe ||4||

(20,105.5 ) indraṃ taṃ śumbha puruhanmann avase yasya dvitā vidhartari |
(20,105.5 ) hastāya vajraḥ prati dhāyi darśato maho dive na sūryaḥ ||5||


(20,106.1 ) tava tyadindriyaṃ bṛhattava śuṣmamuta kratum |
(20,106.1 ) vajraṃ śiśāti dhiṣaṇā vareṇyam ||1||

(20,106.2 ) tava dyaurindra pauṃsyaṃ pṛthivī vardhati śravaḥ |
(20,106.2 ) tvāmāpaḥ parvatāsaśca hinvire ||2||

(20,106.3 ) tvāṃ viṣṇurbṛhan kṣayo mitro gṛṇāti varuṇaḥ |
(20,106.3 ) tvāṃ śardho madatyanu mārutam ||3||



(20,107.1 ) samasya manyave viśo viśvā namanta kuṣṭayaḥ |
(20,107.1 ) samudrāyeva sindhavaḥ ||1||

(20,107.2 ) ojastadasya titviṣa ubhe yatsamavartayat|
(20,107.2 ) indraścarmeva rodasī ||2||

(20,107.3 ) vi cidvṛtrasya dodhato vajreṇa śataparvaṇā |
(20,107.3 ) śiro bibhedvṛṣṇinā ||3||

(20,107.4 ) tadidāsa bhuvaneṣu jyeṣṭhaṃ yato jajña ugrastveṣanṛmṇaḥ |
(20,107.4 ) sadyo jajñāno ni riṇāti śatrūn anu yadenaṃ madanti viśva ūmāḥ ||4||

(20,107.5 ) vāvṛdhānaḥ śavasā bhūryojāḥ śatrurdāsāya bhiyasaṃ dadhāti |
(20,107.5 ) avyanacca vyanacca sasni saṃ te navanta prabhṛtā madeṣu ||5||

(20,107.6 ) tve kratumapi pṛñcanti bhūri dviryadete trirbhavantyūmāḥ |
(20,107.6 ) svādoḥ svādīyaḥ svādunā sṛjā samadaḥ su madhu madhunābhi yodhīḥ ||6||

(20,107.7 ) yadi cin nu tvā dhanā jayantaṃ raṇeraṇe anumadanti viprāḥ |
(20,107.7 ) ojīyaḥ śuṣmintsthiramā tanuṣva mā tvā dabhan durevāsaḥ kaśokāḥ ||7||

(20,107.8 ) tvayā vayaṃ śāśadmahe raṇeṣu prapaśyanto yudhenyāni bhūri |
(20,107.8 ) codayāmi ta āyudhā vacobhiḥ saṃ te śiśāmi brahmaṇā vayāṃsi ||8||

(20,107.9 ) ni taddadhiṣe'vare pare ca yasminn āvithāvasā duroṇe |
(20,107.9 ) ā sthāpayata mātaraṃ jigatnumata invata karvarāṇi bhūri ||9||

(20,107.10 ) stuṣva varṣman puruvartmānaṃ samṛbhvāṇaminatamamāptamāptyānām |
(20,107.10 ) ā darśati śavasā bhūryojāḥ pra sakṣati pratimānaṃ pṛthivyāḥ ||10||

(20,107.11 ) imā brahma bṛhaddivaḥ kṛṇavadindrāya śūṣamagniyaḥ svarṣāḥ |
(20,107.11 ) maho gotrasya kṣayati svarājā turaścidviśvamarṇavattapasvān ||11||

(20,107.12 ) evā mahān bṛhaddivo atharvāvocatsvāṃ tanvamindrameva |
(20,107.12 ) svasārau mātaribhvarī aripre hinvanti caine śavasā vardhayanti ca ||12||

(20,107.13 ) citraṃ devānāṃ keturanīkaṃ jyotiṣmān pradiśaḥ sūrya udyan |
(20,107.13 ) divākaro'ti dyumnaistamāṃsi viśvātārīdduritāni śukraḥ ||13||

(20,107.14 ) citraṃ devānāmudagādanīkaṃ cakṣurmitrasya varuṇasyāgneḥ |
(20,107.14 ) āprāddyāvāpṛthivī antarikṣaṃ sūrya ātmā jagatastasthuṣaśca ||14||

(20,107.15 ) sūryo devīmuṣasaṃ rocamānāṃ maryo na yoṣāmabhyeti paścāt|
(20,107.15 ) yatrā naro devayanto yugāni vitanvate prati bhadrāya bhadram ||15||



(20,108.1 ) tvaṃ na indrā bharamojo nṛmṇaṃ śatakrato vicarṣaṇe |
(20,108.1 ) ā vīraṃ pṛtanāṣaham ||1||
(20,108.2 ) tvaṃ hi naḥ pitā vaso tvaṃ mātā śatakrato babhūvitha |
(20,108.2 ) adhā te sumnamīmahe ||2||

(20,108.3 ) tvāṃ śuṣmin puruhūta vājayantamupa bruve śatakrato |
(20,108.3 ) sa no rāsva suvīryam ||3||



(20,109.1 ) svādoritthā viṣuvato madhvaḥ pibanti gauryaḥ |
(20,109.1 ) yā indreṇa sayāvarīrvṛṣṇā madanti śobhase vasvīranu svarājyam ||1||

(20,109.2 ) tā asya pṛśanāyuvaḥ somaṃ śrīṇanti pṛśnayaḥ |
(20,109.2 ) priyā indrasya dhenavo vajraṃ hinvanti sāyakaṃ vasvīranu svarājyam ||2||

(20,109.3 ) tā asya namasā sahaḥ saparyanti pracetasaḥ |
(20,109.3 ) vratānyasya saścire purūṇi pūrvacittaye vasvīranu svarājyam ||3||



(20,110.1 ) indrāya madūne sutaṃ pari ṣṭobhantu no giraḥ |
(20,110.1 ) arkamarcantu kāravaḥ ||1||

(20,110.2 ) yasmin viśvā adhi śriyo raṇanti sapta saṃsadaḥ |
(20,110.2 ) indraṃ sute havāmahe ||2||

(20,110.3 ) trikadrukeṣu cetanaṃ devāso yajñamatnata |
(20,110.3 ) tamidvardhantu no giraḥ ||3||



(20,111.1 ) yatsomamindra viṣṇavi yadvā gha trita āptye |
(20,111.1 ) yadvā marutsu mandase samindubhiḥ ||1||

(20,111.2 ) yadvā śakra parāvati samudre adhi mandase |
(20,111.2 ) asmākamitsute raṇā samindubhiḥ ||2||

(20,111.3 ) yadvāsi sunvato vṛdho yajamānasya satpate |
(20,111.3 ) ukthe vā yasya raṇyasi samindubhiḥ ||3||


(20,112.1 ) yadadya kacca vṛtrahann udagā abhi sūrya |
(20,112.1 ) sarvaṃ tadindra te vaśe ||1||

(20,112.2 ) yadvā pravṛddha satpate na marā iti manyase |
(20,112.2 ) uto tatsatyamittava ||2||

(20,112.3 ) ye somāsaḥ parāvati ye arvāvati sunvire |
(20,112.3 ) sarvāṃstāmindra gachasi ||3||



(20,113.1 ) ubhayaṃ śṛṇavacca na indro arvāgidaṃ vacaḥ |
(20,113.1 ) satrācyā maghavā somapītaye dhiyā śaviṣṭha ā gamat||1||
(20,113.2 ) taṃ hi svarājaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ tamojase dhiṣaṇe niṣṭatakṣatuḥ |
(20,113.2 ) utopamānāṃ prathamo ni ṣīdasi somakāmaṃ hi te manaḥ ||2||



(20,114.1 ) abhrātṛvyo'nā tvamanāpirindra januṣā sanādasi |
(20,114.1 ) yudhedāpitvamichase ||1||

(20,114.2 ) nakī revantaṃ sakhyāya vindase pīyanti te surāśvaḥ |
(20,114.2 ) yadā kṛṇoṣi nadanuṃ samūhasyāditpiteva hūyase ||2||



(20,115.1 ) ahamiddhi pituṣpari medhāmṛtasya jagrabha |
(20,115.1 ) ahaṃ sūrya ivājani ||1||

(20,115.2 ) ahaṃ pratnena manmanā giraḥ śumbhāmi kaṇvavat|
(20,115.2 ) yenendraḥ śuṣmamiddadhe ||2||

(20,115.3 ) ye tvāmindra na tuṣṭuvurṛṣayo ye ca tuṣṭuvuḥ |
(20,115.3 ) mamedvardhasva suṣṭutaḥ ||3||


(20,116.1 ) mā bhūma niṣṭyā ivendra tvadaraṇā iva |
(20,116.1 ) vanāni ni prajahitānyadrivo duroṣāso amanmahi ||1||

(20,116.2 ) amanmahīdanāśavo'nugrāsaśca vṛtrahan |
(20,116.2 ) sukṛtsu te mahatā śūra rādhasānu stomaṃ mudīmahi ||2||



(20,117.1 ) pibā somamindra mandatu tvā yaṃ te suṣāva haryaśvādriḥ |
(20,117.1 ) soturbāhubhyāṃ suyato nārvā ||1||

(20,117.2 ) yaste mado yujascārurasti yena vṛtrāṇi haryaśva haṃsi |
(20,117.2 ) sa tvāmindra prabhūvaso mamattu ||2||

(20,117.3 ) bodhā su me maghavan vācamemāṃ yāṃ te vasiṣṭho arcati praśastim |
(20,117.3 ) imā brahma sadhamāde juṣasva ||3||



(20,118.1 ) śagdhyū ṣu śacīpata indra viśvābhirūtibhiḥ |
(20,118.1 ) bhagaṃ na hi tvā yaśasaṃ vasuvidamanu śūra carāmasi ||1||

(20,118.2 ) pauro aśvasya purukṛdgavāmasyutso deva hiraṇyayaḥ |
(20,118.2 ) nakirhi dānaṃ parimardhiṣattve yadyadyāmi tadā bhara ||2||

(20,118.3 ) indramiddevatātaye indraṃ prayatyadhvare |
(20,118.3 ) indraṃ samīke vanino havāmaha indraṃ dhanasya sātaye ||3||

(20,118.4 ) indro mahnā rodasī paprathacchava indraḥ sūryamarocayat|
(20,118.4 ) indre ha viśvā bhuvanāni yemira indre suvānāsa indavaḥ ||4||



(20,119.1 ) astāvi manma pūrvyaṃ brahmendrāya vocata |
(20,119.1 ) pūrvīrṛtasya bṛhatīranūṣata stoturmeghā asṛkṣata ||1||

(20,119.2 ) turaṇyavo madhumantaṃ ghṛtaścutaṃ viprāso arkamānṛcuḥ |
(20,119.2 ) asme rayiḥ paprathe vṛṣṇyaṃ śavo'sme suvānāsa indavaḥ ||2||



(20,120.1 ) yadindra prāgapāgudaṅnyagvā hūyase nṛbhiḥ |
(20,120.1 ) simā purū nṛṣūto asyānave'si praśardha turvaśe ||1||

(20,120.2 ) yadvā rume ruśame śyāvake kṛpa indra mādayase sacā |
(20,120.2 ) kaṇvāsastvā brahmabhi stomavāhasa indrā yachantyā gahi ||2||


(20,121.1 ) abhi tvā śūra nonumo'dugdhā iva dhenavaḥ |
(20,121.1 ) īśānamasya jagataḥ svardṛśamīśānamindra tasthuṣaḥ ||1||

(20,121.2 ) na tvāvāmanyo divyo na pārthivo na jāto na janiṣyate |
(20,121.2 ) aśvāyanto maghavann indra vājino gavyantastvā havāmahe ||2||



(20,122.1 ) revatīrnaḥ sadhamāda indre santu tuvivājāḥ |
(20,122.1 ) kṣumanto yābhirmadema ||1||

(20,122.2 ) ā gha tvāvān tmanāpta stotṛbhyo dhṛṣṇaviyānaḥ |
(20,122.2 ) ṛṇorakṣaṃ na cakrayoḥ ||2||

(20,122.3 ) ā yadduvaḥ śatakratavā kāmaṃ jaritṝṇām |
(20,122.3 ) ṛṇorakṣaṃ na śacībhiḥ ||3||



(20,123.1 ) tatsūryasya devatvaṃ tan mahitvaṃ madhyā kartorvitataṃ saṃ jabhāra |
(20,123.1 ) yadedayukta haritaḥ sadhasthādādrātrī vāsastanute simasmai ||1||

(20,123.2 ) tan mitrasya varuṇasyābhicakṣe sūryo rūpaṃ kṛṇute dyorupasthe |
(20,123.2 ) anantamanyadruśadasya prājaḥ kṛṣṇamanyaddharitaḥ saṃ bharanti ||2||



(20,124.1 ) kayā naścitra ā bhuvadūtī sadāvṛdhaḥ sakhā |
(20,124.1 ) kayā śaciṣṭhayā vṛtā ||1||

(20,124.2 ) kastvā satyo madānāṃ maṃhiṣṭho matsadandhasaḥ |
(20,124.2 ) dṛlhā cidāruje vasu ||2||

(20,124.3 ) abhī ṣu naḥ sakhīnāmavitā jaritṝṇām |
(20,124.3 ) śataṃ bhavāsyūtibhiḥ ||3||

(20,124.4 ) imā nu kaṃ bhuvanā sīṣadhāmendraśca viśve ca devāḥ |
(20,124.4 ) yajñaṃ ca nastanvaṃ ca prajāṃ cādityairindraḥ saha cīkḷpāti ||4||

(20,124.5 ) ādityairindraḥ sagaṇo marudbhirasmākaṃ bhūtvavitā tanūnām |
(20,124.5 ) hatvāya devā asurān yadāyan devā devatvamabhirakṣamāṇāḥ ||5||

(20,124.6 ) pratyañcamarkamanayaṃ chacībhirāditsvadhāmiṣirāṃ paryapaśyan |
(20,124.6 ) ayā vājaṃ devahitaṃ sanema madema śatahimāḥ suvīrāḥ ||6||



(20,125.1 ) apendra prāco maghavann amitrān apāpāco abhibhūte nudasva |
(20,125.1 ) apodīco apa śūrādharāca urau yathā tava śarman madema ||1||

(20,125.2 ) kuvidaṅga yavamanto yavaṃ cidyathā dāntyanupūrvaṃ viyūya |
(20,125.2 ) ihehaiṣāṃ kṛṇuhi bhojanāni ye barhiṣo namovṛktiṃ na jagmuḥ ||2||

(20,125.3 ) nahi sthūryṛtuthā yātamasti nota śravo vivide saṃgameṣu |
(20,125.3 ) gavyanta indraṃ sakhyāya viprā aśvāyanto vṛṣaṇaṃ vājayantaḥ ||3||

(20,125.4 ) yuvaṃ surāmamaśvinā namucāvāsure sacā |
(20,125.4 ) vipipānā śubhaspatī indraṃ karmasvāvatam ||4||

(20,125.5 ) putramiva pitarāvaśvinobhendrāvathuḥ kāvyairdaṃsanābhiḥ |
(20,125.5 ) yatsurāmaṃ vyapibaḥ śacībhiḥ sarasvatī tvā maghavann abhiṣṇak||5||

(20,125.6 ) indraḥ sutrāmā svavāmavobhiḥ sumṛḍīko bhavatu viśvavedāḥ |
(20,125.6 ) bādhatāṃ dveṣo abhayaṃ naḥ kṛṇotu suvīryasya patayaḥ syāma ||6||

(20,125.7 ) sa sutrāmā svavāmindro asmadārācciddveṣaḥ sanutaryuyotu |
(20,125.7 ) tasya vayaṃ sumatau yajñiyasyāpi bhadre saumanase syāma ||7||



(20,126.1 ) vi hi sotorasṛkṣata nendraṃ devamamaṃsata |
(20,126.1 ) yatrāmadadvṛṣākapiraryaḥ puṣṭeṣu matsakhā viśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||1||

(20,126.2 ) parā hīndra dhāvasi vṛṣākaperati vyathiḥ |
(20,126.2 ) no aha pra vindasyanyatra somapītaye viśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||2||

(20,126.3 ) kimayaṃ tvāṃ vṛṣākapiścakāra harito mṛgaḥ |
(20,126.3 ) yasmā irasyasīdu nvaryo vā puṣṭimadvasu viśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||3||

(20,126.4 ) yamimaṃ tvaṃ vṛṣākapiṃ priyamindrābhirakṣasi |
(20,126.4 ) śvā nvasya jambhiṣadapi karṇe varāhayurviśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||4||

(20,126.5 ) priyā taṣṭāni me kapirvyaktā vyadūduṣat|
(20,126.5 ) śiro nvasya rāviṣaṃ na sugaṃ duṣkṛte bhuvaṃ viśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||5||

(20,126.6 ) na matstrī subhasattarā na suyāśutarā bhuvat|
(20,126.6 ) na matpraticyavīyasī na sakthyudyamīyasī viśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||6||

(20,126.7 ) uve amba sulābhike yathevāṅgaṃ bhaviṣyati |
(20,126.7 ) bhasan me amba sakthi me śiro me vīva hṛṣyati viśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||7||

(20,126.8 ) kiṃ subāho svaṅgure pṛthuṣṭo pṛthujāghane |
(20,126.8 ) kiṃ śūrapatni nastvamabhyamīṣi vṛṣākapiṃ viśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||8||

(20,126.9 ) avīrāmiva māmayaṃ śarārurabhi manyate |
(20,126.9 ) utāhamasmi vīriṇīndrapatnī marutsakhā viśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||9||

(20,126.10 ) saṃhotraṃ sma purā nārī samanaṃ vāva gachati |
(20,126.10 ) vedhā ṛtasya vīriṇīndrapatnī mahīyate viśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||10||

(20,126.11 ) indrāṇīmāsu nāriṣu subhagāmahamaśravam |
(20,126.11 ) nahyasyā aparaṃ cana jarasā marate patirviśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||11||

(20,126.12 ) nāhamindrāṇi rāraṇa sakhyurvṛṣākaperṛte |
(20,126.12 ) yasyedamapyaṃ haviḥ priyaṃ deveṣu gachati viśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||12||

(20,126.13 ) vṛsākapāyi revati suputra ādu susnuṣe |
(20,126.13 ) ghasatta indra ukṣaṇaḥ priyaṃ kācitkaraṃ havirviśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||13||

(20,126.14 ) ukṣṇo hi me pañcadaśa sākaṃ pacanti viṃsatim |
(20,126.14 ) utāhamadmi pīva idubhā kukṣī pṛṇanti me viśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||14||

(20,126.15 ) vṛṣabho na tigmaśṛṅgo'ntaryūtheṣu roruvat|
(20,126.15 ) manthasta indra śaṃ hṛde yaṃ te sunoti bhāvayurviśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||15||

(20,126.16 ) na seśe yasya rambate'ntarā sakthyā kapṛt|
(20,126.16 ) sedīśe yasya romaśaṃ niṣeduṣo vijṛmbhate viśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||16||

(20,126.17 ) na seśe yasya romaśaṃ niṣeduṣo vijṛmbhate |
(20,126.17 ) sedīśe yasya rambate'ntarā sakthyā kapṛtviśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||17||

(20,126.18 ) ayamindra vṛṣākapiḥ parasvantaṃ hataṃ vidat|
(20,126.18 ) asiṃ sūnāṃ navaṃ carumādedhasyāna ācitaṃ viśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||18||

(20,126.19 ) ayamemi vicākaśadvicinvan dāsamāryam |
(20,126.19 ) pibāmi pākasutvano'bhi dhīramacākaśaṃ viśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||19||

(20,126.20 ) dhanva ca yatkṛntatraṃ ca kati svittā vi yojanā |
(20,126.20 ) nedīyaso vṛṣākape'stamehi gṛhāmupa viśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||20||

(20,126.21 ) punarehi vṛṣākape suvitā kalpayāvahai |
(20,126.21 ) ya eṣa svapnanaṃśano'stameṣi pathā punarviśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||21||

(20,126.22 ) yadudañco vṛṣākape gṛhamindrājagantana |
(20,126.22 ) kva sya pulvagho mṛgaḥ kamagaṃ janayopano viśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||22||

(20,126.23 ) parśurha nāma mānavī sākaṃ sasūva viṃśatim |
(20,126.23 ) bhadraṃ bhala tyasyā abhūdyasyā udaramāmayadviśvasmādindra uttaraḥ ||23||


(20,127.1 ) idaṃ janā upa śruta narāśaṃsa staviṣyate |
(20,127.1 ) ṣaṣṭiṃ sahasrā navatiṃ ca kaurama ā ruśameṣu dadmahe ||1||

(20,127.2 ) uṣṭrā yasya pravāhaṇo vadhūmanto dvirdaśa |
(20,127.2 ) varṣmā rathasya ni jihīḍate diva īṣamāṇā upaspṛśaḥ ||2||

(20,127.3 ) eṣa iṣāya māmahe śataṃ niṣkān daśa srajaḥ |
(20,127.3 ) trīṇi śatānyarvatāṃ sahasrā daśa gonām ||3||

(20,127.4 ) vacyasva rebha vacyasva vṛkṣe na pakve śakunaḥ |
(20,127.4 ) naṣṭe jihvā carcarīti kṣuro na bhurijoriva ||4||

(20,127.5 ) pra rebhāso manīṣā vṛṣā gāva iverate |
(20,127.5 ) amotaputrakā eṣāmamota gā ivāsate ||5||

(20,127.6 ) pra rebha dhīṃ bharasva govidaṃ vasuvidam |
(20,127.6 ) devatremāṃ vācaṃ srīṇīhīṣurnāvīrastāram ||6||

(20,127.7 ) rājño viśvajanīnasya yo devomartyāmati |
(20,127.7 ) vaiśvānarasya suṣṭutimā sunotā parikṣitaḥ ||7||

(20,127.8 ) parichinnaḥ kṣemamakarottama āsanamācaran |
