(20,127.8 ) kulāyan kṛṇvan kauravyaḥ patirvadati jāyayā ||8||

(20,127.9 ) kataratta ā harāṇi dadhi manthāṃ pari śrutam |
(20,127.9 ) jāyāḥ patiṃ vi pṛchati rāṣṭre rājñaḥ parikṣitaḥ ||9||

(20,127.10 ) abhīvasvaḥ pra jihīte yavaḥ pakvaḥ patho bilam |
(20,127.10 ) janaḥ sa bhadramedhati rāṣṭre rājñaḥ parikṣitaḥ ||10||

(20,127.11 ) indraḥ kārumabūbudhaduttiṣṭha vi carā janam |
(20,127.11 ) mamedugrasya carkṛdhi sarva itte pṛṇādariḥ ||11||

(20,127.12 ) iha gāvaḥ pra jāyadhvamihāśvā iha pūruṣāḥ |
(20,127.12 ) iho sahasradakṣiṇopi pūṣā ni ṣīdati ||12||

(20,127.13 ) nemā indra gāvo riṣan mo āsāṃ gopa rīriṣat|
(20,127.13 ) māsāmamitrayurjana indra mā stena īśata ||13||

(20,127.14 ) upa no na ramasi sūktena vacasā vayaṃ bhadreṇa vacasā vayam |
(20,127.14 ) vanādadhidhvano giro na riṣyema kadā cana ||14||


(20,128.1 ) yaḥ sabheyo vidathyaḥ sutvā yajvātha pūruṣaḥ |
(20,128.1 ) sūryaṃ cāmū riśādasastaddevāḥ prāgakalpayan ||1||

(20,128.2 ) yo jāmyā aprathayastadyatsakhāyaṃ dudhūrṣati |
(20,128.2 ) jyeṣṭho yadapracetāstadāhuradharāgiti ||2||

(20,128.3 ) yadbhadrasya puruṣasya putro bhavati dādhṛṣiḥ |
(20,128.3 ) tadvipro abravīdu tadgandharvaḥ kāmyaṃ vacaḥ ||3||

(20,128.4 ) yaśca paṇi raghujiṣṭhyo yaśca devāmadāśuriḥ |
(20,128.4 ) dhīrāṇāṃ śaśvatāmahaṃ tadapāgiti śuśruma ||4||

(20,128.5 ) ye ca devā ayajantātho ye ca parādadiḥ |
(20,128.5 ) sūryo divamiva gatvāya maghavā no vi rapśate ||5||

(20,128.6 ) yo'nāktākṣo anabhyakto amaṇivo ahiraṇyavaḥ |
(20,128.6 ) abrahmā brahmaṇaḥ putrastotā kalpeṣu saṃmitā ||6||
(20,128.7 ) ya āktākṣaḥ subhyaktaḥ sumaṇiḥ suhiraṇyavaḥ |
(20,128.7 ) subrahmā brahmaṇaḥ putrastotā kalpeṣu saṃmitā ||7||

(20,128.8 ) aprapāṇā ca veśantā revāmapratidiśyayaḥ |
(20,128.8 ) ayabhyā kanyā kalyāṇī totā kalpeṣu saṃmitā ||8||

(20,128.9 ) suprapāṇā ca veśantā revāntsupratidiśyayaḥ |
(20,128.9 ) suyabhyā kanyā kalyāṇī totā kalpeṣu saṃmitā ||9||

(20,128.10 ) parivṛktā ca mahiṣī svastyā ca yudhiṃ gamaḥ |
(20,128.10 ) anāśuraścāyāmī totā kalpeṣu saṃmitā ||10||

(20,128.11 ) vāvātā ca mahiṣī svastyā ca yudhiṃ gamaḥ |
(20,128.11 ) śvāśuraścāyāmī totā kalpeṣu saṃmitā ||11||

(20,128.12 ) yadindrādo dāśarājñe mānuṣaṃ vi gāhathāḥ |
(20,128.12 ) virūpaḥ sarvasmā āsītsaha yakṣāya kalpate ||12||
(20,128.13 ) tvaṃ vṛṣākṣuṃ maghavann amraṃ maryākaro raviḥ |
(20,128.13 ) tvaṃ rauhiṇaṃ vyāsyo vi vṛtrasyābhinacchiraḥ ||13||

(20,128.14 ) yaḥ parvatān vyadadhādyo apo vyagāhathāḥ |
(20,128.14 ) indro yo vṛtrahānmahaṃ tasmādindra namo'stu te ||14||

(20,128.15 ) pṛṣṭhaṃ dhāvantaṃ haryorauccaiḥ śravasamabruvan |
(20,128.15 ) svastyaśva jaitrāyendramā vaha susrajam ||15||

(20,128.16 ) ye tvā śvetā ajaiśravaso hāryo yuñjanti dakṣiṇam |
(20,128.16 ) pūrvā namasya devānāṃ bibhradindra mahīyate ||16||



(20,129.1 ) etā aśvā ā plavante ||1||

(20,129.2 ) pratīpaṃ prāti sutvanam ||2||

(20,129.3 ) tāsāmekā hariknikā ||3||

(20,129.4 ) hariknike kimichāsi ||4||

(20,129.5 ) sādhuṃ putraṃ hiraṇyayam ||5||

(20,129.6 ) kvāhataṃ parāsyaḥ ||6||

(20,129.7 ) yatrāmūstisraḥ śiṃśapāḥ ||7||
(20,129.8 ) pari trayaḥ ||8||

(20,129.9 ) pṛdākavaḥ ||9||

(20,129.10 ) śṛṅgaṃ dhamanta āsate ||10||

(20,129.11 ) ayanmahā te arvāhaḥ ||11||

(20,129.12 ) sa ichakaṃ saghāghate ||12||

(20,129.13 ) saghāghate gomīdyā gogatīriti ||13||

(20,129.14 ) pumāṃ kuste nimichasi ||14||

(20,129.15 ) palpa baddha vayo iti ||15||

(20,129.16 ) baddha vo aghā iti ||16||

(20,129.17 ) ajāgāra kevikā ||17||

(20,129.18 ) aśvasya vāro gośapadyake ||18||

(20,129.19 ) śyenīpatī sā ||19||

(20,129.20 ) anāmayopajihvikā ||20||



(20,130.1 ) ko arya bahulimā iṣūni ||1||

(20,130.2 ) ko asidyāḥ payaḥ ||2||

(20,130.3 ) ko arjunyāḥ payaḥ ||3||

(20,130.4 ) kaḥ kārṣṇyāḥ payaḥ ||4||

(20,130.5 ) etaṃ pṛcha kuhaṃ pṛcha ||5||

(20,130.6 ) kuhākaṃ pakvakaṃ pṛcha ||6||

(20,130.7 ) yavāno yatiṣvabhiḥ kubhiḥ ||7||
(20,130.8 ) akupyantaḥ kupāyakuḥ ||8||

(20,130.9 ) āmaṇako maṇatsakaḥ ||9||

(20,130.10 ) deva tvapratisūrya ||10||

(20,130.11 ) enaścipaṅktikā haviḥ ||11||

(20,130.12 ) pradudrudo maghāprati ||12||

(20,130.13 ) śṛṅga utpanna ||13||

(20,130.14 ) mā tvābhi sakhā no vidan ||14||

(20,130.15 ) vaśāyāḥ putramā yanti ||15||

(20,130.16 ) irāvedumayaṃ data ||16||

(20,130.17 ) atho iyanniyann iti ||17||

(20,130.18 ) atho iyanniti ||18||

(20,130.19 ) atho śvā asthiro bhavan ||19||

(20,130.20 ) uyaṃ yakāṃśalokakā ||20||



(20,131.1 ) āminoniti bhadyate ||1||

(20,131.2 ) tasya anu nibhañjanam ||2||

(20,131.3 ) varuṇo yāti vasvabhiḥ ||3||

(20,131.4 ) śataṃ vā bhāratī śavaḥ ||4||

(20,131.5 ) śatamāśvā hiraṇyayāḥ |
(20,131.5 ) śataṃ rathyā hiraṇyayāḥ |
(20,131.5 ) śataṃ kuthā hiraṇyayāḥ |

(20,131.6 ) ahula kuśa varttaka ||6||

(20,131.7 ) śaphena iva ohate ||7||

(20,131.8 ) āya vanenatī janī ||8||

(20,131.9 ) vaniṣṭhā nāva gṛhyanti ||9||

(20,131.10 ) idaṃ mahyaṃ madūriti ||10||

(20,131.11 ) te vṛkṣāḥ saha tiṣṭhati ||11||

(20,131.12 ) pāka baliḥ ||12||

(20,131.13 ) śaka baliḥ ||13||

(20,131.14 ) aśvattha khadiro dhavaḥ ||14||

(20,131.15 ) araduparama ||15||

(20,131.16 ) śayo hata iva ||16||

(20,131.17 ) vyāpa pūruṣaḥ ||17||

(20,131.18 ) adūhamityāṃ pūṣakam ||18||

(20,131.19 ) atyardharca parasvataḥ ||19||

(20,131.20 ) dauva hastino dṛtī ||20||



(20,132.1 ) ādalābukamekakam ||1||

(20,132.2 ) alābukaṃ nikhātakam ||2||

(20,132.3 ) karkariko nikhātakaḥ ||3||

(20,132.4 ) tadvāta unmathāyati ||4||

(20,132.5 ) kulāyaṃ kṛṇavāditi ||5||

(20,132.6 ) ugraṃ vaniṣadātatam ||6||

(20,132.7 ) na vaniṣadanātatam ||7||

(20,132.8 ) ka eṣāṃ karkarī likhat||8||

(20,132.9 ) ka eṣāṃ dundubhiṃ hanat||9||

(20,132.10 ) yadīyaṃ hanatkathaṃ hanat||10||

(20,132.11 ) devī hanatkuhanat||11||

(20,132.12 ) paryāgāraṃ punaḥpunaḥ ||12||

(20,132.13 ) trīṇyuṣṭrasya nāmāni ||13||

(20,132.14 ) hiraṇya ityeke abravīt||14||

(20,132.15 ) dvau vā ye śiśavaḥ ||15||

(20,132.16 ) nīlaśikhaṇḍavāhanaḥ ||16||



(20,133.1 ) vitatau kiraṇau dvau tāvā pinaṣṭi pūruṣaḥ |
(20,133.1 ) na vai kumāri tattathā yathā kumāri manyase ||1||

(20,133.2 ) mātuṣṭe kiraṇau dvau nivṛttaḥ puruṣānṛte |
(20,133.2 ) na vai kumāri tattathā yathā kumāri manyase ||2||

(20,133.3 ) nigṛhya karṇakau dvau nirāyachasi madhyame |
(20,133.3 ) na vai kumāri tattathā yathā kumāri manyase ||3||

(20,133.4 ) uttānāyai śayānāyai tiṣṭhantī vāva gūhasi |
(20,133.4 ) na vai kumāri tattathā yathā kumāri manyase ||4||
(20,133.5 ) ślakṣṇāyāṃ ślakṣṇikāyāṃ ślakṣṇamevāva gūhasi |
(20,133.5 ) na vai kumāri tattathā yathā kumāri manyase ||5||

(20,133.6 ) avaślakṣṇamiva bhraṃśadantarlomamati hrade |
(20,133.6 ) na vai kumāri tattathā yathā kumāri manyase ||6||



(20,134.1 ) ihettha prāgapāgudagadharāgarālāgudabhartsatha ||1||

(20,134.2 ) ihettha prāgapāgudagadharāgvatsāḥ puruṣanta āsate ||2||

(20,134.3 ) ihettha prāgapāgudagadharāksthālīpāko vi līyate ||3||

(20,134.4 ) ihettha prāgapāgudagadharāksa vai pṛthu līyate ||4||

(20,134.5 ) ihettha prāgapāgudagadharāgāste lāhaṇi līśāthī ||5||

(20,134.6 ) ihettha prāgapāgudagadharāgakṣlilī puchilīyate ||6||


(20,135.1 ) bhugityabhigataḥ śalityapakrāntaḥ phalityabhiṣṭhitaḥ |
(20,135.1 ) dundubhimāhananābhyāṃ jaritarothāmo daiva ||1||

(20,135.2 ) kośabile rajani grantherdhānamupānahi pādam |
(20,135.2 ) uttamāṃ janimāṃ janyānuttamāṃ janīn vartmanyāt||2||

(20,135.3 ) alābūni pṛṣātakānyaśvatthapalāśam |
(20,135.3 ) pipīlikāvataśvaso vidyutsvāparṇaśapho gośapho jaritarothāmo daiva ||3||

(20,135.4 ) vīme devā akraṃsatādhvaryo kṣipraṃ pracara |
(20,135.4 ) susatyamidgavāmasyasi prakhudasi ||4||

(20,135.5 ) patnī yadṛśyate patnī yakṣyamāṇā jaritarothāmo daiva |
(20,135.5 ) hotā viṣṭīmena jaritarothāmo daiva ||5||

(20,135.6 ) ādityā ha jaritaraṅgirobhyo dakṣiṇāmanayan |
(20,135.6 ) tāṃ ha jaritaḥ pratyāyaṃstāmu ha jaritaḥ pratyāyan ||6||

(20,135.7 ) tāṃ ha jaritarnaḥ pratyagṛbhṇaṃstāmu ha jaritarnaḥ pratyagṛbhṇaḥ |
(20,135.7 ) ahānetarasaṃ na vi cetanāni yajñān etarasaṃ na purogavāmaḥ ||7||

(20,135.8 ) uta śveta āśupatvā uto padyābhiryaviṣṭhaḥ |
(20,135.8 ) utemāśu mānaṃ piparti ||8||

(20,135.9 ) ādityā rudrā vasavastvenu ta idaṃ rādhaḥ prati gṛbhṇīhyaṅgiraḥ |
(20,135.9 ) idaṃ rādho vibhu prabhu idaṃ rādho bṛhatpṛthu ||9||

(20,135.10 ) devā dadatvāsuraṃ tadvo astu sucetanam |
(20,135.10 ) yuṣmāmastu divedive pratyeva gṛbhāyat||10||

(20,135.11 ) tvamindra śarmariṇā havyaṃ pārāvatebhyaḥ |
(20,135.11 ) viprāya stuvate vasuvaniṃ duraśravase vaha ||11||

(20,135.12 ) tvamindra kapotāya chinnapakṣāya vañcate |
(20,135.12 ) śyāmākaṃ pakvaṃ pīlu ca vārasmā akṛṇorbahuḥ ||12||

(20,135.13 ) araṃgaro vāvadīti tredhā baddho varatrayā |
(20,135.13 ) irāmaha praśaṃsatyanirāmapa sedhati ||13||



(20,136.1 ) yadasyā aṃhubhedyāḥ kṛdhu sthūlamupātasat|
(20,136.1 ) muṣkāvidasyā ejato gośaphe śakulāviva ||1||

(20,136.2 ) yadā sthūlena pasasāṇau muṣkā upāvadhīt|
(20,136.2 ) viṣvañcā vasyā vardhataḥ sikatāsveva gardabhau ||2||

(20,136.3 ) yadalpikāsvalpikā karkadhūkevaṣadyate |
(20,136.3 ) vāsantikamiva tejanaṃ yantyavātāya vitpati ||3||

(20,136.4 ) yaddevāso lalāmaguṃ praviṣṭīminamāviṣuḥ |
(20,136.4 ) sakulā dediśyate nārī satyasyākṣibhuvo yathā ||4||

(20,136.5 ) mahānagnyatṛpnadvi mokradadasthānāsaran |
(20,136.5 ) śaktikānanā svacamaśakaṃ saktu padyama ||5||

(20,136.6 ) mahānagnyulūkhalamatikrāmantyabravīt|
(20,136.6 ) yathā tava vanaspate niraghnanti tathaiveti ||6||

(20,136.7 ) mahānagnyupa brūte bhraṣṭothāpyabhūbhuvaḥ |
(20,136.7 ) yathaiva te vanaspate pippati tathaiveti ||7||

(20,136.8 ) mahānagnyupa brūte bhraṣṭothāpyabhūbhuvaḥ |
(20,136.8 ) yathā vayo vidāhya svarge namavadahyate ||8||

(20,136.9 ) mahānagnyupa brūte svasāveśitaṃ pasaḥ |
(20,136.9 ) itthaṃ phalasya vṛkṣasya śūrpe śūrpaṃ bhajemahi ||9||

(20,136.10 ) mahānagnī kṛkavākaṃ śamyayā pari dhāvati |
(20,136.10 ) ayaṃ na vidma yo mṛgaḥ śīrṣṇā harati dhāṇikām ||10||

(20,136.11 ) mahānagnī mahānagnaṃ dhāvantamanu dhāvati |
(20,136.11 ) imāstadasya gā rakṣa yabha māmaddhyaudanam ||11||

(20,136.12 ) sudevastvā mahānagnīrbabādhate mahataḥ sādhu khodanam |
(20,136.12 ) kusaṃ pīvaro navat||12||

(20,136.13 ) vaśā dagdhāmimāṅguriṃ prasṛjatogrataṃ pare |
(20,136.13 ) mahān vai bhadro yabha māmaddhyaudanam ||13||

(20,136.14 ) videvastvā mahānagnīrvibādhate mahataḥ sādhu khodanam |
(20,136.14 ) kumārikā piṅgalikā kārda bhasmā ku dhāvati ||14||

(20,136.15 ) mahān vai bhadro bilvo mahān bhadra udumbaraḥ |
(20,136.15 ) mahāmabhikta bādhate mahataḥ sādhu khodanam ||15||

(20,136.16 ) yaḥ kumārī piṅgalikā vasantaṃ pīvarī labhet|
(20,136.16 ) tailakuṇḍamimāṅguṣṭhaṃ rodantaṃ śudamuddharet||16||



(20,137.1 ) yaddha prācīrajagantoro maṇḍūradhāṇikīḥ |
(20,137.1 ) hatā indrasya śatravaḥ sarve budbudayāśavaḥ ||1||

(20,137.2 ) kapṛn naraḥ kapṛthamuddadhātana codayata khudata vājasātaye |
(20,137.2 ) niṣṭigryaḥ putramā cyāvayotaya indraṃ sabādha iha somapītaye ||2||

(20,137.3 ) dadhikrāvṇo akāriṣaṃ jiṣṇoraśvasya vājinaḥ |
(20,137.3 ) surabhi no mukhā karatpra ṇa āyūṃṣi tāriṣat||3||

(20,137.4 ) sutāso madhumattamāḥ somā indrāya mandinaḥ |
(20,137.4 ) pavitravanto akṣaran devān gachantu vo madāḥ ||4||

(20,137.5 ) indurindrāya pavata iti devāso abruvan |
(20,137.5 ) vācaspatirmakhasyate viśvasyeśāna ojasā ||5||

(20,137.6 ) sahasradhāraḥ pavate samudro vācamīṅkhayaḥ |
(20,137.6 ) somaḥ patī rayīṇāṃ sakhendrasya divedive ||6||

(20,137.7 ) ava drapso aṃśumatīmatiṣṭhadiyānaḥ kṛṣṇo daśabhiḥ sahasraiḥ |
(20,137.7 ) āvattamindraḥ śacyā dhamantamapa snehitīrnṛmaṇā adhatta ||7||

(20,137.8 ) drapsamapaśyaṃ viṣuṇe carantamupahvare nadyo aṃśumatyāḥ |
(20,137.8 ) nabho na kṛṣṇamavatasthivāṃsamiṣyāmi vo vṛṣaṇo yudhyatājau ||8||

(20,137.9 ) adha drapso aṃśumatyā upasthe'dhārayattanvaṃ titviṣāṇaḥ |
(20,137.9 ) viśo adevīrabhyācarantīrbṛhaspatinā yujendraḥ sasāhe ||9||

(20,137.10 ) tvaṃ ha tyatsaptabhyo jāyamāno'śatrubhyo abhavaḥ śatrurindra |
(20,137.10 ) gūlhe dyāvāpṛthivī anvavindo vibhumadbhyo bhuvanebhyo raṇaṃ dhāḥ ||10||

(20,137.11 ) tvaṃ ha tyadapratimānamojo vajreṇa vajrin dhṛṣito jaghantha |
(20,137.11 ) tvaṃ śuṣṇasyāvātiro vadhatraistvaṃ gā indra śacyedavindaḥ ||11||

(20,137.12 ) tamindraṃ vājayāmasi mahe vṛtrāya hantave |
(20,137.12 ) sa vṛṣā vṛṣabho bhuvat||12||

(20,137.13 ) indraḥ sa dāmane kṛta ojiṣṭhaḥ sa made hitaḥ |
(20,137.13 ) dyumnī ślokī sa somyaḥ ||13||

(20,137.14 ) girā vajro na saṃbhṛtaḥ sabalo anapacyutaḥ |
(20,137.14 ) vavakṣa ṛṣvo astṛtaḥ ||14||



(20,138.1 ) mahāmindro ya ojasā parjanyo vṛṣṭimāmiva |
(20,138.1 ) stomairvatsasya vāvṛdhe ||1||

(20,138.2 ) prajāmṛtasya piprataḥ pra yadbharanta vahnayaḥ |
(20,138.2 ) viprā ṛtasya vāhasā ||2||

(20,138.3 ) kaṇvāḥ indraṃ yadakrata stomairyajñasya sādhanam |
(20,138.3 ) jāmi bruvata āyudham ||3||



(20,139.1 ) ā nūnamaśvinā yuvaṃ vatsasya gantamavase |
(20,139.1 ) prāsmai yachatamavṛkaṃ pṛthu chardiryuyutaṃ yā arātayaḥ ||1||

(20,139.2 ) yadantarikṣe yaddivi yatpañca mānuṣāmanu |
(20,139.2 ) nṛmnaṃ taddhattamaśvinā ||2||

(20,139.3 ) ye vāṃ daṃsāṃsyaśvinā viprāsaḥ parimāmṛśuḥ |
(20,139.3 ) evetkāṇvasya bodhatam ||3||

(20,139.4 ) ayaṃ vāṃ gharmo aśvinā stomena pari ṣicyate |
(20,139.4 ) ayaṃ somo madhumān vājinīvasū yena vṛtraṃ ciketathaḥ ||4||

(20,139.5 ) yadapsu yadvanaspatau yadoṣadhīṣu purudaṃsasā kṛtam |
(20,139.5 ) tena māviṣṭamaśvinā ||5||



(20,140.1 ) yan nāsatyā bhuraṇyatho yadvā deva bhiṣajyathaḥ |
(20,140.1 ) ayaṃ vāṃ vatso matibhirna vindhate haviṣmantaṃ hi gachathaḥ ||1||

(20,140.2 ) ā nūnamaśvinorṛṣi stomaṃ ciketa vāmayā |
(20,140.2 ) ā somaṃ madhumattamaṃ gharmaṃ siñcādatharvaṇi ||2||

(20,140.3 ) ā nūnaṃ raghuvartaniṃ rathaṃ tiṣṭhātho aśvinā |
(20,140.3 ) ā vāṃ stomā ime mama nabho na cucyavīrata ||3||

(20,140.4 ) yadadya vāṃ nāsatyokthairācucyuvīmahi |
(20,140.4 ) yadvā vāṇībhiraśvinevetkaṇvasya bodhatam ||4||

(20,140.5 ) yadvāṃ kakṣīvāmuta yadvyaśva ṛṣiryadvāṃ dīrghatamā juhāva |
(20,140.5 ) pṛthī yadvāṃ vainyaḥ sādaneṣvevedato aśvinā cetayethām ||5||



(20,141.1 ) yātaṃ chardiṣpā uta paraspā bhūtaṃ jagatpā uta nastanūpā |
(20,141.1 ) vartistokāya tanayāya yātam ||1||

(20,141.2 ) yadindreṇa sarathaṃ yātho aśvinā yadvā vāyunā bhavathaḥ samokasā |
(20,141.2 ) yadādityebhirṛbhubhiḥ sajoṣasā yadvā viṣṇorvikramaṇeṣu tiṣṭhathaḥ ||2||

(20,141.3 ) yadadyāśvināvahaṃ huveya vājasātaye |
(20,141.3 ) yatpṛtsu turvaṇe sanastacchreṣṭhamaśvinoravaḥ ||3||

(20,141.4 ) ā nūnaṃ yātamaśvinemā havyāni vāṃ hitā |
(20,141.4 ) ime somāso adhi turvaśe yadāvime kaṇveṣu vāmatha ||4||

(20,141.5 ) yan nāsatyā parāke arvāke asti bheṣajam |
(20,141.5 ) tena nūnaṃ vimadāya pracetasā chardirvatsāya yachatam ||5||



(20,142.1 ) abhutsyu pra devyā sākaṃ vācāhamaśvinoḥ |
(20,142.1 ) vyāvardevyā matiṃ vi rātiṃ martyebhyaḥ ||1||

(20,142.2 ) pra bodhayoṣo aśvinā pra devi sūnṛte mahi |
(20,142.2 ) pra yajñahotarānuṣakpra madāya śravo bṛhat||2||

(20,142.3 ) yaduṣo yāsi bhānunā saṃ sūryeṇa rocase |
(20,142.3 ) ā hāyamaśvino ratho vartiryāti nṛpāyyam ||3||

(20,142.4 ) yadāpītāso aṃśavo gāvo na duhra ūdhabhiḥ |
(20,142.4 ) yadvā vāṇīranuṣata pra devayanto aśvinā ||4||

(20,142.5 ) pra dyumnāya pra śavase pra nṛṣāhyāya śarmaṇe |
(20,142.5 ) pra dakṣāya pracetasā ||5||

(20,142.6 ) yan nūnaṃ dhībhiraśvinā pituryonā niṣīdathaḥ |
(20,142.6 ) yadvā sumnebhirukthyā ||6||



(20,143.1 ) taṃ vāṃ rathaṃ vayamadyā huvema pṛthujrayamaśvinā saṃgatiṃ goḥ |
(20,143.1 ) yaḥ sūryāṃ vahati vandhurāyurgirvāhasaṃ purutamaṃ vasūyum ||1||

(20,143.2 ) yuvaṃ śriyamaśvinā devatā tāṃ divo napātā vanathaḥ śacībhiḥ |
(20,143.2 ) yuvorvapurabhi pṛkṣaḥ sacante vahanti yatkakuhāso rathe vām ||2||

(20,143.3 ) ko vāmadyā karate rātahavya ūtaye vā sutapeyāya vārkaiḥ |
(20,143.3 ) ṛtasya vā vanuṣe pūrvyāya namo yemāno aśvinā vavartat||3||

(20,143.4 ) hiraṇyayena purubhū rathenemaṃ yajñaṃ nāsatyopa yātam |
(20,143.4 ) pibātha in madhunaḥ somyasya dadhatho ratnaṃ vidhate janāya ||4||

(20,143.5 ) ā no yātaṃ divo acha pṛthivyā hiraṇyayena suvṛtā rathena |
(20,143.5 ) mā vāmanye ni yaman devayantaḥ saṃ yaddade nābhiḥ pūrvyā vām ||5||

(20,143.6 ) nū no rayiṃ puruvīraṃ bṛhantaṃ dasrā mimāthāmubhayeṣvasme |
(20,143.6 ) naro yadvāmaśvinā stomamāvantsadhastutimājamīlhāso agman ||6||

(20,143.7 ) iheha yadvāṃ samanā papṛkṣe seyamasme sumatirvājaratnā |
(20,143.7 ) uruṣyataṃ jaritāraṃ yuvaṃ ha śritaḥ kāmo nāsatyā yuvadrik||7||

(20,143.8 ) madhumatīroṣadhīrdyāva āpo madhuman no bhavatvantarikṣam |
(20,143.8 ) kṣetrasya patirmadhumān no astvariṣyanto anvenaṃ carema ||8||

(20,143.9 ) panāyyaṃ tadaśvinā kṛtaṃ vāṃ vṛṣabho divo rajasaḥ pṛthivyāḥ |
(20,143.9 ) sahasraṃ śaṃsā uta ye gaviṣṭau sarvāmittāmupa yātā pibadhyai ||9||
